Explanatory Remarks:

1. This text includes only Nāradapurāṇa chapters 176-292, as the remaining chapters are already available in the Gretil.
2. Structural elements are enclosed in <>.

Text:

<176>

atha ṣaṭsaptatyadhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
matsya uvāca
evamastviti saṃhṛṣṭaḥ śakrastridaśavardhanaḥ
saṃdideśāgrataḥ somaṃ yuddhāya śiśirāyudham || 1 ||
gaccha soma sahāyatvaṃ kuru pāśadharasya vai
asurāṇāṃ vināśāya jayārthaṃ ca divaukasām || 2 ||
tvaṃ mattaḥ prativīryaśca jyotiṣāṃ ceśvareśvaraḥ
tvanmayaṃ sarvalokeṣu rasaṃ rasavido viduḥ || 3 ||
kṣayavṛddhī tava vyakte sāgarasyeva maṇḍale
parivartasyahorātraṃ kālaṃ jagati yojayan || 4 ||
lokacchāyāmayaṃ lakṣma tavāṅkaḥ śaśasaṃnibhaḥ
na viduḥ soma devāpi ye ca nakṣatrayonayaḥ || 5 ||
tvamādityapathādūrdhvaṃ jyotiṣāṃ copari sthitaḥ
tamaḥ protsārya mahasā bhāsayasyakhilaṃ jagat || 6 ||
śvetabhānurhimatanurjyotiṣāmadhipaḥ śaśī
adhikṛtkālayogātmā iṣṭo yajñaraso'vyayaḥ || 7 ||
oṣadhīśaḥ kriyāyonirharaśekharabhāktathā
śītāṃśuramṛtādhāraścapalaḥ śvetavāhanaḥ || 8 ||
tvaṃ kāntiḥ kāntivapuṣāṃ tvaṃ somaḥ somapāyinām
saumyastvaṃ sarvabhūtānāṃ timiraghnastvamṛkṣarāṭ || 9 ||
tadgaccha tvaṃ mahāsena varuṇena varūthinā
śamaya tvāsurīṃ māyāṃ yayā dahyāma saṃyuge || 10 ||
soma uvāca
yanmāṃ vadasi yuddhārthe devarāja varaprada
eṣa varṣāmi śiśiraṃ daityamāyāpakarṣaṇam || 11 ||
etānmacchītanirdagdhānpaśya tvaṃ himaveṣṭitān
vimāyānvimadāṃścaiva daityasiṃhānmahāhave || 12 ||
ityuktvā tārakādhīśaḥ sajaleśaḥ śivodakaiḥ
plāvayāmāsa sainyāni surāṇāṃ śāntivṛddhaye || 13 ||
teṣāṃ himakarotsṛṣṭāḥ sapāśā himavṛṣṭayaḥ
veṣṭayanti sma tānghorāndaityānmeghagaṇā iva || 14 ||
tau pāśaśītāṃśudharau varuṇendū mahābalau
jaghnuturhimapātaiśca pāśapātaiśca dānavān || 15 ||
dvāvambunāthau samare tau pāśahimayodhinau
mṛdhe ceraturambhombhobhiḥ kṣubdhāviva mahārṇavau || 16 ||
tābhyāmāplāvitaṃ sainyaṃ taddānavamadṛśyata
jagatsaṃvartakāmbhodaiḥ praviṣṭairiva saṃvṛtam || 17 ||
tāvudyatāmbunāthau tu śaśāṅkavaruṇāvubhau
śamayāmāsaturmāyāṃ devau daityendra nirmitām || 18 ||
śītāṃśujālanirdagdhāḥ pāśaiśca spanditā raṇe
na śekuścalituṃ daityā viśiraskā ivādra yaḥ || 19 ||
śītāṃśunihatāste tu daityāstoyahimārditāḥ
himāplāvitasarvāṅgā nirūṣmāṇa ivāgnayaḥ || 20 ||
teṣāṃ tu divi daityānāṃ viparītaprabhāṇi vai
vimānāni vicitrāṇi prapatantyutpatanti ca || 21 ||
tānpāśahastagrathitāṃśchāditāñchītaraśmibhiḥ
mayo dadarśa māyāvī dānavāndivi dānavaḥ || 22 ||
sa śilājālavitatāṃ khaḍgacarmāṭṭahāsinīm
pādapotkaṭakūṭāgrāṃ kandarākīrṇakānanām || 23 ||
siṃhavyāghragaṇākīrṇāṃ nadadbhirgajayūthapaiḥ
īhāmṛgagaṇākīrṇāṃ pavanāghūrṇitadrumām || 24 ||
nirmitāṃ svena yatnena kūjitāṃ divi kāmagām
prathitāṃ pārvatīṃ māyāmasṛjatsa samantataḥ || 25 ||
sāsiśabdaiḥ śilāvarṣaiḥ saṃpatadbhiśca pādapaiḥ
jaghāna devasaṃghāṃśca dānavāṃścāpyajīvayat || 26 ||
naiśākarī vāruṇī ca māye'ntardadhatustataḥ
asibhiścā''yasagaṇaiḥ kirandevagaṇānraṇe || 27 ||
sāśmayantrāyudhaghanā drumaparvatasaṃkaṭā
abhavadghorasaṃcārā pṛthivī parvatairiva || 28 ||
aśmanā prahatāḥ kecicchilābhiḥ śakalīkṛtāḥ
nāniruddho drumagaṇairdevo'dṛśyata kaścana || 29 ||
tadapadhvastadhanuṣaṃ bhagnapraharaṇāvilam
niṣprapatnaṃ surānīkaṃ varjayitvā gadādharam || 30 ||
sa hi yuddhagataḥ śrīmānīśo na sma vyakampata
sahiṣṇutvājjagatsvāmī na cukrodha gadādharaḥ || 31 ||
kālajñaḥ kālameghābhaḥ samīkṣankālamāhave
devāsuravimardaṃ tu dra ṣṭukāmastadā hariḥ || 32 ||
tato bhagavatā dṛṣṭau raṇe pāvakamārutau
coditau viṣṇuvākyena tau māyāmapakarṣatām || 33 ||
tābhyāmudbhrāntavegābhyāṃ pravṛddhābhyāṃ mahāhave
dagdhā sā pārvatī māyā bhasmībhūtā nanāśa ha || 34 ||
so'nilo'nalasaṃyuktaḥ so'nalaścānilākulaḥ
daityasenāṃ dadahaturyugānteṣviva mūrchitau || 35 ||
vāyuḥ pradhāvitastatra paścādagnistu mārutam
ceraturdānavānīke krīḍantāvanilānalau || 36 ||
bhasmāvayavabhūteṣu prapatatsūtpatatsu ca
dānavānāṃ vimāneṣu nipatatsu samantataḥ || 37 ||
vātaskandhāpaviddheṣu kṛtakarmaṇi pāvake
māyābandhe nivṛtte tu stūyamāne gadādhare || 38 ||
niṣprayatneṣu daityeṣu trailokye muktabandhane
saṃprahṛṣṭeṣu deveṣu sādhu sādhviti sarvaśaḥ || 39 ||
jaye daśaśatākṣasya daityānāṃ ca parājaye
dikṣu sarvāsu śuddhāsu pravṛtte dharmavistare || 40 ||
apāvṛte candra masi svasthānasthe divākare
prakṛtistheṣu lokeṣu triṣu cāritrabandhuṣu || 41 ||
yajamāneṣu bhūteṣu praśānteṣu ca pāpmasu
abhinnabandhane mṛtyau hūyamāne hutāśane || 42 ||
yajñaśobhiṣu deveṣu svargārthaṃ darśayatsu ca
lokapāleṣu sarveṣu dikṣu saṃyānavartiṣu || 43 ||
bhāve tapasi siddhānāmabhāve pāpakarmaṇām
devapakṣe pramudite daityapakṣe viṣīdati || 44 ||
tripādavigrahe dharme adharme pādavigrahe
apāvṛtte mahādvāre vartamāne ca satpathe || 45 ||
loke pravṛtte dharmeṣu saddharmeṣvāśrameṣu ca
prajārakṣaṇayukteṣu bhrājamāneṣu rājasu || 46 ||
praśāntakalmaṣe loke śānte tamasi dānave
agnimārutayostatra vṛtte saṅgrāmakarmaṇi || 47 ||
tanmayā vipulā lokāstābhyāṃ kṛtajayakriyāḥ
pūrvaṃ daityabhayaṃ śrutvā mārutāgnikṛtaṃ mahat || 48 ||
kālanemīti vikhyāto dānavaḥ pratyadṛśyata
bhāskarākāramukuṭaḥ śiñjitābharaṇāṅgadaḥ || 49 ||
mandarādri pratīkāśo mahārajatasaṃvṛtaḥ
śatapraharaṇodagraḥ śatabāhuḥ śatānanaḥ || 50 ||
śataśīrṣaḥ sthitaḥ śrīmāñchataśṛṅga ivācalaḥ
kakṣe mahati saṃvṛddho nidāgha iva pāvakaḥ || 52 ||
dhūmrakeśo haricchmaśruḥ saṃdaṣṭauṣṭhapuṭānanaḥ
trailokyāntaravistāri dhārayanvipulaṃ vapuḥ || 52 ||
bāhubhistulayanvyoma kṣipanpadbhyāṃ mahīdharān
īrayanmukhaniḥśvāsairvṛṣṭiyuktānbalāhakān || 53 ||
tiryagāyataraktākṣaṃ mandarodagravarcasam
didhakṣantamivā''yāntaṃ sarvāndevagaṇānmṛdhe || 54 ||
tarjayantaṃ suragaṇāṃśchādayantaṃ diśo daśa
saṃvartakāle tṛṣitaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ mṛtyumivotthitam || 55 ||
sutalenocchrayavatā vipulāṅguliparvaṇā
lambābharaṇapūrṇena kiṃciccalitakarmaṇā || 56 ||
ucchritenāgrahastena dakṣiṇena vapuṣmatā
dānavāndevanihatānuttiṣṭhadhvamiti bruvan || 57 ||
taṃ kālanemiṃ samare dviṣatāṃ kālaceṣṭitam
vīkṣante sma surāḥ sarve bhayavitrastalocanāḥ || 58 ||
taṃ vīkṣante sma bhūtāni kramantaṃ kālaneminam
trivikramādhikamataṃ nārāyaṇamivāparam || 59 ||
so'tyucchrayapuraḥpādamārutāghūrṇitāmbaraḥ
prakrāmannasuro yuddhe trāsayāmāsa devatāḥ || 60 ||
sa mayenāsurendre ṇa pariṣvaktastato raṇe
kālanemirbabhau daityaḥ saviṣṇuriva mandaraḥ || 61 ||
atha vivyathire devāḥ sarve śakrapurogamāḥ
kālanemiṃ samāyāntaṃ dṛṣṭvā kālamivāparam || 62 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe tārakāmayayuddhe ṣaṭsaptatyadhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 176 ||

<177>

atha saptasaptatyadhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
matsya uvāca
dānavānāmanīkeṣu kālanemirmahāsuraḥ
vyavardhata mahātejāstapānte jalado yathā || 1 ||
taṃ trailokyāntaragataṃ dṛṣṭvā te dānaveśvarāḥ
uttasthurapariśrāntāḥ pītvā'mṛtamanuttamam || 2 ||
te vītabhayasaṃtrāsā mayatārapurogamāḥ
tārakāmayasaṅgrāme satataṃ jitakāśinaḥ || 3 ||
rejurāyodhanagatā dānavā yuddhakāṅkṣiṇaḥ
mantramabhyasatāṃ teṣāṃ vyūhaṃ ca paridhāvatām || 4 ||
prekṣatāṃ cābhavatprītirdānavaṃ kālaneminam
ye tu tatraṃ mayasyā''sanmukhyā yuddhapuraḥsarāḥ || 5 ||
te tu sarve bhayaṃ tyaktvā hṛṣṭā yoddhumupasthitāḥ
mayastāro varāhaśca hayagrīvaśca vīryavān || 6 ||
vipracittisutaḥ śvetaḥ kharalambāvubhāvapi
ariṣṭo baliputraśca kiśorākhyastathaiva ca || 7 ||
svarbhānuścāmaraprakhyo vaktrayodhī mahāsuraḥ
ete'stravedinaḥ sarve sarve tapasi susthitāḥ || 8 ||
dānavāḥ kṛtino jagmuḥ kālanemiṃ tamuddhatam
te gadābhirbhuśuṇḍībhiścakrairatha paraśvadhaiḥ || 9 ||
kālakalpaiśca musalaiḥ kṣepaṇīyaiśca mudgaraiḥ
aśmabhiścādri sadṛśairgaṇḍaśailaiśca dāruṇaiḥ || 10 ||
paṭṭiśairbhindipālaiśca parighaiścottamāyasaiḥ
ghātanībhiḥ sugurvībhiḥ śataghnībhistathaiva ca || 11 ||
yugairyantraiśca nirmuktairmārgaṇairugratāḍitaiḥ
dorbhiścā''yatadīptaiśca prāsaiḥ pāśaiśca mūrchanaiḥ || 12 ||
bhujaṃgavadanaiḥ krūrairlelihānaiśca sāyakaiḥ
vajraiḥ praharaṇīyaiśca dīpyamānaiśca tomaraiḥ || 13 ||
vikośairasibhistīkṣṇaiḥ śūlaiśca śitanirmalaiḥ
daityāḥ saṃdīptamanasaḥ pragṛhītaśarāsanāḥ || 14 ||
tataḥ puraskṛtya tadā kālanemiṃ mahāhave
sā dīptaśastrapravarā daityānāṃ ruruce camūḥ || 15 ||
dyaurnimīlitasarvāṅgā ghanānīlāmbudāgame
devatānāmapi camūrmumude śakrapālitā || 16 ||
upetā sitakṛṣṇābhyāṃ tārābhyāṃ candra sūryayoḥ
vāyuvegavatī saumyā tārāgaṇapatākinī || 17 ||
toyadā viddhavasanā grahanakṣatrahāsinī
yamendra varuṇairguptā dhanadena ca dhīmatā || 18 ||
saṃpradīptāgninayanā nārāyaṇaparāyaṇā
sā samudrau ghasadṛśī divyā devamahācamūḥ || 19 ||
rarājāstravatī bhīmā yakṣagandharvaśālinī
tayoścamvostadānīṃ tu babhūva sa samāgamaḥ || 20 ||
dyāvāpṛthivyoḥ saṃyogo yathā syādyugaparyaye
tadyuddhamabhavaddhoraṃ devadānavasaṃkulam || 21 ||
kṣamāparākramaparaṃ darpasya vinayasya ca
niścakramurbalābhyāṃ tu bhīmāstatra surāsurāḥ || 22 ||
pūrvāparābhyāṃ saṃrabdhāḥ sāgarābhyāmivāmbudāḥ
tābhyāṃ balābhyāṃ saṃhṛṣṭāśceruste devadānavāḥ || 23 ||
vanābhyāṃ pārvatīyābhyāṃ puṣpitābhyāṃ yathā''gamāḥ
samājaghnustato bherīḥ śaṅkhāndadhmuranekaśaḥ || 24 ||
sa śabdo dyāṃ bhuvaṃ khaṃ ca diśaśca samapūrayat
jyāghātatalanirghoṣo dhanuṣāṃ kūjitāni ca || 25 ||
duṃdabhīnāṃ ca ninado daityamantardadhuḥ svanam
te'nyonyamabhisaṃpetuḥ pātayantaḥ parasparam || 26 ||
babhañjurbāhubhirbāhūndvaṃdvamanye yuyutsavaḥ
devāstu cāśaniṃ ghoraṃ parighāṃścottamāyasān || 27 ||
nistriṃśānsasṛjuḥ saṃkhye gadā gurvīśca dānavāḥ
gadānipātairbhagnāṅgā bāṇaiśca śakalīkṛtāḥ || 28 ||
paripeturbhṛśaṃ kecitpunaḥ kecittu jaghnire
tato rathaiḥ saturagairvimānaiścā''śugāmibhiḥ || 29 ||
samīyuste susaṃrabdhā roṣādanyonyamāhave
saṃvartamānāḥ samare saṃdaṣṭauṣṭhapuṭānanāḥ || 30 ||
rathā rathairnirudhyante pādātāśca padātibhiḥ
teṣāṃ rathānāṃ tumulaḥ sa śabdaḥ śabdavāhinām || 31 ||
nabhonabhaśca hi yathā nabhasyairjaladasvanaiḥ
babhañjustu rathānkecitkecitsaṃmarditā rathaiḥ || 32 ||
saṃbādhamanye saṃprāpya na śekuścalitu rathāḥ
anyonyamanye samare dorbhyāmutkṣipya daṃśitāḥ || 33 ||
saṃhrādamānāmaraṇā jaghnustatrāpi carmiṇaḥ
astrairanye vinirbhinnā vemū raktaṃ hatā yudhi || 34 ||
kṣarajjalānāṃ sadṛśā jaladānāṃ samāgame
tadastraśastragrathitaṃ kṣiptotkṣiptagadāvilam || 35 ||
devadānavasaṃkṣubdhaṃ saṃkulaṃ yuddhamābabhau
taddānavamahāmeghaṃ devāyudhavirājitam || 36 ||
anyonyabāṇavarṣeṇa yuddhadurdinamābabhau
etasminnantare kruddhaḥ kālanemiḥ sa dānavaḥ || 37 ||
vyavardhata samudrau ghaiḥ pūryamāṇa ivāmbudaḥ
tasya vidyuccalāpīḍāḥ pradīptāśanivarṣiṇaḥ || 38 ||
gātrairnāgagiriprakhyā vinipeturbalāhakāḥ
krodhānniḥśvasatastasya bhrūbhedasvedavarṣiṇaḥ || 39 ||
sāgnisphuliṅgapratatā mukhānniṣpeturarciṣaḥ
tiryagūrdhvaṃ ca gagane vavṛdhustasya bāhavaḥ || 40 ||
parvatādiva niṣkrāntāḥ pañcāsyā iva pannagāḥ
so'strajālairbahuvidhairdhanurbhiḥ parighairapi || 41 ||
divyamākāśamāvavre parvatairucchritairiva
so'niloddhūtavasanastasthau saṅgrāmalālasaḥ || 42 ||
saṃdhyātapagrastaśilaḥ sākṣānmerurivācalaḥ
uruvegapramathitaiḥ śailaśṛṅgāgrapādapaiḥ || 43 ||
apātayaddevagaṇānvajreṇeva mahāgirīn
bahubhiḥ śastranistriṃśaiśchinnabhinnaśiroruhāḥ || 44 ||
na śekuścalituṃ devāḥ kālanemihatā yudhi
muṣṭibhirnihatāḥ kecitkecittu vidalīkṛtāḥ || 45 ||
yakṣagandharvapatayaḥ petuḥ saha mahoragaiḥ
tena vitrāsitā devāḥ samare kālaneminā || 46 ||
na śekuryatnavanto'pi yatnaṃ kartuṃ vicetasaḥ
tena śakraḥ sahasrākṣaḥ spanditaḥ śarabandhanaiḥ || 47 ||
airāvatagataḥ saṃkhye calituṃ na śaśāka ha
nirjalāmbhodasadṛśo nirjalārṇavasaprabhaḥ || 48 ||
nirvyāpāraḥ kṛtastena vipāśo varuṇo mṛdhe
raṇe vaiśravaṇastena parighaiḥ kāmarūpiṇā || 49 ||
vittado'pi kṛtaḥ saṃkhye nirjitaḥ kālaneminā
yamaḥ sarvaharastena mṛtyupraharaṇo raṇe || 50 ||
yāmyāmavasthāṃ saṃtyajya bhītaḥ svāṃ diśamāviśat
sa lokapālānutsārya kṛtvā teṣāṃ ca karma tat || 51 ||
dikṣu sarvāsu dehaṃ svaṃ caturdhā vidadhe tadā
sa nakṣatrapathaṃ gatvā divyaṃ svarbhānudarśanam || 52 ||
jahāra lakṣmīṃ somasya ta cāsya viṣayaṃ mahat
cālayāmāsa dīptāṃśuṃ svargadvārātsa bhāskaram || 53 ||
sāyanaṃ cāsya viṣayaṃ jahāra dinakarma ca
so'gniṃ devamukhaṃ dṛṣṭvā cakārā''tmamukhāśrayam || 54 ||
vāyuṃ ca tarasā jitvā cakārā''tmavaśānugam
sa samudrā nsamānīya sarvāśca sarito balāt || 55 ||
cakārā''tmamukhe vīryāddehabhūtāśca sindhavaḥ
apaḥ svavaśagāḥ kṛtvā divijā yāśca bhūmijāḥ || 56 ||
sa svayaṃ bhūrivā''bhāti mahābhūtapatiryathā
sarvalokamayo daityaḥ sarvabhūtabhayāvahaḥ || 57 ||
sa lokapālaikavapuścandrā dityagrahātmavān
sthāpayāmāsa jagatīṃ suguptāṃ dharaṇīdharaiḥ || 58 ||
pāvakānilasaṃpāto rarāja yudhi dānavaḥ
pārameṣṭhye sthitaḥ sthāne lokānāṃ prabhavāpyaye
taṃ tuṣṭuvurdaityagaṇā devā iva pitāmaham || 59 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe tārakāmayayuddhe saptasaptatyadhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 177 ||

<178>

athāṣṭasaptatyadhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
matsya uvāca
pañca taṃ nābhyavartanta viparītena karmaṇā
vedo dharmaḥ kṣamā satyaṃ śrīśca nārāyaṇāśrayā || 1 ||
sa teṣāmanupasthānātsakrodho dānaveśvaraḥ
vaiṣṇavaṃ padamanvicchanyayau nārāyaṇāntikam || 2 ||
sa dadarśa suparṇasthaṃ śaṅkhacakragadādharam
dānavānāṃ vināśāya bhrāmayantaṃ gadāṃ śubhām || 3 ||
sajalāmbhodasadṛśaṃ vidyutsadṛśavāsasam
svārūḍhaṃ svarṇapakṣāḍhyaṃ śikhinaṃ kāśyapaṃ khagam || 4 ||
dṛṣṭvā daityavināśāya raṇe svasthamavasthitam
dānavo viṣṇumakṣobhyaṃ babhāṣe kṣubdhamānasaḥ || 5 ||
ayaṃ sa ripurasmākaṃ pūrveṣāṃ prāṇanāśanaḥ
arṇavāvāsinaścaiva madhorvai kaiṭabhasya ca || 6 ||
ayaṃ sa vigraho'smākamāśāmyaḥ kila kathyate
anena saṃyugeṣvadya dānavā bahavo hatāḥ || 7 ||
ayaṃ sa nirghṛṇo loke strībālanirapatrapaḥ
yena dānavanārīṇāṃ sīmantoddharaṇaṃ kṛtam || 8 ||
ayaṃ sa viṣṇurdevānāṃ vaikuṇṭhaśca divaukasām
ananto bhogināmapsu svapannādyaḥ svayaṃbhuvaḥ || 9 ||
ayaṃ sa nātho devānāmasmābhirvyathitātmanām
asya krodhaṃ samāsādya hiraṇyakaśipurhataḥ || 10 ||
asya cchāyāmupāśritya devā makhamukhe śritāḥ
ājyaṃ maharṣibhirdattamaśnuvanti vidhau hutam || 11 ||
ayaṃ sa nidhane hetuḥ sarveṣāmamaradviṣām
yasya vaktraṃ praviṣṭāni kulānyasmākamāhave || 12 ||
ayaṃ sa kila yuddheṣu surārthe tyaktajīvitaḥ
savitustejasā tulyaṃ cakraṃ kṣipati śatruṣu || 13 ||
ayaṃ sa kālo daityānāṃ kālabhūtaḥ samāsthitaḥ
atikrāntasya kālasya phalaṃ prāpsyati keśavaḥ || 14 ||
diṣṭyedānīṃ samakṣaṃ me viṣṇureṣa samāgataḥ
adya madbāhuniṣpiṣṭo māmeva praṇayiṣyati || 15 ||
yāsyāmyapacitiṃ diṣṭyā pūrveṣāmadya saṃyuge
imaṃ nārāyaṇaṃ hatvā dānavānāṃ bhayāvaham || 16 ||
kṣiprameva haniṣyāmi raṇe'maragaṇāṃstataḥ
jātyantaragato'pyeṣa bādhate dānavānmṛdhe || 17 ||
eṣo'nantaḥ purā bhūtvā padmanābha iti śrutaḥ
jaghānaikārṇave ghore tāvubhau madhukaiṭabhau || 18 ||
dvidhā bhūtaṃ vapuḥ kṛtvā siṃhasyārdhaṃ narasya ca
pitaraṃ me jaghānaiko hiraṇyakaśipuṃ purā || 19 ||
śubhaṃ garbhamadhattainamaditirdevatāraṇiḥ
trīn lokānujjahāraikaḥ kramamāṇastribhiḥ kramaiḥ || 20 ||
bhūyastvidānīṃ saṅgrāme saṃprāpte tārakāmaye
mayā saha samāgamya saha devairvinaṅkṣyati || 21 ||
evamuktvā bahuvidhaṃ kṣipannārāyaṇaṃ raṇe
vāgbhirapratirūpābhiryuddhamevābhyarocayat || 22 ||
kṣipyamāṇo'surendre ṇa na cukopa gadādharaḥ
kṣamābalena mahatā sasmitaṃ cedamabravīt || 23 ||
alpaṃ darpabalaṃ daitya sthiramakrodhajaṃ balam
hatastvaṃ darpajairdoṣairhitvā yadbhāṣase kṣamām || 24 ||
adhīrastvaṃ mama mato dhigetattava vāgbalam
na yatra puruṣāḥ santi tatra garjanti yoṣitaḥ || 25 ||
ahaṃ tvāṃ daitya paśyāmi pūrveṣāṃ mārgagāmiṇam
prajāpatikṛtaṃ setuṃ bhittvā kaḥ svastimānvrajet || 26 ||
adya tvāṃ nāśayiṣyāmi devavyāpāraghātakam
sveṣu sveṣu ca sthāneṣu sthāpayiṣyāmi devatāḥ || 27 ||
evaṃ bruvati vākyaṃ tu mṛdhe śrīvatsadhāriṇi
jahāsa dānavaḥ krodhāddhastāṃścakre sahāyudhān || 28 ||
sa bāhuśatamudyamya sarvāstragrahaṇaṃ raṇe
krodhāddviguṇaraktākṣo viṣṇuṃ vakṣasyatāḍayat || 29 ||
dānavāścāpi samare mayatārapurogamāḥ
udyatāyudhanistriṃśā viṣṇumabhyadra vanraṇe || 30 ||
sa tāḍyamāno'tibalairdaityaiḥ sarvodyatāyudhaiḥ
na cacāla tato yuddhe'kampamāna ivācalaḥ || 31 ||
saṃsaktaśca suparṇena kālanemī mahāsuraḥ
sarvaprāṇena mahatīṃ gadāmudyamya bāhubhiḥ || 32 ||
ghorāṃ jvalantīṃ mumuce saṃrabdho garuḍopari
karmaṇā tena daityasya viṣṇurvismayamāviśat || 33 ||
yadā tena suparṇasya pātitā mūrdhni sā gadā
suparṇaṃ vyathitaṃ dṛṣṭvā kṣataṃ ca vapurātmanaḥ || 34 ||
krodhasaṃraktanayano vaikuṇṭhaścakramādade
vyavardhata sa vegena suparṇena samaṃ vibhuḥ || 35 ||
bhujāścāsya vyavardhanta vyāpnuvanto diśo daśa
pradiśaścaiva khaṃ gāṃ vai pūrayāmāsa keśavaḥ || 36 ||
vavṛdhe ca punarlokānkrāntukāma ivaujasā
tarjanāyāsurendrā ṇāṃ vardhamānaṃ nabhastale || 38 ||
ṛṣayaścaiva gandharvāstuṣṭuvurmadhusūdanam
sarvānkirīṭena lihansābhramambaramambaraiḥ || 39 ||
padbhyāmākramya vasudhāṃ diśaḥ pracchādya bāhubhiḥ
sa sūryakaratulyābhaṃ sahasrāramarikṣayam || 40 ||
dīptāgnisadṛśaṃ ghoraṃ darśanena sudarśanam
suvarṇareṇuparyantaṃ vajranābhaṃ bhayāvaham || 41 ||
medosthimajjārudhiraiḥ siktaṃ dānavasaṃbhavaiḥ
advitīyapraharaṇaṃ kṣuraparyantamaṇḍalam || 42 ||
snagdāmamālāvitataṃ kāmagaṃ kāmarūpiṇam
svayaṃ svayaṃbhuvā sṛṣṭaṃ bhayadaṃ sarvavidviṣām || 43 ||
maharṣiroṣairāviṣṭaṃ nityamāhavadarpitam
kṣepaṇādyasya muhyanti lokāḥ sasthāṇujaṅgamāḥ || 44 ||
kravyādāni ca bhūtāni tṛptiṃ yānti mahāmṛdhe
tadapratimakarmograṃ samānaṃ sūryavarcasā || 45 ||
cakramudyamya samare krodhadīpto gadādharaḥ
sa muṣṇandānavaṃ tejaḥ samare svena tejasā || 46 ||
ciccheda bāhuṃ cakreṇa śrīdharaḥ kālaneminaḥ
tacca vaktraśataṃ ghoraṃ sāgnipūrṇāṭṭahāsi vai || 47 ||
tasya daityasya cakreṇa pramamātha balāddhariḥ
sa cchinnabāhurviśirā na prākampata dānavaḥ || 48 ||
kabandho'vasthitaḥ saṃkhye viśākha iva pādapaḥ
saṃvitatya mahāpakṣau vāyoḥ kṛtvā samaṃ javam || 49 ||
urasā pātayāmāsa garuḍaḥ kālaneminam
sa tasya deho vimukho vibāhuḥ khātparibhraman || 50 ||
nipapāta divaṃ tyaktvā kṣobhayandharaṇītalam
tasminnipatite daitye devāḥ sarṣigaṇāstadā || 51 ||
sādhu sādhviti vaikuṇṭhaṃ sametāḥ pratyapūjayan
apare ye tu daityāśca yuddhe dṛṣṭaparākramāḥ || 52 ||
te sarve bāhubhirvyāptā na śekuścalituṃ raṇe
kāṃścitkeśeṣu jagrāha kāṃścitkaṇṭheṣu pīḍayan || 52 ||
cakarṣa kasyacidvaktraṃ madhye'gṛhṇādathāparam
te gadā cakranirdagdhā gatasattvā gatāsavaḥ || 53 ||
gaganādbhraṣṭasarvāṅgā nipeturdharaṇītale
teṣu daityeṣu sarveṣu hateṣu puruṣottamaḥ || 54 ||
tasthau śakrapriyaṃ kṛtvā kṛtakarmā gadādharaḥ
tasminvimarde saṅgrāme nivṛtte tārakāmaye || 55 ||
taṃ deśamājagāmā''śu brahmā lokapitāmahaḥ
sarvairbrahmarṣibhiḥ sārdhaṃ gandharvāpsarasāṃ gaṇaiḥ || 56 ||
devadevo hariṃ devaṃ pūjayanvākyamabravīt
kṛtaṃ deva mahatkarma surāṇāṃ śalyamuddhṛtam
vadhenānena daityānāṃ vayaṃ ca paritoṣitāḥ || 57 ||
yo'yaṃ tvayā hato viṣṇo kālanemirmahāsuraḥ
tvameko'sya mṛdhe hantā nānyaḥ kaścana vidyate || 58 ||
eṣa devānparibhavam̐ llokāṃśca sacarācarān
ṛṣīṇāṃ kadanaṃ kṛtvā māmapi prati garjati || 59 ||
tadanena tavāgryeṇa parituṣṭo'smi karmaṇā
yadayaṃ kālakalpastu kālanemirnipātitaḥ || 60 ||
tadāgaccha svabhavanaṃ gacchāmi divamuttamam
brahmarṣayastvāṃ tatrasthāḥ pratīkṣante sadogatāḥ || 61 ||
kaṃ cāhaṃ tava dāsyāmi varaṃ varavatāṃ vara
sureṣvatha ca daityeṣu varāṇāṃ varado bhavān || 62 ||
niryātayaitastrilokyaṃ sphītaṃ nihatakaṇṭakam
asminneva mṛdhe viṣṇo śakrāya sumahātmane || 63 ||
evamukto bhagavatā brahmaṇā hariravyayaḥ
devāñchakramukhānsarvānuvāca śubhayā girā || 64 ||
viṣṇuruvāca
śṛṇvantu tridaśāḥ sarve yāvanto'tra samāgatāḥ
śravaṇāvahitaiḥ śrotraiḥ puraskṛtya puraṃdaram || 65 ||
asmābhiḥ samare sarve kālanemimukhā hatāḥ
dānavā vikramopetāḥ śakrādapi mahattarāḥ || 66 ||
asminmahati saṅgrāme daiteyau dvau viniḥsṛtau
virocanaśca daityendraḥ svarbhānuśca mahāgrahaḥ || 67 ||
svāṃ diśaṃ bhajatāṃ śakro diśaṃ varuṇa eva ca
yāmyāṃ yamaḥ pālayitāmuttarāṃ ca dhanādhipaḥ || 68 ||
ṛkṣaiḥ saha yathāyogaṃ gacchatāṃ caiva candra māḥ
abdamṛtumukhe sūryo bhajatāmayanaiḥ saha || 69 ||
ājyabhāgāḥ pravartantāṃ sadasyairabhipūjitāḥ
hūyantāmagnayo viprairvedadṛṣṭena karmaṇā || 70 ||
devāścāpyagnihomena svādhyāyena maharṣayaḥ
śrāddhena pitaraścaiva tṛptiṃ yāntu yathāsukham || 71 ||
vāyuścaratu mārgasthastridhā dīpyatu pāvakaḥ
trīṃstu varṇāṃśca lokāṃstrīṃstarpayaṃścā''tmajairguṇaiḥ || 72 ||
kratavaḥ saṃpravartantāṃ dīkṣaṇīyairdvijātibhiḥ
dakṣiṇāścopapādyantāṃ yājñikebhyaḥ pṛthakpṛthak || 73 ||
gāṃ tu sūryo rasānsomo vāyuḥ prāṇāṃśca prāṇiṣu
tarpayantaḥ pravartantāṃ sarva eva svakarmabhiḥ || 74 ||
yathāvadānupūrvyeṇa mahendra malayodbhavāḥ
trailokyamātaraḥ sarvāḥ samudraṃ yāntu sindhavaḥ || 75 ||
daityebhyastyajyatāṃ bhīśca śāntiṃ vrajata devatāḥ
svasti vo'stu gamiṣyāmi brahmalokaṃ sanātanam || 76 ||
svagṛhe svargaloke vā saṅgrāme vā viśeṣataḥ
viśrambho vo na mantavyo nityaṃ kṣudrā hi dānavāḥ || 77 ||
chidre ṣu praharantyete na teṣāṃ saṃsthitirdhruvā
saumyānāmṛjubhāvānāṃ bhavatāmārjavaṃ dhanam || 78 ||
evamuktvā suragaṇānviṣṇuḥ satyaparākramaḥ
jagāma brahmaṇā sārdhaṃ svalokaṃ tu mahāyaśāḥ || 79 ||
etadāścaryamabhavatsaṅgrāme tārakāmaye
dānavānāṃ ca viṣṇośca yanmāṃ tvaṃ paripṛṣṭavān || 80 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe padmodbhavaprādurbhāvasaṃgraho nāmāṣṭasaptatyadhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 178 ||

<179>

atha navasaptatyadhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
ṛṣaya ūcuḥ
śrutaḥ padmodbhavastāta vistareṇa tvayeritaḥ
samāsādbhavamāhātmyaṃ bhairavasyābhidhīyatām || 1 ||
sūta uvāca
tasyāpi devadevasya śṛṇudhvaṃ karma cottamam
āsīddaityo'ndhako nāma bhinnāñjanacayopamaḥ || 2 ||
tapasā mahatā yukto hyavadhyastridivaukasām
sa kadācinmahādevaṃ pārvatyā sahitaṃ prabhum || 3 ||
krīḍamānaṃ tadā dṛṣṭvā hartuṃ devīṃ pracakrame
tasya yuddhaṃ tadā ghoramabhavatsaha śaṃbhunā || 4 ||
avantiviṣaye ghore mahākālavanaṃ prati
tasminyuddhe tadā rudra ścāndhakenātipīḍitaḥ || 5 ||
mumuca bāṇamatyugraṃ nāmnā pāśupataṃ hi tat
rudra bāṇavinirbhedādrudhirādandhakasya tu || 6 ||
andhakāśca samutpannāḥ śataśo'tha sasraśaḥ
teṣāṃ vidāryamāṇānāṃ rudhirādapare punaḥ || 7 ||
babhūvurandhakā ghorā yairvyāptamakhilaṃ jagat
evaṃ māyāvinaṃ dṛṣṭvā taṃ ca devastadā'ndhakam || 8 ||
pānārthamandhakāsrasya so'sṛjanmāta rastadā
māheśvarīṃ tathā brāhmī kaumārī mālinī tathā || 9 ||
sauparṇī hyatha vāyavyā śākrī vai nairṛtī tathā
saurī saumyā śivā dūtī cāmuṇḍā cātha vāruṇī || 10 ||
vārāhī nārasiṃhī ca vaiṣṇavī ca calacchikhā
śatānandā bhagānandā picchilā bhagamālinī || 11 ||
balā cātibalā raktā surabhī mukhamaṇḍikā
mātṛnandā sunandā ca biḍālī śakunī tathā || 12 ||
revatī ca mahāraktā tathaiva pilapicchikā
jayā ca vijayā caiva jayantī cāparājitā || 13 ||
kālī caiva mahākālī dūtī caiva tathaiva ca
subhagā durbhagā caiva karālī nandinī tathā || 14 ||
aditiśca ditiścaiva mārī vai mṛtyureva ca
karṇamoṭī tathā grāmyā ulūkī ca ghaṭodarī || 15 ||
kapālī vajrahastā ca piśācī rākṣasī tathā
bhuśuṇḍī śāṃkarī caṇḍā lāṅgalī kuṭabhī tathā || 16 ||
kheṭā sulocanā dhūmrā ekavīrā karālinī
viśāladaṃṣṭriṇī śyāmā trijaṭī kukkuṭī tathā || 17 ||
vaināyakī ca vaitālī unmattodumbarī tathā
siddhiśca lelihānā ca kekarī gardabhī tathā || 18 ||
bhrukuṭī bahuputrī ca pretayānā viḍambinī
krauñcā śailamukhī caiva vinatā surasā danuḥ || 19 ||
uṣā rambhā menakā ca salilā citrarūpiṇī
svāhā svadhā vaṣaṭkārā dhṛtirjyeṣṭhā kapardinī || 20 ||
māyā vicitrarūpā ca kāmarūpā ca saṃgamā
mukhevilā maṅgalā ca mahānāsā mahāmukhī || 21 ||
kumārī rocanā bhīmā sadāhā sā madoddhatā
alambākṣī kālaparṇī kumbhakarṇī mahāsurī
keśinī śaṅkhinī lambā piṅgalā lohitāmukhī
ghaṇṭāravā'tha daṃṣṭrālā rocanā kākajaṅghikā || 23 ||
gokarṇikā'jamukhikā mahāgrīvā mahāmukhī
ulkāmukhī dhūmaśihā kampinī parikampinī || 24 ||
mohanā kalpanā kṣvelā nirbhayā bāhuśālinī
sarpakarṇī tathaikākṣī viśokā nandinī tathā || 25 ||
jyotsnāmukhī ca rabhasā nikumbhā raktakampanā
avikārā mahācitrā candra senā manoramā || 26 ||
adarśanā haratpāpā mātaṅgī lambemakhalā
abālā vañcanā kālī pramodā lāṅgalāvatī || 27 ||
cittā cittajalā koṇā śāntikā'ghavināśinī
lambastanī lambasaṭā visaṭā vāsacūrṇinī || 28 ||
skhalantī dīrghakeśī ca sucirā sundarī śubhā
ayomukhī kaṭumukhī krodhanī ca tathā'śanī || 29 ||
kuṭumbikā muktikā ca candri kā balamohinī
sāmānyā hāsinī lambā kovidārī samāsavī || 30 ||
śaṅkukarṇī mahānādā mahādevī mahodarī
hūṃkārī rudra susaṭā rudre śī bhūtaḍāmarī || 31 ||
piṇḍajihvā calajjvālā śivā jvālāmukhī tathā
etāścānyāśca deveśaḥ so'sṛjanmātarastadā || 32 ||
andhakānāṃ mahāghorāḥ papustadrudhiraṃ tadā
tato'ndhakāsṛjaḥ sarvāḥ parāṃ tṛptimupāgatāḥ || 33 ||
tāsu tṛptāsu saṃbhūtā bhūya evāndhakaprajāḥ
arditastairmahādevaḥ śūlamudgarapāṇibhiḥ || 34 ||
tataḥ sa śaṃkaro devastvandhakairvyākulīkṛtaḥ
jagāma śaraṇaṃ devaṃ vāsudevamajaṃ vibhum || 35 ||
tatastu bhagavānviṣṇuḥ sṛṣṭavāñśuṣkarevatīm
yā papau sakalaṃ teṣāmandhakānāmasṛkkṣaṇāt || 36 ||
yathā yathā ca rudhiraṃ pibantyandhakasaṃbhavam
tathā tathā'dhikaṃ devī saṃśuṣyati janādhipa || 37 ||
pīyamāne tayā teṣāmandhakānāṃ tathā'sṛji
andhakāstu kṣayaṃ nītāḥ sarve te tripurāriṇā || 38 ||
mūlāndhakaṃ tu vikramya tadā śarvastrilokadhṛk
cakāra vegācchūlāgne sa ca tuṣṭāva śaṃkaram || 39 ||
andhakastu mahāvīryastasya tuṣṭo'bhavadbhavaḥ
sāmīpyaṃ pradadau nityaṃ gaṇeśatvaṃ tathaiva ca || 40 ||
tato mātṛgaṇāḥ sarve śaṃkaraṃ vākyamabruvan
bhagavanbhakṣayiṣyāmaḥ sadevāsuramānuṣān
tvatprasādājjagatsarvaṃ tadanujñātumarhasi || 41 ||
śaṃkara uvāca
bhavatībhiḥ prajā sarvā rakṣaṇīyā na saṃśayaḥ
tasmādghorādabhiprāyānmanaśīghraṃ nivartyatām || 42 ||
ityevaṃ śaṃkareṇoktamanādṛtya vacastadā
bhakṣayāmāsuratyugrāstrailokyaṃ sacarācaram || 43 ||
trailokye bhakṣyamāṇe tu tadā mātṛgaṇena vai
nṛsiṃhamūrtiṃ deveśaṃ pradadhyau bhagavāñchivaḥ || 44 ||
anādinidhanaṃ devaṃ sarvalokabhavodbhavam
daityendra vakṣorudhiracarcitāgramahānakham || 45 ||
vidyujjihvaṃ mahādaṃṣṭraṃ sphuratkesarakaṇṭakam
kalpāntamārutakṣubdhaṃ saptārṇavasamasvanam || 46 ||
vajratīkṣṇanakhaṃ ghoramākarṇavyāditānanam
meruśailapratīkāśamudayārkasameṣaṇam || 47 ||
himādri śikharākāraṃ cārudaṃṣṭrojjvalānanam
nakhaniḥsṛtaroṣāgnijvālākesaramālinam || 48 ||
baddhāṅgadaṃ sumukuṭaṃ hārakeyūrabhūṣaṇam
śroṇīsūtreṇa mahatā kāñcanena virājitam || 49 ||
nīlotpaladalaśyāmaṃ vāsoyugavibhūṣaṇam
tejasā''krāntasakalabrahmāṇḍāgārasaṃkulam || 50 ||
pavanabhrāmyamāṇānāṃ hutahavyavahārciṣām
āvartasadṛśākāraiḥ saṃyuktaṃ dehalomajaiḥ || 51 ||
sarvapuṣpavicitrāṃ ca dhārayantaṃ mahāsrajam
sa dhyātamātro bhagavānpradadau tasya darśanam || 52 ||
yādṛśenaiva rūpeṇa dhyāto rudre ṇa dhīmatā
tādṛśenaiva rūpeṇa durnirīkṣyeṇa daivataiḥ || 53 ||
praṇipatya tu deveśaṃ tadā tuṣṭāva śaṃkaraḥ || 54 ||
śaṃkara uvāca
namaste'stu jagannātha narasiṃhavapurdhara
daityanāthāsṛjāpūrṇanakhaśuktivirājita || 55 ||
tataḥ sakalasaṃlagna hemapiṅgalavigraha
nato'smi padmanābha tvāṃ suraśakrajagadguro || 56 ||
kalpāntāmbhodanirghoṣa sūryakoṭisamaprabha
sahasrayamasaṃkrodha sahasrendra parākrama || 57 ||
sahasradhanadasphīta sahasravaruṇātmaka
sahasrakālaracita sahasraniyatendri ya || 58 ||
sahasrabhūrmahādhairya sahasrānantamūrtiman
sahasracandra pratima sahasragrahavikrama || 59 ||
sahasrarudra tejaska sahasrabrahmasaṃstuta
sahasrabāhuvegogra sahasrāsyanirīkṣaṇa
sahasrayantramathana sahasravadhamocana || 60 ||
andhakasya vināśāya yāḥ sṛṣṭā mātaro mayā
anādṛtya tu madvākyaṃ bhakṣayantyudyatāḥ prajāḥ || 61 ||
kṛtvā tāśca na śakto'haṃ saṃhartumaparājita
svayaṃ kṛtvā kathaṃ tāsāṃ vināśamabhikāraye || 62 ||
evamuktaḥ sa rudre ṇa narasiṃhavapurdharaḥ
sasarja devo jihvāyāstadā vāgīśvarīṃ hariḥ || 63 ||
hṛdayācca tathā māyā guhyācca bhavamālinī
asthibhyaśca tathā kālī sṛṣṭā pūrvaṃ mahātmanā || 64 ||
yayā tadrudhiraṃ pītamandhakānāṃ durātmanām
yā cāsminkathitā loke nāmataḥ śuṣkarevatī || 65 ||
dvātriṃśanmātaraḥ sṛṣṭā gātrebhyaścakriṇā tataḥ
tāsāṃ nāmāni vakṣyāmi tāni me gadataḥ śṛṇu || 66 ||
sarvāstāstu mahābhāgā ghaṇṭākarṇī tathaiva ca
trailokyamohinī puṇyā sarvasattvavaśaṃkarī || 67 ||
tathā ca cakrahṛdayā pañcamī vyomacariṇī
śaṅkhinī lekhinī caiva kālasaṃkarṣaṇī tathā || 68 ||
ityetāḥ pṛṣṭhagā rājanvāgīśānucarāḥ smṛtāḥ
saṃkarṣaṇī tathā'śvatthā bījabhāvā'parāṃjetā || 69 ||
kalyāṇī madhudaṃṣṭrī ca kamalotpalahastikā
iti devyaṣṭakaṃ rājanmāyānucaramucyate || 70 ||
ajitā sūkṣmahṛdayā vṛddhāveśāśmadaṃśanā
nṛsiṃhabhairavā bilvā garutmaddhṛdayā jayā || 71 ||
bhavamālinyanucarā ityaṣṭau nṛpa mātaraḥ
ākarṇanī saṃbhaṭā ca tathaivottaramālikā || 72 ||
jvālāmukhī bhīṣaṇikā kāmadhenuśca bālikā
tathā padmakarā rājanrevatyanucarāḥ smṛtāḥ || 73 ||
aṣṭau mahābalāḥ sarvā devagātrasamudbhavāḥ
trailokyasṛṣṭisaṃhārasamarthāḥ sarvadevatāḥ || 74 ||
tāḥ sṛṣṭamātrā devena kruddhā mātṛgaṇasya tu
pradhāvitā mahārāja krodhavisphāritekṣaṇāḥ || 75 ||
aviṣahyatamaṃ tāsāṃ dṛṣṭitejaḥ sudāruṇam
tameva śaraṇaṃ prāptā nṛsiṃho vākyamabravīt || 76 ||
yathā manuṣyāḥ paśavaḥ palayanti cirātsutān
jayanti te tathaivā'śu yathā vai devatāgaṇāḥ || 77 ||
bhavantyastu tathā lokānpālayantu mayeritāḥ
manujaiśca tathā devairyajadhvaṃ tripurāntakam || 78 ||
na ca bādhā prakartavyā ye bhaktāstripurāntake
ye ca māṃ saṃsmarantīha te ca rakṣyāḥ sadā narāḥ || 79 ||
balikarma kariṣyanti yuṣmākaṃ ye sadā narāḥ
sarvakāmapradāsteṣāṃ bhaviṣyadhvaṃ tathaiva ca || 80 ||
ucchāsanādikaṃ ye ca kathayanti mayeritam
te ca rakṣyāḥ sadā lokā rakṣitavyaṃ ca śāsanam || 81 ||
raudrīṃ caiva parāṃ mūrtiṃ mahādevaḥ pradāsyati
yuṣmanmukhyā mahādevyastaduktaṃ parirakṣyatha || 82 ||
mayā mātṛgaṇaḥ sṛṣṭo yo'yaṃ vigatasādhvasaḥ
eṣa nityaṃ viśālākṣo mayaiva saha raṃsyate || 83 ||
mayā sārdhaṃ tathā pūjāṃ narebhyaścaiva lapsyatha
pṛthaksupūjitā lokaiḥ sarvānkāmānpradāsyatha || 84 ||
śuṣkāṃ saṃpūjayiṣyanti ye ca putrārthino janāḥ
teṣāṃ putrapradā devī bhaviṣyati na saṃśayaḥ || 85 ||
evamuktvā tu bhagavānsaha mātṛgaṇane tu
jvālāmālākulavapustatraivāntaradhīyata || 86 ||
tatra tīrthaṃ samutpannaṃ kṛtaśauceti yajjaguḥ
tatrāpi pūrvajo devo jagadārtiharo haraḥ || 87 ||
raudra sya mātṛvargasya dattvā rudra stu pārthiva
raudrīṃ divyāṃ tanuṃ tatra mātṛmadhye vyavasthitaḥ || 88 ||
sapta tā mātaro devyaḥ sārdhanārīśvaraḥ śivaḥ
niveśya raudraṃ tatsthānaṃ tatraivāntaradhīyata || 89 ||
samātṛvargasya harasya mūrtiryadā yadā yāti ca tatsamīpe
deveśvarasyāpi nṛsiṃhamūrteḥ pūjāṃ vidhatte tripurāndhakāriḥ || 90 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe'ndhakavadho nāmaikonāśītyadhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 179 ||

<180>

athāśītyadhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
ṛṣaya ūcuḥ
śruto'ndhakavadhaḥ sūta yathāvattvadudīritaḥ
vārāṇasyāstu māhātmyaṃ śrotumicchāma sāṃpratam || 1 ||
bhagavānpiṅgalaḥ kena gaṇatvaṃ samupāgataḥ
annadatvaṃ ca saṃprāpto vārāṇasyāṃ mahādyutiḥ || 2 ||
kṣetrapālaḥ kathaṃ jātaḥ priyatvaṃ ca kathaṃ gataḥ
etadicchāma kathitaṃ śrotuṃ brahmasuta tvayā || 3 ||
sūta uvāca
śṛṇudhvaṃ vai yathā lebhe gaṇeśatvaṃ sa piṅgalaḥ
annadatvaṃ ca lokānāṃ sthānaṃ vārāṇasī tviha || 4 ||
pūrṇabhadra sutaḥ śrīmānāsīdyakṣaḥ pratāpavān
harikeśa iti khyāto brahmaṇyo dhārmikaśca ha || 5 ||
tasya janmaprabhṛtyeva śarve bhaktiranuttamā
tadāsīttannamaskārastanniṣṭhastatparāyaṇaḥ || 6 ||
āsīnaśca śayānaśca gacchaṃstiṣṭhannanuvrajan
bhuñjāno'tha pibanvā'pi rudra mevānvacintayat || 7 ||
tamevaṃ yuktamanasaṃ pūrṇabhadraḥ pitā'bravīt
na tvāṃ putramahaṃ manye durjāto yastvamanyathā || 8 ||
na hi yakṣakulīnānāmetadvṛttaṃ bhavatyuta
guhyakā bata yūyaṃ vai svabhāvātkrūracetasaḥ || 9 ||
kravyādāścaiva kiṃbhakṣā hiṃsāśīlāśca putraka
maivaṃ kārṣīrna te vṛttirevaṃ dṛṣṭā mahātmanā || 10 ||
svayaṃbhuvā yathā''diṣṭā tyaktavyā yadino bhavet
āśramāntarajaṃ karma na kuryurgṛhiṇastu tat || 11 ||
hitvā manuṣyabhāvaṃ ca karmabhirvividhaiścara
yattvamevaṃ vimārgastho manuṣyājjāta eva ca || 12 ||
yathāvadvividhaṃ teṣāṃ karma tajjātisaṃśrayam
mayā'pi vihitaṃ paśya karmaitannātra saṃśayaḥ || 13 ||
sūta uvāca
evamuktvā sa taṃ putraṃ pūrṇabhadraḥ pratāpavān
uvāca niṣṭhuraṃ kṣipraṃ gaccha putra yathecchasi || 14 ||
tataḥ sa nirgatastyaktvā gṛhaṃ saṃbandhinastathā
vārāṇasīṃ samāsādya tapastepe suduścaram || 15 ||
sthāṇubhūto hyanimiṣaḥ śuṣkakāṣṭhopalopamaḥ
saṃniyamyendri yagrāmamavātiṣṭhata niścalaḥ || 16 ||
atha tasyaiva tapasi saṃsthitasya mahātmanaḥ
sahasramekaṃ varṣāṇāṃ divyamapyabhyavartata || 17 ||
valmīkena samākrānto bhakṣyamāṇaḥ pipīlikaiḥ
vajrasūcimukhaistīkṣṇairvidhyamānastathaiva ca || 18 ||
nirmāṃsarudhiratvakca kundaśaṅkhendusaprabhaḥ
asthiśeṣo'bhavaccharvaṃ devaṃ vai cintayannapi || 19 ||
etasminnantare devī vyajñāpayata śaṃkaram || 20 ||
devyuvāca
udyānaṃ punarevedaṃ dra ṣṭumicchāmi sarvadā
kṣetrasya deva māhātmyaṃ śrotuṃ kautūhalaṃ hi me
yataśca priyametatte tathā'sya phalamuttamam || 21 ||
iti vijñāpito devaḥ śarvāṇyā parameśvaraḥ
sarvaṃ pṛṣṭhaṃ yathātathyamākhyātumupacakrame || 22 ||
nirjagāma ca deveśaḥ pārvatyā saha śaṃkaraḥ
udyānaṃ darśayāmāsa devyā devaḥ pinākadhṛk || 23 ||
devadeva uvāca
protphullanānāvidhagulmaśobhitaṃ latāpratānāvanataṃ manoharam
virūḍhapuṣpaiḥ paritaḥ priyaṅgubhiḥ supuṣpitaiḥ kaṇṭakitaiśca ketakaiḥ || 24 ||
tamālagulmairnicitaṃ sugandhibhiḥ sakarṇikārairbakulaiśca sarvaśaḥ
aśokapuṃnāgavaraiḥ supuṣpitairdvirephamālākulapuṣpasaṃcayaiḥ || 25 ||
kvacitpraphullāmbujareṇurūṣitairvihaṅgamaiścārukalapraṇādibhiḥ
vināditaṃ sārasamaṇḍanādibhiḥ pramattadātyūharutaiśca valgubhiḥ || 26 ||
kvacicca cakrāhvaravopanāditaṃ kvacicca kādambakadambakairyutam
kvacicca kāraṇḍavanādanāditaṃ kvacicca mattālikulākulīkṛtam || 27 ||
madākulābhistvamarāṅganādibhirniṣevitaṃ cārusugandhipuṣpam
kvacitsupuṣpaiḥ sahakāravṛkṣairlatopagūḍhaistilakadrumaiśca || 28 ||
pragītavidyādharasiddhacāraṇaṃ pravṛttanṛtyāpsarasāṃ gaṇākulam
prahṛṣṭanānāvidhapakṣisevitaṃ pramattahārītakulopanāditam || 29 ||
mṛgendra nādākulasattvamānasaiḥ kvacitkvaciddvaṃdvakadambakairmṛgaiḥ
praphullanānāvidhacārupaṅkajaiḥ sarastaḍāgairupaśobhitaṃ kvacit || 30 ||
nibiḍaniculanīlaṃ nīlakaṇṭhābhirāmaṃ madamuditavihaṅgavrātanādābhirāmam
kusumitataruśākhālīnamattadvirephaṃ navakisalayaśobhāśobhitaprāntaśākham || 31 ||
kvacicca dantikṣatacāruvīrudhaṃ kvacillatāliṅgitacāruvṛkṣakam
kvacidvilāsālasagāmibarhiṇaṃ niṣevitaṃ kiṃpuruṣavrajaiḥ kvacit || 32 ||
pārāvatadhvanivikūjitacāruśṛṅgairabhraṃkaṣaiḥ sitamanoharacārurūpaiḥ
ākīrṇapuṣpanikurambavimuktahāsairvibhrājitaṃ tridaśadevakulairanekaiḥ || 33 ||
phullotpalāgurusahasravitānayuktaistoyāśayaiḥ samanuśobhitadeyamārgam
mārgāntarāgalitapuṣpavicitrabhāktisaṃbaddhagulmaviṭapairvihagairupetam || 34 ||
tuṅgāgrairnīlapuṣpastabakabharanataprāntaśākhairaśokairmattālivrātagītaśrutisukhajananairbhāsitāntarmanojñaiḥ ।
rātrau candra sya bhāsā kusumitatilakairekatāṃ saṃprayātaṃ chāyāsuptaprabuddhasthitahariṇakulāluptadarbhāṅkurāgram || 35 ||
haṃsānāṃ pakṣapātapracalitakamalasvacchavistīrṇatoyaṃ
toyānāṃ tīrajātapravikacakadalīvāṭanṛtyanmayūram ।
sāyūraiḥ pakṣacandraiḥ kvacidapi patita rañjitakṣmāpradeśaṃ
deśe deśe vikīrṇapramuditavilasanmattahārītavṛkṣam || 36 ||
sāraṅgaiḥ kvacidapi sevitapradeśaṃ saṃchannaṃ kusumacayaiḥ kvacidvicitraiḥ
hṛṣṭābhiḥ kvacidapi kiṃnarāṅganābhiḥ kṣībābhiḥ samadhuragītavṛkṣakhaṇḍam || 37 ||
saṃsṛṣṭaiḥ kvacidupaliptakīrṇapuṣpairāvāsaiḥ parivṛtapādapaṃ munīnām
āmūlātphalanicitaiḥ kvacidviśālairuttuṅgaiḥ panasamahīruhairupetam || 38 ||
phullātimuktakalatāgṛhasiddhalīlaṃ siddhāṅganākanakanūpuranādaramyam
ramyapriyaṅgutarumañjarisaktabhṛṅgaṃ bhṛṅgāvalīskhalitacārukadambapuṣpam || 39 ||
puṣpotkarānilavighūrṇitapādapāgramagresarairbhuvi nipātitavaṃśagulmam
gulmāntaraprasṛtabhītamṛgīsamūhaṃ saṃmuhyatāṃ tanubhṛtāmapavargadātṛ || 40 ||
candrāṃ śujāladhavalaistilakairmanojñaiḥ sindūrakuṅkumakusumbhanibhairaśokaiḥ
cāmīkarapratisamairatha karṇikāraiḥ phullāravindaracitaṃ suviśālaśākhaiḥ || 41 ||
kvacidra jataparṇābhaiḥ kvacidvidrumasaṃnibhaiḥ
kvacitkāñcanasaṃkāśaiḥ puṣpairācitabhūtalam || 42 ||
punnāgesu dvijagaṇavirutaṃ raktāśokastabakabharanamitam
ramyopāntaśramaharapavanaṃ phullābjeṣu bhramaravilasitam || 43 ||
sakalabhuvanabhartā lokanāthastadānīṃ tuhinaśikhariputryāḥ sārdhamiṣṭairgaṇeśaiḥ
vividhataruviśālaṃ mattahṛṣṭānyapuṣṭamupavanataruramyaṃ darśayāmāsa devyāḥ || 44 ||
devyuvāca
udyānaṃ darśitaṃ deva śobhayā parayā yutam
kṣetrasya tu guṇānsarvānpunarvaktumihārhasi || 45 ||
asya kṣetrasya māhātmyamavimuktasya tattathā
śrutvā'pi hi na me tṛptirato bhūyo vadasva me || 46 ||
devadeva uvāca
idaṃ guhyatamaṃ kṣetraṃ sadā vārāṇasī mama
sarveṣāmeva bhūtānāṃ heturmokṣasya sarvadā || 47 ||
asminsiddhāḥ sadā devi madīyaṃ vratamāsthitāḥ
nānāliṅgadharā nityaṃ mama lokābhikāṅkṣiṇaḥ || 48 ||
abhyasyanti paraṃ yogaṃ muktātmāno jitendri yāḥ
nānāvṛkṣasamākīrṇe nānāvihagakūjite || 49 ||
kamalotpalapuṣpāḍhyaiḥ sarobhiḥ samalaṃkṛte
apsarogaṇagandharvaiḥ sadā saṃsevite śubhe || 50 ||
rocate me sadā vāso yena kāryeṇa tacchṛṇu
manmanā mama bhaktaśca mayi sarvārpitakriyaḥ || 51 ||
yathā mokṣamihā''pnoti hyanyatra na tathā kvacit
etanmama puraṃ divyaṃ guhyādguhyataraṃ mahat || 52 ||
brahmādayastu jānanti ye'pi siddhā mumukṣavaḥ
ataḥ priyatamaṃ kṣetraṃ tasmācceha ratirmama || 53 ||
vimuktaṃ na mayā yasmānmokṣyate vā kadā ca na
mahatkṣetramidaṃ tasmādavimuktamidaṃ smṛtam || 54 ||
naimiṣe'tha kurukṣetre gaṅgādvāre ca puṣkare
snānātsaṃsevitādvā'pi na mokṣaḥ prāpyate yataḥ || 55 ||
iha saṃprāpyate yena tata etadviśiṣyate
prayāge ca bhavenmokṣa iha vā matparigrahāt || 56 ||
prayāgādapi tīrthāgryādidameva mahatsmṛtam
jaigīṣavyaḥ parāṃ siddhiṃ yo gataḥ sa mahātapāḥ || 57 ||
asya kṣetrasya māhātmyādbhaktyā ca mama bhāvanāt
jaigīṣavyo mahāśreṣṭho yogināṃ sthānamiṣyate || 58 ||
dhyāyatastatra māṃ nityaṃ yogāgnirdīpyate bhṛśam
kaivalyaṃ paramaṃ yāti devānāmapi durlabham || 59 ||
avyaktaliṅgairmunibhiḥ sarvasiddhāntavedibhiḥ
iha saṃprāpyate mokṣo durlabho devadānavaiḥ || 60 ||
tebhyaścāhaṃ prayacchāmi bhogaiśvaryamanuttamam
ātmanaścaiva sāyujyamīpsitaṃ sthānameva ca || 61 ||
kuberastu mahāyakṣastathā sarvārpitakriyaḥ
kṣetrasaṃvasanādeva gaṇeśatvamavāpa ha || 62 ||
saṃvarto bhavitā yaśca so'pi bhakto mamaiva tu
ihaivā''rādhya māṃ devi siddhiṃ yāsyatyanuttamām || 63 ||
parāśarasuto yogī ṛṣirvyāso mahātapāḥ
dharmakartā bhaviṣyaśca vedasaṃsthāpravartakaḥ || 64 ||
raṃsyate so'pi padmākṣi kṣetre'sminmunipuṃgavaḥ
brahmā devarṣibhiḥ sārdhaṃ viṣṇurvāyurdivākaraḥ || 65 ||
devarājastathā śakro ye'pi cānye divaukasaḥ
upāsate mahātmānaḥ sarve māmeva suvrate || 66 ||
anye'pi yoginaḥ siddhāśchannarūpā mahāvratāḥ
ananyamanaso bhūtvā māmihopāsate sadā || 67 ||
alarkaśca purīmetāṃ matprasādādavāpsyati
sa caināṃ pūrvavatkṛtvā cāturvarṇyāśramākulām || 68 ||
sphītāṃ janasamākīrṇāṃ bhaktyā ca suciraṃ nṛpaḥ
mayi sarvārpitaprāṇo māmeva pratipatsyate || 69 ||
tataḥ prabhṛti cārvaṅgi ye'pi kṣetranivāsinaḥ
gṛhiṇo liṅgino vā'pi madbhaktā matparāyaṇāḥ || 70 ||
matprasādādbhajiṣyanti mokṣaṃ paramadurlabham
viṣayāsaktacitto'pi tyaktadharmaratirnaraḥ || 71 ||
iha kṣetre mṛtaḥ so'pi saṃsāraṃ na punarviśet
ye punarnirmamā dhīrāḥ sattvasthā vijitendri yāḥ || 72 ||
vratinaśca nirārambhāḥ sarve te mayi bhāvitāḥ
dehabhaṅgaṃ samāsādya dhīmantaḥ saṅgavarjitāḥ
gatā eva paraṃ mokṣam prasādānmama suvrate || 73 ||
janmāntarasahasreṣu yuñjanyogamavāpnuyāt
tamihaiva paraṃ mokṣaṃ maraṇādadhigacchati || 74 ||
etatsaṃkṣepato devi kṣetrasyāsya mahatphalam
avimuktasya kathitaṃ mayā te guhyamuttamam || 75 ||
ataḥ parataraṃ nāsti siddhiguhyaṃ maheśvari
etadbudhyanti yogajñā ye ca yogeśvarā bhuvi || 76 ||
etadeva paraṃ sthānametadeva paraṃ śivam
etadeva paraṃ brahma etadeva paraṃ padam || 77 ||
vārāṇasī tu bhuvanatrayasārabhūtā ramyā sadā mama purī girirājaputri
atrā''gatā vividhaduṣkṛtakāriṇo'pi pāpakṣayādvirajasaḥ pratibhānti martyāḥ || 78 ||
etatsmṛtaṃ priyatamaṃ mama devi nityaṃ kṣetraṃ vicitratarugulmalatāsupuṣpam
asminmṛtāstanubhṛtaḥ padamāpnuvanti mūrkhāgamena rahitāpi na saṃśayo'tra || 79 ||
sūta uvāca
etasminnantare devo devīṃ prāha girīndra jām
dātuṃ prasādaṃ yakṣāya varaṃ bhaktāya bhāmini || 80 ||
bhakto mama varārohe tapasā hatakilbiṣaḥ
arho varamasau labdhumasmatto bhuvaneśvari || 81 ||
evamuktvā tato devaḥ saha devyā jagatpatiḥ
jagāma yakṣo yatrā''ste kṛśo dhamanisaṃtataḥ || 82 ||
tatastaṃ guhyakaṃ devī dṛṣṭipātairnirīkṣatī
śvetavarṇaṃ vicarmāṇam snāyubaddhāsthipañjaram || 83 ||
devī prāha tadā devaṃ darśayantī ca guhyakam
satyaṃ nāma bhavānugro devairuktastu śaṃkara || 84 ||
īdṛśe cāsya tapasi na prayacchasi yadvaram
ataḥ kṣetre mahādeva puṇye samyagupāsite || 85 ||
kathamevaṃ parikleśaṃ prāpto yakṣakumārakaḥ
śīghramasya varaṃ yaccha prasādātparameśvara || 86 ||
evaṃ manvādayo deva vadanti paramarṣayaḥ
ruṣṭādvā cātha tuṣṭādvā siddhistūbhayato bhavet
bhogaprāptistathā rājyamante mokṣaḥ sadāśivāt || 87 ||
evamuktastato devaḥ saha devyā jagatpatiḥ
jagāma yakṣo yatrā''ste kṛśo dhamanisaṃtataḥ || 88 ||
taṃ dṛṣṭvā praṇataṃ bhaktyā harikeśaṃ vṛṣadhvajaḥ
divyaṃ cakṣuradāttasmai yenāpaśyatsa śaṃkaram || 89 ||
atha yakṣastadādeśācchanairunmīlya cakṣuṣī
apaśyatsagaṇaṃ devaṃ vṛṣadhvajamupasthitam || 90 ||
devadeva uvāca
varaṃ dadāmi te pūrvaṃ trailokye darśanaṃ tathā
sāvarṇyaṃ ca śarīrasya paśya māṃ vigatajvaraḥ || 91 ||
sūta uvāca
tataḥ sa labdhvā tu varaṃ śarīreṇākṣatena ca
pādayoḥ praṇatastasthau kṛtvā śirasi cāñjalim || 92 ||
uvācātha tadā tena varado'smīti coditaḥ
bhagavanbhaktimavyagrāṃ tvayyananyāṃ vidhatsva me || 93 ||
annadatvaṃ ca lokānāṃ gāṇapatyaṃ tathā'kṣayam
avimuktaṃ ca te sthānaṃ paśyeyaṃ sarvadā yathā
etadicchāmi deveśa tvatto varamanuttamam || 94 ||
devadeva uvāca
jarāmaraṇasaṃtyaktaḥ sarvarogavivarjitaḥ
bhaviṣyasi gaṇādhyakṣo dhanadaḥ sarvapūjitaḥ || 95 ||
ajeyaścāpi sarveṣāṃ yogaiśvaryaṃ samāśritaḥ
annadaścāpi lokebhyaḥ kṣetrapālo bhaviṣyasi || 96 ||
mahābalo mahāsattvo brahmaṇyo mama ca priyaḥ
tryakṣaśca daṇḍapāṇiśca mahāyogī tathaiva ca
udbhramaḥ saṃbhramaścaiva gaṇau te paricārakau
tavā''jñayā kariṣyete lokasyodbhramasaṃbhramau || 98 ||
sūta uvāca
evaṃ sa bhagavāṃstatra yakṣaṃ kṛtvā gaṇeśvaram
jagāma vāsaṃ deveśaḥ saha tena maheśvaraḥ || 99 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe vārāṇasīmāhātmye daṇḍapāṇivarapradānaṃ nāmāśītyadhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 180 ||

<181>

athaikāśītyadhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
sūta uvāca
imāṃ puṇyodbhavāṃ snigdhāṃ kathāṃ pāpapraṇāśinīm
śṛṇvantu ṛṣayaḥ sarve suviśuddhāstapodhanāḥ || 1 ||
gaṇeśvarapatiṃ divyaṃ rudra tulyaparākramam
sanatkumāro bhagavānapṛcchannandikeśvaram || 2 ||
brūhi guhyaṃ yathātattvaṃ yatra nityaṃ bhavaḥ sthitaḥ
mahātmā sarvabhūtānāṃ paramātmā maheśvaraḥ || 3 ||
ghorarūpaṃ samāsthāya duṣkaraṃ devadānavaiḥ
ābhūtasaṃplavaṃ yāvatsthāṇubhūto maheśvaraḥ || 4 ||
nandikeśvara uvāca
purā devena yatproktaṃ purāṇaṃ puṇyamuttamam
tatsarvaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi namaskṛtya maheśvaram || 5 ||
tato devena tuṣṭena umāyāḥ priyakāmyayā
kathitaṃ bhuvi vikhyātaṃ yatra nityaṃ svayaṃ sthitaḥ || 6 ||
rudra syārdhāsanagatā meruśṛṅge yaśasvinī
mahādevaṃ tato devī praṇatā paripṛcchati || 7 ||
devyuvāca
bhagavandevadeveśa candrā rdhakṛtaśekhara
dharmaṃ prabrūhi martyānāṃ bhuvi caivordhvaretasām || 8 ||
japtaṃ dattaṃ hutaṃ ceṣṭaṃ tapastaptaṃ kṛtaṃ ca yat
dhyānādhyayanasaṃpannaṃ kathaṃ bhavati cākṣayam || 9 ||
janmāntarasahasreṇa yatpāpaṃ pūrvasaṃcitam
kathaṃ tatkṣayamāyāti tanmamā''cakṣva śaṃkara || 10 ||
yasminvyavasthito bhaktyā tuṣyasi tvaṃ maheśvara
vratāni niyamāścaiva ācāro dharma eva ca || 11 ||
sarvasiddhikaraṃ yatra hyakṣayyagatidāyakam
vaktumarhasi tatsarvaṃ paraṃ kautūhalaṃ hi me || 12 ||
maheśvara uvāca
śṛṇu devi pravakṣyāmi guhyānāṃ guhyamuttamam
sarvakṣetreṣu vikhyātamavimuktaṃ priyaṃ mama || 13 ||
aṣṭaṣaṣṭiḥ purā proktā sthānānāṃ sthānamuttamam
yatra sākṣātsvayaṃ rudraḥ kṛttivāsāḥ svayaṃ sthitaḥ || 14 ||
yatra saṃnihito nityamavimukte nirantaram
tatkṣetraṃ na mayā muktamavimuktaṃ tataḥ smṛtam || 15 ||
avimukte parā siddhiravimukte parā gatiḥ
japtaṃ dattaṃ hutaṃ ceṣṭaṃ tapastaptaṃ kṛtaṃ ca yat || 16 ||
dhyānamadhyayanaṃ dānaṃ sarvaṃ bhavati cākṣayam
janmāntarasahasreṇa yatpāpaṃ pūrvasaṃcitam || 17 ||
avimuktaṃ praviṣṭasya tatsarvaṃ vrajati kṣayam
avimuktāgninā dagdhamagnau tūlamivā''hitam || 18 ||
brāhmaṇāḥ kṣattriyā vaiśyāḥ śūdrā vai varṇasaṃkarāḥ
kṛmimlecchāśca ye cānye saṃkīrṇāḥ pāpayonayaḥ || 19 ||
kīṭāḥ pipīlikāścaiva ye cānye mṛgapakṣiṇaḥ
kālena nidhanaṃ prāptā avimukte śṛṇu priye || 20 ||
candrā rdhamaulinaḥ sarve lalāṭākṣā vṛṣadhvajāḥ
śive mama pure devi modante tatra mānavāḥ || 21 ||
akāmo vā sakāmo vā hyapi tiryaggato'pi vā
avimukte tyajanprāṇānmama loke mahīyate || 22 ||
avimuktaṃ yadā gacchetkadācitkālaparyayāt
aśmanā caraṇau bhittvā tatraiva nidhanaṃ vrajet || 23 ||
avimuktaṃ gato devi na nirgacchettataḥ punaḥ
so'pi matpadamāpnoti nātra kāryā vicāraṇā || 24 ||
vastrapadaṃ rudra koṭiṃ siddheśvaramahālayam
gokarṇaṃ rudra karṇaṃ ca suvarṇākṣaṃ tathaiva ca || 25 ||
amaraṃ ca mahākālaṃ tathā kāyāvarohaṇam
etāni hi pavitrāṇi sāṃnidhyātsaṃdhyayordvayoḥ || 26 ||
kāliñjaravanaṃ caiva śaṅkukarṇaṃ sthaleśvaram
etāni ca pavitrāṇi sāṃnidhyāddhi mama priye
avimukte varārohe trisaṃdhyaṃ nātra saṃśayaḥ || 27 ||
hariścandraṃ paraṃ guhyaṃ guhyamāmrātakeśvaram
jāleśvaraṃ paraṃ guhyaṃ guhyaṃ śrīparvataṃ tathā || 28 ||
mahālayaṃ tathā guhyaṃ kṛmicaṇḍeśvaraṃ śubham
guhyātiguhyaṃ kedāraṃ mahābhairavameva ca || 29 ||
aṣṭāvetāni sthānāni sāṃnidhyāddhi gama priye
avimukte varārohe trisaṃdhyaṃ nātra saṃśayaḥ || 30 ||
yāni sthānāni śrūyante triṣu lokeṣu suvrate
avimuktasya pādeṣu nityaṃ saṃnihitāni vai || 31 ||
athottarāṃ kathāṃ divyāmavimuktasya śobhane
skando vakṣyati māhātmyamṛṣīṇāṃ bhāvitātmanām || 32 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe'vimuktamāhātmya ekāśītyadhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 181 ||

<182>

atha dvyaśītyadhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
sūta uvāca
kailāsapṛṣṭhamāsīnaṃ skandaṃ brahmavidāṃ varam
papracchurṛṣayaḥ sarve sanakādyāstapodhanāḥ || 1 ||
tathā rājarṣayaḥ sarve ye bhaktāstu maheśvare
brūhi tvaṃ skanda bhūrloke yatra nityaṃ bhavaḥ sthitaḥ || 2 ||
skanda uvāca
mahātmā sarvabhūtātmā devadevaḥ sanātanaḥ
ghorarūpaṃ samāsthāya duṣkaraṃ devadānavaiḥ || 3 ||
ābhūtasaṃplavaṃ yāvatsthāṇubhūtaḥ sthitaḥ prabhuḥ
guhyānāṃ paramaṃ guhyamavimuktamiti smṛtam || 4 ||
avimukte sadā siddhiryatra nityaṃ bhavaḥ sthitaḥ
asya kṣetrasya māhātmyaṃ yaduktaṃ tvīśvareṇa tu || 5 ||
sthānaṃ paraṃ pavitraṃ ca tīrthamāyatanaṃ tathā
śmaśānasaṃsthitaṃ veśma divyamantarhitaṃ ca yat || 6 ||
bhūrloke naiva saṃyuktamantarikṣe śivālayam
ayuktāstu na paśyanti yuktāḥ paśyanti cetasā || 7 ||
brahmacaryavratopetāḥ siddhā vedāntakovidāḥ
ā dehapatanādyāvattatkṣetraṃ yo na muñcati || 8 ||
brahmacaryavrataiḥ samyaksamyagiṣṭaṃ makhairbhavet
apāpātmā gatiḥ sarvā yā tūktā ca kriyāvatām || 9 ||
yastatra nivasedvipro'saṃyuktātmā'samāhitaḥ
trikālamapi bhuñjāno vāyubhakṣasamo bhavet || 10 ||
nimeṣamātramapi yo hyavimukte tu bhaktimān
brahmacaryasamāyuktaḥ paramaṃ prāpnuyāttapaḥ || 11 ||
yo'tra māsaṃ vaseddhīro laghvāhāro jitendri yaḥ
samyaktena vrataṃ cīrṇaṃ divyaṃ pāśupataṃ mahat || 12 ||
janmamṛtyubhayaṃ tīrtvā sa yāti paramāṃ gatim
naiḥśreyasīṃ gatiṃ puṇyāṃ tathā yogagatiṃ vrajet || 13 ||
na hi yogagatirdivyā janmāntaraśatairapi
prāpyate kṣetramāhātmyātprabhāvācchaṃkarasya tu || 14 ||
brahmahā yo'bhigacchettu avimuktaṃ kadācana
tasya kṣetrasya māhātmyādbrahmahatyā nivartate || 15 ||
ā dehapatanādyāvatkṣetraṃ yo na vimuñcati
na kevalaṃ brahmahatyā prākṛtaṃ ca nivartate || 16 ||
prāpya viśveśvaraṃ devaṃ na sa bhūyo'bhijāyate
ananyamānaso bhūtvā yo'vimuktaṃ na muñcati || 17 ||
tasya devaḥ sadā tuṣṭaḥ sarvānkāmānprayacchati
dvāraṃ yatsāṃkhyayogānāṃ sa tatra vasati prabhuḥ || 18 ||
sagaṇo hi bhavo devo bhaktānāmanukampayā
avimuktaṃ paraṃ kṣetramavimukte parā gatiḥ || 19 ||
avimukte parā siddhiravimukte paraṃ padam
avimuktaṃ niṣeveta devarṣigaṇasevitam || 20 ||
yadīcchenmānavo dhīmānna punarjāyate kvacit
meroḥ śakto guṇānvaktuṃ dīpānāṃ ca tathaiva ca || 21 ||
samudrā ṇāṃ ca sarveṣāṃ nāvimuktasya śakyate
antakāle manuṣyāṇāṃ chidyamāneṣu marmasu || 22 ||
vāyunā preryamāṇānāṃ smṛtirnaivopajāyate
avimukte hyantakāle bhaktānāmīśvaraḥ svayam || 23 ||
karmabhiḥ preryamāṇānāṃ karṇajāpaṃ prayacchati
maṇikarṇyāṃ tyajandehaṃ gatimiṣṭāṃ vrajennaraḥ || 24 ||
īśvaraprerito yāti duṣprāpāmakṛtātmabhiḥ
aśāśvatamidaṃ jñātvā mānuṣyaṃ bahukilbiṣam || 25 ||
avimuktaṃ niṣeveta saṃsāramayamocanam
yogakṣemapradaṃ divyaṃ bahuvighnavināśanam || 26 ||
vighnaiścā''loḍyamāno'pi yo'vimuktaṃ na muñcati
sa muñcati jarāṃ mṛtyuṃ janma caitadaśāśvatam
avimuktaprasādāttu śivasāyujyamāpnuyāt
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe'vimuktamāhātmye dvyaśītyadhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 182 ||

<183>

atha tryaśītyadhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
devyuvāca
himavantaṃ giriṃ tyaktvā mandaraṃ gandhamādanam
kailāsaṃ niṣadhaṃ caiva merupṛṣṭhaṃ mahādyuti || 1 ||
ramyaṃ triśikharaṃ caiva mānasaṃ sumahāgirim
devodyānāni ramyāṇi nandanaṃ vanameva ca || 2 ||
surasthānāni mukhyāni tīrthānyāyatanāni ca
tāni sarvāṇi saṃtyajya avimukte ratiḥ katham || 3 ||
kimatra sumahatpuṇyaṃ paraṃ guhyaṃ vadasva me
yena tvaṃ ramase nityaṃ bhūtasaṃpadguṇairyutaḥ || 4 ||
kṣetrasya pravaratvaṃ ca ye ca tatra nivāsinaḥ
teṣāmanugrahaḥ kaścittatsarvaṃ brūhi śaṃkara || 5 ||
śaṃkara uvāca
atyadbhutamimaṃ praśnaṃ yattvaṃ pṛcchasi bhāmini
tatsarvaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi tanme nigadataḥ śṛṇu || 6 ||
vārāṇasyāṃ nadī puṇyā siddhagandharvasevitā
praviṣṭā tripathā gaṅgā tasminkṣetre mama priye || 7 ||
mamaiva prītiratulā kṛttivāse ca sundari
sarveṣāṃ caiva sthānānāṃ sthānaṃ tattu yathā'dhikam || 8 ||
tena kāryeṇa suśroṇi tasminsthāne ratirmama
tasmim̐ lliṅge ca sāṃnidhyaṃ mama devi sureśvari || 9 ||
kṣetrasya ca pravakṣyāmi guṇānguṇavatāṃ vare
yāñchrutvā sarvapāpebhyo mucyate nātra saṃśayaḥ || 10 ||
yadi pāpo yadi śaṭho yadi vā'dhārmiko naraḥ
mucyate sarvapāpebhyo hyavimuktaṃ vrajedyadi || 11 ||
pralaye sarvabhūtānāṃ loke sthāvarajaṅgame
na hi tyajāmi tatsthānaṃ mahāgaṇaśatairvṛtaḥ || 12 ||
yatra devāḥ sagandharvāḥ sayakṣoragarākṣasāḥ
vaktraṃ mama mahābhāge praviśanti yugakṣaye || 13 ||
teṣāṃ sākṣādahaṃ pūjāṃ pratigṛhṇāmi pārvati
sarvaguhyottamaṃ sthānaṃ mama priyatamaṃ śubham || 14 ||
dhanyāḥ praviṣṭāḥ suśroṇi mama bhaktā dvijātayaḥ
madbhaktiparamā nityaṃ ye madbhaktāstu te narāḥ || 15 ||
tasminprāṇānparityajya gacchanti paramāṃ gatim
sadā yajati rudre ṇa sadā dānaṃ prayacchati || 26 ||
sadā tapasvī bhavati avimuktasthito naraḥ
yo māṃ pūjayate nityaṃ tasya tuṣyāmyahaṃ priye || 27 ||
sarvadānāni yo dadyātsarvayajñeṣu dīkṣitaḥ
sarvatīrthābhiṣiktaśca sa prapadyeta māmiha || 18 ||
avimuktaṃ sadā devī ye vrajanti suniścitāḥ
te tiṣṭhantīha suśroṇi madbhaktāśca triviṣṭape || 19 ||
matprasādāttu te devi dīpyante śubhalocane
durdharāścaiva durdharṣā bhavanti vigatajvarāḥ || 20 ||
avimuktaṃ śubhaṃ prāpya madbhaktāḥ kṛtaniścayāḥ
nirdhūtapāpā vimalā bhavanti vigatajvarāḥ || 21 ||
pārvatyuvāca
dakṣayajñastvayā deva matpriyārthe niṣūditaḥ
avimuktaguṇānāṃ tu na tṛptiriha jāyate || 22 ||
īśvara uvāca
krodhena dakṣayajñastu tvatpriyārthe vināśitaḥ
mahāpriye mahābhāge nāśito'yaṃ varānane || 23 ||
avimukte yajante tu madbhaktāḥ kṛtaniścayāḥ
na teṣāṃ punarāvṛttiḥ kalpakoṭiśatairapi || 24 ||
devyuvāca
durlabhāstu guṇā deva avimukte tu kīrtitāḥ
sarvāṃstānmama tattvena kathayasva maheśvara || 25 ||
kautūhalaṃ mahādeva hṛdisthaṃ mama vartate
tatsarvaṃ mama tattvena ākhyāhi parameśvara || 26 ||
īśvara uvāca
akṣayā hyamarāścaiva hyadehāśca bhavanti te
matprasādādvarārohe māmeva praviśanti vai || 27 ||
brūhi brūhi viśālākṣi kimanyacchrotumarhasi || 28 ||
devyuvāca
avimukte mahākṣetre aho puṇyamaho guṇāḥ
na tṛptimadhigacchāmi brūhi deva punarguṇān || 29 ||
īśvara uvāca
maheśvari varārohe śṛṇu tāṃstu mama priye
avimukte guṇā ye tu tathā'nyānapi tacchṛṇu || 30 ||
śākaparṇāśino dāntāḥ saṃprakṣālyā marīcipāḥ
dantolūkhalinaścānye aśmakuṭṭāstathā pare || 31 ||
māsi māsi kuśāgreṇa jalamāsvādayanti vai
vṛkṣamūlaniketāśca śilāśayyāstathā pare || 32 ||
ādityavapuṣaḥ sarve jitakrodhā jitendri yāḥ
evaṃ bahuvidhairdharmairanyatra varitavratāḥ || 33 ||
trikālamapi bhuñjānā ye'vimuktanivāsinaḥ
tapaścaranti vā'nyatra kalāṃ nārhanti ṣoḍaśīm
ye'vimukte vasantīha svarge prativasanti te || 34 ||
matsamaḥ puruṣo nāsti tvatsamā nāsti yoṣitām
avimuktasamaṃ kṣetraṃ na bhūtaṃ na bhaviṣyati || 35 ||
avimukte paro yogo hyavimukte parā gatiḥ
avimukte paro mokṣaḥ kṣetraṃ naivāsti tādṛśam || 36 ||
paraṃ guhyaṃ pravakṣyāmi tattvena varavarṇini
avimukte mahākṣetre yaduktaṃ hi mayā purā || 37 ||
janmāntaraśatairdevi yogo'yaṃ yadi labhyate
mokṣaḥ śatasahasreṇa janmanā labhyate na vā || 38 ||
avimukte na saṃdeho madbhaktaḥ kṛtaniścayaḥ
ekena janmanā so'pi yogaṃ mokṣaṃ ca vindati || 39 ||
avimuktaṃ narā devi ye vrajanti suniścitāḥ
te viśanti paraṃ sthānaṃ mokṣaṃ paramadurlabham || 40 ||
pṛthivyāmīdṛśaṃ kṣetraṃ na bhūtaṃ na bhaviṣyati
caturmūrtiḥ sadā dharmastasminsaṃnihitaḥ priye
caturṇāmapi varṇānāṃ gatistu paramā smṛtā || 41 ||
devyuvāca
śrutā guṇāste kṣetrasya iha cānyatra ye prabho
vadasva bhuvi viprendrāḥ kaṃ vā yajñairyajanti te || 42 ||
īśvara uvāca
ijyayā caiva mantreṇa māmeva hi yajanti ye
na teṣāṃ bhayamastīti bhavaṃ rudraṃ yajanti yat || 43 ||
amantro mantrako devi dvividho vidhirucyate
sāṃkhyaṃ caivātha yogaśca dvividho yoga ucyate || 44 ||
sarvabhūtasthitaṃ yo māṃ bhajatyekatvamāsthitaḥ
sarvathā vartamāno'pi sa yogī mayi vartate || 45 ||
ātmaupamyena sarvatra sarvaṃ ca mayi paśyati
tasyāhaṃ na praṇaśyāmi sa ca me na praṇaśyati || 46 ||
nirguṇaḥ saguṇo vā'pi yogaśca kathito bhuvi
saguṇaścaiva vijñeyo nirguṇo manasaḥ paraḥ
etatte kathitaṃ devi yanmāṃ tvaṃ paripṛcchasi || 48 ||
devyuvāca
yā bhaktistrividhā proktā bhaktānāṃ bahudhā tvayā
tāmahaṃ śrotumicchāmi tattvataḥ kathayasva me || 49 ||
īśvara uvāca
śṛṇu pārvati deveśi bhaktānāṃ bhaktivatsale
prāpya sāṃkhyaṃ ca yogaṃ ca duḥkhāntaṃ ca niyacchati || 50 ||
sadā yaḥ sevate bhikṣāṃ tato bhavati rañjitaḥ
rañjanāttanmayo bhūtvā līyate sa tu bhaktimān || 51 ||
śāstrāṇā tu varārohe bahukāraṇadarśinaḥ
na māṃ paśyanti te devi jñānavākyavivādinaḥ || 52 ||
paramārthajñānatṛptā yuktā jānanti yoginaḥ
vidyayā viditātmāno yogasya ca dvijātayaḥ || 53 ||
pratyāhāreṇa śuddhātmā nānyathā cintayecca tat
tuṣṭiṃ ca paramāṃ prāpya yogaṃ mokṣaṃ paraṃ tathā
tribhirguṇaiḥ samāyukto jñānavānpaśyatīha mām || 54 ||
etatte kathitaṃ devi kimanyacchrotumarhasi
bhūya eva varārohe kathayiṣyāmi suvrate || 55 ||
guhyaṃ pavitramathavā yaccāpi hṛdi vartate
tatsarvaṃ kathayiṣyāmi śṛṇuṣvaikamanāḥ priye || 56 ||
devyuvāca
tvadrū paṃ kīdṛśaṃ deva yuktāḥ paśyanti yoginaḥ
etaṃ me saṃśayaṃ brūhi namaste surasattama || 57 ||
śrībhagavānuvāca
amūrtaṃ caiva mūrtaṃ ca jyotīrūpaṃ hi tatsmṛtam
tasyopalabdhimanvicchanyatnaḥ kāryo vijānatā || 58 ||
guṇairviyukto bhūtātmā evaṃ vaktuṃ na śakyate
śakyate yadi vaktuṃ vai divyairvarṣaśatairna vā || 59 ||
devyuvāca
kiṃ pramāṇaṃ tu tatkṣetraṃ samantātsarvatodiśam
yatra nityaṃ sthito devo mahādevo gaṇairyutaḥ || 60 ||
īśvara uvāca
dviyojanaṃ tu tatkṣetraṃ pūrvapaścimataḥ smṛtam
ardhayojanavistīrṇaṃ tatkṣetraṃ dakṣiṇottaram || 61 ||
varaṇā'sī nadī yāvattāvacchuklanadī tu vai
bhīṣmacaṇḍikamārabhya parvateśvaramantike || 62 ||
gaṇā yatrāvatiṣṭhante saṃniyuktā vināyakāḥ
kūṣmāṇḍagajatuṇḍaśca jayantaśca madotkaṭāḥ || 63 ||
siṃhavyāghramukhāḥ kecidvikaṭāḥ kubjavāmanāḥ
yatra nandī mahākālaścaṇḍaghaṇṭo maheśvaraḥ || 64 ||
daṇḍacaṇḍeśvaraścaiva ghaṇṭākarṇo mahābalaḥ
ete cānye ca bahavo gaṇāścaiva gaṇeśvarāḥ || 65 ||
mahodarā mahākāyā vajraśaktidharāstathā
rakṣanti satataṃ devi hyavimuktaṃ tapovanam
dvāre dvāre ca tiṣṭhanti śūlamudgarapāṇayaḥ || 66 ||
suvarṇaśṛṅgīṃ raupyakhurāṃ cailājinapayasvinīm
vārāṇasyāṃ tu yo dadyātsavatsāṃ kāṃsyabhājanām || 67 ||
gāṃ dattvā tu varārohe brāhmaṇe vedapārage
āsaptamaṃ kulaṃ tena tāritaṃ nātra saṃśayaḥ || 68 ||
yo dadyādbrāhmaṇe kiṃcittasminkṣetre varānane
kanakaṃ rajataṃ vastramannādyaṃ bahuvistaram
akṣayaṃ cāvyayaṃ caiva syātāṃ tasya sulocane || 69 ||
śṛṇu tattvena tīrthasya vibhūtiṃ vyuṣṭimeva ca
tatra snātvā mahābhāge bhavanti nīrujā narāḥ || 70 ||
daśānāmaśvamedhānāṃ phalaṃ prāpnoti mānavaḥ
tadavāpnoti dharmātmā tatra snātvā varānane || 71 ||
bahusvalpe ca yo dadyādbrāhmaṇe vedapārage
śubhāṃ gatimavāpnoti agnivaccaiva dīpyate || 72 ||
vārāṇasījāhnavībhyāṃ saṃgame lokaviśrute
dattvā'nnaṃ ca vidhānena na sa bhūyo'bhijāyate || 73 ||
etatte kathitaṃ devi tīrthasya phalamuttamam || 74 ||
punaranyatpravakṣyāmi tīrthasya phalamuttamam
upavāsaṃ tu yaḥ kṛtvā viprānsaṃtarpayennaraḥ
sautrāmaṇeśca yajñasya phalaṃ prāpnoti mānavaḥ || 75 ||
ekāhārastu yastiṣṭhenmāsaṃ tatra varānane
yāvajjīvakṛtaṃ pāpaṃ sahasā tasya naśyati || 76 ||
agnipraveśaṃ ye kuryuravimukte vidhānataḥ
praviśanti mukhaṃ te me niḥsaṃdigdhaṃ varānane || 77 ||
kurvantyanaśanaṃ ye tu madbhaktāḥ kṛtaniścayāḥ
na teṣāṃ punarāvṛttiḥ kalpakoṭiśatairapi || 78 ||
arcayedyastu māṃ devi avimukte tapovane
tasya dharmaṃ pravakṣyāmi yadavāpnoti mānavaḥ || 79 ||
daśāśvamedhikaṃ puṇyaṃ labhate nātra saṃśayaḥ || 80 ||
daśasauvarṇikaṃ puṣpaṃ yo'vimukte prayacchati
agnihotraphalaṃ dhūpe gandhadāne tathā śṛṇu
bhūmidānena tattulyaṃ gandhadānaphalaṃ smṛtam || 81 ||
saṃmārjane pañcaśataṃ sahasramanulepane
mālayā śatasāhasramanantaṃ gītavādyataḥ || 82 ||
devyuvāca
atyadbhutamidaṃ deva sthānametatprakīrtitam
rahasyaṃ śrotumicchāmi yadarthaṃ tvaṃ na muñcasi || 83 ||
īśvara uvāca
āsītpūrvaṃ varārohe brahmaṇastu śiro varam
pañcamaṃ śṛṇu suśroṇi jātaṃ kāñcanasaprabham || 84 ||
jvalattatpañcamaṃ śīrṣaṃ jātaṃ tasya mahātmanaḥ
tadevamabravīddevi janma jānāmi te hyaham || 85 ||
tataḥ krodhaparītena saṃraktanayanena ca
vāmāṅguṣṭhanakhāgreṇa cchinnaṃ tasya śiro mayā || 86 ||
brahmovāca
yadā niraparādhasya śiraśchinnaṃ tvayā mama
tasmācchāpasamāyuktaḥ kapālī tvaṃ bhaviṣyasi
brahmahatyākulo bhūtvā cara tīrthāni bhūtale || 87 ||
tato'haṃ gatavāndevi himavantaṃ śiloccayam
tatra nārāyaṇaḥ śrīmānmayā bhikṣāṃ prayācitaḥ || 88 ||
tatastena svakaṃ pārśvaṃ nakhāgreṇa vidāritam
sravato mahatī dhārā tasya raktasya niḥsṛtā || 89 ||
prayātā sā'tivistīrṇā yojanārdhaśataṃ tadā
na saṃpūrṇaṃ kapālaṃ tu ghoramadbhutadarśanam || 90 ||
divyaṃ varṣasahasraṃ tu sā ca dhārā pravāhitā
provāca bhagavānviṣṇuḥ kapālaṃ kuta īdṛśam || 91 ||
āścaryabhūtaṃ deveśa saṃśayo hṛdi vartate
kutaśca saṃbhavo deva sarvaṃ me brūhi pṛcchataḥ || 92 ||
devadeva uvāca
śrūyatāmasya he deva kapālasya tu saṃbhavaḥ
śataṃ varṣasahasrāṇāṃ tapastaptvā sudāruṇam || 93 ||
brahmā'sṛjadvapurdivyamadbhutaṃ lomaharṣaṇam
tapasaśca prabhāveṇa divyaṃ kāñcanasaṃnibham || 94 ||
jvalattatpañcamaṃ śīrṣaṃ jātaṃ tasya mahātmanaḥ
nikṛttaṃ tanmayā deva tadidaṃ paśya durjayam || 95 ||
yatra yatra ca gacchāmi kapālaṃ tatra gacchati
evamuktastato devaḥ provāca puruṣottamaḥ || 96 ||
śrībhagavānuvāca
gaccha gaccha svakaṃ sthānaṃ brahmaṇastvaṃ priyaṃ kuru
tasminsthāsyati bhadraṃ te kapālaṃ tasya tejasā || 97 ||
tataḥ sarvāṇi tīrthāni puṇyānyāyatanāni ca
gato'smi pṛthulaśroṇi na kvacitpratyatiṣṭhata || 98 ||
tato'haṃ samanuprāpto hyavimukte mahāśaye
avasthitaḥ svake sthāne śāpaśca vigato mama || 99 ||
viṣṇuprasādātsuśroṇi kapālaṃ tatsahasradhā
sphuṭitaṃ bahudhā jātaṃ svapnalabdhaṃ dhanaṃ yathā || 100 ||
brahmahatyāpahaṃ tīrthaṃ kṣetrametanmayā kṛtam
kapālamocanaṃ devi devānāṃ prathitaṃ bhuvi || 101 ||
kālo bhūtvā jagatsarvaṃ saṃharāmi sṛjāmi ca
tatastatpatitaṃ tatra śāpaśca vigato mama || 102 ||
kapālamocanaṃ tīrthamabhūddhatyāvināśanam
tatrastho'smi jagatsarvaṃ sukaromi sureśvari
deveśi sarvaguhyānāṃ sthānaṃ priyataraṃ mama || 103 ||
madbhaktāstatra gacchanti viṣṇubhaktāstathaiva ca
ye bhaktā bhāskare devi lokanāthe divākare
tatrastho yastyajeddehaṃ māmeva praviśettu saḥ || 104 ||
devyuvāca
atyadbhutamidaṃ deva yaduktaṃ padmayoninā
tripurāntakara sthānaṃ guhyametanmahādyute || 105 ||
yānyanyāni sutīrthāni kalāṃ nārhanti ṣoḍaśīm
yatra tiṣṭhati deveśo yatra tiṣṭhati śaṃkaraḥ || 106 ||
gaṅgā tīrthasahasrāṇāṃ tulyā bhavati vā na vā
tvameva bhaktirdeveśa tvameva gatiruttamā || 107 ||
brahmādīnāṃ tu te deva gatiruktā sanātanī
śrāvyametaddvijātīnāṃ bhaktānāmanukampayā || 108 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe'vimuktamāhātmye tryaśītyadhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 183 ||

<184>

atha caturaśītyadhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
maheśvara uvāca
sevitaṃ bahubhiḥ siddhairapunarbhavakāṅkṣibhiḥ
viditvā tu paraṃ kṣetramavimuktanivāsinām || 1 ||
tadguhyaṃ devadevasya tattīrthaṃ tattapovanam
paraṃ sthānaṃ tu te yānti saṃbhavanti na te punaḥ || 2 ||
jñāne vihitaniṣṭhānāṃ paramānandamicchatām
yā gatirvihitā sadbhiḥ sā'vimukte mṛtasya tu || 3 ||
bhavasya prītiratulā hyavimukte hyanuttamā
asaṃkhyeyaṃ phalaṃ tatra hyakṣayā ca gatirbhavet || 4 ||
paraṃ guhyaṃ samākhyātaṃ śmaśānamiti saṃjñitam
avimuktaṃ na sevante vañcitāste narā bhuvi || 5 ||
avimukte sthitaiḥ puṇyaiḥ pāṃśubhirvāyuneritaiḥ
api duṣkṛtakarmāṇo yāsyanti paramāṃ gatim || 6 ||
avimuktaguṇānvaktuṃ devadānavamānavaiḥ
na śakyate'prameyatvātsvayaṃ yatra bhavaḥ sthitaḥ || 7 ||
anāhitāgnirno yaṣṭā no'śucistaskaro'pi vā
avimukte vasedyastu sa vasedīśvarālaye || 8 ||
tatra nāpuṇyakṛtkaścitprasādādīśvarasya ca
ajñānājjñānato vā'pi striyā vā puruṣeṇa vā || 9 ||
yatkiṃcidaśubhaṃ karma kṛtaṃ mānuṣabuddhinā
avimukte praviṣṭasya tatsarvaṃ bhasmasādbhavet || 10 ||
saritaḥ sāgarāḥ śailāstīrthānyāyatanāni ca
bhūtapretapiśācāśca gaṇā mātṛgaṇāstathā || 11 ||
śmaśānikaparīvārāḥ priyāstasya mahātmanaḥ
na te muñcanti bhūteśaṃ tānbhavastu na muñcati || 12 ||
ramate ca gaṇaiḥ sārdhamavimukte sthitaḥ prabhuḥ
dṛṣṭvaitānbhītakṛpaṇānpāpaduṣkṛtakāriṇaḥ || 13 ||
anukampayā tu devasya prayānti paramāṃ gatim
bhaktānukampī bhagavāṃstiryagyonigatānapi || 14 ||
nayatyeva varaṃ sthānaṃ yatra yānti ca yājñikāḥ
bhārgavāṅgirasaḥ siddhā ṛṣayaśca mahāvratāḥ || 15 ||
avimuktāgninā dagdhā agnau tūlamivā'hitam
na sā gatiḥ kurukṣetre gaṅgādvāre ca puṣkare || 16 ||
sā gatirvihitā puṃsāmavimuktanivāsinām
tiryagyonigatāḥ sattvā ye'vimukte kṛtālayāḥ
kālena nidhanaṃ prāptāste yānti paramāṃ gatim || 17 ||
merumandaramātro'pi rāśiḥ pāpasya karmaṇaḥ
avimuktaṃ samāsādya tatsarvaṃ vrajati kṣayam || 18 ||
śmaśānamiti vikhyātamavimuktaṃ śivālayam
tadguhyaṃ devadevasya tattīrthaṃ tattapovanam || 19 ||
tatra brahmādayo devā nārāyaṇapurogamāḥ
yoginaśca tathā sādhyā bhagavantaṃ sanātanam || 20 ||
upāsante śivaṃ muktā madbhaktā matparāyaṇāḥ
yā gatirjñānatapasāṃ yā gatiryajñayājinām || 21 ||
avimukte mṛtānāṃ tu sā gatirvihitā śubhā
saṃhartāraśca kartārastasminbrahmādayaḥ surāḥ || 22 ||
samrāḍvirāṇmayā lokā jāyante hyapunarbhavāḥ
maharjanastapaścaiva satyalokastathaiva ca || 23 ||
manasaḥ paramo yogo bhūtabhavyabhavasya ca
brahmādisthāvarāntasya yoniḥ sāṃkhādimokṣayoḥ || 24 ||
ye'vimuktaṃ na muñcanti narāste naiva vañcitāḥ
uttamaṃ sarvatīrthānāṃ sthānānāmuttamaṃ ca yat || 25 ||
kṣetrāṇāmuttamaṃ caiva śmaśānānāṃ tathaiva ca
taḍāgānāṃ ca sarveṣāṃ kūpānāṃ srotasāṃ tathā || 26 ||
śailānāmuttamaṃ śailaṃ tīrthānāmuttamaṃ tathā
puṇyakṛdbhavabhaktaiśca hyavimuktaṃ tu sevyate || 27 ||
brahmaṇaḥ paramaṃ sthānaṃ brahmaṇā'dhyāsitaṃ ca yat
brahmaṇā sevitaṃ nityaṃ brahmaṇā caiva rakṣitam || 28 ||
atraiva saptabhuvanaṃ kāñcano meruparvataḥ
manasaḥ paramo yogaḥ prītyarthaṃ brahmaṇaḥ sa tu || 29 ||
brahmā tu tatra bhagavāṃstrisaṃdhyaṃ ceśvare sthitaḥ
puṇyātpuṇyatamaṃ kṣetraṃ puṇyakṛdbhirniṣevitam || 30 ||
ādityopāsanaṃ kṛtvā viprāścāmaratāṃ gatāḥ
anye'pi ye trayo varṇā bhavabhaktyā samāhitāḥ || 31 ||
avimukte tanuṃ tyaktvā gacchanti paramāṃ gatim
aṣṭau māsānvihārasya yatīnāṃ saṃyatātmanām || 32 ||
ekatra caturo māsānmāsau vā nivasetpunaḥ
avimukte praviṣṭānāṃ vihārastu na vidyate || 33 ||
na deho bhavitā tatra dṛṣṭaṃ śāstre purātane
mokṣo hyasaṃśayastatra pañcatvaṃ tu gatasya vai || 34 ||
striyaḥ pativratā yāśca bhavabhaktāḥ samāhitāḥ
avimukte vimuktāstā yāsyanti paramāṃ gatim || 35 ||
anyā yāḥ kāmacāriṇyaḥ striyo bhogaparāyaṇāḥ
kālena nidhanaṃ prāptā gacchanti paramāṃ gatim || 36 ||
yatra yogaśca mokṣaśca prāpyate durlabho naraiḥ
avimuktaṃ samāsādya nānyadgacchettapovanam || 37 ||
sarvātmanā tapaḥ sevyaṃ brāhmaṇairnātra saṃśayaḥ
avimukte vasedyastu mama tulyo bhavennaraḥ || 38 ||
yato mayā na muktaṃ hi tvavimuktaṃ tataḥ smṛtam
avimuktaṃ na sevante mūḍhā ye tamasā''vṛtāḥ || 39 ||
viṇmūtraretasāṃ madhye te vasanti punaḥpunaḥ
kāmaḥ krodhaśca lobhaśca dambhastambho'timatsaraḥ || 40 ||
nidrā tandrā tathā''lasyaṃ paiśunyamiti te daśa
avimukte sthitā vighnāḥ śakreṇa vihitāḥ svayam || 41 ||
nivāyakopasargāśca satataṃ mūrdhni tiṣṭhati
puṇyametadbhavetsarvaṃ bhaktānāmanukampayā || 42 ||
paraṃ guhyamiti jñātvā tataḥ śāstrānudarśanāt
vyāhṛtaṃ devadevaistu munibhistattvadarśibhiḥ || 43 ||
medasā viplutā bhūmiravimukte tu varjitā
pūtā samabhavatsarvā mahādevena rakṣitā || 44 ||
saṃskārastena kriyate bhūmeranyatra sūribhiḥ
ye bhaktyā varadaṃ devamakṣaraṃ paramaṃ padam || 45 ||
devadānavagandharvayakṣarakṣomahoragāḥ
avimuktamupāsante tanniṣṭhāstatparāyaṇāḥ || 46 ||
te viśanti mahādevamājyāhutirivānalam
taṃ vai prāpya mahādevamīśvarādhyuṣitaṃ śubham || 47 ||
avimuktaṃ kṛtārtho'smītyātmānamupalabhyate
ṛṣidevāsuragaṇairjapahomaparāyaṇaiḥ || 48 ||
yatibhirmokṣakāmaiśca hyavimuktaṃ niṣevyate
nāvimukte mṛtaḥ kaścinnarakaṃ yāti kilbiṣī || 49 ||
īśvarānugṛhītā hi sarve yānti parāṃ gatim
dviyojanamathārdhaṃ ca tatkṣetraṃ pūrvapaścimam || 50 ||
ardhayojanavistīrṇaṃ dakṣiṇottarataḥ smṛtam
vārāṇasī tadīyā ca yāvacchuklanadī tu vai || 51 ||
eṣa kṣetrasya vistāraḥ prokto devena dhīmatā
labdhvā yogaṃ ca mokṣaṃ ca kāṅkṣanto jñānamuttamam || 52 ||
avimuktaṃ na muñcanti tanniṣṭhāstatparāyaṇāḥ
tasminvasanti ye martyā na te śocyāḥ kadācana || 53 ||
yogakṣetraṃ tapaḥkṣetraṃ siddhagandharvasevitam
saritaḥ sāgarāḥ śailā nāvimuktasamā bhuvi || 54 ||
bhūrloke cāntarikṣe ca divi tīrthāni yāni ca
atītya vartate cānyadavimuktaṃ prabhāvataḥ || 55 ||
ye tu dhyānaṃ samāsādya yuktātmānaḥ samāhitāḥ
saṃniyamyendri yagrāmaṃ japanti śatarudri yam || 56 ||
avimukte sthitā nityaṃ kṛtārthāste dvijātayaḥ
bhavabhaktiṃ samāsādya ramante tu suniścitāḥ || 57 ||
saṃhṛtya śaktitaḥ kāmānviṣayebhyo bahiḥ sthitāḥ
śaktitaḥ sarvato muktāḥ śaktitastapasi sthitāḥ || 58 ||
karaṇānīha cā''tmānamapunarbhavabhāvitāḥ
taṃ vai prāpya mahātmānamīśvaraṃ nirbhayāḥ sthitāḥ || 59 ||
na teṣāṃ punarāvṛttiḥ kalpakoṭiśatairapi
avimukte tu gṛhyante bhavena vibhunā svayam || 60 ||
utpāditaṃ mahākṣetraṃ sidhyante yatra mānavāḥ
uddeśamātraṃ kathitā avimuktaguṇāstathā || 61 ||
samudra syeva ratnānāmavimuktasya vistaram
mohanaṃ tadabhaktānāṃ bhaktānāṃ bhaktivardhanam || 62 ||
mūḍhāste tu na paśyanti śmaśānamiti mohitāḥ
hanyamāno'pi yo vidvānvasedvighnaśatairapi || 63 ||
sa yāti paramaṃ sthānaṃ yatra gatvā na śocati
janmamṛtyujarāmuktaḥ paraṃ yāti śivālayam || 64 ||
apunarmaraṇānāṃ hi sā gatirmokṣakāṅkṣiṇām
yāṃ prāpya kṛtakṛtyaḥ syāditi manyeta paṇḍitaḥ || 65 ||
na dānairna tapobhirvā na yajñairnāpi vidyayā
prāpyate gatiriṣṭā yā hyavimukte tu labhyate || 66 ||
nānāvarṇā vivarṇāśca caṇḍālā ye jugupsitāḥ
kilbiṣaiḥ pūrṇadehāśca prakṛṣṭaiḥ pātakaistathā || 67 ||
bheṣajaṃ paramaṃ teṣāmavimuktaṃ vidurbudhāḥ
jātyantarasahasreṣu hyavimukte mriyeta yaḥ || 68 ||
bhakto viśveśvare deve na sa bhūyo'bhijāyate
yatra ceṣṭaṃ hutaṃ dattaṃ tapastaptaṃ kṛtaṃ ca yat || 69 ||
sarvamakṣayametasminnavimukte na saṃśayaḥ
kālenoparatā yānti bhave sāyujyamakṣayam || 70 ||
kṛtvā pāpasahasrāṇi paścātsaṃtāpametya vai
yo'vimukte viyujyeta sa yāti paramāṃ gatim || 71 ||
uttaraṃ dakṣiṇaṃ cāpi ayanaṃ na vikalpayet
sarvastasya śubhaḥ kālo hyavimukte mriyeta yaḥ || 72 ||
na tatra kālo mīmāṃsyaḥ śubho vā yadi vā'śubhaḥ
tasya devasya māhātmyātsthānamadbhutakarmaṇaḥ
sarveṣāmeva nāthasya sarveṣāṃ ca vibhoḥ svayam || 73 ||
śrutvedamṛṣayaḥ sarve skandena kathitaṃ purā
avimuktāśramaṃ puṇyaṃ bhāvayetkaraṇaiḥ śubhaiḥ || 74 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe'vimuktamāhātmyaṃ nāma caturaśītyadhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 184 ||

<185>

atha pañcāśītyadhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
sūta uvāca
avimukte mahāpuṇye cā''stikāḥ śubhadarśanāḥ
vismayaṃ paramaṃ janmurharṣagadgadanisvanāḥ || 1 ||
ūcuste hṛṣṭamanasaḥ skandaṃ brahmavidāṃ varam
brahmaṇyo devaputrastvaṃ brāhmaṇo brāhmaṇapriyaḥ || 2 ||
brahmiṣṭho brahmavidbrahmā brahmendro brahmalokakṛt
brahmakṛdbrahmacārī tvaṃ brahmādirbrahmavatsalaḥ || 3 ||
brahmatulyodbhavakaro brahmatulya namo'stu te
ṛṣayo bhāvitātmānaḥ śrutvedaṃ pāvanaṃ mahat || 4 ||
tattvaṃ tu paramaṃ jñātaṃ yajjñātvā'mṛtamaśnute
svasti te'stu gamiṣyāmo bhūrlokaṃ śaṃkarālayam || 5 ||
yatrāsau sarvabhūtātmā sthāṇubhūtaḥ sthitaḥ prabhuḥ
sarvalokahitārthāya tapasyugre vyavasthitaḥ || 6 ||
saṃyojya yogenā''tmānaṃ raudrīṃ tanumupāśritaḥ
guhyakairātmabhūtastu ātmatulyaguṇairvṛtaḥ || 7 ||
tato brahmādibhirdevaiḥ siddhaiśca paramarṣibhiḥ
vijñaptaḥ parayā bhaktyā tvatprasādādgaṇeśvara || 8 ||
vastumicchāma niyatamavimukte suniścitāḥ
evaṃguṇo tathā martyā hyavimukte vasanti ye || 9 ||
dharmaśīlā jitakrodhā nirmamā niyatendri yāḥ
dhyānayogaparāḥ siddhiṃ gacchanti paramāvyayām || 10 ||
yogino yogasiddhāśca yogamokṣapradaṃ vibhum
upāsate bhaktiyuktā guhyaṃ devaṃ sanātanam || 11 ||
avimuktaṃ samāsādya prāptayogānmaheśvarāt
sapta brahmarṣayo nītā bhavasāyujyamāgatāḥ || 12 ||
etattu paramaṃ kṣetramavimuktaṃ vidurbudhāḥ
aprabuddhā na paśyanti bhavamāyāvimohitāḥ || 13 ||
tenaiva cābhyanujñātāstanniṣṭhāstatparāyaṇāḥ
avimukte tanuṃ tyaktvā śāntā yogagatiṃ gatāḥ || 14 ||
sthānaṃ guhyaṃ śmaśānānāṃ sarveṣāmetaducyate
na hi yogādṛte mokṣaḥ prāpyate bhuvi mānavaiḥ || 15 ||
avimukte nivasatāṃ yogo mokṣaśca sidhyati
eka eva prabhāvo'sti kṣetrasya parameśvari
anena janmanaiveha prāpyate gatiruttamā || 16 ||
avimukte nivasatā vyāsenāmitatejasā
naiva labdhā kvacidbhikṣā bhramamāṇena yatnataḥ || 17 ||
kṣudhāviṣṭastataḥ kruddho'cintayacchāpamuttamam
dinaṃ dinaṃ prati vyāsaḥ ṣaṇmāsaṃ yo'vatiṣṭhati || 18 ||
kathaṃ mamedaṃ nagaraṃ bhikṣādoṣāddhataṃ tvidam
vipro vā kṣattriyo vā'pi brāhmaṇī vidhavā'pi vā || 19 ||
saṃskṛtā'saṃskṛtā vā'pi paripakvāḥ kathaṃ nu me
na prayacchanti vai lokā brāhmaṇāścaryakārakam || 20 ||
eṣāṃ śāpaṃ pradāsyāmi tīrthasya nagarasya tu
tīrthaṃ cātīrthatāṃ yātu nagaraṃ śāpayāmyaham || 21 ||
mā bhūttripauruṣī vidyā mā bhūttripauruṣaṃ dhanam
mā bhūttripauruṣaṃ sakhyaṃ vyāso vārāṇasīṃ śapan || 22 ||
avimukte nivasatāṃ janānāṃ puṇyakarmaṇām
vighnaṃ sṛjāmi sarveṣāṃ yena siddhirna vidyate || 23 ||
vyāsacittaṃ tadā jñātvā devadeva umāpatiḥ
bhītabhītastadā gaurīṃ tāṃ priyāṃ paryabhāṣata || 24 ||
śṛṇu devi vaco mahyaṃ yādṛśaṃ pratyupasthitam
kṛṣṇadvaipāyanaḥ kopācchāpaṃ dātuṃ samudyataḥ || 25 ||
devyuvāca
kimarthaṃ śapate kruddho vyāsaḥ kena prakopitaḥ
kiṃ kṛtaṃ bhagavaṃstasya yena śāpaṃ prayacchati || 26 ||
devadeva uvāca
anena sutapastaptaṃ bahūnvarṣagaṇānpriye
mauninā dhyānayuktena dvādaśābdānvarānane || 27 ||
tataḥ kṣudhā susaṃjātā bhikṣāmaṭitumāgataḥ
naivāsya kenacidbhikṣā grāsārdhamapi bhāmini || 28 ||
evaṃ bhagavataḥ kāla āsītṣāṇmāsiko muneḥ
tataḥ krodhaparītātmā śāpaṃ dāsyati so'dhunā || 29 ||
yāvannaiṣa śapettāvadupāyastatra cintyatām
kṛṣṇadvaipāyanaṃ vyāsaṃ viddhi nārāyaṇaṃ priye || 30 ||
ko'sya śāpānna bibheti hyapi sākṣātpitāmahaḥ
adaivaṃ daivataṃ kuryāddaivaṃ cāpyapadaivatam || 31 ||
āvāṃ tu mānuṣau bhūtvā gṛhasthāvihavāsinau
tasya tṛptikarīṃ bhikṣāṃ prayacchāvo varānane || 32 ||
evamuktā tato devī devena śaṃbhunā tadā
vyāsasya darśanaṃ dattvā kṛtvā veṣaṃ tu mānuṣam || 33 ||
ehyehi bhagavansādho bhikṣāṃ grāhaya sattama
asmadgṛhe kadācittvaṃ nā''gato'smi mahāmune || 34 ||
etacchrutvā prītamanā bhikṣāṃ grahītumāgataḥ
bhikṣāṃ dattvā tu vyāsāya ṣaḍrasāmamṛtopamām || 35 ||
anāsvāditapūrvā sā bhakṣitā muninā tadā
bhikṣāṃ vyāsastato bhuktvā cintayanhṛṣṭamānasaḥ || 36 ||
vavande varadaṃ devaṃ devīṃ ca girijāṃ tadā
vyāsaḥ kamalapatrākṣa idaṃ vacanamabravīt || 37 ||
devo devī nadī gaṅgā miṣṭamannaṃ śubhā gatiḥ
vārāṇasyāṃ viśālākṣi vāsaḥ kasya na rocate || 38 ||
evamuktvā tato vyāso nagarīmavalokayan
cintayānastato bhikṣāṃ hṛdayānandakāriṇīm || 39 ||
apaśyatpurato devaṃ devīṃ ca girijāṃ tadā
gṛhāṅgaṇasthitaṃ vyāsaṃ devadevo'bravīdidam || 40 ||
iha kṣetre na vastavyaṃ krodhanastvaṃ mahāmune
evaṃ vismayamāpanno devaṃ vyāso'bravīdvacaḥ || 41 ||
vyāsa uvāca
caturdaśyāmathāṣṭamyāṃ pradeśaṃ dātumarhasi
evamastvityanujñāya tatraivāntaradhīyata || 42 ||
na tadgṛhaṃ na sā devī na devo jñāyate kvacit
evaṃ trailokyavikhyātaḥ purā vyāso mahātapāḥ || 43 ||
jñātvā kṣetraguṇānsarvānsthitastasyaiva pārśvataḥ
evaṃ vyāsaṃ sthitaṃ jñātvā kṣetraṃ śaṃsanti paṇḍitāḥ || 45 ||
avimuktaguṇānsarvānkaḥ samartho vadiṣyati
devabrāhmaṇavidviṣṭā devabhaktiviḍambakāḥ || 46 ||
brahmaghnāśca kṛtaghnāśca tathā naiṣkṛtikāśca ye
lokadviṣo gurudviṣastīrthāyatanadūṣakāḥ || 47 ||
sadā pāparatāścaiva ye cānye kutsitā bhuvi
teṣāṃ nāstīti vāso vai sthito'sau daṇḍanāyakaḥ
rakṣaṇārthaṃ niyuktaṃ vai daṇḍanāyakamuttamam
pūjayitvā yathāśaktyā gandhapuṣpādidhūpakaiḥ || 48 ||
namaskāraṃ tataḥ kṛtvā nāyakasya tu mantravit
sarvavarṇāvṛte kṣetre nānāvidhasarīsṛpe || 49 ||
īśvarānugṛhītā hi gatiṃ gāṇeśvarīṃ gatāḥ
nānārūpadharā divyā nānāveṣadharāstathā || 50 ||
surā vai ye tu sarve ca tanniṣṭhāstatparāyaṇāḥ
yadicchanti paraṃ sthānamakṣayaṃ tadavāpnuyuḥ || 51 ||
paraṃ puraṃ daivapurādviśiṣyate taduttaraṃ brahmapurātpuraḥ sthitam
tapobalādīśvarayoganirmitaṃ na tatsamaṃ brahmadivaukasālayam
manoramaṃ kāmagamaṃ hyanāmayamatītya tejāṃsi tapāṃsi yogavat || 52 ||
adhiṣṭhitastu tatsthāne devadevo virājate
tapāṃsi yāni tapyante vratāni niyamāśca ye || 53 ||
sarvatīrthābhiṣekaṃ tu sarvadānaphalāni ca
sarvayajñeṣu yatpuṇyamavimukte tadāpnuyāt || 54 ||
atītaṃ vartamānaṃ ca ajñānājjñānato'pi vā
sarvaṃ tasya ca yatpāpaṃ kṣetraṃ dṛṣṭvā vinaśyati || 55 ||
śāntairdāntaistapastaptaṃ yatkiṃciddharmasaṃjñitam
sarvaṃ ca tadavāpnoti avimukte jitendri yaḥ || 56 ||
avimuktaṃ samāsādya liṅgamarcayate naraḥ
kalpakoṭiśataiścāpi nāsti tasya punarbhavaḥ || 57 ||
amarā hyakṣayāścaiva krīḍanti bhavasaṃnidhau
kṣetratīrthopaniṣadamavimuktaṃ na saṃśayaḥ || 58 ||
avimukte mahādevamarcayanti stuvanti vai
sarvapāpavinirmuktāste tiṣṭhantyajarāmarāḥ || 59 ||
sarvakāmāśca ye yajñāḥ punarāvṛttikāḥ smṛtāḥ
avimukte mṛtā ye ca sarve te hyanivartakāḥ || 60 ||
grahanakṣatratārāṇāṃ kālena patanādbhayam
avimukte mṛtānāṃ tu patanaṃ naiva vidyate || 61 ||
kalpakoṭisahasraistu kalpakoṭiśatairapi
na teṣāṃ punarāvṛttirmṛtā ya kṣatra uttame || 62 ||
saṃsārasāgare ghore bhramantaḥ kālaparyayāt
avimuktaṃ samāsādya gacchanti paramāṃ gatim || 63 ||
jñātvā kaliyugaṃ ghoraṃ hāhābhūtamacetanam
avimuktaṃ na muñcanti kṛtārthāste narā bhuvi || 64 ||
avimuktaṃ praviṣṭastu yadi gacchettataḥ punaḥ
tadā hasanti bhūtāni anyonyakaratāḍanaiḥ || 65 ||
kāmakrodhena lobhena grastā ye bhuvi mānavāḥ
niṣkramante narā devi daṇḍanāyakamohitāḥ || 66 ||
japadhyānavihīnānāṃ jñānavarjitacetasām
tato duḥkhahatānāṃ ca gatirvārāṇasī nṛṇām || 67 ||
tīrthānāṃ pañcakaṃ sāraṃ viśveśānandakānane
daśāśvamedhaṃ lolārkaḥ keśavo bindumādhavaḥ || 68 ||
pañcamī tu mahāśreṣṭhā procyate maṇikarṇikā
ebhistu tīrthavaryaiśca varṇyate hyavimuktam || 69 ||
eka eva prabhāvo'sti kṣetrasya parameśvari
ekena janmanā devi mokṣaṃ paśyantyanuttamam || 70 ||
etadvai kathitaṃ sarvaṃ devyai devena bhāṣitam
avimuktasya kṣetrasya tatsarvaṃ kathitaṃ dvijāḥ || 71 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe'vimuktamāhātmyaṃ nāma pañcāśītyadhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 185 ||

<186>

atha ṣaḍaśītyadhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
ṛṣaya ūcuḥ
māhātmyamavimuktasya yathāvatkathitaṃ tvayā
idānīṃ narmadāyāstu māhātmyaṃ vada sattama || 1 ||
yatraoṃkārasya māhātmyaṃ kapilāsaṃgamasya ca
amareśasya caivā''hurmāhātmyaṃ pāpanāśanam || 2 ||
kathaṃ pralayakāle tu na naṣṭā narmadā purā
mārkaṇḍeyaśca bhagavānna vinaṣṭastadā kila
tvayoktaṃ tadidaṃ sarvaṃ punarvistarato vada || 3 ||
sūta uvāca
etadeva purā pṛṣṭaḥ pāṇḍavena mahātmanā
narmadāyāstu māhātmyaṃ mārkaṇḍeyo mahāmuniḥ || 4 ||
ugreṇa tapasā yukto vanastho vanavāsinā
pṛṣṭaḥ pūrvāṃ mahāgāthāṃ dharmaputreṇa dhīmatā || 5 ||
yudhiṣṭhira uvāca
śrutā me vividhā dharmāstvatprasādāddvijottama
bhūyaśca śrotumicchāmi tanme kathaya suvrata || 6 ||
kathameṣā mahāpuṇyā nadī sarvatra viśrutā
narmadā nāma vikhyātā tanme brūhi mahāmune || 7 ||
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca
narmadā saritāṃ śreṣṭhā sarvapāpapraṇāśinī
tārayetsarvabhūtāni sthāvarāṇi carāṇi ca || 8 ||
narmadāyāstu māhātmyaṃ purāṇe yanmayā śrutam
tadetaddhi mahārāja tatsarvaṃ kathayāmi te || 9 ||
puṇyā kanakhale gaṅgā kurukṣetre sarasvatī
grāme vā yadi vā'raṇye puṇyā sarvatra narmadā || 10 ||
tribhiḥ sārasvataṃ toyaṃ saptāhena tu yāmunam
sadyaḥ punāti gāṅgeyaṃ darśanādeva nārmadam || 11 ||
kaliṅgadeśe paścārdhe parvate'marakaṇṭake
puṇyā ca triṣu lokeṣu ramaṇīyā manoramā || 12 ||
sadevāsuragandharvā ṛṣayaśca tapodhanāḥ
tapastaptvā mahārāja siddhiṃ ca paramāṃ gatāḥ || 13 ||
tatra snātvā naro rājanniyamastho jitendri yaḥ
upoṣya rajanīmekāṃ kulānāṃ tārayecchatam || 14 ||
jaleśvare naraḥ snātvā piṇḍaṃ dattvā yathāvidhi
pitarastasya tṛpyanti yāvadābhūtasaṃplavam || 15 ||
parvatasya samantāttu rudra koṭiḥ pratiṣṭhitā
snātvā yaḥ kurute tatra gandhamālyānulepanaiḥ || 16 ||
prītastasya bhaveccharvo rudra koṭirna saṃśayaḥ
paścime parvatasyānte svayaṃ devo maheśvaraḥ || 17 ||
tatra snātvā śucirbhūtvā brahmacārī jitendri yaḥ
pitṛkāryaṃ ca kurvīta vidhivanniyatendri yaḥ || 18 ||
tilodakena tatraiva tarpayetpitṛdevatāḥ
āsaptamaṃ kulaṃ tasya svarge modeta pāṇḍava || 19 ||
ṣaṣṭirvarṣasahasrāṇi svargaloke mahīyate
apsarogaṇasaṃkīrṇe siddhacāraṇasevite || 20 ||
divyagandhānuliptaśca divyālaṃkārabhūṣitaḥ
tataḥ svargātparibhraṣṭo jāyate vipule kule || 21 ||
dhanavāndānaśīlaśca dhārmikaścaiva jāyate
punaḥ smarati tattīrthaṃ gamanaṃ tatra rocate || 22 ||
kulāni tārayetsapta rudra lokaṃ sa gacchati
yojanānāṃ śataṃ sāgraṃ śrūyate sariduttamā || 23 ||
vistāreṇa tu rājendra yojanadvayamāyatā
ṣaṣṭistīrthasahasrāṇi ṣaṣṭikoṭyastathaiva ca || 24 ||
sarvaṃ tasya samantāttu tiṣṭhatyamarakaṇṭake
brahmacārī śucirbhūtvā jitakrodho jitendri yaḥ || 25 ||
sarvahiṃsānivṛttastu sarvabhūtahite rataḥ
evaṃ sarvasamācāro yastu prāṇānparityajet || 26 ||
tasya puṇyaphalaṃ rājañśṛṇuṣvāvahito mama
śataṃ varṣasahasrāṇāṃ svarge modeta pāṇḍava || 27 ||
apsarogaṇasaṃkīrṇe siddhacāraṇasevite
divyagandhānuliptaśca divyapuṣpopaśobhitaḥ || 28 ||
krīḍate devalokastho daivataiḥ saha modate
tataḥ svargātparibhraṣṭo rājā bhavati vīryavān || 29 ||
gṛhaṃ tu labhate vai sa nānāratnavibhūṣitam
stambhairmaṇimayairdivyairvrajravaiḍūryabhūṣitaiḥ || 30 ||
ālekhyasahitaṃ divyaṃ dāsīdāsasamanvitam
mattamātaṅgaśabdaiśca hayānāṃ hreṣitena ca || 31 ||
kṣubhyate tasya taddvāramindra sya bhavanaṃ yathā
rājarājeśvaraḥ śrīmānsarvastrījanavallabhaḥ || 32 ||
tasmingṛha uṣitvā tu krīḍābhogasamanvite
jīvedvarṣaśataṃ sāgraṃ sarvarogavivarjitaḥ || 33 ||
evaṃ bhogo bhavettasya yo mṛto'marakaṇṭake
agnau viṣajale vā'pi tathā caiva hyanāśake || 34 ||
anivartikā gatistasya pavanasyāmbare yathā
patanaṃ kurute yastu amareśe narādhipa || 35 ||
kanyānāṃ trisahasrāṇi ekaikasyāpi cāpare
tiṣṭhanti bhuvane tasya preṣaṇaṃ prārthayanti ca
divyabhogaiḥ susaṃpannaḥ krīḍate kālamakṣayam || 36 ||
pṛthivyāmāsamudrā yāmīdṛśo naiva jāyate
yādṛśo'yaṃ nṛpaśreṣṭha parvate'marakaṇṭake || 37 ||
tāvattīrthaṃ tu vijñeyaṃ parvatasya tu paścime
hrado jaleśvaro nāma triṣu lokeṣu viśrutaḥ || 38 ||
tatra piṇḍapradānena saṃdhyopāsanakarmaṇā
pitaro daśa varṣāṇi tarpitāstu bhavanti vai || 39 ||
dakṣiṇe narmadākūle kapileti mahānadī
sakalārjunasaṃchannā nātidūre vyavasthitā || 40 ||
sā'pi puṇyā mahābhāgā triṣu lokeṣu viśrutā
tatra koṭiśataṃ sāgraṃ tīrthānāṃ tu yudhiṣṭhira || 41 ||
purāṇe śrūyate rājansarvaṃ koṭiguṇaṃ bhavet
tasyāstīre tu ye vṛkṣāḥ patitāḥ kālaparyayāt || 42 ||
narmadātoyasaṃspṛṣṭāste'pi yānti parāṃ gatim
dvitīyā tu mahābhāgā viśalyakaraṇī śubhā || 43 ||
tatra tīrthe naraḥ snātvā viśalyo bhavati kṣaṇāt
tatra devagaṇāḥ sarve sakiṃnaramahoragāḥ || 44 ||
yakṣarākṣasagandharvā ṛṣayaśca tapodhanāḥ
sarve samāgatāstatra parvate'marakaṇṭake || 45 ||
taiśca sarvaiḥ samāgamya munibhiśca tapodhanaiḥ
narmadāmāśritā puṇyā viśalyā nāma nāmataḥ || 46 ||
utpāditā mahābhāgā sarvapāpapraṇāśinī
tatra snātvā naro rājanbrahmacārī jitendri yaḥ || 47 ||
upoṣya rajanīmekāṃ kulānāṃ tārayecchatam
kapilā ca viśalyā ca śrūyate rājasattama || 48 ||
īśvareṇa purā prokte lokānāṃ hitakāmyayā
tatra snātvā naro rājannaśvamedhaphalaṃ labhet || 49 ||
anāśakaṃ tu yaḥ kuryāttasmiṃstīrthe narādhipa
sarvapāpaviśuddhātmā rudra lokaṃ sa gacchati || 50 ||
narmadāyāstu rājendra purāṇe yanmayā śrutam
yatra yatra naraḥ snātvā cāśvamedhaphalaṃ labhet || 51 ||
ye vasantyuttare kūle rudra loke vasanti te
sarasvatyāṃ ca gaṅgāyāṃ narmadāyāṃ yudhiṣṭhira || 52 ||
samaṃ snānaṃ ca dānaṃ ca yathā me śaṃkaro'bravīt
parityajati yaḥ prāṇānparvate'marakaṇṭake || 53 ||
varṣakoṭiśataṃ sāgraṃ rudra loke mahīyate
narmadāyā jalaṃ puṇyaṃ phenormibhiralaṃkṛtam || 54 ||
pavitraṃ śirasā vandyaṃ sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate
narmadā ca sadā puṇyā brahmahatyāpahāriṇī || 55 ||
