ahorātropavāsena mucyate brahmahatyayā
evaṃ ramyā ca puṇyā narmadā pāṇḍunandana || 56 ||
trayāṇāmapi lokānāṃ puṇyā hyeṣā mahānadī
vaṭeśvare mahāpuṇye gaṅgādvāre tapovane || 57 ||
eteṣu sarvasthāneṣu dvijāḥ syuḥ saṃśitavratāḥ
śrutaṃ daśaguṇaṃ puṇyaṃ narmadodadhisaṃgame || 58 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe narmadāmāhātmye ṣaḍaśītyadhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 186 ||

<187>

atha saptāśītyadhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca
narmadā tu nadī śreṣṭhā puṇyā puṇyatamā hitā
munibhistu mahābhāgairvibhaktā mokṣakāṅkṣibhiḥ || 1 ||
yajñopavītamātrāṇi pravibhaktāni pāṇḍava
teṣu snātvā tu rājendra sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate || 2 ||
śaleśvaraṃ paraṃ tīrthaṃ triṣu lokeṣu viśrutam
tasyotpattiṃ kathayataḥ śṛṇu tvaṃ pāṇḍunandana || 3 ||
purā suragaṇāḥ sarve sendrā ścaiva marudgaṇāḥ
stuvanti te mahātmānaṃ devadevaṃ maheśvaram
stuvantaste tu saṃprāptā yatra devo maheśvaraḥ || 4 ||
vijñāpayanti deveśaṃ sendrā ścaiva marudgaṇāḥ
bhayodvignā virūpākṣaṃ paritrāyasva naḥ prabho || 5 ||
bhagavānuvāca
svāgataṃ tu suraśreṣṭhāḥ kimarthamiha cā''gatāḥ
kiṃ duḥkhaṃ ko nu saṃtāpaḥ kuto vā bhayamāgatam || 6 ||
kathayadhvaṃ mahābhāgā evamicchāmi veditum
evamuktāstu rudre ṇākathayansaṃśitavratāḥ || 7 ||
ṛṣaya ūcuḥ
ativīryo mahāghoro dānavo baladarpitaḥ
bāṇo nāmeti vikhyāto yasya vai tripuraṃ puram || 8 ||
gagane satataṃ divyaṃ bhramate tasya tejasā
tato bhītā virūpākṣa tvāmeva śaraṇaṃ gatāḥ || 9 ||
trāyasva mahato duḥkhāttvaṃ hi naḥ paramā gatiḥ
evaṃ prasādaṃ deveśa sarveṣāṃ kartumarhasi || 10 ||
yena devāḥ sagandharvāḥ sukhamedhanti śaṃkara
parāṃ nirvṛtimāyānti tatprabho kartumarhasi || 11 ||
bhagavānuvāca
etatsarvaṃ kariṣyāmi mā viṣādaṃ gamiṣyatha
acireṇaiva kālena kuryāṃ yuṣmatsukhāvaham || 12 ||
āśvāsya sa tu tānsarvānnarmadātaṭamāśritaḥ
cintayāmāsa deveśastadvadhaṃ prati mānada || 13 ||
atha kena prakāreṇa hantavyaṃ tripuraṃ mayā
evaṃ saṃcintya bhagavānnāradaṃ cāsmarattadā
smaraṇādeva saṃprāpto nāradaḥ samupasthitaḥ || 14 ||
nārada uvāca
ājñāpaya mahādeva kimarthaṃ ca smṛto hyaham
kiṃ kāryaṃ tu mayā deva kartavyaṃ kathayasva me || 15 ||
śrībhagavānuvāca
gaccha nārada tatraiva yatraitattripuraṃ mahat
bāṇasya dānavendra sya śīghraṃ gatvā ca tatkuru || 16 ||
tā bhartṛdevatāstatra striyaścāpsarasāṃ samāḥ
tāsāṃ vai tejasā vipra bhramate tripuraṃ divi || 17 ||
tatra gatvā tu viprendra matimanyāṃ pracodaya
devasya vacanaṃ śrutvā munistvaritavikramaḥ || 18 ||
strīṇāṃ hṛdayanāśāya praviṣṭastatpuraṃ prati
śobhate yatpuraṃ divyaṃ nānāratnopaśobhitam || 19 ||
śatayojanavistīrṇaṃ tato dviguṇamāyatam
tato'paśyaddhi tatraiva bāṇaṃ tu baladarpitam || 20 ||
maṇikuṇḍalakeyūramukuṭena virājitam
hemahāraśatai ratnaiścandra kāntavibhūṣitam || 21 ||
raśanā tasya ratnāḍhyā bāhū kanakamaṇḍitau
candra kāntamahāvajramaṇividrumabhūṣite || 22 ||
dvādaśārkadyutinibhe niviṣṭaṃ paramāsane
utthito nāradaṃ dṛṣṭvā dānavendro mahābalaḥ || 23 ||
bāṇa uvāca
devarṣe tvaṃ svayaṃ prāpto hyarghyaṃ pādyaṃ nivedaye
so'bhivādya yathānyāyaṃ kriyatāṃ kiṃ dvijottama || 24 ||
cirāttvamāgato vipra āsyatāṃ munipuṃgava
evaṃ saṃbhāṣayitvā tu nāradamṛṣisattamam
tasya bhāryā mahādevī hyanaupamyā tu nāmataḥ || 25 ||
anaupamyovāca
bhagavanmānuṣe loke kena tuṣyati keśavaḥ
vratena niyamenātha dānena tapasā'pi vā || 26 ||
nārada uvāca
tiladhenuṃ ca yo dadyādbrāhmaṇe vedapārage
sasāgaravanadvīpā dattā bhavati medinī || 27 ||
sūryakoṭipratīkāśairvimānaiḥ sārvakāmikaiḥ
modate cākṣayaṃ kālaṃ yāvaccandrā rkatārakam || 28 ||
āmrāmalakapitthāni badarāṇi tathaiva ca
kadambacampakāśokapuṃnāgavividhadrumān || 29 ||
aśvatthapippalaṃ caiva kadalīvaṭadāḍimān
picumandaṃ madhūkaṃ ca upoṣya strī dadāti yā || 30 ||
stanau kapitthasadṛśāvūrū ca kadalīsamau
aśvatthe vandanīyā ca picumande sugandhinī || 31 ||
campake campakāmā syādaśoke śokavarjitā
madhūke madhuraṃ vakti vaṭe ca mṛdugātrikā || 32 ||
badarī sarvadā strīṇāṃ mahāsaubhāgyadāyinī
kukkuṭī karkaṭī caiva dra vyaṣaṣṭhī na śasyate || 33 ||
kadambamiśrakanakamañjarīpūjanaṃ tathā
anagnipakvamannaṃ ca pakvānnānāmabhakṣaṇam || 34 ||
phalānāṃ ca parityāgaḥ saṃdhyāmaunaṃ tathaiva ca
prathamaṃ kṣetrapālasya pūjā kāryā prayatnataḥ || 35 ||
tasyā bhavati vai bhartā mukhaprekṣī sadā'naghe
aṣṭamī ca caturthī ca pañcamī dvādaśī tathā || 36 ||
saṃkrāntirviṣuvaccaiva dinacchidra mukhaṃ tathā
etāṃstu divasāndivyānupavasanti yāḥ striyaḥ
tāsāṃ tu dharmayuktānāṃ svargavāso na saṃśayaḥ || 37 ||
kalikāluṣyanirmuktāḥ sarvapāpavivarjitāḥ
upavāsaratāṃ nārīṃ nopasarpati tāṃ yamaḥ || 38 ||
anaupamyovāca
asminkṛtena puṇyena purājanmakṛtena vā
bhavadāgamanaṃ jātaṃ kiṃcitpṛcchāmyahaṃ vratam || 39 ||
asti vindhyāvalirnāma balipatnī yaśasvinī
śvaśrūrmamāpi viprendra na tuṣyati kadācana || 40 ||
śvaśuro'pi sarvakālaṃ dṛṣṭvā cāpi na paśyati
asti kumbhīnasī nāma nanāndā pāpakāriṇī || 41 ||
dṛṣṭvā caivāṅgulībhaṅgaṃ sadākālaṃ karoti mām
divyena tu pathā yāti mama saukhyaṃ kathaṃ vada || 42 ||
ūṣare na prarohanti bījāṅkurāḥ kathaṃcana
yena vratena cīrṇena bhavanti vaśagā mama
tadvrataṃ brūhi viprendra dāsabhāvaṃ vrajāmi te || 43 ||
nārada uvāca
yadetatte mayā pūrvaṃ vratamuktaṃ śubhānane
anena pārvatī devī cīrṇena varavarṇini || 44 ||
śaṃkarasya śarīrasthā viṣṇorlakṣmīstathaiva ca
sāvitrī brahmaṇaścaiva vasiṣṭhasyāpyarundhatī || 45 ||
etenopoṣiteneha bhartā sthāsyati te vaśe
śvaśrūśvaśurayoścaiva mukhabandho bhaviṣyati || 46 ||
evaṃ śrutvā tu suśroṇi yatheṣṭaṃ kartumarhasi
nāradasya vacaḥ śrutvā rājñī vacanamabravīt || 47 ||
prasādaṃ kuru viprendra dānaṃ grāhyaṃ yathepsitam
suvarṇamaṇiratnāni vastrāṇyābharaṇāni ca || 48 ||
tava dāsyāmyahaṃ vipra yaccānyadapi durlabham
pragṛhāṇa dvijaśreṣṭha prīyetāṃ hariśaṃkarau || 49 ||
nārada uvāca
anyasmai dīyatāṃ bhadre kṣīṇavṛttistu yo dvijaḥ
ahaṃ tu sarvasaṃpanno madbhaktiḥ kriyatāmiti || 50 ||
evaṃ tāsāṃ mano hṛtvā sarvāsāṃ tu pativratāt
jagāma bharataśreṣṭha svakīyaṃ sthānakaṃ punaḥ || 51 ||
tato hyahṛṣṭahṛdayā anyatogatamānasā
pativratātvamutsṛjya tāsāṃ tejo gataṃ tataḥ
pure cchidraṃ samutpannaṃ bāṇasya tu mahātmanaḥ || 52 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe narmadāmāhātmye saptāśītyadhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 187 ||

<188>

athāṣṭāśītyadhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca
yanmāṃ pṛcchasi kaunteya tanme kathayataḥ śṛṇu
etasminnantare rudro narmadātaṭamāsthitaḥ || 1 ||
nāmnā māheśvaraṃ sthānaṃ triṣu lokeṣu viśrutam
tasminsthāne mahādevo'cintayattripurakṣayam || 2 ||
gāṇḍīvaṃ mandaraṃ kṛtvā guṇaṃ kṛtvā ca vāsukim
sthānaṃ kṛtvā tu vaiśākhaṃ viṣṇuṃ kṛtvā śarottamam || 3 ||
śalye cāgniṃ pratiṣṭhāpya mukhe vāyuṃ samarpayan
hayāṃśca caturo vedānsarvadevamayaṃ ratham || 4 ||
abhīṣavo'śvinau devāvakṣo vajradharaḥ svayam
sa tasyā'jñāṃ samādāya toraṇe dhanadaḥ sthitaḥ || 5 ||
yamastu dakṣiṇe haste vāme kālastu dāruṇaḥ
cakre tvamarakoṭyastu gandharvā lokaviśrutāḥ || 6 ||
prajāpatiratha śreṣṭho brahmā caiva tu sārathiḥ
evaṃ kṛtvā tu deveśaḥ sarvadevamayaṃ ratham || 7 ||
so'tiṣṭhatsthāṇubhūtastu sahasraparivatsarān
yadā trīṇi sametāni antarikṣe sthitāni vai || 8 ||
triparvaṇā triśalyena tadā tāni vyabhedayat
śaraḥ pracoditastena rudre ṇa tripuraṃ prati || 9 ||
bhraṣṭatejāḥ striyo jātā balaṃ tāsāṃ vyaśīryata
utpātāśca pure tasminprādurbhūtāḥ sahasraśaḥ || 10 ||
tripurasya vināśāya kālarūpā bhavaṃstadā
aṭṭahāsaṃ pramuñcanti hayā kāṣṭhamayāstadā || 11 ||
nimeṣonmeṣaṇaṃ caiva kurvanti citrarūpiṇaḥ
svapne paśyanti cā''tmānaṃ raktāmbaravibhūṣitam || 12 ||
svapne tu sarve paśyanti viparītāni yāni tu
etānpaśyanti utpātāṃstatra sthāne tu ye janāḥ || 13 ||
teṣāṃ balaṃ ca buddhiśca harakopena nāśite
tataḥ sāṃvartako vāyuryugāntapratimo mahān || 14 ||
samīrito'nalastena uttamāṅgena dhāvati
jvalanti pādapāstatra patanti śikharāṇi ca || 15 ||
sarvato vyākulībhūtaṃ hāhākāramacetanam
bhagnodyānāni sarvāṇi kṣipraṃ tatpratyabhajyata || 16 ||
tenaiva pīḍitaṃ sarvaṃ jvalitaṃ triśikhaiḥ śaraiḥ
drumāścā''rāmakhaṇḍāni gṛhāṇi vividhāni ca || 17 ||
daśadikṣu pravṛtto'yaṃ samṛddho havyavāhanaḥ
manaḥśilāpuñjanibho diśo daśa vibhāgaśaḥ || 18 ||
śikhāśatairanekaistu prajajvāla hutāśanaḥ
sarvaṃ kiṃśukavarṇābhaṃ jvalitaṃ dṛśyate puram || 19 ||
gṛhādgṛhāntaraṃ naiva gantuṃ dhūmena śakyate
harakopānalairdagdhaṃ krandamānaṃ suduḥkhitam || 20 ||
pradīptaṃ sarvato dikṣu dahyate tripuraṃ puram
prāsādaśikharāgrāṇi vyaśīryanta sahasraśaḥ || 21 ||
nānāmaṇivicitrāṇi vimānānyapyanekadhā
gṛhāṇi caiva ramyāṇi dahyante dīptavahninā || 22 ||
dhāvanti drumakhaṇḍeṣu valabhīṣu tathā janāḥ
devāgāreṣu sarveṣu prajvalantaḥ pradhāvitāḥ || 23 ||
krandanti cānalapluṣṭā rudanti vividhaiḥ svaraiḥ
girikūṭanibhāstatra dṛśyante'ṅgārarāśayaḥ || 24 ||
gajāśca girikūṭābhā dahyamānā yatastataḥ
stuvanti devadeveśaṃ paritrāyasva naḥ prabho || 25 ||
anyonyaṃ ca pariṣvajya hutāśanapradīpitāḥ
snehātpradahyamānāśca tathaiva vilayaṃ gatāḥ || 26 ||
dahyante dānavāstatra śataśo'tha sahasraśaḥ || 27 ||
haṃsakāraṇḍavākīrṇā nalinyaḥ sahapaṅkajāḥ
dṛśyante'naladagdhāni purodyānāni dīrghikāḥ || 28 ||
amlānapaṅkajacchannā vistīrṇā yojanāyatāḥ
girikūṭanibhāstatra prāsādā ratnabhūṣitāḥ || 29 ||
patantyanalanirdagdhā nistoyā jaladā iva
varastrībālavṛddheṣu goṣu pakṣiṣu vājiṣu || 30 ||
nirdayo vyadahadvahnirharakrodhena preritaḥ
sahasraśaḥ prabuddhāśca suptāśca bahavo janāḥ || 31 ||
putramāliṅgya te gāḍhaṃ dahyante tripurāgninā
nidāgho'bhūnmahāvahnerantakālo yathā tathā || 32 ||
kecidguptāḥ pradagdhāstu bhāryotsaṅgagatāstathā
pitrā mātrā ca suśliṣṭā dagdhāste tripurāgninā
atha tasminpure dīpte striyaścāpsarasopamāḥ || 33 ||
agnijvālāhatāstatra hyapatandharaṇītale
kācicchyāmā viśālākṣī muktāvalivibhūṣitā || 34 ||
dhūmenā''kulitā sā tu patitā dharaṇītale
kācitkanakavarṇābhā indra nīlavibhūṣitā || 35 ||
bhartāraṃ patitaṃ dṛṣṭvā patitā tasya copari
kācidādityasaṃkāśā prasuptā ca gṛhe sthitā || 36 ||
agnijvālāhatā sā tu patitā gatacetanā
utthito dānavastatra khaḍgahasto mahābalaḥ || 37 ||
vaiśvānarahataḥ so'pi patito dharaṇītale
meghavarṇā'parā nārī hārakeyūrabhūṣitā || 38 ||
śvetavastraparīdhānā bālaṃ stanyaṃ nyadhāpayat
dahyantaṃ bālakaṃ dṛṣṭvā rudatī meghaśabdavat || 39 ||
evaṃ sa tu dahannagnirharakrodhena preritaḥ
kāciccandra prabhā saumyā vajravaiḍūryabhūṣitā || 40 ||
sutamāliṅgya vepantī dagdhā patati bhūtale
kācitkundenduvarṇābhā krīḍantī sva gṛhe sthitā || 41 ||
gṛhe prajvalite sā tu pratibuddhā śikhārditā
paśyantī jvalitaṃ sarvaṃ hā suto me kathaṃ gataḥ || 42 ||
sutaṃ saṃdagdhamāliṅgya patitā dharaṇītale
ādityodayavarṇābhā lakṣmīvadanaśobhanā || 43 ||
tvaritā dahyamānā sā patitā dharaṇītale
kācitsuvarṇavarṇābhā nīlaratnairvibhūṣitā || 44 ||
dhūmenā''kulitā sā tu prasuptā dharaṇītale
anyā gṛhītahastā tu sakhi dahyati bālikā || 45 ||
anekadiśyaratnāḍhyā dṛṣṭvā dahanamohitā
śirasi hyañjaliṃ kṛtvā vijñāpayati pāvakam || 46 ||
bhagavanyadi vairaṃ te puruṣeṣvapakāriṣu
striyaḥ kimaparādhyanti gṛhapañjarakokilāḥ || 47 ||
pāpa nirdaya nirlajja kaste kopaḥ striyaḥ prati
na dākṣiṇyaṃ na te lajjā na satyaṃ śauryavarjita || 48 ||
anena hyupasargeṇa tūpālambhaṃ śikhinyadāt
kiṃ tvayā na śrutaṃ loke hyavadhyāḥ śatruyoṣitaḥ || 49 ||
kiṃ tu tubhyaṃ guṇā hyete dahanotsādanaṃ prati
na kāruṇyaṃ bhayaṃ vā'pi dākṣiṇyaṃ na striyaḥ prati || 50 ||
dayāṃ kurvanti mlecchāpi dahantīṃ vīkṣya yoṣitam
mlecchānāmapi kaṣṭo'si durnivāro hyacetanaḥ || 51 ||
ete caiva guṇāstubhyaṃ dahanotsādanaṃ prati
āsāmapi durācāra strīṇāṃ kiṃ te nipātane || 52 ||
duṣṭa nirghṛṇa nirlajja hutāśinmandabhāgyaka
nirāśatvaṃ durāvāsa balāddahasi nirdaya || 53 ||
evaṃ vilapamānāstā jalpantyaśca bahūnyapi
anyāḥ krośanti saṃkruddhā bālaśokena mohitāḥ || 54 ||
dahate nirdayo vahniḥ saṃkruddhaḥ pūrvaśatruvat
puṣkariṇyāṃ jalaṃ dagdhaṃ kūpeṣvapi tathaiva ca || 55 ||
asmānsaṃdahya mleccha tvaṃ kāṃ gatiṃ prāpayiṣyasi
evaṃ pralapitaṃ tāsāṃ śrutvā devo vibhāvasuḥ
mūrtimānsahasotthāya vahnirvacanamabravīt || 56 ||
agniruvāca
svavaśo naiva yuṣmākaṃ vināśaṃ tu karomyaham
ahamādeśa kartā vai nāhaṃ kartā'smyanugraham || 57 ||
rudra krodhasamāviṣṭo vicarāmi yathecchayā
tato bāṇo mahātejāstripuraṃ vīkṣya dīpitam || 58 ||
siṃhāsanasthaḥ provāca hyahaṃ devairvināśitaḥ
alpasattvairdurācārairīśvarasya niveditam || 59 ||
aparīkṣya tvahaṃ dagdhaḥ śaṃkareṇa mahātmanā
nānyaḥ śaktastu māṃ hantuṃ varjayitvā trilocanam || 60 ||
utthitaḥ śirasā kṛtvā liṅgaṃ tribhuvaneśvaram
nirgataḥ sa puradvārātparityajya suhṛtsutān || 61 ||
ratnāni yānyanarghāṇi striyo nānāvidhāstathā
gṛhītvā śirasā liṅgaṃ gacchangaganamaṇḍalam || 62 ||
stuvaṃśca devadeveśaṃ trilokādhipatiṃ śivam
tyaktā purī mayā deva yadi vadhyo'smi śaṃkara || 63 ||
tvatprasādānmahādeva mā me liṅgaṃ vinaśyatu
arcitaṃ hi mayā deva bhaktyā paramayā sadā || 64 ||
tvatkopādyadi vadhyo'haṃ tadidaṃ mā vinaśyatu
ślāghyametanmahādeva tvatkopāddahanaṃ mama || 65 ||
pratijanma mahādeva tvatpādanirato hyaham
toṭakacchandasā deva staumi tvāṃ parameśvara || 66 ||
śiva śaṃkara śarva harāya namo bhava bhīma maheśvara sarva namaḥ
kusumāyudhadehavināśakara tripurāntaka andhakaśūladhara || 67 ||
pramadāpriya kānta virakta namaḥ sasurāsurasiddhagaṇairnamita
hayavānarasiṃhagajendra mukhairatihrasvasudīrghaviśālamukhaiḥ || 68 ||
upalabdhumaśakyatarairasuraiḥ prathito'smi ca bāhuśatairbahubhiḥ
praṇato'smi bhavaṃ bhavabhaktirataścalacandra kalāṅkura deva namaḥ || 69 ||
na ca putrakalatrahayādi dhanaṃ mama tu tvadanusmaraṇaṃ śaraṇam
vyathito'smi śarīraśatairbahubhirgamitā ca mahānarakasya gatiḥ || 70 ||
na nivartati janma na pāpamatiḥ śucikarma nibaddhamapi tyajati
anukampati vibhramati trasati mama caiva kukarma nivārayati || 71 ||
yaḥ paṭhettoṭakaṃ divyaṃ prayataḥ śucimānasaḥ
bāṇasyeva yathā rudra stasyāpi varado bhavet || 72 ||
imaṃ stavaṃ mahādivyaṃ śrutvā devo maheśvaraḥ
prasannastu tadā tasya svayaṃ vacanamabravīt || 73 ||
maheśvara uvāca
na bhetavyaṃ tvayā vatsa sauvarṇe tiṣṭha dānava
putrapautrasuhṛdbandhubhāryābhṛtyajanaiḥ saha || 74 ||
adyaprabhṛti bāṇa tvamavadhyastridaśairapi
bhūyastasya varo datto devadevena pāṇḍava || 75 ||
akṣayaścāvyayo loke vicarasvākutobhayaḥ
tato nivārayāmāsa rudraḥ saptaśikhaṃ tadā || 76 ||
tṛtīyaṃ rakṣitaṃ tasya śaṃkareṇa mahātmanā
bhramattu gagane divyaṃ rudra tejaḥ prabhāvataḥ || 77 ||
evaṃ tu tripuraṃ dagdhaṃ śaṃkareṇa mahātmanā
jvālāmālāpradīptaṃ tatpatitaṃ dharaṇītale || 78 ||
ekaṃ nipatitaṃ tatra śrīśaile tripurāntake
dvitīyaṃ patitaṃ tasminparvate'marakaṇṭake || 79 ||
dagdheṣu teṣu rājendra rudra koṭiḥ pratiṣṭhitā
jvalattadapatattatra tena jvāleśvaraḥ smṛtaḥ || 80 ||
ūrdhvena prasthitāstasya divyajvālā divaṃ gatāḥ
hāhākārastadā jāto devāsurakṛto mahān || 81 ||
śaramastambhayadrudro māheśvarapurottame
evaṃ vṛttaṃ tadā tasminparvate'marakaṇṭake || 82 ||
caturdaśākhyaṃ bhuvanaṃ sa bhuktvā pāṇḍunandana
varṣakoṭisahasraṃ tu triṃśatkoṭyastathā'parāḥ || 83 ||
tato mahītalaṃ prāpya rājā bhavati dhārmikaḥ
pṛthivīmekacchatreṇa bhuṅkte sa tu na saṃśayaḥ || 84 ||
evaṃ puṇyo mahārāja parvato'marakaṇṭakaḥ
candra sūryoparāge tu gacchedyo'markaṇṭakam || 85 ||
aśvamedhāddaśaguṇaṃ pravadanti manīṣiṇaḥ
svargalokamavāpnoti dṛṣṭvā tatra maheśvaram || 86 ||
brahmahatyā gamiṣyanti rāhugraste divākare
tadevaṃ nikhilaṃ puṇyaṃ parvate'marakaṇṭake || 87 ||
manasā'pi smaredyastaṃ giriṃ tvamarakaṇṭakam
cāndrā yaṇaśataṃ sāgraṃ labhate nātra saṃśayaḥ || 88 ||
trayāṇāmapi lokānāṃ vikhyāto'marakaṇṭakaḥ
eṣa puṇyo giriśreṣṭhaḥ siddhagandharvasevitaḥ || 89 ||
nānādrumalatākīrṇā nānāpuṣpopaśobhitaḥ
mṛgavyāghrasahasraistu sevyamāno mahāgiriḥ || 90 ||
yatra saṃnihito devo devyā saha maheśvaraḥ
brahmā viṣṇustathā cendro vidyādharagaṇaiḥ saha || 91 ||
ṛṣibhiḥ kiṃnarairyakṣairnityameva niṣevitaḥ
vāsukiḥ sahitastatra krīḍate pannagottamaiḥ || 92 ||
pradakṣiṇaṃ tu yaḥ kuryātparvate'marakaṇṭake
pauṇḍarīkasya yajñasya phalaṃ prāpnoti mānavaḥ || 93 ||
tatra jvāleśvaraṃ nāma tīrthaṃ siddhaniṣevitam
tatra snātvā divaṃ yānti ye mṛtāste'punarbhavāḥ || 94 ||
jvāleśvare mahārāja yastu prāṇānparityajet
candra sūryoparāgeṣu tasyāpi śṛṇu yatphalam || 95 ||
sarvakarmavinirmukto jñānavijñānasaṃyutaḥ
rudra lokamavāpnoti yāvadābhūtasaṃplavam || 96 ||
amareśvaradevasya parvatasya ubhe taṭe
tatra tā ṛṣikoṭyastu tapastapyanti suvrata || 97 ||
samantādyojanakṣetro giriścāmarakaṇṭakaḥ || 98 ||
akāmo vā sakāmo vā narmadāyāṃ śubhe jale
snātvā mucyeta pāpebhyo rudra lokaṃ sa gacchati || 99 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe narmadāmāhātmye'ṣṭāśītyadhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 188 ||

<189>

atha navāśītyadhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
sūta uvāca
pṛcchanti te mahātmāno mārkaṇḍeyaṃ mahāmunim
yudhiṣṭhirapurogāste ṛṣayaśca tapodhanāḥ || 1 ||
ākhyāhi bhayavaṃstathyaṃ kāverīsaṃgamaṃ mahat
lokānāṃ ca hitārthāya asmākaṃ ca vivṛddhaye || 2 ||
sadā pāparatā ye ca narā duṣkṛtakāriṇaḥ
mucyante sarvapāpebhyo gacchanti paramaṃ padam
etadicchāma vijñātuṃ bhagavanvaktumarhasi || 3 ||
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca
śṛṇvantvavahitāḥ sarve yudhiṣṭhirapurogamāḥ
asti vīro mahāyakṣaḥ kuberaḥ satyavikramaḥ || 4 ||
idaṃ tīrthamanuprāpya rājā yakṣādhipo'bhavat
siddhiṃ prāpto mahārāja tanme nigadataḥ śṛṇu || 5 ||
kāverī narmadā yatra saṃgamo lokaviśrutaḥ
tatra snātvā śucirbhūtvā kuberaḥ satyavikramaḥ || 6 ||
tapo'tapyata yakṣendro divyaṃ varṣaśataṃ mahat
tasya tuṣṭo mahādevaḥ prādādvaramanuttamam || 7 ||
bho bho yakṣa mahāsattva varaṃ brūhi yathepsitam
brūhi kāryaṃ yatheṣṭaṃ tu yatte manasi vartate || 8 ||
kubera uvāca
yadi tuṣṭo'si me deva yadi deyo varo mama
adyaprabhṛti sarveṣāṃ yakṣāṇāmadhipo bhave || 9 ||
kuberasya vacaḥ śrutvā parituṣṭo maheśvaraḥ
evamastu tato devastatraivāntaradhīyata || 10 ||
so'pi labdhavaro yakṣaḥ śīghraṃ labdhaphalodayaḥ
pūjitaḥ sa tu yakṣaiśca hyabhiṣiktastu pārthiva || 11 ||
kāverīsaṃgamaṃ tatra sarvapāpapraṇāśanam
ye narā nābhijānanti vañcitāste na saṃśayaḥ || 12 ||
tasmātsarvaprayatnena tatra snāyīta mānavaḥ
kāverī ca gahāpuṇyā narmadā ca mahānadī || 13 ||
tatra snātvā tu rājendra hyarcayedvṛṣabhadhvajam
aśvamedhaphalaṃ prāpya rudra loke mahīyate || 14 ||
agnipraveśaṃ yaḥ kuryādyaśca kuryādanāśakam
anivartyā gatistasya yathā me śaṃkaro'bravīt || 15 ||
sevyamāno varastrībhiḥ krīḍate divi rudra vat
ṣaṣṭirvarṣasahasrāṇi ṣaṣṭikoṭyastathā parāḥ || 16 ||
modate rudra lokastho yatra tatraiva gacchati
puṇyakṣayātparibhraṣṭo rājā bhavati dhārmikaḥ || 17 ||
bhogavāndānaśīlaśca mahākulasamudbhavaḥ
tatra pītvā jalaṃ samyakcāndrā yaṇaphalaṃ labhet || 18 ||
svargaṃ gacchanti te martyā ye pibanti śubhaṃ jalam
gaṅgāyamunayormadhye yatphalaṃ prāpnuyānnaraḥ
kāverīsaṃgame snātvā tatphalaṃ tasya jāyate || 19 ||
evamādi tu rājendra kāverīsaṃgame mahat
puṇyaṃ mahatphalaṃ tatra sarvapāpapraṇāśanam || 20 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe narmadāmāhātmya ekonanavatyadhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 189 ||

<190>

atha navatyadhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca
nārmade cottare kūle tīrthaṃ yojanavistṛtam
yantreśvareti vikhyātaṃ sarvapāpaharaṃ param || 1 ||
tatra snātvā naro rājandaivataiḥ saha modate
pañca varṣasahasrāṇi krīḍate kāmarūpadhṛk || 2 ||
garjanaṃ ca tato gacchedyatra meghacayotthitaḥ
indra jinnāma saṃprāptastasya tīrthaprabhāvataḥ || 3 ||
meghanādaṃ tato gacchedyatra meghānugarjitam
meghanādo gaṇastatra paramāṃ gaṇatāṃ gataḥ || 4 ||
tato gacchettu rājendra tīrthamāmratakeśvaram
tatra snātvā naro rājangosahasraphalaṃ labhet || 5 ||
narmadottaratīre tu dhārātīrthaṃ tu viśrutam
tasmiṃstīrthe naraḥ snātvā tarpayetpitṛdevatāḥ || 6 ||
sarvānkāmānavāpnoti manasā ye vicintitāḥ
tato gacchettu rājendra brahmāvartamiti smṛtam || 7 ||
tatra saṃnihito brahmā nityameva yudhiṣṭhira
tatra snātvā tu rājendra brahmaloke mahīyate || 8 ||
tato'ṅgāreśvaraṃ gacchenniyato niyatāśanaḥ
sarvapāpavinirmukto rudra lokaṃ sa gacchati || 9 ||
tato gacchecca rājendra kapilātīrthamuttamam
tatra snātvā naro rājankapilādānamāpnuyāt || 10 ||
gacchetkarañjatīrthaṃ tu devarṣigaṇasevitam
tatra snātvā naro rājangolokaṃ samavāpnuyāt || 11 ||
tato gacchettu rājendra kuṇḍaleśvaramuttamam
tatra saṃnihito rudra stiṣṭhate hyumayā saha || 12 ||
tatra snātvā tu rājendra sa vandyastridaśairapi
pippaleśaṃ tato gacchetsarvapāpapraṇāśanam || 13 ||
tatra snātvā tu rājendra rudra loke mahīyate
tato gacchettu rājendra vimaleśvaramuttamam || 14 ||
tatra devaśilā ramyā ceśvareṇa vinirmitā
tatra prāṇaparityāgādrudra lokamavāpnuyāt || 15 ||
tataḥ puṣkariṇīṃ gacchettatra snānaṃ samācaret
snātamātro narastatra hīndra syārdhāsanaṃ labhet || 16 ||
narmadā saritāṃ śreṣṭhā rudra dehādviniḥsṛtā
tārayetsarvabhūtāni sthāvarāṇi carāṇi ca || 17 ||
sarvadevādhidevena tvīśvareṇa mahātmanā
kathitā ṛṣisaṃghebhyo hyasmākaṃ ca viśeṣataḥ || 18 ||
munibhiḥ saṃstutā hyeṣā narmadā pravarā nadī
rudra dehādviniṣkrāntā lokānāṃ hitakāmyayā || 19 ||
sarvapāpaharā nityaṃ sarvadevanamaskṛtā
saṃstutā devagandharvairapsarobhistathaiva ca || 20 ||
namaḥ puṇyajale hyādye namaḥ sāgaragāminī
namaste pāpanirdāhe namo devi varānane || 21 ||
namo'stu te ṛṣigaṇasiddhasevite namo'stu te śaṃkaradehaniḥsṛte
namo'stu te dharmabhṛtāṃ varaprade namo'stu te sarvapavitrapāvane || 22 ||
yastvidaṃ paṭhate stotraṃ nityaṃśraddhāsamanvitaḥ
brāhmaṇo vedamāpnoti kṣattriyo vijayī bhavet || 23 ||
vaiśyastu labhate lābhaṃ śūdra ścaiva śubhāṃ gatim
arthārthī labhate hyarthaṃ smaraṇādeva nityaśaḥ || 24 ||
narmadāṃ sevate nityaṃ svayaṃ devo maheśvaraḥ
tena puṇyā nadī jñeyā brahmahatyāpahāriṇī || 25 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe narmadāmāhātmye navatyadhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 190 ||

<191>

athaikanavatyadhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca
tadāprabhṛti brahmādyā ṛṣayaśca tapodhanāḥ
sevante narmadāṃ rājanrāgakrodhavivarjitāḥ || 1 ||
yudhiṣṭhira uvāca
kasminnipatitaṃ śūlaṃ devasya tu mahītale
tatra puṇyaṃ samākhyāhi yathāvanmunisattama || 2 ||
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca
śūlabhedamiti khyātaṃ tīrthaṃ puṇyatamaṃ mahat
tatra snātvā'rcayeddevaṃ gosahasraphalaṃ labhet || 3 ||
trirātraṃ kārayedyastu tasmiṃstīrthe narādhipa
arcayitvā mahādevaṃ punarjanma na vidyate || 4 ||
bhīmeśvaraṃ tato gacchennāradeśvaramuttamam
ādityeśaṃ mahāpuṇyaṃ smṛtaṃ kilbiṣanāśanam || 5 ||
nandikeśaṃ pariṣvajya paryāptaṃ janmanaḥ phalam
varuṇeśaṃ tataḥ paśyetsvatantreśvarameva ca
sarvatīrthaphalaṃ tasya pañcāyatanadarśanāt || 6 ||
tato gacchettu rājendra yuddhaṃ yatra susādhitam
koṭitīrthaṃ tu vikhyātamasurā yatra mohitāḥ || 7 ||
yatraiva nihatā rājandānavā baladarpitāḥ
teṣāṃ śirāṃsyagṛhṇanta sarve devāḥ samāgatāḥ || 8 ||
taistu saṃsthāpito devaḥ śūlapāṇirvṛṣadhvajaḥ
koṭirvinihatā tatra tena koṭīśvaraḥ smṛtaḥ || 9 ||
darśanāttasya tīrthasya sadehaḥ svargamāruhet
yadā tvindre ṇa kṣudra tvādvajraṃ kīlena yantritam || 10 ||
tadāprabhṛtilokānāṃ svargamārgo nivāritaḥ
yaḥ stutaṃ śrīphalaṃ dadyātkṛtvā cānte pradakṣiṇām || 11 ||
parvataṃ sahadīpaṃ tu śirasā caiva dhārayet
sarvakāmasusaṃpanno rājā bhavati pāṇḍava || 12 ||
mṛto rudra tvamāpnoti tato'sau jāyate punaḥ
svargādetya bhavedrā jā rājyaṃ kṛtvā divaṃ vrajet || 13 ||
bahunetraṃ tataḥ paśyettrayodaśyāṃ tu mānavaḥ
snātamātro narastatra sarvayajñaphalaṃ labhet || 14 ||
tato gacchettu rājendra tīrthaṃ paramaśobhanam
narāṇāṃ pāpanāśāya hyagastyeśvaramuttamam || 15 ||
tatra snātvā naro rājanbrahmaloke mahīyate
kārtikasya tu māsasya kṛṣṇapakṣe caturdaśī || 16 ||
ghṛtena snāpayeddevaṃ samādhistho jitendri yaḥ
ekaviṃśakulopeto na cyavedaiśvarātpadāt || 17 ||
dhenumupānahau chatraṃ dadyācca ghṛtakambalam
bhojanaṃ caiva viprāṇāṃ sarvaṃ koṭiguṇaṃ bhavet || 18 ||
tato gacchecca rājendra balākeśvaramuttamam
tatra snātvā naro rājansiṃhāsanapatirbhavet || 19 ||
narmadādakṣiṇe kūle tīrthaṃ śakrasya viśrutam
upoṣya rajanīmekāṃ snānaṃ tatra samācaret || 20 ||
snānaṃ kṛtvā yathānyāyamarcayecca janārdanam
gosahasraphalaṃ tasya viṣṇulokaṃ sa gacchati || 21 ||
ṛṣitīrthaṃ tato gacchetsarvapāpaharaṃ nṛṇām
snātamātro narastatra śivalokaṃ ca gacchati || 22 ||
nāradasya tu tatraiva tīrthaṃ paramaśobhanam
snātamātro narastatra gosahasraphalaṃ labhet || 23 ||
devatīrthaṃ tato gacchedbrahmaṇā nirmitaṃ purā
tatra snātvā naro rājanbrahmaloke mahīyate || 24 ||
amarakaṇṭakaṃ gacchedamaraiḥ sthāpitaṃ purā
snātamātro narastatra rudra loke mahīyate || 25 ||
tato gacchecca rājendra rāvaṇeśvaramuttamam
nityaṃ cā''yatanaṃ dṛṣṭva mucyate brahmahatyayā || 26 ||
ṛṇatīrthaṃ tato gacchedṛṇebhyo mucyate dhruvam
vaṭeśvaraṃ tato dṛṣṭvā paryāptaṃ janmanaḥ phalam || 27 ||
bhīmeśvaraṃ tato gacchetsarvavyādhivināśanam
snātamātro naro rājansarvaduḥkhaiḥ pramucyate || 28 ||
tato gacchettu rājendra turāsaṅgamanuttamam
tatra snātvā mahādevamarcayansiddhimāpnuyāt || 29 ||
somatīrthaṃ tato gacchetpaśyeccandra manuttamam
tatra snātvā naro rājanbhaktyā paramayā yutaḥ || 30 ||
tatkṣaṇāddivyadehasthaḥ śivavanmodate ciram
ṣaṣṭirvarṣasahasrāṇi rudra loke mahīyate || 31 ||
tato gacchettu rājendra piṅgaleśvaramuttamam
ahorātropavāsena trirātraphalamāpnuyāt || 32 ||
tasmiṃstīrthe tu rājendra kapilāṃ yaḥ prayacchati
yāvanti tasyā romāṇi tatprasūtikuleṣu ca || 33 ||
tāvadvarṣasahasrāṇi rudra loke mahīyate
yastu prāṇaparityāgaṃ kuryāttatra narādhipa || 34 ||
akṣayaṃ modate kālaṃ yāvaccandra divākarau
narmadātaṭamāśritya tiṣṭheyurye narottamāḥ || 35 ||
te bhṛtāḥ svargamāyānti santaḥ sukṛtino yathā
sureśvaraṃ tato gacchennāmnā karkoṭakeśvaram || 36 ||
gaṅgā'vatarate tatra dine puṇye na saṃśayaḥ
nanditīrthaṃ tato gacchetsnānaṃ tatra samācaret || 37 ||
tuṣyate tasya nandīśaḥ somaloke mahīyate
tato dīpeśvaraṃ gacchedvyāsatīrthaṃ tapovanam || 38 ||
nivartitā purā tatra vyāsabhītā mahānadī
huṃkāritā tu vyāsena dakṣiṇena tato gatā || 39 ||
pradakṣiṇāṃ tu yaḥ kuryāttasmiṃstīrthe narādhipa
akṣayaṃ modate kālaṃ yāvaccandra divākarau || 40 ||
vyāsastasya bhavetprītaḥ prāpnuyādīpsitaṃ phalam
sūtreṇa veṣṭayitvā tu dīpo deyaḥ savedikaḥ || 41 ||
krīḍate cākṣayaṃ kālaṃ yathā rudra stathaiva ca
tato gacchecca rājendra airaṇḍītīrthamuttamam || 42 ||
saṃgame tu naraḥ snātvā mucyate sarvapātakaiḥ
airaṇḍī triṣu lokeṣu vikhyātā pāpanāśinī || 43 ||
athavā''śvayuje māsi śuklapakṣe tu cāṣṭamī
śucirbhūtvā naraḥ snātvā sopavāsaparāyaṇaḥ || 44 ||
brāhmaṇaṃ bhojayedekaṃ koṭirbhavati bhojitā
airaṇḍīsaṃgame snātvā bhaktibhāvānurañjitaḥ
mṛttikāṃ śirasi sthāpya hyavagāhya ca vai jalam || 45 ||
narmadodakasaṃmiśraṃ mucyate sarvakilbiṣaiḥ
pradakṣiṇaṃ tu yaḥ kuryāttasmiṃstīrthe narādhipa || 46 ||
pradakṣiṇīkṛtā tena saptadvīpā vasuṃdharā
tataḥ suvarṇasalile snātvā dattvā tu kāñcanam || 47 ||
kāñcanena vimānena rudra loke mahīyate
tataḥ svargāccyutaḥ kālādrā jā bhavati vīryavān || 48 ||
tato gacchecca rājendra ikṣunadyāstu saṃgamam
trailokyaviśrutaṃ divyaṃ tatra saṃnihitaḥ śivaḥ || 49 ||
tatra snātvā naro rājangāṇapatyamāpnuyāt
skandatīrthaṃ tato gacchetsarvapāpapraṇāśanam || 50 ||
ājanma janitaṃ pāpaṃ snānamātrādvyapohati
liṅgasāraṃ tato gacchetsnānaṃ tatra samācaret || 51 ||
gosahasraphalaṃ tasya rudra loke mahīyate
bhaṅgatīrthaṃ tato gacchetsarvapāpapraṇāśanam || 52 ||
tatra gatvā tu rājendra snānaṃ tatra samācaret
saptajanmakṛtaiḥ pāpairmucyate nātra saṃśayaḥ || 53 ||
vaṭeśvaraṃ tato gacchetsarvatīrthamanuttamam
tatra snātvā naro rājangosahasraphalaṃ labhet || 54 ||
saṃgameśaṃ tato gacchetsarvadevanamaskṛtam
snātamātrānnarastatra cendra tvaṃ labhate dhruvam || 55 ||
koṭitīrthaṃ tato gacchetsarvapāpaharaṃ param
tatra snātvā naro rājyaṃ labhate nātra saṃśayaḥ || 56 ||
tatra tīrthaṃ samāsādya dattvā dānaṃ tu yo naraḥ
tasya tīrthaprabhāveṇa sarvaṃ koṭiguṇaṃ bhavet || 57 ||
atha nārī bhavetkācittatra snānaṃ samācaret
gaurītulyā bhavetsā'pi tvindra patnī na saṃśayaḥ || 58 ||
aṅgāreśaṃ tato gacchetsnānaṃ tatra samācaret
snātamātro narastatra rudra loke mahīyate || 59 ||
aṅgārakacaturthyāṃ tu snānaṃ tatra samācaret
akṣayaṃ modate kālaṃ śuciḥ prayatamānasaḥ || 60 ||
ayonisaṃbhave snātvā na paśyedyonisaṃkaṭam
pāṇḍaveśaṃ tu tatraiva snānaṃ tatra samācaret || 61 ||
akṣayaṃ modate kālamavadhyastridaśairapi
viṣṇulokaṃ tato gatvā krīḍate bhogasaṃyutaḥ || 62 ||
tatra bhuktvā mahābhogānmartyarājo'bhijāyate
kaṭheśvaraṃ tato gacchettatra snānaṃ samācaret || 63 ||
uttarāyaṇasaṃprāptau yadicchettasya tadbhavet
candra bhāgāṃ tato gacchettatra snānaṃ samācaret || 64 ||
snātamātro naro rājansomaloke mahīyate
tato gacchettu rājendra tīrthaṃ śakrasya viśrutam || 65 ||
pūjitaṃ devarājena devairapi namaskṛtam
tatra snātvā naro rājandānaṃ dattvā tu kāñcanam || 66 ||
athavā nīlavarṇābhaṃ vṛṣabhaṃ yaḥ samutsṛjet
vṛṣabhasya tu romāṇi tatprasūtikuleṣu ca || 67 ||
tāvadvarṣasahasrāṇi naro harapure vaset
tataḥ svargātparibhraṣṭo rājā bhavati vīryavān || 68 ||
aśvānāṃ śvetavarṇānāṃ sahasrāṇāṃ narādhipa
svāmī bhavati martyeṣu tasya tīrthaprabhāvataḥ || 69 ||
tato gacchettu rājendra brahmāvartamanuttamam
tatra snātvā naro rājaṃstarpayetpitṛdevatāḥ || 70 ||
upoṣya rajanīmekāṃ piṇḍaṃ dattvā yathāvidhi
kanyāgate tathā''ditye akṣayaṃ syānnarādhipa || 71 ||
tato gacchecca rājendra kapilātīrthamuttamam
tatra snātvā naro rājankapilāṃ yaḥ prayacchati || 72 ||
saṃpūrṇāṃ pṛthivīṃ dattvā yatphalaṃ tadavāpnuyāt
narmadeśaṃ paraṃ tīrthaṃ na bhūtaṃ na bhaviṣyati || 73 ||
tatra snātvā naro rājannaśvamedhaphalaṃ labhet
narmadādakṣiṇe kūle saṃgameśvaramuttamam || 74 ||
tatra snātvā naro rājansarvayajñaphalaṃ labhet
tatra sarvodyato rājā pṛthivyāmeva jāyate || 75 ||
sarvalakṣaṇasaṃpūrṇaḥ sarvavyādhivivarjitaḥ
nārmade cottare kūle tīrthaṃ paramaśobhanam || 76 ||
ādityāyatanaṃ divyamīśvareṇa tu bhāṣitam
tatra snātvā tu rājendra dānaṃ dattvā tu śaktitaḥ
tasya tīrthaprabhāveṇa dattaṃ bhavati cākṣayam || 77 ||
daridrā vyādhitā ye tu ye tu duṣkṛtakarmiṇaḥ
mucyante sarvapāpebhyaḥ sūryalokaṃ tu yānti te || 78 ||
māghamāse tu saṃprāpte śuklapakṣasya saptamī
vasedāyatane tatra nirāhāro jitendri yaḥ || 79 ||
na jarāvyādhito mūko na cāndho badhiro'thavā
subhago rūpasaṃpannaḥ strīṇāṃ bhavati vallabhaḥ || 80 ||
evaṃ tīrthaṃ mahāpuṇyaṃ mārkaṇḍeyena bhāṣitam
ye na jānanti rājendra vañcitāste na saṃśayaḥ || 81 ||
gargeśvaraṃ tato gacchetsnānaṃ tatra samācaret
snātamātro narastatra svargalokamavāpnuyāt || 82 ||
modate svargalokastho yāvadindrā ścaturdaśa
samīpataḥ sthitaṃ tasya nāgeśvaratapovanam || 83 ||
tatra snātvā tu rājendra nāgalokamavāpnuyāt
bahvībhirnāgakanyābhiḥ krīḍate kālamakṣayam || 84 ||
kuberabhavanaṃ gacchetkubero yatra saṃsthitaḥ
kāleśvaraṃ paraṃ tīrthaṃ kubero yatra toṣitaḥ || 85 ||
tatra snātvā tu rājendra sarvasaṃpadamāpnuyāt
tataḥ paścimato gacchenmārutālayamuttamam || 86 ||
tatra snātvā tu rājendra śucirbhūtvā samāhitaḥ
kāñcanaṃ tu tato dadyādyathāśakti subuddhimān || 87 ||
puṣpakeṇa vimānena vāyulokaṃ sa gacchati
yavatīrthaṃ tato gacchenmāghamāse yudhiṣṭhira || 88 ||
kṛṣṇapakṣe caturdaśyāṃ snānaṃ tatra samācaret
naktabhojyaṃ tataḥ kuryānna paśyedyonisaṃkaṭam || 89 ||
ahalyātīrthaṃ tato gacchetsnānaṃ tatra samācaret
snātamātro narastatra hyapsarobhiḥ pramodate || 90 ||
ahalyā ca tapastaptvā tatra muktimupāgatā
caitramāse tu saṃprāpte śuklapakṣe caturdaśī || 91 ||
kāmadevadine tasminnahalyāṃ yastu pūjayet
yatra yatra narotpanno narastatra priyo bhavet || 92 ||
strīvallabho bhavecchrīmānkāmadeva ivāparaḥ
ayodhyāṃ tu samāsādya tīrthaṃ rāmasya viśrutam || 93 ||
snātamātro narastatra sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate
somatīrthaṃ tato gacchetsnānaṃ tatra samācaret || 94 ||
snātamātro narastatra sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate
somagrahe tu rājendra pāpakṣayakaraṃ nṛṇām || 95 ||
trailokyaviśrutaṃ rājansomatīrthaṃ mahāphalam
yastu cāndrā yaṇaṃ kuryāttasmiṃstīrthe narādhipa || 96 ||
sarvapāpaviśuddhātmā somalokaṃ sa gacchati
agnipraveśe'tha jale athavā'pi hyanāśake || 97 ||
somatīrthe mṛto yastu nāsau martye'bhijāyate
śubhatīrthaṃ tato gacchetsnānaṃ tatra samācaret || 98 ||
snātamātro narastatra goloke tu mahīyate
tato gacchecca rājendra viṣṇutīrthamanuttamam || 99 ||
yodhanīpuramākhyātaṃ viṣṇusthānamanuttamam
asurā yodhitāstatra vāsudevena koṭiśaḥ || 100 ||
tatra tīrthaṃ samutpannaṃ viṣṇuḥ prīto bhavediha
ahorātropavāsena brahmahatyāṃ vyapohati || 101 ||
tato gacchettu rājendra tāpaseśvaramuttamam
hariṇāṃ vyādhasaṃtrastā patitā yatra sā mṛgī || 102 ||
jale prakṣiptagātrā tu antarikṣaṃ gatā ca sā
vyādho vismitacittastu paraṃ vismayamāgataḥ || 103 ||
tena tāpeśvaraṃ tīrthaṃ na bhūtaṃ na bhaviṣyati
tato gacchettu rājendra brahmatīrthamanuttamam || 104 ||
amohakamiti khyātaṃ pitṝṃścaivātra tarpayet
paurṇamāsyāmamāyāṃ tu śrāddhaṃ kuryādyathāvidhi || 105 ||
tatra snātvā naro rājanpitṛpiṇḍaṃ tu dāpayet
gajarūpā śilā tatra toyamadhye pratiṣṭhitā || 106 ||
tasyāṃ tu dāpayetpiṇḍaṃ vaiśākhyāṃ tu viśeṣataḥ
tṛpyanti pitarastatra yāvattiṣṭhati medinī || 107 ||
tato gacchecca rājendra siddheśvaramanuttamam
tatra snātvā naro rājangaṇapatyantikaṃ vrajet || 108 ||
tato gacchettu rājendra liṅgaṃ yatra janārdanaḥ
tatra snātvā tu rājendra viṣṇuloke mahīyate || 109 ||
narmadādakṣiṇe kūle tīrthaṃ paramaśobhanam
kāmadevaḥ svayaṃ tatra tapo'tapyata vai mahat || 110 ||
divyaṃ varṣasahasraṃ tu śaṃkaraṃ paryupāsata
samādhibhaṅgadagdhastu śaṃkareṇa mahātmanā || 111 ||
śvetaparvā yamaścaiva hutāśaḥ śukraparvaṇi
ete dagdhāstu te sarve kusumeśvarasaṃsthitāḥ || 112 ||
divyavarṣasahasreṇa tuṣṭasteṣāṃ maheśvaraḥ
umayā sahito rudra stuṣṭasteṣāṃ varapradaḥ || 113 ||
mokṣayitvā tu tānsarvānnarmadātaṭamāsthitaḥ
tatastīrthaprabhāveṇa punardevatvamāgatāḥ || 114 ||
ūcuśca parayā bhaktyā devadevaṃ vṛṣadhvajam
tvatprasādānmahādeva tīrthaṃ bhavatu cottamam
ardhayojanavistīrṇaṃ kṣetraṃ dikṣu samantataḥ || 115 ||
tasmiṃstīrthe naraḥ snātvā vopavāsaparāyaṇaḥ
kusumāyudharūpeṇa rudra loke mahīyate || 116 ||
vaiśvānaro yamaścaiva kāmadevastasthā marut
tapastaptvā tu rājendra parāṃ siddhimavāpnuyuḥ || 117 ||
aṅkolasya samīpe tu nātidūre tu tasya vai
snānaṃ dānaṃ ca tatraiva bhojanaṃ piṇḍapātanam || 118 ||
agnipraveśe'tha jale athavā tu hyanāśake
anivartikā gatistasya mṛtasyāmutra jāyate || 119 ||
tryambakeṇa tu toyena yaścaruṃ śrapayennaraḥ
aṅkolamūle dattvā tu piṇḍaṃ caiva yathāvidhi || 120 ||
tṛpyanti pitarastasya yāvaccandra divākarau
uttare tvayane prāpte ghṛtasnānaṃ karoti yaḥ || 121 ||
puruṣo vā'tha strī vā'pi vasedāyatane śuciḥ
siddheśvarasya devasya prātaḥ pūjāṃ prakalpayet || 122 ||
sa yāṃ gatimavāpnoti na tāṃ sarvairmahāmakhaiḥ
yadā'vatīrṇaḥ kālena rūpavānsubhago bhavet || 123 ||
martye bhavati rājā ca tvāsamudrā ntagocare
kṣetrapālaṃ na paśyettu daṇḍapāṇiṃ mahābalam || 124 ||
vṛthā tasya bhavedyātrā hyadṛṣṭvā karṇakuṇḍalam
evaṃ tīrthaphalaṃ jñātvā sarve devāḥ samāgatāḥ
muñcanti kusumairvṛṣṭiṃ tena tatkusumeśvaram || 125 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe narmadāmāhātmya ekanavatyadhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 191 ||

<192>

atha dvinavatyadhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca
bhārgaveśaṃ tato gacchedbhagno yatra janārdanaḥ
asuraistu mahāyuddhe mahābalaparākramaiḥ || 1 ||
huṃkāritāstu devena dānavāḥ pralayaṃ gatāḥ
tatra snātvā tu rājendra sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate || 2 ||
śuklatīrthasya cotpattiṃ śṛṇu tvaṃ pāṇḍunandana
himavacchikhare ramye nānādhātuvicitrite || 3 ||
taruṇādityasaṃkāśe taptakāñcanasaprabhe
vajrasphaṭikasopāne citrapaṭṭaśilātale || 4 ||
jāmbūnadamaye divye nānāpuṣpopaśobhite
tatrā'sīnaṃ mahādevaṃ sarvajñaṃ prabhumavyayam || 5 ||
lokānugrahakartāraṃ gaṇavṛndaiḥ samāvṛtam
skandanandimahākālairvīrabhadra gaṇādibhiḥ
umayā sahitaṃ devaṃ mārkaṇḍiḥ paryapṛcchata || 6 ||
devadeva mahādeva brahmaviṣṇvindra saṃstuta
saṃsārabhayabhīto'haṃ sukhopāyaṃ bravīhi me || 7 ||
bhagavanbhūtabhavyeśa sarvapāpapraṇāśanam
tīrthānāṃ paramaṃ tīrthaṃ tadvadasva maheśvara || 8 ||
īśvara uvāca
śṛnu vipra mahāprājña sarvaśāstraviśārada
snānāya gaccha subhaga ṛṣisaṃghaiḥ samāvṛtaḥ || 9 ||
manvatrikaśyapāścaiva yājñavalyośanoṅgirāḥ
yamāpastambasaṃvartāḥ kātyāyanabṛhaspatī || 10 ||
nārado gautamaścaiva sevante dharmakāṅkṣiṇaḥ
gaṅgā kanakhale puṇyā prayāgaṃ puṣkaraṃ gayām || 11 ||
kurukṣetraṃ mahāpuṇyaṃ rāhugraste divākare
divā vā yadi vā rātrau śuklatīrthaṃ mahāphalam || 12 ||
darśanātsparśanāccaiva snānāddānāttapojapāt
homāccaivopavāsācca śuklatīrthaṃ mahāphalam || 13 ||
śuklatīrthaṃ mahāpuṇyaṃ narmadāyāṃ vyavasthitam
cāṇakyo nāma rājarṣiḥ siddhiṃ tatra samāgataḥ || 14 ||
etatkṣetraṃ suvipulaṃ yojanaṃ vṛttasaṃsthitam
śuklatīrthaṃ mahāpuṇyaṃ sarvapāpapraṇāśanam || 15 ||
pādapāgreṇa dṛṣṭena brahmahatyāṃ vyapohati
jagatīdarśanāccaiva bhrūṇahatyāṃ vyapohati || 16 ||
ahaṃ tatra ṛṣiśreṣṭha tiṣṭhāmi hyumayā saha
vaiśākhe caitramāse tu kṛṣṇapakṣe caturdaśī || 17 ||
kailāsāccāpi niṣkramya tatra saṃnihito hyaham
daityadānavagandharvāḥ siddhavidyādharāstathā || 18 ||
gaṇāścāpsaraso nāgāḥ sarve devāḥ samāgatāḥ
gaganasthāstu tiṣṭhanti vimānaiḥ sārvakāmikaiḥ || 19 ||
śuklatīrthaṃ tu rājendra hyāgatā dharmakāṅkṣiṇaḥ
rajakena yathā vastraṃ śuklaṃ bhavati vāriṇā || 20 ||
ājanmajanitaṃ pāpaṃ śuklaṃ tīrthaṃ vyapohati
snānaṃ dānaṃ mahāpuṇyaṃ mārkaṇḍa ṛṣisattama || 21 ||
śuklatīrthātparaṃ tīrthaṃ na bhūtaṃ na bhaviṣyati
pūrve vayasi karmāṇi kṛtvā pāpāni mānavaḥ || 22 ||
ahorātropavāsena śuklatīrthe vyapohati
tapasā brahmacaryeṇa yajñairdānena vā punaḥ || 23 ||
devārcanena yā puṣṭirna sā kratuśatairapi
kārtikasya tu māsasya kṛṣṇapakṣe caturdaśī || 24 ||
ghṛtena snāpayeddevamupoṣya parameśvaram
ekaviṃśakulopeto na cyavedaiśvarātpadāt || 25 ||
śuklatīrthaṃ mahāpuṇyamṛṣisiddhaniṣevitam
tatra snātvā naro rājanna punarjanmabhāgbhavet || 26 ||
snātvā vai śuklatīrthe tu hyarcayedvṛṣabhadhvajam
kapālapūraṇaṃ kṛtvā tuṣyatyatra maheśvaraḥ || 27 ||
ardhanārīśvaraṃ devaṃ paṭe bhaktyā likhāpayet
śaṅkhatūryaninādaiśca brahmaghoṣaiśca sadvijaiḥ || 28 ||
jāgaraṃ kārayettatra nṛtyagītādimaṅgalaiḥ
prabhāte śuklatīrthe tu snānaṃ vai devatārcanam || 29 ||
ācāryānbhojayetpaścācchivavrataparāñśucīn
dakṣiṇāṃ ca yathāśakti vittaśāṭhyaṃ vivarjayet || 30 ||
pradakṣiṇaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā śanairdevāntikaṃ vrajet
evaṃ vai kurute yastu tasya puṇyaphalaṃ śṛṇu || 31 ||
divyayānaṃ samārūḍho gīyamāno'psarogaṇaiḥ
śivatulyabalopetastiṣṭhatyābhūtasaṃplavam || 32 ||
śuklatīrthe tu yā nārī dadāti kanakaṃ śubham
ghṛtena snāpayeddevaṃ kumāraṃ cāpi pūjayet || 33 ||
evaṃ yā kurute bhaktyā tasyāḥ puṇyaphalaṃ śṛṇu
modate śarvalokasthā yāvadindrā ścaturdaśa || 34 ||
paurṇamāsyāṃ caturdaśyāṃ saṃkrāntau viṣuve tathā
snātvā tu sopavāsaḥ sanvijitātmā samāhitaḥ || 35 ||
dānaṃ dadyādyathāśaktyā prīyatāṃ hariśaṃkarau
evaṃ tīrthaprabhāveṇa sarvaṃ bhavati cākṣayam || 36 ||
anāthaṃ durgataṃ vipraṃ nāthavantamathāpi vā
udvāhayati yastīrthe tasya puṇyaphalaṃ śṛṇu || 37 ||
yāvattadro masaṃkhyā ca tatprasūtikuleṣu ca
tāvadvarṣasahasrāṇi śivaloke mahīyate || 38 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe narmadāmāhātmye dvinavatyadhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 192 ||

<193>

atha trinavatyadhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca
tatastvanarakaṃ gacchetsnānaṃ tatra samācaret
snātamātro narastatra narakaṃ ca na paśyati || 1 ||
tasya tīrthasya māhātmyaṃ śṛṇu tvaṃ pāṇḍunandana
tasmiṃstīrthe tu rājendra yasyāsthīni vinikṣipet || 2 ||
vilayaṃ yānti pāpāni rūpavājāyate naraḥ
gotīrthaṃ tu tato gatvā sarvapāpātpramucyate || 3 ||
tato gacchettu rājendra kapilātīrthamuttamam
tatra snātvā naro rājangosahasraphalaṃ labhet || 4 ||
jyeṣṭhamāse tu saṃprāpte caturdaśyāṃ viśeṣataḥ
tatropoṣya naro bhaktyā kapilāṃ yaḥ prayacchati || 5 ||
ghṛtena dīpaṃ prajvālya ghṛtena snāpayecchivam
saghṛtaṃ śrīphalaṃ jagdhvā dattvā cānte pradakṣiṇam || 6 ||
ghaṇṭābharaṇasaṃyuktāṃ kapilāṃ yaḥ prayacchati
śivatulyabalo bhūtvā naivāsau jāyate punaḥ || 7 ||
aṅgārakadine prāpte caturthyāṃ tu viśeṣataḥ
pūjayettu śivaṃ bhaktyā brāhmaṇebhyaśca bhojanam || 8 ||
aṅgārakanavamyāṃ tu amāyāṃ ca viśeṣataḥ
snāpayettatra yatnena rūpavānsubhago bhavet || 9 ||
ghṛtena snāpayelliṅgaṃ pūjayedbhaktito dvijān
puṣpakeṇa vimānena sahasraiḥ parivāritaḥ || 10 ||
śaivaṃ padamavāpnoti yatra cābhimataṃ bhavet
akṣayaṃ modate kālaṃ yathā rudra stathaiva saḥ || 11 ||
yadā tu karmasaṃyogānmartyalokamupāgataḥ
rājā bhavati dharmiṣṭho rūpavāñjāyate kule || 12 ||
tato gacchecca rājendra ṛṣitīrthamanuttamam
tṛṇabindurnāma ṛṣiḥ śāpadagdho vyavasthitaḥ || 13 ||
tattīrthasya prabhāveṇa śāpamukto'bhavaddvijaḥ
tato gacchettu rājendra gaṅgeśvaramanuttamam || 14 ||
śrāvaṇe māsi saṃprāpte kṛṣṇapakṣe caturdaśī
snātamātro narastatra rudra loke mahīyate || 15 ||
pitṝṇāṃ tarpaṇaṃ kṛtvā mucyate ca ṛṇatrayāt
gaṅgeśvarasamīpe tu gaṅgāvadanamuttamam || 16 ||
akāmo vā sakāmo vā tatra snātvā tu mānavaḥ
ājanmajanitaiḥ pāpairmucyate nātra saṃśayaḥ || 17 ||
tatra tīrthe naraḥ snātvā vrajedvai yatra śaṃkaraḥ
sarvadā parvadivase snānaṃ tatra samācaret || 18 ||
pitṝṇāṃ tarpaṇaṃ kṛtvā hyaśvamedhaphalaṃ labhet
prayāge yatphalam dṛṣṭaṃ śaṃkareṇa mahātmanā || 19 ||
tadeva nikhilaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ gaṅgāvadanasaṃgame
tasyaiva paścime sthāne samīpe nātidūrataḥ || 20 ||
daśāśvamedhajananaṃ triṣu lokeṣu viśrutam
upoṣya rajanīmekāṃ māsi bhādra pade tathā || 21 ||
amāyāṃ ca naraḥ snātvā vrajate yatra śaṃkaraḥ
sarvadā parvadivase snānaṃ tatra samācaret || 22 ||
pitṝṇāṃ tarpaṇaṃ kṛtvā cāśvamedhaphalaṃ labhet
daśāśvamedhātpaścimato bhṛgurbrāhmaṇasattamaḥ || 23 ||
divyaṃ varṣasahasraṃ tu īśvaraṃ paryupāsate
valmīkaveṣṭitaścāsau pakṣiṇāṃ ca niketanaḥ || 24 ||
āścaryaṃ sumahajjātamumāyāḥ śaṃkarasya ca
gaurī papraccha deveśaṃ ko'yamevaṃ tu saṃsthitaḥ
devo vā dānavo vā'tha kathayasva maheśvara || 25 ||
maheśvara uvāca
bhṛgurnāma dvijaśreṣṭha ṛṣīṇāṃ pravaro muniḥ
māṃ dhyāyate samādhistho varaṃ prārthayate priye || 26 ||
tataḥ prahasitā devī īśvaraṃ pratyabhāṣata
dhūmavattacchikhā jātā tato'dyāpi na tuṣyase
durārādhyo'si tena tvaṃ nātra kāryā vicāraṇā || 27 ||
maheśvara uvāca
na jānāsi mahādevi hyayaṃ krodhena veṣṭitaḥ
darśayāmi yathātathyaṃ pratyayaṃ te karomyaham || 28 ||
tataḥ smṛto'tha devena dharmarūpo vṛṣastadā
smaraṇāttasya devasya vṛṣaḥ śīghramupasthitaḥ
vadaṃstu mānuṣīṃ vācamādeśo dīyatāṃ prabho || 29 ||
bhagavānuvāca
valmīkaṃ tvaṃ khanasvainaṃ vipraṃ bhūmau nipātaya
yogasthastu tato dhyāyanbhṛgustena nipātitaḥ || 30 ||
tatkṣaṇātkrodhasaṃtapto hastamutkṣipya so'śapat
evaṃ saṃbhāṣamāṇastu kutra gacchasi bho vṛṣa
adyāhaṃ saṃprakopeṇa pralayaṃ tvāṃ naye vṛṣa || 31 ||
dharṣitastu tadā vipraścāntarikṣaṃ gato vṛṣam
ākāśe prekṣate vipra etadadbhutamuttamam || 32 ||
tatra prahasito rudra ṛṣiragre vyavasthitaḥ
tṛtīyalocanaṃ dṛṣṭvā vailakṣyātpatito bhuvi
praṇamya daṇḍavadbhūmau tuṣṭāva parameśvaram || 33 ||
praṇipatya bhūtanāthaṃ bhavodbhavaṃ tvāmahaṃ divyarūpam
bhavātīto bhuvanapate prabho tu vijñāpaye kiṃcit || 34 ||
tvadguṇanikarānvaktuṃ kaḥ śakto bhavati mānuṣo nāma
vāsukirapi hi kadācidvadanasahasraṃ bhavedyasya || 35 ||
bhaktyā tathā'pi śaṃkara bhuvanapate tvatstutau mukharaḥ
vadataḥ kṣamasva bhagavanprasīda me tava caraṇapatitasya || 36 ||
sattvaṃ rajastamastvaṃ sthityutpattyorvināśane deva
tvāṃ muktvā bhuvanapate bhuvaneśvara naiva daivataṃ kiṃcit || 37 ||
yamaniyamayajñadānavedābhyāsāśca dhāraṇā yogaḥ
tvadbhakteḥ sarvamidaṃ nārhati hi kalāsahasrāṃśam || 38 ||
ucchiṣṭarasarasāyanakhaḍgāñjanapādukāvivarasiddhirvā
cihnaṃ bhavavratānāṃ dṛśyati ceha janmani prakaṭam || 39 ||
śāṭhyena namati yadyapi dadāsi tvaṃ bhūtimicchato deva
bhaktirbhavabhedakarī mokṣāya vinirmitā nātha || 40 ||
paradāraparasvarataṃ paraparibhavaduḥkhaśokasaṃtaptam
paravadanavīkṣaṇaparaṃ parameśvaraṃ māṃ paritrāhi || 41 ||
mithyābhimānadagdhaṃ kṣaṇabhaṅguradehavilasitaṃ krūram
kupathābhimukhaṃ patitaṃ tvaṃ mā pāpātparitrāhi || 42 ||
dīne dvijagaṇasārthe bandhujanenaiva dūṣitā hyāśā
tṛṣṇā tathā'pi śaṃkara kiṃ mūḍhaṃ māṃ viḍambayati || 43 ||
tṛṣṇāṃ harasva śīghraṃ lakṣmīṃ pradatsva yāvadāsinīṃ nityam
chindhi madamohapāśānuttāraya māṃ mahādeva || 44 ||
karuṇābhyudayaṃ nāma stotramidaṃ sarvasiddhidaṃ divyam
yaḥ paṭhati bhaktiyuktastasya tuṣyedbhṛgoryathā ca śivaḥ || 45 ||
īśvara uvāca
ahaṃ tuṣṭo'smi te vatsa prārthayasvepsitaṃ varam
umayā sahito devo varaṃ tasya hyadāpayat || 46 ||
bhṛguruvāca
yadi tuṣṭo'si deveśa yadi deyo varo mama
rudra vedī bhavedevametatsaṃpādayasva me || 47 ||
īśvara uvāca
evaṃ bhavatu viprendra krodhastvā na bhaviṣyati
na pitāputrayoścaiva tvaikamatyaṃ bhaviṣyati || 48 ||
tadāprabhṛti brahmādyā sarve devāḥ sakiṃnarāḥ
upāsate bhṛgostīrthaṃ tuṣṭo yatra maheśvaraḥ || 49 ||
darśanāttasya tīrthasya sadyaḥ pāpātpramucyate
avaśāḥ svavaśā vā'pi mriyante yatra jantavaḥ || 50 ||
guhyātiguhyā sugatisteṣāṃ niḥsaṃśayaṃ bhavet
etatkṣetraṃ suvipulaṃ sarvapāpapraṇāśanam || 51 ||
tatra snātvā divaṃ yānti ye mṛtāste'punarbhavāḥ
upānahau ca cchatraṃ ca deyamannaṃ ca kāñcanam || 52 ||
bhojanaṃ ca yathāśaktyā hyakṣayaṃ ca tathā bhavet
sūryoparāge yo dadyāddānaṃ caiva yathecchayā || 53 ||
dīyamānaṃ tu taddānamakṣayaṃ tasya tadbhavet
candra sūryoparāgeṣu yatphalaṃ tvamarakaṇṭake || 54 ||
tadeva nikhilaṃ puṇyaṃ bhṛgutīrthe na saṃśayaḥ
kṣaranti sarvadānāni yajñadānatapaḥkriyāḥ || 55 ||
na kṣarettu tapastaptaṃ bhṛgutīrthe yudhiṣṭhira
yasya vai tapasogreṇa tuṣṭenaiva tu śaṃbhunā || 56 ||
sāṃnidhyaṃ tatra kathitaṃ bhṛgutīrthe narādhipa
prakhyātaṃ triṣu lokeṣu yatra tuṣṭo maheśvaraḥ || 57 ||
evaṃ tu vadato devīṃ bhṛgutīrthamanuttamam
na jānanti narā mūḍhā viṣṇumāyāvimohitāḥ || 58 ||
narmadāyāṃ sthitaṃ divyaṃ bhṛgutīrthaṃ narādhipa
bhṛgutīrthasya māhātmyaṃ yaḥ śṛṇoti naraḥ kvacit || 59 ||
vimuktaḥ sarvapāpebhyo rudra lokaṃ sa gacchati
tato gacchettu rājendra gautameśvaramuttamam || 60 ||
tatra snātvā naro rājannupavāsaparāyaṇaḥ
kāñcanena vimānena brahmaloke mahīyate || 61 ||
dhautapāpaṃ tato gacchetkṣetraṃ yatra vṛṣeṇa tu
narmadāyāṃ kṛtaṃ rājansarvapātakanāśanam || 62 ||
tatra tīrthe naraḥ snātvā brahmahatyāṃ vimuñcati
tasmiṃstīrthe tu rājendra prāṇatyāgaṃ karoti yaḥ || 63 ||
caturbhujastrinetraśca śivatulyabalo bhavet
vasetkalpāyutaṃ sāgraṃ śivatulyaparākramaḥ || 64 ||
kālena mahatā prāptaḥ pṛthivyāmekarāḍbhavet
tato gacchecca rājendra airaṇḍītīthamuttamam || 65 ||
prayāge yatphalaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ mārkaṇḍeyena bhāṣitam
tatphalaṃ labhate rājansnātamātro hi mānavaḥ || 66 ||
māsi bhādra pade caiva śuklapakṣe caturdaśī
upoṣya rajanīmekāṃ tasminsnānaṃ samācaret
yamadūtairna bādhyeta rudra lokaṃ sa gacchati || 67 ||
tato gacchettu rājendra siddho yatra janārdanaḥ
hiraṇyadvīpavikhyātaṃ sarvapāpapraṇāśanam || 68 ||
tatra snātvā naro rājandhanavānrūpavānbhavet
tato gacchettu rājendra tīrthaṃ kanakhalaṃ mahat || 69 ||
garuḍena tapastaptaṃ tasmiṃstīrthe narādhipa
prakhyātaṃ triṣu lokeṣu yoginī tatra tiṣṭhati || 70 ||
krīḍate yogibhiḥ sārdhaṃ śivena saha nṛtyati
tatra snātvā naro rājanrudra loke mahīyate || 71 ||
tato gacchettu rājendra haṃsatīrthamanuttamam
haṃsāstatra vinirmuktā gatā ūrdhvaṃ na saṃśayaḥ || 72 ||
tato gacchettu rājendra siddho yatra janārdanaḥ
vārāhaṃ rūpamāsthāya arcitaḥ parameśvaraḥ || 73 ||
varāhatīrthe naraḥ snātvā dvādaśyāṃ tu viśeṣataḥ
viṣṇulokamavāpnoti narakaṃ na ca paśyati || 74 ||
tato gacchettu rājendra candra tīrthamanuttamam
paurṇamāsyāṃ viśeṣeṇa snānaṃ tatra samācaret || 75 ||
snātamātro narastatra candra loke mahīyate
dakṣiṇena tu dvāreṇa kanyātīrthaṃ tu viśrutam || 76 ||
śuklapakṣe tṛtīyāyāṃ snānaṃ tatra samācaret
praṇipatya tu ceśānaṃ balistena prasīdati || 77 ||
hariścandra puraṃ divyamantarikṣe ca dṛśyate
śakradhvaje samāvṛtte gupte nāgariketane || 78 ||
narmadāsalilaughena tarūnsaṃplāvayiṣyati
asminsthāne nivāsaḥ syādviṣṇuḥ śaṃkaramabravīt || 79 ||
dvīpeśvare naraḥ snātvā labhedbahu suvarṇakam
tato gacchettu rājendra kanyātīrthe susaṃgame || 80 ||
snātamātro narastatra devyāḥ sthānamavāpnuyāt
devatīrthaṃ tato gacchetsarvatīrthamanuttamam || 81 ||
tatra snātvā tu rājendra daivataiḥ saha modate
tato gacchecca rājendra śikhitīrthamanuttamam || 82 ||
yattatra dīyate dānaṃ sarvaṃ koṭiguṇaṃ bhavet
aparapakṣe tvamāyāṃ tu snānaṃ tatra samācaret || 83 ||
brāhmaṇaṃ bhojayedekaṃ koṭirbhavati bhojitā
bhṛgutīrthe tu rājendra tīrthakoṭirvyavasthitā || 84 ||
akāmo vā sakāmo vā tatra snānaṃ samācaret
aśvamedhamavāpnoti daivataiḥ saha modate || 85 ||
tatra siddhiṃ parāṃ prāpto bhṛgustu munipuṃgavaḥ
avatāraḥ kṛtastatra śaṃkareṇa mahātmanā || 86 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe narmadāmāhātmye trinavatyadhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 193 ||

<194>

atha caturnavatyadhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
mārkaṇḍeya uvāca
tato gacchettu rājendra hyaṅkuśeśvaramuttamam
darśanāttasya devasya mucyate sarvapātakaiḥ || 1 ||
tato gacchecca rājendra narmadeśvaramuttamam
tatra snātvā naro rājansvargaloke mahīyate || 2 ||
aśvatīrthaṃ tato gacchetsnānaṃ tatra samācaret
subhago darśanīyaśca bhogavāñjāyate naraḥ || 3 ||
paitāmahaṃ tato gacchedbrahmaṇā nirmitaṃ purā
tatra snātvā naro bhaktyā pitṛpiṇḍaṃ tu dāpayet || 4 ||
tiladarbhavimiśraṃ tu hyudakaṃ tatra dāpayet
tasya tīrthaprabhāveṇa sarvaṃ bhavati cākṣayam || 5 ||
sāvitrītīrthamāsādya yastu snānaṃ samācaret
vidhūya sarvapāpāni brahmaloke mahīyate || 6 ||
manorahaṃ tato gacchettīrthaṃ paramaśobhanam
tatra snātvā naro rājanpitṛloke mahīyate || 7 ||
tato gacchecca rājendra mānasaṃ tīrthamuttamam
tatra snātvā naro rājanrudra loke mahīyate || 8 ||
tato gacchecca rājendra kuñjatīrthamanuttamam
vikhyātaṃ triṣu lokeṣu sarvapāpapraṇāśanam || 9 ||
yānyānkāmayate kāmānpaśuputradhanāni ca
prāpnuyāttāni sarvāṇi tatra snātvā narādhipa || 10 ||
tato gacchettu rājendra tridaśajyotiviśrutam
yatra tā ṛṣikanyāstu tapo'tapyanta suvratāḥ || 11 ||
bhartā bhavatu sarvāsāmīśvaraḥ prabhuravyayaḥ
prītastāsāṃ mahādevo daṇḍarūpadharo haraḥ || 12 ||
vikṛtānanabībhatsurvratī tīrthamupāgataḥ
tatra kanyā mahārāja varayanparameśvaraḥ || 13 ||
kanyāmṛṣervarayataḥ kanyādānaṃ pradīyatām
tīthaṃ tatra mahārāja ṛṣikanyeti viśrutam || 14 ||
tatra snātvā naro rājansarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate
tato gacchecca rājendra svarṇabindu tviti smṛtam || 15 ||
tatra snātvā naro rājandurgatiṃ na ca paśyati
apsareśaṃ tato gacchetsnānaṃ tatra samācaret
krīḍate nāgalokastho'psarobhiḥ saha modate
tato gacchettu rājendra narakaṃ tīrthamuttamam || 17 ||
tatra snātvārcayeddevaṃ narakaṃ ca na paśyati
bhārabhūtiṃ tato gacchedupavāsaparo janaḥ || 18 ||
etattīrthaṃ samāsādya cāvatāraṃ tu śāṃbhavam
arcayitvā virūpākṣaṃ rudra loke mahīyate || 19 ||
asmiṃstīrthe naraḥ snātvā bhārabhūtau mahātmanaḥ
yatra yatra mṛtasyāpi dhruvaṃ gāṇeśvarī gatiḥ || 20 ||
kārtikasya tu māsasya hyarcayitvā maheśvaram
aśvamedhāddaśaguṇaṃ pravadanti manīṣinaḥ || 21 ||
dīpakānāṃ śataṃ tatra ghṛtapūrṇaṃ tu dāpayet
vimānaiḥ sūryasaṃkāśairvrajate yatra śaṃkaraḥ || 22 ||
vṛṣabhaṃ yaḥ prayacchettu śaṅkhakundendusaprabham
vṛṣayuktena yānena rudra lokaṃ sa gacchati || 23 ||
dhenumekāṃ tu yo dadyāttasmiṃstīrthe narādhipa
pāyasaṃ madhusaṃyuktaṃ bhakṣyāṇi vividhāni ca || 24 ||
yathāśaktyā ca rājendra brāhmaṇānbhojayettataḥ
tasya tīrthaprabhāveṇa sarvaṃ koṭiguṇaṃ bhavet || 25 ||
narmadāyā jalaṃ pītvā hyarcayitvā vṛṣadhvajam
durgatiṃ ca na paśyanti tasya tīrthaprabhāvataḥ || 26 ||
etattīrthaṃ samāsādya yastu prāṇānvimuñcati
sarvapāpavinirmukto vrajedvai yatra śaṃkaraḥ
jalapraveśaṃ yaḥ kuryāttasmiṃstīrthe narādhipa || 27 ||
haṃsayuktena yānena brahmalokaṃ sa gacchati
yāvaccandra śca sūryaśca himavāṃśca mahodadhiḥ || 28 ||
gaṅgādyāḥ sarito yāvattāvatsvarge mahīyate
anāśakaṃ tu yaḥ kuryāttasmiṃstīrthe narādhipa || 29 ||
garbhavāse tu rājendra na punarjāyate pumān
tato gacchettu rājendra āṣāḍhītīrthamuttamam || 30 ||
tatra snātvā naro rājannindra syārdhāsanaṃ labhet
striyāstīrthaṃ tato gacchetsarvapāpapraṇāśanam || 31 ||
tatrāpi snātamātrasya dhruvaṃ gāṇeśvarī gatiḥ
airaṇḍīnarmadayośca saṃgamaṃ lokaviśrutam || 32 ||
tacca tīrthaṃ mahāpuṇyaṃ sarvapāpapraṇāśanam
upavāsaparo bhūtvā nityavrataparāyaṇaḥ || 33 ||
tatra snātvā tu rājendra mucyate brahmahatyayā
tato gacchecca rājendra narmadodadhisaṃgamam || 34 ||
jāmadagnyamiti khyātaṃ siddho yatra janārdanaḥ
yatreṣṭvā bahubhiryajñairindro devādhipo'bhavat || 35 ||
tatra snātvā tu rājendra narmadodadhisaṃgame
triguṇaṃ cāśvamedhasya phalaṃ prāpnoti mānavaḥ || 36 ||
paścimasyodadheḥ saṃdhau svargadvāravighaṭṭanam
tatra devāḥ sagandharvā ṛṣayaḥ siddhacāraṇāḥ || 37 ||
ārādhayanti deveśaṃ trisaṃdhyaṃ vimaleśvaram
tatra snātvā naro rājanrudra loke mahīyate || 38 ||
vimaleśātparaṃ tīrthaṃ na bhūtaṃ na bhaviṣyati
tatropavāsaṃ kṛtvā ye paśyanti vimaleśvaram || 39 ||
saptajanmakṛtaṃ pāpaṃ hitvā yānti śivālayam
tato gacchettu rājendra kauśikītīrthamuttamam || 40 ||
tatra snātvā naro rājannupavāsaparāyaṇaḥ
upoṣya rajanīmekāṃ niyato niyatāśanaḥ || 41 ||
etattīrthaprabhāveṇa mucyate brahmahatyayā
sarvatīrthābhiṣekaṃ tu yaḥ paśyetsāgareśvaram || 42 ||
yojanābhyantare tiṣṭhannāvarte saṃsthitaḥ śivaḥ
taṃ dṛṣṭvā sarvatīrthāni dṛṣṭānyeva na saṃśayaḥ || 43 ||
sarvapāpavinirmukto yatra rudraḥ sa gacchati
narmadāsaṃgamaṃ yāvadyāvaccāmarakaṇṭakam || 44 ||
atrāntare mahārāja tīrthakoṭyo daśa smṛtāḥ
tīrthāttīrthāntaraṃ yatra ṛṣikoṭiniṣevitam || 45 ||
sāgnihotraistu vidvadbhiḥ sarvairdhyānaparāyaṇaiḥ
sevitā'nena rājendra tvīpsitārthapradāyikā || 46 ||
yastvidaṃ vai paṭhennityaṃ śṛṇuyādvā'pi bhāvataḥ
tasya tīrthāni sarvāṇi hyabhiṣiñcati pāṇḍava || 47 ||
narmadā ca sadā prītā bhavedvai nātra saṃśayaḥ
prītastasya bhavedrudro mārkaṇḍeyo mahāmuniḥ || 48 ||
bandhyā caiva labhetputrāndurbhagā subhagā bhavet
kanyā labheta bhartāraṃ yaśca vāñchettu yatphalam
tadeva labhate sarvaṃ nātra kāryā vicāraṇā || 49 ||
brāhmaṇo vedamāpnoti kṣatriyo vijayī bhavet
vaiśyastu labhate lābhaṃ śūdraḥ prāpnoti sadgatim || 50 ||
murkhastu labhate vidyāṃ trisaṃdhyaṃ yaḥ paṭhennaraḥ
narakaṃ ca na paśyettu viyogaṃ ca na gacchati || 51 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe narmadāmāhātmyaṃ nāma caturnavatyadhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 194 ||

<195>

atha pañcanavatyadhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
sūta uvāca
ityākarṇya sa rājendra oṃkārasyābhivarṇanam
tataḥ papraccha deveśaṃ matsyarūpaṃ jalārṇave || 1 ||
manuruvāca
ṛṣīṇāṃ nāma gotrāṇi vaṃśāvataraṇaṃ tathā
pravarāṇāṃ tathā sāmyamasāmyaṃ vistarādvada || 2 ||
mahādevena ṛṣayaḥ śaptāḥ svāyaṃbhuve'ntare
teṣāṃ vaivasvate prāpte saṃbhavaṃ mama kīrtaya || 3 ||
dākṣāyaṇīnāṃ ca tathā prajāḥ kīrtaya me prabho
ṛṣīṇāṃ ca tathā vaṃśaṃ bhṛguvaṃśavivardhanam || 4 ||
matsya uvāca
manvantare'sminsaṃprāpte pūrvaṃ vaivasvate tathā
caritraṃ kathyate rājanbrahmaṇaḥ parameṣṭhinaḥ || 5 ||
mahādevasya śāpena tyaktvā dehaṃ svayaṃ tathā
ṛṣayaśca samudbhūtā hute śukre mahātmanā || 6 ||
devānāṃ mātaro dṛṣṭvā devapatnyastathaiva ca
skannaṃ śukraṃ mahārāja brahmaṇaḥ parameṣṭhinaḥ || 7 ||
tajjuhāva tato brahmā tato jātā hutāśanāt
tato jāto mahātejā bhṛguśca tapasāṃ nidhiḥ || 8 ||
aṅgāreṣvaṅgirā jāto harcirbhyo'tristathaiva ca
marīcibhyo marīcistu tato jāto mahātapāḥ || 9 ||
keśaistu kapiśo jātaḥ pulastyaśca mahātapāḥ
keśaiḥ pralambaiḥ pulahastato jāto mahātapāḥ || 10 ||
vasumadhyātsamutpanno vasiṣṭhastu tapodhanaḥ
bhṛguḥ pulomnastu sutāṃ divyāṃ bhāryāmavindata || 11 ||
tasyāmasya sutā jātā devā dvādaśa yājñikāḥ
bhuvano bhauvanaścaiva sujanyaḥ sujanastathā || 12 ||
kraturvasuśca mūrdhā ca tyājyaśca vasudaśca ha
prabhavaścāvyayaścaiva dakṣo'tha dvādaśastathā || 13 ||
ityete bhṛgavo nāma devā dvādaśa kīrtitāḥ
paulomyāṃ janayanviprā devānāṃ tu kanīyasaḥ || 14 ||
cyavanaṃ tu mahābhāgamāpnuvānaṃ tathaiva ca
āpnuvānātmajaścaurvo jamadagnistadātmajaḥ || 15 ||
aurvo gotrakarasteṣāṃ bhārgavāṇāṃ mahātmanām
tatra gotrakarānvakṣye bhṛgorvai dīptatejasaḥ || 16 ||
bhṛguśca cyavanaścaiva āpnuvānastathaiva ca
aurvaśca jamadagniśca vātsyo daṇḍirnaḍāyanaḥ || 17 ||
vaigāyano vītihavyaḥ pailaścaivātra śaunakaḥ
śaunakāyanajīvantirāvedaḥ kārṣaṇistathā || 18 ||
vaihīnarirvirūpākṣo rauhityāyanireva ca
vaiśvānaristathā nīlo lubdhaḥ sāvarṇikaśca saḥ || 19 ||
viṣṇuḥ pauro'pi bālākirailiko'nantabhāginaḥ
mṛgamārgeyamārkaṇḍajavino nītinastathā || 20 ||
maṇḍamāṇḍavyamāṇḍūkaphenapāḥ stanitastathā
sthalapiṇḍaḥ śikhāvarṇaḥ śārkarākṣistathaiva ca || 21 ||
jāladhiḥ saudhikaḥ kṣubhyaḥ kutso'nyo maudgalāyanaḥ
māṅkāyano devapatiḥ pāṇḍurociḥ sagālavaḥ || 22 ||
sāṃkṛtyaścātakiḥ sārpiryajñapiṇḍāyanastathā
gārgyāyaṇo gāyanaśca ṛṣirgārhāyaṇastathā || 23 ||
goṣṭhāyano vāhyayano vaiśampāyana eva ca
vaikarṇiniḥ śārṅgaravo yājñeyirbhrāṣṭrakāyaṇiḥ || 24 ||
lālāṭirnākuliścaiva laukṣiṇyoparimaṇḍalau
ālukiḥ saucakiḥ kautsastathā'nyaḥ paiṅgalāyaniḥ || 25 ||
sātyāyanirmālayaniḥ kauṭiliḥ kaucahastikaḥ
sauhaḥ soktiḥ sakauvākṣiḥ kausiścāndra masistathā || 26 ||
naikajihvo jihvakaśca vyādhājyo lauhavairiṇaḥ
śāradvatikanetiṣyau lolākṣiścalakuṇḍalaḥ || 27 ||
vāṃgāyaniścānumatiḥ pūrṇimāgatiko'sakṛt
sāmānyena yathā teṣāṃ pañcaite pravarā matāḥ || 28 ||
bhṛguśca cyavanaścaiva āpnuvānastathaiva ca
aurvaśca jamadagniśca pañcaite pravarā matāḥ || 29 ||
ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi śṛṇu tvanyānbhṛgūdvahān
jamadagnirbidaścaiva paulastyo baijabhṛttathā || 30 ||
ṛṣiścobhayajātaśca kāyaniḥ śākaṭāyanaḥ
aurveyā mārutāścaiva sarveṣāṃ pravarāḥ śubhāḥ || 31 ||
bhṛguśca cyavanaścaiva apnuvānastathaiva ca
parasparamavaivāhyā ṛṣayaḥ parikīrtitāḥ || 32 ||
bhṛgudāso mārgapatho grāmyāyaṇikaṭāyanī
āpastambistathā bilvirnaikaśiḥ kapireva ca || 33 ||
ārṣṭiṣeṇo gārdabhiśca kārdamāyanireva ca
āśvāyanistathā rūpiḥ pañcā''rṣeyāḥ prakīrtitāḥ || 34 ||
bhṛguśca cyavanaścaiva āpnuvānastathaiva ca
ārṣṭiṣeṇastathā rūpiḥ pravarāḥ pañca kīrtitāḥ || 35 ||
parasparamavaivāhyā ṛṣayaḥ parikīrtitāḥ
yasko vā vītihavyo vā mathitastu tathā damaḥ || 36 ||
jaivantyāyanirmauñjaśca piliścaiva calistathā
bhāgilo bhāgavittiśca kauśāpistvatha kāśyapiḥ || 37 ||
bālapiḥ śramadāgepiḥ saurastithistathaiva ca
gārgīyastvatha jābālistathā pauṣṇyāyano hyṛṣiḥ || 38 ||
rāmodaśca tathaiteṣāmārṣeyāḥ pravarā matāḥ
bhṛguśca vītihavyaśca tathā raivasavaivasau || 39 ||
parasparamavaivāhyā ṛṣayaḥ parikīrtitāḥ
śālāyaniḥ śākaṭākṣo maitreyaḥ khāṇḍavastathā || 40 ||
drau ṇāyano rakumāyaṇirāpiśiścāpikāyaniḥ
haṃsajihvastathaiteṣāmārṣeyāḥ pravarā matāḥ || 41 ||
bhṛguścaivātha vaddhryaśvo divodāsastathaiva ca
parasparamavaivāhyā ṛṣayaḥ parikīrtitāḥ || 42 ||
ekāyano yajñapatirmatsyagandhastathaiva ca
pratyahaśca tathā sauriścaukṣirvai kārdamāyaniḥ || 43 ||
tathā gṛtsamado rājansanakaśca mahānṛṣiḥ
pravarāstu tathoktānāmārṣeyāḥ parikīrtitāḥ || 44 ||
bhṛgurgṛtsamadaścaiva ārṣāvetau prakīrtitau
parasparamavaivāhyā ityete parikīrtitāḥ || 45 ||
ete tavoktā bhṛguvaṃśajātā mahānubhāvā nṛpagotrakārāḥ
eṣāṃ tu nāmnāṃ parikīrtanena pāpaṃ samagraṃ vijahāti jantuḥ || 46 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe bhṛguvaṃśapravarakīrtanaṃ nāma pañcanavatyadhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 195 ||

<196>

atha ṣaṇṇavatyadhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
matsya uvāca
marīcitanayā rājansurūpā nāma viśrutā
bhāryā cāṅgiraso devāstasyāḥ putrā daśa smṛtāḥ || 1 ||
ātmāyurdamano dakṣaḥ sadaḥ prāṇastathaiva ca
haviṣmāṃśca gaviṣṭhaśca ṛtaḥ satyaśca te daśa || 2 ||
ete cā''ṅgiraso nāma devā vai somapāyinaḥ
surūpā janayāmāsa ṛṣīnsarveśvarānimān || 3 ||
bṛhaspatiṃ gautamaṃ ca saṃvartamṛṣimuttamam
utathyaṃ vāmadevaṃ ca ajasyamṛṣijaṃ tathā || 4 ||
ityete ṛṣayaḥ sarve gotrakārāḥ prakīrtitāḥ
teṣāṃ gotrasamutpannāngotrakārānnibodha me || 5 ||
utathyo gautamaścaiva tauleyo'bhijitastathā
sārdhanemiḥ salaugākṣiḥ kṣīraḥ kauṣṭikireva ca || 6 ||
rāhukarṇiḥ saupuriśca kairātiḥ sāmalomakiḥ
pauṣājitirbhārgavato hyṛṣiścairīḍavastathā || 7 ||
kāroṭakaḥ sajīvī ca upabindusuraiṣiṇau
vāhinīpativaiśālī kroṣṭā caivāruṇāyaniḥ || 8 ||
somo'trāyanikāsorukauśalyāḥ pārthivāstathā
rauhiṇyāyanirevāgnī mūlayaḥ pāṇḍureva ca || 9 ||
kṣapāviśvakaro'riśca pārikārārireva ca
ārṣeyāḥ pravarāścaiva teṣāṃ ca pravarāñchṛṇu || 10 ||
aṅgirāḥ suvacotathya uśijaśca mahānṛṣiḥ
parasparamavaivāhyā ṛṣayaḥ parikīrtitāḥ || 11 ||
ātreyāyaṇisauveṣṭyāvagniveśyaḥ śilāsthaliḥ
bāliśāyaniścaikepī vārāhirbāṣkalistathā || 12 ||
sauṭiśca tṛṇakarṇiśca prāvahiścā''śvalāyaniḥ
vārāhirbarhisādī ca śikhāgrīvistathaiva ca || 13 ||
kārakiśca mahākāpistathā coḍupatiḥ prabhuḥ
kaucakirdhamitaścaiva puṣpānveṣistathaiva ca || 14 ||
somatanvirbrahmatanviḥ sālaḍirbālaḍistathā
devarārirdevasthānirhārikarṇiḥ saridbhuviḥ || 15 ||
prāvepiḥ sādyasugrīvistathā gomedagandhikaḥ
matsyācchādyo mūlaharaḥ phalāhārastathaiva ca || 16 ||
gāṅgodadhiḥ kaurupatiḥ kaurukṣetristathaiva ca
nāyakirjaityadrau ṇiśca jaihvalāyanireva ca || 17 ||
āpastambirmauñjavṛṣṭirmārṣṭapiṅgalireva ca
pailaścaiva mahātejāḥ śālaṅkāyanireva ca || 18 ||
dvyākhyeyo mārutaścaiṣāṃ sarveṣāṃ pravaro nṛpa
aṅgirāḥ prathamasteṣāṃ dvitīyaśca bṛhaspatiḥ || 19 ||
tṛtīyaśca bharadvājaḥ pravarāḥ parikīrtitāḥ
parasparamavaivāhyā ityete parikīrtitāḥ || 20 ||
kāṇvāyanāḥ kopacayāstathā vātsyatarāyaṇāḥ
bhrāṣṭrakṛdrā ṣṭrapiṇḍī ca laindrā ṇiḥ sāyakāyaniḥ || 21 ||
kroṣṭākṣī bahuvītī ca tālakṛnmadhurāvahaḥ
lāvakṛdgālavidgāthī mārkaṭiḥ paulikāyaniḥ || 22 ||
skandasaśca tathā cakrī gārgyaḥ śyāmāyanistathā
balākiḥ sāhariścaiva pañcā''rṣeyāḥ prakīrtitāḥ || 23 ||
aṅgirāśca mahātejā devācāryo bṛhaspatiḥ
bharadvājastathā gargaḥ saityaśca bhagavānṛṣiḥ || 24 ||
parasparamavaivāhyā ṛṣayaḥ parikīrtitāḥ
kapītaraḥ svastitaro dākṣiḥ śaktiḥ patañjaliḥ || 25 ||
bhūyasirjalasaṃdhiśca bindurmādiḥ kusīdakiḥ
ūrvastu rājakeśī ca vauṣaḍiḥ śaṃsapistathā || 26 ||
śāliśca kalaśīkaṇṭa ṛṣiḥ kārīrayastathā
kāṭyo dhānyāyaniścaiva bhāvāsyāyanireva ca || 27 ||
bharadvājiḥ saubudhiśca laghvī devamatistathā
tryārṣeyo'bhimataścaiṣāṃ pravaro bhūmipottama || 28 ||
aṅgirā damavāhyaśca tathā caivāpyurukṣayaḥ
parasparamavaivāhyā ṛṣayaḥ parikīrtitāḥ || 29 ||
saṃkṛtiśca trimārṣṭiśca manuḥ saṃbadhireva vā
taṇḍiścenātakiścaiva tailakā dakṣa eva ca || 30 ||
nārāyaṇiścārṣiṇiśca laukṣirgārgyaharistathā
gālavaśca anehaśca sarveṣāṃ pravaro mataḥ || 31 ||
aṅgirāḥ saṃkṛtiścaiva gauravītistathaiva ca
parasparamavaivāhyā ṛṣayaḥ parikīrtitāḥ || 32 ||
kātyāyano haritakaḥ kautsaḥ piṅgastathaiva ca
haṇḍidāso vātsyāyanirmādri rmauliḥ kuberaṇiḥ || 33 ||
bhīmavegaḥ śāśvadarbhiḥ sarve tripravarāḥ smṛtāḥ
aṅgirā bṛhadaśvaśca jīvanāśvastathaiva ca || 34 ||
parasparamavaivāhyā ṛṣayaḥ parikīrtitāḥ
bṛhaduktho vāmadevastathā tripravarā matāḥ || 35 ||
aṅgirā bṛhadukthaśca vāmadevastathaiva ca
parasparamavaivāhyā ityete parikīrtitāḥ || 36 ||
kutsagotrodbhavāścaiva tathā tripravarā matāḥ
aṅgirāśca sadasyuśca purukutsastathaiva ca
kutsāḥ kutsairavaivāhyā evamāhuḥ purātanāḥ || 37 ||
rathītarāṇāṃ pravarāstryārṣeyāḥ parikīrtitāḥ
aṅgirāśca virūpaśca tathaiva ca rathītaraḥ
rathītarā hyavaivāhyā nityameva rathītaraiḥ || 38 ||
viṣṇusiddhiḥ śivamatirjatṛṇaḥ kattṛṇastathā
putravaśca mahātejāstathā vairaparāyaṇaḥ || 39 ||
tryārṣeyo'bhimatasteṣāṃ sarveṣāṃ pravaro nṛpa
aṅgirāśca virūpaśca vṛṣaparvastathaiva ca
parasparamavaivāhyā ṛṣayaḥ parikīrtitāḥ || 40 ||
sātyagugrirmahātejā hiraṇyastambimudgalau
tryārṣeyo hi matasteṣāṃ sarveṣāṃ pravaro nṛpa || 41 ||
aṅgirā matsyadagdhaśca mudgalaśca mahātapāḥ
parasparamavaivāhyā ṛṣayaḥ parikīrtitāḥ || 42 ||
haṃsajihvo devajihvo hyagnijihvo virāḍapaḥ
apāgneyastvaśvayuśca paraṇyastā vimaudgalāḥ || 43 ||
tryārṣeyābhimatāsteṣāṃ sarveṣāṃ pravarāḥ śubhāḥ
aṅgirāścaiva tāṇḍiśca maudgalyaśca mahātapāḥ || 44 ||
parasparamavaivāhyā ṛṣayaḥ parikīrtitāḥ
apāṇḍuśca guruścaiva tṛtīyaḥ śākaṭāyanaḥ
tataḥ prāgāthamā nārī mārkaṇḍo maraṇaḥ śivaḥ || 45 ||
kaṭurmarkaṭapaścaiva tathā nāḍāyano hyṛṣiḥ
śyāmāyanastathaivaiṣāṃ tryārṣeyāḥ pravarāḥ śubhāḥ || 46 ||
aṅgirāścājamīḍhaśca kaṭyaścaiva mahātapāḥ
parasparamavaivāhyā ṛṣayaḥ parikīrtitāḥ || 47 ||
tittiriḥ kapibhūścaiva gārgyaścaiva mahānṛṣiḥ
tryārṣeyo he matasteṣāṃ sarveṣāṃ pravaraḥ śubhaḥ || 48 ||
aṅgirāstittiriścaiva kapibhūśca mahānṛṣiḥ
parasparamavaivāhyā ṛṣayaḥ parikīrtitāḥ || 49 ||
atha ṛkṣabharadvājau ṛṣivānmānavastathā
ṛṣirmaitravaraścaiva pañcārṣeyāḥ prakīrtitāḥ || 50 ||
aṅgirāḥ sabharadvājastathaiva ca bṛhaspatiḥ
ṛṣirmitravaraścaiva ṛṣivānmānavastathā
parasparamavaivāhyā ṛṣayaḥ parikīrtitāḥ || 51 ||
bhāradvājo hutaḥ śauṅgaḥ śaiśireyastathaiva ca
ityete kathitāḥ sarve dvyāmuṣyāyaṇagotrajāḥ || 52 ||
pañcā''rṣeyāstathā hyeṣāṃ pravarāḥ parikīrtitāḥ
aṅgirāśca bharadvājastathaiva ca bṛhaspatiḥ || 53 ||
maudgalyaḥ śaiśiraścaiva pravarāḥ parikīrtitāḥ
parasparamavaivāhyā ṛṣayaḥ parikīrtitāḥ || 54 ||
ete tavoktāṅgirasastu vaṃśe mahānubhāvā ṛṣigotrakārāḥ
yeṣāṃ tu nāmnā parikīrtitena pāpaṃ samagraṃ puruṣo jahāti || 55 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe pravarānukīrtane'ṅgirovaṃśakīrtanaṃ nāma ṣaṇṇavatyadhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 196 ||

<197>

atha saptanavatyadhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
matsya uvāca
atrivaṃśasamutpannāngotrakārānnibodha me
kardamāyanaśākheyāstathā śārāyaṇāśca ye || 1 ||
uddālakiḥ śauṇakarṇirathau śaukratavaśca ye
gauragrīvā gaurajinastathā caitrāyaṇāśca ye || 2 ||
ardhapaṇyā vāmarathyā gopanāstakibindavaḥ
karṇajihvo haraprītirlaidrā ṇiḥ śākalāyaniḥ || 3 ||
tailapaśca savaileyo atrirgoṇīpatistathā
jalado bhagapādaśca saupuṣpiśca mahātapāḥ || 4 ||
chandogeyastathaiteṣāṃ tryārṣeyāḥ pravarā matāḥ
śyāvāśvaśca tathā'triśca ārcanānaśa eva ca || 5 ||
parasparamavaivāhyā ṛṣayaḥ parikīrtitāḥ
dākṣirbaliḥ parṇaviśca ūrṇanābhiḥ śilārdaniḥ || 6 ||
bījavāpi śirīṣaśca mauñjakeśo gaviṣṭhiraḥ
bhalandanastathaiteṣāṃ tryārṣeyāḥ pravarā matāḥ || 7 ||
atrirgaviṣṭhiraścaiva tathā pūrvātithiḥ smṛtaḥ
parasparamavaivāhyā ṛṣayaḥ parikīrtitāḥ || 8 ||
ātreyaputrikāputrānata ūrdhvaṃ nibodha me
kāleyāśca sabāleyā vāmarathyāstathaiva ca || 9 ||
dhātreyāścaiva maitreyāstryārṣeyāḥ parikīrtitāḥ
atriśca vāmarathyaśca pautriścaiva mahānṛṣiḥ
parasparamavaivāhyā ṛṣayaḥ parikīrtitāḥ || 10 ||
ityatrivaṃśaprabhavāstavoktā mahānubhāvā nṛpa gotrakārāḥ
yeṣāṃ tu nāmnā parikīrtitena pāpaṃ samagraṃ puruṣo jahāti || 11 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe pravarānukīrtane'trivaṃśānukīrtanaṃ nāma saptanavatyadhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 197 ||

<198>

athāṣṭanavatyadhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
matsya uvāca
atrerevāparaṃ vaṃśaṃ tava vakṣyāmi pārthiva
atreḥ somaḥ sutaḥ śrīmāṃstasya vaṃśodbhavo nṛpa || 1 ||
viśvāmitrastu tapasā brāhmaṇyaṃ samavāptavān
tasya vaṃśamahaṃ vakṣye tanme nigadataḥ śṛṇu || 2 ||
viśvāmitro devarātastathā vaikṛtigālavaḥ
vataṇḍaśca śalaṅkaśca hyabhayaścāyatāyanaḥ || 3 ||
śyāmāyanā yājñavaklyā jābālāḥ saindhavāyanāḥ
bābhravyāśca karīṣāśca saṃśrutyā atha saṃśrutāḥ || 4 ||
ulūpā aupahāvāśca payodajanapādapāḥ
kharavāco halayamāḥ sādhitā vāsukauśikāḥ || 5 ||
tryārṣeyāḥ pravarāsteṣāṃ sarveṣāṃ parikīrtitāḥ
viśvāmitro devarāta uddālaśca mahayaśāḥ || 6 ||
parasparamavaivāhyā ṛṣayaḥ parikīrtitāḥ
devaśravāḥ sujāteyāḥ saumukāḥ kārukāyaṇāḥ || 7 ||
tathā vaideharātā ye kuśikāśca narādhipa
tryārṣeyo'bhimatasteṣāṃ sarveṣāṃ pravaraḥ śubhaḥ || 8 ||
devaśravā devarāto viśvāmitrastathaiva ca
parasparamavaivāhyā ṛṣayaḥ parikīrtitāḥ || 9 ||
dhanaṃjayaḥ kapardeyaḥ parikūṭaśca pārthiva
pāṇiniścaiva tryārṣeyāḥ sarva ete prakīrtitāḥ || 10 ||
viśvāmitrastathā''dyaśca mādhucchandasa eva ca
tryārṣeyāḥ pravarā hyete ṛṣayaḥ parikīrtitāḥ || 11 ||
viśvāmitro madhucchandāstathā caivāghamarṣaṇaḥ
parasparamavaivāhyā ṛṣayaḥ parikīrtitāḥ || 12 ||
kāmalāyanijaścaiva aśmarathyastathaiva ca
vañjuliścāpi tryārṣeyaḥ sarveṣāṃ pravaro mataḥ || 13 ||
viśvāmitraścāśvaratho vañculiśca mahātapāḥ
parasparamavaivāhyā ṛṣayaḥ parikīrtitāḥ || 14 ||
viśvāmitro lohitaśca aṣṭakaḥ pūraṇastathā
viśvāmitraḥ pūraṇaśca tayordvau pravarau smṛtau || 15 ||
parasparamavaivāhyāḥ pūraṇāśca parasparam
lohitā aṣṭakāścaiṣāṃ tryārṣeyāḥ parikīrtitāḥ || 16 ||
viśvāmitro lohitaśca aṣṭakaśca mahātapāḥ
aṣṭakā lohitairnityamavaivāhyāḥ parasparam || 17 ||
udareṇuḥ krathakaśca ṛṣiścodāvahistathā
ārṣeyo'bhimatasteṣāṃ sarveṣāṃ pravaraḥ smṛtaḥ || 18 ||
ṛṇavāngatinaścaiva viśvāmitrastathaiva ca
parasparamavaivāhyā ṛṣayaḥ parikīrtitāḥ || 19 ||
udumbaraḥ saiṣiriṭirṛṣistrākṣāyaṇistathā
śāṭhyāyaniḥ karīrāśī śālaṅkāyanilāvakī
mauñjāyaniśca bhagavāṃtryārṣeyāḥ parikīrtitāḥ || 20 ||
khilikhilistathā vidyo viśvāmitrastathaiva ca
parasparamavaivāhyā ṛṣayaḥ parikīrtitāḥ || 21 ||
ete tavoktāḥ kuśikā narendra mahānubhāvāḥ satataṃ dvijendrāḥ
yeṣāṃ tu nāmnā parikīrtitena pāpaṃ samagraṃ puruṣo jahāti || 22 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe pravarānukīrtane viśvāmitravaṃśānuvarṇanaṃ nāmāṣṭanavatyadhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 198 ||

<199>

atha navanavatyadhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
matsya uvāca
marīceḥ kaśyapaḥ putraḥ kaśyapasya tathā kule
gotrakārānṛṣīnvakṣye teṣāṃ nāmāni me śṛṇu || 1 ||
āśrāyaṇiṛṣīgaṇo meṣakīriṭakāyanāḥ
udagrajā māṭharāśca bhojā vinayalakṣaṇāḥ || 2 ||
śālāhaleyāḥ kauriṣṭāḥ kanyakāścā''surāyaṇāḥ
mandākinyāṃ vai mṛgayāḥ śrotanā bhautapāyanāḥ || 3 ||
devayānā gomayānā hyadhaśchāyābhayāśca ye
kātyāyanāḥ śakrayaṇā barhiryogagadāyanāḥ || 4 ||
bhavanandirmahācakrirdākṣapāyaṇa eva ca
yodhayānāḥ kārtivayo hastidānāstathaiva ca || 5 ||
vātsyāyanā nikṛtajā hyāśvalāyaninastathā
prāgāyaṇāḥ pailamaulirāśvavātāyanastathā || 6 ||
kauberakāśca śyākārā agniśarmāyaṇāśca ye
meṣapāḥ kaikarasapāstathā caiva tu babhravaḥ || 7 ||
prāceyo jñānasaṃjñeyā āgnā prāsevya eva ca
śyāmodarā vaivaśapāstathā caivodbalāyanāḥ || 8 ||
kāṣṭhāhāriṇamārīcā ājihāyanahāstikāḥ
vaikarṇeyāḥ kāśyapeyāḥ sāsisāhāritāyanāḥ || 9 ||
mātaṅginaśca bhṛgavastryārṣeyāḥ parikīrtitāḥ
vatsaraḥ kaśyapaścaiva nidhruvaśca mahātapāḥ || 10 ||
parasparamavaivāhyā ṛṣayaḥ parikīrtitāḥ
ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi dvāmuṣyāyaṇagotrajān || 11 ||
anasūyo nākurayaḥ snātapo rājavartapaḥ
śaiśirodavahiścaiva sairandhrī raupasevakiḥ || 12 ||
yāmuniḥ kādrupiṅgākṣiḥ sajātambistathaiva ca
divāvaṣṭāśca ityete bhaktyā jñeyāśca kāśyapāḥ || 13 ||
tryārṣeyāśca tathaivaiṣāṃ sarveṣāṃ pravarāḥ śubhāḥ
vatsaraḥ kaśyapaścaiva vasiṣṭhaśca mahātapāḥ || 14 ||
parasparamavaivāhyā ṛṣayaḥ parikīrtitāḥ
saṃyātiśca nabhaścobhau pippalyo'tha jalaṃdharaḥ || 15 ||
bhujātapūraḥ pūryaśca kardamo gardabhīmukhaḥ
hiraṇyabāhukairātāvubhau kāśyapagobhilau || 16 ||
kulaho vṛṣakaṇḍaśca mṛgaketustathottaraḥ
nidāghamasṛṇau bhartsyā mahāntaḥ keralāśca ye || 17 ||
śāṇḍilyo dānavaścaiva tathā vai devajātayaḥ
paippalādiḥ sapravarā ṛṣayaḥ parikīrtitāḥ || 18 ||
tryārṣeyābhimatāścaiṣāṃ sarveṣāṃ pravarāḥ śubhāḥ
asito devalaścaiva kaśyapaśca mahātapāḥ
parasparamavaivāhyā ṛṣayaḥ parikīrtitāḥ || 19 ||
ṛṣipradhānasya ca kaśyapasya dākṣāyaṇībhyaḥ sakalaṃ prasūtam
jagatsamagraṃ manusiṃha puṇyaṃ kiṃ te pravakṣyāmyahamuttaraṃ tu || 20 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe pravarānukīrtane kaśyapavaṃśavarṇanaṃ nāma navanavatyadhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 199 ||

<200>

atha dviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
matsya uvāca
vasiṣṭhavaṃśajānviprānnibodha vadato mama
ekārṣeyastu pravaro vāsiṣṭhānāṃ prakīrtitaḥ || 1 ||
vasiṣṭhā eva vāsiṣṭhā avivāhyā vasiṣṭhajaiḥ
vyāghrapādā aupagavā vaiklavāḥ śādvalāyanāḥ || 2 ||
kapiṣṭhalā aupalomā alabdhāśca śaṭhāḥ kaṭhāḥ
gaupāyanā bodhapāśca dākavyā hyatha vāhyakāḥ || 3 ||
bāliśayāḥ pāliśayāstato vāggranthayaśca ye
āpasthūṇāḥ śītavṛttāstathā brāhmapureyakāḥ || 4 ||
lomāyanāḥ svastikarāḥ śāṇḍilirgauḍinistathā
vāḍohaliśca sumanāścopāvṛddhistathaiva ca || 5 ||
caulirvaulirbrahmabalaḥ pauliḥ śravasa eva ca
pauḍavo yājñavalkyaśca akārṣeyā maharṣayaḥ || 6 ||
vasiṣṭha eṣāṃ pravaro hyavaivāhyāḥ parasparam
śailālayo mahākarṇaḥ kauravyaḥ krodhinastathā || 7 ||
kapiñjalā vālakhilyā bhāgavittāyanāśca ye
kaulāyanaḥ kālaśikhaḥ korakṛṣṇāḥ surāyaṇāḥ || 8 ||
śākāhāryāḥ śākadhiyaḥ kāṇvā upalapāśca ye
śākāyanā uhākāśca atha māṣaśarāvayāḥ || 9 ||
dākāyanā bālavayo vākayo gorathāstathā
lambāyanāḥ śyāmavayo ye ca kroḍodarāyaṇāḥ || 10 ||
pralambāyanāśca ṛṣaya aupamanyava eva ca
sāṃkhyāyanāśca ṛṣayastathā vai vedaśerakāḥ || 11 ||
pālaṅkāyana udgāhā ṛṣayaśca balekṣavaḥ
māteyā brahmamalinaḥ pannāgāristathaiva ca || 12 ||
tryārṣeyo'bhimataścaiṣāṃ sarveṣāṃ pravarastathā
bhagīvasurvasiṣṭhaśca indra pramadireva ca || 13 ||
parasparamavaivāhyā ṛṣayaḥ parikīrtitāḥ
aupasthalāsvasthalayo bālo hālo halāśca ye || 14 ||
mādhyaṃdino mākṣatayaḥ paippalādirvicakṣuṣaḥ
traiśṛṅgāyaṇasaibalkāḥ kuṇḍinaśca narottama || 15 ||
tryārṣeyābhimatāścaiṣāṃ sarveṣāṃ pravarāḥ śubhāḥ
vasiṣṭhamitrāvaruṇau kuṇḍinaśca mahātapāḥ || 16 ||
dānakāyā mahāvīryā nāgeyāḥ paramāstathā
ālambā vāyanaścāpi ye cakroḍādayo narāḥ || 17 ||
parasparamavaivāhyā ṛṣayaḥ parikīrtitāḥ
śivakarṇo vayaścaiva pādapaśca tathaiva ca || 18 ||
tryārṣeyo'bhimataścaiṣāṃ sarveṣāṃ pravarastathā
jātūkarṇyo vasiṣṭhaśca tathaivātriśca pārthiva
parasparamavaivāhyā ṛṣayaḥ parikīrtitāḥ || 19 ||
vasiṣṭhavaṃśe'bhihitā mayaite ṛṣipradhānāḥ satataṃ dvijendrāḥ
yeṣāṃ tu nāmnāṃ parikīrtitena pāpaṃ samagraṃ puruṣo jahāti || 20 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe pravarānukīrtane vasiṣṭhagotrānuvarṇanaṃ nāma dviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 200 ||

<201>

athaikādhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
matsya uvāca
vasiṣṭhastu mahātejā nimeḥpūrvapurohitaḥ
babhūvuḥ pārthivaśreṣṭha yajñāstasya samantataḥ || 1 ||
śrāntātmā pārthivaśreṣṭha viśaśrāma tadā guruḥ
taṃ gatvā pārthivaśreṣṭho nimirvacanamabravīt || 2 ||
bhagavanyaṣṭumicchāmi tanmāṃ yājaya mā ciram
tamuvāca mahātejā vasiṣṭhaḥ pārthivottamam || 3 ||
kaṃcitkālaṃ pratīkṣasva tava yajñaiḥ susattamaiḥ
śrānto'smi rājanviśramya yājayiṣyāmi te nṛpa || 4 ||
evamuktaḥ pratyuvāca vasiṣṭhaṃ nṛpasattamaḥ
pāralaukikakārye tu kaḥ pratīkṣitumutsahet || 5 ||
na ca me sauhṛdaṃ brahmankṛtāntena balīyasā
dharmakārye tvarā kāryā calaṃ yasmāddhi jīvitam || 6 ||
dharmapathyodano janturmṛto'pi sukhamaśnute
śvaḥ kāryamadya kurvīta pūrvāhṇe cā''parāhṇikam || 7 ||
na hi pratīkṣate mṛtyuḥ kṛtaṃ cāsya na vā kṛtam
kṣetrāpaṇagṛhāsaktamanyatragatamānasam || 8 ||
vṛkīvoraṇamāsādya mṛtyurādāya gacchati
na kālasya priyaḥ kaściddveṣyaścāsya na vidyate || 9 ||
āyuṣye karmaṇi kṣīṇe prasahya harate janam
prāṇavāyoścalatvaṃ ca tvayā viditameva ca || 10 ||
yadatra jīvyate brahmankṣaṇamātraṃ tadadbhutam
śarīraṃ śāśvataṃ manye vidyābhyāse dhanārjane || 11 ||
aśāśvataṃ dharmakārye ṛṇavānasmi saṃkaṭe
so'haṃ saṃbhṛtasaṃbhāro bhavanmūlamupāgataḥ || 12 ||
ne cedyājayase māṃ tvamanyaṃ yāsyāmi yājakam
evamuktastadā tena niminā brāhmaṇottamaḥ || 13 ||
śaśāpa taṃ nimiṃ krodhādvidehastvaṃ bhaviṣyasi
śrāntaṃ māṃ tvaṃ samutsṛjya yasmādanyaṃ dvijottamam
dharmajñastu narendra tvaṃ yājakaṃ kartumicchasi
nimistaṃ pratyuvācātha dharmakāryaratasya me || 15 ||
vighnaṃ karoṣi nānyena yājanaṃ ca tathecchasi
śāpaṃ dadāmi tasmāttvaṃ videho'tha bhaviṣyasi || 16 ||
evamukte tu tau jātau videhau dvijapārthivau
dehahīnau tayorjīvau brahmāṇamupajagmatuḥ || 17 ||
tāvāgatau samīkṣyāya brahmā vacanamabravīt
adyaprabhṛti te sthānaṃ nime jīva dadāmyaham || 18 ||
netrapakṣmasu sarveṣāṃ manuṣyāṇāṃ bhaviṣyasi
tvatsaṃbandhāttathā teṣāṃ nimeṣaḥ saṃbhaviṣyati || 19 ||
cālayiṣyanti tu tadā netrapakṣmāṇi mānavāḥ
evamukto manuṣyāṇāṃ netrapakṣmasu sarvaśaḥ || 20 ||
jagāma nimijīvastu varadānātsvayaṃbhuvaḥ
vasiṣṭhajīvaṃ bhagavānbrahma vacanamabravīt || 21 ||
mitrāvaruṇayoḥ putro vasiṣṭha tvaṃ bhaviṣyasi
vasiṣṭheti ca te nāma tatrāpi ca bhaviṣyati || 22 ||
janmadvayamatītaṃ ca tatrāpi tvaṃ smariṣyasi
etasminneva kāle tu mitraśca varuṇastathā || 23 ||
badaryāśramamāsādya tapastepaturavyayam
tapasyatostayorevaṃ kadācinmādhave ṛtau || 24 ||
puṣpitadrumasaṃsthāne śubhe dayitamārute
urvaśī tu varārohā kurvatī kusumoccayam || 25 ||
susūkṣmaraktavasanā tayordṛṣṭipathaṃ gatā
tāṃ dṛṣṭvendumukhīṃ subhrūṃ nīlanīrajalocanām || 26 ||
ubhau cukṣubhaturdevau tadrū paparimohitau
tapasyatostayorvīryamaskhalacca mṛgāsane || 27 ||
skannaṃ retastato dṛṣṭvā śāpabhītā varāpsarā
cakāra kalaśe śukraṃ toyapūrṇe manorame || 28 ||
tasmādṛṣivarau jātau tejasā'pratimau bhuvi
vasiṣṭhaścāpyagastyaśca mitrāvaruṇayoḥ sutau || 29 ||
vasiṣṭhastūpayeme'tha bhaginīṃ nāradasya tu
arundhatīṃ varārohāṃ tasyāṃ śaktimajījanat || 30 ||
śakteḥ parāśaraḥ putrastasya vaṃśaṃ nibodha me
yasya dvaipāyanaḥ putraḥ svayaṃ viṣṇurajāyata || 31 ||
prakāśo janito yena loke bhāratacandra māḥ
yenājñānatamondhasya lokasyonmīlanaṃ kṛtam
parāśarasya tasya tvaṃ śṛṇuvaṃśamanuttamam || 32 ||
kauṇḍaśayo vāhanapo jaihmapo bhaumatāpanaḥ
gopālireṣāṃ pañcama ete gaurāḥ parāśarāḥ || 33 ||
prapohayā vāhyamayāḥ khyāteyāḥ kautujātayaḥ
haryaśviḥ pañcamo hyeṣāṃ nīlā jñeyāḥ parāśarāḥ || 34 ||
kārṣṇāyanāḥ kapimukhāḥ kākeyasthā japātayaḥ
puṣkaraḥ pañcamaścaiṣāṃ kṛṣṇā jñeyāḥ parāśarāḥ || 35 ||
śrāviṣṭhāyanabāleyāḥ svāyaṣṭāścopayāśca ye
iṣīkahastaścaite vai pañca śvetāḥ parāśarāḥ || 36 ||
vāṭiko bādariścaiva stambā vai krodhanāyanāḥ
kṣaimireṣāṃ pañcamastu ete śyāmāḥ parāśarāḥ || 37 ||
khalyāyanā vārṣṇāyanāstaileyāḥ khalu yūthapāḥ
tantireṣāṃ pañcamastu ete dhūmrāḥ parāśarāḥ || 38 ||
parāśarāṇāṃ sarveṣāṃ tryārṣeyaḥ pravaro mataḥ
parāśaraśca śaktiśca vasiṣṭhaśca mahātapāḥ
parasparamavaivāhyā sarva ete parāśarāḥ || 39 ||
uktāstavaite nṛpa vaṃśamukhyāḥ parāśarāḥ sūryasamaprabhāvāḥ
yeṣāṃ tu nāmnāṃ parikīrtitena pāpaṃ samagraṃ puruṣo jahāti || 40 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe pravarānukīrtane parāśaravaṃśavarṇanaṃ nāmaikādhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 201 ||

<202>

atha dvyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
matsya uvāca
ataḥ paramagastyasya vakṣye vaṃśodbhavāndvijān
agastyaśca karambhaśca kauśalyāḥ śakaṭāstathā || 1 ||
sumedhaso mayobhuvastathā gāndhārakāyaṇāḥ
paulastyā paulahāścaiva kratuvaṃśabhavāstathā || 2 ||
tryārṣeyābhimatāścaiṣāṃ sarveṣāṃ pravarāḥ śubhāḥ
agastyaśca mahendra śca ṛṣiścaiva mayobhuvaḥ || 3 ||
parasparamavaivāhyā ṛṣayaḥ parikīrtitāḥ
paurṇamāsāḥ pāraṇāśca tryārṣeyāḥ parikīrtitāḥ || 4 ||
agastyaḥ paurṇamāsaśca pāraṇaśca mahātapāḥ
parasparamavaivāhyāḥ paurṇamāsāstu pāraṇaiḥ || 5 ||
evamukta ṛṣīṇāṃ tu vaṃśa uttamapauruṣaḥ
ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi kiṃ bhavānadya kathyatām || 6 ||
manuruvāca
pulahasya pulastyasya kratoścaiva mahātmanaḥ
agastyasya tathā caiva kathaṃ vaṃśastaducyatām || 7 ||
matsya uvāca
kratuḥ khalvanapatyo'bhūdrā janvaivasvate'ntare
idhmavāhaṃ sa putratve jagrāha ṛṣisattamaḥ || 8 ||
agastyaputraṃ dharmajñamāgastyāḥ kratavastataḥ
pulahasya tathā putrāstrayaśca pṛthivīpate || 9 ||
teṣāṃ tu janma vakṣyāmi uttaratra yathāvidhi
pulahastu prajāṃ dṛṣṭvā nātiprītamanāḥ svakām || 10 ||
agastyajaṃ dṛḍhāsyaṃ tu putratve vṛtavāṃstataḥ
paulahāśca tathā rājannāgastyāḥ parikīrtitāḥ || 11 ||
pulastyānvayasaṃbhūtāndṛṣṭvā rakṣaḥ samudbhavān
agastyasya sutaṃ dhīmānputratve vṛtavāṃstataḥ || 12 ||
paulastyāśca tathā rājannāgastyāḥ parikīrtitāḥ
sagotratvādime sarve parasparamananvayāḥ || 13 ||
ete tavoktāḥ pravarā dvijānāṃ mahānubhāvā nṛpa vaṃśakārāḥ
eṣāṃ tu nāmnā parikīrtitena pāpaṃ samagraṃ puruṣo jahāti || 14 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe pravarānukīrtane dvyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 202 ||

<203>

atha tryadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
matsya uvāca
asminvaivasvate prāpte śṛṇu dharmasya pārthiva
dākṣāyaṇībhyaḥ sakalaṃ vaṃśaṃ daivatamuttamam || 1 ||
parvatādimahādurgaśarīrāṇi narādhipa
arundhatyāḥ prasūtāni dharmādvaivasvate'ntare || 2 ||
aṣṭau ca vasavaḥ putrāḥ somapāśca vibhostathā
dharo dhruvaśca somaśca āpaścaivānalānilau || 3 ||
pratyūṣaśca prabhāsaśca vasavo'ṣṭau prakīrtitāḥ
dharasya putro dra viṇaḥ kālaḥ putro dhruvasya tu || 4 ||
kālasyāvayavānāṃ tu śarīrāṇi narādhipa
mūrtimanti ca kālāddhi saṃprasūtānyaśeṣataḥ || 5 ||
somasya bhavagānvarcāḥ śrīmāṃścā''pasya kīrtyate
anekajanmajananaḥ kumārastvanalasya tu || 6 ||
purojavāścānilasya pratyūṣasya tu devalaḥ
viśvakarmā prabhāsasya tridaśānāṃ sa vardhakiḥ || 7 ||
samīhitakarāḥ proktā nāgavīthyādayo nava
lambāputraḥ smṛto ghoṣo bhānoḥ putrāśca bhānavaḥ || 8 ||
graharkṣāṇāṃ ca sarveṣāmanyeṣāṃ cāmitaujasām
marutvatyāṃ marutvantaḥ sarve putrāḥ prakīrtitāḥ || 9 ||
saṃkalpāyāśca saṃkalpastathā putraḥ prakīrtitaḥ
muhūrtāśca muhūrtāyāḥ sādhyāḥ sādhyāsutāḥ smṛtāḥ || 10 ||
manormanuśca prāṇaśca naroṣā noca vīryavān
cittahāryo'yanaścaiva haṃso nārāyaṇastathā || 11 ||
vibhuścāpi prabhuścaiva sādhyā dvādaśa kīrtitāḥ
viśvāyāśca tathā putrā viśve devāḥ prakīrtitāḥ || 12 ||
kraturdakṣo vasuḥ satyaḥ kālakāmo munistathā
kurajo manujo vījo rocamānaśca te daśa || 13 ||
etāvaduktastava dharmavaṃśaḥ saṃkṣepataḥ pārthivavaṃśamukhya
vyāsena vaktuṃ na hi śakyamasti rājanvinā varṣaśatairanekaiḥ || 14 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe dharmavaṃśavarṇane dharmapravarānukīrtanaṃ nāma tryadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 203 ||

<204>

atha caturadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
matsya uvāca
etadvaṃśabhavā viprāḥ śrāddhe bhojyāḥ prayatnataḥ
pitṝṇāṃ vallabhaṃ yasmādeṣu śrāddhaṃ nareśvara || 1 ||
ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi pitṛbhiryāḥ prakīrtitāḥ
gāthāḥ pārthivaśārdūla kāmavadbhiḥ pure svake || 2 ||
api syātsa kule'smākaṃ yo no dadyājjalāñjalim
nadīṣu bahutoyāsu śītalāsu viśeṣataḥ || 3 ||
api syātsa kule'smākaṃ yaḥ śrāddhaṃ nityamācaret
payomūlaphalairbhakṣyaistilatoyena vā punaḥ || 4 ||
api syātsa kule'smākaṃ yo no dadyāttrayodaśīm
pāyasaṃ madhusarpirbhyāṃ varṣāsu ca maghāsu ca || 5 ||
api syātsa kule'smākaṃ khaḍragamāṃsena yaḥ sakṛt
śrāddhaṃ kuryātprayatnena kālaśākena vā punaḥ || 6 ||
kālaśākaṃ mahāśākaṃ madhu munyannameva ca
viṣāṇavarjā ye khaḍgā āsūryaṃ tadaśīmahi || 7 ||
gayāyāṃ darśane rāhoḥ khaḍgamāṃsena yoginām
bhojayetkaḥ kule'smākaṃ chāyāyāṃ kuṃjarasya ca || 8 ||
ākalpakālikī tṛptistenāsmākaṃ bhaviṣyati
