dātā sarveṣu lokeṣu kāmacāro bhaviṣyati || 9 ||
ābhūtasaṃplavaṃ kālaṃ nātra kāryā vicāraṇā
yadetatpañcakaṃ tasmādekenāpi vayaṃ sadā || 10 ||
tṛptiṃ prāpsyāma cānantāṃ kiṃ punaḥ sarvasaṃpadā
api syātsa kule'smākaṃ dadyātkṛṣṇājinaṃ ca yaḥ || 11 ||
api syātsa kule'smākaṃ kaścitpuruṣasattamaḥ
prasūyamānāṃ yo dhenuṃ dadyādbrāhmaṇapuṃgave || 12 ||
api syātsa kule'smākaṃ vṛṣabhaṃ yaḥ samutsṛjet
sarvavarṇaviśeṣeṇa śuklaṃ nīlaṃ vṛṣaṃ tathā || 13 ||
api syātsa kule'smākaṃ yaḥ kuryācchraddhayā'nvitaḥ
suvarṇadānaṃ godānaṃ pṛthivīdānameva ca || 14 ||
api syātsa kule'smākaṃ kaścitpuruṣasattamaḥ
kūpārāmataḍāgānāṃ vāpīnāṃ yaśca kārakaḥ || 15 ||
api syātsa kule'smākaṃ sarvabhāvena yo harim
prayāyāccharaṇaṃ viṣṇuṃ deveśaṃ madhusūdanam || 16 ||
api naḥ sa kule bhūyātkaścidvidvānvicakṣaṇaḥ
dharmaśāstrāṇi yo dadyādvidhinā viduṣāmapi || 17 ||
etāvaduktaṃ tava bhūmipāla śrāddhasya kalpaṃ munisaṃpradiṣṭam
pāpāpahaṃ puṇyavivardhanaṃ ca lokeṣu mukhyatvakaraṃ tathaiva || 18 ||
ityetāṃ pitṛgāthāṃ tu śrāddhakāle tu yaḥ pitṝn
śrāvayettasya pitaro labhante dattamakṣayam || 19 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe
pitṛgāthākīrtanaṃ nāma caturadhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 204 ||

<205>

atha pañcādhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
manuruvāca
prasūyamānā dātavyā dhenurbrāhmaṇapuṃgave
vidhinā kena dharmajña dānaṃ dadyācca kiṃ phalam || 1 ||
matsya uvāca
svarṇaśṛṅgīṃ raupyakhurāṃ muktālāṅgūla bhūṣitām
kāṃsyopadohanāṃ rājansavatsāṃ dvijapuṃgave || 2 ||
prasūyamānāṃ gāṃ dattvā mahatpuṇyaphalaṃ labhet
yāvadvatso yonigato yāvadgarbhaṃ na muñcati || 3 ||
tāvadvai pṛthivī jñeyā saśailavanakānanā
prasūyamānāṃ yo dadyāddhenuṃ dra viṇasaṃyutām || 4 ||
sasamudra guhā tena saśailavanakānanā
caturantā bhaveddattā pṛthivī nātra saṃśayaḥ || 5 ||
yāvanti dhenuromāṇi vatsasya ca narādhipa
tāvatsaṃkhyaṃ yugagaṇaṃ devaloke mahīyate || 6 ||
pitṝnpitāmahāṃścaiva tathaiva prapitāmahān
uddhariṣyatyasaṃdehaṃ narakādbhūridakṣiṇaḥ || 7 ||
ghṛtakṣīravahāḥ kulyā dadhipāyasakardamāḥ
yatra tatra gatistasya drumāścepsita kāmadāḥ
golokaḥ sulabhastasya brahmalokaśca pārthiva || 8 ||
striyaśca taṃ candra samānavaktrāḥ prataptajāmbūnadatulyarūpāḥ
mahānitambāstanuvṛttamadhyā bhajantyajasraṃ nalinābhanetrāḥ || 9 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe
dhenudānaṃ nāma pañcādhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 205 ||

<206>

atha ṣaḍadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
manuruvāca
kṛṣṇājinapradānasya vidhikālau mamānagha
brāhmaṇaṃ ca tathā'cakṣva tatra me saṃśayo mahān || 1 ||
matsya uvāca
vaiśākhī paurṇamāsī ca grahaṇe śaśisūryayoḥ
paurṇamāsī tu yā māghī hyāṣāḍhī kārtikī tathā || 2 ||
uttarāyaṇe ca dvādaśyāṃ tasyāṃ dattaṃ mahāphalam
āhitāgnirdvijo yastu taddeyaṃ tasya pārthiva || 3 ||
yathā yena vidhānena tanme nigadataḥ śṛṇu
gomayenopalipte tu śucau deśe narādhipa || 4 ||
ādāveva samāstīrya śobhanaṃ vastramāvikam
tataḥ saśṛṅgaṃ sakhuramāstaretkṛṣṇamārgakam || 5 ||
kartavyaṃ rukmaśṛṅgaṃ tadrau pyadantaṃ tathaiva ca
lāṅgūlaṃ mauktikairyuktaṃ tilacchannaṃ tathaiva ca || 6 ||
tilaiḥ supūritaṃ kṛtvā vāsasā''cchādayedbudhaḥ
suvarṇanābhaṃ tatkuryādalaṃ kuryādviśeṣataḥ || 7 ||
ratnairgandhairyathāśaktyā tasya dikṣu ca vinyaset
kāṃsyapātrāṇi catvāri teṣu dadyādyathākramam
mṛnmayeṣu ca pātreṣu pūrvādiṣu yathākramam
ghṛtaṃ kṣīraṃ dadhi kṣaudra mevaṃ dadyādyathāvidhi || 9 ||
campakasya tathā śākhāmavraṇaṃ kumbhameva ca
bāhyopasthānakaṃ kṛtvā śubhacito niveśayet || 10 ||
sūkṣmavastraṃ śubhaṃ pītaṃ mārjanārthaṃ prayojayet
tathā dhātumayaṃ pātraṃ pādayostasya dāpayet || 11 ||
yāni kāni ca pāpāni mayā lobhātkṛtāni vai
lohapātrādidānena praṇaśyantu mamā''śu vai || 12 ||
tilapūrṇaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā vāmapāde niveśayet
yāni kāni ca pāpāni karmotthāni kṛtāni ca || 13 ||
kāṃsyapātrapradānena tāni naśyantu me sadā
madhupūrṇaṃ tu tatkṛtvā pāde vai dakṣiṇe nyaset || 14 ||
parāpavādapaiśunyādvṛthā māṃsasya bhakṣaṇāt
tatrotthitaṃ ca me pāpaṃ tāmrapātrātpraṇaśyatu || 15 ||
kanyānṛtādgavāṃ caiva paradārābhimarṣaṇāt
raupyapātrapradānāddhi kṣipraṃ nāśaṃ prayātu me || 16 ||
ūrdhvapāde tvime kāryaṃ tāmrasya rajatasya ca
janmāntarasahasreṣu kṛtaṃ pāpaṃ kubuddhinā || 17 ||
suvarṇapātradānāttu nāśayā''śu janārdana
hemamuktā vidrumaṃ ca dāḍimaṃ bījapūrakam || 18 ||
praśastapātre śravaṇe khure śṛṅgāṭakāni ca
evaṃ kṛtvā yathoktena sarvaśākaphalāni ca || 19 ||
tatpratigrahavidvidvānāhitāgnirdvijottamaḥ
snāto vastrayugacchannaḥ svaśaktyā cāpyalaṃ kṛtaḥ || 20 ||
pratigrahaśca tasyoktaḥ pucchadeśe mahīpate
tata evaṃ samīpe tu mantramenamudīrayet || 21 ||
kṛṣṇājineti kṛṣṇānhiraṇyaṃ madhusarpiṣī
dadāti yastu viprāya sarvaṃ tarati duṣkṛtam || 22 ||
yastu kṛṣṇājinaṃ dadyātsakhuraṃ śṛṅgasaṃyutam
tilaiḥ pracchādya vāsobhiḥ sarvavastrairalaṃkṛtam || 23 ||
vaiśākhyāṃ paurṇamāsyāṃ tu viśākhāyāṃ viśeṣataḥ
sasamudra guhā tena saśailavanakānanā || 24 ||
saptadvīpānvitā dattā pṛthivī nātra saṃśayaḥ
kṛṣṇakṛṣṇāṅgalo devaḥ kṛṣṇājina namo'stu te || 25 ||
suvarṇadānāttvaddānāddhutapāpasya prīyatām
trayastriṃśatsurāṇāṃ tvamādhāratve vyavasthitaḥ || 26 ||
kṛṣṇo'si mūrtimānsākṣātkṛṣṇājina namo'stu te
suvarṇanābhikaṃ dadyātprīyatāṃ vṛṣabhadhvajaḥ || 27 ||
kṛṣṇaḥ kṛṣṇagalo devaḥ kṛṣṇājinadharastathā
taddānāddhūtapāpasya prīyatāṃ vṛṣabhadhvajaḥ || 28 ||
anena vidhinā dattvā yathāvatkṛṣṇamārgakam
na spṛśyo'sau dvijo rājaṃścitiyūpasamo hi saḥ || 29 ||
taṃ dāne śrāddhakāle ca dūrataḥ parivarjayet
svagṛhātpreṣya taṃ vipraṃ maṅgalasnānamācaret || 30 ||
pūrṇakumbhena rājendra śākhayā campakasya tu
kṛtvā''cāryaśca kalaśaṃ mantreṇānena mūrdhani || 31 ||
āpyāyasva samudra jyeṣṭhā ṛcā saṃsnāpya ṣoḍaśa
ahate vāsasī vīta ācāntaḥ śucitāmiyāt || 32 ||
tadvāsaḥ kumbhahitaṃ nītvā kṣepyaṃ catuṣpathe
tato maṇḍalamāviśetkṛtvā devānpradakṣiṇam || 33 ||
pīte vṛtte sapatnīkaṃ mārjayedyājyakaṃ dvijaḥ
mārjayenmuktikāmaṃ tu brāhmaṇena ghaṭena vai || 34 ||
śrīkāmaṃ vaiṣṇaveneha kalaśena tu pārthiva
rājyakāmaṃ tathā mūrdhni aindre ṇa kalaśena tu || 35 ||
dra vyapratāpakāmaṃ tu āgneyaghaṭavāriṇā
mṛtyuṃjayavidhānāya yāmyena kalaśena tu || 36 ||
tatastu tilakaṃ kāryaṃ brāhmaṇebhyastu dakṣiṇām
dattvā tatkarmasiddhyarthaṃ grāhyā''śīstu viśeṣataḥ || 37 ||
kṛtenānena yā tuṣṭirna sā śaktyā surairapi
vaktuṃ hi nṛpatiśreṣṭha tathā'pyuddeśataḥ śṛṇu || 38 ||
samagrabhūmidānasya phalaṃ prāpnotyasaṃśayam
sarvān lokāṃśca jayati kāmacārī vihaṅgavat || 39 ||
ābhūtasaṃplavaṃ tāvatsvargamāpnotyasaṃśayam
na pitā putramaraṇaṃ viyogaṃ bhāryayā saha
dhanadeśaparityāgaṃ na caivehā''pnuyātkvacit || 40 ||
kṛṣṇopsitaṃ kṛṣṇamṛgasya carma dattvā dvijendrā ya samāhitātmā
yathoktametanmaraṇaṃ na śocetprāpnotyabhīṣṭaṃ manasaḥ phalaṃ tat || 41 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe kṛṣṇājivapradānaṃ nāma ṣaḍadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 206 ||

<207>

atha saptādhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
manuruvāca
bhagavañchrotumicchāmi vṛṣabhasya tu lakṣaṇam
vṛṣotsargavidhiṃ caiva tathā puṇyaphalaṃ mahat || 1 ||
matsya uvāca
dhenumādau parīkṣeta suśīlāṃ ca guṇānvitām
avyaṅgāmaparikliṣṭāṃ jīvavatsāmarogiṇīm || 2 ||
snigdhavarṇāṃ snigdhakhurāṃ snigdhaśṛṅgīṃ tathaiva ca
manoharākṛtiṃ saumyāṃ supramāṇāmanuddhatām || 3 ||
āvartairdakṣiṇāvartairyuktāṃ dakṣiṇatastathā
vāmāvartairvāmataśca vistīrṇajaghanāṃ tathā || 4 ||
mṛdusaṃhatatāmroṣṭhīṃ raktagrīvāsuśobhitām
aśyāmadīrghā sphuṭitā raktajihvā tathā ca yā || 5 ||
visrāvāmalanetrā ca śaphairaviralairdṛḍhaiḥ
vaidūryamadhuvarṇaiśca jalabudbudasaṃnibhaiḥ || 6 ||
raktasnidhaiśca nayanaistathā raktakanīnikaiḥ
saptacaturdaśadantā tathā vā śyāmatālukā || 7 ||
ṣaḍunnatā suṣārśvoruḥ pṛthupañcasamāyatā
aṣṭāyataśirogrīvā yā rājansā sulakṣaṇāḥ || 8 ||
manuruvāca
ṣaḍunnatāḥ ke bhagavanke ca pañca samāyatāḥ
āyatāśca tathaivāṣṭau dhenūnāṃ ke śubhāvahāḥ || 9 ||
matsya uvāca
uraḥ pṛṣṭhaṃ śiraḥ kukṣī śroṇī ca vasudhādhipa
ṣaḍunnatāni dhenūnāṃ pūjayanti vicakṣaṇāḥ || 10 ||
karṇau netre lalāṭaṃ ca pañca bhāskaranandana
samāyatāni śasyante pucchaṃ sāsnā ca sakthinī || 11 ||
catvāraśca stanā rājañjñeyā hyaṣṭau manīṣibhiḥ
śiro grīvāyatāścaite bhūmipāla daśa smṛtāḥ || 12 ||
tasyāḥ sutaṃ parīkṣeta vṛṣabhaṃ lakṣaṇānvitam
unnataskandhakakudamṛjulāṅgūlakambalam || 13 ||
mahākaṭitaṭaskandhaṃ vaidūryamaṇilocanam
pravālagarbhaśṛṅgāgraṃ sudīrghapṛthuvāladhim || 14 ||
navāṣṭādaśasaṃkhyairvā tīkṣṇāgrairdaśanaiḥ śubhaiḥ
mallikākṣaśca moktavyo gṛhe'pi dhanadhānyadaḥ || 15 ||
varṇatastāmrakapilo brāhmaṇasya praśasyate
śveto raktaśca kṛṣṇaśca gauraḥ pāṭala eva ca || 16 ||
madri ṇastāmrapṛṣṭhaśca śabalaḥ pañcavālakaiḥ
pṛthukarṇo mahāskandhaḥ ślakṣṇaromā ca yo bhavet
raktākṣaḥ kapilo yaśca raktaśṛṅgatalo bhavet || 17 ||
śvetodaraḥ kṛṣṇapārśvo brāhmaṇasya tu śasyate
snigdho raktena varṇena kṣatriyasya praśasyate || 18 ||
kāñcanābhena vaiśyasya kṛṣṇenāpyantyajanmanaḥ
yasya prāgāyate śṛṅge bhrūmukhābhimukhe sadā || 19 ||
sarveṣāmeva varṇānāṃ sarvaḥ sarvārthasādhakaḥ
mārjārapādaḥ kapilo dhanyaḥ kapilapiṅgalaḥ || 20 ||
śveto mārjārapādastu dhanyo maṇinibhekṣaṇaḥ
karaṭaḥ piṅgalaścaiva śvetapādastathaiva ca || 21 ||
sarvapādasito yaśca dvipādaśveta eva ca
kapilañjalanibho dhanyastathā tittirisaṃnibhaḥ || 22 ||
ākarṇamūlaṃ śvetaṃ tu mukhaṃ yasya prakāśate
nandīmukhaḥ sa vijñeyo raktavarṇo viśeṣataḥ || 23 ||
śvetaṃ tu jaṭharaṃ yasya bhavetpṛṣṭhaṃ ca gopateḥ
vṛṣabhaḥ sa samudrā kṣaḥ satataṃ kulavardhanaḥ || 24 ||
mallikāpuṣpacitraśca dhanyo bhavati puṃgavaḥ
kamalairmaṇḍalaiścāpi citro bhavati bhāgyadaḥ || 25 ||
atasīpuṣpavarṇaśca tathā dhanyataraḥ smṛtaḥ
ete dhanyāstathā'dhanyānkīrtayiṣyāmi te nṛpa || 26 ||
kṛṣṇatālvoṣṭhavadanā rūkṣaśṛṅgaśaphāśca ye
avyaktavarṇā hrasvāśca vyāghrasiṃhanibhāśca ye || 27 ||
dhvāṅkṣagṛdhrasavarṇāśca tathā mūṣakasaṃnibhāḥ
kuṇṭhāḥ kāṇāstathā khañjāḥ kekarākṣāstathaiva ca || 28 ||
viṣamaśvetapādāśca udbhrāntanayanāstathā
naite vṛṣāḥ pramoktavyā na ca dhāryāstathā gṛhe || 29 ||
bhoktavyānāṃ ca dhāryāṇāṃ bhūyo vakṣyāmi lakṣaṇam
svastikākāraśṛṅgāśca tathā meghaughanisvanāḥ || 30 ||
mahāpramāṇāśca tathā mattamātaṅgagāminaḥ
mahoraskā mahocchrāyā mahābalaparākramāḥ
śiraḥkarṇau lalāṭaṃ ca vāladhiścaraṇāstathā || 31 ||
netre pārśve ca kṛṣṇāni śasyante candra bhāsinām
śvetānyetāni śasyante kṛṣṇasya tu viśeṣataḥ
bhūmau karṣati lāṅgulaṃ pralambasthūlavāladhiḥ
purastādudyato nīlo vṛṣabhaśca praśasyate || 33 ||
śaktidhvajapatākāḍhyā yeṣāṃ rājī virājate
anaḍvāhastu te dhanyāścitrasiddhijayāvahāḥ || 34 ||
pradakṣiṇaṃ nivartante svayaṃ ye vinivartitāḥ
samunnataśirogrīvā dhanyāste yūthavardhanāḥ || 35 ||
raktaśṛṅgāgranayanaḥ śvetavarṇo bhavedyadi
śaphaiḥ pravālasadṛśairnāsti dhanyatarastataḥ || 36 ||
ete dhāryāḥ prayatnena moktavyā yadi vā vṛṣāḥ
dhāritāśca tathā muktā dhanadhānyapravardhanāḥ || 37 ||
caraṇāni mukhaṃ pucchaṃ yasya śvetāni gopateḥ
lākṣārasasavarṇaśca taṃ nīlamiti nirdiśet || 38 ||
vṛṣa eva sa moktavyo na saṃdhāryo gṛhe bhavet
tadarthameṣā carati loke gāthā purātanī || 39 ||
eṣṭavyā bahavaḥ putrā yadyeko'pi gayāṃ vrajet
gaurīṃ cāpyudvahetkanyāṃ nīlaṃ vā vṛṣamutsṛjet || 40 ||
evaṃ vṛṣaṃ lakṣaṇasaṃprayuktaṃ gṛhodbhavaṃ krītamathāpi rājan
muktvā na śocenmaraṇaṃ mahātmā mokṣaṃ gataścāhamato'bhidhāsye || 41 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe vṛṣabhalakṣaṇaṃ nāma saptādhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 207 ||

<208>

athāṣṭādhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
sūta uvāca
tataḥ sa rājā deveśaṃ papracchābhitavikramaḥ
pativratānāṃ māhātmyaṃ tatsaṃbaddhāṃ kathāmapi || 1 ||
manuruvāca
pativratānāṃ kā śreṣṭhā kayā mṛtyuḥ parājitaḥ
nāmasaṃkīrtanaṃ kasyāḥ kīrtanīyaṃ sadā naraiḥ
sarvapāpakṣayakaramidānīṃ kathayasva me || 2 ||
matsya uvāca
vailomyaṃ dharmarājo'pi nā''caratyatha yoṣitām
pativratānāṃ dharmajña pūjyāstasyāpi tāḥ sadā || 3 ||
atra te varṇayiṣyāmi kathāṃ pāpapraṇāśinīm
yathā vimokṣito bhartā mṛtyupāśagataḥ striyā || 4 ||
madre ṣu śākalo rājā babhūvāśvapatiḥ purā
aputrastapyamāno'sau putrārthī sarvakāmadām || 5 ||
ārādhayati sāvitrīṃ lakṣito'sau dvijottamaiḥ
siddhārthakairhūyamānāṃ sāvitrīṃ pratyahaṃ dvijaiḥ || 6 ||
śatasaṃkhyaiścaturthyāṃ tu māsāddaśadine gate
kāle tu darśayāmāsa svāṃ tanuṃ manujeśvaram || 7 ||
sāvitryuvāca
rājanbhakto'si me nityaṃ dāsyāmi tvāṃ sutāṃ sadā
tāṃ dattāṃ matprasādena putrīṃ prāpsyasi śobhanām || 8 ||
etāvaduktvā sā rājñaḥ praṇatasyaiva pārthiva
jagāmādarśanaṃ devī khe tathā nṛpa cañcalā || 9 ||
mālatī nāma tasyā''sīdrā jñaḥ patnī pativratā
suṣuve tanayāṃ kāle sāvitrīmiva rūpataḥ || 10 ||
sāvitryā hutayā dattā tadrū pasadṛśī tathā
sāvitrī ca bhavatveṣā jagāda nṛpatirdvijān || 11 ||
nāmākurvandvijaśreṣṭhāḥ sāvitrīti nṛpottama
kālena yauvanaṃ prāptā dadau satyavate pitā
nāradastu tataḥ prāha rājānaṃ dīptatejasam || 12 ||
saṃvatsareṇa kṣīṇāyurbhaviṣyati nṛpātmajaḥ
sakṛtkanyāḥ pradīyante cintayitvā narādhipaḥ || 13 ||
tathā'pi pradadau kanyāṃ dyumatsenātmaje śubhe
sāvitryapi ca bhartāramāsādya nṛpamandire || 14 ||
nāradasya tu vākyena dūyamānena cetasā
śuśrūṣāṃ paramāṃ cakre bhartṛśvaśurayorvane || 15 ||
rājyādbhraṣṭaḥ sabhāryastu naṣṭacakṣurnarādhipaḥ
na tutoṣa samāsādya rājaputrīṃ tathā snuṣām || 16 ||
caturthe'hani martavyaṃ tathā satyavatā dvijāḥ
śvaśureṇābhyanujñātā tadā rājasutā'pi sā || 17 ||
cakre trirātraṃ dharmajñā prāpte tasmiṃstadā dine
dārupuṣpaphalāhārī satyavāṃstu yayau vanam || 18 ||
śvaśureṇābhyanujñātā yācanābhaṅgabhīruṇā
sāvitryapi jagāmā''rtā saha bhartrā mahadvanam || 19 ||
cetasā dūyamānena gūhamānā mahadbhayam
vane papraccha bhartāraṃ drumāṃścāsadṛśāṃstathā || 20 ||
jāśvāsayāmāsa sa rājaputrīṃ klāntāṃ vane padmaviśālanetrām
saṃdarśanenātha drumadvijānāṃ tathā mṛgāṇāṃ vipine nṛvīraḥ || 21 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe sāvitryupākhyāne sāvitrīvanapraveśo nāmāṣṭādhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 208 ||

<209>

atha navādhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
satyavānuvāca
vane'smiñśādbalākīrṇe sahakāraṃ manoharam
netraghrāṇasukhaṃ paśya vasante rativardhanam || 1 ||
vane'pyaśokaṃ dṛṣṭvainaṃ rāgavantaṃ supuṣpitam
vasanto hasatīvāyaṃ māmevā''yatalocane || 2 ||
dakṣiṇe dakṣiṇenaitāṃ paśya ramyāṃ vanasthalīm
puṣpitaiḥ kiṃśukairyuktāṃ jvalitānalasaprabhaiḥ || 3 ||
sugandhikusumāmodo vanarājivinirgataḥ
karoti vāyurdākṣiṇyamāvayoḥ klamanāśanam || 4 ||
paścimena viśālākṣi karṇikāraiḥ supuṣpitaiḥ
kāñcanena vibhātyeṣā vanarājī manoramā || 5 ||
atimuktalatājālaruddhamārgā vanasthalī
ramyā sā cārusarvāṅgi kusumotkarabhūṣaṇā || 6 ||
madhumattālijhaṅkāravyājena varavarṇini
cāpākṛṣṭiṃ karotīva kāmaḥ pānthajighāṃsayā || 7 ||
phalāsvādalasadvaktrapuṃskokilavināditā
vibhāti cārutilakā tvamivaiṣā vanasthalī || 8 ||
kokilaścataśikhare mañjarīreṇupiñjaraḥ
gaditairvyaktatāṃ yāti kulīnaśceṣṭitairiva || 9 ||
puṣpareṇuviliptāṅgīṃ priyāmanusaranvane
kusumaṃ kusumaṃ yāti kūjankāmī śilīmukhaḥ || 10 ||
mañjarīṃ sahakārasya kāntā cañcvagrakhaṇḍitām
svadate bahupuṣpe'pi puṃskokilayuvā vane || 11 ||
kākaḥ prasūtāṃ vṛkṣāgre svāmekāgreṇa cañcunā
kākīṃ saṃbhāvayatyeṣa pakṣācchāditaputrakām || 12 ||
bhūbhāgaṃ nimnamāsādya dayitāsahito yuvā
nā''hāramapi cā''datte kāmākrāntaḥ kapiñjalaḥ || 13 ||
kalaviṅkastu ramayanpriyotsaṅgaṃ samāsthitaḥ
muhurmuhurviśālākṣi utkaṇṭhayati kāminaḥ || 14 ||
vṛkṣaśākhāṃ samārūḍhaḥ śuko'yaṃ saha bhāryayā
kareṇa lambayañchākhāṃ karoti saphalāmiva || 15 ||
vane'tra piśitāsvādatṛpto nidrā mupāgataḥ
śete siṃhayuvā kāntā caraṇāntaragāminī || 16 ||
vyāghrayormithunaṃ paśya śailakaṃdarasaṃsthitam
yayornetraprabhāloke guhā bhinneva lakṣyate || 17 ||
ayaṃ dvīpī priyāṃ leḍhi jihvāgreṇa punaḥpunaḥ
prītimāyāti ca tayā lihyamānaḥ svakāntayā || 18 ||
utsaṅgakṛtamūrdhānaṃ nidrā pahṛtacetasam
jantūddharaṇataḥ kāntaṃ sukhayatyeva vānarī || 19 ||
bhūmau nipatitāṃ rāmāṃ mārjāro darśitodarīm
nakhairdantairdaśatyeṣa na ca pīḍayate tathā || 20 ||
śaśakaḥ śaśakī cobhe saṃsupte pīḍite ime
saṃlīnagātracaraṇe karṇairvyaktimupāgate || 21 ||
snātvā sarasi padmāḍhye nāgastu madanapriyaḥ
saṃbhāvayati tanvaṅgi mṛṇālakavalaiḥ priyām || 22 ||
kāntaprothasamutthānaiḥ kāntamārgānugāminī
karoti kavalaṃ mustairvarāhī potakānugā || 23 ||
dṛḍhāṅgasaṃdhirmahiṣaḥ kardamāktatanurvane
anuvrajati dhāvantīṃ priyāmuddhatamutsukaḥ || 24 ||
paśya cārvaṅgi sāraṅgaṃ tvaṃ kaṭākṣavibhāvanaiḥ
sabhāryaṃ māṃ hi paśyantaṃ kautūhalasamanvitam || 25 ||
paśya paścimapādena rohī kaṇḍūyate mukham
snehārdra bhāvātkarṣantī bhartāraṃ śṛṅgakoṭinā || 26 ||
drā gimāṃ camarīṃ paśya sitavālāmagacchatīm
anvāste camaraḥ kāmī māṃ ca paśyati garvitaḥ || 27 ||
ātape gavayaṃ paśya prahṛṣṭaṃ bhāryayā saha
romanthanaṃ prakurvāṇaṃ kākaṃ kakudi vārayan || 28 ||
paśyājaṃ bhāryayā sārdhaṃ nyastāgracaraṇadvayam
vipule badarīskandhe badarāśanakāmyayā || 29 ||
haṃsaṃ sabhāryaṃ sarasi vicarantaṃ sunirmalam
sumuktasyendubimbasya paśya vai śriyamudvahan || 30 ||
sabhāryaścakravāko'yaṃ kamalākaramadhyagaḥ
karoti padminīṃ kāntāṃ supuṣpāmiva sundari || 31 ||
mayā phaloccayaḥ subhru tvayā puṣpoccayaḥ kṛtaḥ
indhanaṃ na kṛtaṃ subhru tatkariṣyāmi sāṃpratam || 32 ||
tvamasya sarasastīre drumacchāyāṃ samāśritā
kṣaṇamātraṃ pratīkṣasva viśramasva ca bhāmini || 33 ||
sāvitryuvāca
evametatkariṣyāmi mama dṛṣṭipathastvayā
dūraṃ kānta na kartavyo bibhemi gahane vane || 34 ||
matsya uvāca
tataḥ sa kāṣṭhāni cakāra tasminvane tadā rājasutāsamakṣam
tasyā hyadūre sarasastadānīṃ mene ca sā taṃ mṛtameva rājan || 35 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe sāvitryupākhyāne vanadarśanaṃ nāma navādhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 209 ||

<210>

atha daśādhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
matsya uvāca
tasya pāṭayataḥ kāṣṭhaṃ jajñe śirasi vedanā
sa vedanārtaḥ saṃgamya bhāryāṃ vacanamabravīt || 1 ||
āyāsena mamānena jātā śirasi vedanā
tamaśca praviśāmīva na ca jānāmi kiṃcana || 2 ||
tvadutsaṅge śiraḥ kṛtvā svaptumicchāmi sāṃpratam
rājaputrīmevamuktvā tadā suṣvāpa pārthiva || 3 ||
tadutsaṅge śiraḥ kṛtvā nidra yā''vilalocanaḥ
pativratā mahābhāgā tataḥ sā rājakanyakā || 4 ||
dadarśa dharmarājaṃ tu svayaṃ taṃ deśamāgatam
nīlotpaladalaśyāmaṃ pītāmbaradharaṃ prabhum || 5 ||
vidyullatānibaddhāṅgaṃ satoyamiva toyadam
kirīṭenārkavarṇena kuṇḍalābhyāṃ virājitam || 6 ||
hārabhārārpitoraskaṃ tathā'ṅgadavibhūṣitam
tathā'nugamyamānaṃ ca kālena saha mṛtyunā || 7 ||
sa tu saṃprāpya taṃ deśaṃ dehātsatyavatastadā
aṅguṣṭhamātraṃ puruṣaṃ pāśabaddhaṃ vaśaṃ gatam || 8 ||
ākṛṣya dakṣiṇāmāśāṃ prayayau satvaraṃ tadā
sāvitryapi varārohā dṛṣṭvā taṃ gatajīvitam || 9 ||
anuvavrāja gacchantaṃ dharmarājamatandri tā
kṛtāñjaliruvācātha hṛdayena pravepatā || 10 ||
imaṃ lokaṃ mātṛbhaktyā pitṛbhaktyā tu madhyamam
guruśuśrūṣayā caiva brahmalokaṃ samaśnute || 11 ||
sarve tasyā''dṛtā dharmā yasyaite traya ādṛtāḥ
anādṛtāstu yasyaite sarvāstasyāphalāḥ kriyāḥ || 12 ||
yāvattrayaste jīveyustāvannānyaṃ samācaret
teṣāṃ ca nityaṃ śuśrūṣāṃ kuryātpriyahite rataḥ || 13 ||
teṣāmanuparodhena pāratantryaṃ yadā caret
tattannivedayettebhyo manovacanakarmabhiḥ
triṣvapyeteṣu kṛtyaṃ hi puruṣasya samasyate || 14 ||
yama uvāca
kṛtena kāmena nivartayā''śu dharmo na tebhyo'pi hi ucyate ca
mamoparodhastava ca klamaḥ syāttathā'dhunā tena tava bravīmi || 15 ||
gurupūjāratirbhartā tvaṃ ca sādhvī pativratā
vinivartasva dharmajñe glānirbhavati te'dhunā || 16 ||
sāvitryuvāca
patirhi daivataṃ strīṇāṃ patireva parāyaṇam
anugamyaḥ striyā sādhvyā patiḥ prāṇadhaneśvaraḥ || 17 ||
mitaṃ dadāti hi pitā mitaṃ bhrātā mitaṃ sutaḥ
amitasya pradātāraṃ bhartāraṃ kā na pūjayet || 18 ||
nīyate yatra bhartā me svayaṃ vā yatra gacchati
mayā'pi tatra gantavyaṃ yathāśakti surottama || 19 ||
patimādāya gacchantamanugantumahaṃ yadā
tvāṃ deva na hi śakṣyāmi tadā tyakṣyāmi jīvitam || 20 ||
manasvinī tu yā kācidvaidhavyākṣaradūpitā
muhūrtamapi jīveta maṇḍanārhā hyamaṇḍitā || 21 ||
yama uvāca
pativrate mahābhāge parituṣṭo'smi te śubhe
vinā satyavataḥ prāṇairvaraṃ varaya mā ciram || 22 ||
sāvitryuvāca
vinaṣṭacakṣuṣo rājyaṃ cakṣuṣā saha kāraya
cyutarājyasya dharmajña śvaśurasya mahātmanaḥ || 23 ||
yama uvāca
dūre pathe gaccha nivarta bhadre bhaviṣyatīdaṃ sakalaṃ tvayoktam
mamoparodhastava ca klamaḥ syāttathā'dhunā tena tava bravīmi || 24 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe sāvitryupākhyāne prathamavaralābho nāma daśādhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 210 ||

<211>

athaikādaśādhidadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
sāvitryuvāca
kutaḥ klamaḥ kuto duḥkhaṃ sadbhiḥ saha samāgame
satāṃ tasmānna me glānistvatsamīpe surottama || 1 ||
sādhūnāṃ vā'pyasādhūnāṃ santa eva sadā gatiḥ
naivāsatāṃ naiva satāmasanto naivamātmanaḥ || 2 ||
viṣāgnisarpaśastrebhyo na tathā jāyate bhayam
akāraṇajagadvairikhalebhyo jāyate yathā || 3 ||
santaḥ prāṇānapi tyaktvā parārthaṃ kurvate yathā
tathā'santo'pi saṃtyajya parapīḍāsu tatparāḥ || 4 ||
tyajatyasūnayaṃ lokastṛṇavadyasya kāraṇāt
paropaghātaśaktāstaṃ paralokaṃ tathā'sataḥ || 5 ||
nikāyeṣu nikāyeṣu tathā brahmā jagadguruḥ
asatāmupaghātāya rājānaṃ jñātavānsvayam || 6 ||
narānparīkṣayedrā jā sādhūnsaṃmānayetsadā
nigrahaṃ cāsatāṃ kuryātsa loke lokajittamaḥ || 7 ||
nigraheṇāsatāṃ rājā satāṃ ca paripālanāt
etāvadeva kartavyaṃ rājñā svargamabhīpsunā || 8 ||
rājakṛtyaṃ hi lokeṣu nāstyanyajjagatīpate
asatāṃ nigrahādeva satāṃ ca paripālanāt || 9 ||
rajabhiścāpyaśāstānāmasatāṃ śāsitā bhavān
tena tvamadhiko devo devebhyaḥ pratibhāsi me || 10 ||
jagattu dhāryate sadbhiḥ satāmagryastathā bhavān
tena tvāmanuyāntyā me klamo deva na vidyate || 11 ||
yama uvāca
tuṣṭo'smi te viśālākṣi vacanairdharmasaṃgataiḥ
vinā satyavataḥ prāṇādvaraṃ varaya mā ciram || 12 ||
sāvitryuvāca
sahodarāṇāṃ bhrātṝṇāṃ kāmayāmi śataṃ vibho
janapatyaḥ pitā prītiṃ putralābhātprayātu me || 13 ||
tāmuvāca yamo gaccha yathāgatamanindite
aurdhvadehikakāryeṣu yatnaṃ bhartuḥ samācara || 14 ||
nānugantumayaṃ śaktyastvayā lokāntaraṃ gataḥ
pativratā'si tena tvaṃ muhūrtaṃ mama yāsyasi || 15 ||
guruśuśrūṣaṇādbhadre tathā satyavatā mahat
puṇyaṃ samarjitaṃ yena nayāmyenamahaṃ svayam || 16 ||
etāvadeva kartavyaṃ puruṣeṇa vijānatā
mātuḥ pituśca śuśrūṣā gurośca varavarṇini || 17 ||
toṣitaṃ trayametacca sadā satyavatā vane
pūjitaṃ vijitaḥ svargastvayā'nena ciraṃ śubhe || 18 ||
tapasā brahmacaryeṇa agniśuśrūṣayā śubhe
puruṣāḥ svargamāyānti guruśuśrūṣayā tathā || 19 ||
ācāryaśca pitā caiva mātā bhrātā ca pūrvajaḥ
na cai'tepyavamantavyā brāhmaṇena viśeṣataḥ || 20 ||
ācāryo brahmaṇo mūrtiḥ pitā mūrtiḥ prajāpateḥ
mātā pṛthivyā mūrtistu bhrātā vai mūrtirātmanaḥ || 21 ||
janmanā pitarau kleśaṃ sahete saṃbhave nṛṇām
na tasya niṣkṛtiḥ śakyā kartuṃ varṣaśatairapi || 22 ||
tayornityaṃ priyaṃ kuryādācāryasya tu sarvadā
teṣveva triṣu tuṣṭeṣu tapaḥ sarvaṃ samāpyate || 23 ||
teṣāṃ trayāṇāṃ śuśrūṣā paramaṃ tapa ucyate
na ca tairananujñāto dharmamanyaṃ samācaret || 24 ||
ta eva hi trayo lokāsta eva traya āśramāḥ
ta eva ca trayo vedāstathaivoktāstrayo'gnayaḥ || 25 ||
pitā vai gārhapatyo'gnirmātā dakṣiṇataḥ smṛtaḥ
gururāhavanīyaśca sā'gnitretā garīyasī || 26 ||
triṣu pramādyate naiṣu trīṃllokāñjayate gṛhī
dīpyamānaḥ svavapuṣā devavaddivi modate || 27 ||
kṛtena kāmena nivarta bhadre bhaviṣyatīdaṃ sakalaṃ tvayoktam
mamoparodhastava ca klamaḥ syāttathā'dhunā tena tava bravīmi || 28 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe sāvitryupākhyāne dvitīyavaralābho nāmaikādaśādhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 211 ||

<212>

atha dvādaśādhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
sāvitryuvāca
dharmārjane suraśreṣṭha kuto glāniḥ klamastathā
tvatpādamūlasevā ca paramaṃ dharmakāraṇam || 1 ||
dharmārjanaṃ tathā kāryaṃ puruṣeṇa vijānatā
tallābhaḥ sarvalābhebhyo yadā deva viśiṣyate || 2 ||
dharmaścārthaśca kāmaśca trivargo janmanaḥ phalam
dharmahīnasya kāmārthau vandhyāsutasamau prabho || 3 ||
dharmādarthastathā kāmo dharmāllokadvayaṃ tathā
dharma eko'nuyātyenaṃ yatrakvacanagāminam || 4 ||
śarīreṇa samaṃ nāśaṃ sarvamanyaddhi gacchati
eko hi jāyate jantureka eva vipadyate || 5 ||
dharmastamanu yātyeko na suhṛnna ca bāndhavāḥ
kriyā saubhāgyalāvaṇyaṃ sarvaṃ dharmeṇa labhyate || 6 ||
brahmendro pendra śarvenduyamārkāgnyanilāmbhasām
vasvaśvidhanadādyānāṃ ye lokāḥ sarvakāmadāḥ || 7 ||
dharmeṇa tānavāpnoti puruṣaḥ puruṣāntaka
manoharāṇi dvīpāni varṣāṇi susukhāni ca || 8 ||
prayānti dharmeṇa narāstathaiva naragaṇḍikāḥ
nandanādīni mukhyāni devodyānāni yāni ca || 9 ||
tāni puṇyena labhyante nākapṛṣṭhaṃ tathā naraiḥ
vimānāni vicitrāṇi tathaivāpsarasaḥ śubhāḥ || 10 ||
taijasāni śarīrāṇi sadā puṇyavatāṃ phalam
rājyaṃ nṛpatipūjā ca kāmasiddhistathepsitā || 11 ||
saṃskārāṇi ca mukhyāni phalaṃ puṇyasya dṛśyate
rukmavaidūryadaṇḍāni caṇḍāṃśusadṛśāni ca || 12 ||
cāmarāṇi surādhyakṣa bhavanti śubhakarmaṇām
pūrṇendumaṇḍalābhena ratnāṃśukavikāśinā || 13 ||
dhāryatāṃ yāti cchatreṇa naraḥ puṇyena karmaṇā
jayaśaṅkhasvaraugheṇa sūtamāgadhaniḥsvanaiḥ || 14 ||
varāsanaṃ sabhṛṅgāraṃ phalaṃ puṇyasya karmaṇaḥ
varānnapānaṃ gītaṃ ca bhṛtyamālyānulepanam || 15 ||
ratnavastrāṇi mukhyāni phalaṃ puṇyasya karmaṇaḥ
rūpaudāryaguṇopetāḥ striyaścātimanoharāḥ || 16 ||
vāsāḥ prāsādapṛṣṭheṣu bhavanti śubhakarmiṇām
suvarṇakiṅkiṇīmiśracāmarāpīḍadhāriṇaḥ || 17 ||
vahanti turagā deva naraṃ puṇyena karmaṇā
haimakakṣaiśca mātaṅgaiścalatparvatasaṃnibhaiḥ || 18 ||
kheladbhiḥ pādavinyāsairyānti puṇyena karmaṇā
sarvakāmaprade devasarvāghaduritāpahe || 19 ||
vahanti bhaktiṃ puruṣāḥ sadā puṇyena karmaṇā
tasya dvārāṇi yajanaṃ tapo dānaṃ damaḥ kṣamā || 20 ||
brahmacaryaṃ tathā satyaṃ tīrthānusaraṇaṃ śubham
svādhyāyasevā sādhūnāṃ sahavāsaḥ surārcanam || 21 ||
gurūṇāṃ caiva śuśrūṣā brāhmaṇānāṃ ca pūjanam
indri yāṇāṃ jayaścaiva brahmacaryamamatsaram || 22 ||
tasmāddharmaḥ sadā kāryo nityameva vijānatā
na hi pratīkṣate mṛtyuḥ kṛtamasya na vā kṛtam || 23 ||
bāla eva careddharmamanityaṃ deva jīvitam
ko hi jānāti kasyādya mṛtyurevā''patiṣyati || 24 ||
paśyato'pyasya lokasya maraṇaṃ purataḥ sthitam
amarasyeva caritamatyāścaryaṃ surottama || 25 ||
yuvatvāpekṣayā bālo vṛddhatvāpekṣayā yuvā
mṛtyorutsaṅgamārūḍhaḥ sthaviraḥ kimapekṣate || 26 ||
tatrāpi vindatastrāṇaṃ mṛtyunā tasya kā gatiḥ
na bhayaṃ maraṇādeva prāṇināmabhayaṃ kvacit
tatrāpi nirbhayāḥ santaḥ sadā sukṛtakāriṇaḥ || 27 ||
yama uvāca
tuṣṭo'smi te viśālākṣi vacanairdharmasaṃgataiḥ
vinā satyavataḥ prāṇānvaraṃ varaya mā ciram || 28 ||
sāvitryuvāca
varayāmi tvayā dattaṃ putrāṇāṃ śatamaurasam
anapatyasya lokeṣu gatiḥ kila na vidyate || 29 ||
yama uvāca
kṛtena kāmena nivarta bhadre bhaviṣyatīdaṃ saphalaṃ yathoktam
mamoparodhastava ca klamaḥ syāttathā'dhunā tena tava bravīmi || 30 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe sāvitryupākhyāne tṛtīyavaralābho nāma dvādaśādhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 212 ||

<213>

atha trayodaśādhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
sāvitryuvāca
dharmādharmavidhānajña sarvadharmapravartaka
tvameva jagato nāthaḥ prajāsaṃyamano yamaḥ || 1 ||
karmaṇāmanurūpeṇa yasmādyamayase prajāḥ
tasmādvai procyase deva yama ityeva nāmataḥ || 2 ||
dharmeṇemāḥ prajāḥ sarvā yasmādra ñjayase prabho
tasmātte dharmarājeti nāma sadbhirnigadyate || 3 ||
sukṛtaṃ duṣkṛtaṃ cobhe purodhāya yadā janāḥ
tvatsakāśaṃ mṛtā yānti tasmāttvaṃ mṛtyurucyase || 4 ||
kālaṃ kalārdhaṃ kalayansarveṣāṃ tvaṃ hi tiṣṭhasi
tasmātkāleti te nāma procyate tattvadarśibhiḥ || 5 ||
sarveṣāmeva bhūtānāṃ yasmādantakaro mahān
tasmāttvamantakaḥ proktaḥ sarvadevairmahādyute || 6 ||
vivasvatastvaṃ tanayaḥ prathamaḥ parikīrtitaḥ
tasmādvaivasvato nāmnā sarvalokeṣu kathyase || 7 ||
āyuṣye karmaṇi kṣīṇe gṛhṇāsi prasamaṃ janam
tadā tvaṃ kathyase loke sarvaprāṇahareti vai || 8 ||
tava prasādāddeveśa trayīdharmo na naśyati
tava prasādāddeveśa dharme tiṣṭhanti jantavaḥ
tava prasādāddeveśa saṃkaro na prajāyate || 9 ||
satāṃ sadā gatirdeva tvameva parikīrtitaḥ
jagato'sya jagannātha maryādāparipālakaḥ || 10 ||
pāhi māṃ tridaśaśreṣṭha duḥkhitāṃ śaraṇāgatām
pitarau ca tathaivāsya rājaputrasya duḥkhitau || 11 ||
yama uvāca
stavena bhaktyā gharmajñe mayā tuṣṭena satyavān
tava bhartā vimukto'yaṃ labdhakāmā vrajābale || 12 ||
rājyaṃ kṛtvā tvayā sārdhaṃ vatsarāśītipañcakam
nākapṛṣṭhamathā''ruhya tridaśaiḥ saha raṃsyate || 13 ||
tvayi putraśataṃ cāpi satyavāñjanayiṣyati
te cāpi sarve rājānaḥ kṣattriyāstridaśopamāḥ || 14 ||
mukhyāstvannāma putrāste bhaviṣyanti hi śāśvatāḥ
pituśca te putraśataṃ bhavitā tava mātari || 15 ||
mālavyāṃ mālavā nāma śāśvatāḥ putrapautriṇaḥ
bhrātaraste bhaviṣyanti kṣattriyāstridaśopamāḥ || 16 ||
stotreṇānena dharmajñe kalyamutthāya yastu mām
kīrtayiṣyati tasyāpi dīrghamāyurbhaviṣyati || 17 ||
matsya uvāca
etāvaduktvā bhagavānyamastu pramucya taṃ rājasutaṃ mahātmā
adarśanaṃ tatra yamo jagāma kālena sārdhaṃ saha mṛtyunā ca || 18 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe sāvitryupākhyāne satyavajjīvitalābho nāma trayodaśādhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 213 ||

<214>

atha caturdaśādhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
matsya uvāca
sāvitrī tu tataḥ sādhvī jagāma varavarṇinī
pathā yathāgatenaiva yatrā''sītsatyavānmṛtaḥ || 1 ||
sā samāsādya bhartāraṃ tasyotsaṅgagataṃ śiraḥ
kṛtvā viveśa tanvaṅgī lambamāne divākare || 2 ||
satyavānapi nirmukto dharmarājācchanaiḥ śanaiḥ
unmīlayata netrābhyāṃ prāsphuracca narādhipa || 3 ||
tataḥ pratyāgataprāṇaḥ priyāṃ vacanamabravīt
kvāsau prayātaḥ puruṣo yo māmapyapakarṣati || 4 ||
na jānāmi varārohe kaścāsau puruṣaḥ śubhe
vane'smiṃścārusarvāṅgi suptasya ca dinaṃ gatam || 5 ||
upavāsapariśrāntā duḥkhitā bhavatī mayā
admaddurhṛdayenādya pitarau duḥkhitau tathā
dra ṣṭumicchāmyahaṃ subhru gamane tvaritā bhava || 6 ||
sāvitryuvāca
āditye'stamanuprāpte yadi te rucitaṃ prabho
āśramaṃ tu prayāsyāvaḥ śvaśurau hīnacakṣuṣau || 7 ||
yathāvṛttaṃ ca tatraiva tava vakṣyāmyathā''śrame
etāvaduktvā bhartāraṃ saha bhartrā tadā yayau || 8 ||
āsasādā''śramaṃ caiva saha bhartrā nṛpātmajā
etasminneva kāle tu labdhacakṣurmahīpatiḥ || 9 ||
dyumatsenaḥ sabhāryastu paryatapyata bhārgava
priyaputramapaśyanvai snuṣāṃ caivātha karśitām || 10 ||
āśvāsvamānastu tathā sa tu rājā tapodhanaiḥ
dadarśa putramāyāntaṃ snuṣayā saha kānanāt || 11 ||
sāvitrī tu varārohā saha satyavatā tadā
vavande tatra rājānaṃ sabhāryaṃ kṣattrapuṃgavam || 12 ||
pariṣvaktastadā pitrā satyavānrājanandanaḥ
abhivādya tataḥ sarvānvane tasmiṃstapodhanān || 13 ||
uvāsa tatra tāṃ rātrimṛṣibhiḥ sarvadharmavit
sābitryapi jagādātha yathāvṛttamaninditā || 14 ||
vrataṃ samāpayāmāsa tasyāmeva tadā niśi
tatastūryaistriyāmānte sasainyastasya bhūpateḥ || 15 ||
ājagāma janaḥ sarvo rājyārthāya nimantraṇe
vijñāpayāmāsa tadā tatra prakṛtiśāsanam || 16 ||
vicakṣuṣaste nṛpate yena rājyaṃ purā hṛtam
amātyaiḥ sa hato rājā bhavāṃstasminpure nṛpaḥ || 17 ||
etacchrutvā yayau rājā balena caturaṅgiṇā
lebhe ca sakalaṃ rājyaṃ dharmarājānmahātmanaḥ || 18 ||
bhrātṝṇāṃ tu śataṃ lebhe sāvitryapi varāṅganā
evaṃ pativratā sādhvī pitṛpakṣaṃ nṛpātmajā || 19 ||
ujjahāra varārohā bhartṛpakṣaṃ tathaiva ca
mocayāmāsa bhartāraṃ mṛtyupāśavaśaṃ gatam || 20 ||
tasmātsādhvyaḥ striyaḥ pūjyāḥ satataṃ devavannaraiḥ
tāsāṃ rājanprasādena dhāryate vai jagattrayam || 21 ||
tāsāṃ tu vākyaṃ bhavatīha mithyā na jātu lokeṣu carācareṣu
tasmātsadā tāḥ paripūjanīyāḥ kāmānsamagrānabhikāmayānaiḥ || 22 ||
yaścedaṃ śṛṇuyānnityaṃ sāvitryākhyānamuttamam
sa sukhī sarvasiddhārtho na duḥkhaṃ prāpnuyānnaraḥ
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe sāvitryupākhyānasamāptirnāma caturdaśādhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 214 ||

<215>

atha pañcadaśādhikadvitatamo'dhyāyaḥ
manuruvāca
rājño'bhiṣiktamātrasya kiṃ nu kṛtyatamaṃ bhavet
etanme sarvamācakṣva samyagvetti yato bhavān || 1 ||
matsya uvāca
abhiṣekārdra śirasā rājñā rājyāvalokinā
sahāyavaraṇaṃ kāryaṃ tatra rājyaṃ pratiṣṭhitam || 2 ||
yadapyalpataraṃ karma tadapyekena duṣkaram
puruṣeṇāsahāyena kimu rājyaṃ mahodayam || 3 ||
tasmātsahāyānvarayetkulīnānnṛpatiḥ svayam
śūrānkulīnajātīyānbalayuktāñchriyā'nvitān || 4 ||
rūpasattvaguṇopetānsajjanānkṣamayā'nvitān
kleśakṣamānmahotsāhāndharmajñāṃśca priyaṃvadān || 5 ||
hitopadeśakālajñānsvāmibhaktānyaśorthinaḥ
evaṃvidhānsahāyāṃśca śubhakarmasu yojayet || 6 ||
guṇahīnānapi tathā vijñāya nṛpatiḥ svayam
karmasveva niyuñjīta yathāyogyeṣu bhāgaśaḥ || 7 ||
kulīnaḥ śīlasaṃpanno dhanurvedaviśāradaḥ
hastiśikṣāśvaśikṣāsu kuśalaḥ ślakṣṇabhāṣitā || 8 ||
nimitte śakunajñāne vettā caiva cikitsite
kṛtajñaḥ karmaṇāṃ śūrastathā kleśasahastvṛjuḥ || 9 ||
vyūhatattvavidhānajñaḥ phalgusāraviśeṣavit
rājñā senāpatiḥ kāryo brāhmaṇaḥ kṣattriyo'thavā || 10 ||
prāṃśuḥ surūpo dakṣaśca priyavādī na coddhataḥ
cittagrāhaśca sarveṣāṃ pratīhāro vidhīyate || 11 ||
yathoktavādī dūtaḥ syāddeśabhāṣāviśāradaḥ
śaktaḥ kleśasaho vāggmī deśakālavibhāgavit || 12 ||
vijñātadeśakālaśca dūtaḥ sa syānmahīkṣitaḥ
vaktā nayasya yaḥ kāle sa dūto nṛpaterbhavet || 13 ||
prāṃśavo'śayanāḥ śūrā dṛḍhabhaktā nirākulāḥ
rājñā tu rakṣiṇaḥ kāryāḥ sadā kleśasahā hitāḥ || 14 ||
anāhāryo'nṛśaṃsaśca dṛḍhabhaktiśca pārthive
tāmbūladhārī bhavati nārī vā'pyatha tadguṇā || 15 ||
ṣāḍguṇyavidhitattvajño deśabhāṣāviśāradaḥ
sāṃdhivigrahikaḥ kāryo rājñā nayaviśāradaḥ || 16 ||
kṛtākṛtajño bhṛtyānāṃ jñeyaḥ syāddeśarakṣitā
āyavyayajño lokajño deśotpattiviśāradaḥ || 17 ||
surūpastaruṇaḥ prāṃśurdṛḍhabhaktiḥ kulocitaḥ
śūraḥ kleśasahaścaiva khaḍgadhārī prakīrtitaḥ || 18 ||
śūraśca balayuktaśca gajāśvarathakovidaḥ
dhanurdhārī bhavedrā jñaḥ sarvakleśasahaḥ śuciḥ || 19 ||
nimittaśakunajñānī hayaśikṣāviśāradaḥ
hayāyurvedatattvajño bhuvo bhāgavicakṣaṇaḥ || 20 ||
balābalajño rathinaḥ sthiradṛṣṭiḥ priyaṃvadaḥ
śūraśca kṛtavidyaśca sārathiḥ parikīrtitaḥ || 21 ||
anāhāryaḥ śucirdakṣaścikitsitavidāṃ varaḥ
sūpaśāstraviśeṣajñaḥ sūdādhyakṣaḥ praśasyate || 22 ||
sūdaśāstravidhānajñāḥ parābhedyāḥ kulodgatāḥ
sarve mahānase dhāryāḥ kṛttakeśanakhā narāḥ || 23 ||
samaḥ śatrau ca mitre ca dharmaśāstraviśāradaḥ
vipramukhyaḥ kulīnaśca dharmādhikaraṇo bhavet || 24 ||
kāryāstathāvidhāstatra dvijamukhyāḥ sabhāsadaḥ
sarvadeśākṣarābhijñaḥ sarvaśāstraviśāradaḥ || 25 ||
lekhakaḥ kathito rājñaḥ sarvādhikaraṇeṣu vai
śīrṣopetānsusaṃpūrṇānsamaśreṇigatānsamān || 26 ||
akṣarānvai likhedyastu lekhakaḥ sa varaḥ smṛtaḥ
upāyavākyakuśalaḥ sarvaśāstraviśāradaḥ || 27 ||
bahvarthavaktā cālpena lekhakaḥ syānnṛpottama
vākyābhiprāyatattvajño deśakālavibhāgavit || 28 ||
anāhārye bhavetsakto lekhakaḥ syānnṛpottama
puruṣāntaratattvajñāḥ prāṃśavaścāpyalolupāḥ || 29 ||
dharmādhikāriṇaḥ kāryā janā dānakarā narāḥ
evaṃvidhāstathā kāryā rājñā dauvārikā janāḥ || 30 ||
lohavastrājinādīnāṃ ratnānāṃ ca vidhānavit
vijñātā phalgusārāṇāmanāhāryaḥ śuciḥ sadā || 31 ||
nipuṇaścāpramattaśca dhanādhyakṣaḥ prakīrtitaḥ || 32 ||
āyadvāreṣu sarveṣu dhanādhyakṣasamā narāḥ
vyayadvāreṣu ca tathā kartavyāḥ pṛthivīkṣitā || 33 ||
paramparāgato yaḥ syādaṣṭāṅge sucikitsite
anāhāryaḥ sa vaidyaḥ syāddharmātmā ca kulodgataḥ || 34 ||
prāṇācāryaḥ sa vijñeyo vacanaṃ tasya bhūbhujā
rājanrājñā sadā kāryaṃ yathā kāryaṃ pṛthagjanaiḥ || 35 ||
hastiśikṣāvidhānajño vanajātiviśāradaḥ
kleśakṣamastathā rājño gajādhyakṣaḥ praśasyate || 36 ||
etaireva guṇairyuktaḥ sthaviraśca viśeṣataḥ
gajārohī narendra sya sarvakarmasu śasyate || 37 ||
hayaśikṣāvidhānajñaścikitsitaviśāradaḥ
aśvādhyakṣo mahībhartuḥ svāsanaśca praśasyate || 38 ||
anāhāryaśca śūraśca tathā prājñaḥ kulodgataḥ
durgādhyakṣaḥ smṛto rājña udyuktaḥ sarvakarmasu || 39 ||
vāstuvidyāvidhānajño laghuhasto jitaśramaḥ
dīrghadarśī ca śūraśca sthapatiḥ parikīrtitaḥ || 40 ||
yantramukte pāṇimukte vimukte muktadhārite
astrācāryo nirudvegaḥ kuśalaśca viśiṣyate || 41 ||
vṛddhaḥ kulodgataḥ sūktaḥ pitṛpaitāmahaḥ śuciḥ
rājñāmantaḥ purādhyakṣo vinītaśca tatheṣyate || 42 ||
evaṃ saptādhikāreṣu puruṣāḥ sapta te pure
parīkṣya cādhikāryaḥ syū rājñā sarveṣu karmasu
sthāpanājātitattvajñāḥ satataṃ pratijāgṛtāḥ || 43 ||
rājñaḥ syādāyudhāgāre dakṣaḥ karmasu codyataḥ
karmāṇyaparimeyāni rājño nṛpakulodvaha || 44 ||
uttamādhamamadhyāni buddhvā karmāṇi pārthivaḥ
uttamādhamamadhyeṣu puruṣeṣu niyojayet || 45 ||
narakarmaviparyāsādrā jā nāśamavāpnuyāt
niyogaṃ pauruṣaṃ bhaktiṃ śrutaṃ śauryaṃ kulaṃ nayam || 46 ||
jñātvā vṛttirvidhātavyā puruṣāṇāṃ mahīkṣitā
puruṣāntaravijñānatattvasāranibandhanāt || 47 ||
bahubhirmantrayetkāmaṃ rājā mantraṃ pṛthakpṛthak
mantriṇāmapi no kuryānmantrimantraprakāśanam || 48 ||
kvacinna kasya viśvāso bhavatīha sadā nṛṇām
niścayastu sadā mantre kārya ekena sūriṇā || 49 ||
bhavedvā niścayāvāptiḥ parabuddhyupajivanāt
ekasyaiva mahībharturbhūyaḥ kāryo viniścayaḥ || 50 ||
brāhmaṇānparyupāsīta trayīśāstrasuniścitān
nāsacchāstravato mūḍhāṃste hi lokasya kaṇṭakāḥ || 51 ||
vṛddhānhi nityaṃ seveta viprānvedavidaḥ śucīn
tebhyaḥ śikṣeta vinayaṃ vinītātmā ca nityaśaḥ
samagrāṃ vaśagāṃ kuryātpṛthivīṃ nātra saṃśayaḥ || 52 ||
bahavo'vinayādbhraṣṭā rājānaḥ saparicchadāḥ
vanasthāścaiva rājyāni vinayātpratipedire || 53 ||
traividyebhyastrayīṃ vidyāṃ daṇḍanītiṃ ca śāśvatīm
ānvīkṣikīṃ tvātmavidyāṃ vārtārambhāśca lokataḥ || 54 ||
indri yāṇāṃ jaye yogaṃ samātiṣṭheddivāniśam
jitendri yo hi śaknoti vaśe sthāpayituṃ prajāḥ || 55 ||
yajeta rājā bahubhiḥ kratubhiśca sadakṣiṇaiḥ
dharmārthaṃ caiva viprebhyo dadyādbhogāndhanāni ca || 56 ||
sāṃvatsarikamāptaiśca rāṣṭrādāhārayedbalim
syātsvādhyāyaparo loke varteta pitṛbandhuvat || 57 ||
āvṛttānāṃ gurukulāddvijānāṃ pūjako bhavet
nṛpāṇāmakṣayo hyeṣa vidhirbrāhmo'bhidhīyate || 58 ||
taṃ ca stenā na vā'mitrā haranti na vinaśyati
tasmādrā jñā vidhātavyo brāhmo vai hyakṣayo vidhiḥ || 59 ||
samottamādhamai rājā hyāhūya pālayetprajāḥ
na nivarteta saṅgrāmātkṣāttraṃ vratamanusmaran || 60 ||
saṅgrāmeṣvanivartitvaṃ prajānāṃ paripālanam
śuśrūṣā brāhmaṇānāṃ ca rājñāṃ niḥśreyasaṃ param || 61 ||
kṛpaṇānāthavṛddhānāṃ vidhavānāṃ ca yoṣitām
yogakṣemaṃ ca vṛttiṃ ca tathaiva parikalpayet || 62 ||
varṇāśramavyavasthānaṃ tathā kāryaṃ viśeṣataḥ
svadharmapracyutānrājā svadharme sthāpayettathā || 63 ||
āśrameṣu tathā kāryamannaṃ tailaṃ ca bhājanam
svayamevā''nayedrā jā satkṛtānnāvamānayet || 64 ||
tāpase sarvakāryāṇi rājyamātmānameva ca
nivedayetprayatnena devavacciramarcayet || 65 ||
dve prajñe veditavye ca ṛjvī vakrā ca mānavaiḥ
vakrāṃ jñātvā na seveta pratibādheta cā''gatām || 66 ||
nāsya cchidraṃ paro vidyādvidyācchidraṃ parasya tu
gūhetkūrma ivāṅgāni rakṣedvivaramātmanaḥ || 67 ||
na viśvasedaviśvaste viśvaste nātiviśvaset
viśvāsādbhayamutpannaṃ mūlānyapi nikṛntati || 68 ||
viśvāsayeccāpyaparaṃ tattvabhūtena hetunā
bakavaccintayedarthānsiṃhavacca parākramet || 69 ||
vṛkavaccāpi lumpeta śaśavacca vinikṣipet
dṛḍhaprahārī ca bhavettathā śūkaravannṛpaḥ || 70 ||
citrākāraśca śikhivaddṛḍhabhaktastathā śvavat
tathā ca madhurābhāṣī bhavetkokilavannṛpaḥ || 71 ||
kākaśaṅkī bhavennityamajñātavasatiṃ vaset
nāparīkṣitapūrvaṃ ca bhojanaṃ śayanaṃ vrajet
vastraṃ puṣpamalaṃkāraṃ yaccānyanmanujottama || 72 ||
na gāhejjanasaṃbādhaṃ na cājñātajalāśayam
aparīkṣitapūrvaṃ ca puruṣairāptakāribhiḥ || 73 ||
nā''rohetkuñjaraṃ vyālaṃ nādāntaṃ turagaṃ tathā
nāvijñātāṃ striyaṃ gacchennaiva devotsave vaset || 74 ||
narendra lakṣmyā dharmajña trātā'śrānto bhavennṛpaḥ
sadbhṛtyāśca tathā puṣṭāḥ satataṃ pratimānitāḥ || 75 ||
rājñā sahāyāḥ kartavyāḥ pṛthivīṃ jetumicchatā
yathārhaṃ cāpyasubhṛto rājā karmasu yojayet || 76 ||
dharmiṣṭhāndharmakāryeṣū śūrānsaṅgrāmakarmasu
nipuṇānarthakṛtyeṣu sarvatraiva tathā śucīn || 77 ||
strīṣu ṣaṇḍaṃ niyuñjīta tīkṣṇaṃ dāruṇakarmasu
dharme cārthe ca kāme ca naye ca ravinandana || 78 ||
rājā yathārhaṃ kuryācca upadhābhiḥ parīkṣaṇam
samatītopadānbhṛtyānkuryācchastavanecarān || 79 ||
tatpādānveṣiṇo yattāṃstadadhyakṣāṃstu kārayet
evamādīni karmāṇi nṛpaiḥ karyāṇi pārthiva || 80 ||
sarvathā neṣyate rājastīkṣṇopakaraṇakramaḥ
karmāṇi pāpasādhyāni yāni rājño narādhipa || 81 ||
santastāni na kurvanti tasmāttāni tyajennṛpaḥ
neṣyate pṛthivīśānāṃ tīkṣṇopakaraṇakriyā || 82 ||
yasminkarmaṇi yasya syādveśeṣeṇa ca kauśalam
tasminkarmaṇi taṃ rājā parīkṣya viniyojayet
pitṛpaitāmahānbhṛtyānsarvakarmasu yojayet || 83 ||
vinā dāyādakṛtyeṣu tatra te hi samāgatāḥ
rājā dāyādakṛtyeṣu parīkṣya tu kṛtānnarān
niyuñjīta mahābhāga tasya te hitakāriṇaḥ || 84 ||
pararājagṛhātprāptāñjanasaṃgrahakāmyayā
duṣṭānvā'pyathavā'duṣṭānāśrayīta prayatnataḥ || 85 ||
duṣṭaṃ vijñāya viśvāsaṃ na kuryāttatra bhūmipaḥ
vṛttiṃ tasyāpi varteta janasaṃgrahakāmyayā || 86 ||
rājā deśāntaraprāptaṃ puruṣaṃ pūjayedbhṛśam
mamāyaṃ deśasaṃprāpto bahumānena cintayet || 87 ||
kāmaṃ bhṛtyārjanaṃ rājā naiva kuryānnarādhipa
na ca vā'saṃvibhaktāṃstānbhṛtyānkuryātkathaṃcana || 88 ||
śatravo'gnirviṣaṃ sarpo nistriṃśa iti caikataḥ
bhṛtyā manujaśārdūla ruṣitāśca tathaikataḥ || 89 ||
teṣāṃ cāreṇa cāritraṃ rājā vijñāya nityaśaḥ
guṇināṃ pūjanaṃ kuryānnirguṇānāṃ ca śāsanam
kathitāḥ satataṃ rājanrājānaścāracakṣuṣaḥ || 90 ||
svake deśe pare deśe jñānaśīlānvicakṣaṇān
anāhāryānkleśasahānniyuñjīta tathā carān || 91 ||
janasyāviditānsaumyāṃstathā'jñātānparasparam
vaṇijo mantrakuśalānsāṃvatsaracikitsakān
tathā pravrājitākārāṃścārānrājā viyojayet || 92 ||
naikasya rajā śraddadhyāccārasyāpi subhāṣitam
dvayoḥ saṃbandhamājñāya śraddadhyānnṛpatistadā || 93 ||
parasparasyāviditau yadi syātāṃ ca tāvubhau
tasmādrā jā prayatnena gūḍhāṃścārānniyojayet || 94 ||
rājyasya mūlametāvadyā rājñaścāradarśitā
cārāṇāmapi yatnena rājñā kāryaṃ parīkṣaṇam || 95 ||
rāgāparāgau bhṛtyānāṃ janasya ca guṇāguṇān
sarvaṃ rājñāṃ carāyattaṃ teṣu yatnaparo bhavet || 96 ||
karmaṇā kena me loke janaḥ sarvo'nurajyate
virajyate kena tathā vijñeyaṃ tanmahīkṣitā || 97 ||
anurāgakaraṃ loke karma kāryaṃ mahākṣitā
virāgajanakaṃ loke varjanīyaṃ viśeṣataḥ || 98 ||
janānurāgaprabhavā hi lakṣmī rājñāṃ yato bhāskaravaṃśacandra
tasmātprayatnena narendra mukhyaiḥ kāryo'nurāgo bhuvi mānaveṣu || 99 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe
rājñāṃ sahāyasaṃpattirnāma pañcadaśādhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 215 ||

<216>

atha ṣoḍaśādhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
matsya uvāca
yathā'nuvartitavyaṃ syānmano rājño'nujīvibhiḥ
tathā te kathayiṣyāmi nibodha gadato mama
jñātvā sarvātmanā kāryaṃ svaśaktyā ravinandana
rājā yattu vadedvākyaṃ śrotavyaṃ tatprayatnataḥ
ākṣipya vacanaṃ tasya na vaktavyaṃ tathā vacaḥ || 2 ||
anukūlaṃ priyaṃ tasya vaktavyaṃ janasaṃsadi
rahogatasya vaktavyamapriyaṃ taddhitaṃ bhavet || 3 ||
parārthamasya vaktavyaṃ svasthe cetasi pārthiva
svārthaḥ suhṛdbhirvaktavyo na svayaṃ tu kathaṃ cana || 4 ||
kāryātipātaḥ sarveṣu rakṣitavyaḥ prayatnataḥ
na ca hiṃsyaṃ dhanaṃ kiṃcinniyuktena ca karmaṇi || 5 ||
nopekṣyastasya mānaśca tathā rājñaḥ priyo bhavet
rājñaśca na tathā kāryaṃ veṣabhāṣitaceṣṭitam || 6 ||
rājalīlā na kartavyā tadvidviṣṭaṃ ca varjayet
rājñaḥ samo'dhiko vā na kāryo veṣo vijānatā || 7 ||
dyūtādiṣu tathaivānyatkauśalaṃ tu pradarśayet
pradarśya kauśalaṃ cāsya rājānaṃ tu viśeṣayet || 8 ||
antaḥpurajanādhyakṣairvairidūtairnirākṛtaiḥ
saṃsargaṃ na vrajedrā janvinā pārthivaśāsanāt || 9 ||
niḥsnehatāṃ cāvamānaṃ prayatnena tu gopayet
yacca guhyaṃ bhavedrā jño na talloke prakāśayet || 10 ||
nṛpeṇa śrāvitaṃ yatsyādvācyāvācyaṃ nṛpottama
na tatsaṃśrāvayelloke tathā rājño'priyo bhavet || 11 ||
ājñāpyamāne vā'nyasminsamutthāya tvarānvitaḥ
kimahaṃ karavāṇīti vācyo rājā vijānatā || 12 ||
kāryāvasthāṃ ca vijñāya kāryameva yathā bhavet
satata kriyamāṇe'smim̐ llāghavaṃ tu vrajeddhruvam || 13 ||
rājñaḥ priyāṇi vākyāni na cātyarthaṃ punaḥ punaḥ
na hāsyaśīlastu bhavenna cāpi bhṛkuṭīmukhaḥ || 14 ||
nātivaktā na nirvaktā na ca mātsarikastathā
ātmasaṃbhāvitaścaiva na bhavettu kathaṃcana || 15 ||
duṣkṛtāni narendra sya na tu saṃkīrtayetkvacit
vastramastramalaṃkāraṃ rājñā dattaṃ tu dhārayet || 16 ||
audāryeṇa na taddeyamanyasmai bhūtimicchatā
na caivātyaśanaṃ kāryaṃ divā svapnaṃ na kārayet || 17 ||
nānirdiṣṭe tathā dvāre praviśettu kathaṃcana
na ca paśyettu rājānamayogyāsu ca bhūmiṣu || 18 ||
rājñastu dakṣiṇo pārśve vāme copaviśettadā
purastācca tathā paścādāsanaṃ tu vigarhitam || 19 ||
jṛmbhāṃ niṣṭhīvanaṃ kāsaṃ kopaṃ paryastikāśrayam
bhṛkuṭiṃ vāntamudgāraṃ tatsamīpe vivarjayet || 20 ||
svayaṃ tatra na kurvīta svaguṇākhyāpanaṃ budhaḥ
svaguṇākhyāpane yuktyā parameva prayojayet || 21 ||
hṛdayaṃ nirmalaṃ kṛtvā parāṃ bhaktimupāśritaiḥ
anujīvigaṇairbhāvyaṃ nityaṃ rājñāmatandri taiḥ || 22 ||
śāṭhyaṃ laulyaṃ ca paiśunyaṃ nāstikyaṃ kṣudra tā tathā
cāpalpaṃ ca parityājyaṃ nityaṃ rājño'nujīvibhiḥ || 23 ||
śrutena vidyāśīlaiśca saṃyojyā''tmānamātmanā
rājasevāṃ tataḥ kuryādbhūtaye bhūtivardhanīm || 24 ||
namaskāryāḥ sadā cāsya putravallabhamantriṇaḥ
sacivaiścāsya viśvāso na tu kāryaḥ kathaṃ cana || 25 ||
apṛṣṭaścāsya na brūyātkāmaṃ brūyāttathā yadi
hitaṃ tathyaṃ ca vacanaṃ hitaiḥ saha suniścitam || 26 ||
cittaṃ caivāsya vijñeyaṃ nityamevānujīvinā
bharturārādhanaṃ kuryāccittajño mānavaḥ sukham || 27 ||
rāgāparāgrau caivāsya vijñeyau bhūtimicchatā
tyajedviraktaṃ nṛpatiṃ raktādvṛttiṃ tu kārayet || 28 ||
viraktaḥ kārayennāśaṃ vipakṣābhyudayaṃ tathā
āśāvardhanakaṃ kṛtvā phalanāśaṃ karoti ca || 29 ||
akopo'pi sakopābhaḥ prasanno'pi ca niṣkalaḥ
vākyaṃ ca samadaṃ vakti vṛtticchedaṃ karoti vai || 30 ||
pradeśavākyamudito na ca saṃbhāvayettathā
ārādhanāsu sarvāsu suptavacca viceṣṭate || 31 ||
kathāsu doṣaṃ kṣipati vākyabhaṅgaṃ karoti ca
lakṣyate vimukhaścaiva guṇasaṃkīrtane'pi ca || 32 ||
dṛṣṭiṃ kṣipati cānyatra kriyamāṇe ca karmaṇi
viraktalakṣaṇaṃ caitacchṛṇu raktasya lakṣaṇam || 33 ||
dṛṣṭvā prasanno bhavati vākyaṃ gṛhṇāti cā''darāt
kuśalādiparipraśnaṃ saṃprayacchati cā''sanam || 34 ||
viviktadarśane cāsya rahasyenaṃ na śaṅkate
jāyate hṛṣṭavadanaḥ śrutvā tasya tu tatkathām || 35 ||
apriyāṇyapi vākyāni taduktānyabhinandate
upāyanaṃ ca gṛhṇāti stokamapyādarāttathā || 36 ||
kathāntareṣu smarati prahṛṣṭavadanastathā
iti raktasya kartavyā sevā ravikulodvaha
āpatsu na tyajetpūrvaṃ viraktamapi sevitam || 37 ||
mitraṃ na cā''patsu tathā ca mṛtyaṃ tyajanti ye nirguṇamaprameyam
vibhuṃ viśeṣeṇa ca te vrajanti surendra dhāmāmaravṛndajuṣṭam || 38 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe rājadharme'nujīvivartanaṃ nāma ṣoḍaśādhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 216 ||

<217>

atha saptadaśādhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
matsya uvāca
rājā sahāyasaṃyuktaḥ prabhūtayavasendhanam
ramyamānatasāmantaṃ madhyamaṃ deśamāvaset || 1 ||
vaiśyaśūdra janaprāyamanāhāryaṃ tathā paraiḥ
kiṃcidbrāhmaṇasaṃyuktaṃ bahukarmakaraṃ tathā || 2 ||
adevamātṛkaṃ ramyamanuraktajanānvitam
karairapīḍitaṃ cāpi bahupuṣpaphalaṃ tathā || 3 ||
agamyaṃ paracakrāṇāṃ tadvāsagṛhamāpadi
samaduḥkhasukhaṃ rājñaḥ satataṃ priyamāsthitam || 4 ||
sarīsṛpavihīnaṃ ca vyāghrataskaravarjitam
evaṃvidhaṃ yathālābhaṃ rājā viṣayamāvaset || 5 ||
tatra durgaṃ nṛpaḥ kuryātṣaṇṇāmekatamaṃ budhaḥ
dhanurdurgaṃ mahīdurgaṃ naradurgaṃ tathaiva ca || 6 ||
vārkṣaṃ caivāmbudurgaṃ ca giridurgaṃ ca pārthiva
sarveṣāmeva durgāṇāṃ giridurgaṃ praśasyate || 7 ||
durgaṃ ca parikhopetaṃ vaprāṭṭālakasaṃyutam
śataghnīyantramukhyaiśca śataśaśca samāvṛtam || 8 ||
gopuraṃ sakapāṭaṃ ca tatra syātsumanoharam
sapatākaṃ gajārūḍho yena rājā viśetpuram || 9 ||
catasraśca tathā tatra kāryāstvāyatavīthayaḥ
ekasmiṃstatra vīthyagre devaveśma bhaveddṛḍham || 10 ||
vīthyagre ca dvitīye ca rājaveśma vidhīyate
dharmādhikaraṇaṃ kāryaṃ vīthyagre ca tṛtīyake || 11 ||
caturthe tvatha vīthyagre gopuraṃ ca vidhīyate
āyataṃ caturasraṃ vā vṛttaṃ vā kārayetpuram || 12 ||
muktihīnaṃ trikoṇaṃ ca yavamadhyaṃ tathaiva ca
ardhacandra prakāraṃ ca vajrākāraṃ ca kārayet || 13 ||
ardhacandraṃ praśaṃsanti nadītīreṣu tadvasan
anyatra tanna kartavyaṃ prayatnena vijānatā || 14 ||
rājñā kośagṛhaṃ kāryaṃ dakṣiṇe rājaveśmanaḥ
tasyāpi dakṣiṇe bhāge gajasthānaṃ vidhīyate || 15 ||
gajānāṃ prāṅmukhī śālā kartavyā vā'pyudaṅmukhī
āgneye ca tathā bhāge āyudhāgāramiṣyate || 16 ||
mahānasaṃ ca dharmajña karmaśālāstathā parāḥ
gṛhaṃ purodhasaḥ kāryaṃ vāmato rājaveśmanaḥ || 17 ||
mantrivedavidāṃ caiva cikitsākartureva ca
tatraiva ca tathā bhāge koṣṭhāgāraṃ vidhīyate || 18 ||
gavāṃ sthānaṃ tathaivātra turagāṇāṃ tathaiva ca
uttarābhimukhā śreṇī turagāṇāṃ vidhīyate || 19 ||
dakṣiṇābhimukhā vā'tha pariśiṣṭāstu garhitāḥ
turagāste tathā dhāryāḥ pradīpaiḥ sārvarātrikaiḥ || 20 ||
kukkuṭānvānarāṃścaiva markaṭāṃśca viśeṣataḥ
dhārayedaśvaśālāsu savatsāṃ dhenumeva ca || 21 ||
ajāśca dhāryā yatnena turagāṇāṃ hitaiṣiṇā
gogajāśvādiśālāsu tatpurīṣasya nirgamaḥ || 22 ||
astaṃ gate na kartavyo devadeve divākare
tatra tatra yathāsthānaṃ rājā vijñāya sārathīn || 23 ||
dadyādāvasathasthānaṃ sarveṣāmanupūrvaśaḥ
yodhānāṃ śilpināṃ caiva sarveṣāmaviśeṣataḥ || 24 ||
dadyādāvasathāndurge kālamantravidāṃ śubhān
govaidyānaśvavaidyāṃśca gajavaidyāṃstathaiva ca || 25 ||
āhareta bhṛśaṃ rājā durge hi prabalā rujaḥ
kuśīlavānāṃ viprāṇāṃ durge sthānaṃ vidhīyate || 26 ||
na bahūnāmato durge vinā kāryaṃ tathā bhavet
durge ca tatra kartavyā nānāpraharaṇānvitāḥ || 27 ||
sahasraghātino rājaṃstaistu rakṣā vidhīyate
durge dvārāṇi guptāni kāryāṇyapi ca bhūbhujā || 28 ||
saṃcayaścātra sarveṣāmāyudhānāṃ praśasyate
dhanuṣāṃ kṣepaṇīyānāṃ tomarāṇāṃ ca pārthiva || 29 ||
śarāṇāmatha khaḍgānāṃ kavacānāṃ tathaiva ca
laguḍānāṃ guḍānāṃ ca huḍānāṃ parighaiḥ saha || 30 ||
aśmanāṃ ca prabhūtānāṃ mudgarāṇāṃ tathaiva ca
triśūlānāṃ paṭṭiśānāṃ kuṭhārāṇāṃ ca pārthiva || 31 ||
prāsānāṃ ca saśūlānāṃ śaktīnāṃ ca narottama
paraśvadhānāṃ cakrāṇāṃ varmaṇāṃ carmabhiḥ saha || 32 ||
kuddālarajjuvetrāṇāṃ pīṭhakānāṃ tathaiva ca
tuṣāṇāṃ caiva dātrāṇāmaṅgārāṇāṃ ca saṃcayaḥ || 33 ||
sarveṣāṃ śilpibhāṇḍānāṃ saṃcayaścātra ceṣyate
vāditrāṇāṃ ca sarveṣāmoṣadhīnāṃ tathaiva ca || 34 ||
yavasānāṃ prabhūtānāmindhanasya ca saṃcayaḥ
guḍasya sarvatailānāṃ gorasānāṃ tathaiva ca || 35 ||
vasānāmatha majjānāṃ snāyūnāmasthibhiḥ saha
gocarmapaṭahānāṃ ca dhānyānāṃ sarvatastathā || 36 ||
tathaivābhrapaṭānāṃ ca yavagodhūmayorapi
ratnānāṃ sarvavastrāṇāṃ lohānāmapyaśeṣataḥ || 37 ||
kalāyamudgamāṣāṇāṃ caṇakānāṃ tilaiḥ saha
tathā ca sarvasasyānāṃ pāṃsugomayayorapi || 38 ||
śaṇasarjarasaṃ bhūrjaṃ jatu lākṣā ca ṭaṅkaṇam
rājā saṃcinuyāddurge yaccānyadapi kiṃcana || 39 ||
kubhāścā''śīviṣaiḥ kāryā vyālasiṃhādayastathā
mṛgāśca pakṣiṇaścaiva rakṣyāste ca parasparam || 40 ||
sthānāni ca viruddhānāṃ suguptāni pṛthakpṛthak
kartavyāṇi mahābhāga yatnena pṛthivīkṣitā || 41 ||
uktāni cāpyanuktāni rājadra vyāṇyaśeṣataḥ
suguptāni pure kuryājjanānāṃ hitakāmyayā || 42 ||
jīvakarṣabhakākolamāmalakyāṭarūṣakān
śālaparṇī pṛśniparṇī mudgaparṇī tathaiva ca || 43 ||
māṣaparṇī ca mede dve śārive dve balātrayam
vīrā śvasantī vṛṣyā ca bṛhatī kaṇṭakārikā || 44 ||
śṛṅgī śṛṅgāṭakī dro ṇī varṣābhūrdarbhareṇukā
madhuparṇī vidārye dve mahākṣīrā mahātapāḥ || 45 ||
dhanvanaḥ sahadevāhvā kaṭukairaṇḍakaṃ viṣaḥ
parṇī śatāhvā mṛdvīkā phalgukharjūrayaṣṭikāḥ || 46 ||
śukrātiśukrakāśmaryaśchatrāticchatravīraṇāḥ
ikṣurikṣuvikārāśca phāṇitādyāśca sattama || 47 ||
siṃhī ca sahadevī ca viśvedevāśvarodhakam
madhukaṃ puṣpahaṃsākhyā śatapuṣpā madhūlikā || 48 ||
śatāvarīmadhūke ca pippalaṃ tālameva ca
ātmaguptā kaṭphalākhyā dārvikā rājaśīrṣakī || 49 ||
rājasarṣapadhānyākamṛṣyaproktā tathotkaṭā
kālaśākaṃ padmabījaṃ govallī madhuvallikā || 50 ||
śītapākī kuliṅgākṣī kākajihvorupuṣpikā
parvatatrapusau cobhau guñjātakapunarnave || 51 ||
kaserukā tu kāśmīrī bilvaśālūkakesaram
śūkadhānyāni sarvāṇi śamīdhānyāni caiva hi || 52 ||
kṣīraṃ kṣaudraṃ tathā takraṃ tailaṃ majjā vasāghṛtam
nīpaścāriṣṭakakṣoḍavātāmāsomabāṇakam || 53 ||
evamādīni cānyāni vijñeyo madhuro gaṇaḥ
rājā saṃcinuyātsarvaṃ pure niravaśeṣataḥ || 54 ||
dāḍimāmrātakau caiva tintiḍīkāmlavetasam
bhavyakarkandhulakucakaramardakarūṣakam || 55 ||
bījapūrakakaṇḍūre mālatī rājabandhukam
kolakadvayaparṇāni dvayorāmrātayorapi || 56 ||
pārāvataṃ nāgarakaṃ prācīnārukameva ca
kapitthāmalakaṃ cukraphalaṃ dantaśaṭhasya ca || 57 ||
jāmbavaṃ navanītaṃ ca sauvīrakaruṣodake
surāsavaṃ ca madyāni maṇḍatakradadhīni ca || 58 ||
śuklāni caiva sarvāṇi jñeyamāmlagaṇaṃ dvija
evamādīni cānyāni rājā saṃcinuyātpure || 59 ||
saindhavodbhidapāṭheyapākyasāmudra lomakam
kupyasauvarcalābilvaṃ bālakeyaṃ yavāhvakam || 60 ||
aurvaṃ kṣāraṃ kālabhasma vijñeyo lavaṇo gaṇaḥ
evamādīni cānyāni rājā saṃcinuyātpure || 61 ||
pippalī pippalīmūlacavyacitrakanāgaram
kuberakaṃ ca marikaṃ śigrubhallātasarṣapāḥ || 62 ||
kuṣṭhājamodā kiṇihī hiṅgumūlakadhānyakam
kāravī kuñcikā yājyā sumukhā kālamālikā || 63 ||
phaṇijjhako'tha laśunaṃ bhūstṛṇaṃ surasaṃ tathā
kāyasthā ca vayaḥsthā ca haritālaṃ manaḥśilā || 64 ||
amṛtā ca rudantī ca rohiṣaṃ kuṅkumaṃ tathā
jayā eraṇḍakāṇḍīraṃ śallakī hañjikā tathā || 65 ||
sarvapittāni mūtrāṇi prāyo haritakāni ca
saṃgatāni ca mūlāni yaṭiścātiviṣāṇi ca
phalāni caiva hi tathā sūkṣmailā hiṅgupatrikā || 66 ||
evamādīni cānyāni gaṇaḥ kaṭukasaṃjñitaḥ
rājā saṃcinuyāddurge prayatnena nṛpottama || 67 ||
mustaṃ candanahrīberakṛtamālakadāravaḥ
haridrā naladośīranaktamālakadambakam || 68 ||
dūrvā paṭolakaṭukā dantītvakpatrakaṃ tvacā
kirātatiktabhūtumbī viṣā cātiviṣā tathā || 69 ||
tālīsapatratagaraṃ saptaparṇavikaṅkatāḥ
kākodumbarikā divyāstathā caiva surodbhavā || 70 ||
ṣaḍgranthā rohiṇī māṃsī parpaṭaścātha dantikā
rasāñjanaṃ bhṛṅgarājaṃ pataṅgī paripelavam || 71 ||
duḥsparśā guruṇī kāmā śyāmākaṃ gandhanākulī
tuṣaparṇī vyāghranakhaṃ mañjiṣṭhā caturaṅgulā || 72 ||
rambhā caivāṅkurāsphotā tālāsphotā hareṇukā
vetrāgravetasastumbī viṣāṇī lodhrapuṣpiṇī || 73 ||
mālatīkarakṛṣṇākhyā vṛścikā jīvitā tathā
parṇikā ca guḍūcī ca sa gaṇastiktasaṃjñakaḥ || 74 ||
evamādīni cānyāni rājā saṃcinuyātpure
abhayāmalake cobhe tathaiva ca bibhītakam || 75 ||
priyaṅgudhātakīpuṣpaṃ mocākhyā cārjunāsanāḥ
anantā strī tuvarikā śyoṇākaṃ kaṭphalaṃ tathā || 76 ||
bhūrjapatraṃ śilāpatraṃ pāṭalāpatralomakam
samaṅgātrivṛtāmūlakārpāsagairikāñjanam || 77 ||
vidrumaṃ samadhūcchiṣṭaṃ kumbhikā kumudotpalam
nyagrodhodumbarāśvatthakiṃśukāḥ śiṃśapā śamī || 78 ||
priyālapīlukāsārīśirīṣāḥ padmakaṃ tathā
bilvo'gnimanyaḥ plakṣaśca śyāmākaṃ ca bako ghanam || 79 ||
rājādanaṃ karīraṃ ca dhānyakaṃ priyakastathā
kaṅkolāśokabadarāḥ kadambakhadiradvayam || 80 ||
eṣāṃ patrāṇi sārāṇi mūlāni kusumāni ca
evamādīni cānyāni kaṣāyākhyo gaṇo mataḥ || 81 ||
prayatnena nṛpaśreṣṭha rājā saṃcinuyātpure
kīṭāśca māraṇe yogyā vyaṅgatāyāṃ tathaiva ca || 82 ||
tātadhūmāmbumārgāṇāṃ dūṣaṇāni tathaiva ca
dhāryāṇi pārthivairdurge tāni vakṣyāmi pārthiva || 83 ||
viṣāṇāṃ dhāraṇaṃ kāryaṃ prayatnena mahībhujā
vicitrāścāgadā dhāryā viṣasya śamanāstathā || 84 ||
rakṣobhūtapiśācaghnāḥ pāpaghnāḥ puṣṭivardhanāḥ
kalāvidaśca puruṣāḥ pure dhāryāḥ prayatnataḥ || 85 ||
bhītānpramattānkupitāṃstathaiva ca vimānitān
kubhṛtyānpāpaśīlāṃśca na rājā vāsayetpure || 86 ||
yantrāyudhāṭṭālacayopapannaṃ samagradhānyauṣadhisaṃprayuktam
vaṇigjanaiścā''vṛtamāvaseta durgaṃ suṣuptaṃ nṛpatiḥ sadaiva || 87 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe rājñāmoṣadhyādisaṃcayakathanaṃ nāma saptadaśādhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 217 ||

<218>

athāṣṭādaśādhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
manuruvāca
rakṣoghnāni viṣaghnāni yāni dhāryāṇi bhūbhujā
agadāni samācakṣva tāni dharmabhṛtāṃ vara || 1 ||
matsya uvāca
bilvāṭakī yavakṣāraṃ pāṭalā bāhlikoṣaṇā
śrīparṇī śallakīyukto nikvāthaḥ prokṣaṇaṃ param || 2 ||
saviṣaṃ prokṣitaṃ tena sadyo bhavati nirviṣam
yavasaindhavapānīyavastraśayyāsanodakam || 3 ||
kavacābharaṇaṃ chatraṃ vālavyajanaveśmanām
śeluḥ pāṭalyativiṣā śigru mūrvā punarnavā || 4 ||
samaṅgā vṛṣamūlaṃ ca kapitthavṛṣaśoṣitam
mahādantaśaṭhaṃ tadvatprokṣaṇaṃ viṣanāśanam || 5 ||
lākṣāpriyaṅgumañjiṣṭhā samamelā hareṇukā
yaṣṭyāhvā madhurā caiva babhrupittena kalpitāḥ || 6 ||
nikhanedgoviṣāṇasthaṃ saptarātraṃ mahītale
tataḥ kṛtvā maṇiṃ hemnā baddhaṃ hastena dhārayet || 7 ||
saṃsṛṣṭaṃ saviṣaṃ tena sadyo bhavati nirviṣam
manohvayā śamīpatraṃ tumbikā śvetasarṣapāḥ || 8 ||
kapitthakuṣṭhamañjiṣṭhāḥ pittena ślakṣṇakalpitāḥ
śuno goḥ kapilāyāśca saumyākṣipto'paro gadaḥ || 9 ||
viṣajitparamaṃ kāryaṃ maṇiratnaṃ ca pūrvavat
mūṣikā jatukā cāpi haste baddhvā viṣāpahā || 10 ||
hareṇumāṃsī mañjiṣṭhā rajanī madhukā madhu
akṣatvaksurasaṃ lākṣā śvapittaṃ pūrvavadbhuvi || 11 ||
vāditrāṇi patākāśca piṣṭairetaiḥ pralepitāḥ
śrutvā dṛṣṭvā samāghrāya sadyo bhavati nirviṣaḥ || 12 ||
tryūṣaṇaṃ pañcalavaṇaṃ mañjiṣṭhā rajanīdvayam
sūkṣmailā trivṛtāpatraṃ viḍaṅgānīndra vāruṇī || 13 ||
madhukaṃ vetasaṃ kṣaudraṃ viṣāṇe ca nidhāpayet
tasmāduṣṇāmbunā mātraṃ prāguktaṃ yojayettataḥ || 14 ||
viṣabhuktaṃ jaraṃ yāti nirviṣaṃ pittadoṣakṛt
śuklaṃ sarjarasopetaṃ sarṣapā elavālukaiḥ || 15 ||
suvegā taskarasurau kusumairarjunasya tu
dhūpo vāsagṛhe hanti viṣaṃ sthāvarajaṅgamam || 16 ||
na tatra kīṭā na viṣaṃ dardurā na sarīsṛpāḥ
na kṛtyā karmaṇāṃ cāpi dhūpo'yaṃ yatra dahyate || 17 ||
kalpitaiścandanakṣīrapalāśadrumavalkalaiḥ
mūrvailāvālusarasānākulītaṇḍulīyakaiḥ || 18 ||
kvāthaḥ sarvodakāryeṣu kākamācīyuto hitaḥ
rocanāpatranepālīkuṅkumaistilakānvahan || 19 ||
viṣairna bādhyate'smācca naranārīnṛpapriyaḥ
cūrṇairharidrā mañjiṣṭhākiṇihīkaṇanimbajaiḥ || 20 ||
digdhaṃ nirviṣatāmeti gātraṃ sarvaviṣārditam
śirīṣasya phalaṃ patraṃ puṣpaṃ tvaṅmūlameva ca || 21 ||
gomūtraghṛṣṭo hyagadaḥ sarvakarmakaraḥ smṛtaḥ
ekavīra mahauṣadhyaḥ śṛṇu cātaḥ paraṃ nṛpa || 22 ||
vandhyā karkoṭakī rājanviṣṇukrāntā tathotkaṭā
śatamūlī sitānandā balā mocā paṭolikā || 23 ||
somā piṇḍā niśā caiva tathā dagdharuhā ca yā
sthale kamalinī yā ca viśālī śaṅkhamūlikā || 24 ||
cāṇḍālī hastimagadhā go'jāparṇī karambhikā
raktā caiva mahāraktā tathā barhiśikhā ca yā || 25 ||
kośātakī naktamālaṃ priyālaṃ ca sulocanī
vāruṇī vasugandhā ca tathā vai gandhanākulī || 26 ||
īśvarī śivagandhā ca śyāmalā vaṃśanālikā
jatukālī mahāśvetā śvetā ca madhuyaṣṭikā || 27 ||
vajrakaḥ pāribhadra śca tathā vai sindhuvārakāḥ
jīvānandā vasucchidrā natanāgarakaṇṭakā || 28 ||
nālaṃ ca jālī jātī ca tathā ca vaṭapatrikā
kārtasvaraṃ mahānīlā kundururhaṃsapādikā || 29 ||
maṇḍūkaparṇī vārāhī dve tathā taṇḍulīyake
sarpākṣī lavalī brāhmī viśvarūpā sukhākarā || 30 ||
rujāpahā vṛddhikarī tathā caiva tu śalyadā
patrikā rohiṇī caiva raktamālā mahauṣadhī || 31 ||
tathā''malakavandākaṃ śyāmacitraphalā ca yā
kākolī kṣīrakākolī pīluparṇī tathaiva ca || 32 ||
keśinī vṛścikālī ca mahānāgā śatāvarī
garuḍī ca tathā vegā jale kumudinī tathā || 33 ||
sthale cotpalinī yā ca mahābhūmilatā ca yā
unmādinī somarājī sarvaratnāni pārthiva || 34 ||
viśikhā'marakanyā ca kīṭapakṣaṃ viśeṣataḥ
jīvajātāśca maṇayaḥ sarve dhāryāḥ prayatnataḥ || 35 ||
rakṣoghnāśca viṣaghnāśca kṛtyā vaitālanāśanāḥ
viśeṣānnaranāgāśca gokharoṣṭrasamudbhavāḥ || 36 ||
sarpatittiragomāyubabhrumaṇḍukajāśca ye
siṃhavyāghrarkṣamārjāradvīpivānarasaṃbhavāḥ
kapiñjalā gajā vājimahiṣaiṇabhavāśca ye || 37 ||
ityevametaiḥ sakalairupetairdra vyaiḥ parārghyaiḥ parirakṣitaḥ syāt
rājā vasettatra gṛhe suśubhre guṇānvite lakṣaṇasaṃprayukte || 38 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe'gadādhyāyo nāmāṣṭādaśādhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 218 ||

<219>

athaikonaviṃśatyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
manuruvāca
rājarakṣārahasyāni yāni durge nidhāpayet
kārayedvā mahībhartā brūhi tattvāni tāni me || 1 ||
matsya uvāca
śirīṣodumbaraśamībījapūraṃ ghṛtaplutam
kṣudyogaḥ kathito rājanmāsārdhasya purātanaiḥ || 2 ||
kaśeruphalamūlāni ikṣumūlaṃ tathā viṣam
dūrvākṣīraghṛtairmaṇḍaḥ siddho'yaṃ māsikaḥ paraḥ || 3 ||
naraṃ śastrahataṃ prāpto na tasya maraṇaṃ bhavet
kalmāṣaveṇunā tatra janayettu vibhāvasum || 4 ||
gṛhe trirapasavyaṃ tu kriyate yatra pārthiva
nānyo'gnirjvalate tatra nātra kāryā vicāraṇā || 5 ||
kārpāsāsthni bhujaṅgasya tena nirmocanaṃ bhavet
sarpanirvāsane dhūpaḥ praśastaḥ satataṃ gṛhe || 6 ||
sāmudra saindhavayavā vidyuddagdhā ca mṛttikā
tayā'nuliptaṃ yadveśma nāgninā dahyate nṛpa || 7 ||
divā ca durge rakṣyo'gnirvāti vāte viśeṣataḥ
viṣācca rakṣyo nṛpatistatra yuktiṃ nibodha me || 8 ||
krīḍānimittaṃ nṛpatirdhārayenmṛgapakṣiṇaḥ
annaṃ vai prākparīkṣeta vahnau cānyatareṣu ca || 9 ||
vastraṃ puṣpamalaṃkāraṃ bhojanācchādanaṃ tathā
nāparīkṣitapūrvaṃ tu spṛśedapi mahīpatiḥ || 10 ||
syāccāsau vaktrasaṃtaptaḥ sodvegaṃ ca nirīkṣate
viṣado'tha viṣaṃ dattaṃ yacca tatra parīkṣate || 11 ||
srastottarīyo vimanāḥ stambhakuḍyādibhistathā
pracchādayati cā''tmānaṃ lajjate tvarate tathā || 12 ||
bhuvaṃ vilikhati grīvāṃ tathā cālayate nṛpa
kaṇḍūyati ca mūrdhānaṃ pariloḍyā''nanaṃ tathā || 13 ||
kriyāsu tvarito rājanviparītāsvapi dhruvam
evamādīni cihnāni viṣadasya parīkṣayet || 14 ||
samīpairvikṣipedvahnau tadannaṃ tvarayā'nvitaḥ
indrā yudhasavarṇaṃ tu rūkṣaṃ sphoṭasamanvitam || 15 ||
ekāvartaṃ tu durgandhi bhṛśaṃ caṭacaṭāyate
taddhūmasevanājjantoḥ śirorogaśca jāyate || 16 ||
saviṣe'nne vilīyante na ca pārthiva makṣikāḥ
nilīnāśca vipadyante saṃspṛṣṭe saviṣe tathā || 17 ||
virajyati cakorasya dṛṣṭiḥ pārthivasattama
vikṛtiṃ ca svaro yāti kokilasya tathā nṛpa || 18 ||
gatiḥ skhalati haṃsasya bhṛṅgarājaśca kūjati
krauñco madamathābhyeti kṛkavākurvirauti ca || 19 ||
vikrośati śuko rājansārikā vamate tataḥ
cāmīkaro'nyato yāti mṛtyuṃ kāraṇḍavastathā || 20 ||
mehate vānaro rājanglāyate jīvajīvakaḥ
hṛṣṭaromā bhavedbabhruḥ pṛṣataścaiva roditi || 21 ||
harṣamāyāti ca śikhī viṣasaṃdarśanānnṛpa
annaṃ ca saviṣaṃ rājaṃścireṇa ca vipadyate || 22 ||
tadā bhavati niḥśrāvyaṃ pakṣaparyuṣitopamam
vyāpannarasagandhaṃ ca candri kābhistathā yutam || 23 ||
vyañjanānāṃ tu śuṣkatvaṃ dra vāṇāṃ budbudodbhavaḥ
sasaindhavānāṃ dra vyāṇāṃ jāyate phenamālitā || 24 ||
sasyarājiśca tāmrā syānnīlā ca payasastathā
kokilābhā ca madyasya toyasya ca nṛpottama || 25 ||
dhānyāmlasya tathā kṛṣṇā kapilā kodra vasya ca
madhuśyāmā ca takrasya nīlā pītā tathaiva ca || 26 ||
ghṛtasyodakasaṃkāśā kapotāmā ca mastunaḥ
haritā mākṣikasyāpi tailasya ca tathā'ruṇā || 27 ||
phalānāmapyapakvānāṃ pākaḥ kṣipraṃ prajāyate
prakopaścaiva pakvānāṃ mālyānāṃ mlānatā tathā || 28 ||
mṛdutā kaṭhinānāṃ syānmṛdūnāṃ ca viparyayaḥ
sūkṣmāṇāṃ rūpadalanaṃ tathā caivātiraṅgatā || 29 ||
śyāmamaṇḍalatā caiva vastrāṇāṃ vai tathaiva ca
lohānāṃ ca maṇīnāṃ ca malapaṅkopadigdhatā || 30 ||
anulepanagandhānāṃ mālyānāṃ ca nṛpottama
vigandhatā ca vijñeyā varṇānāṃ mlānatā tathā
pītāvabhāsatā jñeyā tathā rājañjalasya tu || 31 ||
dantā oṣṭhau tvacaḥ śyāmāstanusattvāstathaiva ca
evamādīni cihnāni vijñeyāni nṛpottama || 32 ||
tasmādrā jā sadā tiṣṭhenmaṇimantrauṣadhāgadaiḥ
uktaiḥ saṃrakṣito rājā pramādaparivarjakaḥ || 33 ||
prajātarormūlamihāvanīśastadra kṣaṇādrā ṣṭramupaiti vṛddhim
tasmātprayatnena nṛpasya rakṣā sarveṇa kāryā ravivaṃśacandra || 34 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe rājadharme rājarakṣā nāmaikonaviṃśatyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 219 ||

<220>

atha viṃśatyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
matsya uvāca
rājanputrasya rakṣā ca kartavyā pṛthivīkṣitā
ācāryaścātra kartavyo nityayuktaśca rakṣibhiḥ || 1 ||
dharmakāmārthaśāstrāṇi dhanurvedaṃ ca śikṣayet
rathe ca kuñjare cainaṃ vyāyāmaṃ kārayetsadā || 2 ||
śilpāni śikṣayeccainaṃ nā''ptairmithyāpriyaṃ vadet
śarīrarakṣāvyājena rakṣiṇo'sya niyojayet || 3 ||
na cāsya saṅgo dātavyaḥ kruddhalubdhāvamānitaiḥ
tathā ca vinayedenaṃ yathā yauvanagocare || 4 ||
indri yairnāpakṛṣyeta satāṃ mārgātsudurgamāt
guṇādhānamaśakyaṃ tu yasya kartuṃ svabhāvataḥ || 5 ||
bandhanaṃ tasya kartavyaṃ guptadeśe sukhānvitam
avinītakumāraṃ hi kulamāśu viśīryate || 6 ||
adhikāreṣu sarveṣu vinītaṃ viniyojayet
ādau svalpe tataḥ paścātkrameṇātha mahatsvapi || 7 ||
mṛgayāpānamakṣāṃśca varjayetpṛthivīpatiḥ
etāṃstu sevamānāstu vinaṣṭāḥ pṛthivīkṣitaḥ || 8 ||
bahavo nṛpaśārdūla teṣāṃ saṃkhyā na vidyate
vṛthāṭanaṃ divāsvapnaṃ viśeṣeṇa vivarjayet || 9 ||
vākpāruṣyaṃ na kartavyaṃ daṇḍapāruṣyameva ca
parokṣanindā ca tathā varjanīyā mahīkṣitā || 10 ||
arthasya dūṣaṇaṃ rājā dviprakāraṃ vivarjayet
arthānāṃ dūṣaṇaṃ caikaṃ tathā'rtheṣu ca dūṣaṇam || 11 ||
prākārāṇāṃ samucchedo durgādīnāmasatkriyā
arthānāṃ dūṣaṇaṃ proktaṃ viprakīrṇatvameva ca || 12 ||
adeśakāle yaddānamapātre dānameva ca
artheṣu dūṣaṇaṃ proktamasatkarmapravartanam || 13 ||
kāmaḥ krodho mado māno lobho harṣastathaiva ca
ete varjyāḥ prayatnena sādaraṃ pṛthivīkṣitā || 14 ||
eteṣāṃ vijayaṃ kṛtvā kāryo bhṛtyajayastataḥ
kṛtvā bhṛtyajayaṃ rājā paurāñjānapadāñjayet || 15 ||
kṛtvā ca vijayaṃ teṣāṃ śatrūnbāhyāṃstato jayet
bāhyāśca vividhā jñeyāstulyābhyantarakṛtrimāḥ || 16 ||
guravaste yathāpūrvaṃ teṣu yatnaparo bhavet
pitṛpaitāmahaṃ mitramamitraṃ ca tathā ripoḥ || 17 ||
kṛtrimaṃ ca mahābhāga mitraṃ trividhamucyate
tathā'pi ca guruḥ pūrvaṃ bhavettatrāpi cā''dṛtaḥ || 18 ||
svāmyamātyo janapado durgaṃ daṇḍastathaiva ca
kośo mitraṃ ca dharmajña saptāṅgaṃ rājyamucyate || 19 ||
saptāṅgasyāpi rājyasya mūlaṃ svāmī prakīrtitaḥ
tanmūlatvāttathā'ṅgānāṃ sa tu rakṣyaḥ prayatnataḥ || 20 ||
ṣaḍaṅgarakṣā kartavyā tathā tena prayatnataḥ
aṅgebhyo yastathaikasya dro hamācarate'lpadhīḥ || 21 ||
vadhastasya tu kartavyaḥ śīghrameva mahīkṣitā
na rājñā mṛdunā bhāvyaṃ mṛdurhi paribhūyate || 22 ||
na bhāvyaṃ dāruṇenātitīkṣṇādudvijate janaḥ
kāle mṛduryo bhavati kāle bhavati dāruṇaḥ || 23 ||
rājā lokadvayāpekṣī tasya lokadvayaṃ bhavet
bhṛtyaiḥ saha mahīpālaḥ parihāsaṃ vivarjayet || 24 ||
bhṛtyāḥ paribhavantīha nṛpaṃ harṣavaśaṃ gatam
vyasanāni ca sarvāṇi bhūpatiḥ parivarjayet || 25 ||
lokasaṃgrahaṇārthāya kṛtakavyasanī bhavet
śauṇḍīrasya narendra sya nityamudri ktacetasaḥ || 26 ||
janā virāgamāyānti sadā duḥsevyabhāvataḥ
smitapūrvābhibhāṣī syātsarvasyaiva mahīpatiḥ || 27 ||
vadhyeṣvapi mahābhāga bhrūkuṭiṃ na samācaret
bhāvyaṃ dharmabhṛtāṃ śreṣṭha sthūlalakṣyeṇa bhūbhujā || 28 ||
sthūlalakṣyasya vaśagā sarvā bhavati medinī
adīrghasūtraśca bhavetsarvakarmasu pārthivaḥ || 29 ||
dīrghasūtrasya nṛpateḥ karmahānirdhruvaṃ bhavet
rāge darpe ca māne ca dro he pāpe ca karmaṇi || 30 ||
apriye caiva kartavye dīrghasūtraḥ praśasyate
rājñā saṃvṛtamantreṇa sadā bhāvyaṃ nṛpottama || 31 ||
tasyāsaṃvṛtamantrasya rājñaḥ sarvāpado dhruvam
kṛtānyeva tu kāryāṇi jñāyante yasya bhūpateḥ || 32 ||
nā''rabdhāni mahābhāga tasya syādvasudhā vaśe
mantramūlaṃ sadā rājyaṃ tasmānmantraḥ surakṣitaḥ || 33 ||
kartavyaḥ pṛthivīpalairmantrabhedabhayātsadā
mantravitsādhito mantraḥ saṃpattīnāṃ sukhāvahaḥ || 34 ||
mantracchalena bahavo vinaṣṭāḥ pṛthivīkṣitaḥ
ākārairiṅgitairgatyā ceṣṭayā bhāṣitena ca || 35 ||
netravaktravikāraiśca gṛhyate'ntargataṃ manaḥ
na yasya kuśalaistasya vaśe sarvā vasuṃdharā || 36 ||
bhavatīha mahībhartuḥ sadā pārthivanandana
naikastu mantrayenmantraṃ rājā na bahubhiḥ saha || 37 ||
nā''rohedviṣamāṃ nāvamaparīkṣitanāvikām
ye cāsya bhūmijayino bhaveyuḥ paripanthinaḥ || 38 ||
tānānayedvaśe sarvānsāmādibhirupakramaiḥ
yathā na syātkṛśībhāvaḥ prajānāmanavekṣayā || 39 ||
tathā rājñā prakartavyaṃ svarāṣṭraṃ parirakṣatā
mohādrā jā svarāṣṭraṃ yaḥ karśayatyanavekṣayā || 40 ||
so'cirādbhraśyate rājyājjīvitācca sabāndhavaḥ
bhṛto vatso jātabalaḥ karmayogyo yathā bhavet || 41 ||
tathā rāṣṭraṃ mahābhāga bhṛtaṃ karmasahaṃ bhavet
yo rāṣṭramanugṛhṇāti rājyaṃ sa parirakṣati || 42 ||
saṃjātamupajīvettu vindate sa mahatphalam
rāṣṭrāddhiraṇyaṃ dhānyaṃ ca mahīṃ rājā surakṣitām || 43 ||
mahatā tu prayatnena svarāṣṭrasya ca rakṣitā
nityaṃ svebhyaḥ parebhyaśca yathā mātā yathā pitā || 44 ||
gopitāni sadā kuryātsaṃyatānīndri yāṇi ca
ajasramupayoktavyaṃ phalaṃ tebhyastathaiva ca || 45 ||
sarvaṃ karmedamāyattaṃ vidhāne devamānuṣe
tayordaivamacintyaṃ ca pauruṣe vidyate kriyā || 46 ||
evaṃ mahīṃ pālayato'sya bharturlokānurāgaḥ paramo bhavettu
lokānurāgaprabhavā ca lakṣmīrlakṣmīvataścāpi parā ca kīrtiḥ || 47 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe
rājadharmānukīrtane viṃśatyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 220 ||

<221>

athaikaviṃśatyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
manuruvāca
daive puruṣakāre ca kiṃ jyāyastadbravīhi me
atra me saṃśayo deva cchettumarhasyaśeṣataḥ || 1 ||
matsya uvāca
svameva karma daivākhyaṃ viddhi dehāntarārjitam
tasmātpauruṣameveha śreṣṭhamāhurmanīṣiṇaḥ || 2 ||
pratikūlaṃ tathā daivaṃ pauruṣeṇa vihanyate
maṅgalācārayuktānāṃ nityamutthānaśālinām || 3 ||
yeṣāṃ pūrvakṛtaṃ karma sāttvikaṃ manujottama
pauruṣeṇa vinā teṣāṃ keṣāṃ ciddṛśyate phalam || 4 ||
karmaṇā prāpyate loke rājasasya tathā phalam
kṛcchreṇa karmaṇā viddhi tāmasasya tathā phalam || 5 ||
pauruṣeṇā''pyate rājanprārthitavyaṃ phalaṃ naraiḥ
daivameva vijānanti narāḥ pauruṣavarjitāḥ || 6 ||
tasmāttrikālaṃ saṃyuktaṃ daivaṃ tu saphalaṃ bhavet
pauruṣaṃ daivasaṃpattyā kāle phalati pārthiva || 7 ||
daivaṃ puruṣakāraśca kālaśca puruṣottama
trayametanmanuṣyasya piṇḍitaṃ syātphalāvaham || 8 ||
kṛṣivṛṣṭisamāyogāddṛśyante phalasiddhayaḥ
tāstu kāle pradṛśyante naivākāle kathaṃ cana || 9 ||
tasmātsadaiva kartavyaṃ sadharmaṃ pauruṣaṃ naraiḥ
vipattāvapi yasyeha paraloke dhruvaṃ phalam || 10 ||
nālasāḥ prāpnuvantyarthānna ca daivaparāyaṇāḥ
tasmātsarvaprayatnena pauruṣe yatnamācaret || 11 ||
tyaktvā'lasāndaivaparānmanuṣyānutthānayuktānpuruṣānhi lakṣmīḥ
anviṣya yatnādvṛṇuyānnṛpendra tasmātsadotthānavatā hi bhāvyam || 12 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe daivapuruṣakāravarṇanaṃ nāmaikaviṃśatyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 221 ||

<222>

atha dvāviṃśatyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
manuruvāca
upāyāṃstvaṃ samācakṣva sāmapūrvānmahādyute
lakṣaṇaṃ ca tathā teṣāṃ prayogaṃ ca surottama || 1 ||
matsya uvāca
sāma bhedastathā dānaṃ daṇḍaśca manujeśvara
upekṣā ca tathā māyā indra jālaṃ ca pārthiva || 2 ||
prayogāḥ kathitāḥ sapta tanme nigadataḥ śṛṇu
dvividhaṃ kathitaṃ sāma tathyaṃ cātathyameva ca || 3 ||
tatrāpyatathyaṃ sādhūnāmākrośāyaiva jāyate
tatra sādhuḥ prayatnena sāmasādhyo narottama || 4 ||
mahākulīnā ṛjavo dharmanityā jitendri yāḥ
sāmasādhyā na cātathyaṃ teṣu sāma prayojayet || 5 ||
tathyaṃ sāma ca kartavyaṃ kulaśīlādivarṇanam
tathā tadupacārāṇāṃ kṛtānāṃ caiva varṇanam || 6 ||
anayaiva tathā yuktyā kṛtajñākhyāpanaṃ svakam
evaṃ sāmnā ca kartavyā vaśagā dharmatatparāḥ || 7 ||
sāmnā yadyapi rakṣāṃsi gṛhṇantīti parā śrutiḥ
tathā'pyetadasādhūnāṃ prayuktaṃ nopakārakam || 8 ||
atiśaṅkitamityevaṃ puruṣaṃ sāmavādinam
asādhavo vijānanti tasmāttatteṣu varjayet || 9 ||
ye śuddhavaṃśā ṛjavaḥ praṇītā dharme sthitāḥ satyaparā vinītāḥ
te sāmasādhyāḥ puruṣāḥ pradiṣṭā mānonnatā ye satataṃ ca rājan || 10 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe rājadharme sāmabodho nāma dvāviṃśatyadhikaśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 222 ||

<223>

atha trayoviṃśatyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
matsya uvāca
parasparaṃ tu ye duṣṭāḥ kruddhā bhītāvamānitāḥ
teṣāṃ bhedaṃ prayuñjīta bhedasādhyā hi te matāḥ || 1 ||
te tu yenaiva doṣeṇa parasmānnāpi bibhyati
te tu taddoṣapātena bhedanīyā bhṛśaṃ tataḥ || 2 ||
ātmīyāṃ darśayedāśāṃ parasmāddarśayedbhayam
evaṃ hi bhedayedbhinnānyathāvadvaśamānayet || 3 ||
saṃhatā hi vinā bhedaṃ śakreṇāpi suduḥsahāḥ
bhedameva praśaṃsanti tasmānnayaviśāradāḥ || 4 ||
svamukhenā''śrayedbhedaṃ bhedaṃ paramukhena ca
parīkṣya sādhu manyeta bhedaṃ paramukhācchrutam || 5 ||
sadyaḥ svakāryamuddiśya kuśalairye hi bheditāḥ
bheditāste vinirdiṣṭā naiva rājñā'rthavādibhiḥ || 6 ||
antaḥ kopo bahiḥ kopo yatra syātāṃ mahīkṣitām
antaḥkopo mahāṃstatra nāśakaḥ pṛthivīkṣitām || 7 ||
sāmantakopo bāhyastu kopaḥ prokto mahīmṛtaḥ
mahiṣīyuvarājābhyāṃ tathā senāpaternṛpa || 8 ||
amātyamantriṇaṃ caiva rājaputre tathaiva ca
antaḥkopo vinirdiṣṭo dāruṇaḥ pṛthivīkṣitām || 9 ||
bāhyakope samutpanne sumahatyapi pārthivaḥ
śuddhāntastu mahābhāga śīghrameva jayī bhavet || 10 ||
api śakrasamo rājā antaḥkopena naśyati
so'ntaḥkopaḥ prayatnena tasmādra kṣyo mahībhṛtā || 11 ||
parataḥ kopamutpādya bhedena vijigīṣuṇā
jñātīnāṃ bhedanaṃ kāryaṃ pareṣāṃ vijigīṣuṇā || 12 ||
rakṣyaścaiva prayatnena jñātibhedastathā''tmanaḥ
jñātayaḥ paritapyante satataṃ paritāpitāḥ || 13 ||
tathā'pi teṣāṃ kartavyaṃ sugambhīreṇa cetasā
grahaṇaṃ dānamānābhyāṃ bhedastebhyo mayaṃkaraḥ || 14 ||
na jñātimanugṛhṇanti na jñātiṃ viśvasanti ca
jñātibhirbhedanīyāstu ripavastena pārthivaiḥ || 15 ||
bhinnā hi śakyā ripavaḥ prabhūtāḥ svalpena sainyena nihantumājau
susaṃhatānāṃ hi tadastu bhedaḥ kāryo ripūṇāṃ nayaśāstravidbhiḥ || 16 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe rājadharme bhedapraśaṃsā nāma trayoviṃśatyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 223 ||

<224>

atha caturviṃśatyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
matsya uvāca
sarveṣāmapyupāyānāṃ dānaṃ śreṣṭhatamaṃ matam
sudatteneha bhavati dānenobhayalokajit || 1 ||
na so'sti rājandānena vaśago yo na jāyate
dānena vaśagā devā bhavantīha sadā nṛṇām || 2 ||
dānamevopajīvanti prajāḥ sarvā nṛpottama
priyo hi dānavām̐ lloke sarvasyaivopajāyate || 3 ||
dānavānacireṇaiva tathā rājā parāñjayet
dānavāneva śaknoti saṃhatānbhedituṃ parān || 4 ||
yadyapyalubdhagambhīrāḥ puruṣāḥ sāgaropamāḥ
na gṛhṇanti tathā'pyete jāyante pakṣapātinaḥ || 5 ||
anyatrāpi kṛtaṃ dānaṃ karotyanyānyathā vaśe
upāyebhyaḥ praśaṃsanti dānaṃ śreṣṭhatamaṃ janāḥ || 6 ||
dānaṃ śreyaskaraṃ puṃsāṃ dānaṃ śreṣṭhatamaṃ param
dānavāneva lokeṣu putratve dhriyate sadā || 7 ||
na kevalaṃ dānaparā jayanti bhūrlokamekaṃ puruṣapravīrāḥ
jayanti te rājasurendra lokaṃ sudurjayaṃ yo vibudhādhivāsaḥ || 8 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe rājadharmadānapraśaṃsā nāma caturviṃśatyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 224 ||

<225>

atha pañcaviṃśatyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
matsya uvāca
na śaktyā ye vaśe kartumupāyatritayena tu
daṇḍena tānvaśī kuryāddaṇḍo hi vaśakṛnnṛṇām || 1 ||
samyakpraṇayanaṃ tasya tathā kāryaṃ mahīkṣitā
dharmaśāstrānusāreṇa susahāyena dhīmatā || 2 ||
tasya samyakpraṇayanaṃ yathā kāryaṃ mahīkṣitā
vānaprasthāṃśca dharmajñānnirmamānniṣparigrahān || 3 ||
svadeśe paradeśe vā dharmaśāstraviśāradān
samīkṣya praṇayeddaṇḍaṃ sarvaṃ daṇḍe pratiṣṭhitam || 4 ||
āśramī yadi vā varṇī pūjyo vā'tha gururmahān
nādaṇḍyo nāma rājño'sti yaḥ svadharmeṇa tiṣṭhati || 5 ||
adaṇḍyāndaṇḍayanrājā daṇḍyāṃścaivāpyadaṇḍayan
iha rājyātparibhraṣṭo narakaṃ ca prapadyate || 6 ||
tasmādrā jñā vinītena dharmaśāstrānusārataḥ
daṇḍapraṇayanaṃ kāryaṃ lokānugrahakāmyayā || 7 ||
yatra śyāmo lohitākṣo daṇḍaścarati nirbhayaḥ
prajāstatra na muhyanti netā cetsādhu paśyati || 8 ||
bālavṛddhāturayatidvijastrīvidhavā yataḥ
mātsyanyāyena bhakṣyeranyadi daṇḍaṃ na pātayet || 9 ||
devadaityoragagaṇāḥ sarve bhūtapatattriṇaḥ
utkrāmayeyurmaryādāṃ yadi daṇḍaṃ na pātayet || 10 ||
eṣa brahmābhiśāpeṣu sarvapraharaṇeṣu ca
sarvavikramakopeṣu vyavasāye ca tiṣṭhati || 11 ||
pūjyante daṇḍino devairna pūjyante tvadaṇḍinaḥ
na brahmāṇaṃ vidhātāraṃ na pūṣāryamaṇāvapi || 12 ||
yajante mānavāḥ kecitpraśāntāḥ sarvakarmasu
rudra magniṃ ca śakraṃ ca sūryācandra masau tathā || 13 ||
viṣṇuṃ devagaṇāṃścānyāndaṇḍinaḥ pūjayanti ca
daṇḍaḥ śāsti prajāḥ sarvā daṇḍa evābhirakṣati || 14 ||
daṇḍaḥ supteṣu jāgarti daṇḍaṃ dharmaṃ vidurbudhāḥ
rājadaṇḍabhayādeva pāpāḥ pāpaṃ na kurvate || 15 ||
yamadaṇḍabhayādeke parasparabhayādapi
evaṃ sāṃsiddhike loke sarvaṃ daṇḍe pratiṣṭhitam || 16 ||
andhe tamasi majjeyuryadi daṇḍaṃ na pātayet
yasmāddaṇḍo damayati durmadāndaṇḍayatyapi
damanāddaṇḍanāccaiva tasmāddaṇḍaṃ vidurbudhāḥ || 17 ||
daṇḍasya bhītaistridaśaiḥ sametairbhāgo dhṛtaḥ śūladharasya yajñe
dattaṃ kumāre dhvajinīpatitvaṃ varaṃ śiśūnāṃ ca bhayādbalastham || 18 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe rājadharme
daṇḍapraśaṃsā nāma pañcaviṃśatyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 225 ||

<226>

atha ṣaḍviṃśatyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
matsya uvāca
daṇḍapraṇayanārthāya rājā sṛṣṭaḥ svayaṃbhuvā
devabhāgānupādāya sarvabhūtādiguptaye || 1 ||
tejasā yadamuṃ kaścinnaiva śaknoti vīkṣitum
tato bhavati lokeṣu rājā bhāskaravatprabhuḥ || 2 ||
yadā'sya darśane lokaḥ prasādamupagacchati
nayanānandakāritvāttadā bhavati candra māḥ || 3 ||
yathā yamaḥ priyadveṣye prāpte kāle prayacchati
tathā rājñā vidhātavyāḥ prajāstaddhi yamavratam || 4 ||
varuṇena yathā pāśairbaddha eva pradṛśyate
tathā pāpānnigṛhṇīyādvratametaddhi vāruṇam || 5 ||
paripūrṇaṃ yathā candraṃ dṛṣṭvā hṛṣyati mānavaḥ
tathā prakṛtayo yasminsa candra pratimo nṛpaḥ || 6 ||
pratāpayuktastejasvī nityaṃ syātpāpakarmasu
duṣṭasāmantahiṃsreṣu rājā''gneyavrate sthitaḥ || 7 ||
yathā sarvāṇi bhūtāni dharā dhārayate svayam
tathā sarvāṇi bhūtāni bibhrataḥ pārthivaṃ vratam || 8 ||
indra syārkasya vātasya yamasya varuṇasya ca
candra syāgneḥ pṛthivyāśca tejovrataṃ nṛpaścaret || 9 ||
vārṣikāṃścaturo māsānyathendro 'pyabhivarṣati
tathā'bhivarṣetsvaṃ rāṣṭraṃ kāmamindra vrataṃ smṛtam || 10 ||
aṣṭau māsānyathā''dityastoyaṃ harati raśmibhiḥ
tathā haretkaraṃ rāṣṭrānnityamarkavrataṃ hi tat || 11 ||
praviśya sarvabhūtāni yathā carati mārutaḥ
tathā cāraiḥ praveṣṭavyaṃ vratametaddhi mārutam || 12 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe rājadharme rājño lokapālasāmyanirdeśo nāma ṣaḍviṃśatyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 226 ||

<227>

atha saptaviṃśatyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
matsya uvāca
nikṣepasya samaṃ mūlyaṃ daṇḍyo nikṣepabhuk tathā
vastrādikasamastasya tadā dharmo na hīyate || 1 ||
yo nikṣepaṃ nārpayati yaścānikṣipya yācate
tāvubhau coravacchāsyau dāpyau vā dviguṇaṃ dhanam || 2 ||
upadhābhiśca yaḥ kaścitparadra vyaṃ harennaraḥ
sasahāyaḥ sa hantavyaḥ prakāmaṃ vividhairvadhaiḥ || 3 ||
yo yācitaṃ samādāya na taddadyādyathākramam
sa nigṛhya balāddāpyo daṇḍyo vā pūrvasāhasam || 4 ||
ajñānādyadi vā kuryātparadra vyasya vikrayam
nirdoṣo jñānapūrvaṃ tu coravadvadhamarhati || 5 ||
mūlyamādāya yo vidyāṃ śilpaṃ vā na prayacchati
daṇḍyaḥ sa mūlyaṃ sakalaṃ dharmajñena mahīkṣitā || 6 ||
dvijabhojye tu saṃprāpte prativeśmamabhojayan
hiraṇyamāṣakaṃ daṇḍyaḥ pāpe nāsti vyatikramaḥ || 7 ||
āmantrito dvijo yastu vartamānaśca sve gṛhe
niṣkāraṇaṃ na gacchedyaḥ sa dāpyo'ṣṭaśataṃ damam
pratiśrutyāpradātāraṃ suvarṇaṃ daṇḍayennṛpaḥ || 8 ||
bhṛtyaścā''jñāṃ na kuryādyo darpātkarma yathoditam
sa daṇḍyaḥ kṛṣṇalānyaṣṭau na deyaṃ cāsya vetanam || 9 ||
saṃgṛhītaṃ na dadyādyaḥ kāle vetanameva ca
akāle tu tyajedmṛtyaṃ daṇḍyaḥ syācchatameva ca || 10 ||
yo grāmadeśasasyānāṃ kṛtvā satyena saṃvidam
visaṃvadennaro lobhāttaṃ rāṣṭrādvipravāsayet || 11 ||
krītvā vikrīya vā kiṃcidyasyehānuśayī bhavet
so'ntardaśāhāttatsāmyaṃ dadyāccaivā''dadīta vā || 12 ||
pareṇa tu daśāhasya na dadyānnaiva dāpayet
ādadanna dadaccaiva rājñā daṇḍyaḥ śatāni ṣaṭ || 13 ||
yastu doṣavatīṃ kanyāmanākhyāya prayacchati
tasya kuryānnṛpo daṇḍaṃ svayaṃ ṣaṇṇavatiṃ paṇān || 14 ||
akanyaiveti yaḥ kanyāṃ brūyāddoṣeṇa mānavaḥ
sa śataṃ prāpnuyāddaṇḍaṃ tasyā doṣamadarśayan || 15 ||
yastvanyāṃ darśayitvā'nyāṃ voḍhuḥ kanyāṃ prayacchati
uttamaṃ tasya kurvīta rājā daṇḍaṃ tu sāhasam || 16 ||
varo doṣānanākhyāya yaḥ kanyāṃ varayediha
dattā'pyadattā sā tasya rājñā daṇḍyaḥ śatadvayam || 17 ||
pradāya kanyāṃ yo'nyasmai punastāṃ saṃprayacchati
daṇḍaḥ kāryo narendre ṇa tasyāpyuttamasāhasaḥ || 18 ||
satyaṃkāreṇa vā vācā yuktaṃ paṇyamasaṃśayam
lubdho hyanyatra vikretā ṣaṭśataṃ daṇḍamarhati || 19 ||
duhituḥ śulkavikretā satyaṃkārāttu saṃtyajet
dviguṇaṃ daṇḍayedenamiti dharmo vyavasthitaḥ || 20 ||
mūlyaikadeśaṃ dattvā tu yadi kretā dhanaṃ tyajet
sa daṇḍyo madhyamaṃ daṇḍaṃ tasya paṇyasya mokṣaṇam || 21 ||
duhyāddhenuṃ ca yaḥ pālo gṛhītvā bhaktavetanam
sa tu daṇḍyaḥ śataṃ rājñā suvarṇaṃ cāpyarakṣitā || 22 ||
daṇḍaṃ dattvā tu virametsvāmitaḥ kṛtalakṣaṇaḥ
baddhaḥ kārṣṇāyasaiḥ pāśaistasya karmakaro bhavet || 23 ||
dhanuḥśataparīṇāho grāmasya tu samantataḥ
dviguṇaṃ triguṇaṃ vā'pi nagarasya tu kalpayet || 24 ||
vṛttiṃ tatra prakurvīta yāmuṣṭro nāvalokayet
chidraṃ vā vārayetsarvaṃ śvaśūkaramukhānugam || 25 ||
yatrāparivṛtaṃ dhānyaṃ vihiṃsyuḥ paśavo yadi
na tatra kārayeddaṇḍaṃ nṛpatiḥ paśurakṣiṇe || 26 ||
anirdaśāhāṃ gāṃ sūtāṃ vṛṣaṃ devapaśuṃ tathā
chidraṃ vā vārayetsarvaṃ na daṇḍyamanurabravīt || 27 ||
ato'nyathā vinaṣṭasya daśāṃśaṃ daṇḍamarhati
pālyasya pālakasvāmī vināśe kṣattriyasya tu || 28 ||
bhakṣayitvopaviṣṭastu dviguṇaṃ daṇḍamarhati
viśaṃ daṇḍyāddaśaguṇaṃ vināśe kṣattriyasya tu || 29 ||
gṛhaṃ taḍāgamārāmaṃ kṣetraṃ vā'pi samāharan
śatāni pañca daṇḍaḥ syādajñānāddviśato damaḥ || 30 ||
sīmābandhanakāle tu sīmāntaṃ yo hi kārayet
teṣāṃ saṃjñāṃ dadānastu jihvācchedanamāpnuyāt || 31 ||
athaināmapi yo dadyātsaṃvidaṃ vā'dhigacchati
uttamaṃ sāhasaṃ daṇḍya iti svāyaṃbhuvo'bravīt || 32 ||
varṇānāmānupūrvyeṇa trayāṇāmaviśeṣataḥ
akāryakāriṇaḥ sarvānprāyaścittāni kārayet || 33 ||
asatyena pramāpya strīṃ śūdra hatyāvrataṃ caret
dānena ca dhanenaikaṃ sarpādīnāmaśaknuvan || 34 ||
ekaikaṃ sa caretkṛcchraṃ dvijaḥ pāpāpanuttaye
phaladānāṃ ca vṛkṣāṇāṃ chedane japyamṛkśatam || 35 ||
gulmavallīlatānāṃ ca puṣpitānāṃ ca vīrudhām
asthimatāṃ ca sattvānāṃ sahasrasya pramāpaṇe
pūrṇe vā'nasyavasthātuṃ śūdra hatyāvrataṃ caret || 36 ||
kiṃciddeyaṃ ca viprāya dadyādasthimatāṃ vadhe
anasthnāṃ caiva hiṃsāyāṃ prāṇāyāmairviśudhyati || 37 ||
annādijānāṃ sattvānāṃ rasajānāṃ ca sarvaśaḥ
phalapuṣpodgatānāṃ ca ghṛtaprāśo viśodhanam || 38 ||
kṛṣṭānāmoṣadhīnāṃ ca jātānāṃ ca svayaṃ vane
vṛthā chedena gaccheta dinamekaṃ payovratī || 39 ||
etairvratairapohyaṃ syādeno hiṃsāsamudbhavam
steyadoṣāpahartṝṇāṃ śrūyatāṃ vratamuttamam || 40 ||
dhānyānnadhanacauryāṇi kṛtvā kāmāddvijottamaḥ
sajātīyagṛhādeva kṛcchrārdhena viśudhyati || 41 ||
manuṣyāṇāṃ tu haraṇe strīṇāṃ kṣetragṛhasya tu
kūpavāpījalānāṃ tu śuddhiścāndrā yaṇaṃ smṛtam || 42 ||
dra vyāṇāmalpasārāṇāṃ steyaṃ kṛtvā'nyaveśmataḥ
caretsāṃtapanaṃ kṛcchraṃ tanniryātya viśuddhaye || 43 ||
bhakṣyabhojyāpaharaṇe yānaśayyāsanasya tu
puṣpamūlaphalānāṃ tu pañcagavyaṃ viśodhanam || 44 ||
tṛṇakāṣṭhadrumāṇāṃ tu śuṣkānnasya guḍasya ca
cailacarmāmiṣāṇāṃ tu trirātraṃ syādabhojanam || 45 ||
maṇimuktāpravālānāṃ tāmrasya rajatasya ca
