ayaskāṃsyopalānāṃ ca dvādaśāhaṃ kaṇānnabhuk || 46 ||
kārpāsakīṭajorṇānāṃ dviśaphaikaśaphasya ca
pakṣigandhauṣadhīnāṃ ca rajjvāścaiva tryahaṃ payaḥ || 47 ||
etairvratairvyapoheta pāpaṃ steyakṛtaṃ dvijaḥ
agamyāgamanīyaṃ tu vratairebhirapānudet || 48 ||
gurutalpavrataṃ kuryādre taḥ siktvā svayoniṣu
sakhyuḥ putrasya ca strīṣu kumārīṣvantyajāsu ca || 49 ||
paitṛṣvasrīyabhaginīṃ svasrīyāṃ mātureva ca
bhrāturduhitaraṃ caiva gatvā cāndrā yaṇaṃ caret || 50 ||
etāḥ striyastu bhāryārthe nopagacchettu buddhimān
jñātīnāṃ ca striyo yāstu patitānugatāśca yāḥ || 51 ||
amānuṣīṣu puruṣo udakyāyāmayoniṣu
retaḥ siktvā jale caiva kṛcchraṃ sāṃtapanaṃ caret || 52 ||
maithunaṃ ca samālokya puṃsi yoṣiti vā dvijaḥ
goyāne'psu divā caiva savāsāḥ snānamācaret || 53 ||
cāṇḍālāntyastriyo gatvā bhuktvā ca pratigṛhya ca
patatyajñānato vipro jñānātsāmyaṃ tu gacchati || 54 ||
vipraduṣṭāṃ striyaṃ bhartā nirundhyādekaveśmani
yatpuṃsaḥ paradāreṣu taccaināṃ cārayedvratam || 55 ||
sā cetpunaḥ praduṣyettu sadṛśenopamantritā
kṛcchraṃ cāndrā yaṇaṃ caiva tattasyāḥ pāvanaṃ smṛtam || 56 ||
yaḥ karotyekarātreṇa vṛṣalīsevanaṃ dvijaḥ
tadbhakṣyabhugjapennityaṃ tribhirvarṣairvyapohati || 57 ||
eṣā pāpakṛtāmuktā caturṇāmapi niṣkṛtiḥ
patitaiḥ saṃprayuktānāmimāṃ śṛṇuta niṣkṛtim || 58 ||
saṃvatsareṇa patati patitena sahā''caran
vājanādhyāpanādyaunāttulyayānāśanāsanāt || 59 ||
yo yena patitenaiṣāṃ saṃsargaṃ yāti mānavaḥ
sa tasyaiva vrataṃ kuryāttatsaṃsargaviśuddhaye || 60 ||
patitasyodakaṃ kāryaṃ sapiṇḍairbāndhavaiḥ saha
nindite'hani sāyāhne jñātibhirgurusaṃnidhau || 61 ||
dāsī ghaṭamapāṃ pūrṇaṃ parvasyetpretavatsadā
ahorātramupāsīrannāśaucaṃ bāndhavaiḥ saha || 62 ||
nivartayeraṃstasmāttu saṃbhāṣaṇasahāsanam
dāyādasya pramāṇaṃ ca yātrāmevaṃ ca laukikīm || 63 ||
jyeṣṭhabhāvānnivarteta jyeṣṭhāvāptaṃ ca yatpunaḥ
jyeṣṭhāṃśaṃ prāpnuyāccāsya yavīyānguṇato'dhikaḥ || 64 ||
sthāpitāṃ cāpi maryādāṃ ye bhindyuḥ pāpakarmiṇaḥ
sarve pṛthakpṛthagdaṇḍyā rājñā prathamasāhasam || 65 ||
śataṃ brāhmaṇamākruśya kṣattriyo daṇḍamarhati
vaiśyastu dviśataṃ rājañchūdra stu vadhamarhati || 66 ||
pañcāśadbrāhmaṇo daṇḍyaḥ kṣattriyasyābhiśaṃsane
vaiśyasyāpyardhapañcāśacchūdre dvādaśako damaḥ || 67 ||
kṣattriyasyā''pnuyādvaiśyaḥ sāhasaṃ punareva ca
śūdraḥ kṣattriyamākruśya jihvācchedanamāpnuyāt || 68 ||
pañcāśatkṣattriyo daṇḍyastathā vaiśyābhiśaṃsane
śūdre caivārdhapañcāśattathā dharmo na hīyate || 69 ||
vaiśvasyā''krośane daṇḍaḥ śūdra ścottamasāhasam
śūdrā krośe tathā vaiśyaḥ śatārdhaṃ daṇḍamarhati || 70 ||
savarṇākrośane daṇḍyastathā dvādaśakaṃ smṛtam
vādeṣvavacanīyeṣu tadeva dviguṇaṃ bhavet || 71 ||
ekajātirdvijātiṃ tu vācā dāruṇayā kṣipan
jihvāyāḥ prāpnuyācchedaṃ jaghanyaḥ prathamo hi saḥ || 72 ||
nāmajātigṛhaṃ teṣāmabhidro heṇa kurvataḥ
nikṣepyo'yomayaḥ śaṅkurjvalannāsye daśāṅgulaḥ || 73 ||
dharmopadeśaṃ śūdra stu dvijānāmabhikurvataḥ
taptamāsecayettailaṃ vaktre śrotre ca pārthivaḥ || 74 ||
śrutiṃ deśaṃ ca jātiṃ ca karma śārīrameva ca
vitathaṃ ca bruvandaḍyo rājñā dviguṇasāhasam || 75 ||
yastu pātakasaṃyuktaḥ kṣipedvarṇāntaraṃ naraḥ
uttamaṃ sāhasaṃ daṇḍaḥ pātyastasminyathākramam || 76 ||
rājño niveśaniyamaṃ vitathaṃ yānti vai mithaḥ
sarve dviguṇadaṇḍyāste vipralambhānnṛpasya tu || 77 ||
prītyā mayā'syābhihitaṃ pramādenāthavā vadet
bhūyo na caivaṃ vakṣyāmi sa tu daṇḍārdhabhāgbhavet || 78 ||
kāṇaṃ vā'pyathavā khañjamandhaṃ cāpi tathāvidham
tathyenāpi bruvandāpyo daṇḍaṃ kārṣāpaṇaṃ dhanam || 79 ||
mātaraṃ pitaraṃ jyeṣṭhaṃ bhrātaraṃ śvaśuraṃ gurum
ākrośayañchataṃ daṇḍyaḥ panthānaṃ cārdayanguroḥ || 80 ||
guruvarjyaṃ tu mānārhaṃ yo hi mārgaṃ na yacchati
sa dāpyaḥ kṛṣṇalaṃ rājastasya pāpasya śāntaye || 81 ||
ekajātirdvijātiṃ tu yenāṅgenāparādhnuyāt
tadeva cchedayettasya kṣipramevāvicārayan || 82 ||
avaniṣṭhīvato darpāddvāvoṣṭhau cchedayennṛpaḥ
avamūtrayato meḍhramapaśabdayato gudam || 83 ||
sahāsanamabhiprepsurutkṛṣṭasyāpakṛṣṭajaḥ
kaṭyāṃ kṛtāṅko nirvāsyaḥ sphicaṃ vā'pyasya kartayet || 84 ||
keśeṣu gṛhṇato hastaṃ chedayedavicārayan
pādayornāsikāyāṃ ca grīvāyāṃ vṛṣaṇeṣu ca || 85 ||
tvagbhedakaḥ śataṃ daṇḍyo lohitasya ca darśakaḥ
māṃsabhettā ca ṣaṇniṣkānnirvāsyastvasthibhedakaḥ || 86 ||
aṅgabhaṅgakarasyāṅgaṃ tadevāpaharennṛpaḥ
daṇḍapāruṣyakṛddaṇḍyaḥ samutthānavyayaṃ tathā || 87 ||
ardhapādakaraḥ kāryo gogajāśvoṣṭraghātakaḥ
paśukṣudra mṛgāṇāṃ ca hiṃsāyāṃ dviguṇo damaḥ || 88 ||
pañcāśacca bhaveddaṇḍyastathaiva mṛgapakṣiṣu
kṛmikīṭeṣu daṇḍyaḥ syādra jatasya ca māṣakam || 89 ||
tasyānurūpaṃ mūlyaṃ ca pradadyātsvāmine tathā
svasvāmikānāṃ sakalaṃ śeṣāṇāṃ daṇḍameva tu || 90 ||
vṛkṣaṃ tu saphalaṃ chittvā suvarṇaṃ daṇḍamarhati
dviguṇaṃ daṇḍayeccainaṃ pathi sīmni jalāśaye || 91 ||
chedanādaphalasyāpi madhyamaṃ sāhasaṃ smṛtam
gulmavallīlatānāṃ ca suvarṇasya ca māṣakam || 92 ||
vṛthācchedī tṛṇasyāpi daṇḍyaḥ kārṣāpaṇaṃ bhavet
tribhāgaṃ kṛṣṇalā daṇḍyāḥ prāṇinastāḍane tathā || 93 ||
deśakālānurūpeṇa mūlyaṃ rājā drumādiṣu
tatsvāminastathā daṇḍyā daṇḍamuktastu pārthiva || 94 ||
yatrātivartate yugyaṃ vaiguṇyātprājakasya tu
tatra svāmī bhaveddaṇḍyo nā''ptaścetprājako bhavet || 95 ||
prājakaśca bhavedāptaḥ prājako daṇḍamarhati
nāsti daṇḍaśca tasyāpi tathā vai hetukalpakaḥ || 96 ||
dra vyāṇi yo haredyasya jñānato'jñānato'pi vā
sa tasyotpādayettuṣṭiṃ rājño dadyāttato damam || 97 ||
yastu rajjuṃ ghaṭaṃ kūpāddharedbhindyācca tāṃ prapām
sa daṇḍaṃ prāpnuyānmāṣaṃ tacca saṃpratipādayet || 98 ||
dhānyaṃ daśabhyaḥ kumbhebhyo harato'bhyadhikaṃ vadhaḥ
śeṣe'pyekādaśaguṇaṃ tasya daṇḍaṃ prakalpayet || 99 ||
tathā bhakṣyānnapānānāṃ na tathā'pyadhike vadhaḥ
suvarṇarajatādīnāmuttamānāṃ ca vāsasām || 100 ||
puruṣāṇāṃ kulīnānāṃ nārīṇāṃ ca viśeṣataḥ
mahāpaśūnāṃ haraṇe śastrāṇāmauṣadhasya ca || 101 ||
mukhyānāṃ caiva ratnānāṃ haraṇe vadhamarhati
dadhnaḥ kṣīrasya takrasya pānīyasya rasasya ca || 102 ||
veṇuvaidalabhāṇḍānāṃ lavaṇānāṃ tathaiva ca
mṛnmayānāṃ ca sarveṣāṃ mṛdo bhasmana eva ca || 103 ||
kālamāsādya kāryaṃ ca rājā daṇḍaṃ prakalpayet
goṣu brāhmaṇasaṃsthāsu mahiṣīṣu tathaiva ca || 104 ||
aśvāpahārakaścaiva sadyaḥ kāryo'rdhapādakaḥ
sūtrakārpāsakiṇvānāṃ gomayasya guḍasya ca || 105 ||
matsyānāṃ pakṣiṇāṃ caiva tailasya ca ghṛtasya ca
māṃsasya madhunaścaiva yaccānyadvastusaṃbhavam || 106 ||
anyeṣāṃ lavaṇādīnāṃ madyānāmodanasya ca
pakvānnānāṃ ca sarveṣāṃ tanmūlyāddviguṇo damaḥ || 107 ||
puṣpeṣu harite dhānye gulmavallīlatāsu ca
anneṣu paripūrṇeṣu daṇḍaḥ syātpañcamāṣakam
paripūrṇeṣu dhānyeṣu śākamūlaphaleṣu ca || 108 ||
niranvaye śataṃ daṇḍyaḥ sānvaye dviśataṃ damaḥ
yena yena yathāṅgena steno'nyeṣu viceṣṭate || 109 ||
tattadeva harettasya pratyādeśāya pārthivaḥ
dvijo'dhvagaḥ kṣīṇavṛttirdvāvikṣū dve ca mūlake || 110 ||
trapusorvārukau dvau ca tāvanmātraṃ phaleṣu ca
tathā ca sarvadhānyānāṃ muṣṭigrāheṇa pārthiva || 111 ||
śāke śākapramāṇena gṛhyamāṇe na duṣyati
vānaspatyaṃ phalaṃ mūlaṃ dārvagnyarthaṃ tathaiva ca || 112 ||
tṛṇaṃ gobhyavahārārthamasteyaṃ manurabravīt
adevavāṭijaṃ puṣpaṃ devatārthaṃ tathaiva ca || 113 ||
ādadānaḥ parakṣetrānna daṇḍaṃ dātumarhati
śṛṅgiṇaṃ nakhinaṃ rājandaṃṣṭriṇaṃ ca vadhodyatam || 114 ||
yo hanyānna sa pāpena lipyate manujeśvara
guruṃ vā bālavṛddhaṃ vā brāhmaṇaṃ vā bahuśrutam || 115 ||
ātatāyinamāyāntaṃ hanyādevāvicārayan
nā''tatāyivadhe doṣo hanturbhavati kaścana || 116 ||
prakāśaṃ vā'prakāśaṃ vā manyustaṃ manyumṛcchati
gṛhakṣetrābhihartārastathā'gamyābhigāminaḥ || 117 ||
agnido garadaścaiva tathā cābhyudyatāyudhaḥ
abhicāraṃ tu kurvāṇo rājagāmi ca paiśunam || 118 ||
ete hi kathitā loke dharmajñairātatāyinaḥ
bhikṣuko'pyathavā nārī yo'pi vā syātkuśīlavaḥ || 119 ||
praviśetpratiṣiddhastu prāpnuyāddviguṇaṃ damaḥ
parastrīṇāṃ tu saṃbhāṣe tīrthe'raṇye gṛhe'pi vā || 120 ||
nadīnāṃ caiva saṃbhede sa saṃgrahaṇamāpnuyāt
na saṃbhāṣetparastrībhiḥ pratiṣiddhaḥ samācaret || 121 ||
pratiṣiddhe samābhāṣya suvarṇaṃ daṇḍamarhati
naiṣa cāraṇadāreṣu vidhirātmopajīviṣu || 122 ||
sajjayanti manuṣyaistā nigūḍhaṃ vā carantyuta
kiṃcideva tu dāpyaḥ syātsaṃbhāṣeṇāpacārayan || 123 ||
preṣyāsu caiva sarvāsu gṛhapravrajitāsu ca
yo'kāmāṃ dūṣayetkanyāṃ sa sadyo vadhamarhati || 124 ||
sakāmāṃ dūṣayāṇastu prāpnuyāddviśataṃ damam
yaśca saṃrakṣakastatra puruṣaḥ sa tathā bhavet || 125 ||
pāradārikavaddaṇḍyo yo'pi syādavakāśadaḥ
balātsaṃdūṣayedyastu parabhāryāṃ naraḥ kvacit || 126 ||
vadho daṇḍo bhavettasya nāparādho bhavetstriyāḥ
rajastṛtīyaṃ yā kanyā svagṛhe pratipadyate || 127 ||
adaṇḍyā sā bhavedrā jñā varayantī patiṃ svayam
svadeśe kanyakāṃ dattvā tāmādāya tathā vrajet || 128 ||
paradeśe bhavedvadhyaḥ strīcoraḥ sa yato bhavet
adra vyāṃ mṛtapatnīṃ tu saṃgṛhṇannāparādhnute || 129 ||
sadra vyāṃ tāṃ saṃgrahītā daṇḍaṃ tu kṣipramarhati
utkṛṣṭaṃ yā bhajetkanyā deyā tasyaiva sā bhavet || 130 ||
yaccānyaṃ sevamānāṃ ca saṃyatāṃ vāsayedgṛhe
uttamāṃ sevamānastu jaghanyo vadhamarhati
jaghanyamuttamā nārī sevamānā tathaiva ca || 131 ||
bhartāraṃ laṅghayedyā strī jñātibhirbaladarpitā
tāṃ ca niṣkāsayedrā jā saṃsthāne bahusaṃsthite || 132 ||
hṛtādhikārāṃ malināṃ piṇḍamātropajīvinīm
vāsayetsvairiṇīṃ nityaṃ savarṇenābhidūṣitām || 133 ||
jyāyasā dūṣitā nārī muṇḍanaṃ samavāpnuyāt
vāsaśca malinaṃ nityaṃ śikhāṃ saṃprāpnuyāddaśa || 134 ||
brāhmaṇaḥ kṣattriyo vaiśyaḥ kṣattraviṭśūdra yoṣitaḥ
vrajandāpyo bhavedrā jñā daṇḍamuttamasāhasam || 135 ||
vaiśyāgame ca viprasya kṣattriyasyāntyajāgame
madhyamaṃ prathamaṃ vaiśyo daṇḍyaḥ śūdrā gamādbhavet || 136 ||
śūdraḥ savarṇāgamane śataṃ daṇḍyo mahīkṣitā
vaiśyastu dviguṇaṃ rājankṣattriyastriguṇaṃ tathā || 137 ||
brāhmaṇaśca bhaveddaṇḍyastathā rājaṃścaturguṇam
aguptāsu bhaveddaṇḍaḥ suguptāsvadhiko bhavet || 138 ||
mātā pitṛṣvasā śvaśrūrmātulānī pitṛvyajā
pitṛvyasakhiśiṣyastrī bhaginī tatsakhī tathā
bhrātṛbhāryāgame pūrvāddaṇḍastu dviguṇo bhavet || 139 ||
bhāgineyī tathā caiva rājapatnī tathaiva ca
tathā pravrajitā nārī varṇotkṛṣṭā tathaiva ca || 140 ||
ityagamyāśca nirdiṣṭāstāsāṃ tu gamane naraḥ
śiśnasyotkartanaṃ kṛtvā tatastu vadhamarhati || 141 ||
caṇḍālīṃ ca śvapākīṃ ca gacchanvadhamavāpnuyāt || 142 ||
tiryagyoniṃ ca govarjyaṃ maithunaṃ yo niṣevate
vapanaṃ prāpnuyāddaṇḍaṃ tasyāśca yavasodakam || 143 ||
suvarṇaṃ ca bhaveddaṇḍyo gāṃ vrajanmanujottama
veśyāgāmī dvijo daṇḍyo veśyāśulkasamaṃ paṇam || 144 ||
gṛhītvā vetanaṃ veśyā lobhādanyatra gacchati
vetanaṃ dviguṇaṃ dadyāddaṇḍaṃ ca dviguṇaṃ tathā || 145 ||
anyamuddiśya yo veśyāṃ nayedanyasya kāraṇāt
tasya daṇḍo bhavedrā jansuvarṇasya ca māṣakam || 146 ||
nītvā bhogānna yo dadyāddāpyo dviguṇavetanam
rājñaśca dviguṇaṃ daṇḍaṃ tathā dharmo na hīyate || 147 ||
bahūnāṃ vrajatāmekāṃ sarve te dviguṇaṃ damam
dadyuḥ pṛthakpṛthaksarve daṇḍaṃ ca dviguṇaṃ param || 148 ||
na mātā na pitā na strī na ṛtvigyājyamānavāḥ
anyonyaṃ patitāstyājyā yoge daṇḍyāḥ śatāni ṣaṭ || 149 ||
patitā guravastyājyā na tu mātā kathaṃ cana
garbhadhāraṇapoṣābhyāṃ tena mātā garīyasī || 150 ||
adhīyāno'pyanadhyāye daṇḍyaḥ kārṣāpaṇatrayam
adhyāpakaśca dviguṇaṃ tathā''cārasya laṅghane || 151 ||
anuktasya bhaveddaṇḍaḥ suvarṇasya ca kṛṣṇalam
bhāryā putraśca dāsaśca śiṣyo bhrātā ca sodaraḥ || 152 ||
kṛtāparādhāstāḍyāḥ syū rajjvā veṇudalena vā
pṛṣṭhatastu śarīrasya nottamāṅgaṃ kathaṃ cana || 153 ||
ato'nyathā praharataḥ prāpnuyāccorakilbiṣam
dūtīṃ samāhvayaṃścaiva yo niṣiddhaṃ samācaret || 154 ||
pracchannaṃ vā prakāśaṃ vā sa daṇḍyaḥ pārthivecchayā
vāsāṃsi phalakaiḥ ślakṣṇairnirṇijyādra jakaḥ śanaiḥ || 155 ||
ato'nyathā hi kurvaṃstu daṇḍyaḥ syādrukmamāṣakam
rakṣāsvadhikṛtānāṃ ca pradeyaṃ yairvilupyate || 156 ||
karṣakebhyo'rthamādāya yaḥ kuryātkaramanyathā
tasya sarvasvamādāya taṃ rājā vipravāsayet || 157 ||
ye niyuktāḥ svakāryeṣu hanyuḥ kāryāṇi kāryiṇām
nirghṛṇāḥ krūramanasaḥ sarve karmāparādhinaḥ || 158 ||
dhanoṣmaṇā pacyamānāstānniḥsvānkārayennṛpaḥ
kūṭaśāsanakartṛṃśca prakṛtīnāṃ ca dūṣakān || 159 ||
strībālabrāhmaṇaghnāṃśca vadhyāttatsevinastathā
amātyaḥ prāṅvivāko vā yaḥ kuryātkāryamanyathā || 160 ||
tasya sarvasvamādāya taṃ rājā vipravāsayet
brahmaghnaśca surāpaśca taskaro gurutalpagaḥ || 161 ||
etānsarvānpṛthagghiṃsyānmahāpātakino narān
mahāpātakino vadhyā brāhmaṇaṃ tu vivāsayet || 162 ||
kṛtacihnaṃ svadeśācca śṛṇu cihnākṛtiṃ tataḥ
gurutalpe bhagaḥ kāryaḥ surāpāne surādhvajaḥ || 163 ||
stene tu śvapadaṃ tadvadbrahmahaṇyaśirāḥ pumān
asaṃbhāṣyā hyasaṃbhojyā asaṃvāhyā viśeṣataḥ || 164 ||
tyaktavyāśca tathā rājañjñātisaṃbandhibāndhavaiḥ
mahāpātakino vittamādāya nṛpatiḥsvayam || 165 ||
apsu praveśayeddaṇḍaṃ varuṇāyopapādayet
sahoḍhaṃ na vinā coraṃ ghātayeddhārmiko nṛpaḥ || 166 ||
sahoḍhaṃ sopakaraṇaṃ ghātayedavicārayan
grāmeṣvapi ca ye keciccorāṇāṃ bhakṣyadāyakāḥ || 167 ||
bhāṇḍāvakāśadāścaiva sarvāṃstānapi ghātayet
rāṣṭreṣu rājñā'dhikṛtāḥ sāmantāścaiva dūṣakāḥ || 168 ||
abhyāghāteṣu madhyasthāḥ kṣipraṃ śāsyāstu coravat
grāmaghāte maṭhābhaṅge pathi moṣābhimardane || 169 ||
śaktito nābhidhāvanto nirvāsyāḥ saparicchadāḥ
rājñaḥ kośāpahartuṃśca pratikūleṣu saṃsthitān || 170 ||
arīṇāmupakartṛṃśca ghātayedvividhairvadhaiḥ
saṃdhiṃ kṛtvā tu ye cauryaṃ rātrau kurvanti taskarāḥ || 171 ||
teṣāṃ chittvā nṛpo hastau tīkṣṇaśūle niveśayet
taḍāgabhedakaṃ hanyādapsu śuddhavadhena tu || 172 ||
yastu pūrvaṃ niviṣṭaṃ syāttaḍāgasyodakaṃ haret
āgamaṃ cāpyapāṃ bhindyātsa dāpyaḥ pūrvasāhasam || 173 ||
koṣṭhāgārāyudhāgāradevāgāravibhedakān
pāpānpāpasamācārānghātayecchīghrameva ca || 174 ||
samutsṛjedrā jamārge yastvamedhyamanāpadi
sa hi kārṣāpaṇaṃ daṇḍyastattvamedhyaṃ ca śodhayet || 175 ||
āpadgato'thavā vṛddho garbhiṇī bāla eva ca
paribhāṣaṇamarhanti na ca śodhyamiti sthitiḥ || 176 ||
prathamaṃ sāhasaṃ daṇḍyo yaśca mithyā cikitsate
puruṣe madhyamaṃ daṇḍamuttamaṃ ca tathottame || 177 ||
chatrasya dhvajayaṣṭīnāṃ pratimānāṃ ca bhedakāḥ
pratikuryustataḥ sarve pañca daṇḍyāḥ śatāni ca || 178 ||
adūṣitānāṃ dra vyāṇāṃ dūṣaṇe bhedane tathā
maṇīnāmapi bhedena daṇḍyaḥ prathamasāhasam || 179 ||
samaṃ ca viṣamaṃ caiva kurute mūlyato'pi vā
samāpnuyātsa vai pūrvaṃ damaṃ madhyamameva ca || 180 ||
bandhanāni ca sarvāṇi rājamārge niveśayet
karṣanto yatra dṛśyante vikṛtāḥ pāpakāriṇaḥ || 181 ||
prākārasya ca bhettāraṃ parikhāṇāṃ ca bhedakam
dvārāṇāṃ caiva bhettāraṃ kṣipraṃ nirvāsayetpurāt || 182 ||
mūlakarmābhicāreṣu kartavyo dviśato damaḥ
abījavikrayī yaśca bījotkarṣaka eva ca || 183 ||
maryādāmedakaścāpi vikṛtaṃ vadhamāpnuyāt
sarvasaṃkarapāpiṣṭhaṃ hemakāraṃ narādhipa || 184 ||
anyāye vartamānaṃ ca cchedayellavaśaḥ kṣuraiḥ
dra vyamādāya vaṇijāmanardheṇāvarundhatām || 185 ||
dra vyāṇāṃ dūṣako yastu praticchannasya vikrayī
madhyamaṃ prāpnuyāddaṇḍaṃ kūṭakartā tathottamam || 186 ||
rājā pṛthakpṛthakkuryāddaṇḍaṃ cottamasāhasam
śāstrāṇāṃ yajñatapasāṃ deśānāṃ kṣepako naraḥ || 187 ||
devatānāṃ satīnāṃ ca uttamaṃ daṇḍamarhati
ekasya daṇḍapāruṣye bahūnāṃ dviguṇo damaḥ || 188 ||
kalaho yadgato dāpyo daṇḍaśca dviguṇastataḥ
madhyamaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ rājā viṣayādvipravāsayet || 189 ||
laśunaṃ ca palāḍuṃ ca śūkaraṃ grāmakukkuṭam
tathā pañcanakhaṃ sarvaṃ bhakṣyādanyattu bhakṣayet || 190 ||
vivāsayetkṣiprameva brāhmaṇaṃ viṣayātsvakāt
abhakṣyabhakṣaṇe daṇḍyaḥ śūdro bhavati kṛṣṇalam || 191 ||
brāhmaṇakṣattriyaviśāṃ catustridviguṇaṃ smṛtam
yaḥ sāhasaṃ kārayati sa daṇḍyo dviguṇaṃ damam || 192 ||
yastvevamuktvā'haṃ dātā kārayetsa caturguṇam
saṃdiṣṭasyāpradātā ca samudra gṛhabhedakaḥ || 193 ||
pañcāśatpaṇiko daṇḍastayoḥ kāryo mahīkṣitā
aspṛśyaṃ ca spṛśannāryo hyayogyo yogyakarmakṛt || 195 ||
puṃstvahartā paśūnāṃ ca dāsīgarbhavināśakṛt
śūdra pravrajitānāṃ ca daive pitrye ca bhojakaḥ || 196 ||
avrajanbāḍhamuktvā tu tathaiva ca nimantraṇe
ete kārṣāpaṇaśataṃ sarve daṇḍyā mahīkṣitā || 197 ||
duḥkhotpādi gṛhe dra vyaṃ kṣipedandhasya kṛṣṇalam
pitāputravirodhe ca sākṣiṇāṃ dviśato damaḥ
syānnaraśca tathā'ryaḥ syāttasyā'pyaṣṭaśato damaḥ || 198 ||
tulāśāsanamānānāṃ kūṭakṛnnānakasya ca
ebhiśca vyavahartā ca sa daṇḍyo damamuttamam || 199 ||
viṣāgnidāṃ patigurunijāpatyapramāpaṇīm
vikarṇanāsikāṃ vyoṣṭhīṃ kṛtvā gobhiḥ pramāpayet || 200 ||
grāmasya dāhakā ye ca ye ca kṣetrasya veśmanaḥ
rājapatnyabhigāmī ca dagdhavyāste kaṭāgninā || 201 ||
ūnaṃ vā'pyadhikaṃ cāpi likhedyo rājaśāsanam
pāradārikacauraṃ vā muñcato daṇḍa uttamaḥ || 202 ||
abhakṣyeṇa dvijaṃ dūṣya daṇḍya uttamasāhasam
kṣattriyaṃ madhyamaṃ vaiśyaṃ prathamaṃ śūdra mardhakam || 203 ||
mṛtāṅgalagnavikreturguruṃ tāḍayatastathā
rājayānāsanāroḍhurdaṇḍa uttamasāhasaḥ || 204 ||
yo manyetājito'smīti nyāyenāpi parājitaḥ
tamāyāntaṃ punarjitvā daṇḍayeddviguṇaṃ damam || 205 ||
āhvānakārī madhyaṃ syādanāhvāne tathā''hvayan
dāṇḍikasya ca yo hastādabhiyuktaḥ palāyate || 206 ||
hīnaḥ puruṣakāreṇa taṃ daṇḍyāddāṇḍiko dhanam
preṣyāparādhātpreṣyastu sa daṇḍyaścārdhameva ca || 207 ||
daṇḍārthaṃ niyamārthaṃ ca nīyamāneṣu bandhanam
yadi kaścitpalāyeta daṇḍaścāṣṭaguṇo bhavet || 208 ||
anindite vivāde tu nakharomāvatāraṇam
kārayedyaḥ sa puruṣo madhyamaṃ daṇḍamarhati || 209 ||
bandhanaṃ cāpyavadhyasya balānmocayate tu yaḥ
vadhyaṃ vimocayedyastu daṇḍyo dviguṇadaṇḍabhāk || 210 ||
durdṛṣṭyavyavahārāṇāṃ sabhyānāṃ dviguṇo damaḥ
rājñā triṃśadguṇo daṇḍaḥ prakṣepya udake bhavet || 211 ||
alpadaṇḍe'dhikaṃ kuryādvipule cālpameva ca
ūnādhikaṃ tu taṃ daṇḍaṃ sabhyo dadyātsvakādgṛhāt || 212 ||
yāvānavadhyasya vadhe tāvānvadhyasya rakṣaṇe
adharmo nṛpaterdṛṣṭa etayorubhayorapi || 213 ||
brāhmaṇaṃ naiva hanyāttu sarvapāpeṣvavasthitam
pravāsayetsvakādrā ṣṭrātsamagradhanasaṃyutam || 214 ||
na jātu brāhmaṇaṃ vadhyātpātakaṃ tvadhikaṃ bhavet
yasmāttasmātprayatnena brahmahatyāṃ vivarjayet || 215 ||
adaṇḍyāndaṇḍayanrājā daṇḍyāṃścaivāpyadaṇḍayan
ayaśo mahadāpnoti narakaṃ cādhigacchati || 216 ||
jñātvā'parādhaṃ puruṣasya rājā kālaṃ tathā cānumataṃ dvijānām
daṇḍyeṣu daṇḍaṃ parikalpayettu yo yasya yuktaḥ sa samīkṣya kuryāt || 217 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe rajadharme daṇḍapraṇayanaṃ nāma saptaviṃśatyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 227 ||

<228>

athāṣṭāviṃśatyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
manuruvāca
divyāntarikṣabhaumeṣu yā śāntirabhidhīyate
tāmahaṃ śrotumicchāmi mahotpāteṣu keśava || 1 ||
matsya uvāca
athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi trividhāmadbhutādiṣu
viśeṣeṇa tu bhaumeṣu śāntiḥ kāryā tathā bhavet || 2 ||
abhayācāntarikṣeṣu saumyā divyeṣu pārthiva
vijigīṣuḥ paraṃ rājanbhūtikāmastu yo bhavet || 3 ||
vijigīṣuḥ parānevamabhiyuktastathā paraiḥ
tathā'bhicāraśaṅkāyāṃ śatrūṇāmabhināśane || 4 ||
bhaye mahati saṃprāpte abhayā śāntiriṣyate
rājayakṣmābhibhūtasya kṣatakṣīṇasya cāpyatha || 5 ||
saumyā praśasyate śāntiryajñakāmasya cāpyatha
bhūkampe ca samutpanne prāpte cānnakṣaye tathā || 6 ||
ativṛṣṭyāmanāvṛṣṭyāṃ śalabhānāṃ bhayeṣu ca
pramatteṣu ca caureṣu vaiṣṇavī śāntiriṣyate || 7 ||
paśūnāṃ bhāraṇe prāpte narāṇāmapi dāruṇe
bhūteṣu dṛśyamāneṣu raudrī śāntistatheṣyate || 8 ||
vedanāśe samutpanne jane jāte ca nāstike
apūjyapūjane jāte brāhmī śāntistatheṣyate || 9 ||
bhaviṣyatyabhiṣeke ca paracakrabhaye'pi ca
svarāṣṭrabhede'rivadhe raudrī śāntiḥ praśasyate || 10 ||
tryahātirikte pavane bhakṣye sarvavigarhite
vaikṛte vātaje vyādhau vāyavī śāntiriṣyate || 11 ||
anāvṛṣṭibhaye jāte prāpte vikṛtivarṣaṇe
jalā śayavikāreṣu vāruṇī śāntiriṣyate || 12 ||
abhiśāpabhaye prāpte bhārgavī ca tathaiva ca
jāte prasavavaikṛtye prājāpatyā mahābhuja || 13 ||
upaskarāṇāṃ vaikṛtye tvāṣṭrī pārthivanandana
bālānāṃ śāntikāmasya kaumārī ca tathā nṛpa || 14 ||
kuryācchāntimathā''gneyīṃ saṃprāpte vahnivaikṛte
ājñābhaṅge tu saṃjāte tathā bhṛtyādisaṃkṣaye || 15 ||
aśvānāṃ śāntikāmasya tadvikāre samutthite
aśvānāṃ kāmayānasya gāndharvī śāntiriṣyate || 16 ||
gajānāṃ śāntikāmasya tadvikāre samutthite
gajānāṃ kāmayānasya śāntirāṅgirasī bhavet || 17 ||
piśācādibhaye jāte śāntirvai nairṛtī smṛtā
apamṛtyubhaye jāte duḥsvapne ca tathā sthite || 18 ||
yāmyāṃ tu kārayecchāntiṃ prāpte tu mārake tathā
dhananāśe samutpanne kauberī śāntiriṣyate || 19 ||
vṛkṣāṇāṃ ca tathā'rthānāṃ vaikṛte samupasthite
bhūtikāmastathā śāntiṃ pārthivīṃ pratiyojayet || 20 ||
prathame dinayāme ca rātrau vā manujottama
haste svātau ca citrāyāmāditye cā''śvine tathā || 21 ||
aryāmṇi saumyajāteṣu vāyavyāṃ tvadbhuteṣu ca
dvitīye dinayāme tu rātrau ca ravinandana || 22 ||
puṣyāgneyaviśākhāsu pitryāsu bharaṇīṣu ca
utpāteṣu tathā bhāga āgneyīṃ teṣu kārayet || 23 ||
tṛtīye dinayāme ca rātrau ca ravinandana
rohiṇyāṃ vaiṣṇave brāhme vāsave vaiśvadevate || 24 ||
jyeṣṭhāyāṃ ca tathā maitre ye bhavantyadbhutāḥ kvacit
aindrī teṣu prayoktavyā śāntī ravikulodvaha || 25 ||
caturthe dinayāme ca rātrau vā ravinandana
sārpe pauṣṇe tathā''rdrā yāmahirbudhnye ca dāruṇe || 26 ||
mūle varuṇadaivatye ye bhavantyadbhutāstathā
vāruṇī teṣu kartavyā mahāśāntirmahīkṣitā || 27 ||
mitramaṇḍalavelāsu ye bhavantyadbhutāḥ kvacit
tatra śāntidvayaṃ kāryaṃ nimitteṣu ca nānyathā
nirnimittakṛtā śāntirnimittenopayujyate || 28 ||
bāṇaprahārā na bhavanti yadvadrā jannṛṇāṃ saṃnahanairyutānām
daivopaghātā na bhavanti tadvaddharmātmanāṃ śāntiparāyaṇānām || 29 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe'dbhutaśāntirnāmāṣṭaviṃśatyadhikadviśatatamo 'dhyāyaḥ || 228 ||

<229>

athaikonatriṃśadadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
manuruvāca
adbhutānāṃ phalaṃ deva śamanaṃ ca tathā vada
tvaṃ hi vetsi viśālākṣa jñeyaṃ sarvamaśeṣataḥ || 1 ||
matsya uvāca
atra te varṇayiṣyāmi yaduvāca mahātapāḥ
atraye vṛddhagargastu sarvadharmabhṛtāṃ varaḥ || 2 ||
sarasvatyāḥ sukhāsīnaṃ gargaṃ srotasi pārthiva
papracchāsau mahātejā atrirmunijanapriyam || 3 ||
atriruvāca
naśyatāṃ pūrvarūpāṇi janānāṃ kathayasva me
nagarāṇāṃ tathā rājñāṃ tvaṃ hi sarvaṃ vadasva mām || 4 ||
garga uvāca
puruṣāpacārānniyatamaparajyanti devatāḥ
tato'parāgāddevānāmupasargaḥ pravartate || 5 ||
divyāntarikṣabhaumaṃ ca trividhaṃ saṃprakīrtitam
graharkṣavaikṛtaṃ divyamāntarikṣaṃ nibodha me || 6 ||
ulkāpāto diśāṃ dāhaḥ pariveṣastathaiva ca
gandharvanagaraṃ caiva vṛṣṭiśca vikṛtā tu yā || 7 ||
evamādīni loke'sminnāntarikṣaṃ vinirdiśet
carasthirabhavo bhaumo bhūkampaścāpi bhūmijaḥ || 8 ||
jalāśayānāṃ vaikṛtyaṃ bhaumaṃ tadapi kīrtitam
bhaume tvalpaphalaṃ jñeyaṃ cireṇa ca vipacyate || 9 ||
abhrajaṃ madhyaphaladaṃ madhyakālaphalapradam
adbhute tu samutpanne yadi vṛṣṭiḥ śivā bhavet || 10 ||
saptāhābhyantare jñeyamadbhutaṃ niṣphalaṃ bhavet
adbhutasya vipākaśca vinā śāntyā na dṛśyate || 11 ||
tribhirvarṣaistathā jñeyaṃ sumahadbhayakārakam
rājñaḥ śarīre loke ca puradvāre purohite || 12 ||
pākamāyāti putreṣu tathā vai kośavāhane
ṛtusvabhāvādrā jendra bhavantyadbhutasaṃjñitāḥ || 13 ||
śubhāvahāste vijñeyāstāṃśca me gadataḥ śṛṇu
vajrāśanimahīkampasaṃdhyānirghātaniḥsvanāḥ || 14 ||
pariveṣarajodhūmaraktārkāstamayodayāḥ
drumodbhedakarasneho bahuśaḥ saphalodgamaḥ || 15 ||
gopakṣimadhuvṛddhiśca śubhāni madhumādhave
ṛkṣolkāpātakaluṣaṃ kapilārkendumaṇḍalam || 16 ||
kṛṣṇaśvetaṃ tathā pītaṃ dhūsaradhvāntalohitam
raktapuṣpāruṇaṃ sāṃdhyaṃ nabhaḥ kṣubdhārṇavopamam || 17 ||
saritāṃ cāmbusaṃśoṣaṃ dṛṣṭvā grīṣme śubhaṃ vadet
śakrāyudhaparīveṣaṃ vidyudulkādhirohaṇam || 18 ||
kampodvartanavaikṛtyaṃ hrasanaṃ dāraṇaṃ kṣiteḥ
nadyudapānasarasāṃ vidhūnataraṇaplavāḥ || 19 ||
śṛṅgiṇāṃ ca varāhāṇāṃ varṣāsu śubhamiṣyate
śītānilatuṣāratvaṃ nardanaṃ mṛgapakṣiṇām || 20 ||
rakṣobhūtapiśācānāṃ darśanaṃ vāgamānuṣī
diśo dhūmāndhakārāśca sanabhovanaparvatāḥ || 21 ||
uccaiḥ sūryodayāstau ca hemante śobhanāḥ smṛtāḥ
divyastrīrūpagandharvavimānādbhutadarśanam || 22 ||
grahanakṣatratārāṇāṃ darśanaṃ vāgamānuṣī
gītavāditranirghoṣo vanaparvatasānuṣu || 23 ||
sasyavṛddhī rasotpattiḥ śaratkāle śubhāḥ smṛtāḥ
himapātānilotpātavirūpādbhutadarśanam || 24 ||
kṛṣṇāñjanābhamākāśaṃ tārolkāpātapiñjaram
citragarbhodbhavaḥ strīṣu gojāśvamṛgapakṣiṣu
patrāṅkuralatānāṃ ca vikārāḥ śiśire śubhāḥ || 25 ||
ṛtusvabhāvena vinā'dbhutasya jātasya dṛṣṭasya tu śīghrameva
yathāgamaṃ śāntiranantaraṃ tu kāryā yathoktā vasudhādhipena || 26 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe
adbhutaśāntikotpattirnāmaikonatriṃśadadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 229 ||

<230>

atha triṃśadadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
garga uvāca
devatārcāḥ pranṛtyanti vepante prajvalanti ca
vamantyagniṃ tathā dhūmaṃ snehaṃ raktaṃ tathā vasām || 1 ||
āraṭanti rudantyetāḥ prasvidyanti hasanti ca
uttiṣṭhanti niṣīdanti pradhāvanti dhamanti ca || 2 ||
bhuñjate vikṣipante vā kośapraharaṇadhvajān
avāṅmukhā vā tiṣṭhanti sthānātsthānaṃ bhramanti ca || 3 ||
vamantyagniṃ tathā dhūmaṃ snahaṃ raktaṃ tathā vasām
evamādyā hi dṛśyante vikārāḥ sahasotthitāḥ
liṅgāyatanavipreṣu tatra vāsaṃ na rocayet || 4 ||
rājño vā vyasanaṃ tatra sa ca deśo vinaśyati
devayātrāsu cotpātāndṛṣṭvā deśabhayaṃ vadet || 5 ||
pitāmahasya harmyeṣu tatra vāsaṃ na rocayet
paśūnāṃ rudra jaṃ jñeyaṃ nṛpāṇāṃ lokapālajam || 6 ||
jñeyaṃ senāpatīnāṃ tu yatsyātskandaviśākhajam
lokānāṃ viṣṇuvasvindra viśvakarmasamudbhavam || 7 ||
vināyakodbhavaṃ jñeyaṃ gaṇānāṃ ye tu nāyakāḥ
devapreṣyānnṛpapreṣyā devastrībhirnṛpastriyaḥ || 8 ||
vāsudevodbhavaṃ jñeyaṃ grahāṇāmeva nānyathā
devatānāṃ vikāreṣu śrutivettā purohitaḥ || 9 ||
devatārcāṃ tu gatvā vai snānamācchādya bhūṣayet
pūjayecca mahābhāga gandhamālyānnasaṃpadā || 10 ||
madhuparkeṇa vidhivadupatiṣṭhedanantaram
talliṅgena ca mantreṇa sthālīpākaṃ yathāvidhi
purodhā juhuyādvahnau saptarātramatandri taḥ || 11 ||
viprāśca pūjyā madhurānnapānaiḥ sadakṣiṇaṃ saptadinaṃ narendra
prāpte'ṣṭame'hni kṣitigopradānaiḥ sakāñcanaiḥ śāntimupaiti pāpam || 12 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe'drutaśāntāvarcādhikāro nāma triṃśadadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 230 ||

<231>

athaikatriṃśadadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
garga uvāca
anagnirdīpyate yatra rāṣṭre yasya nirindhanaḥ
na dīpyate cendhanavāṃstadrā ṣṭraṃ pīḍyate nṛpaiḥ || 1 ||
prajvaledapsu māsaṃ vā tadardhaṃ vā'pi kiṃcana
prākāraṃ toraṇaṃ dvāraṃ nṛpaveśma surālayam || 2 ||
etāni yatra dīpyante tatra rājño bhayaṃ bhavet
vidyutā vā pradahyante tadā'pi nṛpaterbhayam || 3 ||
anaiśāni tamāṃsi syurvinā pāṃśurajāṃsi ca
dhūmaścānagnijo yatra tatra vidyānmahābhayam || 4 ||
taḍittvanabhre gagane bhayaṃ syādṛkṣavarjite
divā satāre gagane tathaiva bhayamādiśet || 5 ||
grahanakṣatravaikṛtye tārāviṣamadarśane
puravāhanayāneṣu catuṣpānmṛgapakṣiṣu || 6 ||
āyudheṣu ca dīpteṣu dhūmāyatsu tathaiva ca
nirgamatsu ca kośācca saṅgrāmastumulo bhavet || 7 ||
vinā'gniṃ visphuliṅgāśca dṛśyante yatra kutracit
svabhāvāccāpi pūryante dhanūṃṣi vikṛtāni ca || 8 ||
vikāraścā''yudhānāṃ syāttatra saṅgrāmamādiśet
trirātropoṣitaścātra purodhāḥ susamāhitaḥ || 9 ||
samidbhiḥ kṣīravṛkṣāṇāṃ sarṣapaiśca ghṛtena ca
homaṃ kuryādagnimantrairbrāhmaṇāṃścaiva bhojayet || 10 ||
dadyātsuvarṇaṃ ca tathā dvijebhyo gāścaiva vastrāṇi tathā bhuvaṃ ca
evaṃ kṛte pāpamupaiti nāśaṃ yadagnivaikṛtyabhavaṃ dvijendra || 11 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe'dbhutaśāntāvagnivaikṛtyaṃ nāmaikatriṃśadadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 231 ||

<232>

atha dvātriṃśadadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
garga uvāca
pureṣu yeṣu dṛśyante pādapā devacoditāḥ
rudanto vā hasanto vā sravanto vā rasānbahūn || 1 ||
arogā vā vinā vātaṃ śākhāṃ muñcantyatha drumāḥ
phalaṃ puṣpaṃ tathā'kāle darśayanti trihāyanāḥ || 2 ||
pūrvavatsvaṃ darśayanti phalaṃ puṣpaṃ tathā'ntare
kṣīraṃ snehaṃ tathā raktaṃ madhu toyaṃ sravanti ca || 3 ||
śuṣyantyarogāḥ sahasā śuṣkā rohanti vā punaḥ
uttiṣṭhantīha patitāḥ patanti ca tathotthitāḥ || 4 ||
tatra vakṣyāmi te brahmanvipākaṃ phalameva ca
rodane vyādhimabhyeti hasane deśavibhramam || 5 ||
śākhāprapatanaṃ kuryātsaṅgrāme yodhapātanam
bālānāṃ maraṇaṃ kuryurakāle puṣpitā drumāḥ || 6 ||
svarāṣṭrabhedaṃ kurute phalapuṣpamathāntare
kṣayaḥ sarvatra gokṣīre snehe durbhikṣalakṣaṇam || 7 ||
vāhanāpacayaṃ madye rakte saṅgrāmamādiśet
madhusrāve bhavedvyādhirjalasrāve na varṣati || 8 ||
arogaśoṣaṇaṃ jñeyaṃ brahmandurbhikṣalakṣaṇam
śuṣkeṣu saṃprarohastu vīryamannaṃ ca hīyate || 9 ||
utthāne patitānāṃ ca bhayaṃ bhedakaraṃ bhavet
sthānātsthānaṃ tu gamane deśabhaṅgastathā bhavet || 10 ||
jvalatsvapi ca vṛkṣeṣu rudatsvapi dhanakṣayam
etatpūjitavṛkṣeṣu sarvaṃ rājño vipadyate || 11 ||
puṣpe phale vā vikṛte rājño mṛtyuṃ tathā''diśet
anyeṣu caiva vṛkṣeṣu vṛkṣotpāteṣvatandri taḥ || 12 ||
ācchādayitvā taṃ vṛkṣaṃ gandhamālyairvibhūṣayet
vṛkṣopari tathā chatraṃ kuryātpāpapraśāntaye || 13 ||
śivamabhyarcayeddevaṃ paśuṃ cāsmai nivedayet
rudre bhya iti vṛkṣeṣu hutvā rudraṃ japettataḥ || 14 ||
madhvājyayuktena tu pāyasena saṃpūjya viprāṃśca bhuvaṃ ca dadyāt
gītena nṛtyena tathā'rcayettu devaṃ haraṃ pāpavināśahetoḥ || 15 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe'dbhutaśāntau vṛkṣotpātapraśamanaṃ nāma dvātriṃśadadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 232 ||

<233>

atha trayastriṃśadadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
garga uvāca
ativṛṣṭiranāvṛṣṭirdurbhikṣādi bhayaṃ matam
anṛtau tu divā'nantā vṛṣṭirjñeyā bhayānakā || 1 ||
anabhre vaikṛtā caiva vijñeyā rājamṛtyave
śītoṣṇānāṃ viparyāse nṛpāṇāṃ ripujaṃ bhayam || 2 ||
śoṇitaṃ varṣate yatra tatra śastrabhayaṃ bhavet
aṅgārapāṃśuvarṣeṣu nagaraṃ tadvinaśyati || 3 ||
majjāsthisnehamāṃsānāṃ janamārabhayaṃ bhavet
phalaṃ puṣpaṃ tathā dhānyaṃ pareṇātibhayāya tu || 4 ||
pāṃśujantūpalānāṃ ca varṣato rogajaṃ bhayam
chidre vā'nnapravarṣeṇa sasyānāṃ bhītivardhanam || 5 ||
virajaske ravau vyabhre yadā chāyā na dṛśyate
dṛśyate tu pratīpā vā tatra deśabhayaṃ bhavet || 6 ||
nirabhre vā'tha rātrau vā śvetaṃ yāmyottareṇa tu
indrā yudhaṃ tathā dṛṣṭvā ulkāpātaṃ tathaiva ca || 7 ||
digdāhapariveṣau ca gandharvanagaraṃ tathā
paracakrabhayaṃ brūyāddeśopadra vameva ca || 8 ||
sūryenduparjanyasamīraṇānāṃ yāgastu kāryo vidhivaddvijendra
dhanāni gauḥ kāñcanadakṣiṇā ca deyā dvijānāmaghanāśahetoḥ || 9 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe'dbhutaśāntau vṛṣṭivaikṛtipraśamanaṃ nāma trayastriṃśadadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 233 ||

<234>

atha catustriṃśatadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
garga uvāca
nagarādapasarpante samīpamupayānti ca
nadyo hradaprasravāṇi virasāśca bhavanti ca || 1 ||
vivarṇaṃ kaluṣaṃ taptaṃ phenavajjantusaṃkulam
snehaṃ kṣīraṃ surāṃ raktaṃ vahante vā''kulodakāḥ || 2 ||
ṣaṇmāsābhyantare tatra paracakrabhayaṃ bhavet
jalāśayā nadante vā prajvalanti kathaṃcana || 3 ||
vimuñcanti tathā brahmañjvālādhūmarajāṃsi ca
akhāte vā jalotpattiḥ susattvā vā jalāśayāḥ || 4 ||
saṃgītaśabdāḥ śrūyante janamārabhayaṃ bhavet
divyamambhobhayaṃ sarpirmadhutailāvasecanam || 5 ||
japtvā vāruṇā mantrāstaiśca homo jale bhavet || 6 ||
madhvājyayuktaṃ paramānnamatra deyaṃ dvijānāṃ dvijabhojanārtham
gāvaśca deyāḥ sitavastrayuktāstathodakumbhāḥ salilāghaśāntyai || 7 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe'dbhutaśāntau salilāśayavaikṛtyaṃ nāma catustriṃśadadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 234 ||

<235>

atha pañcatriṃśadadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
garga uvāca
akālaprasavā nāryaḥ kālātītaprajāstathā
vikṛtaprasavāścaiva yugmasaṃprasavāstathā || 1 ||
amānuṣā hyatuṇḍāśca saṃjātavyasanāstathā
hīnāṅgā adhikāṅgāśca jāyante yadi vā striyaḥ || 2 ||
paśavaḥ pakṣiṇaścaiva tathaiva ca sarīsṛpāḥ
vināśaṃ tasya deśasya kulasya ca vinirdiśet || 3 ||
vivāsayettānnṛpatiḥ svarāṣṭrātstriyaśca pūjyāśca tato dvijendrāḥ
kimicchakairbrāhmaṇatarpaṇaiśca loke tataḥ śāntimupaiti pāpam || 4 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe'dbhutaśāntau strīprasavavaikṛtyaṃ nāma pañcatriṃśadadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 235 ||

<236>

atha ṣaṭtriṃśadadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
garga uvāca
yānti yānānyayuktāni yuktānyapi na yānti ca
codyamānāni tatra syānmahadbhayamupasthitam || 1 ||
vādyamānā na vādyante vādyante cāpyanāhatāḥ
acalāśca calantyeva na calanti calāni ca || 2 ||
ākāśe tūryanādāśca gītagandharvaniḥsvanāḥ
kāṣṭhadarvīkuṭhārādi vikāraṃ kurute yadi || 3 ||
gāvo lāṅgūlasaṃghaiśca striyaḥ strī ca vighātayet
upaskārādivikṛtau ghoraṃ śastrabhayaṃ bhavet || 4 ||
vāyostu pūjāṃ dvijasaktubhiśca kṛtvā niyuktāṃśca japecca mantrān
dadyātprabhūtaṃ paramānnamatra sadakṣiṇaṃ tena śamo'sya bhūyāt || 5 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe'dbhutaśāntāvupaskaravaikṛtyaṃ nāma ṣaṭtriṃśadadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 236 ||

<237>

atha saptatriṃśadadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
garga uvāca
praviśanti yadā grāmamāraṇyā mṛgapakṣiṇaḥ
araṇyaṃ yānti vā grāmyāḥ sthalaṃ yānti jalodbhavāḥ || 1 ||
sthalajāśca jalaṃ yānti ghoraṃ vāśanti nirbhayāḥ
rājadvāre puradvāre śivā cāpyaśivapradā || 2 ||
divā rātriṃcarā vā'pi rātrāvapi divācarāḥ
grāmyāstyajanti grāmaṃ ca śūnyatāṃ tasya nirdiśet || 3 ||
dīptā vāśanti saṃdhyāsu maṇḍalāni ca kurvate
vāśanti visvaraṃ yatra tadā'pyetatphalaṃ labhet || 4 ||
pradoṣe kukkuṭo vāśeddhemante vā'pi kokilaḥ
arkodaye'rkābhimukhī śivā rauti bhayaṃ vadet || 5 ||
gṛhaṃ kapotaḥ praviśetkravyādo mūrdhni līyate
madhu vā makṣikāḥ kuryurmṛtyurgṛhapaterbhavet || 6 ||
prākāradvārageheṣu toraṇāpaṇavīthiṣu
ketucchatrāyudhādyeṣu kravyādaṃ prapatedyada || 7 ||
jāyante vā'tha valmīkā madhu vā syandate yadi
sa deśo nāśamāyāti rājā vā mriyate tathā || 8 ||
mūṣakāñchalabhāndṛṣṭvā prabhūtaṃ kṣudbhayaṃ bhavet
kāṣṭholmukāsthiśṛṅgāḍhyāḥ śvāno markaṭavedanāḥ || 9 ||
durbhikṣavedanā jñeyā kākā dhānyamukhā yadi
janānabhibhavantīha nirbhayā raṇavedinaḥ || 10 ||
kāko maithunasaktaśca śvetastu yadi dṛśyate
rājā vā mriyate tatra sa ca deśo vinaśyati || 11 ||
ulūko vāśate yatra nṛpadvāre tathā gṛhe
jñeyo gṛhapatermṛtyurdhananāśastathaiva ca || 12 ||
mṛgapakṣivikāreṣu kuryāddhomaṃ sadakṣiṇam
devāḥ kapotā iti vā japtavyāḥ pañcabhirdvijaiḥ || 13 ||
gāvaśca deyā vidhivaddvijebhyaḥ sakāñcanā vastrayugottarīyāḥ
evaṃ kṛte śāntimupaiti pāpaṃ mṛgairdvijairvā viniveditaṃ yat || 14 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe'dbhutaśāntau mṛgapakṣivaikṛtyaṃ nāma saptatriṃśadadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 237 ||

<238>

athāṣṭātriṃśadadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
garga uvāca
prāsādatoraṇāṭṭāladvāraprākāraveśmanām
nirnimittaṃ tu patanaṃ dṛḍhānāṃ rājamṛtyave || 1 ||
rajasā vā'tha dhūmena diśo yatra samākulāḥ
ādityacandra tārāśca vivarṇā bhayavṛddhaye || 2 ||
rākṣasā yatra dṛśyante brāhmaṇāśca vidharmiṇaḥ
ṛtavaśca viparyastā apūjyaḥ pūjyate janaiḥ || 3 ||
nakṣatrāṇi viyogīni tanmahadbhayalakṣaṇam
ketūdayoparāgau ca cchidraṃ vā śaśisūryayoḥ || 4 ||
graharkṣavikṛtiryatra tatrāpi bhayamādiśet
striyaśca kalahāyante bālā nighnanti bālakān || 5 ||
kriyāṇāmucitānāṃ ca vicchittiryatra jāyate
hūyamānastu yatrāgnirdīpyate na ca śāntiṣu || 6 ||
pipīlikāśca kravyādā yānti cottaratastathā
pūrṇakumbhāḥ sravante ca havirvā vipralupyate
maṅgalyāśca giro yatra na śrūyante samantataḥ
kṣavathurbādhate vā'tha prahasanti nadanti ca || 8 ||
na ca deveṣu vartante yathāvadbrāhmaṇeṣu ca
mandaghoṣāṇi vādyāni vādyante visvarāṇi ca || 9 ||
gurumitradviṣo yatra śatrupūjāratā narāḥ
brāhmaṇānsuhṛdo mānyāñjano yatrāvamanyate || 10 ||
śāntimaṅgalahomeṣu nāstikyaṃ yatra jāyate
rājā vā mriyate tatra sa deśo vā vinaśyati || 11 ||
rājño vināśe saṃprāpte nimittāni nibodha me
brāhmaṇānprathamaṃ dveṣṭi brāhaṇaiśca virudhyate || 12 ||
brāhmaṇasvāni cā''datte brāhmaṇāṃśca jighāṃsati
na ca smarati kṛtyeṣu yācitaśca prakupyati || 13 ||
ramate nindayā teṣāṃ praśaṃsāṃ nābhinandati
apūrvaṃ tu karaṃ lobhāttathā pātayate jane || 14 ||
eteṣvabhyarcayecchakraṃ sapatnīkaṃ dvijottama
bhojyāni caiva kāryāṇi surāṇāṃ balavastathā
santo viprāśca pūjyāḥ syustebhyo dānaṃ ca dīyatām || 15 ||
gāvaśca deyā dvijapuṃgavebhyo bhuvastathā kāñcanamambarāṇi
homaśca kāryo'marapūjanaṃ ca evaṃ kṛte pāpamupaiti śāntim || 16 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe'dbhutaśāntāvutpātapraśamanaṃ nāmāṣṭātriṃśadadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 238 ||

<239>

athaikonacatvāriṃśadadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
manuruvāca
grahayajñaḥ kathaṃ kāryo lakṣahomaḥ kathaṃ nṛpaiḥ
koṭihomo'pi vā deva sarvapāpapraṇāśanaḥ || 1 ||
kriyate vidhinā yena yaddṛṣṭaṃ śānticintakaiḥ
tatsarvaṃ vistarāddeva kathayasva janārdana || 2 ||
matsya uvāca
idānīṃ kathayiṣyāmi prasaṅgādeva te nṛpa
rājñāṃ dharmaprasaktena prajānāṃ ca hitepsunā || 3 ||
grahayajñaḥ sadā kāryo lakṣahomasamanvitaḥ
nadīnāṃ saṃgame caiva surāṇāmagratastathā || 4 ||
suṣabhe bhūmibhāge ca daivajñādhiṣṭhito nṛpaḥ
guruṇā caiva ṛtvigbhiḥ sārdhaṃ bhūmiṃ parīkṣayet || 5 ||
khanetkuṇḍaṃ ca tatraiva suṣamaṃ hastamātrakam
dviguṇaṃ lakṣahome tu koṭihome caturguṇam || 6 ||
yugmāstu ṛtvijaḥ proktā aṣṭau vai vedapāragāḥ
kandamūlaphalāhārā dadhikṣīrāśino'pi vā || 7 ||
vedyāṃ nidhāpayeccaiva ratnāni vividhāni ca
sikatāpariveṣāśca tato'gniṃ ca samindhayet || 8 ||
gāyatryā daśasāhasraṃ mānastokena ṣaḍguṇaḥ
triṃśadgrahāṇāṃ mantraiśca catvāro viṣṇudaivataiḥ || 9 ||
kūṣmāṇḍairjuhuyātpañca kusumādyaistu ṣoḍaśa
hotavyā daśasāhasra bādarairjātavedasi || 10 ||
śriyo mantreṇa hotavyāḥ sahasrāṇi caturdaśa
śeṣāḥ pañcasahasrāstu hotavyāstvindra daivataiḥ || 11 ||
hutvā śatasahasraṃ tu puṇyasnānaṃ samācaret
kumbhaiḥ ṣoḍaśasaṃkhyaiśca sahiraṇyaiḥ sumaṅgalaiḥ || 12 ||
snāpayedyajamānaṃ tu tataḥ śāntirbhaviṣyati
evaṃ kṛte te yatkiṃcidgrahapīḍāsamudbhavam || 13 ||
tatsarvaṃ nāśamāyāti dattvā vai dakṣiṇāṃ nṛpa
tasmātsarvaprayatnena pradhānā dakṣiṇā smṛtā || 14 ||
hastyaśvarathayānāni bhūmivastrayugāni ca
anaḍudgośataṃ dadyādṛtvijāṃ caiva dakṣiṇām || 15 ||
yathāvibhavasāraṃ tu vittaśāṭhyaṃ na kārayet
māse pūrṇe samāptastu lakṣahomo narādhipa || 16 ||
lakṣahomasya rājendra vidhānaṃ parikīrtitam
idānīṃ koṭihomasya śṛṇu tvaṃ kathayāmyaham || 17 ||
gaṅgātaṭe'tha yamunāsarasvatyornareśvara
narmadādevikāyāstu taṭe homo vidhīyate || 18 ||
tatrāpi ṛtvijaḥ kāryā ravinandana ṣoḍaśa
sarvahome tu rājarṣe dadyādvipre'thavā dhanam || 19 ||
ṛtvigācāryasahito dīkṣāṃ sāṃvatsarīṃ sthitaḥ
caitre māse tu saṃprāpte kārtike vā viśeṣataḥ || 20 ||
prārambhaḥ karaṇīyo vā vatsaraṃ vatsaraṃ nṛpa
yajamānaḥ payobhakṣī phalāśī ca tathā'nagha || 21 ||
yavādivrīhayo māṣāstilāśca saha sarṣapaiḥ
pālāśāḥ samidhaḥ śastā vasordhārā tathopari || 22 ||
māse'tha prathame dadyādṛtvigbhyaḥ kṣīrabhojanam
dvitīye kṛsarāṃ dadyāddharmakāmārthasādhanīm || 23 ||
tṛtīye māsi saṃyāvo deyo vai ravinandana
caturthe modakā deyā viprāṇāṃ prītimāvahan || 24 ||
pañcame dadhibhaktaṃ tu ṣaṣṭhe vai saktubhojanam
pūpāśca saptame deyā hyaṣṭame ghṛtapūpakāḥ || 25 ||
ṣaṣṭyodanaṃ ca navame daśame yavaṣaṣṭikā
ekādaśe samāṣaṃ tu bhojanaṃ ravinandana || 26 ||
dvādaśa tvatha saṃprāpte māse ravikulodvaha
ṣaḍrasaiḥ saha bhakṣyaiśca bhojanaṃ sārvakāmikam || 27 ||
deyā dvijānāṃ rājendra māsi māsi ca dakṣiṇāḥ
ahatavāsaḥsaṃvīto dinārdhaṃ homayecchuciḥ || 28 ||
tasmātsadotthitairbhāvyaṃ yajamānaiḥ saha dvijaiḥ
indrā dyādisurāṇāṃ ca prīṇanaṃ sārvakāmikam || 29 ||
kṛtvā surāṇāṃ rājendra paśughātasamanvitam
sarvadānāni devānāmagniṣṭomaṃ ca kārayet || 30 ||
evaṃ kṛtvā vidhānena pūrṇāhutiḥ śate śate
sahasre dviguṇā deyā yāvacchatasahasrakam || 31 ||
puroḍāśastataḥ sādhyo devatārthe ca ṛtvijaiḥ
yukto vasanmānavaiśca punaḥ prāptārcanāndvijān || 32 ||
prīṇayitvā surānsarvānpitṝneva tataḥ kramāt
kṛtvā śāstravidhānena piṇḍānāṃ ca samarpaṇam || 33 ||
samāsau tasya homasya viprāṇāmatha dakṣiṇām
samāṃ caiva tulāṃ kṛtvā baddhvā śikyadvayaṃ punaḥ || 34 ||
ātmānaṃ tolayettatra patnīṃ caiva dvitīyakām
suvarṇena tathā''tmānaṃ rajatena tathā priyām || 35 ||
tolayitvā dadedrā jā vittaśāṭhyavivarjitaḥ
dadecchatasahasraṃ tu rūpyasya kanakasya ca || 36 ||
sarvasvaṃ vā dadattatra rājasūyaphalaṃ labhat
evaṃ kṛtvā vidhānena viprāṃstāṃśca visarjayet || 37 ||
prīyatāṃ puṇḍarīkākṣaḥ sarvayajñeśvaro hariḥ
tasmiṃstuṣṭe jagattuṣṭaṃ prīṇite prīṇitaṃ bhavet || 38 ||
evaṃ sarvopaghāte tu devamānuṣakārite
iyaṃ śāntistavā''khyātā yāṃ kṛtvā sukṛtī bhavet || 39 ||
na śocejjanmamaraṇe kṛtākṛtavicāraṇe
sarvatīrtheṣu yatsnānaṃ sarvayajñeṣu yatphalam
tatphalaṃ samāvāpnoti kṛtvā yajñatrayaṃ nṛpa || 40 ||
iti śrīmātsya mahāpurāṇe grahayajñavidhānaṃ nāmaikonacatvāriṃśadadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 239 ||

<240>

atha catvāriṃśadadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
manuruvāca
idānīṃ sarvadharmajña sarvaśāstraviśārada
yātrākālavidhānaṃ me kathayasva mahīkṣitām || 1 ||
matsya uvāca
yadā manyeta nṛpatirākrandena balīyasā
pārṣṇigrāhābhibhūto'ristadā yātrāṃ prayojayet || 2 ||
yodhānmatvā prabhūtāṃśca prabhūtaṃ ca balaṃ mama
mūlarakṣāsamartho'smi tadā yātrāṃ prayojayet || 3 ||
aśuddhapārṣṇirnṛpatirna tu yātrāṃ prayojayet
pārṣṇigrāhādhikaṃ sainyaṃ mūle nikṣipya ca vrajet || 4 ||
caitryāṃ vā mārgaśīrṣyāṃ vā yātrāṃ yāyānnarādhipaḥ
caitryāṃ paśyecca naidāghaṃ hanti puṣṭiṃ ca śāradīm || 5 ||
etadeva viparyastaṃ mārgaśīrṣyāṃ narādhipaḥ
śatrorvā vyasane yāyātkāla eva sudurlabhaḥ || 6 ||
divyāntarikṣakṣitijairutpātaiḥ pīḍitaṃ param
ṣaḍakṣapīḍāsaṃtaptaṃ pīḍitaṃ ca tathā grahaiḥ || 7 ||
jvalantī ca tathaivolkā diśaṃ yāṃ ca prapadyate
bhūkampolkādi saṃyāti yāṃ ca ketuḥ prasūyate || 8 ||
nirghātaśca patedyatra tāṃ yāyādvasudhādhipaḥ
svabalavyasanopetaṃ tathā durbhikṣapīḍitam || 9 ||
saṃbhūtāntarakopaṃ ca kṣipraṃ prāyādariṃ nṛpaḥ
yūkāmākṣīkabahulaṃ bahupaṅkaṃ tathā''vilam || 10 ||
nāstikaṃ bhinnamaryādaṃ tathā'maṅgalavādinam
apetaprakṛtiṃ caiva niḥsāraṃ ca tathā jayet || 11 ||
vidviṣṭanāyakaṃ sainyaṃ tathā bhinnaṃ parasparam
vyasanāsaktanṛpatiṃ balaṃ rājā'bhiyojayet || 12 ||
sainikānāṃ na śastrāṇi sphurantyaṅgāni yatra ca
duḥsvapnāni ca paśyanti balaṃ tadabhiyojayet || 13 ||
utsāhabalasaṃpannaḥ svānuraktabalastathā
tuṣṭapuṣṭabalo rājā parānabhimukho vrajet || 14 ||
śarīrasphuraṇe dhanye tathā duḥsvapnanāśane
nimitte śakune dhanye jāte śatrupuraṃ vrajet || 15 ||
ṛkṣeṣu ṣaṭsu śuddheṣu graheṣvanuguṇeṣu ca
praśnakāle śubhe jāte parānyāyānnarādhipaḥ || 16 ||
evaṃ tu daivasaṃpannastathā pauruṣasaṃyutaḥ
deśakālopapannāṃ tu yātrāṃ kuryānnarādhipaḥ || 17 ||
sthale nakrastu nāgasya tasyāpi sajale vaśe
ulūkasya niśi dhvāṅkṣaḥ sa ca tasya divā vaśe || 18 ||
evaṃ deśaṃ ca kālaṃ ca jñātvā yātrāṃ prayojayet
padātināgabahulāṃ senāṃ prāvṛṣi yojayet || 19 ||
hemante śiśire caiva rathavājisamākulām
kharoṣṭrabahulāṃ senāṃ tathā grīṣme narādhipaḥ || 20 ||
caturaṅgabalopetāṃ vasante vā śaradyatha
senā padātibahulā yasya syātpṛthivīpateḥ || 21 ||
abhiyojyo bhavettena śaturviṣamamāśritaḥ
gamye vṛkṣāvṛte deśe sthitaṃ śatruṃ tathaiva ca || 22 ||
kiṃcitpaṅke tathā yāyādbahunāgo narādhipaḥ
rathāśvabahulo yāyācchatruṃ samapathasthitam || 23 ||
tamāśrayanto bahulāstāṃstu rājā prapūjayet
kharoṣṭrabahulo rājā śatrurbandhena saṃsthitaḥ || 24 ||
bandhanastho'bhiyojyo'ristathā prāvṛṣi bhūbhujā
himapātayute deśe sthitaṃ grīṣme'bhiyojayet || 25 ||
yavasendhanasaṃyuktaḥ kālaḥ pārthiva haimanaḥ
śaradvasantau dharmajña kālau sādhāraṇau smṛtau || 26 ||
vijñāya rājā dvijadeśakālau daivaṃ trikālaṃ ca tathaiva buddhvā
yāyātparaṃ kālavidāṃ matena saṃcintya sārdhaṃ dvijamantravidbhiḥ || 27 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe yātrānimittakālayojyacintā nāma catvāriṃśadadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 240 ||

<241>

athaikacatvāriṃśadadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
manuruvāca
brūhi me tvaṃ nimittāni aśubhāni śubhāni ca
sarvadharmabhṛtāṃ śreṣṭhaṃ tvaṃ hi sarvaviducyate || 1 ||
matsya uvāca
aṅgadakṣiṇabhāge tu śastaṃ prasphuraṇaṃ bhavet
apraśastaṃ tathā vāme pṛṣṭhasya hṛdayasya ca || 2 ||
manuruvāca
aṅgānāṃ spandanaṃ caiva śubhāśubhaviceṣṭitam
tanme vistarato brūhi yena syāttadvidho bhuvi || 3 ||
matsya uvāca
pṛthvīlābho bhavenmūrdhni lalāṭe ravinandana
sthānaṃ vivṛddhimāyāti bhrūnasoḥ priyasaṃgamaḥ || 4 ||
bhṛtyalabdhiścākṣideśe dṛgupānte dhanāgamaḥ
utkaṇṭhopagamo madhye dṛṣṭaṃ rājanvicakṣaṇaiḥ || 5 ||
dṛgbandhane saṃgare ca jayaṃ śīghramavāpnuyāt
yoṣidbhogo'pāṅgadeśe śravaṇānte priyāśrutiḥ || 6 ||
nāsikāyāṃ prītisaukhyaṃ prajāptiradharoṣṭhaje
kaṇṭhe tu bhogalābhaḥ syādbhogavṛddhirathāṃsayoḥ || 7 ||
suhṛtsnehaśca bāhubhyāṃ haste caiva dhanāgamaḥ
pṛṣṭhe parājayaḥ sadyo jayo vakṣaḥsthale bhavet || 8 ||
kukṣibhyāṃ prītiruddiṣṭā striyāḥ prajananaṃ stane
sthānabhraṃśo nābhideśe antre caiva dhanāgamaḥ || 9 ||
jānusaṃdhau paraiḥ saṃdhirbalavadbhirbhavennṛpa
deśaikadeśanāśo'tha jaṅghābhyāṃ ravinandana || 10 ||
uttamaṃ sthānamāpnoti padbhyāṃ prasphuraṇānnṛpa
salābhaṃ cādhvagamanaṃ bhavetpādatale nṛpa || 11 ||
lāñchanaṃ piṭakaṃ caiva jñeyaṃ sphuraṇavattathā
viparyayeṇa vihitaḥ sarvaḥ strīṇāṃ phalāgamaḥ || 12 ||
apraśaste tadā vāme tvapraśastaṃ viśeṣataḥ
dakṣiṇe'pi praśaste'ṅge praśastaṃ syādviśeṣataḥ || 13 ||
ato'nyathā siddhiprajalpanāttu phalasya śastasya ca ninditasya
aniṣṭacihnopagame dvijānāṃ kāryaṃ suvarṇena tu tarpaṇaṃ syāt || 14 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe yātrānimittakadehaspandanaṃ nāmaikacatvāriṃśadadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 241 ||

<242>

atha dvicatvāriṃśadadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
manuruvāca
svapnākhyānaṃ kathaṃ deva gamane pratyupasthite
dṛśyante vividhākārāḥ kathaṃ teṣāṃ phalaṃ bhavet || 1 ||
matsya uvāca
idānīṃ kathayiṣyāmi nimittaṃ svapnadarśane
nābhiṃ vinā'nyagātreṣu tṛṇavṛkṣasamudbhavaḥ || 2 ||
cūrṇanaṃ mūrdhni kāṃsyānāṃ muṇḍanaṃ nagnatā tathā
malināmbaradhāritvamabhyaṅgaḥ paṅkadigdhatā || 3 ||
uccātprapatanaṃ caiva dolārohaṇameva ca
arjanaṃ paṅkalohānāṃ hayānāmapi māraṇam || 4 ||
raktapuṣpadrumāṇāṃ ca maṇḍalasya tathaiva ca
varāharkṣakharoṣṭrāṇāṃ tathā cā''rohaṇakriyā || 5 ||
bhakṣaṇaṃ pakṣimatsyānāṃ tailasya kṛsarasya ca
nartanaṃ hasanaṃ caiva vivāho gītameva ca || 6 ||
tantrīvādyavihīnānāṃ vādyānāmabhivādanam
srato'vagāhagamanaṃ snānaṃ gomayavāriṇā || 7 ||
paṅkodakena ca tathā mahītoyena cāpyatha
mātuḥ praveśo jaṭhare citārohaṇameva ca || 8 ||
śakradhvajābhipatanaṃ patanaṃ śaśisūryayoḥ
divyāntarikṣabhaumānāmutpātānāṃ ca darśanam || 9 ||
devadvijātibhūpālagurūṇāṃ krodha eva ca
āliṅganaṃ kumārīṇāṃ puruṣāṇāṃ ca maithunam || 10 ||
hāniścaiva svagātrāṇāṃ virekavamanakriyā
dakṣiṇāśābhigamanaṃ vyādhinā'bhibhavastathā || 11 ||
phalāpahāniśca tathā puṣpahānistathaiva ca
gṛhāṇāṃ caiva pātaśca gṛhasaṃmārjanaṃ tathā || 12 ||
krīḍā piśācakravyādavānararkṣanarairapi
parādabhibhavaścaiva tasmācca vyasanodbhavaḥ || 13 ||
kāṣāyavastradhāritvaṃ tadvatstrīkrīḍanaṃ tathā
snehapānāvagāhau ca raktamālyānulepanam || 14 ||
evamādīni cānyāni duḥsvapnāni vinirdiśet
eṣāṃ saṃkathanaṃ dhanyaṃ bhūyaḥ prasvāpanaṃ tathā || 15 ||
kalkasnānaṃ tilairhomo brāhmaṇānāṃ ca pūjanam
stutiśca vāsudevasya tathā tasyaiva pūjanam || 16 ||
nāgendra mokṣaśravaṇaṃ jñeyaṃ duḥsvapnanāśanam
svapnāstu prathame yāme saṃvatsaravipākinaḥ || 17 ||
ṣaḍbhirmāsairdvitīye tu tribhirmāsaistṛtīyake
caturthe māsamātreṇa paśyato nātra saṃśayaḥ || 18 ||
aruṇodayavelāyāṃ daśāhena phalaṃ bhavet
ekasyāṃ yadi vā rātrau śubhaṃ vā yadi vā'śubham || 19 ||
paścāddṛṣṭastu yastatra tasya pākaṃ vinirdiśet
tasmācchobhanake svapne paścātsvapno na paśyati || 20 ||
śailaprāsādanāgāśvavṛṣabhārohaṇaṃ hitam
drumāṇāṃ śvetapuṣpāṇāṃ gamane ca tathā dvija || 21 ||
drumatṛṇodbhavo nābhau tathaiva bahubāhutā
tathaiva bahuśīrṣatvaṃ phalitodbhava eva ca || 22 ||
suśuklamālyadhāritvaṃ suśuklāmbaradhāritā
candrā rkatārāgrahaṇaṃ parimārjanameva ca || 23 ||
śakradhvajāliṅganaṃ ca taducchrāyakriyā tathā
bhūmyambudhīnāṃ grasanaṃ śatrūṇāṃ ca vadhakriyā || 24 ||
jayo vivāde dyūte ca saṅgrāme ca tathā dvija
bhakṣaṇaṃ cā''rdra māṃsānāṃ matsyānāṃ pāyasasya ca
darśanaṃ rudhirasyāpi snānaṃ vā rudhireṇa ca
surārudhiramadyānāṃ pānaṃ kṣīrasya cātha vā || 26 ||
antrairvā veṣṭanaṃ bhūmau nirmalaṃ gaganaṃ tathā
mukhena dohanaṃ śastaṃ mahiṣīṇāṃ tathā gavām || 27 ||
siṃhīnāṃ hastinīnāṃ ca vaḍavānāṃ tathaiva ca
prasādo devaviprebhyo gurubhyaśca tathā śubhaḥ || 28 ||
ambhasā tvabhiṣekastu gavāṃ śṛṅgasrutena vā
candrā dbhraṣṭena vā rājañjñeyo rājyaprado hi saḥ || 29 ||
rājyābhiṣekaśca tathā chedanaṃ śirasastathā
maraṇaṃ vahnidāhaśca vahnidāho gṛhādiṣu || 30 ||
labdhiśca rājyaliṅgānāṃ tantrīvādyābhivādanam
tathodakānāṃ taraṇaṃ tathā viṣamalaṅghanam || 31 ||
hastinīvaḍavānāṃ ca gavāṃ ca prasavo gṛhe
ārohaṇamathāśvānāṃ rodanaṃ ca tathā śubham || 32 ||
varastrīṇāṃ tathā lābhastathā''liṅganameva ca
nigaḍairbandhanaṃ dhanyaṃ tathā viṣṭhānulepanam || 33 ||
jīvatāṃ bhūmipālānāṃ suhṛdāmapi darśanam
darśanaṃ devatānāṃ ca vimalānāṃ tathā'mbhasām || 34 ||
śubhānyathaitāni narastu dṛṣṭvā prāpnotyayatnāddhruvamarthalābham
svapnāni vai dharmabhṛtāṃ variṣṭha vyādhervimokṣaṃ ca tathā''turo'pi || 35 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe yātrānimitte svapnādhyāyo nāma dvicatvāriṃśadadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 242 ||

<243>

atha tricatvāriṃśadadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
manuruvāca
gamanaṃ prati rājñāṃ tu saṃmukhādarśane ca kim
praśastāṃścaiva saṃbhāṣya sarvānetāṃśca kīrtaya || 1 ||
matsya uvāca
auṣadhāni tvayuktāni dhānyaṃ kṛṣṇaṃ ca yadbhavet
kārpāsaśca tṛṇaṃ rājanśuṣkaṃ gomayameva ca || 2 ||
indhanaṃ ca tathā'ṅgāraṃ guḍaṃ tailaṃ tathā'śubham
abhyaktaṃ malinaṃ muṇḍaṃ tathā nagnaṃ ca mānavam || 3 ||
muktakeśaṃ rujārtaṃ ca kāṣāyāmbaradhāriṇam
unmattakaṃ tathā sattvaṃ dīnaṃ cātha napuṃsakam || 4 ||
ayaḥpaṅkastathā carma keśabandhanameva ca
tathaivoddhṛtasārāṇi piṇyākādīni yāni ca || 5 ||
caṇḍālaśvapacāścaiva rājabandhanapālakāḥ
vadhakāḥ pāpakarmāṇo garbhiṇī strī tathaiva ca || 6 ||
tuṣabhasmakapālāsthi bhinnabhāṇḍāni yāni ca
riktāni caiva bhāṇḍāni mṛtaṃ śārṅgikameva ca || 7 ||
evamādīni cānyāni aśastānyabhidarśane
aśasto vādyaśabdaśca bhinnabhairavajarjaraḥ || 8 ||
purataḥ śabda ehīti śasyate na tu pṛṣṭhataḥ
gaccheti paścāddharmajña purastāttu vigarhitaḥ || 9 ||
kva yāsi tiṣṭha mā gaccha kiṃ te tatra gatasya tu
anye śabdāśca ye'niṣṭāste vipattikarā api || 10 ||
dhvajādiṣu tathā sthānaṃ kravyādānāṃ vigarhitam
skhalanaṃ vāhanānāṃ ca vastrasaṅgastathaiva ca || 11 ||
nirgatasya tu dvārādau śirasaścābhighātitā
chatradhvajānāṃ vastrāṇāṃ patanaṃ ca tathā'śubham || 12 ||
dṛṣṭe nimitte prathamamamaṅgalyavināśanam
keśavaṃ pūjayedvidvānstavena madhusūdanam || 13 ||
dvitīye tu tato dṛṣṭe pratīpe praviśedgṛham
atheṣṭāni pravakṣyāmi maṅgalyāni tathā'nagha || 14 ||
śvetāḥ sumanasaḥ śreṣṭhāḥ pūrṇakumbhāstathaiva ca
jalajāḥ pakṣiṇaścaiva māṃsamatsyāśca pārthiva || 15 ||
gāvasturaṃgamā nāgā baddha ekaḥ paśustvajaḥ
tridaśāḥ suhṛdo viprā jvalitaśca hutāśanaḥ || 16 ||
gaṇikā ca mahābhāga dūrvā cārdraṃ ca gomayam
rukmaṃ rūpyaṃ tathā tāmraṃ sarvaratnāni cāpyatha || 17 ||
auṣadhāni ca dharmajña yavāḥ siddhārthakāstathā
nṛvāhyamānaṃ yānaṃ ca bhadra pīṭhaṃ tathaiva ca || 18 ||
khaḍgaṃ chatraṃ patākā ca mṛdaścā''yudhameva ca
rājaliṅgāni sarvāṇi śavaṃ ruditavarjitam || 19 ||
ghṛtaṃ dadhi payaścaiva phalāni vividhāni ca
svastikaṃ vardhamānaṃ ca nandyāvartaṃ sakaustubham || 20 ||
vāditrāṇāṃ sukhaḥ śabdo gambhīraḥ sumanoharaḥ
gāndhāraṣaḍjaṛṣabhā ye ca śastāstathā svarāḥ || 21 ||
vāyuḥ saśarkaro rūkṣaḥ sarvatra samupasthitaḥ
pratilomastathā nīco vijñeyo bhayakṛddvija || 22 ||
anukūlo mṛduḥ snigdhaḥ sukhasparśaḥ sukhāvahaḥ
rūkṣā rūkṣasvarā bhadrāḥ kravyādāḥ parigacchatām || 23 ||
meghāḥ śastā ghanāḥ snigdhā gajabṛṃhitaniḥsvanāḥ
anulomāstaḍicchastāḥ śakracāpaṃ tathaiva ca || 24 ||
apraśaste tathā jñeye pariveṣapravarṣaṇe
anulomā grahāḥ śastā vākpatistu viśeṣataḥ || 25 ||
āstikyaṃ śraddadhānatvaṃ tathā pūjyābhipūjanam
śastānyetāni dharmajña yacca syānmanasaḥ priyam || 26 ||
manasastuṣṭirevātra paramaṃ jayalakṣaṇam
ekataḥ sarvaliṅgāni manasastuṣṭirekataḥ || 27 ||
yānotsukatvaṃ manasaḥ praharṣaḥ śubhasya lābho vijayapravādaḥ
maṅgalyalabdhiḥ śravaṇaṃ ca rājañjñeyāni nityaṃ vijayāvahāni || 28 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe yātrānimitte maṅgalādhyāyo nāma tricatvāriṃśadadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 243 ||

<244>

atha catuścatvāriṃśadadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
ṛṣaya ūcuḥ
rājadharmastvayā sūta kathito vistareṇa tu
tathaivādbhutamaṅgalyaṃ svapnadarśanameva ca || 1 ||
viṣṇoridānīṃ māhātmyaṃ punarvaktumihārhasi
kathaṃ sa vāmano bhūtvā babandha balidānavam || 2 ||
kramataḥ kīdṛśaṃ rūpamāsīllokatraye hareḥ
sūta uvāca
etadeva purā pṛṣṭaḥ kurukṣetre tapodhanaḥ
śaunakastīrthayātrāyāṃ vāmanāyatane purā || 3 ||
yadā samayabheditvaṃ drau padyāḥ pārthivaṃ prati
arjunena kṛtaṃ tatra tīrthayātrāṃ tadā yayau || 4 ||
dharmakṣetre kurukṣetre vāmanāyatane sthitaḥ
dṛṣṭvā sa vāmanaṃ tatra arjuno vākyamabravīt || 5 ||
arjuna uvāca
kiṃ nimittamayaṃ devo vāmanākṛtirijyate
varāharūpī bhagavānkasmātpūjyo'bhavatpurā
kasmācca vāmanasyedamiṣṭaṃ kṣetramajāyata || 6 ||
śaunaka uvāca
vāmanasya ca vakṣyāmi varāhasya ca dhīmataḥ
tyaktvā'tivistaraṃ bhūyo māhātmyaṃ kurunandana || 7 ||
purā nirvāsite śakre sureṣu vijiteṣu ca
cintayāmāsa devānāṃ jananī punarudbhavam || 8 ||
aditirdevamātā ca paramaṃ duścaraṃ tapaḥ
tīvraṃ cacāra varṣāṇāṃ sahasraṃ pṛthivīpate || 9 ||
ārādhanāya kṛṣṇasya vāgyatā vāyubhojanā
daityairnirākṛtāndṛṣṭvā tanayānkurunandana || 10 ||
vṛthāputrā'hamasmīti nirvedātpraṇatā harim
tuṣṭāva vāgbhiriṣṭābhiḥ paramārthāvabodhinī
devadevaṃ hṛṣīkeśaṃ natvā sarvagataṃ harim || 11 ||
aditiruvāca
namaḥ sarvārtināśāya namaḥ puṣkaramāline
namaḥ paramakalyāṇakalyāṇāyā''divedhase || 12 ||
namaḥ paṅkajanetrāya namaḥ paṅkajanābhaye
śriyaḥ kāntāya dāntāya dāntadṛśyāya cakriṇe || 13 ||
namaḥ paṅkajasaṃbhūtisaṃbhavāyā''tmayonaye
namaḥ śaṅkhāsihastāya namaḥ kanakaretase || 14 ||
tathā''tmajñānavijñānayogicintyātmayogine
nirguṇāyāviśeṣāya haraye brahmarūpiṇe || 15 ||
jagatpratiṣṭhitaṃ yatra jagatā yo na dṛśyate
namaḥ sthūlātisūkṣmāya tasmai devāya śaṅkhine || 16 ||
yaṃ na paśyanti paśyanto jagadapyakhilaṃ narāḥ
apaśyadbhirjagatyatra sa devo hṛdi saṃsthitaḥ || 17 ||
taminneva vinaśyeta yasyaitadakhilaṃ jagat
tasmai samastajagatāmādhārāya namo namaḥ || 18 ||
ādyaḥ prajāpatipatiryaḥ prabhūṇāṃ patiḥ paraḥ
patiḥ surāṇāṃ yastasmai namaḥ kṛṣṇāya vedhase || 19 ||
yaḥ pravṛttau nivṛttau ca ijyate karmabhiḥ svakaiḥ
svargāpavargaphalado namastasmai gadābhṛte || 20 ||
yaścintyamāno manasā sadyaḥ pāpaṃ vyapohati
namastasmai viśuddhāya parāya harivedhase || 21 ||
yaṃ buddhvā sarvabhūtāni devadeveśamavyayam
na punarjanmamaraṇe prāpnuvanti namāmi tam || 22 ||
yo yajñe yajñaparamairijyate yajñasaṃjñitaḥ
taṃ yajñapuruṣaṃ viṣṇuṃ namāmi prabhumīśvaram || 23 ||
gīyate sarvavedeṣu vedavidbhirvidāṃ patiḥ
yastasmai vedavedyāya viṣṇave jiṣṇave namaḥ || 24 ||
yato viśvaṃ samutpannaṃ yasmiṃśca layameṣyati
viśvāgamapratiṣṭhāya namastasmai mahātmane || 25 ||
brahmādistambamaryantaṃ yena viśvamidaṃ tatam
māyājālaṃ samuttartuṃ tamupendraṃ namāmyaham || 26 ||
yastu toyasvarūpastho bibhartyakhilamīśvaraḥ
viśvaṃ viśvapatiṃ viṣṇuṃ taṃ namāmi prajāpatim || 27 ||
yamārādhya viśuddhena manasā karmaṇā girā
tarantyavidyāmakhilāṃ tamupendraṃ namāmyaham || 28 ||
viṣādatoṣaroṣādyairyo'jasraṃ sukhaduḥkhajaiḥ
nṛtyatyakhilabhūtasthastamupendraṃ namāmyaham || 29 ||
mūrtaṃ tamo'suramayaṃ tadvadhādvinihanti yaḥ
rātrijaṃ sūryarūpī tamupendraṃ namāmyaham || 30 ||
kapilādisvarūpastho yaścājñānamayaṃ tamaḥ
hanti jñānapradānena tamupendraṃ namāmyaham || 31 ||
yasyākṣiṇī candra sūryau sarvalokaśubhāśubham
paśyataḥ karma satatamupendraṃ taṃ namāmyaham || 32 ||
yasminsarveśvare sarva satyametanmayoditam
nānṛtaṃ tamajaṃ viṣṇuṃ namāmi prabhavāpyayam || 33 ||
yacca tatsatyamuktaṃ me bhūyāṃścāto janārdanaḥ
satyena tena sakalāḥ pūryantāṃ me manorathāḥ || 34 ||
śaunaka uvāca
evaṃ stutaḥ sa bhagavānvāsudeva uvāca tām
adṛśyaḥ sarvabhūtānāṃ tasyāḥ saṃdarśane sthitaḥ || 35 ||
śrībhagavānuvāca
manorathāṃstvamadite yānicchasyabhivāñchitān
tāṃstvaṃ prāpsyasi dharmajñe matprasādānna saṃśayaḥ || 36 ||
śṛṇuṣva sumahābhāge varo yaste hṛdi sthitaḥ
tamāśu vriyatāṃ kāmaṃ śreyaste saṃbhaviṣyati
maddarśanaṃ hi viphalaṃ na kadācidbhaviṣyati || 37 ||
aditiruvāca
yadi deva prasannastvaṃ madbhaktyā bhaktavatsala
trailokyādhipatiḥ putrastadastu mama vāsavaḥ || 38 ||
hṛtaṃ rājyaṃ hṛtāścāsya yajñabhāgā mahāsuraiḥ
tvayi prasanne varade tānprāpnotu suto mama || 39 ||
hṛtaṃ rājyaṃ na duḥkhāya mama putrasya keśava
sāpatnāddāyanirbhraṃśo bādhāṃ naḥ kurute hṛdi || 40 ||
śrībhagavānuvāca
kṛtaḥ prasādo hi mayā tava devi yathepsitaḥ
svāṃśena caiva te garbhe saṃbhaviṣyāmi kaśyapāt || 41 ||
tava garbhasamudbhūtastataste ye surārayaḥ
tānahaṃ nihaniṣyāmi nivṛttā bhava nandini || 42 ||
aditiruvāca
prasīda devadeveśa namaste viśvabhāvana
nāhaṃ tvāmudare deva voḍhuṃ śakṣyāmi keśava || 43 ||
yasminpratiṣṭhitaṃ viśvaṃ yo viśvaṃ svayamīśvaraḥ
tamahaṃ nodareṇa tvāṃ voḍhuṃ śakṣyāmi durdharam || 44 ||
śrībhagavānuvāca
satyamāttha mahābhāge mayi sarvamidaṃ jagat
pratiṣṭhitaṃ na māṃ śaktā voḍhuṃ sendrā divaukasaḥ || 45 ||
kiṃtvahaṃ sakalām̐ llokānsadevāsuramānuṣān
jaṅgamānsthāvarānsarvāṃstvāṃ ca devi sakaśyapān || 46 ||
dhārayiṣyāmi bhadraṃ te tadalaṃ saṃbhrameṇa te || 46 ||
na te glānirna te khedo garbhasthe bhavitā mayi
dākṣāyaṇi prasādaṃ te karomyanyaiḥ sudurlabham || 47 ||
garbhasthe mayi putrāṇāṃ tava yo'rirbhaviṣyati
tejasastasya hāniṃ ca kariṣye mā vyathāṃ kṛthāḥ || 48 ||
śaunaka uvāca
evamuktvā tataḥ sadyo yāto'ntardhānamīśvaraḥ
sā'pi kālena taṃ garbhamavāpa kurusattama || 49 ||
garbhasthite tataḥ kṛṣṇe cacāla sakalā kṣitiḥ
cakampire mahāśailāḥ kṣobhaṃ jagmustathā'bdhayaḥ || 50 ||
yato yato'ditiryāti dadāti lalitaṃ padam
tatastataḥ kṣitiḥ khedānnanāma vasudhādhipa || 51 ||
daityānāmatha sarveṣāṃ garbhasthe madhusūdane
babhūva tejasāṃ hāniryathoktaṃ parameṣṭhinā || 52 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe vāmanaprādurbhāve'ditivarapradānaṃ nāma catuścatvāriṃśadadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 244 ||

<245>

atha pañcacatvāriṃśadadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
śaunaka uvāca
nistejaso'surāndṛṣṭvā samastānasureśvaraḥ
prahlādamatha papraccha balirātmapitāmaham || 1 ||
baliruvāca
tāta nistejaso daityā nirdagdhā iva vahninā
kimete sahasaivādya brahmadaṇḍahatā iva || 2 ||
ariṣṭaṃ kiṃ nu daityānāṃ kiṃ kṛtvā vairinirmitā
nāśāyaiṣā samudbhūtā yayā nistejaso'surāḥ || 3 ||
śaunaka uvāca
iti daityapatirdhīraḥ pṛṣṭaḥ pautreṇa pārthiva
ciraṃ dhyātvā jagādainamasurendraṃ baliṃ tadā || 4 ||
prahlāda uvāca
calanti girayo bhūmirjahāti sahajāṃ dhṛtim
sarve samudrāḥ kṣubhitā daityā nistejasaḥ kṛtāḥ || 5 ||
sūryodaye yathā pūrvaṃ tathā gacchanti na grahāḥ
devānāṃ ca parā lakṣmīḥ kāraṇairanumīyate || 6 ||
mahadetanmahābāho kāraṇaṃ dānaveśvara
nahyalpamiti mantavyaṃ tvayā kāryaṃ surārdana || 7 ||
śaunaka uvāca
ityuktvā dānavapatiṃ prahlādaḥ so'surottamaḥ
atyantabhakto deveśaṃ jagāma manasā harim || 8 ||
sa dhyānayogaṃ kṛtvā'tha prahlādaḥ sumanoharam
vicārayāmāsa tato yato devo janārdanaḥ || 9 ||
sa dadarśodare'dityāḥ prahlādo vāmanākṛtim
antaḥsthānbibhrataṃ sapta lokānādiprajāpatim || 10 ||
tadantaḥsthānvasūnrudrā naśvinau marutastathā
sādhyānviśvāṃstathā''dityāngandharvoragarākṣasān || 11 ||
virocanaṃ svatanayaṃ baliṃ cāsuranāyakam
jambhaṃ kujambhaṃ narakaṃ bāṇamanyāṃstathā'surān || 12 ||
ātmānumurvīṃ gaganaṃ vāyumambho hutāśanam
samudrā nvai drumasaritsarāṃsi ca paśūnmṛgān || 13 ||
vayomanuṣyānakhilāṃstathaiva ca sarīsṛpān
samastalokasraṣṭāraṃ brahmāṇaṃ bhavameva ca
grahanakṣatranāgāṃśca dakṣādyāṃśca prajāpatīn || 14 ||
sa paśyanvismayāviṣṭaḥ prakṛtisthaḥ kṣaṇātpunaḥ
prahlādaḥ prāha daityendraṃ baliṃ vairocaniṃ tadā || 15 ||
prahlāda uvāca
vatsa jñātaṃ mayā sarvaṃ yadarthaṃ bhavatāmiyam
tejaso hānirutpannā tacchṛṇu tvamaśeṣataḥ || 16 ||
devadevo jagadyonirayonirjagadādikṛt
anādirādirviśvasya vareṇyo varado hariḥ || 17 ||
parāvarāṇāṃ paramaḥ paraḥ paravatāmapi
pramāṇaṃ ca pramāṇānāṃ saptalokagurorguruḥ || 18 ||
prabhuḥ prabhūṇāṃ paramaḥ parāṇāmanādimadhyo bhagavānanantaḥ
trailokyamaṃśena sanāthameṣa kartuṃ mahātmā'ditijo'vatīrṇaḥ || 19 ||
na yasya rudro na ca padmayonirnendro na sūryendumarīcimukhyāḥ
jānanti daityādhipa yatsvarūpaṃ sa vāsudevaḥ kalayā'vatīrṇaḥ || 20 ||
yo'sau kalāṃśena nṛsiṃharūpī jaghāna pūrvaṃ pitaraṃ mameśaḥ
yaḥ sarvayogīśamanonivāsaḥ sa vāsudevaḥ kalayāvatīrṇaḥ || 21 ||
yamakṣaraṃ vedavido viditvā viśanti yajjñānavidhūtapāpāḥ
yasminpraviṣṭā na punarbhavanti taṃ vāsudevaṃ praṇamāmi nityam || 22 ||
bhūtānyaśeṣāṇi yato bhavanti yathormayastoyanidherajasram
layaṃ ca yasminpralaye prayānti taṃ vāsudevaṃ praṇamāmyacintyam || 23 ||
na yasya rūpaṃ balaprabhāvau na yasya bhāvaḥ paramasya puṃsaḥ
vijñāyate śarvapitāmahādyaistaṃ vāsudevaṃ praṇamāmyajasram || 24 ||
rūpasya cakṣurgrahaṇe tvagiṣṭā sparśe grahītrī rasanā rasasya
śrotraṃ ca śabdagrahaṇe narāṇāṃ ghrāṇaṃ ca gandhagrahaṇe niyuktam || 25 ||
yenaikadaṃṣṭrāgrasamuddhṛteyaṃ dharā'calāndhārayatīha sarvān
yasmiṃśca śete sakalaṃ jagacca tamīśamādyaṃ praṇato'smi viṣṇum || 26 ||
na ghrāṇacakṣuḥśravaṇādibhiryaḥ sarveśvaro veditumakṣayātmā
śakyastamīḍyaṃ manasaiva devaṃ grāhyaṃ nato'haṃ harimīśitāram || 27 ||
aṃśāvatīrṇena ca yena garbhe hṛtāni tejāṃsi mahāsurāṇām
namāmi taṃ devamanantamīśamaśeṣasaṃsārataroḥ kuṭhāram || 28 ||
devo jagadyonirayaṃ mahātmā sa ṣoḍaśāṃśena mahāsurendra
sa devamāturjaṭharaṃ praviṣṭo hṛtāni vastena balādvapūṃṣi || 29 ||
baliruvāca
tāta ko'yaṃ harirnāma yato no bhayamāgatam
santi me śataśo daityā vāsudevabalādhikāḥ || 30 ||
vipracittiḥ śibiḥ śaṅkurayaḥśaṅkustathaiva ca
ayaḥ śirāścāśvaśirā bhaṅgakārī mahāhanuḥ || 31 ||
pratāpaḥ praghasaḥ śumbhaḥ kukuraśca sudurjayaḥ
ete cānye ca me santi daiteyā dānavāstathā || 32 ||
mahābalā mahāvīryā bhūbhāroddharaṇakṣamāḥ
eṣāmekaikaśaḥ kṛṣṇo na vīryārdhena saṃmitaḥ || 33 ||
śaunaka uvāca
pautrasyaitadvacaḥ śrutvā prahlādo daityapuṃgavaḥ
dhigdhigityāha sa baliṃ vaikuṇṭhākṣepavādinam || 34 ||
prahlāda uvāca
vināśamupayāsyanti manye daiteyadānavāḥ
yeṣāṃ tvamīdṛśo rājā durbuddhiravivekavān || 35 ||
devadevaṃ mahābhāgaṃ vāsudevamajaṃ vibhum
tvāmṛte pāpasaṃkalpaḥ ko'nya evaṃ vadiṣyati || 36 ||
ya ete bhavatā proktāḥ samastā daityadānavāḥ
sabrahmakāstathā devāḥ sthāvarānantabhūmayaḥ || 37 ||
tvaṃ cāhaṃ ca jagaccedaṃ sādri drumanadīnadam
samudra dvīpalokāśca na samaṃ keśavasya hi || 38 ||
yasyātivandyavandyasya vyāpinaḥ paramātmanaḥ
ekāṃśena jagatsarvaṃ kastamevaṃ pravakṣyati || 39 ||
ṛte vināśābhimukhaṃ tvāmekamavivekinam
kubuddhimajitātmānaṃ vṛddhānāṃ śāsanātigam || 40 ||
śocyo'haṃ yasya me gehe jātastava pitā'dhamaḥ
yasya tvamīdṛśaḥ putro devadevasya nindakaḥ || 41 ||
tiṣṭhatveṣā hi saṃsārasaṃbhṛtāghavināśinī
kṛṣṇe bhaktirahaṃ tāvadavekṣyo bhavatā na kim || 42 ||
na me priyatamaḥ kṛṣṇādapi deho mahātmanaḥ
iti jānātyayaṃ loko na bhavānditijādhama || 43 ||
jānannapa priyataraṃ prāṇebhyo'pi hariṃ mama
nindāṃ karoṣi tasya tvamakurvangauravaṃ mama || 44 ||
virocanastava gururgurustasyāpyahaṃ bale
mamāpi sarvajagatāṃ gurornārāyaṇo guruḥ || 45 ||
nindāṃ karoṣi yastasminkṛṣṇe gurugurorgurau
yasmāttasmādihaiśvaryādacirādbhraṃśameṣyasi || 46 ||
mama devo jagannātho bale tāvajjanārdanaḥ
bhavatvahamupekṣyaste prītimānastu me guruḥ || 47 ||
etāvanmātramapyevaṃ ninditastrijagadguruḥ
nāvekṣitaṃ tvayā yasmāttasmācchāpaṃ dadāmi te || 48 ||
yathā me śirasaśchedādidaṃ gurutaraṃ vacaḥ
tvayoktamacyutākṣepi rājyabhraṣṭastathā pata || 49 ||
yathā ca kṛṣṇānna paraṃ paritrāṇaṃ bhavārṇave
tathā'cireṇa paśyeyaṃ bhavantaṃ rājyavicyutam || 50 ||
śaunaka uvāca
iti daityapatiḥ śrutvā gurorvacanamapriyam
prasādayāmāsa guruṃ praṇipatya punaḥ punaḥ || 51 ||
baliruvāca
prasīda tāta mā kopaṃ kuru mohahate mayi
balāvalepamattena mayaitadvākyamīritam || 52 ||
mohopahatavijñānaḥ pāpo'haṃ ditijottama
yacchapto'smi durācārastatsādhu bhavatā kṛtam || 53 ||
rājyabhraṃśaṃ vasubhraṃśaṃ saṃprāpsyāmīti na tvaham
viṣaṇṇo'smi yathā tāta tavaivāvinaye kṛte || 54 ||
trailokyarājyamaiśvaryamanyadvā nāti durlabham
saṃsāre durlabhāste tu guravo ye bhavadvidhāḥ || 55 ||
tatprasīda na me kopaṃ kartumarhasi daityapa
tvatkopadṛṣṭyā tātāhaṃ paritapye na śāpataḥ || 56 ||
prahlāda uvāca
vatsa kopena moho me janitastena te mayā
śāpo datto vivekaśca mohenāpahṛto mama || 57 ||
yadi mohena me jñānaṃ nā''kṣiptaṃ syānmahāsura
tatkathaṃ sarvagaṃ jānanhariṃ kiṃcicchapāmyaham || 58 ||
yo'yaṃ śāpo mayā datto bhavato'surapuṃgava
bhāvyametena nūnaṃ te tasmānmā tvaṃ viṣīda vai || 59 ||
adyaprabhṛti deveśe bhagavatyacyute harau
bhavethā bhaktimānīśe sa te trātā bhaviṣyati || 60 ||
śāpaṃ prāpyātha māṃ vīra saṃsmarethāḥ smṛtastvayā
yathā tathā yatiṣye'haṃ śreyasā yojyase yathā || 61 ||
evamuktvā sa daityendraṃ virarāma mahāmatiḥ
ajāyata sa govindo bhagavānvāmanākṛtiḥ || 62 ||
avatīrṇe jagannāthe tasminsarvāmareśvare
devāśca mumucurduḥkhaṃ devamātā'ditistathā || 63 ||
vavurvātāḥ sukhasparśā virajaskamabhūnnabhaḥ
dharme ca sarvabhūtānāṃ tadā matirajāyata || 64 ||
nodvegaścāpyabhūttatra manujendrā sureṣvapi
tadādi sarvabhūtānāṃ bhūmyambaradivaukasām || 65 ||
taṃ jātamātraṃ bhagavānbrahmā lokapitāmahaḥ
jātakarmādikaṃ kṛtvā kṛṣṇaṃ dṛṣṭvā ca pārthiva
tuṣṭāva devadeveśamṛṣīṇāṃ caiva śṛṇvatām || 66 ||
brahmovāca
jayā''dyeśa jayājeya jaya sarvātmakātmaka
jaya janmajarāpena jayānanta jayācyuta || 67 ||
jayājita jayāmeya jayāvyaktasthite jaya
paramārthārtha sarvajña jñānajñeyā''tmaniḥsṛta || 68 ||
jayāśeṣajagatsākṣiñjagatkartarjagadguro
jagato'syantakṛddeva sthitiṃ pālayituṃ jaya || 69 ||
jaya śeṣa jayāśeṣa jayākhilahṛdisthita
jayādimadhyānta jaya sarvajñānanidhe jaya || 70 ||
mumukṣubhiranirdeśya svayaṃdṛṣṭa jayeśvara
yogināṃ muktiphalada damādiguṇabhūṣaṇa || 71 ||
jayātisūkṣma durjñeya jaya sthūla jaganmaya
jaya sthūlātisūkṣma tvaṃ jayātīndri ya sendri ya || 72 ||
jaya svamāyāyogastha śeṣabhogaśayākṣara
jayaikadaṃṣṭrāprāntāgrasamuddhṛtavasuṃdhara || 73 ||
nṛkesariñjayārātivakṣaḥsthalavidāraṇa
sāṃprataṃ jaya viśvātmañjaya vāmana keśava || 74 ||
nijamāyāpaṭacchanna jaganmūrte janārdana
jayācintya jayānekasvarūpaikavidha prabho || 75 ||
vardhasva vardhitāśeṣavikāraprakṛte hare
tvayyeṣā jagatāmīśe saṃsthitā dharmapaddhatiḥ || 76 ||
na tvāmahaṃ na ceśāno nendrā dyāstridaśā hare
na jñātumīśā munayaḥ sanakādyā na yoginaḥ || 77 ||
tvanmāyāpaṭasaṃvīto jagatyatra jagatpate
kastvāṃ vetsyati sarveśa tvatprasādaṃ vinā naraḥ || 78 ||
tvamevā''rādhito yena prasādasumukhaprabho
sa eva kevalo deva vetti tvāṃ netare janāḥ || 79 ||
nandīśvareśvareśāna prabho vardhasva vāmana
prabhavāyāsya viśvasya viśvātmanpṛthulocanā || 80 ||
śaunaka uvāca
evaṃ stuto hṛṣīkeśaḥ sa tadā vāmanākṛtiḥ
prahasya bhāvagambhīramuvācābjasamudbhavam || 81 ||
stuto'haṃ bhavatā pūrvamindrā dyaiḥ kaśyapena ca
mayā ca vaḥ pratijñātamindra sya bhuvanatrayam || 82 ||
bhūyaścāhaṃ stuto'dityā tasyāścāpi pratiśrutam
yathā śakrāya dāsyāmi trailokyaṃ hatakaṇṭakam || 83 ||
so'haṃ tathā kariṣyāmi yathendro jagataḥ patiḥ
bhaviṣyati sahasrākṣaḥ satyametadbravīmi vaḥ || 84 ||
tataḥ kṛṣṇājinaṃ brahmā hṛṣīkeśāya dattavān
yajñopavītaṃ bhagavāndadau tasmai bṛhaspatiḥ || 85 ||
āṣāḍhamadadāddaṇḍaṃ marīcirbrahmaṇaḥ sutaḥ
kamaṇḍaluṃ vasiṣṭhaśca kauśaṃ vedamathāṅgirāḥ || 86 ||
akṣasūtraṃ ca pulahaḥ pulastyaḥ sitavāsasī
upatasthuśca taṃ vedāḥ praṇavasvarabhūṣaṇāḥ
śāstrāṇyaśeṣāṇi tathā sāṃkhyayogoktayaśca yāḥ || 87 ||
sa vāmano jaṭī daṇḍī chatrī dhṛtakamaṇḍaluḥ
sarvadevamayo bhūpa baleradhvaramabhyagāt || 88 ||
yatra yatra padaṃ bhūyo bhūbhāge vāmano dadau
dadāti bhūmirvivaraṃ tatra tatrātipīḍitā || 89 ||
sa vāmano jaḍagatirmṛdu gacchansaparvatām
sābdhidvīpavatīṃ sarvāṃ cālayāmāsa medinīm || 90 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe vāmanaprādurbhāve vāmanotpattirnāma pañcacatvāriṃśadadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 245 ||

<246>

atha ṣaṭcatvāriṃśadadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
śaunaka uvāca
saparvatavanāmurvīṃ dṛṣṭvā saṃkṣobhitāṃ baliḥ
papracchośanasaṃ śuddhaṃ praṇipatya kṛtāñjaliḥ || 1 ||
ācārya kṣobhamāyātā sābdhibhūbhṛdvanā mahī
kasmācca nā''surānbhāgānpratigṛhṇanti vahnayaḥ || 2 ||
iti pṛṣṭo'tha balinā kāvyo vedavidāṃ varaḥ
uvāca daityādhipatiṃ ciraṃ dhyātvā mahāmatiḥ || 3 ||
avatīrṇo jagadyoniḥ kaśyapasya gṛhe hariḥ
vāmaneneha rūpeṇa jagadātmā sanātanaḥ || 4 ||
sa eṣa jajñamāyāti tava dānavapuṃgava
tatpādanyāsavikṣobhādiyaṃ pracalitā mahī
kampante girayaścāmī kṣubhito makarālayaḥ || 5 ||
nainaṃ bhūtapatiṃ bhūmiḥ samarthā voḍhumīśvaram
sadevāsuragandharvayakṣarākṣasakiṃnarā || 6 ||
anenaiva dhṛtā bhūmirāpo'gniḥ pavano nabhaḥ
dhārayatyakhilāndevo manvādīṃśca mahāsura || 7 ||
iyameva jagaddhetormāyā kṛṣṇasya gahvarī
dhāryadhārakabhāvena yayā saṃpīḍitaṃ jagat || 8 ||
tatsaṃnidhānādasurā bhāgārhā nāsurottama
bhuñjate nā''surānbhāgānamī tenaiva cāgnayaḥ || 9 ||
baliruvāca
dhanyo'haṃ kṛtapuṇyaśca yanme yajñapatiḥ svayam
yajñamabhyāgato brahmanmattaḥ ko'nyo'dhikaḥ pumān || 10 ||
yaṃ yoginaḥ sadā yuktāḥ paramātmānamavyayam
dra ṣṭumicchanti deveśaṃ sa bhe'dhvaramupaiṣyati || 11 ||
hotā bhāgaprado'yaṃ ca yamudgātā ca gāyati
tamadhvareśvaraṃ viṣṇuṃ mattaḥ ko'nya upaiṣyati || 12 ||
sarveśvareśvare kṛṣṇe madadhvaramupāgate
yanmayā kāvya kartavyaṃ tanmamā''deṣṭumarhasi || 13 ||
śukra uvāca
yajñabhāgabhujo devā vedagrāmāṇyato'sura
tvayā tu dānavā daityā makhabhāgabhujaḥ kṛtāḥ || 14 ||
ayaṃ ca devaḥ sattvasthaḥ karoti sthitipālanam
visṛṣṭeranu cānnena svayamatti prajāḥ prabhuḥ || 15 ||
tvatkṛte bhavitā nūnaṃ devo viṣṇuḥ sthitau sthitaḥ
viditvaitanmahābhāga kuru yatnamanāgatam || 16 ||
tvayā hi daityādhipate svalpake'pi hi vastuni
pratijñā na hi voḍhavyā vācyaṃ sāma vṛthāphalam || 17 ||
nālaṃ dātumahaṃ deva daitya vācyaṃ tvayā vacaḥ
kṛṣṇasya devabhūtyarthaṃ pravṛttasya mahāsura || 18 ||
baliruvāca
brahmankathamahaṃ brūyāmanyenāpi hi yācitaḥ
nāstīti kimu devena saṃsārāghaughahāriṇā || 19 ||
vratopavāsairvividhaiḥ pratisaṃgrāhyate hariḥ
sa cedvakṣyati dehīti govindaḥ kimato'dhikam || 20 ||
yadarthamupahārāḍhyāstapaḥśaucaguṇānvitaiḥ
yajñāḥ kriyante deveśaḥ sa māṃ dehīti vakṣyati || 21 ||
tatsādhu sukṛtaṃ karma tapaḥ sucaritaṃ mama
yanmayā dattamīśeśaḥ svayamādāsyate hariḥ || 22 ||
nāsti nāstītyahaṃ vakṣye tamapyāgatamīśvaram
yadi vañcāmi taṃ prāptaṃ vṛthā tajjanmanaḥ phalam || 23 ||
yajñe'sminyadi yajñeśo yācate māṃ janārdanaḥ
nijamūrdhānamapyatra taddāsyāmyavicāritam || 24 ||
nāstīti yanmayā noktamanyeṣāmapi yācatām
vakṣyāmi kathamāyāte tadanabhyastamacyute || 25 ||
ślāghya eva hi vīrāṇāṃ dānādāpatsamāgamaḥ
nābādhakāri yaddānaṃ tadamaṅgalavatsmṛtam || 26 ||
madrā jye nāsukhī kaścinna daridro na cā'turaḥ
nābhūṣito na codvigno na sragādivivarjitaḥ || 27 ||
hṛṣṭastuṣṭaḥ sugandhiśca tṛptaḥ sarvasukhānvitaḥ
janaḥ sarvo mahābhāga kimutāhaṃ sadā sukhī || 28 ||
etadviśiṣṭapātro'yaṃ dānabījaphalaṃ mama
viditaṃ bhṛguśārdūla mayaitattvatprasādataḥ || 29 ||
etadvijānato dānabījaṃ patati cedguro
janārdanamahāpātre kiṃ na prāptaṃ tato mayā || 30 ||
matto dānamavāpyeśo yadi puṣṇāti devatāḥ
upabhogāddaśaguṇaṃ dānaṃ ślāghyatamaṃ mama || 31 ||
matprasādaparo nūnaṃ yajñenā''rādhito hariḥ
tenābhyeti na saṃdeho darśanādupakārakṛt || 32 ||
atha kopena cābhyeti devabhāgoparodhinam
māṃ nihantumanāścaiva vadhaḥ ślāghyataro'cyutām || 33 ||
tanmayaṃ sarvamevedaṃ nāprāpyaṃ yasya vidyate
sa māṃ yācitumabhyeti nānugrahamṛte hariḥ || 34 ||
yaḥ sṛjatyātmabhūḥ sarvaṃ cetasaiva ca saṃharet
sa māṃ hantuṃ hṛṣīkeśaḥ kathaṃ yatnaṃ kariṣyati || 35 ||
etadviditvā na guro dānavighnakareṇa me
tvayā bhāvyaṃ jagannāthe govinde samupasthite || 36 ||
śaunaka uvāca
ityevaṃ vadatastasya saṃprāptaḥ sa jagatpatiḥ
sarvadevamayo'cintyo māyāvāmanarūpadhṛk || 37 ||
taṃ dṛṣṭvā yajñavāṭāntaḥ praviṣṭamasurāḥ prabhum
jagmuḥ sabhāsadaḥ kṣobhaṃ tejasā tasya niṣprabhāḥ || 38 ||
jepuśca munayastatra ye sametā mahādhvare
baliścaivākhilaṃ janma mene saphalamātmanaḥ || 39 ||
tataḥ saṃkṣobhamāpanno na kaścitkiṃciduktavān
pratyekaṃ devadeveśaṃ pūjayāmāsa cetasā || 40 ||
athāsurapatiṃ prahvaṃ dṛṣṭvā munivarāṃśca tān
devadevapatiḥ sākṣī viṣṇurvāmanarūpadhṛk || 41 ||
tuṣṭāva yajñavahniṃ ca yajamānamatharttvijaḥ
yajñakarmādhikārasthānsadasyāndra vyasaṃpadaḥ || 42 ||
tataḥ prasannamakhilaṃ vāmanaṃ prati tatkṣaṇāt
yajñavāṭasthitaṃ vīraḥ sādhu sādhvityudīrayan || 43 ||
sa cārghamādāya baliḥ prodbhūtapulakastadā
pūjayāmāsa govindaṃ prāha cedaṃ mahāsuraḥ || 44 ||
baliruvāca
suvarṇaratnasaṃghātaṃ gajāśvamamitaṃ tathā
striyo vastrāṇyalaṃkārāṃstathā grāmāṃśca puṣkalān || 45 ||
sarvasvaṃ sakalāmurvīṃ bhavato vā yadīpsitam
taddadāmi vṛṇuṣva tvaṃ yenārthī vāmanaḥ priyaḥ || 46 ||
śaunaka uvāca
ityukto daityapatinā prītigarbhānvitaṃ vacaḥ
prāha sasmitagambhīraṃ bhagavānvāmanākṛtiḥ || 47 ||
vāmana uvāca
mamāgniśaraṇārthāya dehi rājanpadatrayam
suvarṇagrāmaratnāni tadarthibhyaḥ pradīyatām || 48 ||
baliruvāca
tribhiḥ prayojanaṃ kiṃ te pādaiḥ padavatāṃ vara
śataṃ śatasahasrāṇāṃ padānāṃ mārgatāṃ bhavān || 49 ||
vāmana uvāca
dharmabuddhyā daityapate kṛtakṛtyo'smi tāvatā
anyeṣāmarthināṃ vittamīhitaṃ dāsyate bhavān
śaunaka uvāca
etacchrutvā tu gaditaṃ vāmanasya mahātmanaḥ
dadau tasmai mahābāhurvāmanāya padatrayam || 51 ||
pāṇau tu patite toye vāmano'bhūdavāmanaḥ
sarvadevamayaṃ rūpaṃ darśayāmāsa tatkṣaṇāt || 52 ||
candra sūryau ca nayane dyaurmūrdhā caraṇau kṣitiḥ
pādāṅgulyaḥ piśācāstu hastāṅgulyaśca guhyakāḥ || 53 ||
viśve devāśca jānusthā jaṅghe sādhyāḥ surottamāḥ
yakṣā nakheṣu saṃbhūtā rekhāścāpsarasastathā || 54 ||
dṛṣṭau ṛkṣāṇyaśeṣāṇi keśāḥ sūryāṃśavaḥ prabhoḥ
tārakā romakūpāṇi romāṇi ca maharṣayaḥ || 55 ||
bāhavo vidiśastasya diśaḥ śrotre mahātmanaḥ
aśvinau śravaṇe tasya nāsā vāyurmahātmanaḥ || 56 ||
prasādaścandra mā devo mano dharmaḥ samāśritaḥ
satyaṃ tasyābhavadvāṇī jihvā devī sarasvatī || 57 ||
grīvā'ditirdevamātā vidyāstadvalayastathā
svargadvāramabhūnmaitraṃ tvaṣṭā pūṣā ca vai bhruvau || 58 ||
mukhaṃ vaiśvānaraścāsya vṛṣaṇau tu prajāpatiḥ
hṛdayaṃ ca paraṃ brahma puṃstvaṃ vai kaśyapo muniḥ || 59 ||
pṛṣṭhe'sya vasavo devā marutaḥ sarvasaṃdhiṣu
sarvasūktāni daśanā jyotīṃṣi vimalaprabhāḥ || 60 ||
vakṣaḥsthale mahādevo dhairye cāsya mahārṇavāḥ
udare cāsya gandharvāḥ saṃbhūtāśca mahābalāḥ || 61 ||
lakṣmīrmedhā dhṛtiḥ kāntiḥ sarvavidyāśca vai kaṭiḥ
sarvajyotīṃṣi jānīhi tasya tatparamaṃ mahaḥ || 62 ||
tasya devādhidevasya tejaḥ prodbhūtamuttamam
stanau kukṣī ca vedāśca udaraṃ ca mahāmakhāḥ || 63 ||
iṣṭayaḥ paśubandhāśca dvijānāṃ ceṣṭitāni ca
tasya devamayaṃ rūpaṃ dṛṣṭvā viṣṇormahābalāḥ || 64 ||
upāsarpanta daityendrāḥ pataṅgā iva pāvakam
pramathya sarvānasurānpādahastatalairvibhuḥ || 65 ||
kṛtvā rūpaṃ mahākāyaṃ jahārā''śu sa medinīm
tasya vikramato bhūmiṃ candrā dityau stanāntare || 66 ||
nābhau vikramamāṇasya sakthideśasthitāvubhau
paraṃ vikramatastasya jānumūle prabhākarau || 67 ||
viṣṇorāstāṃ mahīpāla devapālanakarmaṇi
jitvā lokatrayaṃ kṛtsnaṃ hatvā cāsurapuṃgavān || 68 ||
puraṃdarāya trailokyaṃ dadau viṣṇururukramaḥ
sutalaṃ nāma pātālamadhastādvasudhātalāt || 69 ||
balerdattaṃ bhagavatā viṣṇunā prabhaviṣṇunā
atha daityeśvaraṃ prāha viṣṇuḥ sarveśvareśvaraḥ || 70 ||
śrībhagavānuvāca
yattvayā salilaṃ dattaṃ gṛhītaṃ pāṇinā mayā
kalpapramāṇaṃ tasmātte bhaviṣyaṃtyāyuruttamam || 71 ||
vaivasvate tathā'tīte bale manvantare hyatha
sāvarṇike tu saṃprāpte bhavānindro bhaviṣyati || 72 ||
sāṃprataṃ devarājāya trailokyaṃ sakalaṃ mayā
dattaṃ caturyugāṇāṃ ca sādhikā hyekasaptatiḥ || 73 ||
niyantavyā mayā sarve ye tasya paripanthinaḥ
tenāhaṃ parayā bhaktyā pūrvamārādhito bale || 74 ||
sutalaṃ nāma pātālaṃ tvamāsādya manoramam
vasāsura mamā''deśaṃ yathāvatparipālayan || 75 ||
tatra divyavanopete prāsādaśatasaṃkule
protphullapadmasarasi sravacchuddhasaridvare || 76 ||
sugandhidhūpasragvastravarābharaṇabhūṣitaḥ
srakcandanādimudito geyanṛtyamanorame || 77 ||
pānānnabhogānvividhānupabhuṅkṣva mahāsura
mamā''jñayā kālamimaṃ tiṣṭha strīśatasaṃvṛtaḥ || 78 ||
yāvatsuraiśca vipraiśca na virodhaṃ kariṣyasi
tāvadetānmahābhogānavāpsyasi mahāsura || 79 ||
yadā ca devaviprāṇāṃ virodhaṃ tvaṃ kariṣyasi
bandhiṣyanti tadā pāśā vāruṇāstvāmasaṃśayam || 80 ||
etadviditvā bhavatā mayā''jñaptamaśeṣataḥ
na virodhaḥ suraiḥ kāryo viprairvā daityasattama || 81 ||
śaunaka uvāca
ityevamukto devena viṣṇunā prabhaviṣṇunā
baliḥ prāha mahārāja praṇipatya mudā yutaḥ || 82 ||
baliruvāca
tatrā''ptato me pātāle bhagavanbhavadājñayā
kiṃ bhaviṣyatyupādānamupabhogopapādakam || 83 ||
śrībhagavānuvāca
dānānyavidhidattāni śrāddhānyaśrotriyāṇi ca
hutānyaśraddhayā yāni tāni dāsyanti te phalam || 84 ||
adakṣiṇāstathā yajñāḥ kriyāścāvidhinā kṛtāḥ
phalāni tava dāsyanti adhītānyavratāni ca || 85 ||
śaunaka uvāca
balervaramimaṃ dattvā śakrāya tridivaṃ tathā
vyāpinā tena rūpeṇa jagāmādarśanaṃ hariḥ || 86 ||
praśaśāsa yathāpūrvamindra strailokyapūjitaḥ
siṣeve ca parānkāmānbaliḥ pātālasaṃsthitaḥ || 87 ||
ihaiva devadevena baddho'sau dānavottamaḥ
devānāṃ kāryakaraṇe bhūyo'pi jagati sthitaḥ || 88 ||
saṃbandhī te mahābhāga dvārakāyāṃ vyavasthitaḥ
dānavānāṃ vināśāya bhārāvataraṇāya ca || 89 ||
yato yadukule kṛṣṇo bhavataḥ śatrunigrahe
sahāyabhūtaḥ sārathyaṃ kariṣyati balānujaḥ || 90 ||
etatsarvaṃ samākhyātaṃ vāmanasya ca dhīmataḥ
avatāraṃ mahāvīra śrotumicchostavārjuna || 91 ||
arjuna uvāca
śrutavāniha te pṛṣṭaṃ māhātmyaṃ keśavasya ca
gaṅgādvāramito yāsyāmyanujñāṃ dehi me vibho || 92 ||
sūta uvāca
evamuktvā yayau pārtho naimiṣaṃ śaunako gataḥ
ityetaddevadevasya viṣṇormāhātmyamuttamam
vāmanasya paṭhedyastu sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate || 93 ||
baliprahlādasaṃvādaṃ mantritaṃ baliśukrayoḥ
balerviṣṇośca kathitaṃ yaḥ smariṣyati mānavaḥ || 94 ||
nā''dhayo vyādhayastasya na ca mohākulaṃ manaḥ
bhaviṣyati dvijaśreṣṭhāḥ puṃsastasya kadācana || 95 ||
cyutarājyo nijaṃ rājyamiṣṭāptiṃ ca viyogavān
avāpnoti mahābhāgo naraḥ śrutvā kathāmimām || 96 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe
vāmanaprādurbhāvo nāma ṣaṭcatvāriṃśadadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 246 ||

<247>

atha saptacatvāriṃśadadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
arjuna uvāca
prādurbhāvānpurāṇeṣu viṣṇoramitatejasaḥ
satāṃ kathayatāṃ vipra vārāha iti naḥ śrutam || 1 ||
jāne na tasya caritaṃ na vidhiṃ na ca vistaram
na karma guṇasaṃsthānaṃ na cāpyantaṃ manīṣiṇaḥ || 2 ||
kimātmako varāho'sau kiṃ mūrtiḥ kāsya devatā
kiṃ pramāṇaḥ kiṃprabhāvaḥ kiṃvā tena purā kṛtam || 3 ||
etanme śaṃsa tattvena vārāhaṃ śrutivistaram
yathārhaṃ ca sametānāṃ dvijātīnāṃ viśeṣataḥ || 4 ||
śaunaka uvāca
etatte kathayiṣyāmi purāṇaṃ brahmasaṃmitam
mahāvarāhacaritaṃ kṛṣṇasyādbhutakarmaṇaḥ || 5 ||
yathā nārāyaṇo rājanvārāhaṃ vapurāsthitaḥ
daṃṣṭrayā gāṃ samudra sthāmujjahārārimardanaḥ || 6 ||
chandogīrbhirudārābhiḥ śrutibhiḥ samalaṃkṛtaḥ
manaḥ prasannatāṃ kṛtvā nibodha vijayādhunā || 7 ||
idaṃ purāṇaṃ paramaṃ puṇyaṃ vedaiśca saṃmitam
nānāśrutisamāyuktaṃ nāstikāya na kīrtayet || 8 ||
purāṇaṃ vedamakhilaṃ sāṃkhyaṃ yogaṃ ca veda yaḥ
kārtsnyena vidhinā proktaṃ so'syārthaṃ vedayiṣyati || 9 ||
viśve devāstathā sādhyā rudrā dityāstathā'śvinau
prajānāṃ patayaścaiva sapta caiva maharṣayaḥ || 10 ||
manaḥsaṃkalpajāścaiva pūrvajā ṛṣayastathā
vasavo marutaścaiva gandharvā yakṣarākṣasāḥ || 11 ||
daityāḥ piśācā nāgāśca bhūtāni vividhāni ca
brāhmaṇāḥ kṣattriyā vaiśyāḥ śūdrā mlecchāśca ye bhuvi || 12 ||
catuṣpadāni sarvāṇi tiryagyoniśatāni ca
jaṅgamāni ca sattvāni yaccānyajjīvasaṃjñitam || 13 ||
pūrṇe yugasahasre tu brāhme'hani tathāgate
nirvāṇe sarvabhūtānāṃ sarvotpātasamudbhave || 14 ||
hiraṇyaretāstriśikhastato bhūtvā vṛṣākapiḥ
śikhābhirvidhamam̐l lokānaśoṣayata vahninā || 15 ||
dahyamānāstatastasya tejorāśibhirudgataiḥ
vivarṇavarṇā dagdhāṅgā hatārciṣmadbhirānanaiḥ || 16 ||
sāṅgopaniṣado vedā itihāsapurogamāḥ
sarvavidyāḥ kriyāścaiva sarvadharmaparāyaṇāḥ || 17 ||
brahmāṇamagrataḥ kṛtvā prabhavaṃ viśvatomukham
sarvadevagaṇāścaiva trayastriṃśattu koṭayaḥ || 18 ||
tasminnahani saṃprāpte taṃ haṃsaṃ mahadakṣaram
praviśanti mahātmānaṃ hariṃ nārāyaṇaṃ prabhum || 19 ||
teṣāṃ bhūyaḥ pravṛttānāṃ nidhanotpattirucyate
yathā sūryasya satatamudayāstamane iha || 20 ||
pūrṇe yugasahasrānte kalpo niḥśeṣa ucyate
yasmiñjīvakṛtaṃ sarve niḥśeṣaṃ samatiṣṭhata || 21 ||
saṃhṛtya lokānakhilānsadevāsuramānuṣān
kṛtvā susaṃsthāṃ bhagavānāsta eko jagadguruḥ || 22 ||
sa sraṣṭā sarvabhūtānāṃ kalpānteṣu punaḥ punaḥ
avyayaḥ śāśvato devo yasya sarvamidaṃ jagat || 23 ||
naṣṭārkakiraṇe loke candra grahavivarjite
tyaktadhūmāgnipavane kṣīṇayajñavaṣaṭkriye || 24 ||
apakṣigaṇasaṃpāte sarvaprāṇihare pathi
amaryādākule raudre sarvatastamasā''vṛte || 25 ||
adṛśye sarvaloke'sminnabhāve sarvakarmaṇām
praśānte sarvasaṃpāte naṣṭe vairaparigrahe || 26 ||
gate svabhāvasaṃsthāne loke nārāyaṇātmake
parameṣṭhī hṛṣīkeśaḥ śayanāyopacakrame || 27 ||
pītavāsā lohitākṣaḥ kṛṣṇo jīmūtasaṃnibhaḥ
śikhāsahasravikacajaṭābhāraṃ samudvahan || 28 ||
śrīvatsalakṣaṇadharaṃ raktacandanabhūṣitam
vakṣo bibhranmahābāhuḥ sa vidyudiva toyadaḥ || 29 ||
puṇḍarīkasahasreṇa sragasya śuśubhe śubhā
patnī cāsya svayaṃ lakṣmīrdehamāvṛtya tiṣṭhati || 30 ||
tataḥ svapiti śāntātmā sarvalokaśubhāvahaḥ
kimapyamitayogātmā nidrā yogamupāgataḥ || 31 ||
tato yugasahasre tu pūrṇe sa puruṣottamaḥ
svayameva vibhurbhūtvā budhyate vibudhādhipaḥ || 32 ||
tataścintayate bhūyaḥ sṛṣṭiṃ lokasya lokakṛt
narāndevagaṇāṃścaiva pārameṣṭhyena karmaṇā || 33 ||
tataḥ saṃcintayankāryaṃ deveṣu samitiṃjayaḥ
saṃbhavaṃ sarvalokasya vidadhāti satāṃ gatiḥ || 34 ||
kartā caiva vikartā ca saṃhartā vai prajāpatiḥ
nārāyaṇaḥ paraṃ satyaṃ nārāyaṇaḥ paraṃ padam || 35 ||
nārāyaṇaḥ paro yajño nārāyaṇaḥ parā gatiḥ
sa svayaṃbhūriti jñeyaḥ sa sraṣṭā bhuvanādhipaḥ || 36 ||
sa sarvamiti vijñeyo hyeṣa yajñaḥ prajāpatiḥ
yadveditavyastridaśaistadeṣa parikīrtyate || 37 ||
yattu vedyaṃ bhagavato devā api na tadviduḥ
prajānāṃ patayaḥ sarve ṛṣayaśca sahāmaraiḥ || 38 ||
nāsyāntamadhigacchanti vicinvanta iti śrutiḥ
yadasya paramaṃ rūpaṃ na tatpaśyanti devatāḥ || 39 ||
prādurbhāve tu yadrū paṃ tadarcanti divaukasaḥ
