darśitaṃ yadi tenaiva tadavekṣanti devatāḥ || 40 ||
yanna darśitavāneṣa kastadanveṣṭumīhate
grāmaṇīḥ sarvabhūtānāmagnimārutayorgatiḥ || 41 ||
tejasastapasaścaiva nidhānamamṛtasya ca
caturāśramadharmeśaścāturhotraphalāśanaḥ || 42 ||
catuḥsāgaraparyantaścaturyuganivartakaḥ
tadeṣa saṃhṛtya jagatkṛtvā garbhasthamātmanaḥ
mumocāṇḍaṃ mahāyogī dhṛtaṃ varṣasahasrakam || 43 ||
surāsuradvijabhujagāpsarogaṇairdrumauṣadhikṣitidharayakṣaguhyakaiḥ
prajāpatiḥ śrutibhirasaṃkulaṃ kila tadā'sṛjajjagadidamātmanā prabhuḥ || 44 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe varāhaprādurbhāve saptacatvāriṃśadadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 247 ||

<248>

athāṣṭacatvāriṃśadadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
śaunaka uvāca
jagadaṇḍamidaṃ pūrvamāsīddivyaṃ hiraṇmayam
prajāpateriyaṃ mūrtiritīyaṃ vaidikī śrutiḥ || 1 ||
tattu varṣasahasrānte bibhedordhvamukhaṃ vibhuḥ
lokasarjanahetostu bibhedādhomukhaṃ punaḥ || 2 ||
bhūyo'ṣṭadhā bibhedāṇḍaṃ viṣṇurvai lokajanmakṛt
cakāra jagataścātra vibhāgaṃ sa vibhāgakṛt || 3 ||
yacchidra mūrdhvamākāśaṃ vivarākṛtitāṃ gatam
vihitaṃ viśvayogena yadadhastadra sātalam || 4 ||
yadaṇḍamakarotpūrvaṃ devo lokacikīrṣayā
tatra yatsalilaṃ skannaṃ so'bhavatkāñcano giriḥ || 5 ||
śailaiḥ sahasrairmahatī medinī viṣamā'bhavat || 6 ||
taiśca parvatajālaughairbahuyojanavistṛtaiḥ
pīḍitā gurubhirdevī vyathitā medinī tadā || 7 ||
mahāmate bhūribalaṃ divyaṃ nārāyaṇātmakam
hiraṇmayaṃ samutsṛjya tejo vai jātarūpiṇam || 8 ||
aśaktā vai dhārayitumadhastātprāviśattadā
pīḍyamānā bhagavatastejasā tasya sā kṣitiḥ || 9 ||
pṛthvīṃ viśantīṃ dṛṣṭvā tu tāmadho madhusūdanaḥ
uddhārārthaṃ manaścakre tasyā vai hitakāmyayā || 10 ||
bhagavānuvāca
matteja eṣā vasudhā samāsādya tapasvinī
rasātalaṃ praviśati paṅke gauriva durbalā || 11 ||
pṛthivyuvāca
trivikramāyāmitavikrāya mahāvarāhāya surottamāya
śrīśārṅgacakrāsigadādharāya namo'stu te devavara prasīda || 12 ||
tava dehājagajjātaṃ puṣkaradvīpamutthitam
brahmāṇamihalokānāṃ bhūtānāṃ śāśvataṃ viduḥ || 13 ||
tava prasādāddevo'yaṃ divaṃ bhuṅkte puraṃdaraḥ
tava krodhāddhi balavāñjanārdana jito baliḥ || 14 ||
dhātā vidhātā saṃhartā tvayi sarvaṃ pratiṣṭhitam
manuḥ kṛtānto'dhipatirjvalanaḥ pavano ghanaḥ || 15 ||
varṇāścā''śramadharmāśca sāgarāstaravo'calāḥ
nadyo dharmaśca kāmaśca yajñā yajñasya ca kriyāḥ || 16 ||
vidyā vedyaṃ ca sattvaṃ ca hrīḥ śrīḥ kīrtirdhṛtiḥ kṣamā
purāṇaṃ vedavedāṅgaṃ sāṃkhyayogau bhavābhavau || 17 ||
jaṅgamaṃ sthāvaraṃ caiva bhaviṣyaṃ ca bhavacca yat
sarvaṃ tacca trilokeṣu prabhāvopahitaṃ tava || 18 ||
tridaśodāraphaladaḥ svargastrīcārupallavaḥ
sarvalokamanaḥkāntaḥ sarvasattvamanoharaḥ || 19 ||
vimānānekaviṭapastoyadāmbumadhusravaḥ
divyalokamahāskandhaḥ satyalokapraśākhavān || 20 ||
sāgarākāraniryāso rasātalajalāśrayaḥ
nāgendra pādapopeto jantupakṣiniṣevitaḥ || 21 ||
śīlācārāryagandhastvaṃ sarvalokamayo drumaḥ
dvādaśārkamayadvīpo rudrai kādaśapattanaḥ || 22 ||
vasvaṣṭācalasaṃyuktastrailokyāmbhomahodadhiḥ
siddhasādhyormikalilaḥ suparṇānilasevitaḥ || 23 ||
daityalokamahāgrāho rakṣoragajhaṣākulaḥ
pitāmahamahādhairyaḥ svargastrīratnabhūṣitaḥ || 24 ||
dhīśrīhrīkāntibhirnityaṃ nadībhirupaśobhitaḥ
kālayogamahāparvaprayāgagativegavān || 25 ||
tvaṃ svayogamahāvīryo nārāyaṇa mahārṇavaḥ
kālo bhūtvā prasannābhiradbhirhlādayase punaḥ || 26 ||
tvayā sṛṣṭāstrayo lokāstvayaiva pratisaṃhṛtāḥ
viśanti yoginaḥ sarve tvāmeva pratiyojitāḥ || 27 ||
yuge yuge yugāntāgniḥ kālamegho yuge yuge
mama bhārāvatārāya deva tvaṃ hi yuge yuge || 28 ||
tvaṃ hi śuklaḥ kṛtayuge tretāyāṃ campakaprabhaḥ
dvāpare raktasaṃkāśaḥ kṛṣṇaḥ kaliyuge bhavān || 29 ||
vaivarṇyamabhidhatse tvaṃ prāpteṣu yugasaṃdhiṣu
vaivarṇyaṃ sarvadharmāṇāmutpādayasi vedavit || 30 ||
bhāsi vāsi pratapasi tvaṃ ca pāsi viceṣṭase
krudhyasi kṣāntimāyāsi tvaṃ dīpayasi varṣasi || 31 ||
tvaṃ hāsyasi na niryāsi nirvāpayasi jāgrasi
niḥśeṣayasi bhūtāni kālo bhūtvā yugakṣaye || 32 ||
śeṣamātmānamālokya viśeṣayasi tvaṃ punaḥ
yugāntāgnyavalīḍheṣu sarvabhūteṣu kiṃcana || 33 ||
yāteṣu śeṣo bhavasi tasmāccheṣo'si kīrtitaḥ
cyavanotpattiyukteṣu brahmendra varuṇādiṣu || 34 ||
yasmānna cyavase sthānāttasmātsaṃkīrtyase'cyutaḥ
brahmāṇamindraṃ ca yamaṃ rudraṃ varuṇameva ca || 35 ||
nigṛhya harase yasmāttasmāddharirihocyase
saṃtānayasi bhūtāni vapuṣā yaśasā śriyā || 36 ||
pareṇa vapuṣā deva tasmāccāsi sanātanaḥ
yasmādbrahmādayo devā munayaścogratejasaḥ || 37 ||
na te'ntaṃ tvadhigacchanti tenānantastvamucyase
na kṣīyase na kṣarase kalpakoṭiśatairapi || 38 ||
tasmāttvamakṣaratvācca akṣaraśca prakīrtitaḥ
viṣṭabdhaṃ yattvayā sarvaṃ jagatsthāvarajaṅgamam || 39 ||
jagadviṣṭambhanāccaiva viṣṇureveti kīrtyase
viṣṭabhya tiṣṭhase nityaṃ trailokyaṃ sacāracaram || 40 ||
yakṣagandharvanagaraṃ sumahadbhūtapannagam
vyāptaṃ tvayaiva viśatā trailokyaṃ sacarācaram || 41 ||
tasmādviṣṇuriti proktaḥ svayameva svayaṃbhuvā
nārā ityucyate hyāpo hyṛṣibhistattvadarśibhiḥ || 42 ||
ayanaṃ tasya tāḥ pūrvaṃ tena nārāyaṇaḥ smṛtaḥ
yuge yuge pranaṣṭāṃ gāṃ viṣṇo vindasi tattvataḥ || 43 ||
govindeti tato nāmnā procyase ṛṣibhistathā
hṛṣīkāṇīndri yāṇyāhustattvajñānaviśāradāḥ || 44 ||
īśitā ca tvameteṣāṃ hṛṣīkeśastathocyase
vasanti tvayi bhūtāni brahmādīni yugakṣaye || 45 ||
tvaṃ vā vasasi bhūteṣu vāsudevastathocyase
saṃkarṣayasi bhūtāni kalpe kalpe punaḥ punaḥ || 46 ||
tataḥ saṃkarṣaṇaḥ proktastattvajñānaviśāradaiḥ
prativyūhena tiṣṭhanti sadevāsurarākṣasāḥ || 47 ||
pravidyuḥ sarvadharmāṇāṃ pradyumnastena cocyase
niroddhā vidyate yasmānna te bhūteṣu kaścana || 48 ||
aniruddhastataḥ proktaḥ pūrvameva maharṣibhiḥ
yattvayā dhāryate viśvaṃ tvayā saṃhriyate jagat || 49 ||
tvaṃ dhārayasi bhūtāni bhuvanaṃ tvaṃ cibharṣi ca
yattvayā dhāryate kiṃcittejasā ca balena ca || 50 ||
mayā hi dhāryate paścānnādhṛtaṃ dhāraye tvayā
na hi tadvidyate bhūtaṃ tvayā yannātra dhāryate || 51 ||
tvameva kuruṣe deva nārāyaṇa yuge yuge
mahābhārāvataraṇaṃ jagato hitakāmyayā || 52 ||
tavaiva tejasā''krāntāṃ rasātalatalaṃ gatām
trāyasva māṃ suraśreṣṭha tvāmeva śaraṇaṃ gatām || 53 ||
dānavaiḥ pīḍyamānā'haṃ rākṣasaiśca durātmabhiḥ
tvāmeva śaraṇaṃ nityamupayāmi sanātanam || 54 ||
tāvanme'sti bhayaṃ deva yāvanna tvāṃ kakudminam
śaraṇaṃ yāmi manasā śataśo'pyupalakṣaye || 55 ||
upamānaṃ na te śaktāḥ kartuṃ sendrā divaukasaḥ
tattvaṃ tvameva tadvetsi niruttaramataḥ param || 56 ||
śaunaka uvāca
tataḥ prītaḥ sa bhagavānpṛthivyai śārṅgacakradhṛk
kāmamasyā yathākāmamabhipūritavānhariḥ || 57 ||
abravīcca mahādevi mādhavīyaṃ stavottamam
dhārayiṣyati yo martyo nāsti tasya parābhavaḥ || 58 ||
lokānniṣkalmaṣāṃścaiva vaiṣṇavānpratipatsyate
etadāścaryasarvasvaṃ mādhavīyaṃ stavottamam || 59 ||
adhītavedaḥ puruṣo muniḥ prītamanā bhavet || 60 ||
mā bhairdharaṇi kalyāṇi śāntiṃ vraja mamāgrataḥ
eṣa tvāmucitaṃ sthānaṃ prāpayāmi manīṣitam || 61 ||
śaunaka uvāca
tato mahātmā manasā divyaṃ rūpamacintayat
kiṃ nu rūpamahaṃ kṛtvā uddhareyaṃ dharāmimām || 62 ||
jalakrāḍārucistasmādvārāhaṃ vapurāsthitaḥ
adhṛṣyaṃ sarvabhūtānāṃ vāṅmayaṃ brahma saṃsthitam || 63 ||
śatayojanavistīrṇamucchritaṃ dviguṇaṃ tataḥ
nīlajīmūtasaṃkāśaṃ meghastanitaniḥsvanam || 64 ||
girisaṃhananaṃ bhīmaṃ śvetatīkṣṇāgradaṃṣṭriṇam
vidyudagnipratīkāśamādityasamatejasam
pīnavṛttāyataskandhaṃ dṛptaśārdūlagāminam || 65 ||
pīnonnatakaṭīdeśaṃ vṛṣalakṣaṇapūjitam
rūpamāsthāya vipulaṃ vārāhamajito hariḥ || 66 ||
pṛthivyuddharaṇāyaiva praviveśa rasātalam
vedapādo yūpadaṃṣṭraḥ kratudantaścitīmukhaḥ || 67 ||
agnijihvo darbhalomā brahmaśīrṣo mahātapāḥ
ahorātrekṣaṇadharo vedāṅgaśrutibhūṣaṇaḥ || 68 ||
ājyanāsaḥ sruvatuṇḍaḥ sāmaghoṣasvano mahān
satyadharmamayaḥ śrīmānkarmavikramasatkṛtaḥ || 69 ||
prāyaścittanakho ghoraḥ paśujānurmakhākṛtiḥ
udgīthahomaliṅgo'tha bījauṣadhimahāphalaḥ || 70 ||
vāyvantarātmā yajñāsthivikṛtiḥ somaśoṇitaḥ
vedaskandho havirgandho havyakavyavibhāgavān || 71 ||
prāgvaṃśakāyo dyutimānnānādīkṣābhiranvitaḥ
dakṣiṇāhṛdayo yogī mahāsatramayo mahān || 72 ||
upākarmoṣṭharucakaḥ pravargyāvartabhūṣaṇaḥ
nānāchandogatipatho guhyopaniṣadāsanaḥ
chāyāpatnīsahāyo vai maṇiśṛṅga ivocchritaḥ || 73 ||
rasātalatale magnāṃ rasātalatalaṃ gatām
prabhurlokahitārthāya daṃṣṭrāgreṇojjahāra tām || 74 ||
tataḥ svasthānamānīya varāhaḥ pṛthivīdharaḥ
mumoca pūrvaṃ manasā dhāritāṃ ca vasuṃdharām || 75 ||
tato jagāma nirvāṇaṃ medinī tasya dhāraṇāt
cakāra ca namaskāraṃ tasmai devāya śaṃbhave || 76 ||
evaṃ yajñavarāheṇa bhūtvā bhūtahitārthinā
uddhṛtā pṛthivī devī sāgarāmbugatā purā || 77 ||
athoddhṛtya kṣitiṃ devo jagataḥ sthāpanecchayā
pṛthivīpravibhāgāya manaścakre'mbujekṣaṇaḥ || 78 ||
rasāṃ gatāmevamacintyavikramaḥ surottamaḥ pravaravarāharūpadhṛk
vṛṣākapiḥ prasabhamathaikadaṃṣṭrayā samuddharaddharaṇimatulyapauruṣaḥ || 79 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe varāhaprādurbhāvo nānāṣṭacatvāriṃśadadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 248 ||

<249>

athaikonapañcāśadadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
ṛṣaya ūcuḥ
nārāyaṇasya māhātmyaṃ śrutvā sūta yathākramam
na tṛptirjāyate'smākamataḥ punarihocyatām || 1 ||
kathaṃ devā gatāḥ pūrvamamaratvaṃ vicakṣaṇāḥ
tapasā karmaṇā vā'pi prasādātkasya tejasā || 2 ||
sūta uvāca
yatra nārāyaṇo devo mahādevaśca śūladhṛk
tatrāmaratve sarveṣāṃ sahāyau tatra tau smṛtau || 3 ||
purā devāsure yuddhe hatāśca śataśaḥ suraiḥ
punaḥ saṃjīvanīṃ vidyāṃ prayojya bhṛgunandanaḥ || 4 ||
jīvāpayati daityendrā nyathā suptotthitāniva
tasya tuṣṭena devena śaṃkareṇa mahātmanā || 5 ||
mṛtasaṃjīvanī nāma vidyā dattā mahāprabhā
tāṃ tu māheśvarīṃ vidyāṃ maheśvaramukhodgatām || 6 ||
bhārgave saṃsthitāṃ dṛṣṭvā mumuduḥ sarvadānavāḥ
tato'maratvaṃ daityānāṃ kṛtaṃ śukreṇa dhīmatā || 7 ||
yā nāsti sarvalokānāṃ devānāṃ yakṣarakṣasām
na nāgānāmṛṣīṇāṃ ca na ca brahmendra viṣṇuṣu || 8 ||
tāṃ labdhvā śaṃkarācchukraḥ parāṃ nirvṛtimāgataḥ
tato daivāsuro ghoraḥ samaraḥ sumahānabhūt || 9 ||
tatra devairhatāndaityāñchukro vidyābalena ca
utthāpayati daityendrān līlayaiva vicakṣaṇaḥ || 10 ||
evaṃvidhena śukrastu bṛhaspatirudāradhīḥ
hanyamānāstato devāḥ śataśo'tha sahasraśaḥ || 11 ||
viṣaṇṇavadanāḥ sarve babhūvurvikalendri yāḥ
tatasteṣu viṣaṇṇeṣu bhagavānkamalodbhavaḥ
merupṛṣṭhe surendrā ṇāmidamāha jagatpatiḥ || 12 ||
brahmovāca
devāḥ śṛṇuta madvākyaṃ tattathaiva nirūpyatām
kriyatāṃ dānavaiḥ sārdhaṃ sakhyamatrābhidhāyatīm || 13 ||
kriyatāmamṛtodyogo mathyatāṃ kṣīravāridhiḥ
sahāyaṃ varuṇaṃ kṛtvā cakrapāṇirvibodhyatām || 14 ||
manthānaṃ mandaraṃ kṛtvā śeṣanetreṇa veṭitam
dānavendro baliḥ svāmī stokakālaṃ niveśyatām || 15 ||
prārthyatāṃ kūrmarūpaśca pātāle viṣṇuravyayaḥ
prārthyatāṃ mandaraḥ śailo manthakāryaṃ pravartyatām || 16 ||
tacchrutvā vacanaṃ devā jagmurdānavamandiram
alaṃ virodhena vayaṃ bhṛtyāstava bale'dhunā || 17 ||
kriyatāmamṛtodyogo vriyatāṃ śeṣanetrakam
tvayā cotpādite daitya amṛte'mṛtamanthane || 18 ||
bhaviṣyāmo'marāḥ sarve tvatprasādānna saṃśayaḥ
evamuktastadā devaiḥ parituṣṭaḥ sa dānavaḥ || 19 ||
yathā vadata he devāstathā kāryaṃ mayā'dhunā
śakto'hameka evātra mathituṃ kṣīravāridhim || 20 ||
āhariṣye'mṛtaṃ divyamamṛtatvāya vo'dhunā
sudūrādāśrayaṃ prāptānpraṇatānapi vairiṇaḥ || 21 ||
yo na pūjayate bhaktyā pretya ceha vinaśyati
pālayiṣyāmi vaḥ sarvānadhunā snehamāsthitaḥ || 22 ||
evamuktvā sa daityendro devaiḥ saha yayau tadā
mandaraṃ prārthayāmāsa sahāyatve dharādharam || 23 ||
manthā bhava tvamasmākamadhunā'mṛtamanthane
surāsurāṇāṃ sarveṣāṃ mahatkāryamidaṃ yataḥ || 24 ||
tatheti mandaraḥ prāha yadyādhāro bhavenmama
yatra sthitvā bhramiṣyāmi mathiṣye varuṇālayam || 25 ||
kalpyatāṃ netrakārye yaḥ śaktaḥ syādbhramaṇe mama
tatastu nirgatau devau kūrmaśeṣau mahābalau || 26 ||
viṣṇorbhāgau caturthāṃśāddharaṇyā dhāraṇe sthitau
ūcaturgarvasaṃyuktaṃ vacanaṃ śeṣakacchapau || 27 ||
kūrma uvāca
trailokyadhāraṇenāpi na glānirmama jāyate
kimu mandarakātkṣudrā dguṭikāsaṃnibhādiha || 28 ||
śeṣa uvāca
brahmāṇḍaveṣṭanenāpi brahmāṇḍamathanena vā
na me glānirbhaveddehe kimu mandaravartane || 29 ||
tata utpāṭya taṃ śailaṃ tatkṣaṇātkṣīrasāgare
cikṣepa līlayā nāgaḥ kūrmaścādhaḥsthitastadā || 30 ||
nirādhāraṃ yadā śailaṃ na śekurdevadānavāḥ
mandarabhrāmaṇaṃ kartuṃ kṣīrodamathane tadā || 31 ||
nārāyaṇanivāsaṃ te jagmurbalisamanvitāḥ
yatrā''ste devadeveśaḥ svayameva janārdanaḥ || 32 ||
tatrāpaśyanta taṃ devaṃ sitapadmaprabhaṃ śubham
yoganidrā sunirataṃ pītavāsasamacyutam || 33 ||
hārakeyūranaddhāṅgamahiparyaṅkasaṃsthitam
pādapadmena padmāyāḥ spṛśantaṃ nābhimaṇḍalam || 34 ||
svapakṣavyajanenātha vījyamānaṃ garutmatā
stūyamānaṃ samantācca siddhacāraṇakiṃnaraiḥ || 35 ||
āmnāyairmūtimadbhiśca stūyamānaṃ samantataḥ
savyabāhūpadhānaṃ taṃ tuṣṭuvurdevadānavāḥ
kṛtāñjalipuṭāḥ sarve praṇatāḥ sarvatodiśam || 36 ||
devadānavā ūcuḥ
namo lokatrayādhyakṣa tejasā jitabhāskara
namo viṣṇo namo jiṣṇo namaste kaiṭabhārdana || 37 ||
namaḥ sargakriyākartre jagatyālayate namaḥ
rudra rūpāya śarvāya namaḥ saṃhārakāriṇe || 38 ||
namaḥ śūlāyudhādṛṣya namo dānavaghātine
namaḥ kramatrayākrānta trailokyāyābhavāya ca || 39 ||
namaḥ pracaṇḍadaityendra kulakālamahānala
namo nābhihradodbhūtapadmagarbha mahābala || 40 ||
padmabhūta mahābhūta kartre hartre jagatpriya
janitā sarvalokeśa kriyākāraṇakāriṇe || 41 ||
amarārivināśāya mahāsamaraśāline
lakṣmīmukhābjamadhupa namaḥ kīrtinivāsine || 42 ||
asmākamamaratvāya dhriyatāṃ dhriyatāmayam
mandaraḥ sarvaśailānāmayutāyutavistṛtaḥ || 43 ||
anantabalabāhubhyāmavaṣṭhabhyaikapāṇinā
mathyatāmamṛtaṃ deva svadhāsvāhārthakāminām || 44 ||
tataḥ śrutvā sa bhagavānstotrapūrvaṃ vacastadā
vihāya yoganidrāṃ tāmuvāca madhusūdanaḥ || 45 ||
śrībhagavānuvāca
svāgataṃ vibudhāḥ sarve kimāgamanakāraṇam
yasmātkāryādiha prāptāstadbrūta vigatajvarāḥ || 46 ||
nārāyaṇenaivamuktāḥ procustatra divaukasaḥ
amaratvāya deveśa mathyamāne mahodadhau || 47 ||
yathā'mṛtatvaṃ deveśa tathā naḥ kuru mādhava
tvayā vinā na tacchakyamasmābhiḥ kaiṭabhārdana || 48 ||
prāptuṃ tadamṛtaṃ nātha tato'gne bhava no vibho
ityuktaśca tato viṣṇurapradhṛṣyo'rimardanaḥ || 49 ||
jagāma devaiḥ sahito yatrāsau mandarācalaḥ
veṣṭito bhogibhogena dhṛtaścāmaradānavaiḥ || 50 ||
viṣabhītāstato devā yataḥ pucchaṃ tataḥ sthitāḥ
mukhato daityasaṃghāstu saiṃhikeyapuraḥsarāḥ || 51 ||
sahasravadanaṃ cāsya śiraḥ savyena pāṇinā
dakṣiṇena balirdehaṃ nāgasyā''kṛṣṭavāṃstathā || 52 ||
dadhārāmūtamanthānaṃ mandaraṃ cārukaṃdaram
nārāyaṇaḥ sa bhagavānbhujayugmadvayena tu || 53 ||
tato devāsuraiḥ sarvairjayaśabdapuraḥsaram
divyaṃ varṣaśataṃ sāgraṃ mathitaḥ kṣīrasāgaraḥ || 54 ||
tataḥ śrāntāstu te sarve devā daityapuraḥsarāḥ
śrānteṣu teṣu devendro megho bhūtvā'mbuśīkarān || 55 ||
vavarṣāmṛtakalpāṃstānvavau vāyuśca śītalaḥ
bhagnaprāyeṣu deveṣu śānteṣu kamalāsanaḥ || 56 ||
mathyatāṃ mathyatāṃ sindhurityuvāca punaḥ punaḥ
avaśyamudyogavatāṃ śrīrapārā bhavetsadā || 57 ||
brahmaprotsāhitā devā mamanthuḥ punarambudhim
bhrāmyamāṇe tataḥ śaile yojanāyutaśekhare || 58 ||
nipeturhastiyūthāni varāhaśarabhādayaḥ
śvāpadāyutalakṣāṇi tathā puṣpaphaladrumāḥ || 59 ||
tataḥ phalānāṃ vīryeṇa puṣpauṣadhirasena ca
kṣīramambudhijaṃ sarvaṃ dadhirūpamajāyata || 60 ||
tatastu sarvajīveṣu cūrṇiteṣu sahasraśaḥ
tadambumedasotsargādvāruṇī samapadyata || 61 ||
vāruṇīgandhamāghrāya mumudurdevadānavāḥ
tadāsvādena balino devadaityādayo'bhavan || 62 ||
tato'tivegājjagṛhurnāgendraṃ sarvato'surāḥ
manthānaṃ manthayaṣṭistu merustatrācalo'bhavat || 63 ||
abhavaccāgrato viṣṇurbhujamandarabandhanaḥ
savāsukiphaṇālagnapāṇiḥ kṛṣṇo vyarājata || 64 ||
yathā nīlotpalairyukto brahmadaṇḍo'tivistaraḥ
dhvanirmeghasahasrasya jaladherutthitastadā || 65 ||
bhāge dvitīye maghavānādityastu tataḥ param
tato rudrā mahotsāhā vasavo guhyakādayaḥ || 66 ||
purato vipracittiśca namucirvṛtraśambarau
dvimūrdhā vajradaṃṣṭraśca saiṃhikeyo balistathā || 67 ||
ete cānye ca bahavo mukhabhāgamupasthitāḥ
mamanthurambudhiṃ dṛptā balatejovibhūṣitāḥ || 68 ||
babhūvātra mahāghoṣo mahāmegharavopamaḥ
udadhermathyamānasya mandareṇa surāsuraiḥ || 69 ||
tatra nānājalacarā vinirdhūtā mahādri ṇā
vilayaṃ samupājagmuḥ śataśo'tha sahasraśaḥ || 70 ||
vāruṇāni ca bhūtāni vividhāni mahīdharaḥ
pātālatalavāsīni vilayaṃ samupānayat || 71 ||
tasmiṃśca bhrāmyamāṇe'drau saṃghṛṣṭāśca parasparam
nyapatanpatagopetāḥ parvatāgrānmahādrumāḥ || 72 ||
teṣāṃ saṃgharṣaṇāccāgnirarṣibhiḥ prajvalanmuhuḥ
vidyudbhiriva nīlābhramāvṛṇonmandaraṃ girim || 73 ||
dadāha kuñjarāṃścaiva siṃhāṃścaiva viniḥsṛtān
vigatāsūni sarvāṇi sattvāni vividhāni ca || 74 ||
tamagnimamaraśreṣṭhaḥ pradahantamitastataḥ
vāriṇā meghajenendraḥ śamayāmāsa sarvataḥ || 75 ||
tato nānārasāstatra susruvuḥ sāgarāmbhasi
mahādrumāṇāṃ niryāsā bahavaścauṣadhīrasāḥ || 76 ||
teṣāmamṛtavīryāṇāṃ rasānāṃ payasaiva ca
amaratvaṃ surā jagmuḥ kāñcanacchavisaṃnibhāḥ || 77 ||
atha tasya samudra sya tajjātamudakaṃ payaḥ
rasāntarairvimiśraṃ ca tataḥ kṣīrādabhūddhṛtam || 78 ||
tato brahmāṇamāsīnaṃ devā vacanamabruvan
śrāntāḥ sma subhṛśaṃ brahmannodbhavatyamṛtaṃ ca yat || 79 ||
ṛte nārāyaṇātsarve daityā devottamāstathā
cirāyitamidaṃ cāpi sāgarasya tu manthanam || 80 ||
tato nārāyaṇaṃ devaṃ brahmā vacanamabravīt
vidhatsvaiṣāṃ balaṃ viṣṇo bhavāneva parāyaṇam || 81 ||
viṣṇuruvāca
balaṃ dadāmi sarveṣāṃ karmaitadye samāsthitāḥ
kṣobhyatāṃ kramaśaḥ sarvairmandaraḥ parivartyatām || 82 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe'mṛtamanthana ekonapañcāśadadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 249 ||

<250>

atha pañcāśadadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
sūta uvāca
nārāyaṇavacaḥ śrutvā balinaste mahodadhau
tatpayaḥ sahitā bhūtvā cakrire bhṛśamākulam || 1 ||
tataḥ śatasahasrāṃśusamāna iva sāgarāt
prasannābhaḥ samutpannaḥ somaḥ śītāṃśurujjvalaḥ || 2 ||
śrīranantaramutpannā ghṛtātpāṇḍuravāsinī
surādevī samutpannā turagāḥ pāṇḍurastathā || 3 ||
kaustubhaśca maṇirdivyaścotpanno'mṛtasaṃbhavaḥ
marīcivikacaḥ śrīmānnārāyaṇa urogataḥ || 4 ||
pārijātaśca vikacakusumastabakāñcitaḥ
anantaramapaśyaṃste dhūmamambarasaṃnibham || 5 ||
āpūritadiśābhāgaṃ duḥsahaṃ sarvadehinām
tamāghrāya surāḥ sarve mūrchitāḥ parilambitāḥ || 6 ||
upāviśannabdhitaṭe śiraḥ saṃgṛhya pāṇinā
tataḥ krameṇa durvāraḥ so'nalaḥ pratyadṛśyata || 7 ||
jvālāmālākulākāraḥ samantādbhīṣaṇo'rciṣā
tenāgninā parikṣiptāḥ prāyaśastu surāsurāḥ || 8 ||
dagdhāścāpyardhadagdhāśca babhramuḥ sakalā diśaḥ
pradhānā devadaityāśca bhīṣitāstena vahninā || 9 ||
anantaraṃ samudbhūtāstasmāḍḍuṇḍubhajātayaḥ
kṛṣṇasarpā mahādaṃṣṭrā raktāśca pavanāśanāḥ || 10 ||
śvetapītāstathā cānye tathā gonasajātayaḥ
maśakā bhramarā daṃśā makṣikāḥ śalabhāstathā || 11 ||
karṇaśalyāḥ kṛkalāsā aneke caiva babhramuḥ
prāṇino daṃṣṭriṇo raudrā stathā hi viṣajātayaḥ || 12 ||
śārṅgahālāhalāmustavatsakaṃgūrubhasmagāḥ
nīlapatrādayaścānye śataśo bahubhedinaḥ
yeṣāṃ gandhena dahyante giriśṛṅgāṇyapi drutam || 13 ||
anantaraṃ nīlarasaughabhṛṅgabhinnāñjanābhaṃ viṣamaṃ śvasantam
kāyena lokāntarapūrakeṇa keśaiśca vahnipratimairjvaladbhiḥ || 14 ||
suvarṇamuktāphalabhūṣitāṅgaṃ kirīṭinaṃ pītadukūlajuṣṭam
nīlotpalābhaṃ kusumaiḥ kṛtārghaṃ garjantamambhodharabhīmavegam || 15 ||
adrā kṣurambhonidhimadhyasaṃsthaṃ savigrahaṃ dehibhayāśrayaṃ tam
vilokya taṃ bhīṣaṇamugranetraṃ bhūtāśca vitresurathāpi sarve || 16 ||
kecidvilokyaiva gatā hyabhāvaṃ niḥsaṃjñatāṃ cāpyapare prapannāḥ
vemurmukhebhyo'pi ca phenamanye kecittvavāptā viṣamāmavasthām || 17 ||
śvāsena tasya nirdagdhāstato viṣṇvindra dānavāḥ
dagdhāṅgāranibhā jātā ye bhūtā divyarūpiṇaḥ
tatastu saṃbhramādviṣṇustamuvāca surātmakam || 18 ||
śrībhagavānuvāca
ko bhavānantakaprakhyaḥ kimicchasi kuto'pi ca
kiṃ kṛtvā te priyaṃ jāyedevamācakṣva me'khilam || 19 ||
tacca tasya vacaḥ śrutvā viṣṇoḥ kālāgnisaṃnibhaḥ
uvāca kālakūṭastu bhinnaduṃdubhinisvanaḥ || 20 ||
kālakūṭa uvāca
ahaṃ hi kālakūṭākhyo viṣṇo'mbudhisamudbhavaḥ
yadā tīvratarāmarṣaiḥ parasparavadhaiṣibhiḥ || 21 ||
surāsurairvimathito dugdhāmbhonidhiradbhutaḥ
saṃbhūto'haṃ tadā sarvānhantuṃ devānsadānavān || 22 ||
sarvānahaṃ haniṣyāmi kṣaṇamātreṇa dehinaḥ
māṃ vā grasata vai sarve yāta vā giriśāntikam || 23 ||
śrutvaitadvacanaṃ tasya tato bhītāḥ surāsurāḥ
brahmaviṣṇū puraskṛtya gatāste śaṃkarāntikam || 24 ||
niveditāstato dvāḥsthaiste gaṇeśaiḥ surāsurāḥ
anujñātāḥ śivenātha viviśurgiriśāntikam || 25 ||
mandarasya guhāṃ haimīṃ muktāmaṇivibhūṣitām
susvacchamaṇisopānāṃ vaidūryastambhamaṇḍitām || 26 ||
tatra devāsuraiḥ sarvairjānubhirdharaṇiṃ gataiḥ
brahmāṇamagrataḥ kṛtvā idaṃ stotramudāhṛtam || 27 ||
devadānavā ūcuḥ
namastubhyaṃ virūpākṣa namaste divyacakṣuṣe
namaḥ pinākahastāya vajrahastāya dhanvine || 28 ||
namastriśūlahastāya daṇḍahastāya dhūrjaṭe
namastrilokyanāthāya bhūtagrāmaśarīriṇe || 29 ||
namaḥ surārihantre ca somāgnyarkāgryacakṣuṣe
brahmaṇe caiva rudrā ya namaste viṣṇurūpiṇe || 30 ||
brahmaṇe vedarūpāya namaste devarūpiṇe
sāṃkhyayogāya bhūtānāṃ namaste śaṃbhavāya te || 31 ||
manmathāṅgavināśāya namaḥ kālakṣayaṃkara
raṃhase devadevāya namaste vasuretase || 32 ||
ekavīrāya sarvāya namaḥ piṅgakapardine
umābhartre namastubhyaṃ yajñatripuraghātine || 33 ||
śuddhabodhaprabuddhāya muktakaivalyarūpiṇe
lokatrayavidhātre ca varuṇendrā gnirūpiṇe || 34 ||
ṛgyajuḥsāmavedāya puruṣāyeśvarāya ca
agryāya caiva cogrāya viprāya śruticakṣuṣe || 35 ||
rajase caiva sattvāya tamase timirātmane
anityanityabhāvāya namo nityacarātmane || 36 ||
vyaktāya caivāvyaktāya vyaktāvyaktāya vai namaḥ
bhaktānāmārtināśāya priyanārāyaṇāya ca || 37 ||
umāpriyāya śarvāya nandivaktrāñcitāya ca
ṛtumanvantakalpāya pakṣamāsadinātmane || 38 ||
nānārūpāya muṇḍāya varūthapṛthudaṇḍine
namaḥ kapālahastāya digvāsāya śikhaṇḍine || 39 ||
dhanvine rathine caiva yataye brahmacāriṇe
ityevamādicaritaiḥ stuta tubhyaṃ namo namaḥ || 40 ||
evaṃ surāsuraiḥ sthāṇuḥ stutastoṣamupāgataḥ
uvāca vākyaṃ bhītānāṃ smitānvitaśumākṣaram || 41 ||
śrīśaṃkara uvāca
kimarthamāgatā brūta trāsamlānamukhāmbujāḥ
kiṃ vā'bhīṣṭaṃ dadāmyadya kāmaṃ prabrūta mā ciram
ityuktāste tu devena procustaṃ sasurāsurāḥ || 42 ||
surāsurā ūcuḥ
amṛtārthe mahādeva mathyamāne mahodadhau
viṣamadbhutamudbhūtaṃ lokasaṃkṣayakārakam || 43 ||
sa uvācātha sarveṣāṃ devānāṃ bhayakārakaḥ
sarvānvo bhakṣayiṣyāmi athavā mā pibantvatha || 44 ||
tamaśaktā vayaṃ grastuṃ so'smāñchakto balotkaṭaḥ
eṣa niḥśvāsamātreṇa śataparvasamadyutiḥ || 45 ||
viṣṇuḥ kṛṣṇaḥ kṛtastena yamaśca viṣamātmavān
mūrchitāḥ patitāścānye vipraṇāśaṃ gatāḥ pare || 46 ||
artho'narthakriyāṃ yāti durbhagāṇāṃ yathā vibho
durbalānāṃ ca saṃkalpo yathā bhavati cā'padi || 47 ||
viṣametatsamudbhūtaṃ tasmādvā'mṛtakāṅkṣayā
asmādbhayānmocaya tvaṃ gatistvaṃ ca parāyaṇam || 48 ||
bhaktānukampī bhāvajño bhuvanādīśvaro vibhuḥ
yajñāgramuksarvahaviḥ saumyaḥ somaḥ smarāntakṛt || 49 ||
tvameko no gatirdeva gīrvāṇagaṇaśarmakṛt
rakṣāsmānmakṣasaṃkalpādvirūpākṣa viṣajvarāt || 50 ||
tacchrutvā bhagavānāha bhaganetrāntakṛdbhavaḥ || 51 ||
devadeva uvāca
bhakṣayiṣyāmyahaṃ ghoraṃ kālakūṭaṃ mahāviṣam
tathā'nyadapi yatkṛtyaṃ kṛcchrasādhyaṃ surāsurāḥ
taccāpi sādhayiṣyāmi tiṣṭhadhvaṃ vigatajvarāḥ || 52 ||
ityuktā hṛṣṭaromāṇo bāṣpagadgadakaṇṭhinaḥ
ānandāśruparītākṣāḥ sanāthā iva menire
surā brahmādayaḥ sarve samāśvastāḥ sumānasāḥ || 53 ||
tato'vrajaddrutagatinā kakudminā haro'mbare pavanagatirjagatpatiḥ ।
pradhāvitairasurasurendra nāyakaiḥ svavāhanairyicalitaśubhracāmaraiḥ ॥
puraḥsaraiḥ sa tu śuśubhe śubhāśrayaiḥ śivo vaśī śikhikapiśordhvajūṭakaḥ || 54 ||
āsādya dugdhasindhuṃ taṃ kālakūṭaṃ viṣaṃ yataḥ
tato devo mahādevo vilokya viṣamaṃ viṣam || 55 ||
chāyāsthānakamāsthāya so'pibadvāmapāṇinā
pīyamāne viṣe tasmiṃstato devā mahāsurāḥ || 56 ||
jaguśca nanṛtuścāpi siṃhanādāṃśca puṣkalān
cakruḥ śakramukhādyāśca hiraṇyākṣādayastathā || 57 ||
stuvantaścaiva deveśaṃ prasannāścābhavaṃstadā
kaṇṭhadeśaṃ tataḥ prāpte viṣe devamathā'bruvan || 58 ||
viriñcipramukhā devā balipramukhato'surāḥ
śobhate deva kaṇṭhaste gātre kundanibhaprabhe || 59 ||
bhṛṅgamālānibhaṃ kaṇṭhe'pyatraivāstu viṣaṃ tava
ityuktaḥ śaṃkaro devastathā prāha purāntakṛt || 60 ||
pīte viṣe devagaṇānvimucya gato haro mandaraśailameva
tasmingate devagaṇāḥ punastaṃ mamanthurabdhiṃ vividhaprakāraiḥ || 61 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe'mṛtamanthane kālakūṭotpattirnāma pañcāśadadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 250 ||

<251>

athaikapañcāśadadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
sūta uvāca
madhyamāne punastasmiñjaladhau samadṛśyata
dhanvantariḥ sa bhagavānāyurvedaprajāpatiḥ || 1 ||
madirā cā''yatākṣī sā lokacittapramāthinī
tato'mṛtaṃ ca surabhiḥ sarvabhūtabhayāpahā || 2 ||
jagrāha kamalāṃ viṣṇuḥ kaustubhaṃ ca mahāmaṇim
gajendraṃ ca sahasrākṣo hayaratnaṃ ca bhāskaraḥ || 3 ||
dhanvantariṃ ca jagrāha lokārogyapravartakam
chatraṃ jagrāha varuṇaḥ kuṇḍale ca śacīpatiḥ || 4 ||
pārijātataruṃ vāyurjagrāha muditastathā
dhanvantaristato devo vapuṣmānudatiṣṭhata || 5 ||
śvetaṃ kamaṇḍaluṃ bibhradamṛtaṃ yatra tiṣṭhati
etadatyadbhutaṃ dṛṣṭvā dānavānāṃ samutthitaḥ || 6 ||
amṛtārthe mahānādo mamedamiti jalpatām
tato nārāyaṇo māyāmāsthito mohinīṃ prabhuḥ || 7 ||
strīrūpamatulaṃ kṛtvā dānavānabhisaṃsṛtaḥ
tatastadamṛtaṃ tasyai daduste mūḍhacetanāḥ
striyai dānavadaiteyāḥ sarve tadgatamānasāḥ || 8 ||
athāstrāṇi ca mukhyāni mahāpraharaṇāni ca
pragṛhyābhyadra vandevānsahitā daityadānavāḥ || 9 ||
tatastadamṛtaṃ devo viṣṇurādāya vīryavān
jahāra dānavendre bhyo nareṇa sahitaḥ prabhuḥ || 10 ||
tato devagaṇāḥ sarve papustadamṛtaṃ tadā
viṣṇoḥ sakāśātsaṃprāpya saṅgrāme tumule sati || 11 ||
tataḥ pibatsu tatkālaṃ deveṣvamṛtamīpsitam
rāhurvibudharūpeṇa dānavo'pyapibattadā || 12 ||
tasya kaṇṭhamanuprāpte dānavasyāmṛte tadā
ākhyātaṃ candra sūryābhyāṃ surāṇāṃ hitakāmyayā || 13 ||
tato bhagavatā tasya śiraśchinnamalaṃkṛtam
cakrāyudhena cakreṇa pibato'mṛtamojasā || 14 ||
tacchailaśṛṅgapratimaṃ dānavasya śiro mahat
cakreṇotkṛttamapataccālayadvasudhātalam || 15 ||
tato vairavinirbandhaḥ kṛto rāhumukhena vai
śāśvataścandra sūryābhyāṃ prasahyādyāpi bādhate || 16 ||
vihāya bhagavāṃścāpi strīrūpamatulaṃ hariḥ
nānāpraharaṇairbhīmairdānavānsamakampayat
prāsāḥ suvipulāstīkṣṇāḥ patantaśca sahasraśaḥ || 17 ||
te'surāścakranirbhinnā vamanto rudhiraṃ bahu
asiśaktigadābhinnā nipeturdharaṇītale || 18 ||
bhinnāni paṭṭiśaiścāpi śirāṃsi yudhi dāruṇaiḥ
taptakāñcanamālyāni nipeturaniśaṃ tadā || 19 ||
rudhireṇāvaliptāṅgā nihatāśca mahāsurāḥ
adri ṇāmiva kūṭāni dhāturaktāni śerate || 20 ||
tato halahalāśabdaḥ saṃbabhūva samantataḥ
anyonyaṃ chindatāṃ śastrairāditye lohitāyati || 21 ||
parighaiścā''yasaiḥ pātaiḥ saṃnikarṣaiśca muṣṭibhiḥ
nighnatāṃ samare'nyonyaṃ śabdo divamivāspṛśat || 22 ||
chindi bhindhi pradhāveti pātayābhisareti vai
viśrūyante mahāghorāḥ śabdāstatra samantataḥ || 23 ||
evaṃ sutumule yuddhe vartamāne mahābhaye
naranārāyaṇau devau samājagmaturāhavam || 24 ||
tatra divyaṃ dhanurdṛṣṭvā narasya bhagavānapi
cintayāmāsa vai cakraṃ viṣṇurdānavasūdanaḥ || 25 ||
tato'mbarāccintitamātramāgataṃ mahāprabhaṃ cakramamitranāśanam
vibhāvasostulyamakuṇṭhamaṇḍalaṃ sudarśanaṃ bhīmamasahyavikramam || 26 ||
tadāgataṃ jvalitahutāśanaprabhaṃ bhayaṃkaraṃ karikarabāhuracyutaḥ
mahāprabhaṃ danukuladaityadāraṇaṃ tathojjvalajjvalanasamānavigraham || 27 ||
mumoca vai tadatulamugravegavānmahāprabhaṃ ripunagarāvadāraṇam
saṃvartakajvalanasamānavarcasaṃ punaḥpunarnyapatata vegavattadā || 28 ||
vyadārayaddititanayānsahasraśaḥ kareritaṃ puruṣavareṇa saṃyuge
dahatkvacijjvalana ivānileritaḥ prasahya tānasuragaṇāna kṛntata || 29 ||
tatpreritaṃ viyati muhuḥ kṣitau tadā papau raṇe rudhiramayo piśācavat
athāsurā giribhiradīnamānasā muhurmuhuḥ suragaṇamardayaṃstadā || 30 ||
mahābalā vigalitameghavarcasaḥ sahasraśo gaganamahāprapātinaḥ
athāsurā bhayajananāḥ prapedire sapādapā bahuvidhamegharūpiṇaḥ || 31 ||
mahādra yaḥ pavigalitāgrasānavaḥ parasparaṃ drutamabhipatya sasvarāḥ
tato mahī pracalitasādri kānanā tadā'dri pātābhihatā samantataḥ
parasparaṃ samabhinigarjatāṃ muhū raṇājire bhṛśamabhisaṃpravartite
narastato varakanakāgrabhūṣaṇairmaheṣubhiḥ pavanapathaṃ samāvṛṇot || 33 ||
vidārayangiriśikharāṇi patribhirmahābhaye suragaṇavigrahe tadā
tato mahīṃ lavaṇajalaṃ ca sāgaraṃ mahāsurāḥ praviviśurarditāḥ suraiḥ || 34 ||
viyadgataṃ jvalitahutāśanaprabhaṃ sudarśanaṃ parikupitaṃ niśamya ca
tataḥ surairvijayamavāpya mandaraḥ svameva deśaṃ gamitaḥ supūjitaḥ || 35 ||
vitatya svaṃ divamatha caiva sarvaśastato gatāḥ saliladharā yathāgatam
tato'mṛtaṃ sunihitameva cakrire surāḥ parāṃ mudamabhigamya puṣphalām
daduśca taṃ nidhimamṛtasya rakṣituṃ kirīṭine balibhirathāmaraiḥ saha || 36 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe'mṛtamanthanaṃ nāmaikapañcāśadadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 251 ||

<252>

atha dvipañcāśadadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
ṛṣaya ūcuḥ
prāsādabhavanādīnāṃ niveśaṃ vistarādvada
kuryātkena vidhānena kaśca vāsturudāhṛtaḥ || 1 ||
sūta uvāca
bhṛguratrirvasiṣṭhaśca viśvakarmā mayastathā
nārado nagnajiccaiva viśālākṣaḥ puraṃdaraḥ || 2 ||
brahmā kumāro nandīśaḥ śaunako garga eva ca
vāsudevo'niruddhaśca tathā śakrubṛhaspatī || 3 ||
aṣṭādaśaite vikhyātā vāstuśāstropadeśakāḥ
saṃkṣepeṇopadiṣṭaṃ yanmanave matsyarūpiṇā || 4 ||
tadidānīṃ pravakṣyāmi vāstuśāstramanuttamam

purāndhakavadhe ghore ghorarūpasya śūlinaḥ || 5lalāṭasvedasalilamapatadbhuvi bhīṣaṇam
karālavadanaṃ tasmādbhūtamudbhūtamulbaṇam || 6 ||
grasamānamivā''kāśaṃ saptadvīpāṃ vasuṃdharām
tato'ndhakānāṃ rudhiramapibatpatitaṃ kṣitau || 7 ||
tena tatsamare sarvaṃ patitaṃ yanmahītale
tathā'pi tṛptimagamanna tadbhūtaṃ yadā tadā || 8 ||
sadāśivasya puratastapaścakre sudāruṇam
kṣudhāviṣṭaṃ tu tadbhūtamāhartuṃ jagatītrayam || 9 ||
tataḥ kālena saṃtuṣṭo bhairavastasya cā''ha vai
varaṃ vṛṣṇīṣva bhadraṃ te yadabhīṣṭaṃ tavānagha || 10 ||
tamuvāca tato bhūtaṃ trailokyagrasanakṣamam
maveyaṃ devadeveśa tathetyuktaṃ ca śūlinā || 11 ||
tatastattridivaṃ sarvaṃ bhūmaṇḍalamaśeṣataḥ
svadehenāntarikṣaṃ ca rundhānaṃ prapatadbhuvi || 12 ||
bhītabhītaistato devairbrahmaṇā cātha śūlinā
dānavāsurarakṣobhiravaṣṭabdhaṃ samantataḥ || 13 ||
yena yatraiva cā''krāntaṃ sa tatraivāvasatpunaḥ
nivāsātsarvadevānāṃ vāsturityabhidhīyate || 14 ||
avaṣṭabdhena tenāpi vijñaptāḥ sarvadevatāḥ
prasīdadhvaṃ surāḥ sarve yuṣmābhirnicalīkṛtaḥ || 15 ||
sthāsyāmyahaṃ kimākāro hyavaṣṭabdho hyadhomukhaḥ
tato brahmādibhiḥ proktaṃ vāstumadhye tu yo baliḥ || 16 ||
āhāro vaiśvadevānte nūnamasya bhaviṣyati
vāstūpaśamano yajñastavā''hāro bhaviṣyati || 17 ||
yajñotsavādau ca balistavā''hāro bhaviṣyati
vāstupūjāmakurvāṇastavā''hāro bhaviṣyati || 18 ||
ajñānāttu kṛto yajñaastavā''hāro bhaviṣyati
evamuktastato hṛṣṭaḥ sa vāsturabhavattadā
vāstuyajñaḥ smṛtastasmāttataḥ prabhṛti śāntaye || 19 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe vāstubhūtodbhavo nāma dvipañcāśadadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 252 ||

<253>

atha tripañcāśadadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
sūta uvāca
athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi gṛhakālavinirṇayam
yathā kālaṃ śubhaṃ jñātvā sadā bhavanamārabhet || 1 ||
caitre vyādhimavāpnoti yo gṛhaṃ kārayennaraḥ
vaiśākhe dhenuratnāni jyeṣṭhe mṛtyuṃ tathaiva ca || 2 ||
āṣāḍhe bhṛtyaratnāni paśuvargamavāpnuyāt
śrāvaṇe bhṛtyalābhaṃ tu hāniṃ bhādra pade tathā || 3 ||
patnīnāśaścā''śvayuje kārtike dhanadhānyakam
mārgaśīrṣe tathā bhaktaṃ pauṣe taskarato mayam || 4 ||
lābhaṃ ca bahuśo vindyādagniṃ māghe vinirdiśet
phālgune kāñcanaṃ putrāniti kālabalaṃ smṛtam || 5 ||
aśvinī rohiṇī mūlamuttarātrayamaindavam
svātī hasto'nurādhā ca gṛhārambhe praśasyate || 6 ||
ādityabhaumavarjaṃ tu sarve vārāḥ śubhāvahāḥ
varjaṃ vyāghātaśūle ca vyatīpātātigaṇḍayoḥ || 7 ||
viṣkambhagaṇḍaparighavajrayogeṣu na kārayet
svātau maitre'tha māhendre gāndharvābhijiti rauhiṇe || 8 ||
tathā vairājasāvitre muhūrte gṛhamārabhet
candrā dityabalaṃ labdhvā śubhalagnaṃ nirīkṣayet || 9 ||
stambhocchrāyādi kartavyamanyattu parivarjayet
prāsādeṣvevamevaṃ syātkūpavāpīṣu caiva hi || 10 ||
pūrvaṃ bhūmiṃ parīkṣeta paścādvāstuṃ prakalpayet
śvetā raktā tathā pītā kṛṣṇā caivānupūrvaśaḥ || 11 ||
viprādeḥ śasyate bhūmirataḥ kāryaṃ parīkṣaṇam
viprāṇāṃ madhurāsvādā kaṭukā kṣattriyasya tu || 12 ||
tiktā kaṣāyā ca tathā vaiśyaśūdre ṣu śasyate
aratnimātre vai garte svanulipte ca sarvaśaḥ || 13 ||
ghṛtamāmaśarāvasthaṃ kṛtvā varticatuṣṭayam
jvālayedbhūparīkṣārthaṃ tatpūrṇaṃ sarvadiṅmukham || 14 ||
dīptau pūrvādi gṛhṇīyādvarṇānāmanupūrvaśaḥ
vāstuḥ sāmūhiko nāma dīpyate sarvatastu yaḥ || 15 ||
śubhadaḥ sarvavarṇānāṃ prāsādeṣu gṛheṣu ca
ratnimātramadhogarte parīkṣyaṃ khātapūraṇe || 16 ||
adhike śriyamāpnoti nyūne hāniṃ same samam
phālakṛṣṭe'thavā deśe sarvabījāni vāpayet || 17 ||
tripañcasaptarātre ca yatrā''rohanti tānyapi
jyeṣṭhottamā kaniṣṭhā bhūrvarjanīyatarā sadā || 18 ||
pañcagavyauṣadhijalaiḥ parīkṣitvā ca secayet
ekāśītipadaṃ kṛtvā rekhābhiḥ kanakena ca || 19 ||
paścātpiṣṭena cā''likhya sūtreṇā''loḍya sarvataḥ
daśa pūrvāyatā lekhā daśa caivottarāyatāḥ || 20 ||
sarvavāstuvibhāgeṣu vijñeyā navakā nava
ekāśītipadaṃ kṛtvā vāsuvitsarvavāstuṣu || 21 ||
padasthānpūjayeddevāṃstriṃśatpañcadaśaiva tu
dvātriṃśadbāhyataḥ pūjyāḥ pūjyāścāntastrayodaśa || 22 ||
nāmatastānpravakṣyāmi sthānāni ca nibodhata
īśānakoṇādiṣu tānpūjayeddhaviṣā naraḥ || 23 ||
śikhī caivātha parjanyo jayantaḥ kuliśāyudhaḥ
sūryaḥ satyo bhṛśaścaiva ākāśo vāyureva ca || 24 ||
pūṣā ca vitathaścaiva gṛhakṣatayamāvubhau
gandharvau bhṛṅgarājaśca mṛgaḥ pitṛgaṇastathā || 25 ||
dauvāriko'tha sugrīvaḥ puṣpadanto jalādhipaḥ
asuraḥ śoṣapāpau ca rogo'himukhya eva ca || 26 ||
bhallāṭaḥ somasarpau ca aditiśca ditistathā
bahirdvātriṃśadete tu tadantastu tataḥ śṛṇu || 27 ||
īśānādicatuṣkoṇasaṃsthitānpūjayedbudhaḥ
āpaścaivātha sāvitro jayo rudra stathaiva ca || 28 ||
madhye navapade brahmā tasyāṣṭau ca samīpagān
sādhyānekāntarānvidyātpūrvādyānnāmataḥ śṛṇu || 29 ||
aryamā savitā caiva vivasvānvibudhādhipaḥ
mitro'tha rājayakṣmā ca tathā pṛthvīdharaḥ kramāt || 30 ||
aṣṭamaścā''pavatsastu parito brahmaṇaḥ smṛtāḥ
āpaścaivā''pavatsaśca parjanyo'gnirditistathā || 31 ||
padikānāṃ tu vargo'yamevaṃ koṇeṣvaśeṣataḥ
tanmadhye tu bahiviśaddvipadāste tu sarvaśaḥ || 32 ||
aryamā ca vivasvāṃśca mitraḥ pṛthvīdharastathā
brahmaṇaḥ parito dikṣu tripadāste tu sarvaśaḥ || 33 ||
vaṃśānidānīṃ vakṣyāmi ṛjūnapi pṛthakpṛthak
vāyuṃ yāvattathā rogātpitṛbhyaḥ śikhinaṃ punaḥ || 34 ||
mukhyādbhṛśaṃ tathā śeṣādvitathaṃ yāvadeva tu
sugrīvādaditiṃ yāvanmṛgātparjanyameva ca || 35 ||
ete vaṃśāḥ samākhyātāḥ kvacicca jayameva tu
eteṣāṃ yastu saṃpātaḥ padaṃ madhyaṃ samaṃ tathā || 36 ||
marma caitatsamākhyātaṃ triśūlaṃ koṇagaṃ ca yat
stambhaṃ nyāseṣu varjyāni tulāvidhiṣu sarvadā || 37 ||
kīlocchiṣṭopaghātādi varjayedyatnato janaḥ
sarvatra vāstunirdiṣṭo pitṛvaiśvānarāyataḥ || 38 ||
mūrdhanyagniḥ samādiṣṭo mukhe cāpaḥ samāśritaḥ
pṛthvīdharo'ryamā caiva skandhayostāvadhiṣṭhitau || 39 ||
vakṣaḥsthale cā''pavatsaḥ pūjanīyaḥ sadā budhaiḥ
netrayorditiparjanyau śrotre'ditijayantakau || 40 ||
sarpendrā vaṃsasaṃsthau tu pūjanīyau prayatnataḥ
sūryasomādayastadvadbāhvoḥ pañca ca pañca ca || 41 ||
rudra śca rājayakṣmā ca vāmahaste samāsthitau
sāvitraḥ savitā tadvaddhastaṃ dakṣiṇamāsthitau || 42 ||
vivasvānatha mitraśca jaṭhare saṃvyavasthitau
pūṣā ca pāpayakṣmā ca hastayormaṇibandhane || 43 ||
tathaivāsuraśeṣau ca vāmapārśvaṃ samāśritau
pārśve tu dakṣiṇe tadvadvitathaḥ sagṛhakṣataḥ || 44 ||
ūrvoryamāmbupau jñeyau jānvorgandharvapuṣpakau
jaṅghayorbhṛṅgasugrīvau sphiksthau dauvāriko mṛgaḥ || 45 ||
jayaśakrau tathā meḍhre pādayoḥ pitarastathā
madhye navapade brahmā hṛdaye sa tu pūjyate || 46 ||
catuḥṣaṣṭipado vāstuḥ prāsāde brahmaṇā smṛtaḥ
brahmā catuṣpadastatra koṇeṣvardhapadāstathā || 47 ||
bahiṣkoṇeṣu vāstau tu sārdhāścobhayasaṃsthitāḥ
viṃśatidvipadāścaiva catuḥṣaṣṭipade smṛtāḥ || 48 ||
gṛhārambheṣu kaṇḍūtiḥ svāmyaṅge yatra jāyate
śalyaṃ tvapanayettatra prāsāde bhavane tathā || 49 ||
saśalyaṃ bhayadaṃ yasmādaśalyaṃ śubhadāyakam
hīnādhikāṅgatāṃ vāstoḥ sarvathā tu vivarjayet || 50 ||
nagaragrāmadeśeṣu sarvatraivaṃ vivarjayet
catuḥśālaṃ triśālaṃ ca dviśālaṃ caikaśālakam
nāmatastānpravakṣyāmi svarūpeṇa dvijottamāḥ || 51 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇa ekāśītipadavāstunirṇayo nāma tripañcāśadadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 253 ||

<254>

atha catuṣpañcāśadadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
sūta uvāca
catuḥśālaṃ pravakṣyāmi svarūpānnāmatastathā
catuḥśālaṃ caturdvārairalindaiḥ sarvatomukham || 1 ||
nāmnā tatsarvatobhadraṃ śubhaṃ devanṛpālaye
paścimadvārahīnaṃ ca nandyāvartaṃ pracakṣate || 2 ||
dakṣiṇadvārahīnaṃ tu vardhamānamudāhṛtam
pūrvadvāravihīnaṃ tatsvastikaṃ nāma viśrutam || 3 ||
rucakaṃ cottaradvāravihīnaṃ tatpracakṣate
saumyaśālāvihīnaṃ yattriśālaṃ dhānyakaṃ ca tat || 4 ||
kṣemavṛddhikaraṃ nṝṇāṃ bahuputraphalapradam
śālayā pūrvayā hīnaṃ sukṣetramiti viśrutam || 5 ||
dhanyaṃ yaśasyamāyuṣyaṃ śokamohavināśanam
śālayā yāmyayā hīnaṃ yadviśālaṃ tu śālayā || 6 ||
kulakṣayakaraṃ nṝṇāṃ sarvavyādhibhayāvaham
hīnaṃ paścimayā yattu pakṣaghnaṃ nāma tatpunaḥ || 7 ||
mitrabandhusutānhanti tathā sarvabhayāvaham
yāmyāparābhyāṃ śālābhyāṃ dhanadhānyaphalapradam || 8 ||
kṣemavṛddhikaraṃ nṛṇāṃ tathā putraphalapradam
yamasūryaṃ ca vijñeyaṃ paścimottaraśālakam || 9 ||
rājāgnibhayadaṃ nṛṇāṃ kulakṣayakaraṃ ca yat
udakpūrve tu śāle dve daṇḍākhye yatra tadbhavet || 10 ||
akālamṛtyubhayadaṃ paracakrabhayāvaham
dhanākhyaṃ pūrvayāmyābhyāṃ śālābhyāṃ yadviśālakam || 11 ||
tacchastrabhayadaṃ nṝṇāṃ parābhavabhayāhavam
cullī pūrvāparābhyāṃ tu sā bhavenmṛtyusūcanī || 12 ||
vaidhavyadāyakaṃ strīṇāmanekabhayakārakam
kāryamuttarayāmyābhyāṃ śālābhyāṃ bhayadaṃ nṛṇām || 13 ||
siddhārthavajravarjyāni viśālāni sadā budhaiḥ
athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi bhavanaṃ pṛthivīpateḥ || 14 ||
pañcaprakāraṃ tatproktamuttamādivibhedataḥ
aṣṭottaraṃ hastaśataṃ vistāraścottamo mataḥ || 15 ||
caturṣvanyeṣu vistāro hīyate cāṣṭabhiḥ karaiḥ
caturthāṃśādhikaṃ dairghyaṃ pañcasvapi nigadyate || 16 ||
yuvarājasya vakṣyāmi tathā bhavanapañcakam
ṣaḍbhiḥ ṣaḍbhistathāśītirhīyate tatra vistarāt || 17 ||
tryaṃśena cādhikaṃ dairghyaṃ pañcasvapi nigadyate
senāpateḥ pravakṣyāmi tathā bhavanapañcakam || 18 ||
catuḥṣaṣṭistu vistārātṣaḍbhiḥ ṣaḍbhistu hīyate
pañcasveteṣu dairghyaṃ ca ṣaḍbhāgenādhikaṃ bhavet || 19 ||
mantriṇāmatha vakṣyāmi tathā bhavanapañcakam
catuścaturbhirhīnā syātkaraṣaṣṭiḥ pravistare || 20 ||
aṣṭāṃśenādhikaṃ dairghyaṃ pañcasvapi nigadyate
sāmantāmātyalokānāṃ vakṣye bhavanapañcakama || 21 ||
catvāriṃśattathā'ṣṭau ca caturbhirhīyate kramāt
caturthāṃśādhikaṃ dairghyaṃ pañcasveteṣu śasyate || 22 ||
śilpināṃ kañcukīnāṃ ca veśyānāṃ gṛhapañcakam
aṣṭāviṃśatkarāṇāṃ tu vihīnaṃ vistare kramāt || 23 ||
dviguṇaṃ dairghyamevoktaṃ madhyameṣvevameva tat
dūtīkarmātakādīnāṃ vakṣye bhavanapañcakam || 24 ||
caturthāṃśādhikaṃ dairghyaṃ vistāro dvādaśaiva tu
ardhārdhakarahāniḥ syādvistārātpañcaśaḥ kramāt || 25 ||
daivajñaguruvaidyānāṃ sabhāstārapurodhasām
teṣāmapi pravakṣyāmi tathā bhavanapañcakam || 26 ||
catvāriṃśattu vistārāccaturbhirhīyate kramāt
pañcasveteṣu dairghyaṃ ṣaḍbhāgenādhikaṃ bhavet || 27 ||
caturvarṇasya vakṣyāmi sāmānyaṃ gṛhapañcakam
dvātriṃśataḥ karāṇāṃ tu caturbhirhīyate kramāt || 28 ||
āṣoḍaśāditi paraṃ nūnamantyādasāyinām
daśāṃśenāṣṭabhāgena tribhāgenātha pādikam || 29 ||
adhikaṃ dairghyamityāhurbrāhmaṇādeḥ praśasyate
senāpaternṛpasyāpi gṛhayorantareṇa tu || 30 ||
nṛpavāsagṛhaṃ kāryaṃ bhāṇḍāgāraṃ tathaiva ca
senāpatergṛhasyāpi cāturvarṇyasya cāntare
vāsāya ca gṛhaṃ kāryaṃ rājapūjyeṣu sarvadā || 31 ||
antaraprabhavāṇāṃ ca svapiturgṛhamiṣyate
tathā hastaśatādardhaṃ gaditaṃ vanavāsinām || 32 ||
senāpaternṛpasyāpi saptatyā sahite'nvite
caturdaśahṛte vyāse śālānyāsaḥ prakīrtitaḥ || 33 ||
pañcatriṃśānvite tasminnalindaḥ samudāhṛtaḥ
tathā ṣaṭtriṃśaddhastā tu saptāṅgulasamanvitā || 34 ||
viprasya mahatī śālā na dairghyaṃ parato bhavet
daśāṅgulādhikā tadvatkṣattriyasya vidhīyate || 35 ||
pañcatriṃśatkarā vaiśye aṅgulāni trayodaśa
tāvatkaraiva śūdra sya yutā pañcadaśāṅgulaiḥ || 36 ||
śālāyāstu tribhāgena yasyāgre vīthikā bhavet
soṣṇīṣaṃ nāma tadvāstu paścācchreyocchrayaṃ bhavet || 37 ||
pārśvayorvīthikā yatra sāvaṣṭambhaṃ taducyate
samantādvīthikā yatra susthitaṃ tadihocyate || 38 ||
śubhadaṃ sarvametatsyāccātuvarṇye caturvidham
vistārātṣoḍaśo bhāgastathā hastacatuṣṭayam || 39 ||
prathamo bhūmikocchrāya upariṣṭātprahīyate
dvādaśāṃśena sarvāsu bhūmikāsu tathocchrayaḥ || 40 ||
pakveṣṭakā bhavedbhittiḥ ṣoḍaśāṃśena vistarāt
dāravairapi kalpyā syāttathā mṛnmayabhittikā || 41 ||
garbhamānena mānaṃ tu sarvavāstuṣu śasyate
gṛhavyāsasya pañcāśadaṣṭādaśabhiraṅgulaiḥ || 42 ||
saṃyuto dvāraviṣkambho dviguṇaścocchrayo bhavet
dvāraśākhāsu bāhulyamucchrāyakarasaṃmitaiḥ || 43 ||
aṅgulaiḥ sarvavāstūnāṃ pṛthutvaṃ śasyate budhaiḥ
udumbarottamāṅgaṃ ca tadārdhārdhapravistarāt || 44 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe vāstuvidyāsu gṛhamānanirṇayo nāma catuṣpañcāśadadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 254 ||

<255>

atha pañcapañcāśadadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
sūta uvāca
athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi stambhamānavinirṇayam
kṛtvā svamavanocchrāyaṃ sadā saptaguṇaṃ budhaiḥ || 1 ||
aśītyaṃśaḥ pṛthutve syādagre navaguṇe sati
rucakaścaturaḥ syāttu aṣṭāstro vajra ucyate || 2 ||
dvivajraḥ ṣoḍaśāstrastu dvātriṃśāstraḥ pralīnakaḥ
madhyapradeśe yaḥ stambho vṛtto vṛtta iti smṛtaḥ || 3 ||
ete pañca mahāstambhāḥ praśastāḥ sarvavāstuṣu
padmavallīlatākumbhapatradarpaṇarūpitāḥ || 4 ||
stambhasya navamāṃśena padmakumbhāntarāṇi tu
stambhatulyā tulā proktā hīnā copatulā tataḥ || 5 ||
tribhāgeneha sarvatra caturbhāgena vā punaḥ
hīnaṃ hīnaṃ caturthāṃśāttathā sarvāsu bhūmiṣu || 6 ||
vāsagehāni sarveṣāṃ praviśeddakṣiṇena tu
dvārāṇi tu pravakṣyāmi praśastānīha yāni tu || 7 ||
pūrveṇendraṃ jayantaṃ ca dvāraṃ sarvatra śasyate
yāmyaṃ ca vitathaṃ caiva dakṣiṇena vidurbudhāḥ || 8 ||
paścime puṣpadantaṃ ca vāruṇaṃ ca praśasyate
uttareṇa tu bhallāṭaṃ saumyaṃ tu śubhadaṃ bhavet || 9 ||
tathā vāstuṣu sarvatra vedhaṃ dvārasya varjayet
dvāre tu rathyayā viddhe bhavetsarvakulakṣayaḥ || 10 ||
taruṇā dveṣabāhulyaṃ śokaḥ paṅkena jāyate
apasmāro bhavennūnaṃ kūpavedhena sarvadā || 11 ||
vyathā prasravaṇena syātkīlenāgnimayaṃ bhavet
vināśo devatāviddhe stambhena strīkṛto bhavet || 12 ||
gṛhabharturvināśaḥ syādgṛheṇa ca gṛhe kṛte
amedhyāvaskarairviddhe gṛhiṇī bandhakī bhavet || 13 ||
tathā śastrabhayaṃ vindyādantyajasya gṛheṇa tu
ucchrāyāddviguṇāṃ bhūmiṃ tyaktvā vedho na jāyate || 14 ||
svayamudghāṭite dvāra unmādo gṛhavāsinām
svayaṃ vā pihite vidyātkulanāśaṃ vicakṣaṇaḥ || 15 ||
mānādhike rājabhayaṃ nyūne taskarato bhavet
dvāropari ca yaddvāratadantakamukhaṃ smṛtam || 16 ||
adhvano madhyadeśe tu adhiko yasya vistaraḥ
vajraṃ tu saṃkaṭaṃ madhye sadyo bharturvināśanam || 17 ||
tathā'nyapīḍitaṃ dvāraṃ bahudoṣakaraṃ bhavet
mūladvārāttathā'nyattu nādhikaṃ śobhanaṃ bhavet || 18 ||
kumbhaśrīparṇivallībhirmūladvāraṃ tu śobhayet
pūjayeccāpi tannityaṃ balinā cākṣatodakaiḥ || 19 ||
bhavanasya vaṭaḥ pūrve digbhāge sārvakāmikaḥ
udumbarastathā yāmye vāruṇyāṃ pippalaḥ śubhaḥ || 20 ||
plakṣaścottarato dhanyo viparītāstvasiddhaye
kaṇṭakī kṣīravṛkṣaśca āsanaḥ saphalo drumaḥ || 21 ||
bhāryāhānau prajāhānau bhavetāṃ kramaśastadā
na cchindyādyadi tānanyānantare sthāpayecchubhān || 22 ||
punnāgāśokabakulaśamītilakacampakān
dāḍimīpippalīdrā kṣāstathā kusumamaṇḍapān || 23 ||
jambīrapūgapanasadrumaketakībhirjātīsarojaśatapatrikamallikābhiḥ
yannārikelakadalīdalapāṭalābhiryuktaṃ tadatra bhavanaṃ śriyamātanoti || 24 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe vāstuvidyāsu vedhaparivarjanaṃ nāma pañcapañcāśadadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 255 ||

<256>

atha ṣaṭpañcāśadadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
sūta uvāca
udagādiplavaṃ vāstu samānaśikharaṃ tathā
parīkṣya pūrvavatkuryātstambhocchrāyaṃ vicakṣaṇaḥ || 1 ||
na devadhūrtasacivacatvarāṇāṃ samīpataḥ
kārayedbhavanaṃ prājño duḥkhaśokabhayaṃ tataḥ || 2 ||
tasya pradeśāścatvārastathotsargo'grataḥ śubhaḥ
pṛṣṭhataḥ pṛṣṭhabhāgastu savyāvataḥ praśasyate || 3 ||
apasavyo vināśāya dakṣiṇe śīrṣakastathā
sarvakāmaphalo nṝṇāṃ saṃpūrṇo nāma vāmataḥ || 4 ||
evaṃ pradeśamālokya yatnena gṛhamārabhet
atha sāṃvatsaraprokte muhūrte śubhalakṣaṇe || 5 ||
ratnopari śilāṃ kṛtvā sarvabījasamanvitām
caturbhirbrāhmaṇaiḥ stambhaṃ kārayitvā supūjitam || 6 ||
śuklāmbaradharaḥ śilpisahito vedapāragaḥ
snāpitaṃ vinyasettadvatsarvauṣadhisamanvitam || 7 ||
nānākṣatasamopetaṃ vastrālaṃkārasaṃyutam
brahmaghoṣeṇa vādyena nītamaṅgalaniḥsvanaiḥ || 8 ||
pāyasaṃ bhojayedviprānhomaṃ tu madhusarpiṣā
vāstoṣpate pratijānīhi mantreṇānena sarvadā || 9 ||
sūtrapāte tathā kāryamevaṃ stambhodaye punaḥ
dvāravaṃśocchraye tadvatpraveśasamaye tathā || 10 ||
vāstūpaśamane tadvadvāstuyajñastu pañcadhā
īśāne sūtrapātaḥ syādāgneye stambharopaṇam || 11 ||
pradakṣiṇaṃ ca kurvīta vāstoḥ padavilekhanam
tarjanī madhyamā caiva tathā'ṅguṣṭhastu dakṣiṇe || 12 ||
pravālaratnakanakaphalaṃ piṣṭvā kṛtodakam
sarvavāstuvibhāgeṣu śastaṃ padavilekhane || 13 ||
na bhasmāṅgārakāṣṭhena nakhaśastreṇa carmabhiḥ
na śṛṅgāsthikapālaiśca kvacidvāstu vilekhayet || 14 ||
ebhirvilikhitaṃ kuryādduḥkhaśokabhayādikam
yadā gṛhapraveśaḥ syācchilpī tatrāpi lakṣayet || 15 ||
stambhasūtrādikaṃ tadvacchubhāśubhaphalapradam
ādityābhimukhaṃ rauti śakuniḥ paruṣaṃ yadi || 16 ||
tulyakālaṃ spṛśedaṅgaṃ gṛhabharturyadātmanaḥ
vāstvaṅge tadvijānīyānnaraśalyaṃ bhayapradam || 17 ||
aṅganānantaraṃ yatra hastyaśvaśvāpadaṃ bhavet
tadaṅgasaṃbhavaṃ vindyāttatra śalyaṃ vicakṣaṇaḥ || 18 ||
prasāryamāṇe sūtre tu śvā gomāyurvilaṅghate
tattu śalyaṃ vijānīyātkharaśabde'tibhairave || 19 ||
yadīśāne tu digbhāge madhuraṃ rauti vāyasaḥ
dhanaṃ tatra vijānīyādbhāge vā svāmyadhiṣṭhite || 20 ||
sūtracchede bhavenmṛtyurvyādhiḥ kīle tvadhomukhe
aṅgāreṣu tathonmādaṃ kapāleṣu ca saṃbhramam || 21 ||
kambuśalyeṣu jānīyātpauṃścalyaṃ strīṣu vāstuvit
gṛhabharturgṛhasyāpi vināśaḥ śilpisaṃbhrame || 22 ||
stambhe skandhacyute kumbhe śirorogaṃ vinirdiśet
kumbhāpahāre sarvasya kulasyāpi kṣayo bhavet || 23 ||
mṛtyuḥ sthānacyute kumbhe bhagne bandhaṃ vidurbudhāḥ
karasaṃkhyāvināśe tu nāśaṃ gṛhapaterviduḥ || 24 ||
bījauṣadhivihīne tu bhūtebhyo bhayamādiśet
prāgdakṣiṇena vinyasya stambhe chatraṃ niveśayet
tataḥ pradakṣiṇenānyānnyasetstambhānvicakṣaṇaḥ || 25 ||
yasmādbhayaṃkarā nṝṇāṃ yojitā hyapradakṣiṇam
rakṣāṃ kurvīta yatnena stambhopadra vanāśinīm || 26 ||
tathā phalavatīṃ śākhāṃ stambhopari niveśayet
prāgudakpravaṇaṃ kuryāddiṅmūḍhaṃ tu na kārayet || 27 ||
stabhaṃ vā bhavanaṃ vā'pi dvāraṃ vāsagṛhaṃ tathā
diṅmūḍhe kulanāśaḥ syānna ca saṃvardhayedgṛham || 28 ||
yadi saṃvardhayedgehaṃ sarvameva vivardhayet
pūrveṇa vardhitaṃ vāstu kuryādvairāṇi sarvadā || 29 ||
dakṣiṇe vardhitaṃ vāstu mṛtyave syānna saṃśayaḥ
paścādvivṛddhaṃ yadvāstu tadarthakṣayakārakam || 30 ||
vardhāpitaṃ tathā saumye bahusaṃtāpakārakam
āgneye yatra vṛddhiḥ syāttadagnibhayadaṃ bhavet || 31 ||
vardhitaṃ rākṣase koṇe śiśukṣayakaraṃ bhavet
vardhāpitaṃ tu vāyavye vātavyādhiprakopakṛt || 32 ||
īśānyāmannahāniḥ syādvāstau saṃvardhite sadā
īśāne devatāgāraṃ tathā śāntigṛhaṃ bhavet || 33 ||
mahānasaṃ tathā''gneye tatpārśve cottare jalam
gṛhasyopaskaraṃ sarvaṃ nairṛtye sthāpayedbudhaḥ || 34 ||
bandhasthānaṃ bahiḥ kuryātsnānamaṇḍapameva ca
dhanadhānyaṃ ca vāyavye karmaśālāṃ tato bahiḥ
evaṃ vāstuviśeṣaḥ syādgṛhabhartuḥ śubhāvahaḥ || 35 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe vāstuvidyāgṛhanirṇayo nāma ṣaṭpañcāśadadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 256 ||

<257>

atha saptapañcāśadadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
sūta uvāca
athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi dārvāharaṇamuttamam
dhaniṣṭhāpañcakaṃ muktvā tviṣṭyādikamataḥ param || 1 ||
tataḥ sāṃvatsarādiṣṭe dine yāyādvaraṃ budhaḥ
prathamaṃ balipūjāṃ ca kuryādvṛkṣasya sarvadā || 2 ||
pūrvottareṇa patitaṃ gṛhadāru praśasyate
anyathā na śubhaṃ vidyādyāmyopari nipātanam || 3 ||
kṣīravṛkṣodbhavaṃ dāru na gṛhe viniveśayet
kṛtādhivāsaṃ vihagairanilānalapīḍitam || 4 ||
gajāvarugṇaṃ ca tathā vidyunnirghātapīḍitam
ardhaśuṣkaṃ tathā dāru bhagnaśuṣkaṃ tathaiva ca || 5 ||
caityadevālayotpannaṃ nadīsaṃgamajaṃ tathā
śmaśānakūpanilayaṃ taḍāgādisamudbhavam || 6 ||
varjayetsarvathā dāru yadīcchedvipulāṃ śriyam
tathā kaṇṭakino vṛkṣānnīpanimbavibhītakān || 7 ||
śleṣmātakānāmratarūnvarjayedgṛhakarmaṇi
āsanāśokamadhukasarjaśālāḥ śubhāvahāḥ || 8 ||
candanaṃ panasaṃ dhanyaṃ suradāru haridra vaḥ
dvābhyāmekena vā kuryāntribhirvā bhuvanaṃ śubham || 9 ||
bahubhiḥ kāritaṃ yasmādanekabhayadaṃ bhavet
ekaikaśiṃśapā dhanyā śrīparṇī tindukī tathā || 10 ||
etā nānyasamāyuktāḥ kadācicchubhakārikāḥ
syandanaḥ panasastadvatsaralārjunapadmakāḥ || 11 ||
ete nānyasamāyuktā vāstukāryaphalapradāḥ
tarucchede mahāpīte godhā vindyādvicakṣaṇaḥ || 12 ||
māñjiṣṭhavarṇe bhekaḥ syānnīle sarpādi nirdiśet
aruṇe saraṭaṃ vidyānmuktāme śukamādiśet || 13 ||
kapile mūṣakānvidyātkhaḍragābhe jalamādiśet
evaṃvidhaṃ sagarbhaṃ tu varjayedvāstukarmaṇi || 14 ||
pūrvacchinnaṃ tu gṛhṇīyānnimittaśakunaḥ śubhaiḥ
vyāsena guṇite dairghye aṣṭābhirvai hṛte tathā || 15 ||
yaccheṣamāyataṃ vindyādaṣṭabhedaṃ vadāmi vaḥ
dhvajo dhūmaśca siṃhaśca kharaḥ śvā vṛṣa eva ca || 16 ||
hastī dhvāṃkṣaśca pūrvādyāḥ karaśeṣā bhavantyamī
dhvajaḥ sarvamukho dhanyaḥ pratyagdvāro viśeṣataḥ || 17 ||
udaṅmukho bhavetsiṃhaḥ prāṅmukho vṛṣabho bhavet
dakṣiṇābhimukho hastī saptabhiḥ samudāhṛtaḥ || 18 ||
ekena dhvaja uddiṣṭastribhiḥ siṃhaḥ prakīrtitaḥ
pañcabhirvṛṣabhaḥ prokto vikoṇasthāṃśca varjayet || 19 ||
tamevāṣṭaguṇaṃ kṛtvā kararāśiṃ vicakṣaṇaḥ
saptaviṃśāhṛte bhāge ṛkṣaṃ vidyādvicakṣaṇaḥ || 20 ||
aṣṭabhirbhājite ṛkṣe yaḥ śeṣaḥ sa vyayo mataḥ
vyayādhikaṃ na kurvīta yato doṣakaraṃ bhavet
āyādhike bhavecchāntirityāha bhagavānhariḥ || 21 ||
kṛtvā'grato dvijavarānatha pūrṇakumbhaṃ dadhyakṣatāmradalapuṣpaphalopaśobham
dattvā hiraṇyavasanāni tadā dvijebhyo māṅgalyaśāntinilayāya gṛhaṃ viśettu || 22 ||
gṛhyoktahomavidhinā balikarma kuryātprāsādavāstuśamane ca vidhirya uktaḥ saṃtarpayeddvijavarānatha bhakṣyabhojyaiḥ śuklāmbaraḥ svabhavanaṃ praviśetsadhūpam || 23 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe vāstuvidyānukīrtanaṃ nāma saptapañcāśadadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 257 ||

<258>

athāṣṭapañcāśadadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
ṛṣaya ūcuḥ
kriyāyogaḥ kathaṃ sidhyedgṛhasthādiṣu sarvadā
jñānayogasahasrāddhi karmayogo viśiṣyate || 1 ||
sūta uvāca
kriyāyogaṃ pravakṣyāmi devatārcānukīrtanam
muktimuktipadaṃ yasmānnānyalokeṣu vidyate || 2 ||
pratiṣṭhāyāṃ surāṇāṃ tu devatārcānukīrtanam
devayajñotsavaṃ cāpi bandhanādyena mucyate || 3 ||
viṣṇostāvatpravakṣyāmi yādṛgrūpaṃ praśasyate
śaṅkhacakradharaṃ śāntaṃ padmahastaṃ gadādharam || 4 ||
chatrākāraṃ śirastasya kambugrīvaṃ śubhekṣaṇam
tuṅganāsaṃ śuktikarṇaṃ praśāntorubhujakramam || 5 ||
kvacidaṣṭabhujaṃ vidyāccaturbhujamathāparam
dvibhujaścapi kartavyo bhavaneṣu purodhasā || 6 ||
devasyāṣṭabhujasyāsya yathāsthānaṃ nibodhata
khaḍgo gadā śiraḥ padmaṃ deyaṃ dakṣiṇato hareḥ || 7 ||
dhanuśca kheṭakaṃ caiva śaṅkhacakre ca vāmataḥ
caturbhujasya vakṣyāmi yathaivā''yudhasaṃsthitim || 8 ||
dakṣiṇena gadā padmaṃ vāsudevasya kārayet
vāmataḥ śaṅkhacakre ca kartavye bhūtimicchatā || 9 ||
kṛṣṇāvatāre tu gadā vāmahaste praśasyate
yathecchayā śaṅkhacakre copariṣṭātprakalpayet || 10 ||
adhastātpṛthivī tasya kartavyā pādamadhyataḥ
dakṣiṇe praṇataṃ tadvadgarutmantaṃ niveśayet || 11 ||
vāmatastu bhavellakṣmīḥ padmahastā śubhānanā
garutmānagrato vā'pi saṃsthāpyo bhūtimicchatā || 12 ||
śrīśca puṣṭiśca kartavye pārśvayoḥ padmasaṃyute
toraṇaṃ copariṣṭāttu vidyādharasamanvitam || 13 ||
devaduṃdubhisaṃyuktaṃ gandharvamithunānvitam
patravallīsamopetaṃ siṃhavyāghrasamanvitam || 14 ||
tathā kalpalatopetaṃ stuvadbhiramareśvaraiḥ
evaṃvidho bhavedviṣṇostribhāgeṇāsya pīṭhikā || 15 ||
navatālapramāṇāstu devadānavakiṃnarāḥ
ataḥparaṃ pravakṣyāmi mānonmānaṃ viśeṣataḥ || 16 ||
jālāntarapraviṣṭānāṃ bhānūnāṃ yadra jaḥ sphuṭam
trasareṇuḥ sa vijñeyo vālāgraṃ tairathāṣṭabhiḥ || 17 ||
tadaṣṭakena likhyā tu yūkā likhyāṣṭakairmatā
yavo yūkāṣṭakaṃ tadvadaṣṭabhistaistadaṅgulam || 18 ||
svakīyāṅulimānena mukhaṃ syāddvādaśāṅgulam
mukhamānena kartavyā sarvāvayavakalpanā || 19 ||
sauvarṇī rājatī vā'pi tāmrī ratnamayī tathā
śailī dārumayī cāpi lohasīsamayī tathā || 20 ||
rītikādhātuyuktā vā tāmrakāṃsyamayī tathā
śubhadārumayī vā'pi devatārcā praśasyate || 21 ||
aṅguṣṭhaparvādārabhya vitastiryāvadeva tu
gṛheṣu pratimā kāryā nādhikā śasyate budhaiḥ || 22 ||
āṣoḍaśā tu prāsāde kartavyā nādhikā tataḥ
madhyottamakaniṣṭhā tu kāryā vittānusārataḥ || 23 ||
dvārocchrāyasya yanmānamaṣṭadhā tattu kārayet
bhāgamekaṃ tatastyaktvā pariśiṣṭaṃ tu yadbhavet || 24 ||
bhāgadvayena pratimā tribhāgīkṛtya tatpunaḥ
pīṭhikā bhāgataḥ kāryā nātinīcā nacocchritā || 25 ||
pratimāmukhamānena nava bhāgānprakalpayet
caturaṅgulā bhavedgrīvā bhāgena hṛdayaṃ punaḥ || 26 ||
nābhistasmādadhaḥ kāryā bhāgenaikena śobhanā
nimnatve vistaratve ca aṅgulaṃ parikīrtitam || 27 ||
nābheradhastathā meḍhraṃ bhāgenaikena kalpayet
dvibhāgenā''yatāvūrū jānunī caturaṅgule || 28 ||
jaṅghe dvibhāge vikhyāte pādau ca caturaṅgulau
caturdaśāṅgulastadvanmaulirasya prakīrtitaḥ || 29 ||
ūrdhvamānamidaṃ proktaṃ pṛthutvaṃ ca nibodhata
sarvāvayavamāneṣu vistāraṃ śṛṇuta dvijāḥ || 30 ||
caturaṅgulaṃ lalāṭaṃ syādūrdhvaṃ nāsā tathaiva ca
dvyaṅgulastu hanurjñeya oṣṭhau dvyaṅgulasaṃmitau || 31 ||
aṣṭāṅgulaṃ lalāṭaṃ ca tāvanmātre bhruvau mate
ardhāṅgulā bhruvorlekhā madhye dhanurivā''natā || 32 ||
unnatāgrā bhavetpārśve ślakṣṇatīkṣṇā praśasyate
akṣiṇī dvyaṅgulāyāme tadardhaṃ caiva vistare || 33 ||
unnatodaramadhye tu raktānte śubhalakṣaṇe
tārakārdhavibhāgena dṛṣṭiḥ syātpañcabhāgikī || 34 ||
dvyaṅgulaṃ tu bhruvormadhye nāsāmūlamathāṅgulam
nāsāgravistaraṃ tadvatpuṭadvayamathā''natam || 35 ||
nāsāpuṭabilaṃ tadvadardhāṅgulamudāhṛtam
kapole dvyaṅgule tadvatkarṇamūlādvinirgate || 36 ||
hanvagramaṅgulaṃ tadvadvistāro dvyaṅgulo bhavet
ardhāṅgulā bhruvo rājī praṇālasadṛśī samā || 37 ||
ardhāṅgulasamastadvaduttaroṣṭhastu vistare
niṣpāvasadṛśaṃ tadvannāsāpuṭadalaṃ bhavet || 38 ||
sṛkkiṇī jyotistulye tu karṇamūlātṣaḍaṅgule
karṇau tu bhrūsamau jñeyāvūrdhvaṃ tu caturaṅgulau || 39 ||
dvyaṅgulau karṇapārśvau tu mātrāmekāṃ tu vistṛtau
karṇayorupariṣṭācca mastakaṃ dvādaśāṅgulam || 40 ||
lalāṭaṃ pṛṣṭhato'rdhena proktamaṣṭādaśāṅgulam
ṣaṭtriṃśadaṅgulaścāsya pariṇāhaḥ śirogataḥ || 41 ||
sakeśanicayo yasya dvicatvāriṃśadaṅgulaḥ
keśāntāddhanukā tadvadaṅgulāni tu ṣoḍaśa || 42 ||
grīvāmadhyaparīṇāhaścaturviṃśatikāṅgulaḥ
aṣṭāṅgulā bhavedgrīvā pṛthutvena praśasyate || 43 ||
stanagrīvāntaraṃ proktamekatālaṃ svayaṃbhuvā
stanayorantaraṃ tadvaddvādaśāṅgulamiṣyate || 44 ||
stanayormaṇḍalaṃ tadvaddvyaṅgulaṃ parikīrtitam
cūcukau maṇḍalasyāntaryavamātrāvubhau smṛtau || 45 ||
dvitālaṃ cāpi vistārādvakṣaḥsthalamudāhṛtam
kakṣe ṣaḍaṅgule prokte bāhumūlastanāntare || 46 ||
caturdaśāṅgulau pādāvaṅguṣṭhau tu triraṅgulau
pañcāṅgulaparīṇāhamaṅguṣṭhāgraṃ tathonnatam || 47 ||
aṅguṣṭhakasamā tadvadāyābhā syātpradeśinī
tasyāḥ ṣoḍaśabhāgena hīyate madhyamāṅgulī || 48 ||
anāmikāṣṭabhāgena kaniṣṭhā cāpi hīyate
parvatrayeṇa cāṅgulyau gulphau dvyaṅgulakau matau || 49 ||
pārṣṇirdvyaṅgulamātrastu kalayoccaiḥ prakīrtitaḥ
dviparvāṅguṣṭhakaḥ proktaḥ parīṇāhaśca dvyaṅgulaḥ || 50 ||
pradeśinīparīṇāhastryaṅgulaḥ samudāhṛtaḥ
kaniṣṭhikāṣṭabhāgena hīyate kramaśo dvijāḥ || 51 ||
aṅgulenocchrayaḥ kāryo hyaṅguṣṭhasya viśeṣataḥ
tadardhena tu śeṣāṇāmaṅgulīnāṃ tathocchrayaḥ || 52 ||
jaṅghāgre pariṇāhastu aṅgulāni caturdaśa
jaṅghāmadhye parīṇāhastathaivāṣṭādaśāṅgulaḥ || 53 ||
jānumadhye parīṇāha ekaviṃśatiraṅgulaḥ
jānūcchrayo'ṅgulaḥ prokto maṇḍalaṃ tu triraṅgulam || 54 ||
ūrumadhye parīṇāho hyaṣṭāviṃśatikāṅgulaḥ
ekatriṃśopariṣṭācca vṛṣaṇau tu triraṅgulau || 55 ||
dvyaṅgulaṃ ca tathā meḍhraṃ parīṇāhe ṣaḍaṅgulam
maṇibandhādadho vidyātkeśarekhāstathaiva ca || 56 ||
maṇikośaparīṇāhaścaturaṅgula iṣyate
vistareṇa bhavettadvatkaṭiraṣṭādaśāṅgulā || 57 ||
dvāviṃśati tathā strīṇāṃ stanau ca dvādaśāṅgulau
nābhimadhyaparīṇāho dvicatvāriṃśadaṅgulaḥ || 58 ||
puruṣe pañcapañcāśatkaṭyāṃ caiva tu veṣṭanam
kakṣayorupariṣṭāttu skandhau proktau ṣaḍaṅgulau || 59 ||
aṣṭāṅgulāṃ tu vistāre grīvāṃ caiva vinirdiśet
pariṇāhe tathā grīvāṃ kalā dvādaśa nirdiśet || 60 ||
āyāmo bhujayostadvaddvicatvāriṃśadaṅgulaḥ
kāryaṃ tu bāhuśikharaṃ pramāṇe ṣoḍaśāṅgulam || 61 ||
ūrdhvaṃ yadbāhuparyantaṃ vidyādaṣṭādaśāṅgulam
tathaikāṅgulahīnaṃ tu dvitīyaṃ parva ucyate || 62 ||
bāhumadhye parīṇāho bhavedaṣṭādaśāṅgulaḥ
ṣoḍaśoktaḥ prabāhustu ṣaṭkalo'grakaro mataḥ || 63 ||
saptāṅgulaṃ karatalaṃ pañca madhyāṅgulī matā
anāmikā madhyamāyāḥ saptabhāgena hīyate || 64 ||
tasyāstu pañcabhāgena kaniṣṭhā parihīyate
madhyamāyāstu hīnā vai pañcabhāgena tarjanī || 65 ||
aṅguṣṭhastarjanīmūlādadhaḥ proktastu tatsamaḥ
aṅguṣṭhapariṇāhastu vijñeyaścaturaṅgulaḥ || 66 ||
śeṣāṇāmaṅgulīnāṃ tu bhāgo bhāgena hīyate
madhyamāparvamadhyaṃ tu aṅguladvayamāyatam || 67 ||
yavo yavena sarvāsāṃ tasyāstasyāḥ prahīyate
aṅguṣṭhaparvamadhyaṃ tu tarjanyā sadṛśaṃ bhavet || 68 ||
yadvadvayādhikaṃ tadvadagraparva udāhṛtam
pūrvārdhe tu nakhānvidyādaṅgulīṣu samantataḥ || 69 ||
snigdhaṃ ślakṣṇaṃ prakurvīta īṣadra ktaṃ tathāgrataḥ
nimnapṛṣṭhaṃ bhavenmadhye pārśvataḥ kalayo cchritam || 70 ||
tathaiva keśavallīyaṃ skandhopari daśāṅgulā
striyaḥ kāryāstu tanvaṅgyaḥ stanorujaghanādhikāḥ || 71 ||
caturdaśāṅgulāyāmamudaraṃ tāsu nirdiśet
nānābharaṇasaṃpannāḥ kiṃcicchlakṣṇabhujāstataḥ || 72 ||
kiṃciddīrghaṃ bhavedvaktramalakāvaliruttamā
nāsā grīvā lalāṭaṃ ca sārdhamātraṃ triraṅgulam || 73 ||
adhyardhāṅgulavistāraḥ śasyate'dharapallavaḥ
adhikaṃ netrayugmaṃ tu caturbhāgena nirdiśet || 74 ||
grīvābaliśca kartavyā kiṃcidardhāṅgulocchrayā || 74 ||
evaṃ nārīṣu sarvāsu devānāṃ pratimāsu ca
navatālamidaṃ proktaṃ lakṣaṇaṃ pāpanāśanam || 75 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe devārcānukīrtane pramāṇānukīrtanaṃ nāmāṣṭapañcāśadadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 258 ||

<259>

atha navapañcāśadadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
sūta uvāca
ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi devākārānviśeṣataḥ
daśatālaḥ smṛto rāmo balirvairocanistathā || 1 ||
vārāho nārasiṃhaśca saptatālastu vāmanaḥ
matsyakūrmau ca nirdiṣṭau yathāśobhaṃ svayaṃbhuvā || 2 ||
ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi rudrā dyākāramuttamam
sa pīnorubhujaskandhastaptakāñcanasaprabhaḥ || 3 ||
śuklo'rkaraśmisaṃghātaścandrā ṅkitajaṭo vibhuḥ
jaṭāmukuṭadhārī ca dvyaṣṭavarṣākṛtiśca saḥ || 4 ||
bāhū vāraṇahastābhau vṛttajaṅghorumaṇḍalaḥ
ūrdhvakeśaśca kartavyo dīrghāyatavilocanaḥ || 5 ||
vyāghracarmaparīdhānaḥ kaṭisūtratrayānvitaḥ
hārakeyūrasaṃpanno bhujaṅgābharaṇastathā || 6 ||
bāhavaścāpi kartavyā nānābharaṇabhūṣitāḥ
pīnorugaṇḍaphalakaḥ kuṇḍalābhyāmalaṃkṛtaḥ || 7 ||
ājānulambabāhuśca saumyamūrtiḥ suśobhanaḥ
kheṭakaṃ vāmahaste tu khaḍgaṃ caiva tu dakṣiṇe || 8 ||
śaktiṃ daṇḍaṃ triśūlaṃ ca dakṣiṇeṣu niveśayet
kapālaṃ vāmapārśve tu nāgaṃ khaṭvāṅgameva ca || 9 ||
ekaśca varado hastastathā'kṣavalayo'paraḥ
vaiśākhaṃ sthānakaṃ kṛtvā nṛtyābhinayasaṃsthitaḥ || 10 ||
nṛtyandaśabhujaḥ kāryo gajacarmadharastathā
tathā tripuradāhe ca bāhavaḥ ṣoḍaśaiva tu || 11 ||
śaṅkhaṃ cakraṃ gadā śārṅgaṃ ghaṇṭā tatrādhikā bhavet
tathā dhanuḥ pinākaśca śaro viṣṇumayastathā || 12 ||
caturbhujo'ṣṭabāhurvā jñānayogeśvaro mataḥ
tīkṣṇanāsāgradaśanaḥ karālavadano mahān || 13 ||
bhairavaḥ śasyate loke pratyāyatanasaṃsthitaḥ
na mūlāyatane kāryo bhairavastu bhayaṃkaraḥ || 14 ||
nārasiṃho varāho vā tathā'nye'pi bhayaṃkarāḥ
nādhikāṅgā na hīnāṅgāḥ kartavyā devatāḥ kvacit || 15 ||
svāminaṃ ghātayennyūnā karālavadanā tathā
adhikā śilpinaṃ hanyātkṛśā caivārthanāśinī || 16 ||
kṛśodarī tu durbhikṣaṃ nirmāṃsā dhananāśinī
vakranāsā tu duḥkhāya saṃkṣiptāṅgī bhayaṃkarī || 17 ||
cipiṭā duḥkhaśokāya anetrā netranāśinī
duḥkhadā hīnavaktrā tu pāṇipādakṛśā tathā || 18 ||
hīnāṅgā hīnajaṅghā ca bhramonmādakarī nṛṇām
śuṣkavaktrā tu rājānaṃ kaṭihīnā ca yā bhavet || 19 ||
pāṇipādavihīno yo jāyate mārako mahān
jaṅghājānuvihīnā ca śatrukalyāṇakāriṇī || 20 ||
putramitravināśāya hīnavakṣaḥsthalā tu yā
saṃpūrṇāvayavā yā tu āyurlakṣmīpradā sadā || 21 ||
evaṃ lakṣaṇamāsādya kartavyaḥ parameśvaraḥ
stūyamānaḥ suraiḥ sarvaiḥ samantāddarśayedbhavam || 22 ||
śakreṇa nandinā caiva mahākālena śaṃkaram
praṇatā lokapālāstu pārśve tu gaṇanāyakāḥ || 23 ||
nṛtyadbhṛṅgiriṭiścaiva bhūtavetālasaṃvṛtāḥ
sarve hṛṣṭāstu kartavyāḥ stuvantaḥ parameśvaram || 24 ||
gandharvavidyādharakiṃnarāṇāmathāpsaroguhyakanāyakānām
gaṇairanekaiḥ śataśo mahendrai rmunipravīrairapi namyamānam || 25 ||
dhṛtākṣasūtraiḥ śataśaḥ pravālapuṣpopahārapracayaṃ dadadbhiḥ
saṃstūyamānaṃ bhagavantamīḍyaṃ netratrayeṇāmaramartyapūjyam || 26 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe pratimālakṣaṇa ekonaṣaṣṭyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 259 ||

<260>

atha ṣaṣṭyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
sūta uvāca
adhunā saṃpravakṣyāmi ardhanārīśvaraṃ param
ardhena devadevasya nārīrūpaṃ suśobhanam || 1 ||
īśārdhe tu jaṭābhāgo bālendukalayā yutaḥ
umārdhe cāpi dātavyau sīmantatilakāvubhau || 2 ||
vāsukiṃ dakṣiṇe karṇe vāme kuṇḍalamādiśet
bālikā copariṣṭāttu kapālaṃ dakṣiṇe kare
triśūlaṃ vā'pi kartavyaṃ devadevasya śūlinaḥ || 3 ||
vāmato darpaṇaṃ dadyādutpalaṃ ca viśeṣataḥ
vāmabāhuśca kartavyaḥ keyūravalayānvitaḥ || 4 ||
upavītaṃ ca kartavyaṃ maṇimuktāmayaṃ tathā || 5 ||
stanabhāraṃ tathā'rdhe tu vāme pītaṃ prakalpayet
hārārdhamujjvalaṃ kuryācchroṇyardha tu tathaiva ca || 6 ||
liṅgārdhamūrdhagaṃ kuryādvyālājinakṛtāmvaram
vāme lambaparīdhānaṃ kaṭisūtratrayānvitam || 7 ||
nānāratnasamopetaṃ dakṣiṇaṃ bhujagānvitam
devasya dakṣiṇaṃ pādaṃ padmopari susaṃsthitam || 8 ||
kiṃcidūrdhvaṃ tathā vāmaṃ bhūṣitaṃ nūpureṇa tu
ratnairvibhūṣitānkuryādaṅgulīṣvaṅgulīyakān || 9 ||
sālaktakaṃ tathā pādaṃ pārvatyā darśayetsadā
ardhanārīśvarasvedaṃ rūpamasminnudāhṛtam || 10 ||
umāmaheśvarasyāpi lakṣaṇaṃ śṛṇuta dvijāḥ
saṃsthānaṃ tu tayorvakṣye līlālalitavibhramam || 11 ||
caturbhujaṃ dvibāhuṃ vā jaṭāmārendubhūṣitam
locanatrayasaṃyuktamumaikaskandhapāṇinam || 12 ||
dakṣiṇenotpalaṃ śūlaṃ vāme kucabhare karam
dvīpicarmaparīdhānaṃ nānāratnopaśobhitam || 13 ||
supratiṣṭhaṃ suveṣaṃ ca tathā'rdhendukṛtāsanam
vāme tu saṃsthitā devī tasyorau bāhugūhitā || 14 ||
śirobhūṣaṇasaṃyuktairalaṃkairlalitānanā
sabālikā karṇavatī lalāṭatilakojjvalā || 15 ||
maṇikuṇḍalasaṃyuktā karṇikābharaṇā kvacit
hārakeyūrabahulā haravaktrāvalokinī || 16 ||
dhāmāṃsaṃ devadevasya spṛśantī līlayā tataḥ
dakṣiṇaṃ tu bahiḥ kṛtvā bāhuṃ dakṣiṇatastathā || 17 ||
skandhe vā dakṣiṇe kukṣau spṛśantyaṅgulijaiḥ kvacit
vāme tu darpaṇaṃ dadyādutpalaṃ vā suśobhanam || 18 ||
kaṭisūtratrayaṃ caiva nitambe syātpralambakam
jayā ca vijayā caiva kārtikeyavināyakau || 19 ||
pārśvayordarśayettatra toraṇe gaṇaguhyakān
mālā vidyādharāṃstadvadvīṇāvānapsarogaṇaḥ || 20 ||
etadrū pamumeśasya kartavyaṃ bhūtimicchatā
śivanārāyaṇaṃ vakṣye sarvapāpapraṇāśanam || 21 ||
vāmārdhe mādhavaṃ vidyāddakṣiṇe śūlapāṇinam
bāhudvayaṃ ca kṛṣṇasya maṇikeyūrabhūṣitam || 22 ||
śaṅkhacakradharaṃ śāntamāraktāṅgulivibhramam
cakrasthāne gadāṃ vā'pi pāṇau dadyādadhastale || 23 ||
śaṅkhaṃ caivottare dadyātkaṭyardhaṃ bhūṣaṇojjvalam
pītavastraparīdhānaṃ caraṇaṃ maṇibhūṣaṇam || 24 ||
dakṣiṇārdhaṃ jaṭābhāramardhendukṛtabhūṣaṇam
bhujaṅgahāravalayaṃ varadaṃ dakṣiṇaṃ karam || 25 ||
dvitīyaṃ cāpi kurvīta triśūlavaradhāriṇam
vyālopavītasaṃyuktaṃ kaṭyardhaṃ kṛtivāsasam || 26 ||
maṇiratnaiśca saṃyuktaṃ pādaṃ nāgavibhūṣitam
śivanārāyaṇasyaivaṃ kalpayedrū pamuttamam || 27 ||
mahāvarāhaṃ vakṣyāmi padmahastaṃ gadādharam
tīkṣṇadaṃṣṭrāgradhoṇāsyaṃ medinī vāmakūrpare || 28 ||
daṃṣṭrāgreṇoddhṛtāṃ dāntāṃ dharaṇīmutpalānvitām
vismayotphullavadanāmupariṣṭātprakalpayet || 29 ||
dakṣiṇaṃ kaṭisaṃsthaṃ tu karaṃ tasyāḥ prakalpayet
kūrmopari tathā pādamekaṃ nāgendra mūrdhani || 30 ||
saṃstūyamānaṃ lokeśaiḥ samantātparikalpayet
nārasiṃhaṃ tu kartavyaṃ bhujāṣṭakasamanvitam || 31 ||
raudraṃ siṃhāsanaṃ tadvadvidāritamukhekṣaṇam
stabdhapīnasaṭākarṇaṃ dārayantaṃ diteḥ sutam || 32 ||
vinirgatāntrajālaṃ ca dānavaṃ parikalpayet
vamantaṃ rudhiraṃ ghoraṃ bhrukuṭīvadanekṣaṇam || 33 ||
yudhyamānaśca kartavyaḥ kvacitkaraṇabandhanaiḥ
pariśrāntena daityena tarjyamāno muhurmuhuḥ || 34 ||
daityaṃ pradarśayettatra khaḍgakheṭakadhāriṇam
stūyamānaṃ tathā viṣṇuṃ darśayedamarādhipaiḥ || 35 ||
tathā trivikramaṃ vakṣye brahmāṇḍakramaṇolbaṇam
pādapārśve tathā bāhumupariṣṭātprakalpayet || 36 ||
adhastādvāmanaṃ tadvatkalpayetsakamaṇḍalum
dakṣiṇe chatrikāṃ dadyānmukhaṃ dīnaṃ prakalpayet || 37 ||
bhṛṅgāradhāriṇaṃ tadvadbaliṃ tasya ca pārśvataḥ
bandhanaṃ cāsya kurvantaṃ garuḍaṃ tasya darśayet || 38 ||
matsyarūpaṃ tathā matsyaṃ kūrmaṃ kūrmākṛtiṃ nyaset
evaṃrūpastu bhagavānkāryo nārāyaṇo hariḥ || 39 ||
brahmā kamaṇḍaludharaḥ kartavyaḥ sa caturmukhaḥ
haṃsārūḍhaḥ kvacitkāryaḥ kvacicca kamalāsanaḥ || 40 ||
varṇataḥ padmagarbhābhaścaturbāhuḥ śubhekṣaṇaḥ
kamaṇḍaluṃ vāmakare sruvaṃ haste tu dakṣiṇe || 41 ||
vāme daṇḍadharaṃ tadvatsruvaṃ cāpi pradarśayet
munibhirdevagandharvaiḥ stūyamānaṃ samantataḥ || 42 ||
kurvāṇamiva lokāṃstrīñchuklāmbaradharaṃ vibhum
mṛgacarmadharaṃ cāpi divyayajñopavītinam || 43 ||
ājyasthālīṃ nyasetpārśve vedāṃśca caturaḥ punaḥ
vāmapārśve'sya sāvitrīṃ dakṣiṇe ca sarasvatīm || 44 ||
agre ca ṛṣayastadvatkāryāḥ paitāmahe pade
kārtikeyaṃ pravakṣyāmi taruṇādityasaprabham || 45 ||
kamalodaravarṇābhaṃ kumāraṃ sukumārakam
daṇḍakaiścīrakairyuktaṃ mayūravaravāhanam || 46 ||
sthāpayetsveṣṭanagare bhujāndvādaśa kārayet
caturbhujaḥ kharvaṭe syādvane grāme dvibāhukaḥ || 47 ||
śaktiḥ pāśastathā khaḍgaḥ śaraḥ śūlaṃ tathaiva ca
varadaścaiva hastaḥ syādatha cābhayado bhavet || 48 ||
ete dakṣiṇato jñeyāḥ keyūrakaṭakojjvalāḥ
dhanuḥ patākāmuṣṭiśca tarjanī tu prasāritā || 49 ||
kheṭakaṃ tāmracūḍaṃ ca vāmahaste tu śasyate
dvibhujasya kare śaktirvāme syātkukkuṭopari || 50 ||
caturbhuje śaktipāśau vāmato dakṣiṇe tvasiḥ
varado bhayado vā'pi dakṣiṇaḥ syātturīyakaḥ || 51 ||
vināyakaṃ pravakṣyāmi gajavaktraṃ trilocanam
lambodaraṃ caturbāhuṃ vyālayajñopavītinam || 52 ||
dhvastakarṇaṃ bṛhattuṇḍamekadaṃṣṭraṃ pṛthūdaram
svadantaṃ dakṣiṇakara utpalaṃ cāpare tathā || 53 ||
modakaṃ paraśuṃ caiva vāmataḥ parikalpayet
bṛhattvātkṣiptavadanaṃ pīnaskandhāṅghripāṇikam || 54 ||
yuktaṃ tu ṛddhibuddhibhyāmadhastānmūṣakānvitam
kātyāyanyāḥ pravakṣyāmi rūpaṃ daśabhujaṃ tathā || 55 ||
trayāṇāmapi devānāmanukārānukāriṇīm
jaṭājūṭasamāyuktāmardhendukṛtalakṣaṇām || 56 ||
locanatrayasaṃyuktāṃ pūrṇendusadṛśānanām
atasīpuṣpavarṇābhāṃ supratiṣṭhāṃ sulocanām || 57 ||
navayauvanasaṃpannāṃ sarvābharaṇabhūṣitām
sucārudaśanāṃ tadvatpīnonnatapayodharām || 58 ||
tribhaṅgasthānasaṃsthānāṃ mahiṣāsuramardinīm
triśūlaṃ dakṣiṇe dadyātkhaḍgaṃ cakraṃ kramādadhaḥ || 59 ||
tīkṣṇabāṇaṃ tathā śaktiṃ vāmato'pi nibodhata
kheṭakaṃ pūrṇacāpaṃ ca pāśamaṅkuśameva ca || 60 ||
ghaṇṭāṃ vā paraśuṃ vā'pi vāmataḥ saṃniveśayet
adhastānmahiṣaṃ tadvadviśiraskaṃ pradarśayet || 61 ||
śiraśchedodbhavaṃ tadvaddānavaṃ khaḍgapāṇinam
hṛdi śūlena nirbhinnaṃ niryadantravibhūṣitam || 62 ||
raktaraktīkṛtāṅgaṃ ca raktavisphāritekṣaṇam
veṣṭitaṃ nāgapāśena bhrukuṭībhīṣaṇānanam || 63 ||
sapāśavāmahastena dhṛtakeśaṃ ca durgayā
vamadrudhiravaktraṃ ca devyāḥ siṃhaṃ pradarśayet || 64 ||
devyāstu dakṣiṇaṃ pādaṃ samaṃ siṃhopari sthitam
kiṃcidūrdhvaṃ tathā vāmamaṅguṣṭhaṃ mahiṣopari || 65 ||
stūyamānaṃ ca tadūpamamaraiḥ saṃniveśayet
idānīṃ surarājasya rūpaṃ vakṣye viśeṣataḥ || 66 ||
sahasranayanaṃ devaṃ mattavāraṇasaṃsthitam
pṛthūruvakṣovadanaṃ siṃhaskandhaṃ mahābhujam || 67 ||
kirīṭakuṇḍaladharaṃ pīvarorubhujekṣaṇam
vajrotpaladharaṃ tadvannānābharaṇabhūṣitam || 68 ||
pūjitaṃ devagandharvairapsarogaṇasevitam
chatracāmaradhāriṇyaḥ striyaḥ pārśve pradarśayet || 69 ||
siṃhāsanagataṃ cāpi gandharvagaṇasaṃyutam
indrā ṇīṃ vāmataścāsya kuryādutpaladhāriṇīm || 70 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe pratimālakṣaṇe ṣaṣṭyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 260 ||

<261>

athaikaṣaṣṭyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
sūta uvāca
prabhākarasya pratimāmidānīṃ śṛṇuta dvijāḥ
rathasthaṃ kārayeddevaṃ padmahastaṃ sulocanam || 1 ||
saptāścaṃ caikacakraṃ ca rathaṃ tasya prakalpayet
mukuṭena vicitreṇa padmagarbhasamaprabham || 2 ||
nānābharaṇabhūṣābhyāṃ bhujābhyāṃ dhṛtapuṣkaram
skandhasthe puṣkare te tu līlayaiva dhṛte sadā || 3 ||
colakacchannavapuṣaṃ kvaciccitreṣu darśayet
vastrayugmasamopetaṃ caraṇau tejasā''vatau || 4 ||
pratīhārau ca kartavyau pārśvayordaṇḍipiṅgalau
kartavyau khaḍgahastau tau pārśvayoḥ puruṣāvubhau || 5 ||
lekhanīkṛtahastaṃ ca pārśve dhātāramavyayam
nānādevagaṇairyuktamevaṃ kuryāddivākaram || 6 ||
aruṇaḥ sārathiścāsya padminīpatrasaṃnibhaḥ
aśvau suvalayagrīvāvantasthau tasya pārśvayoḥ || 7 ||
bhujaṅgarajjubhirbaddhāḥ saptāśvā raśmisaṃyutāḥ
padmasthaṃ vāhanastha vā padmahastaṃ prakalpayet || 8 ||
vahnestu lakṣaṇaṃ vakṣye sarvakāmaphalapradam
dīptaṃ suvarṇavapuṣamardhacandrā sane sthitam || 9 ||
bālārkasadṛśaṃ tasya vadanaṃ cāpi darśayet
yajñopavītinaṃ devaṃ lambakūrcadharaṃ tathā || 10 ||
kamaṇḍaluṃ vāmakare dakṣiṇe tvakṣasūtrakam
jvālāvitānasaṃyuktamajavāhanamujjvalam || 11 ||
kuṇḍasthaṃ vā'pi kurvīta mūrdhni saptaśikhānvitam
tathā yamaṃ pravakṣyāmi daṇḍapāśadharaṃ vibhum || 12 ||
mahāmahiṣamārūḍhaṃ kṛṣṇāñjanacayopamam
siṃhāsanagataṃ cāpi dīptāgnisamalocanam || 13 ||
mahiṣaścitraguptasya karālāḥ kiṃkarāstathā
samantāddarśayettasya saumyāsaumyānsurāsurān || 14 ||
rākṣasendraṃ tathā vakṣye lokapālaṃ ca nairṛtam
narārūḍhaṃ mahākāyaṃ rakṣobhirbahubhirvṛtam || 15 ||
khaḍgahastaṃ mahānīlaṃ kajjalācalasaṃnibham
narayuktavimānasthaṃ pītābharaṇabhūṣitam || 16 ||
varuṇaṃ ca pravakṣyāmi pāśahastaṃ mahābalam
śaṅkhasphaṭikavarṇābhaṃ sitahārāmbarāvṛtam || 17 ||
jhaṣāsanagataṃ śāntaṃ kirīṭāṅgadadhāriṇam
vāyurūpaṃ pravakṣyāmi dhūmraṃ tu mṛgavāhanam || 18 ||
citrāmbaradharaṃ śāntaṃ yuvānaṃ kuñcitabhruvam
mṛgādhirūḍhaṃ varadaṃ patākādhvajasaṃyutam || 19 ||
kuberaṃ ca pravakṣyāmi kuṇḍalābhyāmalaṃkṛtam
mahodaraṃ mahākāyaṃ nidhyaṣṭakasamanvitam || 20 ||
guhyakairbahibhiryuktaṃ dhanavyagrakaraistathā
hārakeyūraracitaṃ sitāmbaradharaṃ sadā || 21 ||
gadādharaṃ ca kartavyaṃ varadaṃ mukuṭānvitam
narayuktavimānasthamevaṃ rītyā ca kārayet || 22 ||
tathaiveśaṃ pravakṣyāmi dhavalaṃ dhavalekṣaṇam
triśūlapāṇinaṃ devaṃ tryakṣaṃ vṛṣagataṃ prabhum || 23 ||
mātṝṇāṃ lakṣaṇaṃ vakṣye yathāvadanupūrvaśaḥ
brahmāṇī brahmasadṛśī caturvaktrā caturbhujā || 24 ||
haṃsādhirūḍhā kartavyā sākṣasūtrakamaṇḍaluḥ
maheśvarasya rūpeṇa tathā māheśvarī matā || 25 ||
jaṭāmukuṭasaṃyuktā vṛṣasthā candra śekharā
kapālaśūlakhaṭvāṅgavaradāḍyacaturbhujā || 26 ||
kumārarūpā kaumārī mayūravaravāhanā
raktavastradharā tadvacchūlaśaktidharā matā || 27 ||
hārakeyūrasaṃpannā kṛkavākudharā tathā
vaiṣṇavī viṣṇusadṛśī garuḍe samupasthitā || 28 ||
caturbāhuśca varadā śaṅkhacakragadādharā
siṃhāsanagatā vā'pi bālakena samanvitā || 29 ||
vārāhīṃ ca pravakṣyāmi mahiṣopari saṃsthitām
varāhasadṛśī devī śiraścāmaradhāriṇī || 30 ||
gadācakradharā tadvaddānavendra vināśinī
indrā ṇīmindra sadṛśīṃ vajraśūlagadādharām || 31 ||
gajāsanagatāṃ devīṃ locanairbahubhirvṛtām
taptakāñcanavarṇābhāṃ divyābharaṇabhūṣitām || 32 ||
tīkṣṇakhaḍgadharāṃ tadvadvakṣye yogeśvarīmimām
dīrghajihvāmūrdhvakeśīmasthikhaṇḍaiśca maṇḍitām || 33 ||
daṃṣṭrākarālavadanāṃ kuryāccaiva kṛśodarīm
kapālamālinīṃ devīṃ muṇḍamālāvibhūṣitām || 34 ||
kapālaṃ vāmahaste tu māṃsaśoṇitapūritam
mastiṣkāktaṃ ca bibhrāṇāṃ śaktikāṃ dakṣiṇe kare || 35 ||
gṛdhrasthā vāyasasthā vā nirmāṃsā vinatodarī
karālavadanā tadvatkartavyā sā trilocanā || 36 ||
cāmuṇḍā baddhaghaṇṭā vā dvīpicarmadharā śubhā
digvāsāḥ kālikā tadvadrā sabhasthā kapālinī || 37 ||
suraktapuṣpābharaṇā vardhanīdhvajasaṃyutā
vināyakaṃ ca kurvīta mātṝṇāmantike sadā || 38 ||
vīreśvaraśca bhagavānvṛṣārūḍho jaṭādharaḥ
vīṇāhastastriśūlī ca mātṝṇāmagrato bhavet || 39 ||
śriyaṃ devīṃ pravakṣyāmi nave vayasi saṃsthitām
suyauvanāṃ pīnagaṇḍāṃ raktauṣṭhīṃ kuñcitabhruvam || 40 ||
pīnānnatastanataṭāṃ maṇikuṇḍaladhāriṇīm
sumaṇḍalaṃ mukhaṃ tasyāḥ śiraḥ sīmantabhūṣaṇam || 41 ||
padmasvastikaśaṅkhairvā bhūṣitāṃ kuṇḍalālakaiḥ
kañcukābaddhagātrī ca hārabhūṣau payodharau || 42 ||
nāgahastopamau bāhū keyūrakaṭakojjvalau
padmaṃ haste pradātavyaṃ śrīphalaṃ dakṣiṇe bhuje || 43 ||
mekhalābharaṇāṃ tadvattaptakāñcanasaprabhām
nānābharaṇasaṃpannāṃ śobhanāmbaradhāriṇīm || 44 ||
pārśve tasyāḥ striyaḥ kāryāścāmaravyagrapāṇayaḥ
padmāsanopaviṣṭā tu padmasiṃhāsanasthitā || 45 ||
karibhyāṃ snāpyamānā'sau bhṛṅgārābhyāmanekaśaḥ
prakṣālayantau kariṇau bhṛṅgārābhyāṃ tathā'parau || 46 ||
stūyamānā ca lokeśaistathā gandharvaguhyakaiḥ
tathaiva yakṣiṇī kāryā siddhāsuraniṣevitā || 47 ||
pārśvayoḥ kalaśau tasyāstoraṇe devadānavāḥ
nāgāścaiva tu kartavyāḥ khaḍgakheṭakadhāriṇaḥ || 48 ||
adhastātprakṛtisteṣāṃ nābherūrdhvaṃ tu pauruṣī
phaṇāśca mūrdhni kartavyā dvijihvā bahavaḥ samāḥ || 49 ||
piśācā rākṣasāścaiva bhūtavetālajātayaḥ
nirmāṃsāścaiva te sarve raudrā vikṛtarūpiṇaḥ || 50 ||
kṣetrapālaśca kartavyo jaṭilo vikṛtānanaḥ
digvāsā jaṭilastadvacchvagomāyuniṣevitaḥ || 51 ||
kapālaṃ vāmahaste tu śiraḥ keśasamāvṛtam
dakṣiṇe śastrikāṃ dadyādasurakṣayakāriṇīm || 52 ||
athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi dvibhujaṃ kusumāyudham
pārśve cāśvamukhaṃ tasya makaradhvajasaṃyutam || 53 ||
dakṣiṇe puṣpabāṇaṃ ca vāme puṣpamayaṃ dhanuḥ
prītiḥ syāddakṣiṇe tasya bhojanopaskarānvitā || 54 ||
ratiṃśca vāmapārśve tu śayanaṃ sārasānvitam
paṭaśca paṭahaścaiva kharaḥ kāmāturastathā || 55 ||
pārśvato jalavāpī ca vanaṃ nandanameva ca
suśobhanaśca kartavyo bhagavānkusumāyudhaḥ || 56 ||
saṃsthānamīṣadvakraṃ syādvismayasmitavaktrakam
etaduddeśataḥ proktaṃ pratimālakṣaṇaṃ mayā
vistareṇa na śaknoti bṛhaspatirapi dvijāḥ || 57 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe devatārcānukīrtane pratimālakṣaṇaṃ nāmaikaṣaṣṭyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 261 ||

<262>

atha dviṣaṣṭyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
sūta uvāca
pīṭhikālakṣaṇaṃ vakṣye yathāvadanupūrvaśaḥ
pīṭhocchrāyaṃ yathāvacca bhāgānṣoḍaśa kārayet || 1 ||
bhūmāvekaḥ praviṣṭaḥ syāccaturbhirjagatī matā
vṛtto bhāgastathaikaḥ syādvṛtaḥ paṭalabhāgataḥ || 2 ||
bhāgaistribhistathā kaṇṭhaḥ kaṇṭhapaṭṭastu bhāgataḥ
bhāgābhyāmūrdhvapaṭṭaśca śeṣabhāgena paṭṭikā || 3 ||
praviṣṭaṃ bhāgamekaikaṃ jagatīṃ yāvadeva tu
nirgamastu punastasya yāvadvai śeṣapaṭṭikā || 4 ||
vārinirgamanārthaṃ tu tatra kāryaḥ praṇālakaḥ
pīṭhikānāṃ tu sarvāsāmetatsāmānyalakṣaṇam || 5 ||
viśeṣāndevatābhedāñchṛṇudhvaṃ dvijasattamāḥ
sthaṇḍilā vā'tha vāpī vā yakṣī vedī ca maṇḍalā || 6 ||
pūrṇacandrā ca vajrā ca padmā vārdhaśaśī tathā
trikoṇā daśamī tāsāṃ saṃsthānaṃ vā nibodhata || 7 ||
sthaṇḍilā caturasrā tu varjitā mekhalādibhiḥ
vāpī dvimekhalā jñeyā yakṣī caiva trimekhalā || 8 ||
caturasrāyatā vedī na tāṃ liṅgeṣu yojayet
maṇḍalā vartulā yā tu mekhalābhirgaṇapriyā || 9 ||
raktā dvimekhalā madhye pūrṇacandrā tu sā bhavet
mekhalātrayasaṃyuktā ṣaḍasrā vajrikā bhavet || 10 ||
ṣoḍaśāsrā bhavetpadmā kiṃciddhrasvā tu mūlataḥ
tathaiva dhanuṣākārā sārdhacandrā praśasyate || 11 ||
triśūlasadṛśī tadvattrikoṇā hyūrdhvato matā
prāgudakpravaṇā tadvatpraśastā lakṣaṇānvitā || 12 ||
pariveṣaṃ tribhāgena nirgamaṃ tatra kārayet
vistāraṃ tatpramāṇaṃ ca mūle cāgre tathordhvataḥ || 13 ||
jalamārgaśca kartavyastribhāgena suśobhanaḥ
liṅgasyārdhavibhāgena sthaulyena samadhiṣṭhitā || 14 ||
mekhalā tattribhāgena khātaṃ caiva pramāṇataḥ
athavā pādahīnaṃ tu śobhanaṃ kārayetsadā || 15 ||
uttarasthaṃ praṇālaṃ ca pramāṇādadhikaṃ bhavet
sthaṇḍilāyāmathā''rogyaṃ dhanaṃ dhānyaṃ ca puṣkalam || 16 ||
gopradā ca bhavedyakṣī vedī saṃpatpradā bhavet
maṇḍalāyāṃ bhavetkīrtirvaradā pūrṇacandri kā || 17 ||
āyuṣpradā bhavevajrā padmā saubhāgyadā bhavet
putrapradā'rdhacandrā syāttrikoṇā śatrunāśinī || 18 ||
devasya yajanārthaṃ tu pīṭhikā daśa kīrtitāḥ
śaile śailamayīṃ dadyātpārthive pārthivīṃ tathā || 19 ||
dāruje dārujāṃ kuryānmiśre miśrāṃ tathaiva ca
nānyayonistu kartavyā sadā śubhaphalepsubhiḥ || 20 ||
acāyāmasamaṃ dairghyaṃ liṅgāyāmasamaṃ tathā
yasya devasya yā patnī tāṃ pīṭhe parikalpayet
etatsarvaṃ samākhyātaṃ samāsātpīṭhalakṣaṇam || 21 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe devatārcānukīrtane pīṭhikānukīrtanaṃ nāma dviṣaṣṭyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 262 ||

<263>

atha triṣaṣṭyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
sūta uvāca
athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi liṅgalakṣaṇamuttamam
susnigdhaṃ ca suvarṇaṃ ca liṅgaṃ kuryādvicakṣaṇaḥ || 1 ||
prāsādasya pramāṇena liṅgamānaṃ vidhīyate
liṅgamānena vā vidyātprāsādaṃ śubhalakṣaṇam || 2 ||
caturasre same garte brahmasūtraṃ nipātayet
vāmena brahmasūtrasya arcāyā liṅgameva ca || 3 ||
prāguttareṇa līnaṃ tu dakṣiṇāparamāśritam
purasyāparadigbhāge pūrvadvāraṃ prakalpayet || 4 ||
pūrveṇa cāparaṃ dvāraṃ māhendraṃ dakṣiṇottaram
dvāraṃ vibhajya pūrvaṃ tu ekaviṃśatibhāgikam || 5 ||
tato madhyagataṃ jñātvā brahmasūtraṃ prakalpayet
tasyārdhaṃ tu tridhā kṛtvā bhāgaṃ cottaratastyajet || 6 ||
evaṃ dakṣiṇatastyaktvā brahmasthānaṃ prakalpayet
bhāgārdhena tu yalliṅgaṃ kāryaṃ tadiha śasyate || 7 ||
pañcabhāgavibhakteṣu tribhāgo jyeṣṭha ucyate
bhājite navadhā garbhe madhyamaṃ pāñcabhāgikam || 8 ||
ekasminneva navadhā garbhe liṅgāni kārayet
samasūtraṃ vibhajyātha navadhā garbhabhājitam || 9 ||
jyeṣṭhamardhaṃ kanīyo'rdhaṃ tathā madhyamamadhyamam
evaṃ garbhaḥ samākhyātastribhirbhāgairvibhājayet || 10 ||
jyeṣṭhaṃ tu trividhaṃ jñeyaṃ madhyamaṃ trividhaṃ tathā
kanīyastrividhaṃ tadvalliṅgabhedā navaiva tu || 11 ||
nābhyardhamaṣṭabhāgena vibhajyātha samaṃ budhaiḥ
bhāgatrayaṃ parityajya viṣkambhaṃ caturasrakam || 12 ||
aṣṭāsraṃ madhyabhaṃ jñeyaṃ bhāgaṃ liṅgasya vai dhruvam
vikīrṇe cettato gṛhya koṇābhyāṃ lāñchayedbudhaḥ || 13 ||
aṣṭāsraṃ kārayettadvadūrdhvamapyevameva tu
ṣoḍaśāsrīkṛtaṃ paścādvartulaṃ kārayettataḥ || 14 ||
āyāmaṃ tasya devasya nābhyāṃ vai kuṇḍalīkṛtam
māheśvaraṃ tribhāgaṃ tu ūrdhvavṛttaṃ tvavasthitam || 15 ||
adhastādbrahmabhāgastu caturasro vidhīyate
aṣṭāsro veṣṇavo bhāgo madhyastasya udāhṛtaḥ || 16 ||
evaṃ pramāṇasaṃyuktaṃ liṅgaṃ vṛddhipradaṃ bhavet
tathā'nyadapi vakṣyāmi garbhamānaṃ pramāṇataḥ || 17 ||
garbhamānapramāṇena yalliṅgamucitaṃ bhavet
caturdhā tadvibhajyātha viṣkambhaṃ tu prakalpayet || 18 ||
devatāyatanaṃ sūtraṃ bhāgatrayavikalpitam
adhastāccaturasraṃ tu aṣṭāsraṃ madhyabhāgataḥ || 19 ||
pūjyabhāgastato'rdhaṃ tu nābhibhāgastathocyate
āyāme yadbhavetsūtraṃ nāhasya caturasrake || 20 ||
caturasraṃ parityajya aṣṭāsrasya tu yadbhavet
tasyāpyardhaṃ parityajya tato vṛttaṃ tu kārayet || 21 ||
śiraḥ pradakṣiṇaṃ tasya saṃkṣiptaṃ mūlato nyaset
bhraṣṭapūjaṃ bhavelliṅgamadhastādvipulaṃ ca yat || 22 ||
śirasā ca sadā nimnaṃ manojñaṃ lakṣaṇānvitam
saumyaṃ tu dṛśyate yattu liṅgaṃ tadvṛddhidaṃ bhavet || 23 ||
atha mūle ca madhye tu pramāṇaṃ sarvataḥ samam
evaṃvidhaṃ tu yalliṅgaṃ bhavettatsārvakāmikam || 24 ||
anyathā yadbhavelliṅgaṃ tadasatsaṃpracakṣate
evaṃ ratnamayaṃ kuryātsphāṭikaṃ pārthivaṃ tathā
śubhaṃ dārumayaṃ cāpi yadvā manasi rocate || 25 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe devatārcānukīrtanaṃ nāma triṣaṣṭyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 263 ||

<264>

atha catuḥṣaṣṭyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
ṛṣaya ūcuḥ
devatānāmathaitāsāṃ pratiṣṭhāvidhimuttamam
vada sūta yathānyāyaṃ sarveṣāmapyaśeṣataḥ || 1 ||
sūta uvāca
athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi pratiṣṭhāvidhimuttamam
kuṇḍamaṇḍapavedīnāṃ pramāṇaṃ ca yathākramam || 2 ||
caitre vā phālgune vā'pi jyeṣṭhe vā mādhave tathā
māghe vā sarvadevānāṃ pratiṣṭhā śubhadā bhavet || 3 ||
prāpya pakṣaṃ śubhaṃ śuklamatīte dakṣiṇāyane
pañcamī ca dvitīyā ca tṛtīyā saptamī tathā || 4 ||
daśamī paurṇamāsī ca tathā śreṣṭhā trayodaśī
āsu pratiṣṭhā vidhivatkṛtā bahuphalā bhavet || 5 ||
āṣāḍhe dve tathā mūlamuttarādvayameva ca
jyeṣṭhāśravaṇarohiṇyaḥ pūrvābhādra padā tathā || 6 ||
hastāśvinī revatī ca puṣyo mṛgaśirastathā
anurādhā tathā svātī pratiṣṭhādiṣu śasyate || 7 ||
budho bṛhaspatiḥ śukrastrayo'pyete śubhagrahāḥ
ebhirnirīkṣitaṃ lagnaṃ nakṣatraṃ ca praśasyate || 8 ||
grahatārābalaṃ labdhvā grahapūjāṃ vidhāya ca
nimittaśakunaṃ labdhvā varjayitvā'dbhutādikam || 9 ||
śubhayoge śubhasthāne krūragrahavivarjite
lagna ṛkṣe prakurvīta pratiṣṭhādikamuttamam || 10 ||
ayane viṣuve tadvatṣaḍaśītimukhe tathā
eteṣu sthāpanaṃ kāryaṃ vidhidṛṣṭena karmaṇā || 11 ||
prājāpatye tu śayanaṃ śvete tūtthāpanaṃ tathā
muhūrte sthāpanaṃ kuryātpunarbrāhme vicakṣaṇaḥ || 12 ||
prāsādasyottare vā'pi pūrve vā maṇḍapo bhavet
hastānṣoḍaśa kurvīta daśa dvādaśa vā punaḥ || 13 ||
madhye vedikayā yuktaḥ parikṣiptaḥ samantataḥ
pañca saptāpi caturaḥ karānkurvīta vedikān || 14 ||
caturbhistoraṇairyukto maṇḍapaḥ syāccaturmukhaḥ
plakṣadvāraṃ bhavetpūrvaṃ yāmye caudumbaraṃ bhavet || 15 ||
paścādaśvatthaghaṭitaṃ naiyagrodhaṃ tathottare
bhūmau hastapraviṣṭāni caturhastāni cocchraye || 16 ||
sūpaliptaṃ tathā ślakṣṇaṃ bhūtalaṃ syātsuśobhanam
vastrairnānāvidhaistadvatpuṣpapallavaśobhitam || 17 ||
kṛtvaivaṃ maṇḍapaṃ pūrvaṃ caturdvāreṣu vinyaset
avraṇānkalaśānaṣṭau jvalatkāñcanagarbhitān || 18 ||
cūtapallavasaṃchannānsitavastrayugānvitān
sarvauṣadhiphalopetāṃścandanodakapūritān || 19 ||
evaṃ niveśya tadgarbhe gandhadhūpārcanādibhiḥ
dhvajādirohaṇaṃ kāryaṃ maṇḍapasya samantataḥ || 20 ||
dhvajāṃśca lokapālānāṃ sarvadikṣu niveśayet
