patākā jaladākārā madhye syānmaṇḍapasya ta || 21 ||
gandhadhūpādikaṃ kuryātsvaiḥ svairmantrairanukramāt
baliṃ ca lokapālebhyaḥ svamantreṇa nivedayet || 22 ||
ūrdhvaṃ tu brahmaṇe deyaṃ tvadhastāccheṣavāsukeḥ
saṃhitāyāṃ tu ye mantrāstadvaivatyāḥ śubhāḥ smṛtāḥ || 23 ||
taiḥ pūjā lokapālānāṃ kartavyā ca samantataḥ
trirātramekarātraṃ vā pañcarātramathāpi vā || 24 ||
athavā saptarātraṃ tu kāryaṃ syādadhivāsanam
evaṃ satoraṇaṃ kṛtvā adhivāsanamuttamam || 25 ||
tasyāpyuttarataḥ kuryātsnānamaṇḍapamuttamam
tadardhena tribhāgena caturbhāgena vā punaḥ || 26 ||
ānīya liṅgamarcāṃ vā śilpinaḥ pūjayedbudhaḥ
vastrābharaṇaratnaiśca ye'pi tatparicārakāḥ || 27 ||
kṣamadhvamiti tānbrūyādyajamāno'pyataḥ param
devaṃ prastaraṇe kṛtvā netrajyotiḥ prakalpayet || 28 ||
akṣṇoruddharaṇaṃ vakṣye liṅgasyāpi samāsataḥ
sarvatastu baliṃ dadyātsiddhārthaghṛtapāyasaiḥ || 29 ||
śuklapuṣpairalaṃkṛtya ghṛtagugguladhūpitam
viprāṇāṃ vācanaṃ kuryāddadyācchaktyā ca dakṣiṇām || 30 ||
gāṃ mahīṃ kanakaṃ caiva sthāpakāya nivedayet
lakṣaṇaṃ kārayedbhaktyā mantreṇānena vai dvijaḥ || 31 ||
oṃnamo bhagavate tubhyaṃ śivāya paramātmane
hiraṇyaretase viṣṇo viśvarūpāya te namaḥ || 32 ||
mantro'yaṃ sarvadevānāṃ netrajyotiḥṣvapi smṛtaḥ
evamāmantrya deveśaṃ kāñcanena vilekhayet || 33 ||
maṅgalyāni ca vādyāni brahmaghoṣaṃ sagītakam
vṛddhyarthaṃ kārayedvidvānamaṅgalyavināśanam || 34 ||
lakṣaṇoddharanaṃ vakṣye liṅgasya susamāhitaḥ
tridhā vibhajya pūjyāyāṃ lakṣaṇaṃ syādvibhājakam || 35 ||
lekhātrayaṃ tu kartavyaṃ yavāṣṭāntarasaṃyutam
na sthūlaṃ na kṛśaṃ tadvanna vakraṃ chedavarjitam || 36 ||
nimnaṃ yavapramāṇena jyeṣṭhaliṅgasya kārayet
sūkṣmāstatastu kartavyā yathā madhyamake nyaset || 37 ||
aṣṭabhaktaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā tyaktvā bhāgatrayaṃ budhaḥ
lambayetsapta rekhāstu pārśvayorubhayoḥ samāḥ || 38 ||
tāvatpralambayedvidvānyāvadbhāgacatuṣṭayam
bhrāmyate pañcabhāgādhvaṃ kārayetsaṃgamaṃ tataḥ || 39 ||
rekhayoḥ saṃgame tadvatpṛṣṭhe bhāgadvayaṃ bhavet
evametatsamākhyātaṃ samāsāllakṣaṇaṃ mayā || 40 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe pratiṣṭhānukīrtanaṃ nāma catuḥṣaṣṭyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 264 ||

<265>

atha pañcaṣaṣṭyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
sūta uvāca
ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi mūrtipānāṃ tu lakṣaṇam
sthāpakasya samāsena lakṣaṇaṃ śṛṇuta dvijāḥ || 1 ||
sarvāvayavasaṃpūrṇo vedamantraviśāradaḥ
purāṇavettā tattvajño dambhalobhavivarjitaḥ || 2 ||
kṛṣṇasāramaye deśe ya utpannaḥ śubhākṛtiḥ
śaucācāraparo nityaṃ pāṣaṇḍakulaniḥspṛhaḥ || 3 ||
samaḥ śatrau ca mitre ca brahmopendra harapriyaḥ
ūhāpohārthatattvajño vāstuśāstrasya pāragaḥ || 4 ||
ācāryastu bhavennityaṃ sarvadoṣavivarjitaḥ
mūrtipāstu dvijāścaiva kulīnā ṛjavastathā || 5 ||
dvātriṃśatṣoḍaśāthāpi aṣṭau vā śrutipāragāḥ
jyeṣṭhamadhyakaniṣṭheṣu mūrtipā vaḥ prakīrtitāḥ || 6 ||
tato liṅgamathārcāṃ vā nītvā snapanamaṇḍapam
gītamaṅgalaśabdena snapanaṃ tatra kārayet || 7 ||
pañcagavyakaṣāyeṇa mṛdbhirbhasmodakena ca
śaucaṃ tatra prakurvīta vedamantracatuṣṭayāt || 8 ||
samudra jyeṣṭhamantreṇa āpo divyeti cāparaḥ
yāsāṃ rājeti mantrastu āpo hi ṣṭheti cāparaḥ || 9 ||
evaṃ snāpya tato devaṃ pūjya gandhānulepanaiḥ
pracchādya vastrayugmena abhivastretyudāhṛtam || 10 ||
utthāpayettato devamuttiṣṭha brahmaṇaspate
āmūrajeti ca tathā rathe tiṣṭheti cāparaḥ || 11 ||
rathe brahmarathe vā'pi dhṛtāṃ śilpigaṇena tu
āropya cā'nayedvidvānākṛṣṇena praveśayet || 12 ||
tataḥ prāstīrya śayyāyāṃ sthāpayecchanakairbudhaḥ
kuśānāstīrya puṣpāṇi sthāpayetprāṅmukhaṃ tataḥ || 13 ||
tatastu nidrā kalaśaṃ vastrakāñcanasaṃyutam
śirobhāge tu devasya japannevaṃ nidhāpayet || 14 ||
āpo devīti mantreṇa āpo'smānmātaro'pi ca
tato dukūlapaṭṭaiśca cchādya netropadhānakam || 15 ||
dadyācchirasi devasya kauśeyaṃ vā vicakṣaṇaḥ
madhunā sarpiṣā'bhyajya pūjya siddhārthakaistataḥ || 16 ||
āpyāyasveti mantreṇa yā te rudra śiveti ca
upaviśyārcayeddevaṃ gandhapuṣpaiḥ samantataḥ || 17 ||
sitaṃ pratisaraṃ dadyādbārhaspatyeti mantrataḥ
dukūlapaṭṭaiḥ kārpāsairnānācitrairathāpi vā || 18 ||
ācchādya devaṃ sarvatra cchatracāmaradarpaṇam
pārśvataḥ sthāpayettatra vitānaṃ puṣpasaṃyutam || 19 ||
ratnānyoṣadhayastatra gṛhopakaraṇāni ca
bhājanāni vicitrāṇi śayanānyāsanāni ca || 20 ||
abhi tvā śūra mantreṇa yathā vibhavato nyaset
kṣīraṃ kṣaudraṃ ghṛtaṃ tadvadbhakṣyabhojyānnapāyasaiḥ || 21 ||
ṣaḍvidhaiśca rasaistadvatsamantātparipūjayet
baliṃ dadyātprayatnena mantreṇānena bhūriśaḥ || 22 ||
tryambakaṃ yajāmaha iti sarvataḥ śanakairbhuvi
mūrtipānsthāpayetpaścātsarvadikṣu vicakṣaṇaḥ || 23 ||
caturo dvārapālāṃśca dvāreṣu viniveśayet
śrīsūktaṃ pāvamānaṃ ca somasūktaṃ sumaṅgalam || 24 ||
tathā ca śāntikādhyāyamindra sūktaṃ tathaiva ca
rakṣoghnaṃ ca tathā sūktaṃ pūrvato bahvṛco japet || 25 ||
raudraṃ puruṣasūktaṃ ca ślokādhyāyaṃ saśukriyam
tathaiva maṇḍalādhyāyamadhvaryurdakṣiṇe japet || 26 ||
vāmadevyaṃ bṛhatsāma jyeṣṭhasāma rathaṃtaram
tathā puruṣasūktaṃ ca rudra sūktaṃ saśāntikam || 27 ||
bhāruṇḍāni ca sāmāni cchandogaḥ paścime japet
atharvāṅgirasaṃ tadvannīlaṃ raudraṃ tathaiva ca || 28 ||
tathā'parājitādevīsaptasūktaṃ saraudra kam
tathaiva śāntikādhyāyamatharvā cottare japet || 29 ||
śiraḥsthāne tu devasya sthāpako homamācaret
śāntikaiḥ pauṣṭikaistadvanmantrairvyāhṛtipūrvakaiḥ || 30 ||
palāśodumbarāśvatthā apāmārgaḥ śamī tathā
hutvā sahasramekaikaṃ devaṃ pāde tu saṃspṛśet || 31 ||
tato homasahasreṇa hutvā hutvā tatastataḥ
nābhimadhyaṃ tathā vakṣaḥ śiraścāpyālabhetpunaḥ || 32 ||
hastamātreṣu kuṇḍeṣu mūrtipāḥ sarvatodiśam
samekhaleṣu te kuryuryonivaktreṣu cā''darāt || 33 ||
vitastimātrā yoniḥ syādgajoṣṭhasadṛśī tathā
āyatā chidra saṃyuktā pārśvataḥ kalayocchritā || 34 ||
kuṇḍātkalānusāreṇa sarvataścaturaṅgulā
vistāreṇocchrayāttadvaccaturasrā samā bhavet || 35 ||
vedibhittiṃ parityajya trayodaśabhiraṅgulaiḥ
evaṃ navasu kuṇḍeṣu lakṣaṇaṃ caiva dṛśyate || 36 ||
āgneyaśākrayāmyeṣu hotavyamudagānanaiḥ
śāntaye lokapālebhyo mūrtibhyaḥ kramaśastathā || 37 ||
tathā mūrtyadhidevānāṃ homaṃ kuryātsamāhitaḥ
vasudhā vasuretāśca yajamāno divākaraḥ || 38 ||
jalaṃ vāyustathā soma ākāśaścāṣṭamaḥ smṛtaḥ
devasya mūrtayastvaṣṭāvetāḥ kuṇḍeṣu saṃsmaret || 39 ||
etāsāmadhipānvakṣye pavitrānmūrtināmataḥ
pṛthvīṃ pāti ca śarvaśca paśupaścāgnimeva ca || 40 ||
yajamānaṃ tathaivogro rudra ścā''dityameva ca
bhavo jalaṃ sadā pāti vāyumīśāna eva ca || 41 ||
mahādevastathā candraṃ bhīmaścā''kāśameva ca
sarvadevapratiṣṭhāsu mūrtipā hyeta eva ca || 42 ||
etebhyo vaidikaimantrairyathāsvaṃ homamācaret
tathā śāntighaṭaṃ kuryātpratikuṇḍeṣu sarvataḥ || 43 ||
śatānte vā sahasrānte saṃpūrṇāhutiriṣyate
samapādaḥ pṛthivyāṃ tu praśāntātmā vinikṣipet || 44 ||
āhutīnāṃ tu saṃpātaṃ pūrṇakumbheṣu vai nyaset
mūlamadhyottamāṅgeṣu devaṃ tenāvasecayet || 45 ||
sthitaṃ ca snāpayettena saṃpātāhutivāriṇā
pratiyāmeṣu dhūpaṃ tu naivedyaṃ candanādikam || 46 ||
punaḥ punaḥ prakurvīta homaḥ kāryaḥ punaḥ punaḥ
punaḥpunaśca dātavyā yajamānena dakṣiṇā || 47 ||
sitavastraiśca te sarve pūjanīyāḥ samantataḥ
vicitrairhemakaṭakairhemasūtrāṅgulīyakaiḥ || 48 ||
vāsobhiḥ śayanīyaiśca pratiyāme ca śaktitaḥ
bhojanaṃ cāpi dātavyaṃ yāvatsyādadhivāsanam || 49 ||
balistrisaṃdhyaṃ dātavyo bhūtebhyaḥ sarvatodiśam
brāhmaṇānbhojayetpūrvaṃ śeṣānvarṇāṃstu kāmataḥ || 50 ||
rātrau mahotsavaḥ kāryo nṛtyagītakamaṅgalaiḥ
sadā pūjyāḥ prayatnena caturthīkarmayāvatā || 51 ||
trirātramekarātraṃ vā pañcarātramathāpi vā
saptarātramatho kuryātkvacitsadyo'dhivāsanam
sarvayajñaphalo yasmādadhivāsotsavaḥ sadā || 52 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe'dhivāsanavidhirnāma pañcaṣaṣṭyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 265 ||

<266>

atha ṣaṭṣaṣṭyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
sūta uvāca
kṛtvā'dhivāsaṃ devānāṃ śubhaṃ kuryātsamāhitaḥ
prāsādasyānurūpeṇa mānaṃ liṅgasya vā punaḥ || 1 ||
puṣpodakena prāsādaṃ prokṣya mantrayutena tu
pātayetpakṣasūtraṃ tu dvārasūtraṃ tathaiva ca || 2 ||
āśrayetkiṃcidīśānīṃ madhyaṃ jñātvā diśaṃ budhaḥ
īśānīmāśritaṃ devaṃ pūjayanti divaukasaḥ || 3 ||
āyurārogyaphaladamathottarasamāśritam
śubhaṃ syādaśubhaṃ proktamanyathā sthāpanaṃ budhaiḥ || 4 ||
adhaḥ kūrmaśilā proktā sadā brahmaśilādhikā
uparyavasthitā tasyā brahmabhāgādhikā śilā || 5 ||
tatastu piṇḍikā kāryā pūrvoktairmānalakṣaṇaiḥ
tataḥ prakṣālitāṃ kṛtvā pañcagavyena piṇḍikām || 6 ||
kaṣāyatoyena punarmantrayuktena sarvataḥ
devatārcāśrayaṃ mantraṃ piṇḍikāsu niyojayet || 7 ||
tata utthāpya deveśamuttiṣṭha brahmaṇeti ca
ānīya garbhabhavanaṃ pīṭhānte sthāpayetpunaḥ || 8 ||
ardhyapādyādikaṃ tatra madhuparkaṃ prayojayet
tato muhūrtaṃ viśramya ratnanyāsaṃ samācaret || 9 ||
vajramauktikavaidūryaśaṅkhasphaṭikameva ca
puṣparāgendra nīlaṃ ca nīlaṃ pūrvādidikkramāt || 10 ||
tālakaṃ ca śilāvajramañjanaṃ śyāmameva ca
kākṣī kāśī samākṣīkaṃ gairikaṃ cā''ditaḥ kramāt || 11 ||
godhūmaṃ ca yavaṃ tadvattilamudgaṃ tathaiva ca
nīvāramatha śyāmākaṃ sarṣapaṃ vrīhimeva ca || 12 ||
nyasya krameṇa pūrvādi candanaṃ raktacandanam
agaruṃ cāñjanaṃ cāpi uśīraṃ ca tataḥ param || 13 ||
vaiṣṇavīṃ sahadevīṃ ca lakṣmaṇāṃ ca tataḥ param
svarlokapālanāmnā tu nyasedaoṃkārapūrvakam || 14 ||
sarvabījāni dhātūṃśca ratnānyoṣadhayastathā
kāñcanaṃ padmarāgaṃ tu pāradaṃ padmameva ca || 15 ||
kūrmaṃ dharāṃ vṛṣaṃ tatra nyasetpūrvāditaḥ kramāt
brahmasthāne tu dātavyāḥ saṃhatāḥ syuḥ parasparam || 16 ||
kanakaṃ vidrumaṃ tāmraṃ kāṃsyaṃ caivā''rakūṭakam
rajataṃ vimalaṃ puṣpaṃ lohaṃ caiva krameṇa tu || 17 ||
kāñcanaṃ haritālaṃ ca sarvābhāve'pi nikṣipet
dadyādbījauṣādhesthāne sahadevīṃ yavānapi || 18 ||
nyāsamantrānato vakṣye lokapālātmakāniha
indra stu mahasā dīptaḥ sarvadevādhipo mahān || 19 ||
vajrahasto mahāsattvastasmai nityaṃ namo namaḥ
āgneyaḥ puruṣo raktaḥ sarvadevamayaḥ śikhī || 20 ||
dhūmaketuranādhṛṣyastasmai nityaṃ namo namaḥ
yamaścotpalavarṇābhaḥ kirīṭī daṇḍadhṛksadā || 21 ||
dharmasākṣī viśuddhātmā tasmai nityaṃ namo namaḥ
nirṛtistu pumānkṛṣṇaḥ sarvarakṣodhipo mahān || 22 ||
khaḍgahasto mahāsattvastasmai nityaṃ namo namaḥ
varuṇo dhavalo viṣṇuḥ puruṣo nimnagādhipaḥ || 23 ||
pāśahasto mahābāhustasmai nityaṃ namo namaḥ
vāyuśca sarvavarṇo vai sarvagandhavahaḥ śubhaḥ || 24 ||
puruṣo dhvajahastaśca tasmai nityaṃ namo namaḥ
gauro yaśca pumānsaumyaḥ sarvauṣadhisamanvitaḥ || 25 ||
nakṣatrādhipatiḥ somastasmai nityaṃ namo namaḥ
īśānapuruṣaḥ śuklaḥ sarvavidyādhipo mahān || 26 ||
śūlahasto virūpākṣastasmai nityaṃ namo namaḥ
padmayoniścaturmūrtirvedavāsāḥ pitāmahaḥ || 27 ||
yajñādhyakṣaścaturvaktrastasmai nityaṃ namo namaḥ
yo'sāvanantarūpeṇa brahmāṇḍaṃ sacarācaram || 28 ||
puṣpavaddhārayenmūrdhni tasmai nityaṃ namo namaḥ
oṃkārapūrvakā hyete nyāse balinivedane || 29 ||
mantrāḥ syuḥ sarvakāryāṇāṃ vṛddhiputraphalapradāḥ
nyāsaṃ kṛtvā tu mantrāṇāṃ pāyasenānulepitam || 30 ||
paṭenā''cchādayecchvabhraṃ śuklenopari yatnataḥ
tata utthāpya deveśamiṣṭadeśe tu śobhane || 31 ||
dhruvā dyauriti mantreṇa śvabhropari niveśayet
tataḥ sthirīkṛtasyāsya hastaṃ dattvā tu mastake || 32 ||
dhyātvā paramasadbhāvāddevadevaṃ ca niṣkalam
devavrataṃ tathā somaṃ rudra sūktaṃ tathaiva ca || 33 ||
ātmānamīśvaraṃ kṛtvā nānābharaṇabhūṣitam
yasya devasya yadrū paṃ taddhyāne saṃsmarettathā || 34 ||
atasīpuṣpasaṃkāśaṃ śaṅkhacakragadādharam
saṃsthāpayāmi deveśaṃ devo bhūtvā janārdanam || 35 ||
akṣaraṃ daśabāhuṃ ca candrā rdhakṛtaśekharam
gaṇeśaṃ vṛṣasaṃsthaṃ ca sthāpayāmi trilocanam || 36 ||
ṛṣibhiḥ saṃstutaṃ devaṃ caturvaktraṃ jaṭādharam
pitāmahaṃ mahābāhuṃ sthāpayāmyambujodbhavam || 37 ||
sahasrakiraṇaṃ śāntamapsarogaṇasaṃyutam
padmahastaṃ mahābāhuṃ sthāpayāmi divākaram || 38 ||
devamantrāṃstathā raudrā nrudra sya sthāpane japet
viṣṇostu vaiṣṇavāṃstadvadbrāhmānvai brahmaṇo budha || 39 ||
saurāḥ sūryasya japtavyāstathā'nyeṣu tadāśrayāḥ
vedamantrapratiṣṭhā tu yasmādānandadāyinī || 40 ||
sthāpayedyaṃ tu deveśaṃ taṃ pradhānaṃ prakalpayet
tasya pārśvasthitānanyānsaṃsmaretparivāritaḥ || 41 ||
gaṇaṃ nandimahākālaṃ vṛṣaṃ bhṛṅgiṃ riṭiṃ guham
devīṃ vināyakaṃ caiva viṣṇuṃ brahmāṇameva ca || 42 ||
rudraṃ śakraṃ jayantaṃ ca lokapālānsamantataḥ
tathaivāpsarasaḥ sarvā gandharvagaṇaguhyakām || 43 ||
yo yatra sthāpyate devastasya tānparitaḥ smaret
āvāhayettathā rudraṃ mantreṇānena yatnataḥ || 44 ||
yasya siṃhā rathe yuktā vyāghrabhūtāstathoragāḥ
ṛṣayo lokapālāśca devaḥ skandastathā vṛṣaḥ || 45 ||
priyo gaṇo mātaraśca somo viṣṇuḥ pitāmahaḥ
nāgā yakṣāḥ sagandharvā ye ca divyā nabhaścarāḥ || 46 ||
tamahaṃ tryakṣamīśānaṃ śivaṃ rudra mumāpatim
āvāhayāmi sagaṇaṃ sapatnīkaṃ vṛṣadhvajam || 47 ||
āgaccha bhagavanrudrā nugrahāya śivo bhava
śāśvato bhava pūjāṃ me gṛhāṇa tvaṃ namo namaḥ || 48 ||
oṃ namaḥ svāgataṃ bhagavate namaḥ oṃ namaḥ somāya sagaṇāya saparivārāya pratigṛhṇātu bhagavanmantrapūtamidaṃ sarvamarghyapādyamācamanīyamāsanaṃ brahmaṇā'bhihitaṃ namo namaḥ svāhā || 49 ||
tataḥ puṇyāhaghoṣeṇa brahmaghoṣaiśca puṣkalaiḥ
snāpayettu tato devaṃ dadhikṣīraghṛtena ca || 50 ||
madhuśarkarayā tadvatpuṣpagandhodakena ca
śivadhyānaikacittastu mantrānetānudīrayet || 51 ||
yajjāgrato dūramudeti tato virāḍajāyata iti ca
sahasraśīrṣā puruṣa iti ca
abhi tvā śūra nonuma iti ca
puruṣa evedaṃ sarvamiti
tripādūrdhvamiti
yenedaṃ bhūtamiti
natvā vām̐ anya iti || 52 ||
sarvāṃścaitānpratiṣṭhāsu mantrāñjaptvā punaḥpunaḥ
catuṣkṛtvaḥ spṛśedadbhirmūle madhye śirasyapi || 53 ||
sthāpite tu tato deve yajamāno'tha mūrtipam
ācāryaṃ pūjayedbhaktyā vastrālaṃkārabhūṣaṇaiḥ || 54 ||
dīnāndhakṛpaṇāṃstadvadye cānye samupasthitāḥ
tatastu madhunā devaṃ prathame'hani lepayet || 55 ||
haridra yā'tha siddhārthairdvitīye'hani tattvataḥ
candanena yavaistadvattṛtīye'hani lepayet || 56 ||
manaḥśilāpriyaṅgubhyāṃ caturthe'hani lepayet
saubhāgyaśubhadaṃ yasmāllepanaṃ vyādhināśanam || 57 ||
paraṃ prītikaraṃ nṝṇāmetadvedavido viduḥ
kṛṣṇāñjanaṃ tilaṃ tadvatpañcame'pi nivedayet || 58 ||
ṣaṣṭhe tu saghṛtaṃ dadyāccandanaṃ padmakesaram
rocanāgarupuṣpaṃ tu saptame'hani dāpayet || 59 ||
yatra sadyodhivāsaḥ syāttatra sarvaṃ nivedayet
sthitaṃ na cālayeddevamanyathā doṣabhāgbhavet || 60 ||
pūrayetsikatābhistu niśchidraṃ sarvato bhavet
lokapālasya digbhāge yasya saṃcalate vibhuḥ || 61 ||
tasya lokapateḥ śāntirdeyāścemāśca dakṣiṇāḥ
indrā ya vāraṇaṃ dadyātkāñcanaṃ cālpavittavān || 62 ||
agneḥ suvarṇameva syādyamasya mahiṣaṃ tathā
ajaṃ ca kāñcanaṃ dadyānnairṛtaṃ rākṣasaṃ prati || 63 ||
varuṇaṃ prati muktāni saśuktīni pradāpayet
rītikaṃ vāyave dadyādvastrayugmena sāṃpratam || 64 ||
somāya dhenurdātavyā rājataṃ vṛṣabhaṃ śive
yasyāṃ yasyāṃ saṃcalanaṃ śāntiḥ syāttatra tatra tu || 65 ||
anyathā tu bhavedghoraṃ bhayaṃ kulavināśanam
acalaṃ kārayettasmātsikatābhiḥ sureśvaram || 66 ||
annaṃ vastraṃ ca dātavyaṃ puṇyāhajayamaṅgalam
tripañca saptadaśa vā dināni syānmahotsavaḥ || 67 ||
caturthe'hni mahāsnānaṃ caturthīkarma kārayet
dakṣiṇā ca punastadvaddeyā tatrātibhaktitaḥ || 68 ||
devapratiṣṭhāvidhireṣa tubhyaṃ niveditaḥ pāpavināśahetoḥ
yasmādbudhaiḥ pūrvamanantamuktamanekavidyādharadevapūjyam || 69 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe pratiṣṭhānukīrtanaṃ nāma ṣaṭṣaṣṭyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 266 ||

<267>

atha saptaṣaṣṭyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
sūta uvāca
athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi devasnapanamuttamam
arghasyāpi samāsena śṛṇu tvaṃ vidhimuttamam || 1 ||
dadhyakṣatakuśāgrāṇi kṣīraṃ dūrvāstathā madhu
yavāḥ siddhārthakāstadvadaṣṭāṅgo'rghaḥ phalaiḥ saha || 2 ||
gajāśvarathyāvalmīkavarāhotkhātamaṇḍalāt
agnyagārāttathā tīrthādvrajādgomaṇḍalādapi || 3 ||
kumbhe tu mṛttikāṃ dadyāduddhṛtā'sītimantravit
śaṃ no devītyapāṃ mantramāpo hi ṣṭheti vai tathā || 4 ||
sāvitryā''dāya gomūtraṃ gandhadvāreti gomayam
āpyāyasveti ca kṣīraṃ dadhikrāvṇeti vai dadhi || 5 ||
tejo'sīti ghṛtaṃ tadvaddevasya tveti codakam
kuśamiśraṃ kṣipedvidvānpañcagavyaṃ bhavettataḥ || 6 ||
snāpyātha pañcagavyena dadhnā śuddhena vai tataḥ
dadhikāvṇetimantreṇa kartavyamabhimantraṇam || 7 ||
āpyāyasveti payasā tejo'sīti ghṛtena ca
madhuvāteti madhunā tataḥ puṣpodakena ca || 8 ||
sarasvatyai bhaiṣajyena kāryaṃ tasyābhimantraṇam
hiraṇyākṣeti mantreṇa snāpayedra tnavāriṇā || 9 ||
kuśāmbhasā tataḥ snānaṃ devasya tveti kārayet
phalodakena ca snānamagna āyāhi kārayet || 10 ||
tatastu gandhatoyena sāvitryā cābhimantrayet
tato ghaṭasahasreṇa sahasrārdhena vā punaḥ || 11 ||
tasyāpyardhena vā kuryātsapādena śatena vā
catuḥṣaṣṭyā tato'rdhena tadardhārdhena vā punaḥ || 12 ||
caturbhirathavā kuryādghaṭānāmalpavittavān
sauvarṇai rājatairvā'pi tāmrairvā rītikodbhavaiḥ || 13 ||
kāṃsyairvā pārthivairvā'pi snapanaṃ śaktito bhavet
sahadevī vacā vyāghrī balā cātibalā tathā || 14 ||
śaṅkhapuṣpī tathā siṃhī hyaṣṭamī ca suvarcalā
mahauṣadhyaṣṭakaṃ hyetanmahāsnāneṣu yojayet || 15 ||
yavagodhūmanīvāratilaśyāmākaśālayaḥ
priyaṅgavo vrīhayaśca snāneṣu parikalpitāḥ || 16 ||
svastikaṃ padmakaṃ śaṅkhamutpalaṃ kamalaṃ tathā
śrīvatsaṃ darpaṇaṃ tadvannandyāvartamathāṣṭakam || 17 ||
etāni gomayaiḥ kuryānmṛdā ca śubhayā tataḥ
pañcavarṇādikaṃ tadvatpañcavarṇaṃ rajastathā || 18 ||
pūrvāḥ kṛṣṇatilāndadyānnīrājanavidhirmataḥ
evaṃ nīrājanaṃ kṛtvā dadyādācamanaṃ budhaḥ || 19 ||
mandākinyāstu yadvāri sarvapāpāpahaṃ śubham
tato vastrayugaṃ dadyānmantreṇānena yatnataḥ || 20 ||
devasūtrasamāyukte yajñadānasamanvite
sarvavarṇe śubhe deva vāsasī te vinirmite || 21 ||
tatastu candanaṃ dadyātsamaṃ karpūrakuṅkumaiḥ
imamuccārayenmantraṃ darbhapāṇiḥ prayatnataḥ || 22 ||
śarīraṃ te na jānāmi ceṣṭāṃ naiva ca naiva ca
mayā niveditāngandhānpratigṛhya vilipyatām || 23 ||
catvāriṃśattato dīpāndadyāccaiva pradakṣiṇān
tvaṃ sūryacandra jyotīṃṣi vidyudagnistathaiva ca || 24 ||
tvameva sarvajyotīṃṣi dīpo'yaṃ pratigṛhyatām
tatastvanena mantreṇa dhūpaṃ dadyādvicakṣaṇaḥ || 25 ||
vanaspatiraso divyo gandhāḍhyo gandha uttamaḥ
mayā nivedito bhaktyā dhūpo'yaṃ pratigṛhyatām || 26 ||
tatastvābharaṇaṃ dadyānmahābhūṣāya te namaḥ
anena vidhinā kṛtvā saptarātraṃ mahotsavam || 27 ||
devakumbhaistataḥ kuryādyajamāno'bhiṣecanam
caturbhiraṣṭabhirvā'pi dvābhyāmekena vā punaḥ || 28 ||
sapañcaratnakalaśaiḥ sitavastrābhiveṣṭitaiḥ
devasya tveti mantreṇa sāmnā cā''tharvaṇena ca || 29 ||
abhiṣeke ca ye mantrā navagrahamakhe smṛtāḥ
sitāmbaradharaḥ snātvā devānsaṃpūjya yatnataḥ || 30 ||
sthāpakaṃ pūjayedbhaktyā vastrālaṃkārabhūṣaṇaiḥ
yajñabhāṇḍāni sarvāṇi maṇḍapopaskarādikam || 31 ||
yaccānyadapi tadgehe tadācāryāya dāpayet
suprasanne gurau yasmāttṛpyanti sarvadevatāḥ || 32 ||
naitadviśīlena ca dāmbhikena na liṅginā sthāpanamatra kāryam
vipreṇa kāryaṃ śrutipārageṇa gṛhasthadharmābhiratena nityam || 33 ||
pāṣaṇḍinaṃ yastu karoti bhaktyā vihāya viprāñchrutidharmayuktān
guruṃ pratiṣṭhādiṣu tatra nūnaṃ kulakṣayaḥ syādacirādapūjyaḥ || 34 ||
sthānaṃ piśācaiḥ parigṛhyate vā apūjyatāṃ yātyacireṇa loke
vipraiḥ kṛtaṃ yacchubhadaṃ kule syātprapūjyatāṃ yāti ciraṃ ca kālam || 35 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe devatāsnānaṃ nāma saptaṣaṣṭyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 267 ||

<268>

athāṣṭaṣaṣṭyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
ṛṣaya ūcuḥ
prāsādāḥ kīdṛśāḥ sūta kartavyā bhūtimicchatā
pramāṇaṃ lakṣaṇaṃ tadvadvada vistarato'dhunā || 1 ||
sūta uvāca
athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi prāsādavidhinirṇayam
vāstau parīkṣite samyagvāstudehavicakṣaṇaḥ || 2 ||
vāstūpaśamanaṃ kuryātsamidbhirbalikarmaṇā
jīrṇoddhāre tathodyāne tathā gṛhaniveśane || 3 ||
navaprāsādabhavane prāsādaparivartane
dvārābhivartane tadvatprāsādeṣu gṛheṣu ca || 4 ||
vāstūpaśamanaṃ kuryātpūrvameva vicakṣaṇaḥ
ekāśītipadaṃ likhya vāstumadhye ca pṛṣṭhataḥ || 5 ||
homastrimekhale kāryaḥ kuṇḍe hastapramāṇake
yavaiḥ kṛṣṇatilaistadvatsamidbhiḥ kṣīravṛkṣajaiḥ || 6 ||
pālāśaiḥ khādiraiścāpi madhusarpiḥsamanvitaiḥ
kuśadūrvāmayairvā'pi madhusarpiḥsamanvitaiḥ || 7 ||
kāryastu pañcabhirbilvairbilvabījairathāpi vā
homānte bhakṣyabhojyaistu vāstudeśe baliṃ haret || 8 ||
tadvadviśeṣanaivedyamevaṃ dadyātkrameṇa tu
īśakoṇe ghṛtāktaṃ tu śikhine vinivedayet || 9 ||
odanaṃ saphalaṃ dadyātparjanyāya ghṛtānvitam
jayāya ca dhvajānpītānpaiṣṭaṃ kūrmaṃ ca saṃnyaset || 10 ||
indrā ya pañcaratnāni paiṣṭaṃ ca kuliśaṃ tathā
vitānakaṃ ca sūryāya dhūmraṃ saktuṃ tathaiva ca || 11 ||
satyāya ghṛtagodhūmaṃ matsyaṃ dadyādbhṛśāya ca
śaṣkulīścāntarikṣāya dadyātsaktūṃśca vāyave || 12 ||
lājāḥ pūṣṇe tu dātavyā vitathe caṇakaudanam
gṛhakṣatāya madhvannaṃ yamāya piśitaudanam || 13 ||
gandhaudanaṃ ca gandharve bhṛṅgarājasya bhṛṅgikām
mṛgāya yāvakaṃ dadyātpitṛbhyaḥ kṛsarā matā || 14 ||
dauvārike dantakāṣṭhaṃ paiṣṭaṃ kṛṣṇabaliṃ tathā
sugrīve'pūpakaṃ dadyātpuṣpadantāya pāyasam || 15 ||
kuśastambena saṃyuktaṃ tathā padmaṃ ca vāruṇe
piṣṭaṃ hiraṇmayaṃ dadyādasurāya surā matā || 16 ||
ghṛtaudanaṃ ca śoṣāya yavānnaṃ pāpayakṣmaṇe
ghṛtalaḍḍukāṃstu rogāya nāge puṣpaphalāni ca || 17 ||
sarpirmukhyāya dātavyaṃ mudgaudanamataḥ param
bhallāṭasthānake dadyātsomāya ghṛtapāyasam || 18 ||
bhagāya śālikaṃ piṣṭamadityai polikāstathā
dityai tu pūrikā dadyādityevaṃ bāhyato baliḥ || 19 ||
kṣīraṃ yamāya dātavyamāpavatsāya vai dadhi
sāvitre laḍḍukāndadyātsamarīcaṃ kuśaudanam || 20 ||
saviturguḍapūpāṃśca jayāya ghṛtacandanam
vivasvate punardadyādra ktacandanapāyasam || 21 ||
haritālaudanaṃ dadyādindrā ya ghṛtasaṃyutam
ghṛtaudanaṃ ca mitrāya rudrā ya ghṛtapāyasam || 22 ||
āmaṃ pakvaṃ tathā māṃsaṃ deyaṃ syādrā jayakṣmaṇe
pṛthvīdharāya māṃsāni kūṣmāṇḍāni ca dāpayet || 23 ||
śarkarāpāyasaṃ dadyādaryamṇe punareva hi
pañcagavyaṃ yavāṃścaiva tilākṣatamayaṃ carum || 24 ||
makṣyaṃ bhojyaṃ ca vividhaṃ brahmaṇe vinivedayet
evaṃ saṃpūjitā devāḥ śāntiṃ kurvanti te sadā || 25 ||
sarvebhyaḥ kāñcanaṃ dadyādbrāhmaṇe gāṃ payasvinīm
rākṣasīnāṃ balirdeyaḥ api yādṛgyathā śṛṇu || 26 ||
māṃsaudanaṃ ghṛtaṃ padmakesaraṃ rudhirānvitam
īśānabhāgamāśritya carakyai vinivedayet || 27 ||
māṃsaudanaṃ ca rudhiraṃ haridrau danameva ca
āgneyīṃ diśamāśritya vidāryai vinivedayet || 28 ||
dadhyodanaṃ sarudhiramasthikhaṇḍaiśca saṃyutam
pītaraktaṃ baliṃ dadyātpūtanāyai sarakṣase || 29 ||
vāyavyāṃ pāparākṣasyai matsyamāṃsaṃ surāsavam
pāyasaṃ cāpi dātavyaṃ svanāmnā sarvataḥ kramāt || 30 ||
namaskārāntayuktena praṇavādyena saṃyutaḥ
tataḥ sarvauṣadhīsnānaṃ yajamānasya kārayet || 31 ||
dvijānsupūjayedbhaktyā ye cānye gṛhamāgatāḥ
etadvāstūpaśamanaṃ kṛtvā karma samārabhet || 32 ||
prāsādabhavanodyānaprārambhe vinivartane
puraveśmapraveśeṣu sarvadoṣāpanuttaye || 33 ||
rakṣoghnapāvamānena sūktena bhavanādikam
nṛtyamaṅgalavādyena kuryādbrāhmaṇavācanam || 34 ||
anena vidhinā yastu pratisaṃvatsaraṃ budhaḥ
gṛhe vā''yatane kuryānna sa duḥkhamavāpnuyāt || 35 ||
na ca vyādhibhayaṃ tasya na ca bandhudhanakṣayaḥ
jīvedvarṣaśataṃ svarge kalpamekaṃ ca tiṣṭhati || 36 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe vāstudoṣopaśamanaṃ nāmāṣṭaṣaṣṭyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 268 ||

<269>

athaikonasaptatyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
sūta uvāca
evaṃ vāstubaliṃ kṛtvā bhajetṣoḍaśabhāgikam
tasya madhye caturbhistu bhāgairgarbhaṃ tu kārayet || 1 ||
bhāgadvādaśakaṃ sārdhaṃ tatastu parikalpayet
caturdikṣu tathā jñeyaṃ nirgamaṃ tu tato budhaiḥ || 2 ||
caturbhāgena bhittīnāmucchrāyaḥ syātpramāṇataḥ
dviguṇaḥ śikharocchrāyo bhittyucchrāyapramāṇataḥ || 3 ||
śikharārdhasya cārdhena vidheyā tu pradakṣiṇā
garbhasūtradvayaṃ cāgre vistāro maṇḍapasya tu || 4 ||
āyataḥ syāttribhirbhāgairbhadra yuktaḥ suśobhanaḥ
pañcabhāgena saṃbhajya garbhamānaṃ vicakṣaṇaḥ || 5 ||
bhāgamekaṃ gṛhītvā tu prāggrīvaṃ kalpayedbudhaḥ
garbhasūtrasamo bhāgādagrato mukhamaṇḍapaḥ || 6 ||
etatsāmānyamuddiṣṭaṃ prāsādasyeha lakṣaṇam
tathā'nyaṃ tu pravakṣyāmi prāsādaṃ liṅgamānataḥ || 7 ||
liṅgapūjāpramāṇena kartavyā pīṭhikā budhaiḥ
piṇḍikārdhena bhāgaḥ syāttanmānena tu bhittayaḥ || 8 ||
bāhyabhittipramāṇena utsedhastu bhavetpunaḥ
bhittyucchrāyāttu dviguṇaḥ śikharasya samucchrayaḥ || 9 ||
śikharasya caturbhāgātkartavyā ca pradakṣiṇā
pradakṣiṇāyāstu samastvagrato maṇḍapo bhavet || 10 ||
tasya cārdhena kartavyastvagrato mukhamaṇḍapaḥ
prāsādānnirgatau kāryau kapolau garbhamānataḥ || 11 ||
ūrdhvaṃ bhittyucchrayāttasya mañjarīṃ tu prakalpayet
mañjaryāścārdhabhāgena śukanāsāṃ prakalpayet || 12 ||
ūrdhvaṃ tathā'rdhabhāgena vedībandho bhavediha
vedyāścopari yaccheṣaṃ kaṇṭhaścāmalasārakaḥ || 13 ||
evaṃ vibhajya prāsādaṃ śobhanaṃ kārayedbudhaḥ
athānyacca pravakṣyāmi prāsādasyeha lakṣaṇam || 14 ||
garbhamānapramāṇena prāsādaṃ śṛṇuta dvijāḥ
vibhajya navadhā garbhaṃ madhye syālliṅgapīṭhikā || 15 ||
pādāṣṭakaṃ tu ruciraṃ pārśvataḥ parikalpayet
mānena tena vistāro bhittīnāṃ tu vidhīyate || 16 ||
pādaṃ pañcaguṇaṃ kṛtvā bhittīnāmucchrayo bhavet
sa eva śikharasyāpi dviguṇaḥ syātsamucchrayaḥ || 17 ||
caturdhā śikharaṃ bhajya ardhabhāgadvayasya tu
śukanāsaṃ prakurvīta tṛtīye vedikā matā || 18 ||
kaṇṭhamāmalasāraṃ tu caturthe parikalpayet
kapolayostu saṃhāro dviguṇo'tra vidhīyate || 19 ||
śobhanaiḥ patravallībhiraṇḍakaiśca vibhūṣitaḥ
prāsādo'yaṃ tṛtīyastu mayā tubhyaṃ niveditaḥ || 20 ||
sāmānyamaparaṃ tadvatprāsādaṃ śṛṇuta dvijāḥ
tribhedaṃ kārayetkṣetraṃ yatra tiṣṭhanti devatāḥ || 21 ||
rathāṅkastena mānena bāhyabhāgavinirgataḥ
nemī pādena vistīrṇā prāsādasya samantataḥ || 22 ||
garbhaṃ tu dviguṇaṃ kuryāttasya mānaṃ bhavediha
sa eva bhitterutsedhā dviguṇaḥ śikharo mataḥ || 23 ||
prāggrīvaḥ pañcabhāgena niṣkāsastasya cocyate
kārayetsuṣiraṃ tadvatprākārasya tribhāgataḥ || 24 ||
prāggrīvaṃ pañcabhāgena niṣkāṣeṇa viśeṣataḥ
kuryādvā pañcabhāgena prāggrīvaṃ karṇamūlataḥ || 25 ||
sthāpayetkanakaṃ tatra garbhānte dvāramūlataḥ
evaṃ tu trividhaṃ kuryājjyeṣṭhamadhyakanīyasam || 26 ||
liṅgamānānubhedena rūpabhedena vā punaḥ
ete samāsataḥ proktā nāmataḥ śṛṇutādhunā || 27 ||
merumandarakailāsakumbhasiṃhamṛgāstathā
vimānacchandakastadvaccaturasrastathaiva ca || 28 ||
aṣṭāsraḥ ṣoḍaśāsraśca vartulaḥ sarvabhadra kaḥ
siṃhāsyo nandanaścaiva nandivardhanakastathā || 29 ||
haṃso vṛṣaḥ suvarṇeśaḥ padmako'tha samudra kaḥ
prāsādā nāmataḥ proktā vibhāgaṃ śṛṇuta dvijāḥ || 30 ||
śataśṛṅgaścaturdvāro bhūmikāṣoḍaśocchritaḥ
nānāvicitraśikharo meruḥ prāsāda ucyate || 31 ||
mandaro dvādaśa proktaḥ kailāso navabhūmikaḥ
vimānacchandakastadvadanekaśikharānanaḥ || 32 ||
sa cāṣṭabhūmikastadvatsaptabhirnandivardhanaḥ
viṣāṇakasamāyukto nandanaḥ sa udāhṛtaḥ || 33 ||
ṣoḍaśāsrasamāyukto nānārūpasamanvitaḥ
anekaśikharastadvatsarvatobhadra ucyate || 34 ||
citraśālāsamopeto vijñeyaḥ pañcabhūmikaḥ
valabhīcchandakastadvadanekaśikharānanaḥ || 35 ||
vṛṣasyocchrāyatastulyo maṇḍalaścāsravarjitaḥ
siṃhaḥ siṃhākṛtirjñeyo gajo gajasamastathā || 36 ||
kumbhaḥ kumbhākṛtistadvadbhūmikānavakocchrayaḥ
aṅgulīpuṭasaṃsthānaḥ pañcāṇḍakavibhūṣitaḥ || 37 ||
ṣoḍaśāsraḥ samantācca vijñeyaḥ sa samudgakaḥ
pārśvayoścandra śāle'sya ucchrāyo bhūmikādvayam || 38 ||
tathaiva padmakaḥ prokta ucchrāyo bhūmikātrayam
ṣoḍaśāsraḥ sa vijñeyo vicitraśikharaḥ śubhaḥ || 39 ||
mṛgarājastu vikhyātaścandra śālo vibhūṣitaḥ
prāggrīveṇa viśālena bhūmikāsu ṣaḍunnataḥ || 40 ||
anekaścandra śālaśca gajaḥ prāsāda iṣyate
paryastagṛharājo vai garuḍo nāma nāmataḥ || 41 ||
saptabhūmyucchrayastadvaccandra śālātrayānvitaḥ
bhūmikāṣaḍaśītistu bāhyataḥ sarvato bhavet || 42 ||
tathā'nyo garuḍastadvaducchrayāddaśabhūmikaḥ
padmakaḥ ṣoḍaśāsrastu bhūmidvayamathādhikaḥ || 43 ||
padmatulyapramāṇena śrīvṛkṣaka iti smṛtaḥ
pañcāṇḍako dvibhūmiśca garbhe hastacatuṣṭayam || 44 ||
vṛṣo bhavati nāmnā'yaṃ prāsādaḥ sārvakāmikaḥ
saptakāḥ pañcakāścaiva prāsādā vai mayoditāḥ || 45 ||
siṃhāsyena samā jñeyā ye cānye tatpramāṇakāḥ
candra śālaiḥ samopetāḥ sarve prāggrīvasaṃyutāḥ
aiṣṭakā dāravāścaiva śailā vā syuḥ satoraṇāḥ || 46 ||
meruḥ pañcāśaddhastaḥ syānmandaraḥ pañcahīnakaḥ
catvāriṃśattu kailāsaścatustriṃśadvimānakaḥ || 47 ||
nandivardhanakastadvaddvātriṃśatsamudāhṛtaḥ
triṃśatā nandanaḥ proktaḥ sarvatobhadra kastathā || 48 ||
vartulaḥ padmakaścaiva viṃśaddhasta udāhṛtaḥ
gajaḥ siṃhaśca kumbhaśca valabhīcchandakastathā || 49 ||
ete ṣoḍaśahastāḥ syuścatvāro devavallabhāḥ
kailāso mṛgarājaśca vimānacchandako mataḥ || 50 ||
ete dvādaśahastāḥ syureteṣāmiha manmatam
garuḍo'ṣṭakaro jñeyo haṃso daśa udāhṛtaḥ || 51 ||
evametaṃ pramāṇena kartavyāḥ śubhalakṣaṇāḥ
yakṣarākṣasanāgānāṃ mātṛhastātpraśasyate || 52 ||
tathā mervādayaḥ sapta jyeṣṭhaliṅge śubhāvahāḥ
śrīvṛkṣakādayaścāṣṭau madhyamasya prakīrtitāḥ || 53 ||
tathā haṃsādayaḥ pañca kanyase śubhadā matāḥ
valabhīcchandake gaurī jaṭāmukuṭadhāriṇī || 54 ||
varadā'bhayadā tadvatsākṣasūtrakamaṇḍaluḥ
gṛhe tu raktamukuṭā utpalāṅkuśadhāriṇī
varadā'bhayadā cāpi pūjaṃnīyā sabhartṛkā || 55 ||
tapovanasthāmitarāṃ tāṃ tu saṃpūjayedbudhaḥ
devyā vināyakastadvadvalabhīcchandake śubhaḥ || 56 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe prāsādānukīrtanaṃ nāmaikonasaptatyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 269 ||

<270>

atha saptatyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
sūta uvāca
athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi maṇḍapānāṃ tu lakṣaṇam
maṇḍapapravarānvakṣye prāsādasyānurūpataḥ || 1 ||
vividhā maṇḍapāḥ kāryā jyeṣṭhamadhyakanīyasaḥ
nāmatastānpravakṣyāmi śṛṇudhvamṛṣisattamāḥ || 2 ||
puṣpakaḥ puṣpabhadra śca suvrato'mṛtanandanaḥ
kauśalyo buddhisaṃkīrṇo gajabhadro jayāvahaḥ || 3 ||
śrīvatso vijayaścaiva vāstukīrtiḥ śrutiṃjayaḥ
yajñabhadro viśālaśca suśliṣṭaḥ śatrumardanaḥ || 4 ||
bhāgapañco nandanaśca mānavo mānabhadra kaḥ
sugrīvo haritaścaiva karṇikāraḥ śatardhikaḥ || 5 ||
siṃhaśca śyāmabhadra śca subhadra śca tathaiva ca
saptaviṃśatirākhyātā lakṣaṇaṃ śṛṇuta dvijāḥ || 6 ||
stambhā yatra catuḥṣaṣṭiḥ puṣpakaḥ samudāhṛtaḥ
dviṣaṣṭiḥ puṣpabhadra stu ṣaṣṭiḥ suvrata ucyate || 7 ||
aṣṭapañcāśakastambhaḥ kathyate'mṛtanandanaḥ
kauśalyaḥ ṣaṭ ca pañcāśaccatuṣpañcāśatā punaḥ || 8 ||
nāmnā tu buddhisaṃkīrṇo dvihīno gajabhadra kaḥ
jayāvahastu pañcāśacchrīvatsastadvihīnakaḥ || 9 ||
vijayastadvihīnaḥ syādvāstukīrtistathaiva ca
dvābhyāmeva prahīyeta tataḥ śrutiṃjayo'paraḥ || 10 ||
catvāriṃśadyajñabhadra stadvihīno viśālakaḥ
ṣaṭtriṃśaccaiva suśliṣṭo dvihīnaḥ śatrumardanaḥ || 11 ||
dvātriṃśadbhāgapañcastu triṃśadbhirnandanaḥ smṛtaḥ
aṣṭāviṃśanmānavastu mānabhadro dvihīnakaḥ || 12 ||
caturviṃśastu sugrīvo dvāviṃśo harito mataḥ
viṃśatiḥ karṇikāraḥ syādaṣṭādaśa śatardhikaḥ || 13 ||
siṃho bhavedvihīnaśca śyāmabhadro dvihīnakaḥ
subhadra stu tathā prokto dvādaśastambhasaṃyutaḥ || 14 ||
maṇḍapāḥ kathitāstubhyaṃ yathāvallakṣaṇānvitāḥ
trikoṇaṃ vṛtamarddhandumaṣṭakoṇaṃ dviraṣṭakam || 15 ||
catuṣkoṇaṃ tu kartavyaṃ saṃsthānaṃ maṇḍapasya tu
rājyaṃ ca vijayaścaiva āyurvardhanameva ca || 16 ||
putralābhaḥ śriyaḥ puṣṭistrikoṇādikramādbhavet
evaṃ tu śubhadāḥ proktāścānyathā tvaśubhāvahāḥ || 17 ||
catuḥṣaṣṭipadaṃ kṛtvā madhye dvāraṃ prakalpayet
vistārāddviguṇocchrāyaṃ tattribhāgaḥ kaṭirbhavet || 18 ||
vistārārdho bhavedgarbho bhittayo'nyāḥ samantataḥ
garbhapādena vistīrṇaṃ dvāraṃ triguṇamāyatam || 19 ||
tathā dviguṇavistīrṇamukhastadvadudumbaraḥ
vistārapādapratimaṃ bāhulyaṃ śākhayoḥ smṛtam || 20 ||
tripañcasaptanavabhiḥ śākhābhirdvāramiṣyate
kaniṣṭhamadhyamaṃ jyeṣṭhaṃ yathāyogaṃ prakalpayet || 21 ||
aṅgulānāṃ śataṃ sārdhaṃ catvāriṃśattathonnatam
triṃśadviṃśottaraṃ cānyaddhanyamuttamameva ca || 22 ||
śataṃ cāśītisahitaṃ vātanirgamane bhavet
adhikaṃ daśabhistadvattathā ṣoḍaśabhiḥ śatam || 23 ||
śatamānaṃ tṛtīyaṃ ca navatyā'śītibhistathā
daśa dvārāṇi caitāni krameṇoktāni sarvadā || 24 ||
anyāni varjanīyāni mānasodvegadāni tu
dvāravedhaṃ prayatnena sarvavāstuṣu varjayet || 25 ||
vṛkṣakoṇabhramidvārastambhakūpadhvajādibhiḥ
kuḍyaśvabhreṇa vā viddhaṃ dvāraṃ na śubhadaṃ bhavet || 26 ||
kṣayaśca durgatiścaiva pravāsaḥ kṣudbhayaṃ tathā
daurbhāgyaṃ bandhanaṃ rogo dāridryaṃ kalahaṃ tathā || 27 ||
virodhaścārthanāśaśca sarvaṃ vedhādbhavetkramāt
pūrveṇa phalino vṛkṣāḥ kṣīravṛkṣāstu dakṣiṇe || 28 ||
paścimena jalaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ padmotpalavibhūṣitam
uttare saralaistālaiḥ śubhā syātpuṣpavāṭikā || 29 ||
sarvatastu jalaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ sthiramasthirameva ca
pārśvataścāpi kartavyaṃ parivārādikālayam || 30 ||
yāmye tapovanasthānamuttare mātṛkāgṛham
mahānasaṃ tathā''gneye nairṛtye'tha vināyakam || 31 ||
vāruṇe śrīnivāsastu vāyavye gṛhamālikā
uttare yajñaśālā tu nirmālyasthānamuttare || 32 ||
vāruṇe somadaivatye balinirvapaṇaṃ smṛtam
purato vṛṣabhasthānaṃ śeṣe syātkusumāyudhaḥ || 33 ||
jalavāpī tathaiśāne viṣṇustu jalaśāyyapi
evamāyatanaṃ kuryātkuṇḍamaṇḍapasaṃyutam || 34 ||
ghaṇṭāvitānakasatoraṇacitrayuktaṃ nityotsavapramuditena janena sārdham
yaḥ kārayetsuragṛhaṃ vividhadhvajāṅkaṃ śrīstaṃ na muñcati sadā divi pūjyate ca || 35 ||
evaṃ gṛhārcanavidhāvapi śaktitaḥ syātsaṃsthāpanaṃ sakalamantravidhānayuktam
govastrakāñcanahiraṇyadharāpradānaṃ deyaṃ gurudvijavareṣu tathā'nnadānam || 36 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe prāsādānukīrtanaṃ nāma saptatyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 270 ||

<271>

athaikasaptatyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
ṛṣaya ūcuḥ
pūrorvaṃśastvayā sūta sabhaviṣyo niveditaḥ
sūryavaṃśe nṛpā ye tu bhaviṣyanti hi tānvada || 1 ||
tathaiva yādave vaṃśe rājānaḥ kīrtivardhanāḥ
kalau yuge bhaviṣyanti tānapīha vadasva naḥ || 2 ||
vaśānte jñātayo yāśca rājyaṃ prāpsyanti suvratāḥ
brūhi saṃkṣepatastāsāṃ yathābhāvyamanukramāt || 3 ||
sūta uvāca
bṛhadbalasya dāyādo vīro rājā hyurukṣayaḥ
urukṣayasutaścāpi vatsadro ho mahāyaśāḥ || 4 ||
vatsadro hātprativyomastasya putro divākaraḥ
tasyaiva madhyadeśe tu ayodhyā nagarī śubhā || 5 ||
divākarasya bhavitā sahadevo mahāyaśāḥ
sahadevasya bhavitā dhruvāśvo vai mahāmanāḥ || 6 ||
tasya bhāvyo mahābhāgaḥ pratīpāśvaśca tatsutaḥ
pratīpāśvasutaścāpi supratīpo bhaviṣyati || 7 ||
marudevaḥ sutastasya sunakṣatrastato'bhavat
kinnarāśvaḥ sunakṣatrādbhaviṣyati paraṃtapaḥ || 8 ||
kiṃnarāśvādantarikṣo bhaviṣyati mahāmanāḥ
suṣeṇaścāntarikṣācca sumitraścāpyamitrajit || 9 ||
sumitrajo bṛhadrā jo bṛhadrā jasya vīryavān
punaḥ kṛtaṃjayo nāma dhārmikaśca bhaviṣyati || 10 ||
kṛtaṃjayasuto vidvānbhaviṣyati raṇejayaḥ
bhavitā saṃjayaścāpi vīro rājā raṇejayāt || 11 ||
saṃjayasya sutaḥ śākyaḥ śākyācchuddhaudano nṛpaḥ
śuddhaudanasya bhavitā siddhārthaḥ puṣkalaḥ sutaḥ || 12 ||
prasenajittato bhāvyaḥ kṣudra ko bhavitā tataḥ
kṣudra kātkulako bhāvyaḥ kulakātsurathaḥ smṛtaḥ || 13 ||
sumitraḥ surathājjāto hyantyastu bhavitā nṛpaḥ
ete caikṣvākavaḥ proktā bhaviṣyā ye kalau yuge || 14 ||
bṛhadbalānvavāye tu bhaviṣyāḥ kulavardhanāḥ
atrānuvaṃśaśloko'yaṃ viprairgītaḥ purātanaiḥ || 15 ||
ikṣvākūṇāmayaṃ vaṃśaḥ sumitrānto bhaviṣyati
sumitraṃ prāpya rājānaṃ saṃsthāṃ prāpsyati vai kalau || 16 ||
ityevaṃ mānavo vaṃśaḥ prāgeva samudāhṛtaḥ
ata ūrdhvaṃ pravakṣyāmi māgadhā ye bṛhadra thāḥ || 17 ||
pūrveṇa ye jarāsaṃdhātsahadevānvaye nṛpāḥ
atītā vartamānāśca bhaviṣyāṃśca nibodhata || 18 ||
saṅgrāme bhārate vṛtte sahadeve nipātite
somādhistasya dāyādo rājā'bhūtsa girivraje || 19 ||
pañcāśataṃ tathā'ṣṭau ca samā rājyamakārayat
śrutaśravāścatuḥṣaṣṭiṃ samāstasyānvaye'bhavat || 20 ||
apratīpī ca ṣaṭtriṃśatsamā rājyamakārayat
catvāriṃśatsamāstasya niramitro divaṃ gataḥ || 21 ||
pañcāśataṃ samāḥ ṣaṭ ca surakṣaḥ prāptavānmahīm
bṛhatkarmā trayoviṃśadabdaṃ rājyamakārayat || 22 ||
senājitsaṃprayātaśca bhuktvā pañcaśataṃ mahīm
śrutaṃjayastu varṣāṇi catvāriṃśadbhaviṣyati || 23 ||
aṣṭāviṃśativarṣāṇi mahīṃ prāpsyati vai vibhuḥ
aṣṭapañcāśataṃ ṣaṭ ca rājye sthāsyati vai śuciḥ || 24 ||
aṣṭāviṃśatsamā rājā kṣemo bhokṣyati vai mahīm
anuvrataścatuḥṣaṣṭiṃ rājyaṃ prāpsyati vīryavān || 25 ||
pañcaviṃśativarṣāṇi sunetro bhokṣyate mahīm
bhokṣyate nirvṛtiścemāmaṣṭapañcāśataṃ samāḥ || 26 ||
aṣṭāviṃśatsamā rājyaṃ trinetro bhokṣyate tataḥ
catvāriṃśattathā'ṣṭau ca dyumatseno bhaviṣyati || 27 ||
trayastriṃśattu varṣāṇi mahīnetraḥ prakāśyate
dvātriṃśattu samā rājā hyacalastu bhaviṣyati || 28 ||
ripuṃjayastu varṣāṇi pañcāśatprāpsyate mahīm
dvātriṃśattu nṛpā hyete bhavitāro bṛhadra thāḥ || 29 ||
pūrṇaṃ varṣasahasraṃ tu teṣāṃ rājyaṃ bhaviṣyati
jayatāṃ kṣattriyāṇāṃ ca bālakaḥ pulako bhavet || 30 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe rājavaṃśānukīrtana ekasaptatyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 271 ||

<272>

atha dvisaptatyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
sūta uvāca
bṛhadra theṣvatīteṣu vītihotreṣvavantiṣu
pulakaḥ svāminaṃ hatvā svaputramabhiṣekṣyati || 1 ||
miṣatāṃ kṣattriyāṇāṃ ca bālakaḥ pulakodbhavaḥ
sa vai praṇatasāmanto bhaviṣyo na ca dharmataḥ || 2 ||
trayoviṃśatsamā rājā bhavitā sa narottamaḥ
aṣṭāviṃśativarṣāṇi pālako bhavitā nṛpaḥ || 3 ||
viśākhyayūpo bhavitā tripañcāśattathā samāḥ
ekaviṃśatsamā rājā sūryakastu bhaviṣyati || 4 ||
bhaviṣyati nṛpastriṃśattatsuto nandivardhanaḥ
dvipañcāśattato bhuktvā pranaṣṭāḥ pañca te nṛpāḥ || 5 ||
hatvā teṣāṃ yaśaḥ kṛtsnaṃ śiśunāko bhaviṣyati
vārāṇasyāṃ sutaṃ sthāpya śrayiṣyati girivrajam
śiśunākastu varṣāṇi catvāriṃśadbhaviṣyati || 6 ||
kākavarṇaḥ sutastasya ṣaḍviṃśatprāpsyate mahīm
ṣaṭtriṃśaccaiva varṣāṇi kṣemadharmā bhaviṣyati || 7 ||
caturviṃśatsamāḥ so'pi kṣemajitprāpsyate mahīm
aṣṭāviṃśativarṣāṇi vindhyaseno bhaviṣyati || 8 ||
bhaviṣyati samā rājā navakānvāyano nṛpaḥ
bhūmimitraḥ sutastasya caturdaśa bhaviṣyati || 9 ||
ajātaśaturbhavitā saptaviṃśatsamā nṛpaḥ
caturviṃśatsamā rājā vaṃśakastu bhaviṣyati || 10 ||
udāsī bhavitā tasmāttrayastriṃśatsamā nṛpaḥ
catvāriṃśatsamā bhāvyo rājā vai nandivardhanaḥ || 11 ||
catvāriṃśattrayaścaiva mahānandī bhaviṣyati
ityete bhavitāro'tra vaṃśe vai śiśunākataḥ || 12 ||
śatāni trīṇi pūrṇāni ṣaṣṭivarṣādhikāni tu
śiśunākā bhaviṣyanti rājānaḥ kṣattrabandhavaḥ || 13 ||
etaiḥ sārdhaṃ bhaviṣyanti yāvatkali nṛpāḥ pare
tulyakālaṃ bhaviṣyanti sarve hyete mahīkṣitaḥ || 14 ||
caturviṃśattathaikṣvākāḥ pāñcālāḥ saptaviṃśatiḥ
kāśeyāstu caturviṃśadaṣṭāviṃśatirhaihayāḥ || 15 ||
kaliṅgāścaiva dvātriṃśadaśmakāḥ pañcaviṃśatiḥ
kuravaścāpi ṣaṭtriṃśadaṣṭāviṃśāstu maithilāḥ || 16 ||
śūrasenāstrayoviṃśadvītihotrāśca viṃśatiḥ
ete sarve bhaviṣyanti ekakālaṃ mahīkṣitaḥ || 17 ||
mahānandisutaścāpi śūdrā yāṃ kalikāṃśajaḥ
utpatsyate mahāpadmaḥ sarvakṣattrāntako nṛpaḥ || 18 ||
tataḥ prabhṛti rājāno bhaviṣyāḥ śūdra yonayaḥ
ekarāṭ sa mahāpadmo ekacchatro bhaviṣyati || 19 ||
aṣṭāśītistu varṣāṇi pṛthivyāṃ ca bhaviṣyati
sarvakṣatramathotsādya bhāvinā'rthena coditaḥ || 20 ||
sukalpādisutā hyaṣṭau samā dvādaśa te nṛpāḥ
mahāpadmasya paryāye bhaviṣyanti nṛpāḥ kramāt || 21 ||
uddhariṣyati kauṭilyaḥ samairdvādaśabhiḥ sutān
bhuktvā mahīṃ varṣaśataṃ tato mauryāngamiṣyati || 22 ||
bhavitā śatadhanvā ca tasya putrastu ṣaṭ samāḥ
bṛhadra thastu varṣāṇi tasya putraśca saptatiḥ || 23 ||
ṣaṭtriṃśattu samā rājā bhavitā śaka eva ca
saptānāṃ daśa varṣāṇi tasya naptā bhaviṣyati || 24 ||
rājā daśaratho'ṣṭau tu tasya putro bhaviṣyati
bhavitā nava varṣāṇi tasya putraśca saptatiḥ || 25 ||
ityete daśa mauryāstu ye mokṣyanti vasuṃdharām
saptatriṃśacchataṃ pūrṇaṃ tebhyaḥ śuṅgāngamiṣyati || 26 ||
puṣyamitrastu senānīruddhṛtya sa bṛhadra thān
kārayiṣyati vai rājyaṃ ṣaṭtriṃśattu samā nṛpaḥ || 27 ||
bhavitā'pi vasujyeṣṭhaḥ sapta varṣāṇi vai nṛpaḥ
vasumitrastathā bhāvyo daśa varṣāṇi vai tataḥ || 28 ||
tato'ntakaḥ same dve tu tasya putro bhaviṣyati
bhaviṣyati samāstasmāttrīṇyevaṃ sa pulindakaḥ || 29 ||
bhavitā vajramitrastu samā rājā punarbhavaḥ
dvātriṃśattu samābhāgaḥ samābhāgāttato nṛpaḥ || 30 ||
bhaviṣyati sutastasya devabhūmiḥ samā daśa
daśaite kṣudra rājāno bhokṣyantīmāṃ vasuṃdharām || 31 ||
śataṃ pūrṇaṃ śate dve ca tataḥ śuṅgāngamiṣyati
amātyo vasudevastu prasahya hyavanīṃ nṛpaḥ || 32 ||
devabhūmimathotsādya śauṅgastu bhavitā nṛpaḥ
bhaviṣyati samā rājā nava kāṇvāyano nṛpaḥ || 33 ||
bhūmimitraḥ sutastasya caturdaśa bhaviṣyati
nārāyaṇaḥ sutastasya bhavitā dvādaśaiva tu || 34 ||
suśarmā tatsutaścāpi bhaviṣyati daśaiva tu
ityete śuṅgabhṛtyāstu smṛtāḥ kāṇvāyanā nṛpāḥ || 35 ||
catvāriṃśaddvijā hyete kaṇvā bhokṣyanti vai mahīm
catvāriṃśatpañca caiva bhokṣyantīmāṃ vasuṃdharām || 36 ||
ete praṇatasāmantā bhaviṣyā dhārmikāśca ye
yeṣāṃ paryāyakāle tu bhūmirāndhrāngamiṣyati || 37 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe rājavaṃśānukīrtane dvisaptatyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 272 ||

<273>

atha trisaptatyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
sūta uvāca
kāṇvāyanāstato bhūpāḥ suśarmāṇaḥ prasahya tām
śuṅgānāṃ caiva yaccheṣaṃ kṣapitvā tu balīyasaḥ || 1 ||
śiśuko'ndhraḥ sajātīyaḥ prāpsyatīmāṃ vasuṃdharām
trayoviṃśasamā rājā śiśukastu bhaviṣyati || 2 ||
kṛṣṇabhrātā yavīyāṃstu aṣṭādaśa bhaviṣyati
śrīmallakarṇibhavitā tasya putrastu vai daśa
pūrṇotsaṅgastato rājā varṣāṇyaṣṭādaśaiva tu || 3 ||
pañcāśacca samāḥ ṣaṭ ca śāntakarṇirbhaviṣyati
daśa cāṣṭau ca varṣāṇi tasya lambodaraḥ sutaḥ || 4 ||
āpītako daśa dve ca tasya putro bhaviṣyati
daśa cāṣṭau ca varṣāṇi meghasvātirbhaviṣyati || 5 ||
svātiśca bhavitā rājā samāstvaṣṭādaśaiva tu
skandasvātistathā rājā saptaiva tu bhaviṣyati || 6 ||
mṛgendraḥ svātikarṇastu bhaviṣyati samāstrayaḥ
kuntalaḥ svātikarṇastu bhavitā'ṣṭau samā nṛpaḥ || 7 ||
ekasaṃvatsaraṃ rājā svātivarṇo bhaviṣyati || 8 ||
bhavitā riktavarṇastu varṣāṇi pañcaviṃśatiḥ
tataḥ saṃvatsarānpañca hālo rājā bhaviṣyati || 9 ||
pañca mandulako rājā bhaviṣyati samā nṛpaḥ
purīndra seno bhavitā tasmātsaumyo bhaviṣyati || 10 ||
sundaraḥ śāntikarṇastu abdamekaṃ bhaviṣyati
cakoraḥ svātikarṇastu ṣaṇmāsānvai bhaviṣyati || 11 ||
aṣṭāviṃśativarṣāṇi śivasvātirbhaviṣyati
rājā ca gautamīputro hyekaviṃśatyato nṛpaḥ || 12 ||
aṣṭāviṃśaḥ sutastasya pulomā vai bhaviṣyati
śivaśrīrvai pulomā tu saptaiva bhavitā nṛpaḥ || 13 ||
śivaskandhaḥ śāntikarṇādbhavitā hyātmajaḥ samāḥ
navaviṃśativarṣāṇi yajñaśrīḥ śāntikarṇikaḥ || 14 ||
ṣaḍeva bhavitā tasyādvijayastu samāstataḥ
caṇḍaśrīḥ śāntikarṇastu tasya putraḥ samā daśa || 15 ||
pulomā sapta varṣāṇi anyasteṣāṃ bhaviṣyati
ekonaviṃśatirhyete āndhrā bhokṣyanti vai mahīm || 16 ||
teṣāṃ varṣaśatāni syuścatvāri ṣaṣṭireva ca
āndhrāṇāṃ saṃsthitā rājye teṣāṃ bhṛtyānvaye nṛpāḥ || 17 ||
saptaivā''ndhrā bhaviṣyanti daśā''bhīrāstathā nṛpāḥ
sapta gardabhilāścāpi śakāścāṣṭādaśaiva tu || 18 ||
yavanāṣṭau bhaviṣyanti tuṣārāśca caturdaśa
trayodaśa guruṇḍāśca hūṇā hyekonaviṃśatiḥ || 19 ||
yavanāṣṭau bhaviṣyanti saptāśītiṃ mahīmimām
sapta gardabhilā bhūyo bhokṣyantīmāṃ vasuṃdharām || 20 ||
saptavarṣasahasrāṇi tuṣārāṇāṃ mahī smṛtā
śatāni trīṇyaśītiṃ ca śatānyaṣṭādaśaiva tu || 21 ||
śatānyardhacatuṣkāṇi bhavitavyāstrayodaśa
guruṇḍā vṛṣalaiḥ sārdhaṃ bhokṣyante mlecchasaṃbhavāḥ || 22 ||
śatāni trīṇi bhokṣyante varṣāṇyekādaśaiva tu
āndhrāḥ śrīpārvatīyāśca te dvipañcāśataṃ samāḥ || 23 ||
saptaṣaṣṭistu varṣāṇi daśā''bhīrāstathaiva ca
teṣūtsanneṣu kālena tataḥ kilakilā nṛpāḥ || 24 ||
bhaviṣyantīha yavanā dharmataḥ kāmato'rthataḥ
tairvimiśrā janapadā āryā mlecchāśca sarvaśaḥ || 25 ||
viparyayeṇa vartante kṣayameṣyanti vai prajāḥ
lubdhānṛtabruvāścaiva bhavitāro nṛpāstathā || 26 ||
kalkinā tu hatāḥ sarve āryā mlecchāśca sarvataḥ
adhārmikāśca ye'tyarthaṃ pāṣaṇḍāścaiva sarvaśaḥ || 27 ||
pranaṣṭe nṛpavaṃśe tu saṃdhyāśiṣṭe kalau yuge
kiṃcicchiṣṭāḥ prajāstā vai dharme naṣṭe'parigrahāḥ || 28 ||
asādhavo hyasattvāśca vyādhiśokena pīḍitāḥ
anāvṛṣṭihatāścaiva parasparavadhepsavaḥ || 29 ||
aśaraṇyāḥ paritrastāḥ saṃkaṭaṃ ghoramāśritāḥ
saritparvatavāsinyo bhaviṣyantyakhilāḥ prajāḥ || 30 ||
nṛpavaṃśeṣu naṣṭeṣu prajāḥ sarvagṛhāṇi ca
naṣṭasnehā nirāpatrāstyaktabhrātṛsuhṛdgaṇāḥ || 31 ||
varṇāśramaparibhraṣṭā adharmaniratāśca tāḥ
patramūlaphalāhārāścīrapatrājināmbarāḥ
vṛttyarthamabhilipsantyaścariṣyanti vasuṃdharām || 32 ||
evaṃ kaṣṭamanuprāptāḥ prajāḥ kaliyugāntake
niḥśeṣāstu bhaviṣyanti sārdhaṃ kaliyugena tu || 33 ||
kṣīṇe kaliyuge tasmindivye varṣasahasrake
sasaṃdhyāṃśe suniḥśeṣe kṛtaṃ tu pratipatsyate || 34 ||
evaṃ vaṃśakramaḥ kṛtsnaḥ kīrtito yo mayā kramāt
atīto vartamānaśca tathaivānāgataśca yaḥ || 35 ||
mahāpadmābhiṣekāttu yāvajjanma parīkṣitaḥ
evaṃ varṣasahasraṃ tu jñeyaṃ pañcāśaduttaram || 36 ||
paulomāstu tathā''ndhrāstu mahāpadmāntare punaḥ
anantaraṃ śatānyaṣṭau ṣaṭtriṃśattu samāstathā || 37 ||
tāvatkālāntaraṃ bhāvyamāndhrāntādā parīkṣitaḥ
bhaviṣye te prasaṃkhyātāḥ purāṇajñaiḥ śrutarṣibhiḥ || 38 ||
saptarṣayastadā prāṃśupradīptenāgninā samāḥ
saptaviṃśatibhāvyānāmāndhrāṇāṃ tu yadā punaḥ || 39 ||
saptarṣayastu vartante yatra nakṣatramaṇḍale
saptarṣayastu tiṣṭhanti paryāyeṇa śataṃ śatam || 40 ||
saptarṣīṇāmuparyetatsmṛtaṃ vai divya saṃjñayā
samā divyāḥ smṛtāḥ ṣaṣṭirdivyābdāni tu saptabhiḥ || 41 ||
ebhiḥ pravartate kālo divyaḥ saptarṣibhistu vai
saptarṣīṇāṃ ca yau pūrvau dṛśyete huditau niśi || 42 ||
tayormadhye tu nakṣatraṃ dṛśyate yatsamaṃ divi
tena saptarṣayo jñeyā yuktā vyomni śataṃ samāḥ || 43 ||
nakṣatrāṇāmṛṣīṇāṃ ca yogasyaitannidarśanam
saptarṣayo maghāyuktāḥ kāle pārīkṣite śatam || 44 ||
brāhmaṇāstu caturviṃśe bhaviṣyanti śataṃ samāḥ
tataḥ prabhṛtyayaṃ sarvo loko vyāpatsyate bhṛśam || 45 ||
anṛtopahatā lubdhā dharmataḥ kāmato'rthataḥ
śrautasmārte'tiśithile naṣṭavarṇāśrame tathā || 46 ||
saṃkaraṃ durbalātmānaḥ pratipatsyanti mohitāḥ
brāhmaṇāḥ śūdra yonisthāḥ śūdrā vai mantrayonayaḥ || 47 ||
upasthāsyanti tānviprāstadarthamabhilipsavaḥ
krameṇaiva ca dṛśyante svavarṇāntaradāyakam || 48 ||
kṣayameva gamiṣyanti kṣīṇaśeṣā yugakṣaye
yasminkṛṣṇo divaṃ yātastasminneva tadā'hani || 49 ||
pratipannaṃ kaliyugaṃ pramāṇaṃ tasya me śṛṇu
catuḥśatasahasraṃ tu varṣāṇāṃ vai smṛtaṃ budhaiḥ || 50 ||
catvāryaṣṭasahasrāṇi saṃkhyātaṃ mānuṣeṇa tu
divyaṃ varṣasahasraṃ tu tadā saṃdhyā pravartate || 51 ||
niḥśeṣe tu tadā tasminkṛtaṃ vai pratipatsyate
ailaścekṣvākuvaṃśaśca sahadevaḥ prakīrtitaḥ || 52 ||
ikṣvākoḥ saṃsmṛtaṃ kṣattraṃ sumitrāntaṃ bhaviṣyati
ailaṃ kṣattraṃ samākrāntaṃ somavaṃśavido viduḥ || 53 ||
ete vivasvataḥ putrāḥ kīrtitāḥ kīrtivardhanāḥ
atītā vartamānāśca tathaivānāgatāśca ye || 54 ||
brāhmaṇāḥ kṣattriyā vaiśyāstathā śūdrā śca vai smṛtāḥ
vaivasvate'ntare tasminniti vaṃśaḥ samāpyate || 55 ||
devāpiḥ pauravo rājā aikṣvāko yaśca te mataḥ
mahāyogabalopetau kalāpagrāmamāśritau || 56 ||
etau kṣatrapraṇetārau navaviṃśe caturyuge
suvarcā manuputrastu aikṣvākādyo bhaviṣyati || 57 ||
navaviṃśe yuge'sau vai vaṃśasyā''dirbhaviṣyati
devāpiputraḥ satyastu ailānāṃ bhavitā nṛpaḥ || 58 ||
kṣattrapravartakāvetau bhaviṣye tu caturyuge
evaṃ sarveṣu vijñeyaṃ saṃtānārthaṃ tu lakṣaṇam || 59 ||
kṣīṇe kaliyuge caiva tiṣṭhantīti kṛte yuge
saptarṣayastu taiḥ sārdhaṃ madhye tretāyuge punaḥ || 60 ||
bījārthaṃ vai bhaviṣyanti brahmakṣattrasya vai punaḥ
evamevaṃ tu sarveṣu tiṣyānteṣvantareṣu ca || 61 ||
saptarṣayo nṛpaiḥ sārdhaṃ saṃtānārthaṃ yuge yuge
evaṃ kṣattrasya cotsedhaḥ saṃbandho vai dvijaiḥ smṛtaḥ || 62 ||
manvantarāṇāṃ saṃtāne saṃtānāśca śrutau smṛtāḥ
atikrāntayugāścaiva brahmakṣattrasya saṃbhavāḥ || 63 ||
yathā praśāntisteṣāṃ vai prakṛtīnāṃ yathā kṣayaḥ
saptarṣayo vidusteṣāṃ dīrghāyuṣṭvaṃ kṣayodayau || 64 ||
etena kramayogena ailā ikṣvākavo nṛpāḥ
utpadyamānāstretāyāṃ kṣīyamāṇāḥ kalau yuge || 65 ||
anuyānti yugākhyaṃ tu yāvanmanvantarakṣayam
jāmadagnyena rāmeṇa kṣatre niravaśeṣite || 66 ||
rikteyaṃ vasudhā sarvā kṣattriyairvasudhādhipaiḥ
dvivaṃśakaraṇaṃ sarvaṃ kīrtayiṣye nibodha me || 67 ||
ailaṃ caikṣvākuvaṃśaṃ ca prakṛtiṃ paricakṣate
rājānaḥ śreṇibaddhāśca tathā'nye kṣattriyā bhuvi || 68 ||
ailavaṃśāstu bhūyāṃso na tathekṣvākavo nṛpāḥ
eṣāmekaśataṃ pūrṇaṃ kulānāmabhirocate || 69 ||
tāvadeva tu bhojānāṃ vistārāddviguṇaṃ smṛtam
bhojānāṃ dviguṇaṃ kṣattraṃ caturdhā tadyathātatham || 70 ||
te hyatītāḥ sanāmāno bruvatastānnibodha me
śataṃ vai prativindhyānāṃ śataṃ nāgāḥ śataṃ hayāḥ || 71 ||
śatamekaṃ dhārtarāṣṭrā hyaśītirjanamejayāḥ
śataṃ vai brahmadattānāṃ vīrāṇāṃ kuravaḥ śatam || 72 ||
tataḥ śataṃ ca pañcālāḥ śataṃ kāśikuśādayaḥ
tathā'pare sahasre dve ye nīpāḥ śaśabindavaḥ || 73 ||
iṣṭavantaśca te sarve sarve niyutadakṣiṇāḥ
evaṃ rājarṣayo'tītāḥ śaptaśo'tha sahasraśaḥ || 74 ||
manorvaivasvatasyā''sanvartamāne'ntare vibhoḥ
teṣāṃ tu nidhanotpattau lokasaṃsthitayaḥ sthitāḥ || 75 ||
na śakyo vistarasteṣāṃ saṃtānasya parasparam
tatpūrvāparayogeṇa vaktuṃ varṣaśatairapi || 76 ||
aṣṭāviṃśasamākhyātā gatā vaivasvate'ntare
ete devagaṇaiḥ sārdhaṃ śiṣṭā ye tānnibodhata || 77 ||
catvāriṃśattrayaścaiva bhaviṣyāste mahātmanaḥ
avaśiṣṭā yugākhyāste tato vaivasvato hyayam || 78 ||
etadvaḥ kīrtitaṃ samyaksamāsavyāsayogataḥ
punarvaktuṃ bahutvāttu na śakyaṃ vistareṇa tu || 79 ||
uktā rājarṣayo ye tu atītāste yugaiḥ saha
ye te yayātivaṃśyānāṃ ye ca vaṃśā viśāṃpate || 80 ||
kīrtitā dyutimantaste ya etāndhārayennaraḥ
labhate sa varānpañca durlabhāniha laukikān || 81 ||
āyuḥ kīrtiṃ dhanaṃ svargaṃ putravāṃścābhijāyate
dhāraṇācchravaṇāccaiva paraṃ svargasya dhīmataḥ || 82 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe bhaviṣyarājānukīrtanaṃ nāma trisaptatyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 273 ||

<274>

atha catuḥsaptatyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
ṛṣaya ūcuḥ
nyāyenārjanamarthānāṃ vardhanaṃ cābhirakṣaṇam
satpātrapratipattiśca sarvaśāstreṣu paṭhyate || 1 ||
kṛtakṛtyo bhavetkena manasvī dhanavānbudhaḥ
mahādānena dattena tanno vistarato vada || 2 ||
sūta uvāca
athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi mahādānānukīrtanam
dānadharme'pi yannoktaṃ viṣṇunā prabhaviṣṇunā || 3 ||
tadahaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi mahādānamanuttamam
sarvapāpakṣayakaraṃ nṛṇāṃ duḥsvapnanāśanam || 4 ||
yattatṣoḍaśadhā proktaṃ vāsudevena bhūtale
puṇyaṃ pavitramāyuṣyaṃ sarvapāpaharaṃ śubham || 5 ||
pūjitaṃ devatābhiśca brahmaviṣṇuśivādibhiḥ
ādyaṃ tu sarvadānānāṃ tulāpuruṣasaṃjñakam || 6 ||
hiraṇyagarbhadānaṃ ca brahmāṇḍaṃ tadanantaram
kalpapādapadānaṃ ca gosahasraṃ ca pañcamam || 7 ||
hiraṇyakāmadhenuśca hiraṇyāśvastathaiva ca
hiraṇyāśvarathastadvaddhemahastirathastathā || 8 ||
pañcalāṅgalakaṃ tadvaddharādānaṃ tathaiva ca
dvādaśa viśvacakra tu tataḥ kalpalatātmakam || 9 ||
saptasāgaradānaṃ ca ratnadhenustathaiva ca
mahābhūtaghaṭastadvatṣoḍaśaṃ parikīrtitam || 10 ||
sarvāṇyetāni kṛtavānpurā śambarasūdanaḥ
vāsudevastu bhagavānambarīṣo'tha bhārgavaḥ || 11 ||
kārtavīryārjuno nāma prahlādaḥ pṛthureva ca
kuryuranye mahīpālāḥ kecicca bharatādayaḥ || 12 ||
yasmādvighnasahasreṇa mahādānāni sarvadā
rakṣante devatāḥ sarvā ekaikamapi bhūtale || 13 ||
eṣāmanyatamaṃ kuryādvāsudevaprasādataḥ
na śakyamanyathā kartumapi śakreṇa bhūtale || 14 ||
tasmādārādhya gauvindamumāpativināyakau
mahādānamakhaṃ kuryādvipraiścaivānumoditaḥ || 15 ||
etadevā''ha manave paripṛṣṭo janārdanaḥ
yathāvadanuvakṣyāmi śṛṇudhvamṛṣisattamāḥ || 16 ||
manuruvāca
mahādānāni yānīha pavitrāṇi śubhāni ca
rahasyāni pradeyāni tāni me kathayācyuta || 17 ||
matsya uvāca
yāni noktāni guhyāni mahādānāni ṣoḍaśa
tāni te kathayiṣyāmi yathāvadanupūrvaśaḥ || 18 ||
tulāpuruṣayogo'yaṃ yeṣāmādau vidhīyate
ayane viṣuve puṇye vyatīpāte dinakṣaye || 19 ||
yugādiṣūparāgeṣu tathā manvantarādiṣu
saṃkrāntau vaidhṛtidine caturdaśyaṣṭamīṣu ca || 20 ||
sitapañcadaśīparvadvādaśīṣvaṣṭakāsu ca
yajñotsavavivāheṣu duḥsvapnādbhutadarśane || 21 ||
dra vyabrāhmaṇalābhe vā śraddhā vā yatra jāyate
tīrthe vā''yatane goṣṭhe kūpārāmasaritsu ca || 22 ||
gṛhe vā''yatane vā'pi taḍāge rucire tathā
mahādānāni deyāni saṃsārabhayabhīruṇā || 23 ||
anityaṃ jīvitaṃ yasmādvasu cātīva cañcalam
keśeṣveva gṛhītaḥ sanmṛtyunā dharmamācaret || 24 ||
puṇyāṃ tithimathā''sādya kṛtvā brāhmaṇavācanam
ṣoḍaśāratnimātraṃ tu daśa dvādaśa vā karān || 25 ||
maṇḍapaṃ kārayedvidvāṃścaturbhadrā nanaṃ budhaḥ
saptahastā bhavedvedī madhye pañcakarā tathā || 26 ||
tanmadhye toraṇaṃ kuryātsāradārumayaṃ budhaḥ
kuryātkuṇḍāni catvāri caturdikṣu vicakṣaṇaḥ || 27 ||
samekhalāyoniyutāni kuryātsaṃpūrṇakumbhāni mahāsanāni
sutāmrapātradvayasaṃyutāni suyajñapātrāṇi suviṣṭarāṇi || 28 ||
hastapramāṇāni tilājyadhūpapuṣpopahārāṇi suśobhanāni
pūrvottare hastamitā'tha vedī grahādideveśvarapūjanāya || 29 ||
atrārcanaṃ brahmaśivācyutānāṃ tatraiva kāryaṃ phalamālyavastraiḥ
lokeśavarṇāḥ paritaḥ patākā madhye dhvajaḥ kiṅkiṇikāyutaḥ syāt || 30 ||
dvāreṣu kāryāṇi ca toraṇāni catvāryapi kṣīravanaspatīnām
dvāreṣu kumbhadvayamatra kāryaṃ sraggandhadhūpāmbararatnayuktam || 31 ||
śāleṅgudīcandanadevadāruśrīparṇibilvapriyakāñcanottham
stambhadvayaṃ hastayugāvakhātaṃ kṛtvā dṛḍhaṃ pañcakarocchritaṃ ca || 32 ||
tadantaraṃ hastacatuṣṭayaṃ syādathodaraṅgaśca tadaṅgameva
samānajātiśca tulā'valambyā haimena madhye puruṣeṇa yuktā || 33 ||
dairghyeṇa sā hastacatuṣṭayaṃ syātpṛthutvamasyāstu daśāṅgulāni
suvarṇapaṭṭābharaṇā tu kāryā sā lohapāśadvayaśṛṅkhalābhiḥ || 34 ||
yutā suvarṇena tu ratnamālā vibhūṣitā mālyavilepanābhyām
cakraṃ likhedvārijagarbhayuktaṃ nānārajobhirbhuvi puṣpakīrṇam || 35 ||
vitānakaṃ copari pañcavarṇaṃ saṃsthāpayetpuṣpaphalopaśobham
athartvijo vedavidaśca kāryāḥ surūpaveśānvayaśīlayuktāḥ || 36 ||
vidhānadakṣāḥ paṭavo'nukūlā ye cā''ryadeśaprabhavā dvijendrāḥ
guruśca vedāntavidāryavaṃśasamudbhavaḥ śīlakulābhirūpaḥ || 37 ||
purāṇaśāstrābhirato'tidakṣaḥ prasannagambhīrasarasvatīkaḥ
sitāmbaraḥ kuṇḍalahemasūtrakeyūrakaṇṭhābharaṇābhirāmaḥ || 38 ||
pūrveṇa ṛgvedavidāvathā''stāṃ yajurvidau dakṣiṇataśca śastau
sthāpyau dvijau sāmavidau tu paścādātharvaṇāvuttaratastu kāryau || 39 ||
vināyakādigrahalokapālavasvaṣṭakādityamarudgaṇānām
brahmācyuteśārkavanaspatīnāṃ svamantrato homacatuṣṭayaṃ syāt || 40 ||
japyāni sūktāni tathaiva caiṣāmanukrameṇāpi yathāsvarūpam
homāvasāne kṛtatūryanādo gururgṛhītvā balipuṣpadhūpam
āvāhayellokapatīnkrameṇa mantrairamībhiryajamānayuktaḥ || 41 ||
ehyehi sarvāmarasiddhasādhyairabhiṣṭuto vajradharo'mareśaḥ
saṃvījyamāno'psarasāṃ gaṇena rakṣādhvaraṃ no bhagavannamaste
oṃ indrā ya namaḥ || 42 ||
ehyehi sarvāmarahavyavāha munipravīrairabhito'bhijuṣṭaḥ
tejasvinā lokagaṇena sārdhaṃ mamādhvaraṃ rakṣa kave namaste
oṃ agnaye namaḥ || 43 ||
ehyehi vaivasvata dharmarāja sarvāmarairarcita divyamūrte
śubhāśubhānandaśucāmadhīśa śivāya naḥ pāhi makhaṃ namaste
oṃ yamāya namaḥ || 44 ||
ehyehi rakṣogaṇanāyakastvaṃ viśālavetālapiśācasaṃghaiḥ
mamādhvaraṃ pāhi śubhādinātha lokeśvarastvaṃ bhagavannamaste
oṃ nirṛtaye namaḥ || 45 ||
ehyehi yādogaṇavāridhīnāṃ gaṇena parjanyamahāpsarobhiḥ
vidyādharendrā maragīyamāna pāhi tvamasmānbhagavannamaste
oṃ varuṇāya namaḥ || 46 ||
ehyehi yajñe mama rakṣaṇāya mṛgādhirūḍhaḥ saha siddhasaṃghaiḥ
prāṇādhipaḥ kālakaveḥ sahāyo gṛhāṇa pūjāṃ bhagavannamaste
oṃ vāyave namaḥ || 47 ||
ehyehi yajñeśvara yajñarakṣāṃ vidhatsva nakṣatragaṇena sārdham
sarvauṣadhībhiḥ pitṛbhiḥ sahaiva gṛhāṇa pūjāṃ bhagavannamaste
oṃ somāya namaḥ || 48 ||
ehyehi viśveśvara nastriśūlakapālakhaṭvāṅgadhareṇa sārdham
lokeśa yajñeśvara yajñasiddhyai gṛhāṇa pūjāṃ bhagavannamaste
oṃ īśānāya namaḥ || 49 ||
ehyehi pātāladharādharendra nāgāṅganākiṃnaragīyamāna
yakṣoragendrā maralokasārdhamananta rakṣādhvaramasmadīyam
oṃ anantāya namaḥ || 50 ||
ehyehi viśvādhipate munīndra lokena sārdhaṃ pitṛdevatābhiḥ
sarvasya dhātā'syamitaprabhāva viśādhvaraṃ no bhagavannamaste
oṃ brahmaṇe namaḥ || 51 ||
trailokye yāni bhūtāni sthāvarāṇi carāṇi ca
brahmaviṣṇuśivaiḥ sārdhaṃ rakṣāṃ kurvantu tāni me || 52 ||
devadānagandharvā yakṣarākṣasapannagāḥ
ṛṣayo madano gāvo devamātara eva ca || 53 ||
sarve mamādhvare rakṣāṃ prakurvantu mudā'nvitāḥ
ityāvāhya surāndadyādṛtvigbhyo hemabhūṣaṇam || 54 ||
kuṇḍalāni ca haimāni sūtrāṇi kaṭakāni ca
aṅgulīyapavitrāṇi vāsāṃsi śayanāni ca || 55 ||
dviguṇaṃ gurave dadyādbhūṣaṇācchādanāni ca
japeyuḥ śāntikādhyāyaṃ jāpakāḥ sarvatodiśam || 56 ||
tatroṣitāstu te sarve kṛtvaivamadhivāsanam
ādāvante ca madhye ca kuryādbrāhmaṇavācanam || 57 ||
tato maṅgalaśabdena snāpito vedapuṃgavaiḥ
triḥpradakṣiṇamāvṛtya gṛhītakusumāñjaliḥ || 58 ||
śuklamālyāmbaro bhūtvā tāṃ tulāmabhimantrayet
namaste sarvadevānāṃ śaktistvaṃ satyamāsthitā || 59 ||
sākṣibhūtā jagaddhātrī nirmitā viśvayoninā
ekataḥ sarvasatyāni tathā'nṛtaśatāni ca || 60 ||
dharmādharmakṛtāṃ madhye sthāpitā'si jagaddhite
tvaṃ tule sarvabhūtānāṃ pramāṇamiha kīrtitā || 61 ||
māṃ tolayantī saṃsārāduddharasva namo'stu te
yo'sau tattvādhipo devaḥ puruṣaḥ pañcaviṃśakaḥ || 62 ||
sa eko'dhiṣṭhito devi tvayi tasmānnamo namaḥ
namo namaste govinda tulāpuruṣasaṃjñaka || 63 ||
tvaṃ hare tārayasvāsmānasmātsaṃsārakardamāt
puṇyakālaṃ samāsādya kṛtvaivamadhivāsanam || 64 ||
punaḥ pradakṣiṇaṃ kṛtvā tulāmārohayedbudhaḥ
sakhaḍgacarmakavacaḥ sarvābharaṇabhūṣitaḥ || 65 ||
dharmarājamathā''dāya haimaṃ sūryeṇa saṃyutam
karābhyāṃ baddhamuṣṭibhyāmāste paśyanharermukham || 66 ||
tato'pare tulābhāge nyaseyurdvijapuṃgavāḥ
samādabhyadhikaṃ yāvatkāñcanaṃ cātinirmalam || 67 ||
puṣṭikāmastu kurvīta bhūmisaṃsthaṃ nareśvaraḥ
kṣaṇamātraṃ tataḥ sthitvā punarevamudīrayet || 68 ||
namaste sarvabhūtānāṃ sākṣibhūte sanātani
pitāmahena devi tvaṃ nirmitā parameṣṭhinā || 69 ||
tvayā dhṛtaṃ jagatsarvaṃ tule sthāvarajaṅgamam
sarvabhūtātmabhūtasthe namaste viśvadhāriṇi || 70 ||
tato'vatīrya gurave pūrvamardhaṃ nivedayet
ṛtvigbhyo'paramardhaṃ tu dadyādudakapūrvakam || 71 ||
gurave grāmaratnāni ṛtvigbhyaśca nivedayet
prāpya teṣāmanujñāṃ tu tathā'nyebhyo'pi dāpayet || 72 ||
dīnānāthaviśiṣṭādīnpūjayedbrāhmaṇaiḥ saha
na ciraṃ dhārayedgehe suvarṇaṃ prokṣitaṃ budhaḥ || 73 ||
tiṣṭhedbhayāvahaṃ yasmācchokavyādhikaraṃ nṛṇām
śīghraṃ parasvīkaraṇācchreyaḥ prāpnoti mānavaḥ || 74 ||
anena vidhinā yastu tulāpuruṣamācaret
pratilokādhipasthāne pratimanvantaraṃ vaset || 75 ||
vimānenārkavarṇena kiṅkiṇījālamālinā
pūjyamāno'psarobhiśca tato viṣṇupuraṃ vrajet
kalpakoṭiśataṃ yāvattasmim̐ lloke mahīyate || 76 ||
karmakṣayādiha punarbhuvi rājarājo bhūpālamaulimaṇirañjitapādapīṭhaḥ
śraddhānvito bhavati yajñasahasrayājī dīptapratāpajitasarvamahīpalokaḥ || 77 ||
yo dīyamānamapi paśyati bhaktiyuktaḥ kālāntare smarati vācayatīha loke
yo vā śṛṇoti paṭhatīndra samānarūpaḥ prāpnoti dhāma sa puraṃdaradevajuṣṭam || 78 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe mahādānānukīrtane tulāpuruṣadānaṃ nāma catuḥsaptatyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 274 ||

<275>

atha pañcasaptatyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
matsya uvāca
athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi mahādānamanuttamam
nāmnā hiraṇyagarbhākhyaṃ mahāpātakanāśanam || 1 ||
puṇyaṃ dinamathā''sādya tulāpuruṣadānavat
ṛtviṅmaṇḍapasaṃbhārabhūṣaṇācchādanādikam || 2 ||
kuryādupoṣitastadvallokeśāvāhanaṃ budhaḥ
puṇyāhavācanaṃ kṛtvā tadvatkṛtvā'dhivāsanam || 3 ||
brāhmaṇairānayetkumbhaṃ tapanīyamayaṃ śubham
dvisaptatyaṅgulocchrāyaṃ hemapaṅkajagarbhavat || 4 ||
tribhāgahīnavistāramājyakṣīrābhipūritam
daśāstrāṇi ca ratnāni dātrasūcīṃ tathaiva ca || 5 ||
hemanālaṃ sapiṭakaṃ bahirādityasaṃyutam
tathaivā''varaṇaṃ nābherupavītaṃ ca kāñcanam || 6 ||
pārśvataḥ sthāpayettadvaddhaimadaṇḍakamaṇḍalū
padmākāraṃ pidhānaṃ syātsamantādaṅgulādhikam || 7 ||
muktāvalīsamopetaṃ padmarāgasamanvitam
tiladro ṇoparigataṃ vedimadhye vyavasthitam || 8 ||
tato maṅgalaśabdena brahmaghoṣaraveṇa ca
sarvauṣadhyudakasnānasnāpito vedapuṃgavaiḥ || 9 ||
śuklamālyāmbaradharaḥ sarvābharaṇabhūṣitaḥ
imamuccāsyenmantraṃ gṛhītakusumāñjaliḥ || 10 ||
namo hiraṇyagarbhāya hiraṇyakavacāya ca
saptalokasurādhyakṣa jagaddhātre namo namaḥ || 11 ||
bhūrlokapramukhā lokāstava garbhe vyavasthitāḥ
brahmādayastathā devā namaste viśvadhāriṇe || 12 ||
namaste bhuvanādhāra namaste bhuvanāśraya
namo hiraṇyagarbhāya garbhe yasya pitāmahaḥ || 13 ||
yatastvameva bhūtātmā bhūte bhūte vyavasthitaḥ
tasmānmāmuddharāśeṣaduḥkhasaṃsārasāgarāt || 14 ||
evamāmantrya tanmadhyamāviśyā''sta udaṅmukhaḥ
muṣṭibhyāṃ parisaṃgṛhya dharmarājacaturmukhau || 15 ||
jānumadhye śiraḥ kṛtvā tiṣṭheducchvāsapañcakam
garbhādhānaṃ puṃsavanaṃ sīmantonnayanaṃ tathā || 16 ||
kuryurhiraṇyagarbhasya tataste dvijapuṃgavāḥ
gītamaṅgalaghoṣeṇa gururutthāpayettataḥ || 17 ||
jātakarmādikāḥ kuryuḥ kriyāḥ ṣoḍaśa cāparāḥ
sūcyādikaṃ ca gurave dadyānmantramimaṃ japet || 18 ||
namo hiraṇyagarbhāya viśvagarbhāya vai namaḥ
carācarasya jagato gṛhabhūtāya vai namaḥ || 19 ||
yathā'haṃ janitaḥ pūrvaṃ martyadharmā surottama
tvadgarbhasaṃbhavādeṣa divyadeho bhavāmyaham || 20 ||
caturbhiḥ kalaśairbhūyastataste dvijapuṃgavāḥ
snāpayeyuḥ prasannāṅgāḥ sarvābharaṇabhūṣitāḥ || 21 ||
devasya tveti mantreṇa sthitasya kanakāsane
adya jātasya te'ṅgāni abhiṣekṣyāmahe vayam || 22 ||
divyenānena vapuṣā ciraṃ jīva sukhī bhava
tato hiraṇyagarbhaṃ taṃ tebhyo dadyādvicakṣaṇaḥ || 23 ||
te pūjyāḥ sarvabhāvena bahavo vā tadājñayā
tatropakaraṇaṃ sarvaṃ gurave vinivedayet || 24 ||
pādukopānahacchatracāmarāsanabhājanam
grāmaṃ vā viṣayaṃ vā'pi yadanyadapi saṃbhavet || 25 ||
anena vidhinā yastu puṇye'hani nivedayet
hiraṇyagarbhadānaṃ sa brahmaloke mahīyate || 26 ||
pureṣu lokapālānāṃ pratimanvantaraṃ vaset
kalpakoṭiśataṃ yāvadbrahmaloke mahīyate || 27 ||
kalikaluṣavimuktaḥ pūjitaḥ siddhasādhyairamaracamaramālābījyamāno'psarobhiḥ ।
pitṛśatamatha bandhūnputrapautrānprapautrānapi narakanimagnāṃstārayedeka eva || 28 ||
iti paṭhati ya itthaṃ yaḥ śṛṇotīha samyaṅmadhuripuriva loke pūjyate so'pi siddhaiḥ ।
matimapi ca janānāṃ yo dadāti priyārthaṃ vibudhapatijanānāṃ nāyakaḥ syādamogham || 29 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe mahādānānukīrtane hiraṇyagarbhapradānavidhirnāma pañcasaptatyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 275 ||

<276>

atha ṣaṭsaptatyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
matsya uvāca
athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi brahmāṇḍavidhimuttamam
yacchreṣṭhaṃ sarvadānānāṃ mahāpātakanāśanam || 1 ||
puṇyaṃ dinamathā''sādya tulāpuruṣadānavat
ṛtviṅmaṇḍapasaṃbhārabhūṣaṇācchādanādikam || 2 ||
lokeśāvāhanaṃ kuryādadhivāsanakaṃ tathā
kuryādviṃśapalādūrdhvamā sahasrācca śaktitaḥ || 3 ||
kalaśadvayasaṃyuktaṃ brahmāṇḍaṃ kāñcanaṃ budhaḥ
diggajāṣṭakasaṃyuktaṃ ṣaḍvedāṅgasamanvitam || 4 ||
lokapālāṣṭakopetaṃ madhyasthitacaturmukham
śivācyutārkaśikharamumālakṣmīsamanvitam || 5 ||
vasvādityamarudgarbhaṃ mahāratnasamanvitam
vitasteraṅgulaśataṃ yāvadāyāmavistaram || 6 ||
kauśeyavastrasaṃvītaṃ tiladro ṇopari nyaset
tathā'ṣṭādaśa dhānyāni samantātparikalpayet || 7 ||
pūrveṇānantaśayanaṃ pradyumnaṃ pūrvadakṣiṇe
prakṛtiṃ dakṣiṇe deśe saṃkarṣaṇamataḥ param || 8 ||
paścime caturo vedānaniruddhamataḥ param
agnimuttarato haimaṃ vāsudevamataḥ param || 9 ||
samantāduḍapīṭhasthānarcayetkāñcanānbudhaḥ
sthāpayedvastrasaṃvītānpūrṇakumbhāndaśaiva tu || 10 ||
daśaiva dhenavo deyāḥ sahemāmbaradohanāḥ
pādukopānahacchatracāmarāsanadarpaṇaiḥ
bhakṣyabhojyānnadīpekṣuphalamālyānulepanaiḥ || 11 ||
homādhivāsanānte ca snāpito vedapuṃgavaiḥ
imamuccārayenmantraṃ triḥ kṛtvā'tha pradakṣiṇam || 12 ||
namo'stu viśveśvara viśvadhāma jagatsavitre bhagavannamaste
saptarṣilokāmarabhūtaleśa garbheṇa sārdhaṃ vitarābhirakṣām || 13 ||
ye duḥkhitāste sukhino bhavantu prayāntu pāpāni carācarāṇām
tvaddānaśastrāhatapātakānāṃ brahmāṇḍadoṣāḥ pralayaṃ vrajantu || 14 ||
evaṃ praṇamyāmaraviśvagarbhaṃ dadyāddvijebhyo daśadhā vibhajya
bhāgadvayaṃ tatra guroḥ prakalpya samaṃ bhajeccheṣamanukrameṇa || 15 ||
svalpe ca homaṃ gurureka eva kuryādathaikāgnividhānayuktyā
sa eva saṃpūjyatamo'lpavitte yathoktavastrābharaṇādikena || 16 ||
itthaṃ ya etadakhilaṃ puruṣo'tra kuryādbrahmāṇḍadānamadhigamya mahadvimānam
nirdhūtakalmaṣaviśuddhatanurmurārerānandakṛtpadamupaiti sahāpsarobhiḥ || 17 ||
saṃtārayetpitṛpitāmahaputrapautrabandhupriyātithikalatraśatāṣṭakaṃ saḥ
brahmāṇḍadānaśakalīkṛtapātakaughamānandayecca jananīkulamapyaśeṣam || 18 ||
iti paṭhati śṛṇoti vā ya etatsurabhavaneṣu gṛheṣu dhārmikāṇām
matimapi ca dadāti modate'sāvamarapaterbhavane sahāpsarobhiḥ || 19 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe mahādānānukīrtane brahmāṇḍapradānavidhirnāma ṣaṭsaptatyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 276 ||

<277>

atha saptasaptatyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
matsya uvāca
kalpapādapadānākhyamataḥ paramanuttamam
mahādānaṃ pravakṣyāmi sarvapātakanāśanam || 1 ||
puṇyaṃ dinamathā''sādya tulāpuruṣadānavat
puṇyāhavācanaṃ kṛtvā lokeśāvāhanaṃ tathā || 2 ||
ṛtviṅmaḍapasaṃbhārabhūṣaṇācchādanādikam
kāñcanaṃ kārayedvṛkṣaṃ nānāphalasamanvitam || 3 ||
nānāvihagavastrāṇi bhūṣaṇāni ca kārayet
śaktitattripalādūrdhvamāsahasraṃ prakalpayet || 4 ||
ardhakḷptasuvarṇasya kārayetkalpapādapam
guḍaprasthopariṣṭācca sitavastrayugānvitam || 5 ||
brahmaviṣṇuśivopetaṃ pañcaśākhaṃ sabhāskaram
kāmadevamadhastācca sakalatraṃ prakalpayet || 6 ||
saṃtānaṃ pūrvatastadvatturīyāṃśena kalpayet
mandāraṃ dakṣiṇe bhāge śriyā sārdhaṃ ghṛtopari || 7 ||
paścime pāribhadraṃ tu sāvitryā saha jīrake
surabhīsaṃyutaṃ tadvattileṣu haricandanam || 8 ||
turīyāṃśena kurvīta saumyena phalasaṃyutam
kauśeyavastrasaṃvītānikṣumālyaphalānvitān || 9 ||
tathā'ṣṭau pūrṇakalaśānpādukāśanabhājanam
dīpikopānahacchatracāmarāsanasaṃyutam || 10 ||
phalamālyayutaṃ tadvadupariṣṭādvitānakam
tathā'ṣṭādaśa dhānyāni samantātparikalpayet || 11 ||
homādhivāsanānte ca snāpito vedapuṃgavaiḥ
triḥ pradakṣiṇamāvṛtya mantrametamudīrayet || 12 ||
namaste kalpavṛkṣāya cintitārthapradāyine
viśvaṃbharāya devāya namaste viśvamūrtaye || 13 ||
yasmāttvameva viśvātmā brahmā sthāṇurdivākaraḥ
mūrtāmūrtaparaṃ bījamataḥ pāhi sanātana || 14 ||
tvamevāmṛtasarvasvamanantaḥ puruṣo'vyayaḥ
saṃtānādyairupetaḥ sanpāhi saṃsārasāgarāt || 15 ||
evamāmantrya taṃ dadyādgurave kalpapādapam
catubhyaścātha ṛtvigbhyaḥ saṃtānādīnprakalpayet || 16 ||
svalpe tvekāgnivatkuryādgurave cābhipūjanam
na vittaśāṭhyaṃ kurvīta na ca vismayavānbhavet || 17 ||
anena vidhinā yastu pradadyātkalpapādapam
sarvapāpavinirmuktaḥ so'śvamedhaphalaṃ bhavet || 18 ||
apsarobhiḥ parivṛtaḥ siddhacāraṇakiṃnaraiḥ
bhūtānbhavyāṃśca manujāṃstārayedgotrasaṃyutān || 19 ||
stūyamāno divaḥ pṛṣṭhe putrapautraprapautrakaiḥ
vimānenārkavarṇena viṣṇulokaṃ sa gacchati || 20 ||
divi kalpaśataṃ tiṣṭhedrā jarājo bhavettataḥ
nārāyaṇabalopeto nārāyaṇaparāyaṇaḥ
nārāyaṇakathāsakto nārāyaṇapuraṃ vrajet || 21 ||
yo vā paṭhetsakalakalpatarupradānaṃ yo vā śṛṇoti puruṣo'lpadhanaḥ smaredvā
so'pīndra lokamadhigamya sahāpsarobhirmanvantaraṃ vasati pāpavimuktadehaḥ || 22 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe mahādānānakīrtane kalpapādapapradānavidhirnāma saptasaptatyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 277 ||

<278>

athāṣṭasaptatyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
matsya uvāca
athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi mahādānamanuttamam
gosahasrapradānākhyaṃ sarvapāpaharaṃ param || 1 ||
puṇyāṃ tithiṃ samāsādya yugamanvantarādikām
payovrataṃ trirātraṃ syādekarātramathāpi vā || 2 ||
lokeśāvāhanaṃ kuryāttulāpuruṣadānavat
puṇyāhavācanaṃ kuryāddhomaḥ kāryastathaiva ca || 3 ||
ṛtviṅmaṇḍapasaṃbhārabhūṣaṇācchādanādikam
vṛṣaṃ lakṣaṇasaṃyuktaṃ vedimadhye'dhivāsayet || 4 ||
gosahasrādviniṣkṛṣya gavāṃ daśakameva ca
gosahasraṃ bahiḥ kuryādvastramālyavibhūṣitam
suvarṇaśṛṅgābharaṇaṃ raupyapādasamanvitam || 5 ||
antaḥ praveśya daśakaṃ vastramālyaiśca pūjayet
suvarṇaghaṇṭikāyuktaṃ kāṃsyadohanakānvitam || 6 ||
suvarṇatilakopetaṃ hemapaṭṭairalaṃkṛtam
kauśeyavastrasaṃvītaṃ mālyagandhasamanvitam || 7 ||
hemaratnamayaiḥ śṛṅgaiścāmarairupaśobhitam
pādukopānahacchatrabhājanāsanasaṃyutam || 8 ||
gavāṃ daśakamadhye syātkāñcano nandikeśvaraḥ
kauśeyavastrasaṃvīto nānābharaṇabhūṣitaḥ || 9 ||
lavaṇadro ṇaśikhare mālyekṣuphalasaṃyutaḥ
kuryātpalaśatādūrdhvaṃ sarvametadaśeṣataḥ || 10 ||
śaktitaḥ palasāhasratritayaṃ yāvadeva tu
gośate'pi daśāṃśena sarvametatsamācaret || 11 ||
puṇyakālaṃ samāsādya gītamaṅgalaniḥsvanaiḥ
sarvauṣadhyudakasnānasnāpito vedapuṃgavaiḥ || 12 ||
imamuccārayenmantraṃ gṛhītakusumāñjaliḥ
namo'stu viśvamūrtibhyo viśvamātṛbhya eva ca || 13 ||
lokādhivāsinābhyaśca rohiṇībhyo namo namaḥ
gavāmaṅgeṣu tiṣṭhanti bhūvanānyekaviṃśatiḥ || 14 ||
brahmādayastathā devā rohiṇyaḥ pāntu mātaraḥ
gāvo me agrataḥ santu gāvaḥ pṛṣṭhata eva ca || 15 ||
gāvaḥ śirasi me nityaṃ gavāṃ madhye vasāmyaham
yasmāttvaṃ vṛṣarūpeṇa dharma eva sanātanaḥ || 16 ||
aṣṭamūrteradhiṣṭhānamataḥ pāhi sanātana
ityāmantrya tato dadyādgurave nandikeśvaram || 17 ||
sarvopakaraṇopetaṃ goyutaṃ ca vicakṣaṇaḥ
ṛtvigbhyo dhenumekaikāṃ daśakādvinivedayet || 18 ||
gavāṃ ca śatamekaikaṃ tadardhaṃ vā'tha viṃśatim
daśa pañcātha vā dadyādanyebhyastadanujñayā || 19 ||
naikā bahubhyo dātavyā yato doṣakarī bhavet
bahvyaścaikasya dātavyā dhīmatā''rogyavṛddhaye || 20 ||
payovrataḥ punastiṣṭhedekāhaṃ gosahasradaḥ
śrāvayecchṛṇuyādvā'pi mahādānānukīrtanam || 21 ||
taddine brahmacārī syādyadīcchedvipulāṃ śriyam
anena vidhinā yastu gosahasraprado bhavet
sarvapāpavinirmuktaḥ siddhacāraṇasevitaḥ || 22 ||
vimānenārkavarṇena kiṅkiṇījālamālinā
sarveṣāṃ lokapālānāṃ loka saṃpūjyate'maraiḥ || 23 ||
pratimanvantaraṃ tiṣṭhetputrapautrasamanvitaḥ
sapta lokānatikramya tataḥ śivapuraṃ vrajet || 24 ||
śatamekottaraṃ tadvatpitṝṇāṃ tārayedbudhaḥ
mātāmahānāṃ tadvacca putrapautrasamanvitaḥ
yāvatkalpaśataṃ tiṣṭhedrā jarājo bhavetpunaḥ || 25 ||
aśvamedhaśataṃ kuryācchivadhyānaparāyaṇaḥ
vaiṣṇavaṃ yogamāsthāya tato mucyeta bandhanāt || 26 ||
pitaraścābhinandanti gosahasrapradaṃ sutam
api syātsa kule'smākaṃ putro dauhitra eva vā
gosahasraprado bhūtvā narakāduddhariṣyati || 27 ||
tasya karmakaro vā syādapi dra ṣṭā tathaiva ca
saṃsārasāgarādasmādyo'smānsaṃtārayiṣyati || 28 ||
iti paṭhati ya etadgosahasrapradāna surabhuvanamupayātsaṃsmaredvā'tha paśyet
anubhavati mudaṃ vā mucyamāno nikāmaṃ prahatakaluṣadehaḥ so'pi yātīndra lokam || 29 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe mahādānānukīrtane gosahasrapradānavidhirnāmāṣṭasaptatyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 278 ||

<279>

atha navasaptatyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
matsya uvāca
athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi kāmadhenuvidhiṃ param
sarvakāmapradaṃ nṝṇāṃ mahāpātakanāśanam || 1 ||
lokeśāvāhanaṃ tadvaddhomaḥ kāryo'dhivāsanam
tulāpuruṣavatkuryātkuṇḍamaṇḍapavedikam || 2 ||
svalpe tvekāgnivatkuryādgururekaḥ samāhitaḥ
kāñcanasyātiśuddhasya dhenuṃ vatsaṃ ca kārayet || 3 ||
uttamā palasāhasrā tadardhena tu madhyamā
kanīyasī tadardhena kāmadhenuḥ prakīrtitā || 4 ||
śaktitastripalādūrdhvamaśakto'pīha kārayet
vedyāṃ kṛṣṇājinaṃ nyasya guḍaprasthasamanvitam || 5 ||
nyasedupari tāṃ dhenuṃ mahāratnairalaṃkṛtām
kumbhāṣṭakasamopetāṃ nānāphalasamanvitām || 6 ||
tathā'ṣṭādaśa dhānyāni samantātparikalpayet
ikṣudaṇḍāṣṭakaṃ tadvannānāphalasamanvitam
bhājanaṃ cā'sanaṃ tadvattāmradohanakaṃ tathā || 7 ||
kauśeyavastradvayasaṃyutāṃ gāṃ dīpātapatrābharaṇābhirāmām
sacāmarāṃ kuṇḍalinīṃ saghaṇṭāṃ suvarṇaśṛṅgīṃ parirūpyapādām || 8 ||
rasaiśca sarvaiḥ parito'bhijuṣṭāṃ haridra yā puṣpaphalairanekaiḥ
ajājikustumburuśarkarādibhirvitānakaṃ copari pañcavarṇam || 9 ||
snātastato maṅgalavedaghoṣaiḥ pradakṣiṇīkṛtya sapuṣpahastaḥ
āvāhayettāṃ guruṇoktamantrairdvijāya dadyādatha darbhapāṇiḥ || 10 ||
tvaṃ sarvadevagaṇamandiramaṅgabhūtā viśveśvari tripathagodadhiparvatānām
tvaddānaśastraśakalīkṛtapātakaughaḥ prāpto'smi nirvṛtimatīva parāṃ namāmi || 11 ||
loke yathepsitaphalārthavidhāyinīṃ tvāmāsādya ko hi bhavaduḥkhamupaiti martyaḥ ।
saṃsāraduḥkhaśamanāya yatasva kāmaṃ tvāṃ kāmadhenumiti devagaṇā vadanti || 12 ||
āmantrya śīlakularūpaguṇānvitāya viprāya yaḥ kanakadhenumimāṃ pradadyāt
prāpnoti dhāma sa puraṃdaradevajuṣṭaṃ kanyāgaṇaiḥ parivṛtaḥ padamindumauleḥ || 13 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe mahādānānukīrtane hiraṇyakāmadhenupradānavidhirnāmaikonāśītyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 279 ||

<280>

athāśītyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
matsya uvāca
athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi hiraṇyāśvavidhiṃ param
yasya pradānādbhuvane cānantaṃ phalamaśnute || 1 ||
puṇyāṃ tithimathā''sādya kṛtvā brāhmaṇavācanam
lokeśāvāhanaṃ kuryāttulāpuruṣadānavat || 2 ||
ṛtviṅmaṇḍapasaṃbhārabhūṣaṇācchādanādikam
svalpe tvekāgnivatkuryāddhemavājimakhaṃ budhaḥ || 3 ||
sthāpayedvedimadhye tu kṛṣṇājinatilopari
kauśeyavastrasaṃvītaṃ kārayeddhemavājinam || 4 ||
śaktitastripalādūrdhvamā sahasrapalādbudhaḥ
pādukopānahacchatracāmarāsanabhājanaiḥ || 5 ||
pūrṇakumbhāṣṭakopetaṃ mālyekṣuphalasaṃyutam
śayyāṃ sopaskarāṃ tadvaddhemamārtaṇḍasaṃyutām || 6 ||
tataḥ sarvauṣadhisnānasnāpito vedapuṃgavaiḥ
imamuccārayenmantraṃ gṛhītakusumāñjaliḥ || 7 ||
namaste sarvadeveśa vedāharaṇalampaṭa
vājirūpeṇa māmasmātpāhi saṃsārasāgarāt || 8 ||
tvameva saptadhā bhūtvā chandorūpeṇa bhāskara
yasmādbhāsayase lokānataḥ pāhi sanātana || 9 ||
evamuccārya gurave tamaśvaṃ vinivedayet
dattvā pāpakṣayādbhānorlokamabhyeti śāśvatam || 10 ||
gobhirvibhavataḥ sarvānṛtvijaścāpi pūjayet
sarvadhānyopakaraṇaṃ gurave vinivedayet || 11 ||
sarvaṃ śayyādikaṃ dattvā bhuñjītātailameva hi
purāṇaśravaṇaṃ tadvatkārayedbhojanādanu || 12 ||
imaṃ hiraṇyāśvavidhiṃ karoti yaḥ puṇyaṃ samāsādya dinaṃ narendra
vimuktapāpaḥ sa puraṃ murāreḥ prāpnoti siddhairabhipūjitaḥ san || 13 ||
iti paṭhati ya etaddhemavājipradānaṃ sakalakaluṣamuktaḥ so'śvamedhena yuktaḥ ।
kanakamayavimānenārkalokaṃ prayāti tridaśapativadhūbhiḥ pūjyate yo'bhipaśyet || 14 ||
yo vā śṛṇoti puruṣo'lpadhanaḥ smaredvā hemāśvadānamabhinandayatīha loka ।
sā'pi prayāti hatakalmaṣaśuddhadehaḥ sthānaṃ puraṃdaramaheśvaradevajuṣṭam || 15 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe mahādānānukīrtane hiraṇyāśvapradānavidhirnāmāśītyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 280 ||

<281>

athaikāśītyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
matsya uvāca
athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi mahādānamanuttamam
puṇyamaśvarathaṃ nāma mahāpātakanāśanam || 1 ||
puṇyaṃ dinamathā'sādya kṛtvā brāhmaṇavācanam
lokeśāvāhanaṃ kuryāttulāpuruṣadānavat || 2 ||
ṛtviṅmaṇḍapasaṃbhārabhūṣaṇācchādanādikam
kṛṣṇājine tilānkṛtvā kāñcanaṃ sthāpayedra tham || 3 ||
saptāśvaṃ caturaśvaṃ vā catuścakraṃ sakūbaram
aindra nīlena kumbhena dhvajarūpeṇa saṃyutam || 4 ||
lokapālāṣṭakopetaṃ padmarāgadalānvitam
caturaḥ pūrṇakalaśāndhānyānyaṣṭādaśaiva tu || 5 ||
kauśeyavastrasaṃyuktamupariṣṭādvitānakam
mālyekṣuphalasaṃyuktaṃ puruṣeṇa samanvitam || 6 ||
yo yadbhaktaḥ pumānkuryātsa tannāmnā'dhivāsanam
chatracāmarakauśeyavastropānahapādukam || 7 ||
gobhirvibhavataḥ sārdhaṃ dadyācca śayanādikam
abhāvāttripalādūrdhvaṃ śaktitaḥ kārayedbudhaḥ || 8 ||
aśvāṣṭakena saṃyuktaṃ caturbhiratha vājibhiḥ
dvābhyāmapi yutaṃ dadyāddhemasiṃhadhvajānvitam || 9 ||
cakrarakṣāvubhau tasya turagasthāvathāśvinau
puṇyakālamathāvāpya pūrvavatsnāpito dvijaiḥ || 10 ||
triḥ pradakṣiṇamāvṛtya gṛhītakusumāñjaliḥ
śuklamālyāmbaro dadyādimaṃ mantramudīrayet || 11 ||
namo namaḥ pāpavināśanāya viśvātmane vedaturaṃgamāya
dhāmnāmadhīśāya divākarāya pāpaughadāvānala dehi śāntim || 12 ||
vasvaṣṭakādityamarudgaṇānāṃ tvameva dhātā paramaṃ nidhānam
yatastato me hṛdayaṃ prayātu dharmaikatānatvamaghaughanāśāt || 13 ||
iti turagarathapradānametadbhavabhayasūdanamatra yaḥ karoti
sa kaluṣapaṭalairvimuktadehaḥ paramamupaiti padaṃ pinākapāṇeḥ || 14 ||
dedīpyamānavapuṣā vijitaprabhāvamākramya maṇḍalamakhaṇḍitacaṇḍabhānoḥ ।
siddhāṅganānayanaṣaṭpadapīyamānavaktrāmbujo'mbujabhavena ciraṃ sahā''ste || 15 ||
iti paṭhati śṛṇoti vā ya itthaṃ kanakaturagarathapradānamasmin
na sa narakapuraṃ vrajetkadācinnarakariporbhavanaṃ prayāti bhūyaḥ || 16 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe mahādānānukīrtane hiraṇyāśvarathapradānavidhirnāmaikāśītyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 281 ||

<282>

atha dvyaśītyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
matsya uvāca
athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi hemahastirathaṃ śubham
yasya pradānādbhavanaṃ vaiṣṇavaṃ yāti mānavaḥ || 1 ||
puṇyāṃ tithimathā''sādya tulāpuruṣadānavat
vipravācanakaṃ kuryāllokeśāvāhanaṃ budhaḥ
ṛtviṅmaṇḍapasaṃbhārabhūṣaṇācchādanādikam || 2 ||
atrāpyupoṣitastadvadbrāhmaṇaiḥ saha bhojanam
kuryātpuṣparathākāraṃ kāñcanaṃ maṇimaṇḍitam || 3 ||
valabhībhirvicitrābhiścatuścakrasamanvitam
kṛṣṇājine tiladro ṇaṃ kṛtvā saṃsthāpayedra tham || 4 ||
lokapālāṣṭakopetaṃ brahmārkaśivasaṃyutam
madhye nārāyaṇopetaṃ lakṣmīpuṣṭisamanvitam || 5 ||
tathā'ṣṭādaśa dhānyāni bhājanāsanacandanaiḥ
dīpikopānahacchatradarpaṇaṃ pādukānvitam || 6 ||
dhvaje tu garuḍaṃ kuryātkūbarāgre vināyakam
nānāphalasamāyuktamupariṣṭādvitānakam || 7 ||
kauśeyaṃ pañcavarṇaṃ tu amlānakusumānvitam
caturbhiḥ kalaśaiḥ sārdhaṃ gobhiraṣṭābhiranvitam || 8 ||
caturbhirhemamātaṅgairmuktādāmavibhūṣitaiḥ
svarūpataḥ karibhyāṃ ca yuktaṃ kṛtvā nivedayet || 9 ||
kuryāpañcapalādūrdhvamā bhārādapi śaktitaḥ
tathā maṅgalaśabdena snāpito vedapuṃgavaiḥ || 10 ||
triḥ pradakṣiṇamāvṛtya gṛhītakusumāñjaliḥ
imamuccārayenmantraṃ brāhmaṇebhyo nivedayet || 11 ||
namo namaḥ śaṃkarapadmajārkalokeśavidyādharavāsudevaiḥ
tvaṃ sevyase vedapurāṇayajñaistejomayasyandana pāhi tasmāt || 12 ||
yattatpadaṃ paramaguhyatamaṃ murārerānandahetuguṇarūpavimuktamatra
yogaikamānasadṛśo munayaḥ samādhau paśyanti tattvamasi nātha rathādhirūḍha || 13 ||
yasmāttvameva bhavasāgarasaṃplutānāmānandabhāṇḍamṛtamadhvarapānapātram
tasmādaghaughaśamanena kuru prasādaṃ cāmīkarebharatha mādhava saṃpradānāt || 14 ||
itthaṃ praṇamya kanakebharathapradānaṃ yaḥ kārayetsakalapāpavimuktadehaḥ
vidyādharāmaramunīndra gaṇābhijuṣṭhaṃ prāpnotyasau padamatīndri yamindumauleḥ || 15 ||
kṛtaduritavitānaprajvaladvahnijālavyatikarakṛtadāhodvegabhājo'pi bandhūn
nayati sa pitṛputrānbāndhavānapyaśeṣānkṛtagajarathadānācchāśvataṃ sadma viṣṇoḥ || 16 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe mahādānānukīrtane hemahastirathapradānavidhirnāma dvyaśītyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 282 ||

<283>

atha tryaśītyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
matsya uvāca
athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi mahādānamanuttamam
pañcalāṅgalakaṃ nāma mahāpātakanāśanam || 1 ||
puṇyāṃ tithimathā''sādya yugādigrahaṇādikām
bhūmidānaṃ naro dadyātpañcalāṅgalakānvitam || 2 ||
kharvaṭaṃ kheṭakaṃ vā'pi grāmaṃ vā sasyaśālinam
nivartanaśataṃ vā'pi tadardhaṃ vā'pi śaktitaḥ || 3 ||
sāradārumayānkṛtvā halānpañca vicakṣaṇaḥ
sarvopakaraṇairyuktānanyānpañca ca kāñcanān
kuryātpañcapalādūrdhvamāsahasrapalāvadhi || 4 ||
vṛṣāllam̐ kṣaṇasaṃyuktāndaśa caiva dhuraṃdharān
suvarṇaśṛṅgābharaṇānmuktālāṅgūlabhūṣaṇān || 5 ||
rūpyapādāgratilakānraktakauśeyabhūṣaṇān
sragdāmacandanayutāñśālāyāmadhivāsayet || 6 ||
parjanyādityarudre bhyaḥ pāyasaṃ nirvapeccarum
ekasminneva kuṇḍe tu gurustebhyo nivedayet || 7 ||
palāśasamidhastadvadājyaṃ kṛṣṇatilāstathā
tulāpuruṣavatkuryāllokeśāvāhanaṃ budhaḥ || 8 ||
tato maṅgalaśabdena śuklamālyāmbaro budhaḥ
āhūya dvijadāṃpatyaṃ hemasūtrāṅgulīyakaiḥ || 9 ||
kauśeyavastrakaṭakairmaṇibhiścābhipūjayet
śayyāṃ sopaskarāṃ dadyāddhenumekāṃ payasvinīm || 10 ||
tathā'ṣṭādaśa dhānyāni samantādadhivāsayet
tataḥ pradakṣiṇīkṛtya gṛhītakusumāñjaliḥ || 11 ||
imamuccārayenmantramatha sarvaṃ nivedayet
yasmāddevagaṇāḥ sarve sthāvarāṇi carāṇi ca || 12 ||
dhuraṃdharāṅge tiṣṭhanti tasmādbhaktiḥ śive'stu me
yasmācca bhūmidānasya kalāṃ nārhanti ṣoḍaśīm || 13 ||
dānānyanyāni me bhaktirdharma eva dṛḍhā bhavet
daṇḍena saptahastena triṃśaddaṇḍaṃ nivartanam || 14 ||
tribhāgahīnaṃ gocarmamānamāha prajāpatiḥ
mānenānena yo dadyānnivartanaśataṃ budhaḥ
vidhinā'nena tasyā''śu kṣīyate pāpasaṃhatiḥ || 15 ||
tadardhamathavā dadyādapi gocarmamātrakam
bhavanasthānamātraṃ vā so'pi pāpaiḥ pramucyate || 16 ||
yāvanti lāṅgalakamārgamukhāni bhūmerbhāsāṃ paterduhituraṅgajaromakāṇi
tāvanti śaṃkarapure sa samā hi tiṣṭhedbhūmipradānamiha yaḥ kurute manuṣyaḥ || 17 ||
gandharvakiṃnarasurāsurasiddhasaṃghairādhūtacāmaramupetya mahadvimānam
saṃpūjyate pitṛpitāmahabandhuyuktaḥ śaṃbhoḥ padaṃ vrajati cāmaranāyakaḥ san || 18 ||
indra tvamapyadhigataṃ kṣayamabhyupaiti gobhūmilāṅgaladhuraṃdharasaṃpradānāt
tasmādaghaughapaṭalakṣayakāri bhūmerdānaṃ vidheyamiti bhūtibhavodbhavāya || 19 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe mahādānānukīrtane pañcalāṅgalapradānavidhirnāma tryaśītyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 283 ||

<284>

atha caturaśītyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
matsya uvāca
athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi dharādānamanuttamam
pāpakṣayakaraṃ nṝṇāmamaṅgalyavināśanam || 1 ||
kārayetpṛthivīṃ haimīṃ jambūdvīpānukāriṇīm
maryādāparvatavatīṃ madhye merusamanvitām || 2 ||
lokapālāṣṭakopetāṃ navavarṣasamanvitām

nadīnadasamopetāṃ saptasāgaraveṣṭitām || 3mahāratnasamākīrṇāṃ vasurudrā rkasaṃyutām
hemnaḥ palasahasreṇa tadardhenātha śaktitaḥ || 4 ||
śatatrayeṇa vā kuryāddviśatena śatena vā
kuryātpañcapalādūrdhvamaśakto'pi vicakṣaṇaḥ || 5 ||
tulāpuruṣavatkuryāllokeśāvāhanaṃ budhaḥ
ṛtviṅmaṇḍapasaṃbhārabhūṣaṇācchādanādikam || 6 ||
vedyāṃ kṛṣṇājinaṃ kṛtvā tilānupari vinyaset
tathā'ṣṭādaśa dhānyāni rasāṃśca lavaṇādikān || 7 ||
tathā'ṣṭau pūrṇakalaśānsamantātparikalpayet
vitānakaṃ ca kauśeyaṃ phalāni vividhāni ca || 8 ||
tathāṃ'śukāni ramyāṇi śrīkhaṇḍaśakalāni ca
ityevaṃ kārayitvā tāmadhivāsanapūrvakam || 9 ||
śuklamālyāmbaradharaḥ śuklābharaṇabhūṣitaḥ
pradakṣiṇaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā gṛhītakusumāñjaliḥ || 10 ||
puṇyaṃ kālamathā''sādya mantrānetānudīrayet
namaste sarvadevānāṃ tvameva bhavanaṃ yataḥ || 11 ||
dhātrī tvaṃ sarvabhūtānāmataḥ pāhi vasuṃdhare
vasu dhārayase yasmādvasu cātīva nirmalam || 12 ||
vasuṃdharā tato jātā tasmātpāhi bhayādalam
caturmukho'pi no gacchedyasmādantaṃ tavācale || 13 ||
anantāyai namastasmātpāhi saṃsārakardamāt
tvameva lakṣmīrgovinde śive gaurīti cā''sthitā || 14 ||
gāyatrī brahmaṇaḥ pārśve jyotsnā candre ravau prabhā
buddhirbṛhaspatau khyātā medhā muniṣu saṃsthitā || 15 ||
viśvaṃ vyāpya sthitā yasmāttato viśvaṃbharā smṛtā
dhṛtiḥ sthitiḥ kṣamā kṣoṇī pṛthvī vasumatī rasā || 16 ||
etābhirmūrtibhiḥ pāhi devi saṃsārasāgarāt
evamuccārya tāṃ devīṃ brāhmaṇebhyo nivedayet || 17 ||
dharārdhaṃ vā caturbhāgaṃ gurave pratipādayet
śeṣaṃ caivātha ṛtvigbhyaḥ praṇipatya visarjayet || 18 ||
anena vidhinā yastu dadyāddhemadharāṃ śubhām
puṇyakāle tu saṃprāpte sa padaṃ yāti vaiṣṇavam || 19 ||
vimānenārkavarṇena kiṅkiṇījālamālinā
nārāyaṇapuraṃ gatvā kalpatrayamathā''vaset
pitṝnputrāṃśca pautrāṃśca tārayedekaviṃśatim || 20 ||
iti paṭhati ya itthaṃ yaḥ śṛṇoti prasaṅgādapi kaluṣavitānairmuktadehaḥ samantāt
divamamaravadhūbhiryāti saṃprārthyamāno padamamarasahasraiḥ sevitaṃ candra mauleḥ || 21 ||
