iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe mahādānānukīrtane hemapṛthivīdānamāhātmyaṃ nāma caturaśītyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 284 ||

<285>

atha pañcāśītyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
matsya uvāca
athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi mahādānamanuttamam
viśvacakramiti khyātaṃ mahāpātakanāśanam || 1 ||
tapanīyasya śuddhasya viśvacakraṃ tu kārayet
śreṣṭhaṃ palasahasreṇa tadardhena tu madhyamam || 2 ||
tasyārdhena kaniṣṭhaṃ syādviśvacakramudāhṛtam
anyadviṃśatpalādūrdhvamaśakto'pi nivedayet || 3 ||
ṣoḍaśāraṃ tataścakraṃ bhramannemyaṣṭakāvṛtam
nābhipadme sthitaṃ viṣṇuṃ yogārūḍhaṃ caturbhujam || 4 ||
śaṅkhacakre'sya pārśve tu devyaṣṭakasamāvṛtam
dvitīyāvaraṇe tadvatpūrvato jalaśāyinam || 5 ||
atrirbhṛgurvasiṣṭhaśca brahmā kaśyapa eva ca
matsyaḥ kūrmo varāhaśca narasiṃho'tha vāmanaḥ || 6 ||
rāmo rāmaśca kṛṣṇaśca buddhaḥ kalkīti ca kramāt
tṛtīyāvaraṇe gaurī mātṛbhirvasubhiryutā || 7 ||
caturthe dvādaśā''dityā vedāścatvāra eva ca
pañcame pañca bhūtāni rudrā ścaikādaśaiva tu || 8 ||
lokapālāṣṭakaṃ ṣaṣṭhe diṅmātaṅgāstathaiva ca
saptame'strāṇi sarvāṇi maṅgalāni ca kārayet || 9 ||
antarāntarato devānvinyasedaṣṭame punaḥ
tulāpuruṣavaccheṣaṃ samantātparikalpayet || 10 ||
ṛtviṅmaṇḍapasaṃbhārabhūṣaṇācchādanādikam
viśvacakraṃ tataḥ kuryātkṛṣṇājinatilopari || 11 ||
tathā'ṣṭādaśa dhānyāni rasāṃśca lavaṇādikān
pūrṇakumbhāṣṭakaṃ caiva vastrāṇi vividhāni ca || 12 ||
mālyekṣuphalaratnāni vitānaṃ cāpi kārayet
tato maṅgalaśabdena snātaḥ śuklāmbaro gṛhī
homādhivāsanānte vai gṛhītakusumāñjaliḥ || 13 ||
imamuccārayenmantraṃ triḥ kṛtvā tu pradakṣiṇam
namo viśvamayāyeti viśvacakrātmane namaḥ || 14 ||
paramānandarūpī tvaṃ pāhi naḥ pāpakardamāt
tejomayamidaṃ yasmātsadā paśyanti yoginaḥ || 15 ||
hṛdi tattvaṃ guṇātītaṃ viśvacakraṃ namāmyaham
vāsudeve sthitaṃ cakraṃ cakramadhye tu mādhavaḥ || 16 ||
anyonyādhārarūpeṇa praṇamāmi sthitāviha
viśvacakramidaṃ yasmātsarvapāpaharaṃ param || 17 ||
āyudhaṃ cāpi vāsaśca bhavāduddhara māmataḥ
ityāmantrya ca yo dadyādviśvacakraṃ vimatsaraḥ || 18 ||
vimuktaḥ sarvapāpebhyo viṣṇuloke mahīyate
vaikuṇṭhalokamāsādya caturbāhuḥ sanātanaḥ || 19 ||
sevyate'psarasāṃ saṃghaistiṣṭhetkalpaśatatrayam
praṇamedvā'tha yaḥ kṛtvā viśvacakraṃ dine dine
tasyā''yurvardhate nityaṃ lakṣmīśca vipulā bhavet || 20 ||
iti sakalajagatsurādhivāsaṃ vitarati yastapanīyaṣoḍaśāram
haribhavanamupāgataḥ sa siddhaiściramabhigamya namasyate śirobhiḥ || 21 ||
asudarśanatāṃ prayāti śatrormadanasudarśanatāṃ ca kāminībhyaḥ
sa sudarśanakeśavānurūpaḥ kanakasudarśanadānadagdhapāpaḥ || 22 ||
kṛtaguruduritāni ṣoḍaśārapravitaraṇe pravarākṛtirmurāreḥ
abhibhavati bhavodbhavāni bhittvā bhavamabhito bhavane bhayāni bhūyaḥ || 23 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe mahādānānukīrtane viśvacakrapradānavidhirnāma pañcāśītyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 285 ||

<286>

atha ṣaḍaśītyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
matsya uvāca
athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi mahādānamanuttamam
mahākalpalatānāma mahāpātakanāśanam || 1 ||
puṇyāṃ tithimathā''sādya kṛtvā brāhmaṇavācanam
ṛtviṅmaṇḍapasaṃbhārabhūṣaṇācchādanādikam || 2 ||
tulāpuruṣavatkuryāllokeśāvāhanaṃ budhaḥ
cāmīkaramayīḥ kuryāddaśa kalpalatāḥ samāḥ || 3 ||
nānāpuṣpaphalopetā nānāṃśukavibhūṣitāḥ
vidyādharasuparṇānāṃ mithunairupaśobhitāḥ || 4 ||
puṣpāṇyāditsubhiḥ siddhaiḥ phalāni ca vihaṃgamaiḥ
lokapālānukāriṇyaḥ kartavyāstāsu devatāḥ || 5 ||
brāhmīmanantaśaktiṃ ca lavaṇasyopari nyaset
adhastāllatayormadhye padmaśaṅkhakare śubhe || 6 ||
ibhāsanasthā tu guḍe pūrvataḥ kuliśāyudhā
rajanyajasthitā'gnāyī suvapāṇirathānale || 7 ||
yāmye ca mahiṣārūḍhā gadinī taṇḍulopari
ghṛte tu nairṛtī sthāpyā sakhaḍgā dakṣiṇāpare || 8 ||
vāruṇe vāruṇī kṣīre jhaṣasthā nāgapāśinī
patākinī ca vāyavye mṛgasthā śarkaropari || 9 ||
saumyā tileṣu saṃsthāpyā śaṅkhinī nidhisaṃsthitā
māheśvarī vṛṣārūḍhā navanīte triśūlinī || 10 ||
maulinyo varadāstadvatkartavyā bālakānvitāḥ
śaktyā pañcapalādūrdhvamā sahasrātprakalpayet || 11 ||
sarvāsāmupari sthāpyaṃ pañcavarṇaṃ vitānakam
dhenavo daśa kumbhāśca vastrayugmāṇi caiva hi || 12 ||
madhyame dve tu gurave ṛtvigbhyo'nyāstathaiva ca
tato maṅgalaśabdena snātaḥ śuklāmbaro budhaḥ
triḥ pradakṣiṇamāvṛtya mantrametamudīrayet || 13 ||
namo namaḥ pāpavināśinībhyo brahmāṇḍalokeśvarapālinībhyaḥ
āśaṃsitādhikyaphalapradābhyo digbhyastathā kalpalatāvadhūbhyaḥ || 14 ||
iti sakaladigaṅganāpradānaṃ bhavabhayasūdanakāri yaḥ karoti
abhimataphalade sa nāgaloke vasati pitāmahavatsarāṇi triṃśat || 15 ||
pitṛśatamatha tārayedbhavābdherbhavaduritaughavighātaśuddhadehaḥ
surapativanitāsahasrasaṃkhyaiḥ parivṛtamambujasaṃsadā'bhivandyaḥ || 16 ||
iti vidhānamidaṃ digaṅganānāṃ kanakakalpalatāvinivedakam
paṭhati yaḥ smaratīha tathaikṣate sa padameti puraṃdarasevitam || 17 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe mahādānānukīrtane kanakakalpalatāpradānavidhirnāma ṣaḍaśītyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 286 ||

<287>

atha saptāśītyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
matsya uvāca
athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi mahādānamanuttamam
saptasāgarakaṃ nāma sarvapāpapraṇāśanam || 1 ||
puṇyaṃ dinamathā''sādya kṛtvā brāhmaṇavācanam
tulāpuruṣavatkuryāllokeśāvāhanaṃ budhaḥ || 2 ||
ṛtviṅmaṇḍapasaṃbhārabhūṣaṇācchādanādikam
kārayetsapta kuṇḍāni kāñcanāni vicakṣaṇaḥ || 3 ||
prādeśamātrāṇi tathā'ratnimātrāṇi vai punaḥ
kuryātsaptapalādūrdhvamā sahasrācca śaktitaḥ || 4 ||
saṃsthāpyāni ca sarvāṇi kṛṣṇājinatilopari
prathamaṃ pūrayetkuṇḍaṃ lavaṇena vicakṣaṇaḥ || 5 ||
dvitīyaṃ payasā tadvattṛtīyaṃ sarpiṣā punaḥ
caturthaṃ tu guḍenaiva dadhnā pañcamameva ca || 6 ||
ṣaṣṭhaṃ śarkarayā tadvatsaptamaṃ tīrthavāriṇā
sthāpayellavaṇasthaṃ tu brahmāṇaṃ kāñcanaṃ śubham || 7 ||
keśavaṃ kṣīramadhye tu ghṛtamadhye maheśvaram
bhāskaraṃ guḍamadhye tu dadhimadhye niśādhipam || 8 ||
śarkarāyāṃ nyasellakṣmīṃ jalamadhye tu pārvatīm
sarveṣu sarvaratnāni dhānyāni ca samantataḥ || 9 ||
tulāpuruṣavaccheṣamatrāpi parikalpayet
tato vāruṇahomānte snāpito vedapuṃgavaiḥ || 10 ||
triḥ pradakṣiṇamāvṛtya mantrānetānudīrayet
namo vaḥ sarvasiṃdhūnāmādhārebhyaḥ sanātanāḥ
jantūnāṃ prāṇadebhyaśca samudre bhyo namo namaḥ || 11 ||
kṣīrodakājyadadhimādhuralāvaṇekṣusārāmṛtena bhuvanatrayajīvasaṃghān
ānandayanti vasubhiśca yato bhavantastasmānmamāpyaghavighātamalaṃ diśantu || 12 ||
yasmātsamastabhuvaneṣu bhavanta eva tīrthāmarāsurasubaddhamaṇipradānam
pāpakṣayāmṛtavilepanabhūṣaṇāya lokasya bibhrati tadastu mamāpi lakṣmīḥ || 13 ||
iti dadāti rasāmṛtasaṃyutāñchuciravismayavāniha sāgarān
amalakāñcanavarṇamayānasau padamupaiti hareramarārcitaḥ || 14 ||
sakalapāpavidhautavirājitaḥ pitṛpitāmahaputrakalatrakam
narakalokasamākulamapyayaṃ jhaṭiti so'pi nayecchivamandiram || 15 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe mahādānānukīrtane saptasāgarapradānavidhirnāma saptāśītyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 287 ||

<288>

athāṣṭāśītyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
matsya uvāca
athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi mahādānamanuttamam
ratnadhenviti vikhyātaṃ golokaphaladaṃ nṛṇām || 1 ||
puṇyaṃ dinamathā''sādya tulāpuruṣadānavat
lokeśāvāhanaṃ kṛtvā tato dhenuṃ prakalpayet || 2 ||
bhūmau kṛṣṇājinaṃ kṛtvā lavaṇadro ṇasaṃyutam
dhenuṃ ratnamayīṃ kuryātsaṃkalpya vidhipūrvakam || 3 ||
sthāpayetpadmarāgāṇāmekāśītiṃ mukhe budhaḥ
puṣparāgaśataṃ tadvadghoṇāyāṃ parikalpayet || 4 ||
lalāṭe hematilakaṃ muktāphalaśataṃ dṛśoḥ
bhrūyuge vidrumaśataṃ śuktī karṇadvaye smṛte || 5 ||
kāñcanāni ca śṛṅgāṇi śiro vajraśatātmakam
grīvāyāṃ netrapaṭalaṃ gomedakaśatānvitam || 6 ||
indra nīlaśataṃ pṛṣṭhe vaidūryaśatapārśvake
sphāṭikairudaraṃ tadvatsaugandhikaśataiḥ kaṭim || 7 ||
khurā hemamayāḥ kāryāḥ pucchaṃ muktāvalīmayam
sūryakāntendukāntau ca ghrāṇe karpūracandane || 8 ||
kuṅkumāni ca romāṇi raupyanābhiṃ ca kārayet
gārutmataśataṃ tadvadapāne parikalpayet || 9 ||
tathā'nyāni ca ratnāni sthāpayetsarvasaṃdhiṣu
kuryāccharkarayā jihvāṃ gomayaṃ ca guḍātmakam || 10 ||
gomūtramājyena tathā dadhidugdhaṃ svarūpataḥ
pucchāgre cāmaraṃ dadyātsamīpe tāmradohanam || 11 ||
kuṇḍalāni ca haimāni bhūṣaṇāni ca śaktitaḥ
kārayedevamevaṃ tu caturthāṃśena vatsakam || 12 ||
tathā dhānyāni sarvāṇi pādāścekṣumayāḥ smṛtāḥ
nānāphalāni sarvāṇi pañcavarṇaṃ vitānakam || 13 ||
evaṃ viracanaṃ kṛtvā tadvaddhomādhivāsanam
ṛtvigbhyo dakṣiṇāṃ dadyāddhenumāmantrayettataḥ
guḍadhenuvadāvāhya idaṃ codāharettataḥ || 14 ||
tvāṃ sarvadevagaṇadhāma yataḥ paṭhanti rudre ndra sūryakamalāsanavāsudevāḥ
tasmātsamastabhuvanatrayadehayuktā māṃ pāhi devi bhavasāgarapīḍyamānam || 15 ||
āmantrya cetthamabhitaḥ parivṛtya bhaktyā dadyāddvijāya gurave jalapūrvikāṃ tām
yaḥ puṇyamāpya dinamatra kṛtopavāsaḥ pāpairvimuktatanureti padaṃ murāreḥ || 16 ||
iti sakalavidhijño ratnadhenupradānaṃ vitarati sa vimānaṃ prāpya dedīpyamānam
sakalakaluṣamukto bandhubhiḥ putrapautraiḥ sa hi madanasarūpaḥ sthānamabhyeti śaṃbhoḥ || 17 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe mahādānānukīrtane ratnadhenupradānavidhirnāmāṣṭāśītyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 288 ||

<289>

athaikonanavatyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
matsya uvāca
athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi mahādānamanuttamam
mahābhūtaghaṭaṃ nāma mahāpātakanāśanam || 1 ||
puṇyāṃ tithi mathā''sādya kṛtvā brāhmaṇavācanam
ṛtviṅmaṇḍapasaṃbhārabhūṣaṇācchādanādikam || 2 ||
tulāpuruṣavatkuryāllokeśāvāhanādikam
kārayetkāñcanaṃ kumbhaṃ mahāratnācitaṃ budhaḥ || 3 ||
prādeśādaṅgulaśataṃ yāvatkuryātpramāṇataḥ
kṣīrājyapūritaṃ tadvatkalpavṛkṣasamanvitam || 4 ||
padmāsanagatāṃstatra brahmaviṣṇumaheśvarān
lokapālānmahendrāṃśca svasvavāhanamāsthitān
varāheṇoddhṛtāṃ tadvatkuryātpṛthvīṃ sapaṅkajām || 5 ||
varuṇaṃ cā''sanagataṃ kāñcanaṃ makaropari
hutāśanaṃ meṣagataṃ vāyuṃ kṛṣṇamṛgāsanam || 6 ||
tathā kośādhipaṃ kuryānmūṣakasthaṃ vināyakam
vinyasya ghaṭamadhye tānvedapañcakasaṃyutān || 7 ||
ṛgvedasyākṣasūtraṃ syādyajurvedasya paṅkajam
sāmavedasya vīṇā syādveṇuṃ dakṣiṇato nyaset || 8 ||
atharvavedasya punaḥ sruksruvau kamalaṃ kare
purāṇavedo varadaḥ sākṣasūtrakamaṇḍaluḥ || 9 ||
paritaḥ sarvadhānyāni cāmarāsanadarpaṇam
pādukopānahacchatraṃ dīpikābhūṣaṇāni ca || 10 ||
śayyāṃ ca jalakumbhāṃśca pañcavarṇaṃ vitānakam
snātvā'dhivāsanānte tu mantrametamudīrayet || 11 ||
namo vaḥ sarvadevānāmādhārebhyaścarācare
mahābhūtādhidevebhyaḥ śāntirastu śivaṃ mama || 12 ||
yasmānna kiṃcidapyasti mahābhūtairvinā kṛtam
brahmāṇḍe sarvabhūteṣu tasmācchrīrakṣayā'stu me || 13 ||
ityuccārya mahābhūtaghaṭaṃ yo vinivedayet
sarvapāpavinirmuktaḥ sa yāti paramāṃ gatim || 14 ||
vimānenārkavarṇena pitṛbandhusamanvitaḥ
stūyamāno varastrībhiḥ padamabhyeti vaiṣṇavam || 15 ||
ṣoḍaśaitāni yaḥ kuryānmahādānāni mānavaḥ
na tasya punarāvṛttiriha loke'bhijāyate || 16 ||
iha paṭhati ya itthaṃ vāsudevasya pārśve sasutapitṛkalatraḥ saṃśṛṇotīha samyak
muraripubhavane vai mandire vā'rkalakṣmyā tvamarapuravadhūbhirmodate so'pi kalpam || 17 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe mahādānānukīrtanaṃ nāmaikonanavatyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 289 ||

<290>

atha navatyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
manuruvāca
kalpamānaṃ tvayā proktaṃ manvantarayugeṣu ca
idānīṃ kalpanāmāni samāsātkathayācyuta || 1 ||
matsya uvāca
kalpānāṃ kīrtanaṃ vakṣye mahāpātakanāśanam
yasyānukīrtanādeva vedapuṇyena yujyate || 2 ||
prathamaḥ śvetakalpastu dvitīyo nīlalohitaḥ
vāmadevastṛtīyastu tato rāthaṃtaro'paraḥ || 3 ||
rauravaḥ pañcamaḥ proktaḥ ṣaṣṭho deva iti smṛtaḥ
saptamo'tha bṛhatkalpaḥ kandarpo'ṣṭama ucyate || 4 ||
sadyo'tha navamaḥ prokta īśāno daśamaḥ smṛtaḥ
tama ekādaśaḥ proktastathā sārasvataḥ paraḥ || 5 ||
trayodaśa udānastu gāruḍo'tha caturdaśaḥ
kaurmaḥ pañcadaśaḥ proktaḥ paurṇamāsyāmajāyata || 6 ||
ṣoḍaśo nārasiṃhastu samānastu tato'paraḥ
āgneyo'ṣṭādaśaḥ proktaḥ somakalpastathā paraḥ || 7 ||
mānavo viṃśatiḥ proktastatpumāniti cāparaḥ
vaikuṇṭhaścāparastadvallakṣmīkalpastathāparaḥ || 8 ||
caturviṃśatimaḥ proktaḥ sāvitrīkalpasaṃjñakaḥ
pañcaviṃśastato ghoro vārāhastu tato'paraḥ || 9 ||
saptaviṃśo'tha vairājo gaurīkalpastathā paraḥ
māheśvarastu sa proktastripuraṃ yatra ghātitam || 10 ||
pitṛkalpastathā'nte tu yā kuhūrbrahmaṇaḥ purā
ityevaṃ brahmaṇo māsaḥ sarvapātakanāśanaḥ || 11 ||
ādāveva hi māhātmyaṃ yasminyasya vidhīyate
tasya kalpasya tannāma vihitaṃ brahmaṇā purā || 12 ||
saṃkīrṇāstāmasāścaiva rājasāḥ sāttvikāstathā
rajastamomayāstadvadete triṃśadudāhṛtāḥ || 13 ||
saṃkīrṇeṣu sarasvatyāḥ pitṝṇāṃ vyuṣṭirucyate
agneḥ śivasya māhātmyaṃ tāmaseṣu divākare
rājaseṣu ca māhātmyamadhikaṃ brahmaṇaḥ smṛtam || 14 ||
yasminkalpe tu yatproktaṃ purāṇaṃ brahmaṇā purā
tasya tasya tu māhātmya tatsvarūpeṇa varṇyate || 15 ||
sāttvikeṣvadhika tadvadviṣṇormāhātmyamuttamam
tathaiva yogasaṃsiddhā gamiṣyanti parāṃ gatim || 16 ||
brāhmaṃ pādmamimaṃ yastu paṭhetparvaṇi parvaṇi
tasya dharme matirbrahmā karoti vipulāṃ śriyam || 17 ||
yastu dadyādimānkṛtvā haimānparvaṇi parvaṇi
brahmaviṣṇupure vāsaṃ munibhiḥ pūjyate divi || 18 ||
sarvapāpakṣayakaraṃ kalpadānaṃ yato bhavet
munirūpāṃstataḥ kṛtvā dadyātkalpānvicakṣaṇaḥ || 19 ||
purāṇasaṃhitā ceyaṃ tava bhūpa mayoditā
sarvapāpaharā nityamārogyaśrīphalapradā || 20 ||
brahmasaṃvatsaraśatādekāha śaivamucyate
śivavarṣaśatādekaṃ nimeṣaṃ vaiṣṇavaṃ viduḥ || 21 ||
yadā sa viṣṇurjāgarti tadedaṃ ceṣṭate jagat
yadā svapiti śāntātmā tadā sarvaṃ nimīlati || 22 ||
sūta uvāca
ityuktvā devadeveśo matsyarūpī janārdanaḥ
paśyatāṃ sarvabhūtānāṃ tatraivāntaradhīyata || 23 ||
vaivasvato hi bhagavānvisṛjya vividhāḥ prajāḥ
svāntaraṃ pālayāmāsa mārtaṇḍakulavardhanaḥ || 24 ||
yasya manvantaraṃ caitadadhunā cānuvartate
puṇyaṃ pavitrametadvaḥ kathitaṃ matsyabhāṣitam
purāṇaṃ sarvaśāstrāṇāṃ yadetanmūrdhni saṃsthitam || 25 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe kalpānukīrtanaṃ nāma navatyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 290 ||

<291>

athaikanavatyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ
sūta uvāca
etadvaḥ kathitaṃ sarvaṃ yaduktaṃ viśvarūpiṇā
mātsyaṃ purāṇamakhilaṃ dharmakāmārthasādhanam || 1 ||
yatrā''dau manusaṃvādo brahmāṇḍakathanaṃ tathā
sāṃkhyaṃ śārīrakaṃ proktaṃ caturmukhamukhodbhavam || 2 ||
devāsurāṇāmutpattirmārutotpattireva ca
madanadvādaśī tadvallokapālābhipūjanam || 3 ||
manvantarāṇāmuddeśo vainyarājābhivarṇanam
sūryavaivasvatotpattirbudhasaṃgamanaṃ tathā || 4 ||
pitṛvaṃśānukathanaṃ śrāddhakālastathaiva ca
pitṛtīrthapravāsaśca somotpattistathaiva ca || 5 ||
kīrtanaṃ somavaṃśasya yayāti caritaṃ tathā
kārtavīryasya māhātmyaṃ vṛṣṇivaṃśānukīrtanam || 6 ||
bhṛguśāpastathā viṣṇordaityaśāpastathaiva ca
kīrtanaṃ puruṣeśasya vaṃśo hautāśanastathā || 7 ||
purāṇakīrtanaṃ tadvatkriyāyogastathaiva ca
vrataṃ nakṣatrasaṃkhyākaṃ mārtaṇḍaśayanaṃ tathā || 8 ||
kṛṣṇāṣṭamīvrataṃ tadvadro hiṇīcandra saṃjñitam
taḍāgavidhimāhātmyaṃ pādapotsarga eva ca || 9 ||
saubhāgyaśayanaṃ tadvadagastyavratameva ca
tathā'nantatṛtīyā tu rasakalyāṇinī tathā || 10 ||
ārdrānandakarī tadvadvrataṃ sārasvataṃ punaḥ
uparāgābhiṣekaśca saptamīsnapanaṃ punaḥ || 11 ||
bhīmākhyā dvādaśī tadvadanaṅgaśayanaṃ tathā
aśūnyaśayanaṃ tadvattathaivāṅgārakavratam || 12 ||
saptamīsaptakaṃ tadvadviśokadvādaśī tathā
merupradānaṃ daśadhā grahaśāntistathaiva ca || 13 ||
grahasvarūpakathanaṃ tathā śivacaturdaśī
tathā sarvaphalatyāgaḥ sūryavāravrataṃ tathā || 14 ||
saṃkrāntisnapanaṃ tadvadvibhūtidvādaśīvratam
ṣaṣṭivratānāṃ māhātmyaṃ tathā snānavidhikramaḥ || 15 ||
prayāgasya tu māhātmyaṃ bhuvanasyānukīrtanam
ailāśramavrataṃ tadvaddvīpalokānukīrtanam || 16 ||
sūryacandra gatistadvadādityarathavarṇanam
tathā'ntarikṣacāraśca dhruvamāhātmyameva ca || 17 ||
bhuvanāni surendrā ṇāṃ tripurāghoṣaṇaṃ tathā
pitṛpracāramāhātmyaṃ manvantaravinirṇayaḥ || 18 ||
vajrāṅgasya tu saṃbhūtistārakotpattireva ca
tārakāsuramāhātmyaṃ brahmadevānumantraṇam || 19 ||
pārvatīsaṃbhavastadvattathā śivatapovanam
anaṅgadehadāhastu ratiśokastathaiva ca || 20 ||
gaurītapovanaṃ tadvadviśvanāthaprasādanam
pārvatī ṛṣisaṃvādastathaivodvāhamaṅgalam || 21 ||
kumārasaṃbhavastadvatkumāravijayastathā
tārakasya vadho ghoro narasiṃhopavarṇanam || 22 ||
padmodbhavavisargastu tathaivāndhakaghātanam
vārāṇasyāstu māhātmyaṃ narmadāyāstathaiva ca || 23 ||
pravarānukramastadvatpitṛgāthānukīrtanam
tathobhayamukhīdānaṃ dānaṃ kṛṣṇājinasya ca || 24 ||
tathā sāvitryupākhyānaṃ rājadharmāstathaiva ca
yātrānimittakathanaṃ svapnamāṅgalyakīrtanam || 25 ||
vāmanasya tu māhātmyaṃ tathaivātha varāhajam
kṣīrodamathanaṃ tadvatkālakūṭābhiśāsanam || 26 ||
devāsuravimardaśca vāstuvidyāstathaiva ca
pratimālakṣaṇaṃ tadvaddevatārādhanaṃ tataḥ || 27 ||
prāsādalakṣaṇaṃ tadvanmaṇḍapānāṃ tu lakṣaṇam
bhaviṣyadrā janirdeśo mahādānānukīrtanam
kalpānukīrtanaṃ tadvadgranthānukramaṇī tathā || 28 ||
etatpavitramāyuṣyametatkīrtivivardhanam
etatpavitraṃ kalyāṇaṃ mahāpāpaharaṃ śubham || 29 ||
asmātpurāṇātsukṛtaṃ narāṇāṃ tīrthāvalīnāmavagāhanānām
samastadharmācaraṇodbhavānāṃ sadaiva lābhaśca mahāphalānām || 30 ||
etatpurāṇaṃ paramaṃ sarvadoṣavighātakam
matsyarūpeṇa hariṇā kathitaṃ manave'rṇave || 31 ||
asmātpurāṇādapi pādamekaṃ paṭhettu yaḥ so'pi vimuktapāpaḥ
nārāyaṇasyā''spadameti nūnamanaṅgavaddivyavapuḥ sukhī syāt || 32 ||
purāṇametatsakalaṃ rahasyaṃ śraddhānvitaḥ puṇyamidaṃ śṛṇoti
sa cāśvamedhāvabhṛthaprabhāvaṃ phalaṃ samāpnoti haraprasādāt || 33 ||
śivaṃ viṣṇuṃ samabhyarcya brahmāṇaṃ sadivākaram
ślokaṃ ślokārdhapādaṃ vā śraddhayā yaḥ śṛṇoti vā
śrāvayedvā'pi dharmajñastatphalaṃ śṛṇuta dvijāḥ || 34 ||
brāhmaṇo labhate vidyāṃ kṣattriyo labhate mahīm
vaiśyo dhanamavāpnoti sukhaṃ śūdra stu vindati || 35 ||
āyuṣmānputravāṃścaiva lakṣmīvānpāpavarjitaḥ
śrutvā purāṇamakhilaṃ śatrubhiścāparājitaḥ || 36 ||
iti śrīmātsye mahāpurāṇe'nukramaṇikā nāmaikanavatyadhikadviśatatamo'dhyāyaḥ || 291 ||

<292>

atha purāṇaśravaṇakālīnadharmāḥ
śraddhābhaktisamāyuktā nānyakāryeṣu lālasāḥ
vāgyatāḥ śucayo'vyagrāḥ śrotāraḥ puṇyabhāginaḥ || 1 ||
abhaktyā ye kathāṃ puṇyāṃ śṛṇvanti manujādhamāḥ
teṣāṃ puṇyaphalaṃ nāsti duḥkhaṃ syājjanmajanmani || 2 ||
purāṇaṃ ye ca saṃpūjya tāmbūlādyairupāyanaiḥ
śṛṇvanti ca kathāṃ bhaktyā'daridrāḥ syurna saṃśayaḥ || 3 ||
kathāyāṃ kīrtyamānāyāṃ ye gacchantyanyato narāḥ
bhogāntare praṇaśyanti teṣāṃ dārāśca saṃpadaḥ || 4 ||
soṣṇīṣamastakā ye ca kathāṃ śṛṇvanti pāvanīm
te balākāḥ prajāyante pāpino manujādhamāḥ || 5 ||
tāmbūlaṃ makṣayanto ye kathāṃ śṛṇvanti pāvanīm
śvaviṣṭhāṃ khādayantyetānnayanti yamakiṃkarāḥ || 6 ||
ye ca tuṅgāsanāruḍhāḥ kathāṃ śṛṇvanti dāmbhikāḥ
akṣayyanarakānbhuktvā te bhavantyeva vāyasāḥ || 7 ||
ye vai varāsanārūḍhā ye ca madhyāsanasthitāḥ
śṛṇvanti satkathāṃ te vai bhavantyarjunapādapāḥ || 8 ||
asaṃpraṇamya śṛṇvanti viṣabhakṣā bhavanti te
tathā śayānāḥ śṛṇvānte bhavantyajagarā narāḥ || 9 ||
yaḥ śṛṇoti kathāṃ vaktuḥ samānāsanasaṃsthitaḥ
gurutalpasamaṃ pāpaṃ saṃprāpya narakaṃ vrajet || 10 ||
ye nindanti purāṇajñānkathāṃ vai pāpahāriṇīm
te vai janmaśataṃ martyāḥ sṛkarāḥ saṃbhavanti hi || 11 ||
kathāyāṃ kīrtyamānāthāṃ ye vradanti duruttaram
te gardabhāḥ prajāyante kṛkalāsāstathaiva ca || 12 ||
kadācidapi ye puṇyāṃ na śṛṇvanti kathāṃ narāḥ
te bhuktvā narakānghorānbhavanti vanasūkarāḥ || 13 ||
ye kathāmanumodante kīrtyamānāṃ narottamāḥ
aśṛṇvanto'pi te yānti śāśvataṃ paramaṃ padam || 14 ||
kathāyāṃ kīrtyamānāyāṃ vighnaṃ kurvanti ye śaṭhāḥ
koṭyabdaṃ narakānbhuktvā bhavanti grāmasūkarāḥ || 15 ||
ye śrāvayanti manujānpuṇyāṃ paurāṇikīṃ kathām
kalpakoṭiśataṃ sāgraṃ tiṣṭhanti brahmaṇaḥ pade || 16 ||
āsanārthaṃ prayacchanti purāṇajñasya ye narāḥ
kambalājinavāsāṃsi mañcaṃ phalakameva ca || 17 ||
svargalokaṃ samāsādya bhuktvā bhogānyathepsitān
sthitvā brahmādilokeṣu padaṃ yānti nirāmayam || 18 ||
purāṇasya prayacchanti ye varāsanamuttamam
bhogino jñānasaṃpannā bhavanti ca bhave bhave || 19 ||
ye mahāpātakairyuktā upapātakinaśca ye
purāṇaśravaṇādeva te prayānti paraṃ padam || 20 ||
evaṃvidhavidhānena purāṇaṃ śṛṇuyānnaraḥ
bhuktvā bhogānyathākāmaṃ viṣṇulokaṃ prayāti saḥ || 21 ||
pustakaṃ pūjayetpaścādvastrālaṃkaraṇādibhiḥ
vācakaṃ viprasaṃyuktaṃ pūjayīta prayatnavān || 22 ||
gobhūmihemavastrāṇi vācakāya nivedayet
brāhmaṇānbhojayetpaścānmaṇḍalaḍḍukapāyasaiḥ || 23 ||
tvaṃ vyāsarūpī bhagavānbuddhyā cā''ṅgirasopamaḥ
puṇyavāñśīlasaṃpannaḥ satyavādī jitendri yaḥ || 24 ||
prasannamānasaṃ kuryāddānamānopacārataḥ
tvatprasādādimāndharmānsaṃpūrṇāñśrutavānaham || 25 ||
evaṃ prārthanakaṃ kṛtvā vyāsasya paramātmanaḥ
yaśasvī ca bhavennityaṃ yaḥ kuryādevamādarāt || 26 ||
nāradoktānimāndharmānyaḥ kuryānniyatendri yaḥ
kṛtsnaṃ phalamavāpnoti purāṇaśravaṇasya vai || 27 ||
sūta uvāca
matsyarūpī sa bhagavānmanave buddhiśāline
avāpodghātasahitamuktvā hyantardadhau tadā || 28 ||
iti purāṇaśravaṇakālīnadharmāḥ

iti śrīmaddvaipāyanamunipraṇītaṃ matsyapurāṇaṃ samāptam
